[p. r625]

ṛjuvimalā

athātaḥ śeṣalakṣaṇam | atra bhāṣyam-nānākarmalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamiti | tadanupapannam |
na hi dvitīye’dhyāye nānākarmalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttaṃ api tu nānāśāstralakṣaṇamityāśaṅkyāha---nānākarma-
lakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamiti | karmānubandhabhedācchastrabhedaḥ ataśśāstrabhedalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamityarthaḥ |
asyārthaḥ-sādhyānubandhabhedābhyāṃ śāstrabheda uktaḥ | tatsidhyarthaṃ cānubandhabhūtakarmabhedaḥ pratipāditaḥ
ato nānākarmalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamiti śāstrabhedahetubhūtanānākarmalakṣaṇavṛttatābhidhāne phalata ida-
mevoktaṃ bhavati śāstrabhedalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamiti | karmaṇo niyogasyānubandhāvacchinnatvācchāstrabhedo
bhavati | ato niyogānubandhabhūtena karmabhedena śāstrabhedasiddheḥ śāstrabhedalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamityevaṃ
phalata uktaṃ bhavati | nanu śāstrabhede satyaidamarthyamaṅgapradhānānāṃ na siddhayati na hi
bhinnaśāstraprameyāṇāṃ darśapūrṇamāsajyotiṣṭāmādyapūrvāṇāṃ parasparabhaṅgāṅgitvamasti | tena hetu-
hetumadbhāvastāvannāsti, kintu vaiparītyameveti yāvat |

uttaram---viniyogataḥ karmabheda uktaḥ | parasparaṃ padārthānāṃ viniyogataḥ kriyā-
kārakaguṇaguṇyādirūpeṇa sambandhādanubandhabhūtakarmaṇo bheda uktaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati
---viniyoganibandhanamanubandhabhedamāśritya śāstrabheda uktaḥ | tannibandhane ca śāstrabhede satyapi
siddhayatyevāṅgapradhānabhāvaḥ | samprati viniyogaśabdabhaidamarthyalakṣaṇasaṃbandhaparaṃ matvā codayati
(1) atra bhāṣyaṃ na mudrāpyate | punānagaramudritaśābarabhāṣyagatā puṭasaṅkhyā
bhāṣyodāharaṇasthaleṣu pradarśyate | (1) puṭa. 647

[p. r626]

---nanvidānīmeva viniyogaścintyate kathaṃ viniyogatobhedaḥ yāvatā bhede sati viniyogaḥ
ata eva ca bhedalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamiti hetvarthatayaiva bhedalakṣaṇopanyāsaḥ śeṣalakṣaṇasya |
saṃprati viniyogaśabdasya svābhimatamarthaṃ prakaṭayati---satyaṃ hetvarthatayopanyāsaḥ satyañca
bhedādviniyogaḥ tathāpi śāstrabhedo’pi viniyogata eva kintvasau viniyogaḥ padanimittakaḥ
na kāryanimittakaḥ | kimidaṃ padanimittaka iti ? pratipattau hetuḥ padanimittakaḥ vākyārtha-
pratipattau hetuḥ sambandhaḥ padanimittakaḥ | vākyārtho hi parasparānvitapadārtharūpaḥ pratīyate
parasparānvayaḥ padārthānāṃ padanimittakaḥ | anviteṣu svārtheṣu bodhyamāneṣvanvayo’rthagṛhīta iti
tadbhūtādhikaraṇe darśitaṃ spaṣṭayati---padārthānāṃ saṃbandhapratipattinimittaka ityartaḥ | padārthānāṃ
saṃbandha1pratipattyā gṛhyata ityarthaḥ| yastvayaṃ tṛtīye cintyate sa parasparasaṃbandhapratipattipūrvakaḥ
kāryanimittakaḥ | grahaṃ sammārṣṭīti kriyājanyaphalasaṃbandhitāmātreṇa padanimittakaḥ sambandhaḥ |
śeṣaśeṣibhāvastu grahasammārgayoḥ kāryanimittakaḥ | kāryāntaropayogitayā hi grahāṇāṃ tān
pratyeva sammārgasya śeṣatvam | ataḥ padanimittakapadārthasaṃbandhena vākyārthabhedena śāstrabhede
sati bhinnānāṃ vākyārthānāṃ parasparaṃ śeṣaśeṣicintā śeṣalakṣaṇamityucyate | nanu vrīhīn
(1) A. saṃbandhini pratipattyā gṛhyata ityarthaḥ

[p. r627]

prokṣatīti padārthānāmapi parasparaṃ śeṣaśeṣicintā yadi vākyārthānāmeva parasparaṃ śeṣaśeṣi-
bhāvaścintyate tadā śāstrabhedaḥ śeṣalakṣaṇasya heturbhavet na tvetadekāntato’sti | padārthānāmapi
parasparaṃ śeṣaśeṣibhāvacintanāt1 | aśravaṇamrāparādhyate bhavataḥ | vrīhibhiryajetetyanena
sahāsya śeṣaśeṣicintā, na punarvrīhipadārthamātratayaiva | vrīhirbhiyajetetyanena viniyoga-
śāstreṇa ye vrīhayo yāge karaṇatayā pratipāditāḥ śāstrārthībhūtāḥ taiḥ saha prokṣaṇasya śāstrārthīṃ2-
bhūtasya śeṣaśeṣicinteti | yadi śāstrārthānāṃ satāṃ śeṣaśeṣicintā, kimiti tarhi
śeṣalakṣaṇamityucyate | yadi tāvat padārthānāmeva śeṣaśeṣitā syāt tadā vrīhīn prokṣatīti
prokṣaṇaṃ prathamameva vrīhiśeṣatayaiva pratipādyata iti śeṣapratipattiparatvāccheṣalakṣaṇamevedaṃ syāt |
śāstrārthībhūtayostu paścāt kāryanimittakaḥ śeṣaśeṣibhāva iti dvayoḥ śāstrapramitayoḥ śeṣaśeṣi-
bhāvaḥ kāryanimittaka iti na śeṣalakṣaṇamevedaṃ syāt api tu śeṣilakṣaṇamapīti | uttaram
---viniyojyo hi śeṣaḥ, tadviṣayāṇi ca śrutyādīni, tasmāccheṣalakṣaṇamityucyate |
yo hi viniyujyate’ṅgatvena pratipādyate sa śeṣaḥ | śrutyādīnyapi tadviṣayāṇi tatpratipādakānyeva,
na śeṣitvapratipādakāni | tathā hi---vrīhīn prokṣatītyanena śāstreṇa śāstrāntarapratipāditān
vrīhin prati śeṣatvena prokṣaṇaṃ pratīyate na śeṣitayā | yadapi vrīhibhiryajeteti śāstraṃ tadapi
yāgaśeṣatayaiva vrīhīn pratyāyayati na prokṣaṇādikaṃ prati śeṣitayā | ataḥ śeṣatayaiva sarvatra
codanālakṣaṇo’rthaḥ | codanālakṣaṇārthavicāraśca mīmāṃsāśāstrāntarbhūta iti | tasmāccheṣalakṣaṇa-
mityucyate | yadi tarhi śeṣatayaiva codanālakṣaṇaṃ śeṣiparijñānaṃ tarhi kiṃ nimittaṃ ?
pramāṇāntaranimittakaṃ tat yadā hi codanayā vrīhyādīn prati prokṣaṇādayaḥ śeṣatayā jñāpitāḥ
(1) A. nirūpaṇāt (2) A. ekārthībhūtasya

[p. r628]

tadaivārthāt vrīhyādīnāmapi śeṣitvaṃ parijñāta bhavati | kasmāt punarna śeṣitā codanālakṣaṇetyatrāha
---lakṣyate hi śeṣī śeṣapratipattau na pratipādyate | yatpratipādanaparo hi śabdaḥ sa śabdārthaḥ
vrīhīn prokṣatītyādivākyāni ca vrīhiśeṣabhūtaprokṣaṇapratipādanaparāṇi prokṣaṇādīnāmeva vidhī-
yamānatvāt vrīhīṇāṃ tūddiśyamānatvāt | ato yuktaṃ śeṣalakṣaṇam | yadyavaṃ yadi yo
viniyujyate sa śeṣaḥ tadāpi viniyojyaḥ śeṣa iti vaktavyam | kimidaṃ śeṣaḥ parārthatvā-
dityucyate | pariharati---uktamasmābhiḥ padanimittako’pi viniyogo vidyata iti |
yadi viniyojyaḥ śeṣa ityucyate tadā padanimittako’pi viniyogo vidyata iti tadyogenāpi
śeṣatā syāt | ataḥ kārye viniyojyaḥ śeṣaḥ kathaṃ syāditi parārthatvādityuktam |
kārye viniyojyaḥ saṃbandhitvena pratipādyamāno yaḥ sa śeṣaḥ iti kathaṃ nāma jñāyetetyevamarthaṃ
śeṣaḥ parārthatvādityuktam | kathaṃ punaḥ parārthatvādityukte yasya kārye viniyogaḥ sa eva pratīyata
ityata āha---yasya kārye viniyogaḥ saṃbandhaḥ sa tadartha eva bhavati | ataḥ kārye
viniyogasvīkaraṇāya parārthatvāditi lakṣitam | kasmāt punaryasya kārye yasya viniyogaḥ
tasya kāryārthataiva bhavati | tatrāha---na kāryaṃ parārthaṃ bhavitumarhati kāryameva hi tanna
syādyadyanyārtham | asyārthaḥ-sarvo hi saṃbandho guṇapradhānabhāvamantareṇa na saṃbhavati | tatra kasya
guṇatā kasya pradhānateti cintāyāṃ yat kāryaṃ tanna tāvada्guṇabhūtaṃ kāryatvāvagamavirodhāt | yattva-
kāryabhūtamaparaṃ tat kāryārthamiti yuktam | atastatra puruṣasya niyopaḥ/?/ kāryārthatvenāvagamyate |

[p. r629]

ato yasya niyogena saha sākṣātsaṃbandhaḥ pratīyate sa niyogārthaḥ yasya ca tena sākṣānniyoga-
saṃbandhenārthāntareṇa saṃbandhaḥ so’pi tadartha eva | tasyāpi kāryārthatvena kāryatvāt | evamapara-
syāpi tatsambandhisaṃbandhasya tādarthyamūhitavtam | ataḥ śeṣaḥ parārthatvādityasyāyamarthaḥ---kārye
yasya viniyogaḥ sa śeṣaḥ parārthalakṣaṇaḥ | tasyedaṃ lakṣaṇamucyate | na punarupayuktetarasya |
kena punarhetunā kārye viniyojyalakṣaṇaḥ śeṣo jñāyate ? parārthatvāt kāryeṇa saha saṃbandhāvagamāt |
atredaṃ vyutpādyam | anvitapadārthātmakatvādvākyārthasya parasparakriyākārakabhāvadimātrātmakatvā-
dvākyārthasya parasparakriyākārakabhāvādimātrātmakatvāccānvayasya padārthānāṃ śeṣatvotpattau heturnāsti |
ato lakṣaṇārambho nirviṣaya1 iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | kāryasaṃbandhasyaivānyathā’nirvāhāt śeṣatocyate
tato lakṣaṇamārambhaṇīyamiti rāddhāntaḥ | grāhakavyutpattiḥ punaranupayoginīti nātra vyutpādyate |
śeṣitvābhimatasya hi kāryataiva kāryapratiyoginaḥ śeṣasya śeṣatve hetuḥ na viniyojyasya grāhaka-
gṛhītatāpi | ata ādhānādīnāmapi viniyogasiddhiḥ | grāhakeṇa punaragṛhītānāṃ viniyoge
pratiprakaraṇaṃ vyavasthā na siddhayatīti saptamādya eva grāhako vyutpādyaḥ | tathā cāha---“na ca
ṣaṭa्keṇāpyayamartho nirūpitaḥ | sambandhamātraparyavasāyāt | ubhayathā ca sambandhopapatteḥ”
 iti |
yata eva ca kārye viniyogo yasya sa tadartha eva bhavati | ata eva garbhadāsadṛṣṭānto
bhāṣye | anyathā garbhadāsatulyatā niṣpramāṇikā syāt | nanu parārthatāmātraṃ parārthaśabde-
nocyate kimidamatyantaparārtha iti | atyantaśabdābhāve kathamatyantārtho labhyata ityarthaḥ |
ucyate---ata evātyantaparārthaḥ yasmāt parārthatāmātrataivocyate yadi parārthaśabdena parārthā-
parārthābhidhānaṃ syāt tadā’tyantaparārthatā na labhyeta | ataḥ parārthaśabdastu parārthatāmātraṃ brūte
iti | ataḥ sādhu vivṛtam | eva sambandhabhāṣyaṃ vyākhyāya prasaktānuprasaktaṃ parisamāpya
(1) A. niṣphalaḥ

[p. r630]

prakṛtaṃ vyācaṣṭe---kaḥ śeṣaḥ kena  hetunā śeṣa iti | kimidaṃ praśnadvayaṃ ? kena hetunā śeṣa
ityetāvadvaktavyaṃ | siddhayati hi parārthatvāccheṣa ityetāvatā’bhimatam | ucyate---syāde-
tadevaṃ yadi siddhavadabhidhānaṃ hetoḥ syāt heturapyatra kathanīya evetyadoṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ |
yadi śeṣatve parārthatvameva heturiti siddham syāttadā kaḥ śeṣa iti praśno’narthakaḥ syāt |
yatastu heturapyatra kathayitavyaḥ | na ca sādhyamanabhidhāya heturabhidhātuṃ śakyata iti sādhya-
viśeṣanirdhāraṇāyārthavānayaṃ praśnaḥ | yata eva sādhyapuraḥ sarameva hetorabhidhānaṃ ata eva ko
dharmaḥ kathaṃ lakṣaṇaścetyuktam | nanu ca śeṣaḥ parārthatvāditi sādhyabhedo3 nirdiṣṭa eva |
na ca śeṣaśabdasyārtho na prasiddhaḥ, | tadāha---kiṃ punaḥ siddhasya kathane prayojanam ?
uttaram---upayuktādanyo yaḥ sa śeṣaḥ ityevamādinivṛttyartham | athātaḥ śeṣalakṣaṇa-
mityetāvatyukte upayuktādanyo yaḥ sa śeṣa ityevamādyapi pratīyeta | na ca tādṛśasya
parārthatā hetuḥ | ato hetuviśeṣābhidhānāya sādhyaviśeṣaṃ vaktuṃ praśno’pyarthavān | kathaṃ ca
viniyujyata iti ? ko’yaṃ praśnaḥ ? kena viniyujyata iti vaktavyam | kimidaṃ
kathaṃ ca viniyujyata iti |

(1) P. 652 (2) P. 652 (3) A. sādhyaviśeṣo 

[p. r631]

atrābhidhīyate---kena viniyujyata ityetāvatyucyamāne śrutyādivyāpāra eva tu
kevalaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ kathitaśca bhavati | na punaḥ saviniyogakānāṃ śrutyādīnām |
bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi ko doṣa ityatrāha---na ca niyogaśūnyāni śrutyādīni śeṣatāmāpādayituṃ
śaknuvanti 1kāryābhāve saktṛn juhotītivat | kāryanimittaṃ ca pārārthyamityuktam |
ataḥ kathaṃ ca viniyujyata ityuktam | sahetukaviniyogasiddhayartham | kāryarūpatvena
hi śeṣiṇaḥ śeṣasya tatra viniyogaḥ na punaḥ śrutyādimātreṇa | yata eva ca niyogavyāpāravaśena
śeṣatvamavagamyate | ata eva ca śrutyādīni ca viniyoge kāraṇānītyuktam | na punaḥ
śrutyādibhiriti | kāraṇamātratā śrutyādīnāṃ na sādhakatamatetyarthaḥ | śrutyādīni ca viniyoge
kāraṇānītyatrāpi kathamityanuṣaṅgādyojyam | eteṣāṃ ca balavadabalavattetyatrāpi katha-
mityanuṣaṅgo vaktavyaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati---kaḥ śeṣa iti upayuktetaro vā, avayavo vā,
parārtho vā ? tatrottaraṃ parārthaḥ śeṣa iti | kena hetunā śeṣo bhavatīti parārthatvena
kāryasaṃbandhitvena śeṣo bhavatītyuktam | kathaṃ ca viniyujyate ? kena prakāreṇa śeṣabhāvo’vagamyata
ityarthaḥ | uttaram---śrutyādisacivakāryabalena | śrutyādīni ca viniyoge kāraṇānīti | katha
śrutyādīni viniyogajñānotpattau sāhāyyaṃ yāntīti | dvāraviśeṣasamarpaṇenetyuttaram | dvayamapyetat
pratyadhikaraṇaṃ vakṣyate | teṣāṃ ca kathaṃ balavadabalattetyatra śrutiliṅgādhikaraṇe uttaraṃ bhaviṣyati |
(1) G. kāryābhāvāt

[p. r632]

kāryata eva śeṣatopapadyate | kāryeṇaiva śrutyādisacivena jñāyata iti tattvārthaḥ | eba tāva-
llakṣaṇārthaḥ | naitāvatā vinā śeṣaparijñānasiddhiḥ | anyadapi sādhanasiddhayarthaṃ kiñcidupo-
ddhātādibhiḥ śeṣaḥ parārthatvāditi sabhāṣyaṃ sūtraṃ vyākhyātameva pūrvam | ato na vyākhyāyate |
atha tatra kiṃ vṛttam ? yaistu dravyaṃ cikīrṣyate ityādi bhāṣyaṃ tadanupapannam | anena
bhāṣyeṇa punaḥ paunaruktya2mivāśaṅkayate taccānupapannam | tatra dravyaguṇādīnāṃ śeṣatvasyā-
pratipāditatvāt | atha saṃskārasya tatra 3śeṣatā pratipāditeti evamapi teṣāmarthena paunaruktyam
tataśca tatraivedaṃ yuktaṃ vaktum | sarveṣāṃ hi śeṣatve tatrokte atra śeṣatvapratipādana-
pratijñākṣepo yukta ityāśaṅkyāha---yaistu dravyaṃ cikīrṣyate guṇastatra pratīyate iti | uktaṃ
śeṣalakṣaṇaṃ manvānasyedaṃ codyam | etaduktaṃ bhavati-saṃskārasya śeṣatvaṃ tatroktam | tenā-
viśeṣeṇa 4idamayuktaṃ vaktum athātaśśeṣalakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyāma iti | uttarabhāṣyam “tatrā-
pūrvārthatā vyāvartitā”
 iti tadanupapannam | saṃskārāṇāmapyapūrvārthatvāditi; ato vyācaṣṭe
uttaraṃ tu niyogaviṣayatā tatrāpanītā śeṣatvaṃ tu ihaiva sarveṣāṃ pratipādyate | idānīmākṣi-
pati---ko’yaṃviniyogaviṣayatvāpanhavaḥ ayukta ityarthaḥ | kutaḥ ? na ca viniyoga-
śūnyāni śrutyādīni śeṣatāmāpādayituṃ śaknuvantītyuktam | caśabdo hiśabdasyārthe
(1) P. 649 (2) R. mityāśaṅkayate | (3) R. sya prati (4) idamuktaṃvaktuṃ

[p. r633]

yasya hi niyogaḥ kāryaḥ tatra puruṣo niyujyate | ataḥ tadabhyupagamenaiva niyogaviṣayatāpvastīti
manyate | tasmāccheṣatvaṃ niyogaviṣayatvāpahnavaśceti vipratiṣiddham | ataḥ śeṣatvaṃ vā
nāsti | apūrvārthatā vā nāpahotuṃ śakyate | ucyate-anabhijño bhavān viṣayaniyojyānām |
asyārthaḥ---guṇakarmaṇāṃ tāvadavaghātādīnāṃ kārakībhūtaṃ padārtha. sādhyatvenāvagamyate | tatra
yadi niyogo’pyaparo’ṅgīkriyate tadā sādhyadvayamekasmin vākye na saṃbadhyate parasparānuguṇatvāt
na ca niyojyaviśeṣaṇatā tasyāśrayituṃ yuktā | kriyāphalatayā tadviśiṣṭasya niyogasaṃbandhānupapatteḥ |
tasmādyo viṣayo niyogasya tasyāparaṃ sādhyāntaraṃ nāstīti abhyupeyam | ato bhavān viṣayaṃ na
jānāti | yena viṣayatāṃ guṇakarmaṇāmicchati | tathā niyojyasvarūpaṃ ca na jātāti |
yataḥ parasparānuguṇyārthaṃ kriyāphalaṃ niyojyaviśeṣaṇaṃ manyate | niyojyo hi niyogārthaṃ
kartavyatayā’vabudhyate | tasya ca tadeva sādhyabhūtaṃ viśeṣaṇatayā’nveti yanniyogādeva
sidhyati | na ca kriyāphalaṃ niyogādeva sidhyati | ataḥ kārakībhūtapadārthaviśiṣṭo
niyojyo bhavati | tena viṣayaniyojyā’nabhijñatayā guṇakarmaviṣayaniyogaṃ bhavānabhivāñchati |
yadyapi guṇakarmasu niyogo nāsti tathāpi kimiti viṣayo na bhavatītyatrāha---na brūmo guṇa-
bhūteṣu puṃso niyojyatā nāstīti | kiṃ tarhi viṣayastu na bhavatīti | adhikāra-
vidhyadhīnā liṅādyanubādyasti preraṇā | guṇabhūteṣu puruṣasya viṣayastu na bhavati | yadi
preraṇāviṣayo na niyogasya viṣayaḥ, kastarhi viṣayaḥ, ityatrāha---yasya punarniyoga-
siddhireva kāryaḥ sa viṣayaḥ | tadidaṃ sarvaṃ teṣāmarthetyatra prakaṭataraṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | ato yukta-
mukraṃ tatrāpūrvārthatā vyāvartiteti |

(1) P. 660

[p. r634]

dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣu bādariḥ

‘kaḥ punaratrābhiprāyo dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣveva pārārthyaṃ vadataḥ’
 | anena pūrvapakṣa-
mākṣipati | ekadeśī samarthayate---ayamabhiprāyaḥ---vibhaktibhiḥ tādarthyaṃ prātipadikārthasya
pratipādyate | tacca dravyaguṇayorupalabhyate ekahānyā krīṇātīti dravyasya tādarthyam |
ekahāyanīśabdasya yaugikatvena dravyavacanatvāt aruṇayeti ca guṇasya tādarthyaṃ saṃskārasya tu
tādarthyaṃ kathaṃ ? uttaram---atrāpi vibhaktayaiva tādarthyaṃ vrīhīnavahanti vrīhīnprokṣatīti
dvitīyayā | nanu ca prātipadikārthasya prādhānyaṃ dvitīyā pratipādayati na karmaṇaḥ
kriyāyā dravyaṃ prati guṇabhāvam | uttaram---akārakajño bhavān | kārakavibhaktayo hi kathaṃ
kriyānirapekṣāḥ | kriyāpekṣitvāt kārakāṇām | tasmāt kriyāpekṣameva prātipadikārthasya
prādhānyaṃ guṇatvaṃ vā | tena śrutyaiva dvitīyayā prādhānyaṃ vadantyā kriyāyā guṇabhāva ukta
eveti vaibhaktikameva saṃskārasyāpi śeṣatvam | yata eva vibhaktibhistādarthyaṃ pratipādyate |
ata eva vibhaktividhāne bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ kārakoddeśaḥ | karmaṇi dvitīyā kartṛkaraṇayo-
stṛtīyetyādi | yadi vibhaktayastādarthyaṃ na pratipādayeyustadā naiva kārakoddeśena vidhīyeran |
(1) P. 660

[p. r635]

kārakoddeśena vidhīyamānāḥ kriyāpekṣaguṇaprādhānyaṃ vadantyaḥ tādarthyaṃ pratipādayantyeva | ataḥ
kārakoddeśena vibhaktividhānādavagamyate tādarthyaṃ vibhaktibhiḥ pratipādyata iti | yadi
vibhaktibhiḥ tādarthyamavagamyate kathaṃ tarhi taddhitādiṣu tādarthyamavagamyate parṇamayī juhūrbhavatīti
taddhite, ‘śaraiḥ staritavya’
miti kṛtpratyaye, ‘yajamānasaṃmitā auduṃbarī bhavati’
 iti samāse |
kathaṃ copapadavibhaktayastādarthyamavabodhayeyuḥ akārakavibhaktitvāt | ato vibhaktivyati-
rekeṇāpi tādarthyadarśanāt tadvayatiriktamanyadapi (tāda)pārārthye pramāṇam dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣu
vaktavyam | saṃprati parasamarthanavādyāha---nanu copakāradarśanameva dravyaguṇasaṃskārāṇāṃ
pārārthye heturiti vādarermatam | kimatra vibhaktyādibhiḥ kriyate | tenopakārakatvāccheṣatā
dravyaguṇasaṃskārāṇām | tena vināpi vibhaktyā yasyopakārakatā tasya śeṣateti | yad्yupakāra-
darśanaṃ pārārthye hetuḥ vrīhayo’pi prokṣaṇasyopakārakāḥ syuḥ utpattebrīṃhyādibhirvinā prokṣaṇa-
syānupapatterityarthaḥ | tena vrīhayo’pi prokṣaṇaśeṣabhūtā bhaveyuḥ | na caivam | tasmānnopakāra-
darśanamātraṃ śepatve heturiti | ekārthena codyavādyeva samarthanavādinaḥ āśaṅkāmāha---atha
prayojanaṃ prati upakāradarśanaṃ hetuḥ | tena niṣprayojanatvāt prokṣaṇasya satyapyupakārye kāryatve na
śoṣitā | prayojanavattvāttu vrīhīṇāmeva śeṣitve sthite prokṣaṇasya saṃskāradvāreṇa vrīhayupakārakatayā
śeṣateti | dūṣayati---prayojanavattājñānamidānīṃ kutaḥ ? prayojanavattaiva vrīhīṇāṃ kuto’va-

[p. r636]

gamyate | punarāśaṅkāntaramāha---niyogata iti cet | niyogo hi kāryasvarūpatvāt svayaṃ
prayojanabhūtaḥ yasyāpi ca tatsaṃbandhaḥ tasyāpi tadvaśenaiva prayojanavatteti niyoganirvṛttyaiva prayo-
janavattāvagamaḥ | dūṣayati---evaṃ tarhi niyoganirvṛttireva pārārthye hetuḥ kimupakāradarśanaṃ
kliṣṭaṃ kariṣyati | yasmin kriyamāṇe niyoganirbṛttirbhavati tat tasya kāryamiti yata eva
niyoganirvṛttyā pārārthyamata eva vibhaktivyatyayo’pi prayājaśeṣeṇetyevamādiṣu siddho bhavati |
prayājaśeṣapratipattirhi vidhyapekṣitā na haviṣāmabhidhāraṇam | ataḥ prayājaśeṣapratipattiriyamiti
niyogavaśena sthite vibhaktivyatyayo bhavati prayojaśeṣaṃ haviṣṣu abhidhārayati iti | anyathā
vibhaktivyatyayo durbhaṇaḥ syāt | kṛttaddhitasamāsāśca tadvaśenaivaidamarthyapratipādakāḥ anyathā
teṣāṃ svabhāvata evaidamarthyabodhakatvābhāvāt | evaṃ copapadavibhakterasaṃkaraḥ (saṃskāraḥ) sidhyati
anyathā hi aniyamena śeṣaśeṣibhāvaḥ syāt | ekagranthetāha---kimiti tarhi bādariḥ
dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣveva śeṣabhāvaṃ manyate yadi niyoganirvṛttiḥ pārārthye hetuḥ | yadi
niyoganirvṛttyā pārārthyamavagamyate tadā yāgasyāpi niyoganirvartakatayā śeṣatā’styeva niyogaṃ
prati | tathā niyojyaviśeṣaṇaṃ svargādikamapi prati niyogasiddhidvāreṇa sādhakatvāt yāgasya
śeṣatā yuktaiva | tathā phalanirvṛttidvāreṇa puruṣamapi prati yāgasya śeṣatā yuktaiva | tathā
phalanirvṛttidvāreṇa puruṣamapi prati yāgasya śeṣatvaṃ nānupapannam | tathā phalasyāpi niyojya-
viśeṣaṇasya puruṣaṃ prati śeṣatā niyogākṣepeṇaivāvagamyata iti śeṣitvamastyeva | tathā phalabhāgita-

[p. r637]

yauvaudumbarīsaṃmānādau puruṣasya śeṣateti yāgaphalapuruṣāṇāmapi śeṣatvaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ | saṃprati
samarthanavādī samarthayate---na niyogaṃ phale puruṣe ca manyate | yāga eva niyogaḥ tannirvṛttyā ca
niyoganirvṛttiḥ | asyārthaḥ---lokavyutpattyanusāreṇa śabdādartho’vagamyate tatra loke grāmaṃ
gacchetyukte grāmagamanamanutiṣṭhan puruṣo dṛśyate | kāryapratipattiścānuṣṭhānaheturiti sarvajanasya
saṃviditameva | yadviṣayameva ca kāryajñānaṃ tadviṣayameva cānuṣṭhānamiti kriyāviṣayamanuṣṭhānaṃ dṛṣṭvā
kriyākāryatvāvagatihetutvaṃ śabdasyāvagamyata iti yajetetyevamādibhiryāga eṃva kāryatayā pratyāyyate
na ca kāryamanyārthaṃ bhavatīti nāsti yāgasya śeṣabhāvaḥ | ataḥ phale niyogo nāsti yāgena
phalaṃ sādhayediti niyogo nāsti tathā puruṣepi niyogo nāsti phalena puruṣaṃ saṃbadhnīyāditi, puruṣa-
viśeṣaṇamātratayaiva svargaśabdasya śravaṇāt | na ca puruṣaviśeṣaṇasya kriyayā saha sādhyasādhanabhāvaḥ
bodhahetvābhāvāt | ato yāganirvṛttimātreṇaiva kṛtaḥ śāstrārtha iti bādarirmanyate dravyaguṇasaṃskāraiśca
yāgo nirvartyata iti | atasteṣāmeva pārārthyaṃ manyate bādarirācāryaḥ | nanu yāgena svargaṃ
kuryāt ātmārthamiti niyogaḥ ātmanepadaprayogāt | asyārthaḥ---yajetetyātmanepadaprayogāt
kartrabhiprāye kriyāphale tasya vṛddhavyavahāre prayogāt | ātmārthaṃ yāgena svargaṃ kuryāt iti niyogaḥ
pratīyate tena phalaṃ prati yāgasya puruṣaṃ prati phalasya śeṣatā’stīti | pariharati---naivaṃ
śakyate śabdāt pratipattum | ayamabhiprāyaḥ---yadi svargasya kriyāphalatā syāt tadā ātma-
nepadaśravaṇāt ātmārthatā tasyāvagamyeta | na ca kriyāphalatā svargasyāvasīyate | atha[yathā]
odanaṃ pacatītyodanasya | kasmāt punaḥ kriyāphalatā nāvagamyata ityāha---dvitīyā hi kārakasya
prādhānyaṃ pratipādayati yasyāḥ kriyāyāstat kārakaṃ tat kriyāpekṣaṃ hi dvitīyā prādhānya

[p. r638]

pratipādayati na ca svargo yāgasya kārakaṃ yāgotpattāvasattvenāvyāpriyamāṇatvāt | odanaṃ
pacatītyatra taṇḍulānāṃ vikāryakarmabhūtānāmasti pāke vyāpāraḥ bhāvyavasthāvācinā tvodanaśabdena
taṇḍulā eva nirdiśyante | ataḥ kārakatvaṃ tatrāviruddham | nanu yadyapyasatvenāvyāpārādyāgaṃ
prati svargasya kārakatā pramāṇāntareṇa nāvasīyate tathāpi vedādevāvasāyiṣyata ityatrāha---na ca
vedāt kriyākārakarūpasaṃbandhāvagatiḥ | siddhasya hi anuṣṭhānaṃ codyate | asyārthaḥ-kārye’rthe
śabdasya prāmāṇyaniyamāt na siddhaṃ kriyākārakasaṃbandhaṃ prati śabdasya prāmāṇyaṃ, kintu
pramāṇāntarasiddhameva kriyākārakasaṃbandhamāśritya vedenānuṣṭhānaṃ pratyāyyate idamanena kurviti | na
punaridamanena sidhyatīti vedādavagatiḥ | yadi vedāt kriyākārakasaṃbandho nāvagamyate kathaṃ tarhi
yadāhṛvanīye juhotīti alaukikasyāhavanīyasya homaṃ prati kārakatvaṃ ? pramāṇāntareṇāna-
vagamāt | uttaraṃ---yuktaṃ pramāṇāntareṇa saṃbhavadarśanāt | asyārthaḥ---yadyapyāhavanīya-
śabdavācyatā pramāṇāntareṇa nāvagamyate tathāpi tasya dravyarūpatvāt vidyamānatvācca homaṃ prati
sādhanabhāvaḥ saṃbhavatīti pramāṇāntareṇāvagamyata eva | svarge punarnaitadupapadyate asatvātsvargasya |
yadi tathābhūtasyāpi vedaḥ kārakatāṃ brūyāt tadā pramāṇāntaravirodhena aṅgulyagrādivākyavat
vedasyāprāmāṇyaprasaṃgāt | kiñca kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ cātra svargaśabdaḥ na kartṛviśeṣaṇamīpsitatamaṃ
bhavati yathā rājapuruṣeṃ paśyeti | yadi tāvat dvitīyayaiva vibhaktyā svargo nirdiṣṭaḥ syāt
tadā kriyayā so’pi īpsita iti gamyate kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ tu svargaśabdaḥ | nanu ṣaṣṭhādye sthitaṃ
niyojyaviśeṣaṇasamarpakaḥ svargaśabdaḥ iti; kathamucyate kartṛviśeṣaṇatā svargaśabdasya | ucyate |
asyādhikaraṇasya pūrvapakṣe kriyaiva kāryatvenāvagamyate, kriyāṃ ca kāryatayā kartaivāvabudhyate iti

[p. r639]

kartureva niyojyateti | ataḥ kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ svargaḥ | na kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ kriyayepsitaṃ bhavati
yathā rājapuruṣaṃ paśyatīti karmaviśeṣaṇaṃ rājā darśaneneṣsito bhavati | nanu kāmipadasaṃbandhāt
svargasyepsitatā’vagamyata eva | satyaṃ, na yāgenepsitatā’vagamyate | dvitīyā hi kāraka-
vibhaktiḥ kriyāpekṣatvāt kārakasya samabhivyāhṛtakriyāpekṣitabhīpsitatvaṃ brūyāta, na kamiriti
niravadyam | upasaṃharati---ato na svargeṃ kuryāditi niyogaviṣayatā śakyate pratipattut |
tadidaṃ paramarahasyaṃ svargādeḥ sākṣāt kriyayā saha janyajanakabhāvo na ghaṭate kriyayāḥ kāraka-
samavāyiphalajanakatvaniyamāt svargādeścākārakasamavāyitvāt | yadyapi prītirūpatvāt
svargasyātmasamavāyitvaṃ tathāpi na kārakadaśāyāṃ samavaiti | na cedamāśaṅkanīyaṃ pūrvaṃ kārakatā
paścāt kriyājanyaphalabhāgiteti | yataḥ kriyāyogena kārakamucyate satyāmeva kriyāyāṃ
kriyāphalenotpannenaikārthasamavāyinā kriyā viruddhayeteti | ata eva kriyāphalānāmanantarabhāvi-
tā’’śrīyate | kathaṃ tarhi rāddhāntepi svargādīnāmutpattiḥ ? apūrvavyavadhāneneti na doṣaḥ | na
cāyamapūrve’pi niyamo dṛṣṭaḥ apūrvatvādeva | apūrvasiddhireva kutaḥ ? kriyāta iti brūmaḥ | nanu
kārakasamavāyiphalaniyamāt kriyāyāḥ kva kārake tadapūrvaṃ samavetamutpadyate kālāntarasthāyinyātma-
nīti na kiñcit duṣyapti | na ca puruṣasaṃskārakatā darśapūrṇamāsādīnāmāpādyate saṃskāryatve
pramāṇābhāvāt | vastusvabhāvena tu āśrayamātratayā puruṣamapūrvamāśrayati ityalamatiprasaṅgena |
atra ca naivaṃ śabdāt pratipattuṃ śakyata ityanena granthena svargayāgayoḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvaśaktireva
nāstītyuktaṃ kiñcetyādinā | athavetyaṅgīkṛtyāpi sādhyasādhanabhāvaṃ dvitīyābhāvādīpsitatvāna-
vagamānna yāgasya śeṣatā’stīti darśitam | evaṃ ca bhāṣyamapi tasmiṃstu kṛte svayameva tadbhavatītya-
bhyupagamyavādena draṣṭavyam | yadi svargasya kārakatā nāsti---kathaṃ tarhi svargakāmapada-
saṃbandhāt svargaṃ bhāvayediti bhāṣyam | atra hi svargaṃ bhāvayediti dvitīyāprayogaḥ
kārakatvābhāve nopapadyate | uttaraṃ---evametadbhāṣyamasmadīyavādānuguṇaṃ etadbhāṣyaṃ, na prati-
pakṣam | anyathā hi pratipakṣatve svargamiti śravaṇāt svargaṃ bhāvayediti syāt yadi

[p. r640]

svargamiti śravaṇādityuktaṃ syāt tadā kārakatā svargasyoktā syāt na caivamuktam | kathaṃ tarhi
svargaṃ bhāvayediti ghaṭate atrāha---ataḥ svayameva tad‌bhavatītyetadabhiprāyaṃ tat yataḥ svarga-
śabdo hi dvitīyānto noktaḥ | ataḥ svayameva phalaṃ bhavatītyetadabhiprāyaṃ na kārakabhūtaṃ
niṣpadyata ityetadabhiprāyam | nimittatvamātrakathanaṃ hi tat karmaṇaḥ svargotpattau nimittamātratā
karmaṇaḥ kathyate | na punaḥ kriyākārakabhāvo vivakṣitaḥ | tena gauṇo’yaṃ dvitīyāprayoga
iti yāvat | ataḥ prayojanaṃ svargo yāgasya na karma pākasyeva grāsaḥ pākasya grāmaḥ
[saḥ]prayojanaṃ, pākāyattatvāt sūpakāryagrāma[sa]siddheḥ odanaṃ phalam | ataḥ nāsti bhāṣya-
virodhaḥ | upasaṃharati---ato dravyaguṇasaṃskārāṇāmeva kriyayāsaha sādhyasādhanabhāvā-
vagateḥ prayojanavattayā kriyānivṛttyā prayojanabhūtayā prayojanavattvāt kriyāyāḥ śeṣa(śeṣi)
bhāvaḥ | na punaḥ prādhānyenāpratīyamāne phale puruṣe vā kriyāyāḥ phalasyāpi śeṣabhāvaḥ |
tasmātsūktaṃ dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣu bādaririti | “ na cet phalayāgau guṇabhāvena codyete kasya
puruṣaḥ pradhānabhūto bhaviṣyati”
 iti | ko’yaṃ prādhānyapratiṣedhaḥ ? śeṣatā hi
puruṣasya pratiṣedhyā, “na yāgaphalapuruṣeṣu śeṣatā’sti”
 iti pratijñānāt | ucyate |
śeṣatānivṛttyarthameva prādhānyaṃ pratiṣidhyate | kathaṃ ? sati hi prādhānye puruṣaḥ śeṣa iti
(1) P. 661-8

[p. r641]

śakyate vaktum | kimiti | puruṣaśabdo hyayaṃ svargakāme prayuktaḥ yadi ca tadarthaḥ svargaḥ
syāt tadā tadviśiṣṭasya śeṣatā syāt | svadravyaviśiṣṭasyeva prayājādeḥ | atādarthye punaḥ
svargasya kevalaṃ kartṛtayaiva śeṣabhāvaḥ dravyamātratayaivetyarthaḥ na puruṣaśeṣatayā, na svargakāma-
padavācyatayetyabhiprāyaḥ | atredamākūtaṃ-svargakāmaśabdena yaḥ kāmanāviṣayībhūtasvargaviśiṣṭaḥ
puruṣo’bhidhīyate sa ta tathābhūtena rūpeṇa tatraiva kartā bhavati yadeva tasya svargasya sādhanam |
yatpunarna tasya svargasya sādhanaṃ tatrāvivakṣitakāmyārthasaṃbandhinaḥ kevalaṃ kartṛtāṃ yāti |
ato’smin pakṣe dravyatayaiva śeṣabhāva iti | tasmādanavadyam |

karmāṇyapi jaiminiḥ phalārthatvāt | kathaṃ phalārthatā karmaṇaḥ ? uttaraṃ---kāmino-
hyadhikāro yāge | sa ca phalasādhanatāṃ yāgasyānāpādayan na siddhayati iti ṣaṣṭhe
vakṣyāmaḥ | ṣaṣṭhādyasiddhāntabalena hi sthitaṃ kartṛtvātiriktaṃ niyojyatvamapi puruṣasyāstīti |
sa hyevaṃ manyate mabhedaṃ kāryamiti | kāmī cātra niyojyatvena śrūyate | sa ca vivakṣitakāmibhāvaḥ
tenaiva kāryeṇa saṃbandhumarhati yadeva kāryaṃ tasya kāmyasādhanaṃ syāt | ataḥ kāmino(niyoge)yāge
(1) 661-12

[p. r642]

niyuktiḥ mamedaṃ kāryamityavagatiḥ phale sādhanatāṃ yāgasya anāpādayantī na siddhayatīti niyojya-
viśeṣānvayabalena yāgasvargayoḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvaḥ | atassiddhaṃ phale yāgasya śeṣatvaṃ ātma-
saṃbadhitvena phalasyeṣṭatvāt neṣṭaṃ parārthaṃ yataḥ | yadi punaḥ yāgaḥ phale sādhanatāṃ na pratipadyate
tatra ko doṣaḥ syāt ? yathā nityādhikāre vinā phalena adhikārānupapattirnāsti tathā
atrāpyastu kiṃ phalakalpanayā ? uttaraṃ---na khalu kaściddoṣaḥ pratīyate | pratīyate tu tathā
kāmino niyojyatvāt sādhyasādhanabhāvākṣepaḥ | evaṃ cātrādhikaraṇe svargayāgayoḥ sādhya-
sādhanabhāvo’stīti vyutpādyate | sthite caitasmin adhikaraṇe apūrvādhikaraṇe kriyātiriktam-
pūrvameva kāryatvenocyata iti siddhayati | ataḥ ṣaṣṭhādyabādaryadhikaraṇāpūrvādhikaraṇaiḥ tribhiḥ
sthitaiḥ apūrvavākyārthatā bhavatīti mantavyam |

nanu śeṣalakṣaṇe śeṣatā’sya yuktā pratipādayitum | kimidamucyate “enamevārthaṃ
ṣaṣṭhe’dhyāye sūtraireva sādhayiṣyati iti 2kiṃ vā śrutyādivyatiriktaṃ kiñcidanyat, śeṣatve
pramāṇamasti | atha śrutyādīnāmevānyatamaṃ tadihaivopanyasanīyam | kimiti sānyā-
sikaṃ kriyate | atrocyate---adhikārāpekṣiṇī hi sā śrutiḥ na śrutyapekṣo’dhikāraḥ
iti yuktaṃ sānyāsikam | asyārthaḥ-kamiśratyā yatra yāgaḥ phale śeṣo bhavati tatra
niyoganiyojyalakṣaṇa eva saṃbandho hetuḥ anyathā anvitābhidhānānupapatteḥ | ato’dhikārā-
pekṣitvādasyāḥ śruteḥ yuktaṃ sānyāsikatvam | uktaṃ yāgasya śeṣatvam |

(1) 661-13 (2) G. kiñcana

[p. r643]

phalaṃ ca puruṣārthatvāt | nanu ca yadyapi svargoṃ yāgāt sidhyati tathāpi kathaṃ tasya
kāminaḥ prati śeṣatetyāha---phalamapi puruṣaṃ pratyupadiśyate yaḥ svargo me bhavedityevaṃ kāmayate
tasya niyogaḥ na svargaḥ ātmānaṃ labheta iti | asyārthaḥ | ātmārthameva yaḥ svargaṃ
kāmayate tasya niyogaḥ tena svārthameva puruṣo yāgena svargaṃ kuryāditi śāstrārthaḥ | tena
kāminaṃ prati phalasya śeṣatā | kutaḥ punaḥ svārthatā svargasyeti | ekadeśimatenottaramāha
---ātmanepadaprayogāditi | asyārthaḥ-yadā3 hi adhikāralakṣaṇasaṃbandhabalena svargaphalatā
karmaṇāmavagatā tadā ātmanepadaśravaṇāt kartṛgāmitā phalasyāvagamyata iti | paramasiddhāntī
dūṣayati---nanu ca svargakāmaśabdādevedamavagamyate svargoṃ me bhavediti kimidaṃ ātmanepada-
prayogāditi | athāyamabhiprāyaḥ parasya duḥkha kāmayata ityapi kameḥ prayogāt anātma-
saṃbandhitvenāpi kāmyamānasya prayogo dṛṣṭa iti | tadayuktaṃ tatra paraśabdaprayogāt
duḥ khasya ca parasaṃbandhitvena abhīṣṭatvāt | yatra punaḥ paraśabdo na śrūyate nāpyaniṣṭaṃ duḥkhaṃ
tatra kenānyena saṃbandho’nyatra kāmayituḥ sarvāhīcchā svakīyasukhaprāptiduḥ khaparihāra-
(1) P. 661 (2) P. 662 (3) G. yathā

[p. r644]

viṣayaivotsargataḥ | paraśabdaprayogāttu duḥ khasya ca svasaṃbandhitāyā aniṣṭatvena parasaṃbandhitayā
kāmyaviṣayā bhavati | tadabhāve kāmayitṛsaṃbandhikāmaviṣayaiveti icchāsvabhāvenaiva svārthatā
svargasya labhyate |

atha manyase kāmyamānenaiva kāmaḥ saṃbadhyate svargo bhavatviti | na puruṣaviśeṣa-
ṇatveneti2 | pariharati---na hi kāmyamātreṇaiva kāmasya saṃbandhaḥ tannibandhanasukhāsmaraṇāt |
yannibandhanaṃ hi sukhaṃ smaryate tatra kāmanā bhavati | na ca sadbhāvamātreṇa sukhaṃ sukhaṃ bhavati api
tu svasaṃbandhitvena | kathaṃ punaḥ sukhahetubhāvenaiva kāmanetyāha---na hi kasyacit 3sadbhāva-
mātre kasyacitkāmo bhavati upekṣaṇīye heye ca kāmādarśanāt | adhunā ekadeśimataṃ
dūṣayitvā paramasiddhāntyevopasaṃharannāha---tasmādātmanepadopanyāso liṅgatayā pratipattavyaḥ na
punaḥ prāpte pramāṇatayā | yata eva svārthatā svargasya ata evātmanepadamapyupapadyata
iti |

puruṣaśca karmārthatvāt | svargaścetpuruṣārthaḥ svargakāmapadavācyatayaiva yajamānasaṃmitā
audubaṃrī bhavati ityevamādiṣu kartuḥ śeṣatvaṃ, kathaṃ ? adhikṛtasya niyuktasya kartṛtvāt
(1) P. 662 (2) G. viśeṣeṇāpīti (3) G. saṃbhava

[p. r645]

kartuśca yajamānaśabdābhidheyatvāt | tasmāt phalabhāgitayaiva karmaśeṣateti | tasmāt
sūktaṃ sūtratrayaṃ jaimineḥ vṛttikāramatopanyāsaḥ pūrvapakṣāparijñānārthaḥ | yadetat bhāṣyakāreṇa
vṛttikāramatamupanyastaṃ dravyaguṇasaṃskāraṇāmeva niyamena śeṣabhāvaḥ āpekṣiko yāgaphalapuruṣāṇāmiti
asau vṛttikārasya pūrvapakṣāparijñānapradarśanārthaḥ kriyāvākyārthābhiprāyeṇātra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | na cātra
pūrvapakṣe yāgādīnāmāpekṣikamapi śeṣatvamasti iti | kimiti kalpāntaraṃ na bhavati | tatra prathama-
sūtreṇa dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣu svaśeṣāntaranirapekṣaṃ paraśeṣatvamucyate tacca bādarerapi matamiti bādareru-
dāharaṇaṃ jaiminerepyetatsammatameva3 | uttarasūtraistu yāgaphalapuruṣāṇāṃ śeṣāntarāpekṣāṇāṃ śeṣatvamucyate
ityevaṃparatayā sūtrāṇāṃ vyākhyānāntaramidamupanyasyate, tatra vṛttikāragrahaṇaṃ pūjārthamiti |
uttaram---nahyāpekṣikaṃ śeṣatvamasti | yasmānnāpekṣikaṃ śeṣatvamasti tasmānnedaṃ kalpāntaraṃ
bhavatītyarthaḥ | viniyojyanibandhaṃ hi tat | yaddhi viniyojyaṃ taccheṣa ityetāvanmātreṇa
śeṣatvaṃ yasmāttasmānnāpekṣikaṃ śeṣatvam | viniyoga evāpekṣikaḥ tena viniyoganibandhanaṃ
śeṣatvamapyāpekṣikamiti cet; satyamāpekṣikaṃ yataḥ śeṣaḥ viniyojyaṃ śeṣiṇaṃ cāpekṣate | na
punarekasya śeṣaśeṣitāmāpādayati | na viniyogaḥ ekasya yāgasya svaśeṣāpekṣayā
śeṣitāmāpādayati śeṣyapekṣayā ca śeṣatāṃ, kintu śeṣyapekṣayā śeṣatāmeva kevalaṃ āpādavati |
tasmānna svaśeṣāntarāpekṣaṃ kasyacidapi śeṣatvam | kiñca vrīhyādīnāmapi svaśeṣabhūtāvaghātādi-
sāpekṣatvamityapi śakyate vaktum | ato nirapekṣatvasāpekṣatvakṛto viśeṣo nāsti | tadāha
(1) G. saṃbhava (2) R. pakṣa (3) G. asaṃmatameva

[p. r646]

---vrīhyādīnāmapi svaśeṣāpekṣatayā tulyatvāt | tasmāt parasya pūrvapakṣāparijñānena
svakauśalapradarśanārthaṃ paramatamupanyastaṃ bhagavatā bhāṣyakāreṇa pūrvapakṣāparijñānaṃ tu parasya
spaṣṭatvānnopabṛṃhitamiti |

teṣāmarthena saṃbandhaḥ | pūrvapakṣamākṣipati---uktaṃ niyogasavyapekṣāṇi śrutyādīni
viniyojakāni ityādinā sandihyata ityayuktamityevamantena | uktaṃ niyogasavyapekṣāṇi
śrutyādīni viniyojakānīti | śrutyādīni kāryabhūtaśeṣiṇo’napekṣamāṇāni santi niyogāpekṣāṇi
viniyojakānītyuktaṃ ādye | śāstrāntaravihitasaṃbandhitayā ca kāryalābhaḥ śāstrāntareṇa
yo vihitaḥ sa śeṣī tatsaṃbandhitayā ca kāryabhūtasya śeṣiṇo lābha ityuktam | 2kasmātpunareva-
mityāha---śāstrāntaravihitaṃ hi śeṣi bhavati na śeṣaśāstrādeva prādhānyamavagamyata iti
saktuṣu pradarśitam | kathaṃ tarhi ‘somena yajeta’
 iti 3svaśāstrapramita eva yāgaḥ śeṣī |
ucyate tatrāpi ‘jyotiṣṭomena yajeta’
 iti śāstrāntareṇa yāvadyāgasyādhikārasaṃbandho nāvagamyate
tāvadviniyogo na sidhyatīti | śāstrāntaravihitāni ca vrīhyādīni vrīhibhiryajeteti
(1) P. 665 (2) G. tasmāt (3) R. svavākya

[p. r647]

śāstrāntaravihitatvāt | kathaṃ tatsaṃyukteṣvavaghātādiṣu taccheṣatāṃ prati saṃśayaḥ ? yadi
śāstrāntaravihitā vrīhyādayo na bhaveyuḥ tadā saktunyāyena teṣāṃ śeṣitā na syāt tadā hi
kāryaśūnyaṃ hi śeṣaśāstraṃ kalpyaṃ kāryamapekṣate | pratyakṣaśrutakāryaṃ tu sandihyate ityayuktam |
ato nissandehatvannāsti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | nacāpūrvaprayuktatayā dharmāṇāṃ tadrūpasya ca sarvatrābhedāt
satyapi vrīhyādīnāṃ śeṣitve pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | kāraṇāvāntaravyāpārabhutotpattiniyogabhedena anyathā’nyathā
karaṇatvādāgneyādīnāṃ pratikaraṇaṃ dharmāṇāṃ vyavasthopapatteḥ | samarthayate---ucyate---niyogādanyat
viniyogagamyaṃ kāryamastīti na śakyate vaktum | na vrīhyādisaṃskāro viniyogasya
parasparapadārthānvayasya kāryabhūto bhavitumarhati aniyogārthatvaprasaṅgāt niyogārthatvenaiva ca vyutpatteḥ |
tasmāt vrīhinavahantītyādiṣvapi niyogaḥ kāryabhūtaḥ prayājādiṣviva | ataḥ prakaraṇaviniyo-
jyatayā sarvapradhānayāgārthateti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | “nanu ca yaistu dravyaṃ cikīrṣyate”
 ityatra
nāpūrvaṃ kāryamityuktam | ataḥ kathamatrāpūrvaṃ kāryamāśaṅkyate | uttaraṃ---satyamuktam |
sthite etasmin śeṣatve tat etadadhikaraṇasiddhānte sthite tadityarthaḥ | śeṣatvameva tvidānīṃ
niyoganiṣpattyā vinā nāvagamyata ityākṣepaḥ | rāddhāntī codayati---kimiti vā niyogo na
niṣpādyate vrīhīn prokṣatītyatrāpi | vrīhisaṃskārotpattipraṇāḍyā niyogo niṣpadyata eveti
bhāvaḥ | pūrvapakṣī samādhatte---yadyatra niyogārthaniṣpattiḥ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmityanena na

[p. r648]

saṃbadhyeta | asyārthaḥ---yadi taṇḍulādinirvṛttidvāreṇa niyoganiṣpattyarthatvasiddhaye taṇḍulādi-
nirvṛttirapi kāryamāśrīyeta tadā darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmityanena na saṃbadhyeta kāryāntaradarśanāditi2
bhāvaḥ | atha ‘phalavatsannidhau’
 iti saṃbandhaḥ3 | nirākaroti---sa tu niyogaikyalakṣaṇa
iti pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ | asyārthaḥ---darśapūrṇamāsādhikārasannidhau śrutāḥ prayājādiyāgāḥ tāda-
rthyena tadanvayina eva pratibhāsante kāryāntarasyādarśanāt | na ca tatra prathamameva kāryāntarabhūto
niyogaḥ pratīyata ityasakṛduktam | atrāpi cet sa eva saṃbandhaḥ yo’sau kāryā-
ntaradarśanena niyogaikyalakṣaṇaḥ na tarhi vrīhīn prokṣatītyetāvataiva niyogasiddhiḥ |
yadi sannipatyopakārakaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ syāt tadā vrīhisaṃbandhamātreṇaiva prokṣaṇasya niyogasiddhau
hetubhāvaḥ ityetāvataiva niyogasiddhiḥ syāt ityuktam | ārādupakārakatve punaḥ na
vrīhisaṃbandhamātreṇaiva niyogasiddhau hetubhāvaḥ | api tu avāntarāpūrvaprasiddhayetyetāvati
na niyogasiddhirityuktam | yato’vāntarāpūrvasiddhayā niyogasiddhiḥ, ataḥtuṣakaṇa-
vimoko na svargāvāptivat nāntarīyako niyogasya yathā svargāṃvāptiḥ niyojya-
saṃbandhasiddhaye niyogenākṣipyamāṇā nāntarīyakī na tathā taṇḍalanirvṛttiḥ tannirapekṣasyaivā-
vadhātasyāpūrvasiddhidvāreṇādhikārānupraveśāt | ato nāsti dravyacikīrṣeti | yadi
prayājādivat prakaraṇaviniyojyatayā pūrvaḥ pakṣa evaṃ tarhi ko’yamupanyāsaḥ ‘kiṃ sarve
ājye auṣadhe sānnāyye ca kartavyā’
 iti | naiṣa hi padārthānāmājyādīnāṃ prādhānyamasmin
pakṣe api tu yāgādīnām | tatrājyauṣadhasānnāyyarūpapadārthe’prādhānyopanyāso yukta iti |
(1) 665-1 (2) G. kāryāntarādarśa (3) G. virodhaḥ

[p. r649]

ucyate---naiva hi padārthānāṃ prādhānyapradarśanaparo’yaṃ granthaḥ kintu sarvayāgaśeṣatābhi-
prāyaparaḥ ājyādiśabdena ājyādisādhanako yāgaḥ lakṣaṇayocyate ityarthaḥ |

nanu cāsminnapi pakṣe sarveṣāṃ prayājādivat sākṣānniyogārthatvena śeṣatvaṃ pakṣe
śeṣaśoṣitā aṅgapradhānayoḥ prayājādyāgneyādyoḥ nopapadyate | sarvaśeṣāṇāṃ svavākyo-
pāttaniyogārthaśeṣatvāt | uttaram---prakaraṇapramāṇanirūpaṇe asya codyasya parihāraṃ
vakṣyāmaḥ | iha tu kāryāntarāsaṃbhavāt pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmityanena1saṃbandhā-
dityuktam | yadi taṇḍulādikāryāntaramāśrīyeta tadā darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmityanena na saṃbadhyeta
svātantrayeṇaiva taṇḍulādeḥ kāryatvāt | prayājādikāryamavāntarāpūrvaṃ tu na svātantryeṇa
kāryatayā’vagamyate | kintu kṣaṇikānāṃ yāgānāmaṅgatvānupapatteḥ atrāvāntarāpūrvāṇyaṅgatva-
siddhaye kalpyamānāni na svātantrayeṇa pratīyante | tasmāt prayājādīnāmiva 2niyogo hyetallakṣaṇaḥ
sarvaiḥ sarveṣāmabhisaṃbandhaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi vrīhin ājyamiti ca dvitīyāvibhaktiḥ |
asmin pakṣe saktuvadetadbhaviṣyati iti | vibhaktivyatyayo bhavatītyarthaḥ | gāṃ dadātīti-
vadvā anīpsitakarmaṇi dvitīyetyarthaḥ | atra bhāṣyam---“avadhātādayastu padārthāḥ śrutyā-
vidhīyante vākyenaiṣāṃ vrīhyādisaṃbandhaḥ”
 iti tadasminneva pūrvapakṣe ghaṭate guṇakarmatvāṅgīkāreṇa
(1) R. nāsaṃ (2) R. niyogābhedalakṣaṇaḥ

[p. r650]

tu pūrvapakṣe ghaṭate | siddhānte ca na śrutyaiva vidhānamiti darśayannāha---ata idamuktaṃ
avaghātādayastu padārthāḥ śrutyā vidhīyanta iti | bhāvārthānāṃ śrutyā niyogasaṃbandha ityarthaḥ |
tenāyaṃ bhāṣyārthaḥ---vidhīyante, vidhinā saṃbadhyate iti | “vākyenaiṣāṃ vrīhyādisaṃbandha”
 iti
bhāvārthānupraveśena vrīhyādīnāṃ niyogasaṃbandha ityarthaḥ | etatkila bhāṣyaṃ avaghātādīnāṃ
vrīhyādisaṃbandhitā vākyeneti vadati | tadā ca vrīhyādiśeṣataivāvaghātāderiti idamāpadyata
iti matvā vyākhyātam | eṣāmavadhātādīnāṃ śeṣitayā vākyenaiva vrīhyādisaṃbandha iti
bhāṣyākṣarārthaḥ na tu pradhānabhūtānāṃ vrīhyādīnāṃ niyogasiddhirityarthaḥ | kutaḥ ? na hi dravya-
paryanto niyogaḥ svayameva sādhyatvāt | na hi dvayamekasmin vākye sādhyaṃ bhavati eka-
vākyatvāvagamavirodhāt | na ca svargavat vrīhayo’pi sādhyatvena saṃbandhamarhanti parasparānanu-
guṇatvāt | na hi te niyojyaviśeṣaṇatvenānvitāḥ | na ca dvitīyāvagatasādhyatvānugrahāya
niyojyaviśeṣaṇatā kalpayitumapi śakyā | dvitīyānirdeśādeva kriyājanyaphalabhāgitvena
aniyogaphalatvāt | yasya hi niyojyaviśeṣaṇasya sataḥ sādhyatvamiṣyate na tasya kriyā-
phalatvaṃ kriyāphalatve tatkāmasya niyogena saṃbandhānupapatteḥ | yadi hi niyogaḥ tasya nimittaṃ bhavet
tadā tatkāminaḥ tatkāmitayaiva niyogasaṃbandhaḥ syāt | anyathā avivakṣitatatkāmasaṃbandho
dravyamātratayaiva saṃbadhyeta niyogaphalatve ca kriyāphalatvānupapattiḥ | yadeva hi kriyāto vyavadhānāna-
pekṣaṃ sākṣānniṣpadyate tadeva kriyāphalaṃ kārakasamavāyiphalatvāt kriyāṇām | etenādhānasya
agnyarthanā niyogaviṣayatā ceti pratyuktam | kathaṃ tarhi āśrayiṣu dravyasaṃskāro’pi vivakṣitaḥ
pratyakṣadṛṣṭatvāt na tu kriyāphalatvāditi | tasmāt sūktaṃ sarve sarvārthā iti prayājādivat 
(1) P. 665-9 (2) R. svayamasā

[p. r651]

prakaraṇaniyojyatayā | kimidamadṛṣṭaṃ bhavatīti | idaṃ hi bhāṣyaṃ dravyaniṣṭhatvena adṛṣṭa-
upakāro bhaviṣyati iti idamāheti manyate | uttaraṃ---adṛṣṭamapūrvaṃ niyoga ityekamida-
mityuktaṃ tena‘apūrvaṃ kṛtvā’
 ityatra | ayamatra pūrvapakṣasaṃkṣepaḥ---tuṣakaṇavimukteḥ adhikāra-
nirapekṣāyā api sādhyatvasaṃbhavāt tādarthye satyavaghātādīnāṃ adhikārānupraveśāsaṃbhavāt
tadanupraveśasiddhayarthaṃ varaṃ prayājāditulyataivāśrīyatām | prayājādyavāntarāpūrvaṃ hi adhikārāpekṣameva
kāryamiti tādarthye yukta evādhikārānupraveśa iti |

phalaṃ tu saha ceṣṭayā śabdārtho’bhāvādviprayoge syāt | atrocyate---syāt sarvārthatā
yadi 1kāryārthe virodho niyogasya syāt | natvatra virodho vidyate | kathaṃ na vidyate
virodhaḥ ? kathaṃ vā vidyate virodhaḥ ? uktaṃ kāryāntaratvāttuṣakaṇavipramokasyeti | na
kāryaṃ kāryāntareṇa saṃbadhyate yathā svargaḥ paśubhiḥ | evaṃ niyogaḥ kāryaṃ tuṣakaṇavipramo-
kaśceti vipratiṣiddham | uttaramāha---anabhijño bhavān viṣayaniyojyānāmityuktam |
idamatrākūtam | dravyaṃ tāvat avadhātādiṣu dvitīyāsaṃyogena sādhyatayā pratīyate | na ca
yasya sādhyāntaramasti tasya niyogaviṣayatā, parasparānanuguṇasādhyadvayayogena ekavākyatvānupapatteḥ |
(1) G. kāryavirodho

[p. r652]

na ca tadeva niyojyaviśeṣaṇamityānuguṇyaṃ vācyam | akārakabhūtapadārthaviśiṣṭasya
niyojyatvāt | kā punaryuktiḥ akārakabhūtapadārthaviśiṣṭasya niyojyatve iti | ucyate |
yatkhalu kriyāsambandhi tatkārakam | tacca dvividham kiñcitkriyāsādhyaṃ kiñcicca na, tatra
yatpūrvaṃ tatkarma dvitīyaṃ karaṇādikaṃ taccobhayamapi na niyojyaviśeṣaṇaṃ bhavati | ubhayathā hi
viśeṣaṇatā svarūpasaṃbandhitayā kāmanāviṣayatayā vā, tatra na tāvat svarūpasaṃbandhitayā,
niyoganiyojyalakṣaṇasambandhātprāgasiddheḥ | na ca kāmanāviṣayatvena karaṇādi 2siddhayati |
kiyājanyāpūrvasādhyatvānupapatteḥ | karaṇādīni karma ca kriyā’sādhyatayaiva na niyojya-
viśeṣaṇaṃ, niyogasādhyaṃ hi kāmyamānatayā niyojyaṃ viśinaṣṭi nānyam[ta] | tasmāt guṇakarmaṇāṃ
niyogaviṣayatvaṃ yo bravīti saḥ viṣayasvarūpaṃ niyojyasvarūpaṃ ca na jānatīti | idameva
vivarītumupakramamāṇaḥ pṛcchati---kathamanabhijña iti | uttaram---itthamanabhijñaḥ | viṣayo hi nāma
2adhikārasaṃbandhī svargakāmo yajeteti | yo hyadhikārasaṃbandhayomyaḥ sa viṣayaḥ | sa eva
cādhikārasaṃbandhayomyaḥ yatra niyogaḥ sādhyabhūtaḥ pratīyate | tathāpi kiṃ ? ityāha---tasya ca
viṣayasya niyogasiddhireva prayojanaṃ dṛṣṭe’rthe saṃbhavatyapi yathā godohanena paśukāmasya
praṇayediti | sādhyadvayasyaikatrānvayavirodhāt sādhyāntarasyānanupraveśāt | nanvatnāpi sādhyāntaraṃ
paśavaḥ ityāha---nanu ca atrāpi phalasiddhiḥ prayojanaṃ na niyogasiddhiḥ uttaraṃ---niyoga
siddhayarthā phalasiddhiriti ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ tenātnāpi niyogasiddhireva prayojanam | yata
eva ca niyogasidhyarthā phalasiddhiḥ, ata eva ca karmāṇyapi jaiminiḥ phalārthatvāt |

(1) kāmādhikāra bṛ. bhā. (2) G. sādhyaṃ (3) G. adhikārapadasaṃ

[p. r653]

etaduktaṃ bhavati-sādhyabhūto vidhyarthaḥ pratīyate sa ca viṣayasiddhayā siddhayati | sa eva
ca viṣayānuṣṭhāne pravartate yo vidhyarthaṃ svasaṃbandhitayā sādhvabhūtamavabudhyate | na cākāmasādhane
niyoge kāmī kāmitayaiva ‘mamedaṃ kāryaṃ’
 iti boda्dhṛtayā’nvayamarhati iti svasiddhihetubhūta-
viṣayasaṃpādanasamarthapuruṣalābhāya phalotpattau nimittabhāvamavalambate svayameva 2vidhyartha iti
niyogasidhyarthā phalasiddhiḥ | ato na phalaṃ nāma pṛthak prayojanāntaramiti | niyojyaḥ
idānīṃ kaḥ ? akārakabhūtapadārthaviśiṣṭa iti ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | idaṃ prāgeva varṇitam---karturanyo
niyojyo na bhavati kartā ca kārakabhūtapadārthaviśiṣṭo’pi syādityabhiprāyavān pṛcchati
---kaḥ punarasau ? iti | uttaraṃ---vakṣyamāṇanyāyabalena adhikṛtaḥ karmaṇi iti
īśvarabhūtaḥ karmaṇītyarthaḥ | punarapi tenaivābhiprāyeṇākṣepagarmaṃ pṛcchati---kimidaṃ adhikṛta iti |
ekagranthenāha---nanu ca kartaivādhikṛtaḥ nāparo’dhikṛto nāmeti | uttaraṃ---yathā na
karturadhikāraḥ | adhikṛtasya kartṛtvaṃ ityetadapi ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | yadyevaṃ bhavetāṃ nāme-
dṛśau viṣayaniyojyau tathāpi kimāyātaṃ “vrīhīn prokṣati”
 ityatra | uttaram---kāryāntare’pi
dravyagate saṃskāre sati 3niyogāvirodha itīdamāyātam |

(1) niyogāsiddhireva (2) G. sidhyarthaḥ (3) G. niyogavirodhaḥ

[p. r654]

ayamāśayaḥ | yadi yasyāpi kāryāntaramasti tasyāpi viṣayatā syāt tadā dravyagata-
kāryāvagame’pi niyogasya svavākya eva śrutatvāt paravākyagataniyogānupraveśābhāvāt
tadasaṃsparśe vrīhyādigataṃ kāryaṃ tadananuguṇamiti kāryadvayavirodhaḥ syāt | yathokte tu
viṣayalakṣaṇe sādhyāntaradarśanena viṣayatvābhāvāt nirviṣayatvācca liṅgādeḥ niyogajñānānupapatteḥ
svavākye niyogābhāvāt niyogasaṃbandhasidhyarthaṃ vākyāntaragataniyogānupraveśāya tatsaṃbandhāvastha-
vrīhyādisaṃskāraḥ tadapekṣita evāyaṃ pratīyata iti tatkāryānupraveśāt kāryāntaramapīdaṃ na
virodhamāvahati | yathoktaniyojyalakṣaṇābhāve tu yathoktaviṣayalakṣaṇameva na bhavati | ekasminnapi
vākye niyojyaviśeṣaṇatayā kriyāphalasyāpyanvayopapatteriti yathoktaniyojyalakṣaṇasyāpyastye-
vopayogaḥ | kutaḥ punaḥ paravākyagataniyogānupraveśa ityatrāha---vrīhīna्prīkṣatīti pradhānabhūta-
viṣayārthavrīhisaṃsparśāt niyogasya ca niyogāntarāvagatiḥ kāryāṃśe samidādīnāmiva
prokṣaṇaṃ prati pradhānabhūta1viṣayārthā ye vrīhayaḥ tanniyogārthaṃ saṃspṛśati | ato vrīhidvāreṇa niyo-
gānupraveśasaṃbhavāt na niyogāntaraṃ svavākyagatamavagamyate yathākāryāṃśe adhikārāśe
samidādīnām |

abhiprāyaṃ vivarītuṃ codyapūrvakamāha---nanu ca kāryāntaramityuktam | kāryāntaratve
ca niyogāntarāvagatiḥ someṣṭīnāmiva | uttaraṃ---satyam 1yadi kāryāntarāvagatiḥ
syāt | kathaṃ na kāryāntaraṃ adhikāraniyogākṣepāt taṇḍulanippatteḥ svargasiddhivat
na kāryāntaram | yathā niyogākṣitā svargasiddhiḥ na kāryāntaraṃ tathā taṇḍulaniṣpattirapi |
(1) G. vidhyarthā (2) G. yadi kāryāntaraṃsyāt tadā niyogāntarāvapatiḥ
syāt

[p. r655]

tasmādadhikāraniyoga evāyaṃ sādhyo vrīhisaṃskāradvāreṇa vrīhīn prokṣatīti, prayājādī-
nāmiva | prayājādibhiḥ iyān viśeṣaḥ | dṛṣṭakāryadvārakastvayam | sākṣānniyogakārya
eva prayājādiḥ | prathamaṃ niradhikāratayā sādhikāraniyogakāryatayā pratīyate prayājādiḥ |
paścāttu svagatakāryavimarśastatretyuktam | evaṃ kāryāntarepi nāsti virodha ityuktam |
saṃprati kāryāntaraṃ vinā’nupraveśānupapatteḥ avaśyamaṅgīkaraṇīyaṃ kāryāntaramityāha---na ca
dvārameva pratiyandhakaṃ bhavitumarhati viśeṣato dṛṣṭasākṣānniyogasaṃbandhināmapi dvāraṃ
karaṇopakāralakṣaṇaṃ kalpyam | idaṃ tu 1klṛptamiti viśeṣaḥ | yayā ca dvārakalpanā-
mantareṇa pradhānetikartavyatāsiddhirna bhavati tathā prakaraṇapramāṇasvarūpanirūpaṇe vakṣyāmaḥ |
anenedamuktam | prayājāditulyatvepi dvārasyāvaśyakalpyatvāt kimiti dṛṣṭadvāraṃ parityajyata
iti | tadidamuktam---pramāṇābhāvānna sarvārthateti | na hi dṛṣṭe saṃbhavati saṃbandhahetau
kalpyaṃ hetvantaramātmānaṃ labhata ityarthaḥ | nanu ca biniyogataḥkāryaṃ na kāryato viniyoga
iti | asyārthaḥ---viniyogapekṣayā kāryaṃ na punaḥ kāryāpekṣo viniyoḥ iti viniyogānusāreṇa
vrīhimātrasaṃskāra evāyaṃ pratīyate | anyathā hi vrīhiśabdasya apūrvasādhanāṅgalakṣaṇāparatvaṃ
syāt | ataḥ kathaṃ kāryāntarānupraveśa iti |

(1) G. dṛṣṭamiti

[p. r656]

uttaram---parihṛtametat-śāstrāntaradṛṣṭaviṣayatvāditi | śāstrāntareṇa yo dṛṣṭaḥ
kāryasaṃbandhitayā sa viniyogasya viṣayaḥ tasminnaparaṃ viniyujyana ityanena idaṃ parihṛtam |
yathā svatantradravyaṃ śeṣi na bhavati iti | yata eva śāstrāntarapratītaṃ śeṣi bhavati
ata eva ca bhedābhedalakṣaṇaṃ heturiti | etacca vyākhyātam | tasmātsūktaṃ phalaṃ tu saha
ceṣṭayeti | dṛṣṭamātreṇa kāryasya svīkāra iti sūtre pratīyate taccānupapannamiti matvā vyācaṣṭe
---kāryānurodhī niyoga ityarthaḥ | avāntarakāryamapi niyogo’nurudhyate apekṣata ityaryaḥ |
ato ye yatra śrūyante iti siddham | ataḥ sūktaṃ phalaṃ tu tatraiva kartavyeti |

dravyaṃ cotpattisaṃyogāt | atra bhāṣyakāreṇa sandehaḥ kṛtaḥ---kiṃ yo yaḥ śakyate
ebhiḥ kartuṃ tasmai tasmai prayojanāya ita yat yena saṃyuktaṃ tasmā eveti | tatra kāraṇabhūtasandehamāha---
idaṃ cintyate---sphyaśca kapālāni ceti sannidhāpanavidhayaḥ saṃyogato’viśeṣāt | prakara-
ṇāviśeṣācceti kiṃ sarvārthatayā’vagamyante uta sphyenoddhantītyevamādibhirupasaṃhriyanta
iti | asyārthaḥ---ye’mī sannidhāpanavidhayaḥ niyogasaṃbandhamātravidhayaḥ te kimupasaṃhriyante atha
vā anupasaṃhriyamāṇāḥ svayameva sarvatra kārye viniyujyante iti | kiṃ tāvadyuktam |
nopasaṃhriyante iti | kutaḥ ? | aśakteḥ upasaṃhārāśakteḥ | kathamaśaktiḥ ? uttaraṃ---

[p. r657]

sannidhāne hi sati niyogaikyāt dvāramātrānujñānācca dṛṣṭadvārakatvameva śakyate vaktum |
kalpyapratipakṣatvāt na punaḥ dṛṣṭaviśeṣadvāramapi | asyārthaḥ---sannidhāpanavidhibalena tāvat
ete sphyādayaḥ kāryaviśeṣanirapekṣā eva niyogārthā avagamyante | tatra kena dvāreṇetyapekṣāyāṃ
dṛṣṭameva dvāraṃ niyogā’numanyate nādṛṣṭam | na punaḥ dṛṣṭatve’pi dvāraviśeṣam | niyogānanumataṃ
ca na śāstrārtha iti | tasmācchaktito vyavasthā | atrābhidhīyate---syādetadevaṃ yadi
dvāramātramevānurudhyate niyogena | kasmātpunarnaivamityāha---saṃbandhānusārī hi niyogaḥ
yadi saṃbandhaṃ niyogo nānurudhyate niyogāsaṃbandhamapi yadi dvāraṃ syāt tadā dvāramātrameva niyoge-
nānurudhyeta saṃbandhānusārī tu niyogaḥ | uktaṃ śāstrāntaravihiṛ/?/taviṣayatvāt (vi)niyoga-
śāstrasyeti | yo hi śāstrāntareṇa niyogena saṃbandhīkriyate tadviṣayo niyogaḥ nānyaviṣaya
iti saṃbandhānusārī niyogaḥ | nanvevamapi mābhūdasaṃbandhe viniyogaḥ saṃbandhe tu sarvatra viniyomaḥ
syāt ityatrāha---ata sannidhāpanavidhayo na saṃbandhavidheḥ pratipakṣā bhavitumarhanti tanmū-
latvāt | saṃbandhavidhinā hi saṃbandhe śeṣiṇaḥ āpādite paścāt sannidhāpanavidhibhiḥ
viniyogaḥ | ataḥ siddhamatrāpi ye yatra śrūyanta iti | yathā śeṣiṇaḥ sannidhānāpādakasya
saṃbandhavidheḥ sannidhāpanavidhiḥ na pratipakṣaḥ tathā sphyenoddhantītyasyāpi na pratipakṣo bhavitumarhati
aviśeṣāditi siddhā atrāpi vyavasthā na kevalamavaghātādīnāmiti |

[p. r658]

arthaikatve | saṃskāradravyavidhayo ye yatra śrūyante te tatraivetyuktam | guṇe idānīṃ
cintyate | kiṃ ye yatra śrūyante tatraiva uta kṛtsne prakaraṇe niveśa iti | anenāvāntara---
saṅgatimāha | saṃskārāṇāṃ śrutyā viniyoga uktaḥ | tadanantaraṃ ca vākyena dravyāṇāṃ
viniyoga uktaḥ dravyaviniyoganirvāhyaśca guṇaviniyoga iti sa tadanantaraṃ cintyate | pūrvapakṣābhāva-
saṃśayaṃ ācikṣipsurāha---nanu cānvitapadārthatvāt padānāṃ naiva kevalaṃ guṇavacanatā
saṃbhavati | anvitāḥ padārthāḥ padaiḥ abhidhīyante | tena dravyānvitā eva guṇā
guṇapadairabhidhīyante tathābhūtāśca śaknuvantyeva kriyāsādhanatāmupagantumiti bhāvaḥ | dṛṣṭānta-
gāha---yathā na kevalākṛtivacanatā ālabheta iti | yathā ca vāyavyaṃ śvetamālabheta iti
na kevalaguṇavacanatā śakyate vaktuṃ, anyathā tadapyatrodāharaṇaṃ syāt | pariharati---satyaṃ
aparityaktadravyasyaivānvayo yatra dṛśyate amūrtasya naiṣa tadudāharaṇam | yatra punaḥ parityakta-
dravyābhiprāye kevalaguṇavacanasyānvayaḥ yathā paṭasya śula iti bhavati tatra saṃśayaḥ
kārakavibhaktau satyāmapi kiṃ kriyānvayī guṇavacanaḥ uta kevaladravyānvayyeveti |
(1) P. 673

[p. r659]

ayamevātra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ ekavākyatayā kraya evāruṇimā viniyujyate uta vākyaṃ bhitvā prakaraṇa-
viniviṣṭeṣu dravyeṣu vidhīyata iti |

nanu ca kevalaguṇavacanatā’sya asmin vākye naiva pratīyate | tatra hi paṭasya
śukla iti ṣaṣṭīnirdiṣṭatvāt dravyasya tatsaṃbandhāvagateśca śuklaśabdasya yuktaṃ kevalaguṇa-
vacanasyānvayitvam | yadi hi dravyasya saṃbandhaḥ śuklaśabdasya na syāt tadā na syāt
kevalaguṇānvayitā | dravyasaṃbandhetu dravyāntarasya dravyāntarasaṃbandhābhāvāt kevalaguṇānvayitā |
iha punaḥ aruṇayā iti kārakanirdeśāt kriyāsaṃbandhāvagatiḥ na dravyasaṃbandhāvagatiriti na
kevalaguṇānvayitvam | yuktyantaraṃ cāha---tadavagateśca kriyāsaṃbandhāvagateśca guṇamātrasyāpi
kriyānuṣṭhānāśakteḥ | aparityaktadravyasyaivānvayo yuktaḥ śvetamālabheta itivat | pari-
harati---ucyate | na dravyasaṃbandhāvagatireva tatra kevalaguṇavacanatve hetuḥ | kastarhi ?
dravyāntarānavagatirapi hetuḥ | yadi hi dravyāntaramapi tatrāvagamyate tadā na syāt
kevalaguṇavacanatā paṭasya śuklo bhāga itivat | tenopātta1dravyavat dravyāntarānupādānamapi
kevalaguṇavacanatve hetuḥ | kathaṃ punaratra dravyāntarāvagatirnāsti atrāha---dravyāntarāvagatau hi
paṭasya śuklo bhāgaḥ itivat guṇaguṇinoḥ saṃbandho na syāt | yadyevaṃ kimāyātaṃ aruṇayeti
(1) R. dravyatvavat

[p. r660]

idamāyātam | atrāpi hi ekahāyanyā na dravyāntaraṃ pratīyate | ekavākyatve sati
ekahāyanyavaruddhe kraye dravyāntarānupraveśābhāvāt | ataḥ ekahāyanīśabdopāttatvāt dravyasya
na aruṇāśabdaḥ dravyāntaropaniviṣṭaṃ guṇamabhidhātuṃ śaknoti | ekahāyanyupaniveśe ca na
krayasaṃbandha ityupariṣṭāt pradarśyate | ataḥ padāntaropāttadravyake vākye kevalaguṇānvayi-
taiveti sthitam | ata upapadyate saṃśayaḥ | yasmādupapadyate saṃśayaḥ kiṃ vākyaṃ bhitvā
prakaraṇe niveśaḥ guṇasya dravyayogitvāt kiṃ prakaraṇaniviṣṭeṣu dravyeṣu niveśaḥ
uta karaṇavibhaktinirdeśāt krayaṇa eveti | atra bhāṣyam | “nanu naivāyaṃ guṇavacana1”

iti | tadākṣipati---kathamiti | evaṃ bhāṣyoktena nyāyena guṇānvayavyatirekānuvidhāna---
lakṣaṇena aruṇāśabdasya sthite guṇavacanatve “nanu naivāyaṃ guṇavacanaḥ kiṃ tarhi dravyavacanaḥ”

ityākṣepaḥ kathamupapadyate | ucyate | strīliṅganirdeśāt dravyāśritatvācca strīliṅgasya na
guṇānāṃ liṅgasamavāyaḥ | nanvākṣiptadravyasamavāyi liṅgaṃ bhaviṣyati ityatrāha---prātipadikārthagata-
tvācca liṅgasya | striyāṃ yatpratipadikaṃ vartate tatra strīpratyaya iti prātipadikārthagatameva
liṅgaṃ pratyayābhidheyatve’pi liṅgasya prakṛtyarthasaṃbandha eveti prātipadikārthāśritameva liṅgamiti | atra
(1) P. 681-6

[p. r661]

pṛcchati---kimaruṇetyādidarśanaṃ nāstyākṣepavādinaḥ, guṇamātravacanādapi strīpratyayo bhava-
tīti kimākṣepavādī na paśyati, yena tanmātradarśanādapi dravyavacanatāmicchatītyarthaḥ |
na nāsti darśanaṃ, astyeva, kintu aruṇayetyevamādiṣu netyabhiprāyaḥ | abhidheya eva
darśanāt | kathaṃ tarhi aruṇādyupanyāsena nirākaraṇam ? aruṇādiṣu prayogasādhuteti |
kathamaruṇāvalliṅgamiti dravyavacanatānirākaraṇam ? uttaraṃ---sthite guṇavacanatve dṛḍha-
tareṇa kāraṇena aruṇāvalliṅgaṃ bhaviṣyati ityabhiprāyaḥ | “atha yadi krīṇātinā na
saṃbadhyeta tasminneva vākye ekahāyanīśabdena saṃbhantsyate na bhaviṣyati vākya bheda”
 iti

kevalaguṇavacanatvāt 1kathaṃ dravyasaṃbandhāśaṅketi | samādhatte---ayamabhiprāyaḥ | yadā dvayoḥ
kriyāsaṃbandhaḥ tadā ekahāyanyavarodhena dravyāntaranirodhāt kevalaguṇavacanatā aruṇa-
yeti | yadā punaḥ aruṇimnaḥ kriyāsaṃbandho nāsti tadā dravyāśritatvāt guṇānāṃ
dravyāṇāṃ ca tadāśrayatvena guṇānāṃ ko nāma dravyasaṃbandhaṃ niruṇaddhi iti yukta evā-
śritatvena2 krayasaṃbandha iti manyate yathā nīlamutpalaṃ ityatra | atrottaraṃ---prātipadikāttā-

vat guṇa eva pratīyate padāntarasaṃbandhāttvaparityaktadravyako’nvayaḥ | liṅgaṃ ca vākyā-
dvalīyaḥ | tena nāsti dravyasaṃbandha iti | vākyālliṅgaṃ balīya ityākṣipati---kimidaṃ
vākyāliṅgaṃ balīya iti | aidamarthye hi pārārthyalakṣaṇe śeṣatve balābalamuktaṃ śruti-
liṅgādhikaraṇe | na punaranvayayogepi kriyākārakaguṇaguṇyādyanvayayogepi balābalaṃ noktam |
ucyate---anvayayoge ca anvayāntarāvagatau satyāṃ kena balābalaṃ nivāryate | yadyapyaidamarthya
eva valābalamuktaṃ tathāpi tulyanyāyatayā’nvayayoge’pi tena balābalaṃ kena vāryate | caśabdo-
’piśabdārthe | uktaṃ cāsmābhiḥ balābalam |

atra bhāṣyaṃ “rūpādaruṇāśabdasya guṇavacanatā”
1 iti vadati | tadanupapannam |
anvitapadārthatvāt na kevalaguṇavacanatetyanayā śaṅkayā bhāṣyārthaṃ darśayati---padāntareṇa dravyo-
pādānāt kriyāsaṃbandhācca padadvayasya kevalaguṇānvayitā na śakyate vākyena bādhitum |
kriyāyāmeva tāvat na dravyānvitaguṇānvayārthaṃ aruṇāpadaṃ, padāntareṇa dravyopādānāt | na ca
dravya eva guṇānvayārthaṃ, kriyāsaṃbandhāt padadvayasya parasparasaṃbandhapratipattau nimittābhāvāt |
ataḥ kevalaguṇānvayitā aruṇāpadasyetyanenaivā/?/bhaprāyeṇa bhāṣye rūpādaruṇāśabdasya guṇavacana-
tetyuktam | atredānīmuttaraṃ kriyāyogitve amūrtatayā’nanvayamālocya ananvayādvaraṃ
dravyānvayaḥ | dravyānvayitvepi2 kriyānvayo nātidūraṃ parityakto bhavati | tadīyadravya

[p. r663]

eva niveśāt | avaśyaṃ caitadevamabhyupagamanīyam | anyathā prakaraṇasaṃbandho’pi na
syāt | prakaraṇe’pi hi kriyayā dravyeṇa vā saṃbandhaḥ | kriyayā tāvat na saṃbadhyate
ityuktaṃ amūrtatvāt | atha liṅgāt dravyeṇāpi na saṃbadhyate hanta ananvayitaivābhyu-
pagatā bhavati | na cāyamananvayitā abhidhānalakṣaṇo dharmaḥ abhidhānasya śabdasya
loke vṛddhavyavahāre dṛṣṭa ityuktam | tasmādekavākyatānugrahāya tasminneva vākye
dravyasaṃbandhābhyupagamo yuktaḥ | yata evānanvayaprasaṅgālliṅgenānvayaḥ ata eva nīlotpalā-
diṣvapyanvayo dṛśyate | anyathā hi saṃbandhābhidhānavibhaktiśūnyānāmapi nīlotpalādi-

tasya tathāvidhasyaiva vibhaktayaḥ saṃbandhamāpādayanti | dravyaviśeṣaṇatvena ca guṇaḥ

eva nāstīti śakyate vaktum |

saṃśayaḥ | kīdṛśaḥ saṃśaya ityatrāha---atra saṃśayaḥ---kiṃ śeṣiṇo viśeṣaṇamekatvādi śeṣitve1

vākyeṣu caikasaṅkhayāyuktā eva grahāḥ śeṣatvena śrūyante | ataḥ kāryayogitvādekatvasya

asyārthaḥ---ubhayasaṃmataṃ tābat viniyuktasya grahasyopādānaśeṣabhūtāḥ saṅkhyādaya iti yathā
śeṣatvāvasthāyāṃ upādīyamānāvasthā(yāṃ)śrayaṇena grahasya saṅkhyādīn prati prādhānyaṃ tathā
śoṣibhāvepi saṃskārayogepi prādhānyamiti syāt | upādīyamānatayaiva saṅkhyādīn prati
prādhānyaṃ syādityarthaḥ | uttaraṃ---bhavatu prādhānyaṃ na kaścidvirodhaḥ saṅkhyādyaparityāgena tu
yathaiva śeṣatāṃ pratipadyate tathā saṃskāryatāmiti vadāmaḥ | saṃskāraṃ pratyupādīyamānatayā
bhavatu anyathā ceti na kaścidasmākaṃ virodhaḥ | rāddhāntī pariharati---atrocyate | satyaṃ śeṣa-
bhūtasyaiva saṃskāraḥ tathāpi saṅkhyādayo na saṃbadhyante saṃskāreṇa | kutaḥ ? sarvatra hi
padārthāntarasaṃbandhaḥ pradhānasyaiva bhavati rājapuruṣamānayetivat | tatra hi rājārthaḥ

grahaṇamanāsthāparam | śrutividhāte hi grahaśruterapi vidhātāt grahepi saṃmārgasyāprāptiḥ
syāt | athāyamabhiprāyaḥ uddiśyamānasya viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitaṃ bhavatīti | asyai-

[p. r675]

dravyameva hi kāryāntarayogitayā pratīyate na punaḥ svasaṃbandhinyabhidheye saṃpratyayaḥ | yataḥ
kāryamātrāvacchinnasya uddeśaḥ ataḥ kāryamātramevāpekṣyate avacchedakatayā na śrutyābhihita-
mekatvādikam | kimityekatvaṃ nāpekṣyate ityāha---upāttataiva tatra viśaṣāvagame hetuḥ
yatpunarupādeyaṃ tacchrutita eva viśiṣṭamavagantavyam | nānyata iti | tena tatra yuktā-
’pekṣā iti viśeṣaḥ | so’yamukto hetuḥ uddiśyamānasya viśeṣaṇaṃ avivakṣitaṃ bhavatī-
tyatrārthe na punaruddeśa evāvivakṣita iti tataḥ upāttāvagateḥ | ato grahaśrutirapi na
vivakṣaṇīyeti yaduktaṃ tatparihṛtam | viśeṣaṇaṃ punaravivakṣitaṃ upāttatvādityuktarūpo hetuḥ |
etenedamuktaṃ bhavati upādānaśeṣatayā saṅkhyā anvayaṃ yāti ākāṅkṣyā ca | yatpunarupāttaṃ
tatropādānābhāvāt dūrata eva taccheṣatā nāsti | ato nirviṣayatayā saṅkhyāpekṣaiva nāstīti |
kā vārtā agnīnādadhīta yūpaṃ chinatti iti | atrāpyuddeśyatvādagnīnāṃ yūpasya ca aviva-
kṣitā saṅkhyā prāpnoti iti manyate | uttaram---vivakṣitā saṅkhyeti | kutaḥ ? atra hi
idamevopādānavākyam | tena anyato’nirjñātatvāt apekṣitā saṅkhyeti | nanu cāgnipada-
muddeśakaṃ pradhānabhūtāścāgnayaḥ na saṅkhyā, ataḥ kathaṃ vivakṣeti | uttaraṃ---satyaṃ yathā
bhavānāha | kintu dvividho hyuddeśaḥ upāttasya anupāttasya ca grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭītyupāttoddeśaḥ

[p. r676]

aindravāyavādivākyeṣu upāttān grahānuddiśya saṃmārgo vidhīyata ityupāttoddeśaḥ | kvacidapyāha-
vanīyādīnāmanupādānāt | anupāttaṃ coddiśyata iti kathamidaṃ ? 1anuvādaṃ uddeśe
caikarūpaṃ manvānasyedaṃ codyam | uttaraṃ---vidheyaviṣayo hyaddeśa ityucyate yasmin kiñci-
dvidhīyate taduddiśyata ityarthaḥ | nopāttatvaṃ anupāttatvaṃ coddeśahetuḥ | ataḥ pramāṇāntara-
siddhaviśeṣaṇamuddiśyamānamavivakṣitaviśeṣaṇaṃ bhavati | yatpunaravijñātaviśeṣaṇamevoddiṣṭaṃ
bhavati taddhidheyavat śrutita eva viśiṣṭamavagantavyam | tasmāt suṣṭhu prāmāṇikaṃ ucyate
---upāttoddeśe viṣaye grahaṃ samārṣṭītyatra avivakṣitaṃ viśeṣaṇamiti2 | nanu ca grahaṃ
saṃmārṣṭīti dvitīyeyaṃ kārakavibhaktiḥ kārakaṃ ca kriyārthaṃ bhavatīti śābdāḥ | atrā-
yamāśayaḥ | dvitīyeyaṃ kārakavibhaktiḥ kārakaṃ ca kriyārthaṃ bhavati | yacca yadarthaṃ tatsiddhayarthaṃ
tadupādeyaṃ bhavati | yaccopādeyaṃ tatrāvacchedakākāṅkṣā asti | nanu nirjñātāvacchedakaṃ
nāvacchedakāntaramapekṣate satyaṃ, yadi saṃmārgaṃ pratyupādāne’vacchedako nirjñātaḥ syāt | na
hyatrāvacchedakatā nirjñātā iti upādānaśeṣatā saṅkhyāyā iti | rāddhāntamāha---nanu
cepsitatame dvitīyā na cepsitatamaṃ kriyārthaṃ kintu kriyaiva tadarthā tenepsitatamasya kriyāṃ
pratyanupādānānnopādānaśeṣateti | pūrvapakṣī tvāha---satyaṃ sā tu kārake dvitīyāvibhaktiḥ
tena kārakatvādasti kriyāṃ pratyupādānam | sa evaikagranthenāha---nanu ca vipratiṣiddhamidaṃ
(1) G. anupādanaṃ (2)G. aviśiṣṭaṃ hi lakṣaṇamiti

[p. r677]

īpsitatamaṃ kārakaṃ ceti | ataḥ kārakatvameva paramārthato’rthaḥ | ārthaṃ tu kārakasyāpi1
kriyāphalayogitvam | rāddhāntī pṛcchati---kathaṃ vipratiṣiddham ? pūrvapakṣī vikalpya
dūṣayati---kimidamīpsitatamaṃ nāma ? yadi tāvat yataḥ kriyāsiddhiḥ 2tatkriyarthaṃ
īpsitatamamiti | evaṃ tarhi kārakamātre karmatvaprasaṅgaḥ tataśca viśeṣābhidhānamanarthakaṃ
idaṃ karma idaṃ karaṇamiti sarvakārakāṇāṃ karmatvāpatteḥ | atha vatkriyayā āpyate yathā
adhyayanena vedaḥ, utpādyate yathā pākenaudanaṃ, saṃskriyate, yathā prokṣaṇena vrīhayaḥ,
vikriyate yathā vilayanenājyaṃ, na vilayanenājyamutpādyate kiṃ tarhi pārthivamājyadravyaṃ gandha-
vatvāt dhanasvabhāvamevāgnisaṃyogena dravībhavati ityaṣṭame vakṣyāmaḥ | tatkarmetyucyate | evaṃ
vadato’sya caturvidhaṃ karmābhipretaṃ saṃskāryavikāryayorbhinnatvāt | dūṣayati---na tarhi kārakaṃ
3āptyādyavasthayepsitatamaḥ san akārakamityarthaḥ | kutaḥ ? kriyāparyavasānāpekṣo
hyāptyādiko guṇaḥ kriyāyāṃ niṣpannāyāṃ paścāt kriyāphalaṃ niṣpādyate | hetorhi phala-
mutpadyate | hetuphalayoḥ paurvāparyaniyamaḥ | paryavasite hi niṣpanne vyāpāre phala-
mutpadyate na phalasya sādhyaiva kriyā | ato na kārakatā | rāddhāntī samādhatte---atrā-
bhidhīyate---nedaṃ vipratiṣiddhaṃ āptyādiko guṇaḥ kārakasthaścākārakasthaśca | yadā kārakasthaḥ
(1) G. athāpi tvasyāsti (2) R. kiyāsiddhayarthaṃ (3) G. āptyādya-
vasthāyasminnaṃśe tatkārakamityarthaḥ

[p. r678]

tadā kārakameva phalam tadā’’ptyādiko guṇo dvidhā anyathaikarūpyameva syāt | pūrvapakṣī
pṛcchati---kimidaṃ kārakameva phalamiti ? uttaraṃ---yadā hi kārakameva kayācidvi-
kārāpattyā vyāpyaṃ utpādyaṃ saṃskāryaṃ vikāryaṃ ceṣṭaṃ tadā tadeva phalamityucyate | tāmevā-
vasthāmaṅgīkṛtya īpsitatamavyapadeśaṃ labhate | kriyāsiddhayarthaṃ phalasiddhayarthaṃ vā | yatkrārakaṃ
tat kriyāsiddhayarthaṃ phalasiddhayarthaṃ ca | tadeva kayācidvikārāpattyā īpsitatamavyapadeśaṃ
labhate | ataḥ kārakamīpsitatamamityaviruddham | kathamaviruddhaṃ ? vidyate hi
kārakaṃ na phalasamavāyi yathā karaṇādi | vidyate ca phalaṃ na kārakasamavāyi yathā
svargādi | tena yatkārakaṃ phalasamavāyi tatkarma yathā syādityuktaṃ īpsitatamaṃ kārakamiti |
etena viśeṣābhidhānārthamanarthakamiti yaduktaṃ tatparihṛtam | nanu svargādyapi kāraka-
samavāyyeva kartṛgāmi kriyāphalamiti | śāstraphalaṃ prayoktarītyatra vacanāt abhi-
dhānāt | uttaraṃ---na kārakatayā phalasamavāyo vidyata ityadhikāralakṣaṇavyāpāro’yaṃ
adhikāralakṣaṇe uktaḥ | 1kathaṃ tarhi bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ sūtraṃ “kartrabhiprāye kriyāphale”

(1) G. ṇa ityuktam

[p. r679]

iti | asyāpi tatraivārtho nirūpyate | prakṛtamupasaṃharannāha---ato grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭīti yadyapi
saṃmārgaṃ graho niṣpādayatveva tathāpi na tasyāṃśasya vācikā dvitīyāvibhaktiḥ īpsitata-
matvamācaṣṭe phalāṃśamityarthaḥ | kriyāniṣpādyamiti yāvat | anenedamuktaṃ yasminnaṃśe
ekatvaṃ śrutaṃ na tasyāṅgasya upādeyatā | yasya copādeyatā na tatra vibhaktiriti | tasmācchrutito
viśeṣāvagama iti siddham | ataḥ upāttaviṣayaḥ saṃskāra iti sthitam | nanu ca
vyabhicāryetaddarśanaṃ īpsitatame dvitīyeti | anīpsitatamepi dvitīyā bhavatyeva upā-
dhyāyāya gāṃ dadātīti | tataḥ kiṃ? idaṃ tato bhavati | anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ kārakāṃ
śābhidhāyinī na phalāṃśābhidhāyinī | evaṃ grahamityetadapi kārakamevācaṣṭe | tataścai-
katvamavivakṣitamiti na śakyate vaktuṃ upādeyadaśāyāmeva śrutatvāt | yadi kārakā-
bhidhāyinī dvitīyā kathaṃ tarhi grahārthaḥ saṃmārgaḥ pramāṇābhāvāt tādarthyamayuktamityarthaḥ |
uttaraṃ---prayojanavattvāt grahāṇāṃ saṃmārgasya tādarthyaṃ, yathā paśupuroḍāśo’gnīṣomadevatārthaḥ
atrābhidhīyate | īpsitatamatvaṃ tāvat dvitīyā’bhidhatte | tathā yuktaṃ cānīpsitamiti
tu kriyānirvāhyamevetyarthaḥ | asyārthaḥ---dvitīyā tāvat phalāṃśamabhidhatte | tathā yuktaṃ

[p. r680]

mityatrāpi tathāyogābhidhānāt kriyājanyaphalāṃśābhidhāyitvameva iti iyān viśeṣaḥ kriyāntara-
saṃbandhitayaivaikasyopādānaṃ abhidhānaṃ yathā ‘vrīhīn avahanti’
 iti niyogasaṃbandhitayā
vrīhaya upādīyante tatraivepsitatamatā | ekasya neti yathā gāṃ dadātīti na goḥ kriyāntara-
saṃbandhitayopādānam | taccānīpsitaṃ kriyājanyaphalayogi | tathā’nīpsitatamamapīti kriyā-
janyaphalayogitve nāsti viśeṣaḥ | dvitīyāpi tāvanmātramevābhidhatte kāryataḥ tadīpsitatamatvaṃ
brīhīṇāmiti mantavyam | evamubhayatrāpi mukhyārthaiva dvitīyā bhavati tena na kārakāṃśābhidhāyinī
dvitīyetyuktaṃ bhavati | yadi tathā yuktabhityatrāpi na dvitīyā kārakāṃśābhidhāyinī evaṃ tarhi
gāṃ dadātītyavivakṣā saṅkhyāyāḥ | pūrvapakṣyevāśaṅkāmutpādya nirasyati---bhavatviti cenna,
prayojanāntarakalpanāprasaṅgāt| yāvaddhi prayojanāntaraṃ gaurna kalpyate tāvadavivakṣitā saṅkhyeti
na śakyate vaktum anirjñātasaṅkhyātvenāvacchedakākāṅkṣitvāt | rāddhāntaikadeśyāha---kimiti
prayojanāntaraṃ neṣyate | uttaraṃ---pramāṇābhāvāt na kāryakalpanāyāṃ pramāṇamasti |
nanu ca dvitīyaiva kāryakalpanāyāṃ pramāṇam---satyam dvitīyā pramāṇam sā tu kāryā-
kāṅkṣāyāṃ viśeṣaṃ samarpayati na punardvitīyāntapadavācyasyārthasya kāryamebāvagamayituṃ kṣamā
saktvādivat | kasmātpunarna kṣametyāha---viniyogaparyavasāyinyaḥ parasparasaṃbandhamātropakṣīṇa-
śaktayo vibhaktayaḥ na kāryaparyavasāyinyaḥ | nanu ca kiṃ prayojanāntarakalpanayā, atraiva
vākye niyogaḥ sādhyo bhavatu ityatrāha---navā’dhikāraśūnyo niyogo’stīti phalabatsannidhā-
vityatra vakṣyāmaḥ| atra ca goḥ sādhyatvadarśanāt nādhikāro’pyaparaḥ sādhyaḥ śakyate’ṅgī-

[p. r681]

kartum | na cādhikāraṃ vinā niyogo’sti | tasmādiyamapi kalpanaiva nāsti | yadi yatra
sādhyāntaramasti tatrādhikāro nāsti kathaṃ tarhi saṃskāravidhīnāmadhikārasaṃsparśaḥ ityāha---saṃskāra-
vidhayastūktāḥ ‘teṣāmarthena’
 ityatra | yathā satyapi teṣāṃ kāryāntare adhikārānupraveśaḥ tathā
teṣāmarthenetyatroktam | rāddhāntī pariharati---uktottaramapi kimiti bhavatā paricoditam
vismaraṇāparādhāditi manyāmahe | tadidaṃ smāryate anupāttaviṣayamidaṃ prādhānyamiti
yūpavat sthitaṃ vivakṣitatvaṃ saṅkhyāyāḥ | amunā ta viśeṣeṇa kāryāntarasaṃbandhāsaṃbandha-
nibandhanena īpsitatamatvānīpsitatamatvalakṣaṇena sūtrāntarapraṇayanaṃ bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ ‘akathitaṃ’

iti | atrāpi tathā yuktamityanuvartate | tasmāt dvitīyā prādhānyaṃ kriyājanyaphalabhāgitāṃ
na vyabhicaratīti sthitam | upātte tu viṣaye pramāṇāntareṇa viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitamiti
siddham | “grahādiṣvekatvaṃ brūyāt na dvitīyādīn pratiṣedhet”
 iti bhāṣyam | tadanu-
papannam | yathā paśugatamekatvaṃ vidadhat ekavacanaṃ dvitīyādīnarthān nivartayati tathehāpi
kiṃ na syādityata āha---upāttaviṣayatvāttu grahaśruteḥ upādeyehi ekatvādayo viśeṣaṇa-
miti | iha punaḥ upāttataiva viśeṣāvagatau heturityuktaṃ iti | vyākhyātārtham |

nanu “parisañcaṣṭa”
 iti ko’bhiprāyaḥ | tridoṣaduṣṭā parisaṅkhyeti manyate | ayamabhiprāyaḥ
---ekatvamananvitaṃ syāt | na cānanvitābhidhānamastītyuktaṃ tada्bhūtādhikaraṇe | ato varaṃ
parisaṅkhyeti manyate | naitadasti | satya/?/nanvitābhidhānamekatvasya prāpnoti | na punaḥ
parisaṅkhyā saṃbhavati aprāptatvāt | grahasaṃmārgasaṃbandhasya prāptau satyāmanyanivṛttau
niyogaḥ 1prabhavati na rāgaprāpte bhakṣaṇa eva “pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyā”
 itivat | aprāpte punaḥ
yathā śveti śrutiḥ na kākādīn nivartayituṃ kṣamā | na cānanvitābhidhānaṃ, tasyāpi
śuno bhakṣaṇe prāptyavagamāt tathehāpi na dvitīyādīnāṃ parisaṅkhyeti prakṛtena saṃbandhaḥ |

[p. r683]

na, asmākamatrāpi saṅkhyā(na)nvayo’bhipreta iti cet, na nañarthe vadhena saṃbadhyamāne ekatva-
manvetuṃ kṣamaṃ, anyataḥ pravṛttyavagamāta | asyārthaḥ---vadho hi nañarthena saṃbadhyamānaḥ pratiṣidhya-
mānaḥ anyataḥ pravṛtto’ṅgīkaraṇīyaḥ | tataścānupādeyatvānna tatropādānaśeṣatā saṅkhyāyā
ityavivakṣaiva | tasmādekatvābhidhānaṃ dharmamātre sādhu iti draṣṭavyam | atra bhāṣyam “ayaṃ
cāparo doṣaḥ”
 iti | tasya cāyamarthaḥ---ekatvagrahasaṃbandhavidhānepi sammārgaviṣaye grahasyaikatvaṃ na
niyantuṃ śakyate viṣayatvena saṃmārgasyāviśeṣāt | iti | sa cāyamanupapanna iva pratibhāti |
yasmādekatvaśravaṇādyathā tasya karmānvayitā na saṃbhavati tathā varṇayituṃ nyāyyam | tena
grahaśabdo’nupāttoddeśyaka eva bhavatviti | sammārgaviṣaya eva grahasyaikatvaniyamaḥ sidhyatītī-
māmanupapattimapanetuṃ ṭīkākāra āha---ayamaparo doṣa iti pratīkena bhāṣyamanubhāṣitam |
anupapattiṃ pariharati---ekatvānvitābhidhāne āśrīyamāṇe grahatvaṃ nopāttaviṣayaṃ syāt
upāttagrahopalakṣaṇārthaṃ na syāt anupāttaviṣayatve ca grahasya sammārgeṇa saha ananvayaḥ
prāpnoti | kāryāntarānupraveśinaḥ saṃskārānarhatvāt | ananvīyamāne ca sammārgavidhānaṃ
nirviṣayatvāt śeṣiśūnyatvādanarthakaṃ syāt | ato nānena doṣeṇāyaṃ2 anupāttoddeśaḥ
upāttoddeśa eveti sthito bhāṣyokto doṣa iti |

“athaikatvasammārgasaṃbandho vidhīyeta3”
 ityādibhāṣyaṃ tadanupapannaṃ, saṃbandhe hi vidhīyamāne
avaśyaṃ guṇapradhānabhāvo’bhyupagamanīyaḥ | tatra yasyaikatvaṃ pradhānaṃ tadekatvamuddiśya sammārgo
(1) P. 715 (2) G. dūṣaṇāyāyaṃ (3) P. 715

[p. r684]

vidhīyeta | tataśca grahaśabdo’nanvitaḥ syāt | atha grahasya caikatvasya ca uddeśyadvayasya
śravaṇāt pratyuddeśyaṃ vākyasamāpteḥ ekena grahasammārgasaṃbandhaṃ vidhāya apareṇaikatvasaṃmārgasambandho
vidhīyetetyucyeta | tanna | aprāpte grahasammārgasaṃbandhe kathamekatvasammārgasaṃbandho vidhīyeta |
sammārgasya cāpradhānatvāt ekatvasya pradhānatvāt | na hi pradhānaṃ guṇa prati vidhīyate | atha
sammārga eva pradhānabhūtaḥ tatrāpyuddeśyabhedena vākyabhedamāśritya grahe sammārgaḥ sammārge hyekatvaṃ
vidhīyate | tadanupapannam | ekatvasyāmūrtatayā sammārgaṃ prati sādhanatvānupapatteḥ 1guṇatvā-
bhāvāditīmāṃ śaṅkāmanuvadan vyākhyānamāha---athaikatvasaṃmārgasambandho vidhīyeta tatrāpi
dvayī gatiḥ syādityanena guṇaphalasaṃbandhavadvidhānaṃ na saṃbhavati nāpyaruṇāvaditi darśayati
etaduktaṃ bhavati---yathā guṇaphalasaṃbandhe pradhānabhūto vaitānāgniḥ guṇabhūtādhānasaṃbaddho
vidhīyate tathehāpyekatvaṃ[tvavad]grahe sammārgasaṃbandho vidhīyeteti yā śaṅkā sāpi na bhavati
yathā cāmūrtasyāpyāruṇyasya krayasādhanatā tathaikatvasyāpi sammārgasādhanatayā śaṅkā yā sāpi na
saṃbhavatīti | kathamasaṃbhavaḥ? ekatvaṃ tāvat nopāttasyopalakṣaṇāyālam | ekatvaṃ
tāvat svavākye upāttasya saṃmārgasya upalakṣaṇāya viśeṣaṇāya alaṃ na bhavati | sammārgeṇa
hi saṃbadhyamānaṃ pradhānaṃ vā bhavati guṇo vā nānyaḥ saṃbandho’sti | kathaṃ nāsti ?
prātipadikāśritaṃ hi saṅkhyābhidhānaṃ vibhaktīnāmityuktam | kasmānna viṣayatayā
uddeśyatayā vidheyatayā vā sammārgasaṃbandha ekatvasya saṃbhavati ? paratantratvāt |
(1) guṇatvābhāvābhāvāt

[p. r685]

prātipadikāśritatvāt saṃmārgeṇa saha saṃbandhābhāvāt | kimiti tarhi bhāṣyakāreṇa grahasya
pradhānatayā sammārgaṃ pratyanupādānāt upādānopayogitayā aruṇāvadekatvasya sammārge guṇabhāvo na
saṃbhavatītyuktam | atrāha---abhyupagamyāpyaruṇāvanna sambhavatītyuktam grahasya pradhānatvāt |
nanu ca pradhānepi sambhavatyeva viśeṣaṇaṃ nimittabhūtaṃ kārakatvamāśrityeti | asya
ayamarthaḥ prādhānyamapi grahādeḥ kārakatvanimittakameva | kārakabhūtā eva grahāḥ pradhānabhūtā
bhavanti | tena tāmavasthāmāśrityaikatvaṃ grahādeḥ viśeṣaṇaṃ bhavati aruṇāvaditi | kena
coktaṃ na saṃbhavati viśeṣaṇamiti | iha punaḥ vidhānāsaṃbhava eva pradarśitaḥ pradhānatvāt
grahasya | asyārthaḥ---yadi grahaḥ sammārgaṃ prati vidhīyeta tadā asyopādeyatvāt viśeṣaṇaṃ
syāt | iha tu graho na vidhīyate pradhānatvāditi | anena ‘pradhānabhūta’
mityādigranthena
1“nanu pradhānabhūta”
mityādibhāṣyaṃ “bhidyeta vākyaṃ”
 ityevamantaṃ vyākhyātam | athocyeta
grahādi lakṣayitvā tasyaikatvasaṃbandho vidhīyate na sammārgasaṃbandha iti---ayamabhiprāyaḥ kaḥ
pakṣapātaḥ sammārgo’nvayī na punarekatvamiti | uttaraṃ---kriyāśūnyo’pyanvayaḥ na
saṅkhyādeḥ sambhavati asati tu vidheye saṃbhavatyapi | asyārthaḥ---sammārge vidheye saṃbhavati
saṅkhyādeḥ kriyāśūnyo’nvayo na saṃbhavati | asati tu vidheye saṃbhavatyapi | na ca śrutyā
grahagatasyaikatvasyānvayo’sti kriyayetyuktam | uttarabhāṣyamapyevaṃ vyākhyeyam | tathā
sammārgādīnāmadhyayanaṃ pramāda ityabhyupagataṃ syāt iti yāvaddhi sammārgādīnāmadhyayanaṃ pramāda iti
(1) P. 716

[p. r686]

nocyate tāvadvidheye sammārgādau saṃbhavati naikatvagrahasaṃbandhavidhimātraparatā vākyasyeti |
“yadyavivakṣitamekatvaṃ3”
 iti bhāṣyeṇa lakṣaṇavirodhaṃ darśayati | bahuṣu bahuvacanamiti
śābdāḥ na vayametadvicārayāma iti pramāṇataḥ sthite lakṣye lakṣaṇaṃ kiṃ kariṣyatītyabhiprāyaḥ |
na cātra lakṣaṇavirodhaḥ na vibhaktervacanamevaikaṃ prayojanam | etacca prāgeva vyākhyātam |
api ca grahaḥ prātipādikārthaḥ ekatvaṃ vibhaktyarthaḥ | kimato yadyevaṃ ? etadato bhavati
prānipadikārthagataṃ hi vibhaktiḥ svārthaṃ śrutyaivābhivadati | iti | anena bhāṣyeṇa
vibhaktyabhidheyasyaikatvasya pāratantryaṃ prātipadikārthaparatantnatvaṃ 4darśayati | tacca
vyākhyātam | atha yaduktaṃ paśupālameteti yathaikatvaṃ vivakṣitaṃ tadvadihāpīti tatra kaḥ
parihāraḥ ? upādeyānāṃ śrutito viśeṣaḥ ityukto viśeṣaḥ |

saṃskārādvāguṇānām |

yadi kāryataḥ upādānaṃ upāttasya lakṣaṇaṃ śeṣitayeti tato grahaśrutirapi soma-
dhāraṇapātramātralakṣaṇaiva ekakāryatvāt | anenāvāntarasaṅgatiṃ pūrvapakṣaṃ cāha---yadi kāryataḥ
upādānaṃ yasya kāryamasti tasyopādānaṃ uddeśakena śabdena upāttasya ca lakṣaṇaṃ upalakṣaṇaṃ
(1) P. 723 (2) P. 724 (3) P. 723 (4) G. adds na

[p. r687]

śeṣitayeti | tato grahaśrutirapi grahaprātipadikamapi somadhāraṇapāvamātralakṣaṇārthameva
grahāṇāṃ camasānāṃ copāttatvāt | somayāgaśeṣabhūtasomadhāraṇaikakāryatvāt sammārgasya ca
somāvasekanirharaṇameva prayojanam | tacca prayojanaṃ graheṣu[cayaseṣu] cāviśiṣṭaṃ ityupapannaṃ
camasānāmapi sammārjanam | darśitaṃ caitadbhāṣyakāreṇa “yasmin gṛhyamāṇe somo vyava-
sicyeta”
 ityanena | anena sāmarthyataśca kāryasiddhau kevalasammārgavidhānalābhaḥ
prakaraṇe mantropadeśavat | asyārthaḥ---avivakṣitagrahasaṃbandhaṃ sammārgamātrameva vidhīyatāṃ tathā
ca vidhilāghavaṃ bhavatīti | kathaṃ tarhi śiṣṭhadvārasaṃbandhaḥ sāmarthyāt | sammārgaḥ tāvat
somavasekanirharaṇaṃ śaknoti kartumiti liṅgena tasya tatraiva viniyogaḥ yathā prakaraṇe mantrasyopadi-
ṣṭasya liṅgena viniyogaḥ tathehāpi | uktaṃ caitadbhāṣyakāreṇa---‘sammārge puruṣaprayatnaṃ’
 ityeva-
mādinā bhāṣyeṇa |

vyavasthāvā’rthasya

atrābhidhīyate---śrutitaḥ upāttaparijñānaṃ, nopādānācchrutiḥ | grahāśca camasāśca
kāryataḥ upāttāḥ ityetāvatā kāraṇena na grahaśrutiḥ sarvapātraparatayā varṇayitavyā | kāryasya hi
etāvatsāmarthyaṃ yat kāryaśūnyasya saṃskārābhāva iti | na punaḥ yāvadeva kāryavat tasya
sarvasya saṃskāraḥ | nanu somāvasiktaparimārjanaṃ śuddhāvasthāprāptiḥ tulyaṃ kāryamityuktaṃ,

tena kāryayogāt sarveṣāmapi sammārgaḥ syāditi | satyamuktaṃ na punaḥ śrutibādhe
tatsamarthamityuktamasmābhiḥ | pūrvapakṣī vrūte---śrute’pi hi yadeva kāryaṃ, tadeva

sammārgasya prayojanam | kimityaśrutepi na bhavati | tatrāpi tasya kāryasyāviśeṣāt kāryārtha-
tvāccānuṣṭhānasya | uttaraṃ---na hi kāryaṃ śrūyate | śrūyate hi kāryamityuktam | asyārthaḥ
---grahaṃ sammārṣṭīti śrūyate | tatra graheṣu sammārgasya kiṃ prayojanaṃ bhavatviti cintāyāṃ somāvaseka-
nirharaṇaṃ śuddhāvasthāprāptilakṣaṇaṃ prayojanaṃ dṛṣṭamiti nānyat kalpyate | na punaḥ kāryameva
śrūyate | tena śrutibalalabdhātmakaṃ kāryaṃ na śrutimeva unmūlayitumalam | codyamāha---kāryaṃ
cennādriyate kathaṃ tarhi prokṣaṇaṃ yaveṣu | vrīhiṣu yaveṣu vā tulyaṃ prokṣaṇakāryam | ato
yaveṣvapi prokṣaṇaṃ yuktamiti ceta nyāyo nādriyate, kathaṃ yaveṣu prokṣaṇamiti bhrāntaścodayati |
yathā tat tathā navame vakṣyāmaḥ apūrvaprayuktatvāddharmāṇāṃ vrīhiśabdaḥ tatsādhanaviśeṣa-
lakṣaṇārtha iti yaveṣvapi prokṣaṇaṃ; iha tu graheṣu camaseṣu ca abhyāsāpūrvabhedāt na tulyaḥ
sādhanabhāvaḥ ityapūrvaprayuktatve’pi nāsti camaseṣu sammārgaḥ | yaduktaṃ sammārgamātravidhānaṃ lābho
labhyata iti tadayuktaṃ, viniyogaviṣayatvāt niyogasya | viniyogaḥ parasparasaṃbandhaḥ | sa
iha viṣayo niyogasya | na cāsau svaviṣayopamarde prabhavati | anyathā niyogasyaivopamardaprasaṅgāt
grahasaṃbandhānvitaśca viṣayaḥ | tadidamuktaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa---2“utsṛjyamāne śrutireva bādhyate
grahamiti”

ānarthakyāttadaṅgeṣu |

paśoḥ yūpo iti | tatra kāraṇasaṃśayamāha---saptadaśāratrirvājapeyasya yūpa ityatroddeśakaṃ padaṃ
kiṃ vājapeyasya saptadaśa, uta yūpasya saptadaśeti kiṃ vājapeyapadamuddeśakaṃ uta yūpapadamiti |
pūrvapakṣiṇo’bhiprāyaḥ | yadi yūpa ityuddeśakaṃ padaṃ tadā vājapeyasyetyananvitābhidhānam
(1) P. 727 (2) G. viśeṣayituṃ (3) G. apavādako’rtha iti manyate

[p. r690]

prāpnoti yūpena vājapeyasyāsaṃbandhāt | asyārthaḥ---yadi yūpasya saptadaśāratnitā vidhīyate
tadā vājapeyasya yūpena sabāsaṃbandhaḥ | na ca yūpena saha saṃbandho’vakalpate | vājapeyasya
ṣaṣṭhyantatvāt ṣaṣṭhyāśca saṃbandhavācitvāt vājapeyayūpayośca saṃbandhābhāvāt | yūpasya ca
paśuyāgamātre paryavasitasaṃbandhatvāt paramāpūrveṇa saha saṃbandhābhāvāditi | tasmāt vājapeyasyai-
voddeśyatvāt tadarthatayaiva saptadaśāratnitā vidhīyate | tatrāśrayāpekṣāyāṃ yūpaśabdo vājapeya-
saṃbandhinaṃ guṇayogādūrdhvapātraṃ pratipādayatīti | tadāha---tasmādvājapeyasaṃbandhini
yūpaśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ | evamanvitapadārthako vākyārthaḥ samarthito bhavati | atrābhidhīyate
---yadi yūpaśabde śrautānvayini vājapeyaśabdo’nanvitaḥ syāt syādetadevaṃ, atra punaḥ
saptadaśāratniryūpa ityapi saṃbandhe vājapeyaśabdo’nvita eva | kathaṃ ? saṃbandha-
saṃbandhinyapi ṣaṣṭhyantasyānvayadarśanāt devadattasya naptā ityevamādiṣu | atrāyaṃ bhāvaḥ---pūrva-
pakṣe yūpaśabdo na śrautārthaḥ asmin mate tu mukhyārthaḥ | na vājapeyasyānanvayaḥ sākṣātsaṃbandhā-
bhāve’pi ṣaṣṭhyantasyānvayadarśanāt | etaduktaṃ bhavati---yadyapi tena saha na saṃbandho’sti tathāpi
ṣaṣṭhī na virudhyate saṃbandhināpi yasya saṃbandhaḥ tadapekṣayāpi ṣaṣṭhīprayoga iti | tasmāda्yūpa-
śabdasya lākṣaṇikatve nāsti pramāṇaṃ iti yūpenaiva saṃbandhaḥ saptadaśāratnitāyāḥ |
kiṃ punaratra vājapeyasyetyanena kṛtamiti pūrvapakṣī codayati | matpakṣe hi uddeśyasamarpa-
katayopapadyate iti manyate | uttaraṃ---kimanenāsmākaṃ anena kṛtaṃ na kṛtamiti

kartṛguṇe tu samavāyāt |

atra bhāṣye sandehaḥ---kimabhikramaṇaṃ prayājeṣveva niviśate uta kṛtsne prakaraṇa iti |
tatra kāraṇasandehamāha---abhikrāmaṃ juhotīti kiṃ karmaṇaḥ karmānvayo vidyate uta neti
cintyate | yadya(pya)nvayo vidyate tadā prayājeṣveva niveśaḥ | atha na vidyate tadā
vākyaṃ bhitvā yogyakartari guṇavidhiriti kṛtsne prakaraṇe niveśaḥ | kathaṃ punaranvayo bhavati ?
kathaṃ vā na ? tatra yathānvayo na bhavati tathā darśayannāha---pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ---na
karma karmasādhyaṃ bhavati | tadāha bhagavān kāśyapaḥ “karma karmasādhyaṃ na bhavati”
 iti
(vai. sū. 1-1-11) yathā guṇasya sādhanabhāvaḥ tathā na karmaṇa iti | tasmādyaḥ kaścidupāttaḥ
prakaraṇe kartā so’bhikrāmaṃ ityanvayaḥ | parasparamabhikramaṇahomayoranvayābhāvāt vākyaṃ
mitvā’bhikrāmatītyanena karmavidhiḥ yogye kartaryabhikramaṇaṃ vidhīyate | nanu ca prayāje’pi yaḥ
kartā tatrāpyanvayo 2bhavatyeva | tena sannidhānaviśeṣāt prayājakartraiva saṃbandho bhavati |
(1) P. 729 (2) G. vidyata eva

[p. r692]

satyaṃ bhavati yadi juhotītyabhidhāyakaṃ syāt | anabhidhāyakatve tasmin na karturuddheśyatvaṃ
saṃbhavati nānṛtaṃ vadeditivat | asyārthaḥ-yadi juhotīti vibhaktiḥ abhidhāyikā syāt tadā
gasyamānakartṛpara eva prayoga iti prayājakartureva sannihitasyoddeśyatvāt tatraiva niveśaḥ syāt |
ahnabhidhāyikā tveṣā | siddhānte abhikramaṇākhyaguṇavidhānārthatvāt | ataḥ karturuddeśyatvaṃ
nāsti nānṛtaṃ vadediti yathā | tasmāt saṃyogato’viśeṣāt prakaraṇāviśeṣācca prakaraṇa-
sthakartrāsaṃbandhādvākyabhedaḥ prayājavākyādidaṃ bhinnaṃ vākyamityarthaḥ | yadi hi tatraivābhi-
kramaṇaṃ niviśeta tadā kevalabhāvavidhānena sahaikavākyataiva syāditi | juhotiridānīṃ
kimarthaḥ ? bhinnavākye abhikrāmaṃ ityetaṃ vākyaṃ juhotītyaparaṃ tadevaṃ vākyabhede sati |
uttaraṃ---yadarthaḥ tadartho bhaviṣyati kiṃ naḥ etena cintanena | abhyāsanyāyena karmāntare
vā paurṇamāsyadhikaraṇanyāyena vā’nuvādaḥ ityevaṃ prāpte brūmaḥ |

sākāṅkṣaṃ tvekavākyaṃ syādasamāptaṃ hi pūrveṇa |

satyamananvayaḥ karmaṇaḥ karmaṇā sākṣāta | kartṛsaṃbandhāt tu aruṇāvat
saṃbhavati kartṛsaṃbandhadvāreṇa karmaṇo’pi karmānvaya upapadyate yathā dravyadvāreṇāruṇyasya |
nanu ca karturupādānaṃ nāsti yathā ekahāyanyā upādānaṃ juhotītyasyānabhidhāyakatvādi-
(1) P. 730

[p. r693]

tyuktam | uttaraṃ---na brūmaḥ karturuddeśyatvamastīti | tannimittakastu karmoddeśe’pyanvayo
na virudhyata iti brūmaḥ | tasya kartuḥ tatra prayāje saṃbhavāt | asyārthaḥ---ṇamulanta-
tvādabhikrāmamityasya juhotītyanena saṃbandhaḥ pratīyate | sa caivaṃ yujyate yadi kenacitprakāreṇa
saṃbandho nirvahati tatkartṛdvāreṇa ca saṃbandho nirvahati | na ca tatra kartā nāsti | yata eva
cātra kartā saṃbandhadvārabhūto nopāttaḥ ekahāyanī tūpāttā, ata evāruṇādhikaraṇena vaiṣamyam |
tatrāruṇādhikaraṇe dvayorapyanvayinoḥ aruṇaikahāyanyaurupādānaṃ iha tu saṃbhavamātreṇaiva
kartṛdvārateti viśeṣaḥ | nanu ca bhāṣyakāraḥ pūrvapakṣe “saṃbandhamātraparijñānāt prayogavacanāt
kartavyatāsiddhiḥ iti vadan prakaraṇepi gamyamānakartṛdvāreṇa karmaṇyeva vidhānamiti darśayati[tat]
kathaṃ karturanupādānāt pūrvapakṣa ityāśaṅkyāha yaduktaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa saṃbandhamātraparijñānāt
prayogavacanāt 1kartravamarśāta kartavyatāsiddhiriti, tatraiva karturuddeśyatvaṃ na virudhyata
ityabhiprāyaḥ asyārthaḥ---prakaraṇapaṭhitamekavedaṃ2padamiti tadyogyenārthāntareṇa ekavākyatānve-
ṣaṇe yogyaśca saṃbandhe[ndhī] karteti kartāramuddiśyābhikramaṇaṃ vidhīyate | ekavākyatve ca na
karturuddeśyatvasaṃbhavaḥ | juhotītyanenaivānvitābhidhānasiddheḥ | evaṃ vaiṣamyapradarśanārthaṃ pūrvapakṣe
kṛte yadi kasyacidāśaṅkā syāt kathaṃ rāddhānta iti taṃ pratyāha---rāddhāntavādī tu saṃbhava-
mātreṇaivānvayasiddheḥ na karturuddeśyatayā prayojanamastīti manyate | atassiddhaṃ karmāpi
karmaṇaḥ uddeśyatāṃ pratipadyata iti |

sandigdhe tu vyavāyāt |

upavyayata ityatra sandihyate---kiṃ sāmidhenyanuvacanasya kartā upavyānena
saṃbadhyate uta kṛtsne prakaraṇe niviśata iti saṃśayamanubhāṣate ākṣeptum | nanu
naivātroddaśakaṃ padaṃ kiñcicchrūyate anuddiṣṭapadārthakaṃ ca yat tat prakaraṇenaiva saṃbadhyate
saṃyogato’viśeṣāt prakaraṇāviśeṣāccetyuktam | asyārthaḥ---asaṃyuktamupavītaṃ prakaraṇe ca
paṭhyate, upavītaṃ ca vāsovinyāsarūpaṃ vāsovinyāsena ca kartā karmaṇyanākulo bhavati iti
karturevāyaṃ dharma upadiśyate | tatra ca prakaraṇagatena kartrāsya saṃbandhaḥ kathaṃ pūrvapakṣābhāvena
saṃśayaḥ ? ucyate---ādhastye’dhikaraṇe anuddiṣṭenāpi sannihitena kartrā anvayo
na virudhyata ityuktam | evamatrāpi sāmidhenīnāṃ kartṛssannidhānāt uktena nyāyena
kriyāntarārthatayā upavyānasya loke’vagatatvāt anuddiṣṭo’pi kartā sannihitaḥ kārya-
paripūraṇāyālaṃ bhavitumarhati | asyārthaḥ---upavyayata ityekamidaṃ padaṃ anvitabhidhānāya
kartāramapekṣate | vinā ca kartrā’paripūrṇamidaṃ tatra sannihitena kartrā paripūraṇasamarthena eka-
vākyatāṃ yātīti yaḥ sannihitaḥ kartā sa evānena saṃskriyate ityarthaḥ | kāryāntarākāṅkṣāpyu-
pavyānasya paripūryata iti | ānantaryamacodanetyatra tu samāptatvātpadadvayaprayogādanvitābhidhānasya
nānantaryamātreṇa saṃbandhaḥ | nanvevamabhikramaṇamapi tarhi pūrvādhikaraṇapūrvapakṣe sannihitakartṛdharma
(1) P. 732
