prakaraṇānupraveśāya śeṣībhūtaḥ kriyāntaragataḥ kartā’pekṣyate tena ca śeṣiṇā’nvitābhidhānam |
iha tu prathamaṃ kartṛtvenaivāpekṣitena sannihitenāpi paripūraṇamaviruddhamiti | “nanu ca darśa-
pūrṇamāsayoreva prakaraṇamidaṃ paraprakaraṇe sāmidhenyaḥ śrūyanta”
 iti | ko’sya bhāṣyasyārthaḥ |
pṛcchataścāyamabhiprāyaḥ | yadyapi paraprakaraṇe sāmidhenyaḥ tathāpi tadīyaḥ kartā sannidhānāt
saṃbadhyata eveti yukta evopavītasya sāmidhenyanuvacana evānupraveśaḥ | uttaram---varṇito’smābhiḥ
uddeśapadābhāvāt prakaraṇāviśeṣaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati---uddeśapadāśravaṇāt prakaraṇagṛhītatvāt
tadanuguṇadvārabhūtakartṛsaṃbandhaḥ syāt iti sāmidhenyanuvacanānupraveśaśaṅkā na yukteti pūrvapakṣākṣepaḥ |
samādhānabhāṣyaṃ tu “satyaṃ paraprakaraṇe sāmidhenyaḥ śrūyante tathāpi tāsāmavāntara-
prakaraṇaṃ bhavati”
 iti | kimidamavāntaraprakaraṇaṃ nāma ? avāntaraprakaraṇamayuktaṃ kutaḥ ?
ekasya hi adhikāravidheḥ prakaraṇaṃ nāṅgavidhīnāmapi prakaraṇamasti | asyārthaḥ
---prakaraṇaśabdena niyogasya grāhakatvamucyate tathā viniyojakatvaṃ ca prakaraṇaṃ tasyaiveti-
kartavyatākāṅkṣatvalakṣaṇam | taccedamubhayamapi adhikāravidhereva tasyaiva puruṣāṇāmanuṣṭheyatayā
pratītatvāt | yatkhalu anuṣṭheyaṃ tatsannidhau yat samāmnāyate tat tadarthameveti | tadetat
(idaṃ tu) grāhakatvamanuṣṭheyasyaiva anuṣṭheyasya ca itikartavyatākāṅkṣatvaṃ iti na niradhikārāṇāma-
vāntaravidhīnāmapi prakaraṇamasti | nanu niradhikārataiva kutaḥ ityatrāha---pradhānavidhyabhedāt |
kathamabheda ityatrāha---pradhānavidhīnāṃ cābhedaṃ caturthe vakṣyāmaḥ | api ca sāmidhenyanuvacanaṃ
(1) 732-9

[p. r696]

saṃskārakarma saṃskārakarmasu cāvāntaraniyoga eva na saṃbhavati | prayājādiṣviva pradhānavidhereva
liṅādibhiranuvāda iti kuto’vāntaraprakaraṇāśaṅkā | tadāha---saṃskāravidhīnāṃ ca teṣāmarthenetya-
trānuvādaḥ pradarśita iti | tasmādavāntaraprakaraṇaṃ grāhakaviniyojakatayā nopapadyate
ataḥ sannidhānamātramavaśiṣyate | samādhātā’ha---ucyate sannidhāna evāyaṃ prakaraṇaśabdaḥ
prayuktaḥ | kathamavagamyate sannidhāna eva prayukta iti | “yena tatsannidhāvabhidhīya-
mānaṃ”
 iti vacanāt yena tatsannidhau sāmidhenyanuvacanasannidhau abhidhīyamānamupavyānaṃ
‘tasyati jñāyata’
 iti bhāṣyakāravacanāt avagamyate | sannidhāne hi saṃbandhakāraṇe
sannidhāvabhidhīyamānamiti yujyate vaktum | nanu ca viniyojakepi prakaraṇe sannidhāna-
śrutiśabdaprayoga upapadyata eva | etena nedamekāntato bhāṣyaṃ liṅgabhūtamiti | pariharati
---kena voktaṃ nopapadyata iti | kintu tanna saṃbhavatīti | nanu caivaṃ sati na liṅgatā bhāṣyasya
samarthitā | ucyate | āñjasyena sannidhānā1bhiprāyeṇaitadbhāṣyamupapadyate tathāpi[yadi] viniyojakaṃ
prakaraṇaṃ saṃbandhakāraṇaṃ atra noktaṃ syāt tadā tatparatvenānāñjasyenāpi bhāṣyaṃ vyākhyāyeta
na tvetadastīti bhavatyeva bhāṣyaṃ liṅgam | vākyabhedaśravaṇācca | rāddhānte vākyabhedaḥ
śrūyate tena pūrvapakṣe ekavākyatāpradarśitā ekavākyatā ca na viniyojakatvāt | nāpi
grāhakatvāt | kintu sannidhānāt tasmādato’pi hetoḥ sannidhāva eva prakaraṇaśabdaḥ |
vyavadhānaṃ ca darśayati | rāddhāntahetutvena | tatra viniyojakaṃ yadi prakaraṇaṃ pūrvapakṣe hetu-
(1) R. dhāna evābhiprā

[p. r697]

tvenopanyastaṃ syāt tadā nedamupapadyate | atha tu sannidhānaṃ pūrvapakṣahetutvenopanyastaṃ syāt
tadā tasya vyavadhānaṃ pratipakṣabhūtaṃ bhavet | ato’pi prakaraṇaśabdena sannidhānamevocyate |
tadidamāha---parārthena vyavadhānamānantaryaṃ vihanti na viniyogam | anyathā hi svāhā-
kārasyāpyaviniyogaḥ syāt samidādibhiḥ vyavadhānāt | atha mataṃ nivitpadānā-
manyārthatvāt grāhakāntarasaṃbandhāt grāhakasyāpi vyavadhānamiti | pariharati---dvāda-
śāhīnasyetyanenāpi vyavadhānaṃ grāhakasya syāt | na ca tadasti | tasmāt na
grāhako vyavadhīyate | kiñca kāmyairavyavadhānaṃ nivitpadānāṃ grāhakasya vyavadhānābhāvāt |
tataśca nividarthatā prāpnoti upavyānasya tena rāddhānto na labhyate | ānantaryavyavadhāne
punaḥ na doṣaḥ teṣāmapi hi kāmyairvyavadhānāta | na caivaṃ sati yadi nivitpadavyavadhānaṃ

vacanavyavadhānamatrābhipretaṃ syāt tadā tadanuguṇaṃ guṇaphalasaṃbandhena anuvacanaṃ na

kartā sannihito bhavati | avidhāne punaḥ karmaṇo’nuṣṭhānabuddhivicchedāt karturvyavadhānamiti |
(1) G. kartṛtānu

[p. r698]

nanu caivaṃ sati pūrvapakṣa eva nāsti guṇaphalasaṃbandhe karmaṇo’vidhānāt2 kartṛsannidhānābhāvāt
ityatrāha---evaṃ sthite pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ guṇaphalasaṃbandhepyanuvacanaśruteḥ kartā
sannihita eveti sāmidhenyarthamupavyānam | karmaṇāpi kartā sannidhāpyata iti bhāvaḥ |
rāddhāntavādī tu nāvidhīyamānaṃ3 sannihitaṃ bhavati svasthānasthitamevānuvadanīyamanūdyate
na punaḥ sannidhāpyate | anūdyamānavaśavartitvādanubādāvanām | asyārthaḥ-anuvādo hi nāma

bhavati iti vidhānena sannidhāpyate | ataḥ sannidhānāviśeṣāt kṛtsne prakaraṇe niveśa
iti siddham |

guṇānāṃ ca parārthatvāt |

māsādiṣu sarvayogeṣviti | tadupapādayannāha---vāraṇo yajñāvacara iti yajñasaṃbandhitvena
pātraṃ ādhānaprakaraṇe śrūyate | tena na tāvadādhāne saṃbadhyate | ānarthakyāttadaṅga-
ṣviti sthito nyāyaḥ | tadidānīṃ yadi pavamānahaviṣāṃ ādhānāṅgatā tataḥ pavamāna-

haviṣṣveva pātravidhānaṃ atha nādhānāṅgatā tadā aṅgadvāreṇāṣi ādhānānanupraveśāt vākyataḥ
utkṛṣṭasya sarvayajñārthatā iti | kiṃ tāvadyuktam ? pavamānahaviṣṣu vidhānam |
ādhānāṅgaṃ hi pacamānahavīṃṣi svavākya evādhānasya sādhyayogitvāt pavamānahaviṣāṃ ca
aphalatvāt ādhānasannidhau na śravaṇāt ādhānaprakaraṇe caitat śrūyate pātram | pradhāne
cāsaṃbhavan dharmaḥ aṅge bhavatītyuktam | nanu ca pavamānahavīṃṣyapi agnyarthānyeva ‘yadā-
havanīye juhvati’
 iti vacanāt | tena anaṅgatā pavamānahaviṣāmiti manyate | etacca
codyaṃ(mayathāśrutārthaṃ) yathāśrutaṃ gṛhṇataḥ | parihāraḥ---anārabhya vādo’yaṃ sarvahomānāṃ
guṇatvenāhavanīyaṃ vidadhāti na punaḥ pavamānahavīṣi pratyāhavanīyasya prādhānyamāheti
na prādhānyaṃ pavamānahaviṣāṃ saṃbhavati kintu ādhānāṅgataiveti | tasmādānarthakyātta-
daṅgeṣviti pavamānahaviṣṣu vidhānam | atrābhidhīyate---naivādhānaprakaraṇe pavamānaharvīṣi
śrūyante nāpyagnyarthānyeva | kathaṃ ? yadāhavanīye juhotīti vacanāt | prākṛte yāge
āhavanīyo vartate | taddharmagrāhiṇīṣvapi vikṛtibhūtāsu pavamāneṣṭiṣu codakenāhavanīyaḥ prāpyate |
pavamāneṣṭīnāṃ ca sādhyabhūtāgnisannidhau paṭhitānāṃ avaśyaṃbhāvitādarthyānāṃ guṇabhāvena saṃbandhāsaṃbhave
sati guṇabhāvamātre asaṃbhavena bādhite sākṣātsambandhasyābādhitatvāt sākṣātsaṃbandhainaiva tādarthya---
mavagamyata iti | bhavanti ca pavamāneṣṭayo’pyagnyarthāḥ nādhānārthā iti | imamabhiprāyamabudhvā

[p. r700]

codayati---nanu guṇatayā vidadhāsyanārabhyavādo’yamityuktam | abhiprāyamāviṣkaroti
---na brūmo na guṇatayā vidadhātīti | kintu atra prakṛtitaḥ prāptipradarśanārthaṃ evaṃ bacana-
mupanyastam yadāhavanīye juhotīti nopadeśārthaṃ sati hyasmin vacane prakṛtāvaṅgatā,
tataśca vikṛtāvatideśataḥ prāptirityetadarthaṃ vacanamupanyastam na punaranenaiva vacanena pradhānatayā
āhavanīya upadiśyata ityevamartham | kathaṃ punaḥ prakṛtitaḥ prāptau prādhānyaṃ bhavati ?
ekagranthenābhiprāyamāviṣkaroti---nanu ca prakṛtito’pyaṅgatayaiva prāptiḥ kāryanibandhanatvāt
prāpteḥ | uttaraṃ---satyamevaṃ ādhānopakārakatā tu agniniṣpattyā bhavati | yo hi
pavamānahaviṣyaṅgatvamādhānasyecchati icchatyevāsau pavamānahaviṣāṃ abhyutpādakatvam | tataśca
teṣvagneḥ guṇabhāvaḥ na saṃbhavati prāgeva tebhyaḥ svarūpāniṣpatteḥ | tataśca guṇabhāvamātre bādhite
sākṣātsaṃbandhāṃśasyābādhanāt yat tadutpādakatvamādhānāṅgatayā’dhyavasitaṃ tatsākṣādeveti
niścīyate | tadāha---agniniṣpattiścetprayojanaṃ kimityādhānopakārakatayā niṣpāda-
yati---na punaḥ sākṣātprakṛtitaḥ saṃbandhāt | nanu ca guṇatayā prakṛtitaḥ prāptirityukta-
mabhiprāyaṃ spaṣṭayituṃ prāguktameva codyamupanyastam | satyamuktam | sā punarādhānāṅgatve-
’ṅgīkiyamāṇe na saṃbhavati aniṣpannatvādagneḥ pavamāneṣṭibhyaḥ prāgiti śeṣaḥ |
niṣpādayatīti cet yadi niṣpādayatītyaṅgīkriyate siddhaṃ tarhi sākṣānniṣpādakatvaṃ uktena

mithaścānarthasaṃbandhaḥ |

nanu ca punaruktamidamadhikaraṇaṃ ānarthakyāttadaṅgeṣvityanenādhikaraṇenetyāśaṅkyāha

iha punaḥ nājyabhāgābhidhānamasti yena 1ya[ta]thā svabhāvasaṃbandhitayā vārtraghnatāṃ vṛghanvattāṃ ca
vidadhyāt ataḥ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate yuktaṃ vājapeye yūpaśabdābhidhānāt vyavadhānepi
vājapeyaśabdasyānvaya iti | iha punaḥ saṃbandhisaṃbandhyanvaye saṃbandhināvājyabhāgau tatsaṃbandhi
yadanuvacanaṃ tadanvaye vārtraghnatāvṛdhanvattayoḥ nasti kiñcitkāraṇamiti | kathaṃ tarhi dvivacanaṃ
anūcyete iti | ekagranthenāha---dvayordvivacanam | nanu caikatvātpradhānasya ekaivānuvākyā

na virodho dvitvasya kiṃ hi vacanaṃ na kuryāt tatra hi dvitvaṃ ayathārthatvaṃ ca mantrasya

ānantaryamacodanā |

yadi vyāpārāntarayogyatā niyamahetuḥ ihāpi staraṇe dīkṣitāvedane ca parisamāptaiva

prāyaḥ---uddeśe hi padārthamātrasyaivāgneḥ anapekṣitadevatābhāvasyopalakṣaṇatvaṃ sambandhimātra-

vyavasthā vā |

atrābhidhīyate | yathā bhavānāha tathāpi tu yathābhidheyaṃ 1lakṣaṇāsambhave
nāyathārthāvagatirbhavatīti | agnividhau devatābhāvena saṃbandhītyupalakṣaṇatvepi tenaiva saṃbandhe-
nopalakṣako yukta iti | na punaḥ anyathopalakṣakatvam | asaṃbhave syādapyevaṃ asti tvatra saṃbhavaḥ
ato nāprasiddhalakṣaṇā bhavitumarhati | yathāprasiddhasaṃbandhābhāve tu aprasiddhasaṃbandha-
(1) R. lakṣaṇasaṃbhave

[p. 706]

nimittatāpi gṛhyate | tasmādāgneyasyaiva caturdhākaraṇamiti siddham | ḍitthādīnāṃ tu
pratyekameva saṃbandhitvena prasiddhiḥ | āgneyastu pratyuktaḥ 1āgneyasya mastakaṃ virujya
iti yaḥ pūrvapakṣiṇoktaḥ sa rāddhāntinā nirākṛto bhāṣya eva |

iti ṛjuvimalāpañcikāyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya
prathamaḥ pādaḥ

[p. r707]

tṛtīyādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

arthābhidhānasaṃyogāt |

atra bhāṣyakāro vicāradvayaṃ kurute---kiṃ gauṇe’pi mantrāṇāṃ viniyogaḥ uta mukhya
eveti | kaḥ punarmukhyaḥ ko vā gauṇa iti | tatra dvitīyavicāramākṣipati---yuktaṃ tāvat
kiṃ mukhya evārthe mantrāṇāṃ viniyogaḥ uta gauṇe’pīti | idantvayuktam---kaḥ punarmukhyaḥ
ko vā gauṇa iti | kathamayuktam ? yaḥ paraśabdaḥ paratra vartate sa gauṇa iti sthitam4 |
ataḥ punarukto’yamartha iti manyate | tathā ca tatsiddhayadhikaraṇe darśitam | kathaṃ punaḥ para-
śabdaḥ paratravartate ? svārthābhidhāneneti | tasmādayukto’yaṃ praśnaḥ | athāyamabhiprāyaḥ lakṣaṇā-
yāmapi paraśabdaḥ paratra vartate tadvayāvṛttyartho’yaṃ praśnaḥ iti | asyārthaḥ---yo’yaṃ gauṇa-
mukhyāpraśnaḥ sa lakṣaṇāgauṇavivekārthaḥ | tatsiddhayadhikaraṇoktaṃ gauṇam | lakṣaṇayā paraśabdaḥ
(1) P. 745 (2) P. 746-1 (3) P. Brhati Part III P. 260
(4) G. iti lakṣaṇāyāmapyaviśiṣṭam

[p. 708]

paratra vartata iti sa gauṇa iti lakṣaṇāyāmapyaviśiṣṭam | iha evaṃ gauṇamukhyapraśne kṛte
gauṇasvarūpaṃ varṇyate yathā mukhyāllākṣaṇikācca bhinnimavabhāsate | tatra mukhyāt bhedaḥ tatsiddhayadhi-
karaṇe jñāpita iti iha lākṣaṇikāt bhedajñānaṃ praśnottarayoḥ prayojanam | tannopapadyate, yadeva
hi gauṇatve lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyati---‘anādṛtyaiva māṇavakapratyayaṃ jvalanamagniśabdāt pratiyanto
dṛśyante na tvanādṛtya jvalanapratyayaṃ māṇavakaṃ pratiyanti iti lakṣaṇāyāmapyevameva | na hyanā-
dṛtyaiva gaṅgāpratyayaṃ gaṅgāsamīpaṃ pratiyanti | asyārthaḥ-lakṣaṇābhedajñānārtho’pi nopapadyate,
yasmāt yadeva gauṇalakṣaṇaṃ praśnāsyottarabhāṣyaṃ vakṣyati ‘arthāntarānapekṣo mukhyārthaḥ, arthāntarasavya-
pekṣaśca gauṇaḥ yathā’gnipratyayāpekṣo’gniśabdo māṇavake’
 iti tadeva lakṣaṇāyāmapi gaṅgāpratyayāpekṣa-
eva gaṅgaśabdāt gaṅgāsamīpapratyaya iti | tasmādekalakṣaṇatvāt tannivṛttyarthaṃ lakṣaṇānivṛttya-
rthamapi praśnottaramidaṃ na śakyate vaktum | atha mataṃ lakṣaṇāyāmanveti lakṣaṇāpadaṃ gaṅgāsaṃbaddha-
syaiva samīpasyānvayāt gauṇe punaḥ na guṇipadamanveti | agnirmāṇavaka iti agnisaṃbandhimāṇa-
vakāpratīte | ato lākṣaṇikagauṇayoḥ evaṃ bhedaṃ vaktuṃ praśnaḥ | tadayuktaṃ pratītau tāvat
dvayamapyanveti | kārye punaḥ naikamapi | gaṅgāpi pratītāvevānvayinī na kārye samīpasyaiva
kāryānvayāt tathā’gnirapi pratītāvevānvayī agnisadṛśapratīteḥ | kārye punaḥ naikamapyanvayi |
ato’nenāpi viśeṣaṇena na viśeṣaḥ | atha vākyārthanivṛttaye yatnaḥ | asyārthaḥ
(1) P. 748

[p. r709]

---atha manyase agnirmāṇavaka ityasmāt agnisadṛśo māṇavaka iti pratītirudeti | sā ca na
padamātranibandhanā agniśabdasyāgnisadṛśe loke saṃbandhānavabodhāt | kintu padadvayaprayogāt
iyamavagatirjāyamānā vākyanimittiketi vyavasthāpyate | tena gauṇārtho vākyārthaḥ iti ye
manyante tannivṛttyarthaṃ svārthasādṛśyāt gauṇaḥ pratīyata iti idaṃ gauṇasya svarūpaṃ darśayituṃ ayaṃ
praśna iti | naivārthāntare vākyaṃ vartate | asyārthaḥ---vākyādeva gauṇaḥ pratīyate iti
atimandeyamāśaṅkā | tathā hi---naiva vākyaṃ padārthebhyaḥ vyatirikte’rthāntare vartate | yadi
tat padārthebhyo vyatirikte arthāntare vākyaṃ vartate tadā syādiyamāśaṅkā, na tvetadasti | nanu
ca svarūpamātreṇa vyavasthito’rthaḥ padārthaḥ | parasparānvitāstvarthāḥ vākyārthaḥ | evamarthāntare
vākyaṃ vartata itīmāmāśaṅkāṃ apanayannāha---anvitābhidhāyīni padānyeva hi vākyam |
anvitāśca padārthāḥ vākyārthaḥ | ekaikameva padaṃ svamarthaṃ arthāntareṇa yogyena ākāṅkṣitena
sannihitena cānvitaṃ svārthaṃ bravīti | tathābhūtānyeva vākyam | anvitāśca padairevārthāḥ
ucyanta iti padārthā eva vākyārthaḥ | yathā bahuṣu padeṣu padārtheṣu vākyamekaṃ vākyārthaścaikaḥ
tathā vākyārthamātṛkāyāmuktam | anvitābhidhānameva kutaḥ ityāha---na cānanvitā-
bhidhānamastīti pradarśitaṃ tad‌bhūtādhikaraṇe; vākyārthamātṛkāyāṃ ca asmābhiḥ
nirloḍitameva | evaṃ ca sati praśnārthaḥ vyākhyeyaḥ praśnaprayojanaṃ kathanīyam | atha
kadāciducyate sopapadāt pratīyamāno gauṇaḥ, mandaprasiddho gauṇaḥ, alpaprayogaviṣayo gauṇaḥ,
iti imān siddhāntābhāsināṃ gauṇahetūn nirākartumayaṃ praśna iti tatrāha---evaṃ sopapadasuṣṭhu-
prasiddhavahuśaḥ prayogāśca yathāyathaṃ gauṇahetu mukhyahetu(matta)tayā pratyuktā bhāṣye | ataḥ
tannirākaraṇārtho’pyayaṃ praśno nopapadyata iti | asyārthaḥ---atimandatvātteṣāṃ hetūnāṃ

[p. r710]

tannirākaraṇāya praśno nopapadyata iti | na hi māṇavakaśabdopapadādapi agniśabdādarthaḥ
pratīyamānastāvat gauṇatvenāvagantuṃ śakyate yāvattu svārthasādṛśyena gauṇapratyaya iti
nopavarṇyate | itarathā pratītyanyathānupapattyā bodhakatvakalpanaiva syāditi sopapadādapyavācakānna
pratītirutpadyate | evaṃ mandaprasiddhayapracuraprayogayorapi yojyam |

ucyate---ayaṃ praśnasyārthaḥ vākyārthāvagamāddhi nimittatayā padārthavibhāgo’va-
gamyate | vākyāditi lyablope pañcamī viśiṣṭārthapratipādakaṃ vākyamupalabhya vṛddhavyavahāre
nimittatayā vākyārthapratipattinimittatayā padārthānāṃ vibhāgo’vagamyate | viśiṣṭarūpo
vākyārtha iti viśeṣaṇabhūtaḥ padārthaḥ tatpratipattau nimittaṃ bhavati nāpratīte viśeṣaṇe viśiṣṭaḥ
śakyate pratipattumiti nimittatayā padārthavibhāgo’vagamyate | tataḥ kimityatrāha---sa cāyaṃ
nimittatvena vibhāgo māṇavake māṇavakaviṣaye agnau agniviṣaye ca prayujyamānasyāgni-
śabdasya tulyaḥ | ubhayatrāpi vācakavṛttyaiva nimittateti yāvat | taddarśayati---agnirmāṇa-
vaka ityukte māṇavakaḥ piṅgalatā ca nimittam | agnirjvalatīti jvalano jvalanakriyā
ca | tasmānna viśeṣaṃ paśyāmaḥ asya śābdo nimittabhāvo’sya gauṇaḥ iti na tu mukhyasya
ca gauṇasya ca bhedaṃ paśyāmaḥ | sarva eva mukhya iti yāvat | nanu cāgnipratyayapurassarā
māṇavake pratītiḥ paiṅgalyasya | asyārthaḥ---agniśabdasya tāvat nānekārthakalpanā nyāyyā |
ekārthapratipādanaśaktyaivārthāntarapratītisiddheḥ | tathā hi---agniśabdādagniḥ pratītaḥ piṅgalatā-
mupasthāpayati | ato’gnipratītipurassaraiva māṇavake pratītiḥ | pariharati---naivaṃ śakyate

[p. r711]

vaktum | uktamidaṃ vākyānnimittabhāvāvagatiriti | tena vākyārthaṃ prati yasya
nimittabhāvo’vagamyate sa padārthaḥ na cātrāgnyarthasya nimittabhāva upapadyate---agnirmāṇavaka
iti kevalaṃ piṅgalataiva nimittatayā’vagamyate | yathā piṅgalo māṇavaka iti |
asyārthaḥ---na svarūpeṇa tāvadagnyarthasya nimittatā, vākyārthe asamavāyāt | na
ca lakṣaṇāyāmiva vākyārthasamavāyipiṅgalopasthāpakatayā; māṇavakavartinyāḥ piṅgalatāyāḥ
agninā saha saṃbandhābhāvāt | artho hyarthāntaraṃ pratyāsattyā upasthāpayati | gaṅgā’pi hi
tīraṃ lakṣayantī svasaṃbandhinameva lakṣayati | ato na sākṣāt nāpi paramparayā agninimittatā
agyarthasya | ataḥ piṅgalatāmeva agniśabdaḥ piṅgalaśabdavat pratipādayatīti sthitam |
tasmāt durnirūpamidaṃ gauṇo’yaṃ mukhyo’yaṃ iti manvānasya praśnaḥ | guṇādāgata iti
tata āgata ityaṇ | mukhyamiti “śākhādibhyo ya iti ivārthe yaḥ | nanvevaṃ gauṇatā
nirūpyate---yadi piṅgalaśabdavadagniśabdo’pi paiṅgalyaṃ vācakavṛttyaivāha, kimiti tarhi
sopapadastatra vartate ? yāvatā nirupapado’pi tatra varteta tathā mandaprasiddhiḥ kimiti laukikānāṃ ?
tathā kimiti vā alpaprayogatā ? suṣṭha prasiddhipracuraprayogate vā syātāṃ aviśeṣādityatrāha
---na hi copapadādibhirapi upādhibhiḥ saṃbhavatīti darśitaṃ bhāṣye | ayamabhiprāyaḥ
---pratītirhi kāryarūpā aṃnyathānupapadyamānā kāraṇaṃ kalpayantī śabde vācakatvakalpanāyāṃ
heturbhavati | agnirmāṇavaka iti ca piṅgalatāvagatiranyathā nopapadyata ityuktam | tena vācakataiva,
anyathā sopapadādapi pratītirna syāt | avācakasya manāk‌prasiddhiralpaprayogatā ca durghaṭaiva |
vācakatve tu prayoganiyamasyāpyanāditvāt nānupapannaṃ kiñcit |

nanu yadi piṅgalaśabdavadagniśabdo’pi piṅgalaṃ māṇavakameva vakti kathaṃ tarhi agni-
sādṛśyaṃ atrāha---agniriva māṇavaka ityatra yuktamagnisādṛśyaṃ ivādīnāṃ sādṛśye prayoga-

[p. r712]

darśanāt | agnirmāṇavaka iti tu ivārthaśūnyo’rthaḥ1 vākyārthaḥ yathā piṅgalo māṇavaka
iti tulyo nimittabhāvaḥ | asyārthaḥ---agnirivāyaṃ māṇavaka ityukte sādṛśyaṃ yatpratīyate
tad‌yuktaṃ ivaśabdaśravaṇāt tasya ca sādṛśye prayogadarśanāt | agnirmāṇavaka iti tu
ivaśabdāprayogāt ivārthaśūnyo vākyārthaḥ yathā piṅgalo māṇavaka iti nāsti sādṛśye pratītiḥ
ivārthaśūnyatvāt vākyārthasyābhimatasya | tasmāt gauṇamukhyābhimatayoḥ tulyo nimittabhāvaḥ
vācakavṛttyaiveti | ato niṣprayojanamidamadhikaraṇaṃ | ke gauṇe’pi viniyogaḥ uta mukhya
eveti manyāmahe gauṇamukhyayorbhedābhāvāditi | nanu yadyapi piṅgalatā’bhidhīyate tathāpi
tadavagatyā sādṛśyamapi pratīyamānaṃ vākyārthe’nvitameva | ataḥ kathaṃ ivārthaśūnyo vākyārthaḥ |
athāvinābhūtamapi2 sādṛśyaṃ vākyārthe nānveti kathaṃ tarhi gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ kūpe gargakulamiti |
atra hi avinābhāvāt samīpadeśaḥ vākyārthe’nvīyata iti manyāmahe | yuktaṃ tatra
prativasatītyasya sāmarthyaṃ na bhavatīti samīpānvayitā3 nāvinābhāvo’vagata ityetāvatā
samīpasyānvayitā, kintu prativasatītyasya padasya sāmarthyaṃ na bhavatīti yuktam | agnirmāṇa-
vaka iti tu piṅgalamāṇavakāvagatiḥ na kathaṃ cinnopapadyate | ataḥ pratītamapi sādṛśyaṃ
vākyārthe nānvīyate | tasmādbhavatu lakṣaṇā saṃbandhabalotthāpitārthāntaratātparyāśrayaṇāt |
gauṇatve tu na kiñcitpramāṇamasti | yadi piṅgalatāmevāgniśabdo vakti kathaṃ tarhi
agnirmāṇavaka ityatrāgnipratyayo loke paiṅgalyādapyupapadyate | agniśabdena paiṅgalyaṃ
(1) G. śūnyatvāt vākyārthasya (2) G. avinābhūtameva (3) G. samīpānva-
yitvena

[p. r713]

vatpratipadita tatkila satiśayaṃ agnau pratītamityagnipratyayaḥ pūrvādiyāparapratyayaḥ | atrābhi-
dhīyate---ivārthanibandhana evāyaṃ pratyayaḥ | asyārthaḥ---ivārthaḥ sādṛśyaṃ tannibandhana evāyaṃ
pratyayaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati | agniśabdenāgniḥ pratipādyate 1sā pratītiḥ sadṛśyamarthāntaramupa-
sthāpayati | ato’nyathāpyarthāntarapratīterupapannatvāt na śaktyantarakalpanopapadyate | tena
śabdādeva yo jvalanaḥ pratītaḥ sa mukhyaḥ yastu śabdapratipādyajvalanasādṛśyādavagamyate sa gauṇaḥ |
kutaḥ punaravagamyate sādṛśyādayaṃ pratyaya iti ? tathāvagateḥ sadṛśāvagateḥ | yadi
tāvat piṅgalatāvagamyate tadā agniśabdaḥ piṅgalatāvacana iti kathyate | yatastvagnisadṛśā-
vagatiḥ ataḥ sādṛśyādevārthāntarāvagatiḥ kalpyate |

kathaṃ punarivaśabdamantareṇevārthaḥ pratīyate ? uttaraṃ---ivaśabdo’pi tāvat
kimitīvārthaḥ kalpyate asyārthaḥ---yathā ivaśabde prayukte sādṛśyaṃ pratīyate tathā yatrāpi
sādṛśyaṃ pratyeṣyate ivaśabdo’pi pratipadikopāttameva sādṛśyaṃ pratipādayati na punaḥ sādṛśya-
śabdavata svatantra eva brabīti | prayogadarśanāditi cet | atha manyase prayogastatra
sādṛśye prātipadikasya dṛśyate ivaśabdabalāt | yatra punaḥ ivaśabdo dyotako nāsti tatra kathaṃ
sādṛśye prayogo’vagamyate | dūṣayati---atrāpi tulyaṃ prayogadarśanam māṇavakaviṣaye agni-
śabdaprayogāta sādṛśye prayuktiravasīyate anyathā māṇavake prayogānupapatteḥ | nanu ca paiṅgalye-
’vagate jvalanasādṛśyamavagamyate aparaśabdavadityuktam | asyārthaḥ---agniśabdaḥ piṅgalatā-
mabhivadati | tasyāṃ cāvagatāyāṃ jvalanasādṛśyāvagatiḥ | na punaḥ jvalanasādṛśyaparo’yaṃ
prayoga iti na gauṇatvam | pariharati---naitadupapadyate māṇavakasadṛśo’gnirityapi pratyayaḥ
(1) G. sa pratītaḥ

[p. r714]

syāt | piṅgale māṇavake’vagate māṇavakasadṛśo’gnirityapi pratyayaḥ syāt | tathā hi
---māṇavakaḥ piṅgalo’gnisadṛśaḥ tathā’gnirapi māṇavakena sadṛśa iti māṇavakasadṛśo’gniriti
pratītiḥ syāt | niyamena tu agnisadṛśatāyāmeva pratipattiḥ tadāha---iha tu punarviparītam |
atiṣaiṅgalyājjvalanasyopamānatvamiti cet | upamīyate yena tadupamānaṃ atipiṅgalatvā-
dagneḥ tenaivānyadupamīyate na punarasāvanyeneti cet | uttaraṃ---nāsyāṃ kalpanāyāṃ pramāṇa-
masti | yasyātiśayo guṇaḥ tasyaivopamānatvamiti | nanvevamapi avagatirastu māṇavaka---
sadṛśo’gniriti; bhavatu piṅgalatāvācaka evāgniśabdaḥ tatrāha---yathāvagamaṃ hi nimittakalpanā
nyāyyā | tanmūlatvannimittābagateḥ | asyārthaḥ---yathā’bhimataṃ hi vādinaḥ tathā
nimittakalpanā nāsti | kintu yathāvagamaṃ, avagamalakṣaṇakāryanibandhanatvāt nimittabhāvā-
vagateḥ | ato yadyapi viparīto’rthaḥ saṃbhavati māṇavakasadṛśo’gnirityevaṃrūpaḥ tathāpi
prasiddhapratipakṣatvānnātmānaṃ labhate | pratipakṣastasya agnisadṛśo māṇavaka iti prasiddhiḥ |
ato nātmānaṃ labhate māṇavakasadṛśo’gniriti | apratipakṣo1 hi saṃbhavaḥ saṃbhavaḥ | na hi
saṃbhavamātreṇa pratipakṣatvaṃ, māṇavakasadṛśo’gnirityasya saṃbhavati | tasmādupapannaṃ prayogapratya-
yābhyāṃ gauṇamukhyābhidhānam | sthite gauṇamukhyatve kiṃ mukhya evābhidheye mantrāṇāṃ
viniyogaḥ uta gauṇe’pīti yukto vicāraḥ | kiṃ tāvatprāptam ? aviśeṣādubhayābhidhānaṃ
gauṇasya ca mukhyasya ca śabdātpratīyamānatayā aviśeṣādubhayoḥ mantrābhidhānaṃ yuktam |
tadāha---agnirjvalatītyapi prayogo dṛṣṭaḥ agnirmāṇavaka ityapi | tasmānna vinigamanāyāṃ
(1) G. kṣo’gnisaṃbhavaḥ

[p. r715]

heturasti | atrābhidhīyate---ayamasti vinigamanāyāṃ hetuḥ mukhyābhidhānaśūnyaṃ nāsti
gauṇābhidhānamiti pradarśitam | tasmānmukhya eva śabdasya vyāpāraḥ gauṇe tvarthādavagatiḥ |
bhavatu mukhya eva śabdavyāpāraḥ tathāpi kimityatrāha---abhidhānena ca mantrā upakurvanti |
mukhye cābhidhānaṃ na gauṇe | ato na mantraviniyogaḥ | kutastarhi gauṇapratyayaḥ |
yadi śabdāt gauṇapratyayo na bhavati kutastarhi sa ityarthaḥ | uttaraṃ---sādṛśyāditi |
pratīto’gniḥ sadṛśamupasthāpayatītyarthaḥ | pūrvapakṣyāha---satyamuktaṃ sādṛśyādgauṇāvagatiriti |
tattūpāyatvena na punaḥ nimittanirākaraṇatayāpi1 | tathāpi nimittatā na nirākṛtetyarthaḥ |
ato nimittatvasyobhayatrāviśeṣāt gauṇepi mantrāṇāṃ viniyogaḥ | rāddhāntī tu brūte---kena voktaṃ
nimittatā nirākriyata iti | mukhye prathamopanipātini viniyogasaṃbhavāta na gauṇepi
pāścātye viniyogaḥ kṣamaḥ grāhakagrahaṇamantrāmnāmayoḥ kṛtārthatvāt ityetāvaducyate yatra punaḥ
mukhye viniyogo na saṃbhavati tatra gauṇepi viniyogaḥ | yadi tasya mukhye viniyogo na
saṃbhavati tasya gauṇe viniyogaḥ | na tarhi pūṣādyanumantraṇādīnāṃ utkarṣaḥ prakaraṇe pūṣṇo-
’bhāvāt | anyatra pauṣṇe karmaṇi sāmānyasaṃbandhābhāvena liṅgādviniyogānupapatteḥ | pari-
harati---samākhyāyā viniyuktānāṃ anumantraṇamantra ityanayā samākhyayā sāmānyānumantraṇa-
(1) G. R. karaṇatayaiva

[p. r716]

saṃbandhasiddheḥ liṅgānmukhya eva prakaraṇāntaravartini viniyogaḥ saṃbhavatītyutkarṣaḥ | upasaṃharati
---tasmāt mukhye yatra saṃpratyayaḥ na tatra gauṇepi mantreṇaiva prakāśanaṃ kāryamiti niyame
pramāṇamasti iti | tadidaṃ sarvaṃ kimarthaṃ mukhye pratyāyayatītyevamādinā pradarśitamiti |

vacanāttvayathārthamaindrī syāt |

‘aindrayā gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhate’
 ityatra saṃśayaḥ kimaindreṇa mantreṇa gārhapatyasyābhidhānaṃ
kartavyaṃ utendrasyeti | anena saṃśayabhāṣyagatamupasthānapadaṃ vyākhyātam | kathaṃ punaḥ
gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhata iti spaṣṭāyāṃ dvitīyāśrutau saṃśayaḥ | uttaraṃ---mukhye’rthe mantro
viniyujyata iti sthitamādhastye’dhikaraṇe | tena gārhapatyopasthānaṃ na saṃbhavatīti manyate |
nanu ca mukhyasyāsaṃbhavāt gauṇepi ca viniyujyata eva | etadeva na vijānīmaḥ saṃbhavo
vidyate neti | nanu “gārhapatyamabhidhatta”
 iti spaṣṭaiva śrutiḥ uttaraṃ---viniyojya-
sāmarthyānusārī viniyoga iti | anīpsitārthā dvitīyā bhavatīti matiḥ | asyārthaḥ
---yadyatra śaktaṃ tatra tat kārakatayā viniyujyate | na gārhapatyābhidhāne samartho’yaṃ mantra iti
(1) P. 752

[p. r717]

na gārhipatye viniyogamarhatīti anīpsitārthā dvitīyā bhaviṣyati | tathā ca smaryate tathā
yuktaṃ cānīpsitamiti | ayameva cāsya viṣayo viniyojyavaśāt viniyojyasya mantasya
sāmarthyavaśāt gārhapatye viniyogābhāvāt kārakaparyavasāyyevaitat sūtram | parihārāntara-
māha---yadvā gahipatyamabhyetya abhidhatte iti dvitīyopapadyata iti | tasmādindrābhidhānam
yuktaṃ caitadevaṃ yadindrābhidhānaṃ anyathā hi alaukikī abhidhānaśaktiḥ kalpanīyā | nanu
vināpi śaktikalpanayā gauṇamabhidhānaṃ bhaviṣyati | tena gārhapatye viniyogo nirvahati |
pariharati---nāsti gauṇamabhidhānaṃ siddhe hi prayoge gauṇamabhidhānam na punargauṇāt prayoga-
siddhiḥ | yatra pramāṇāntareṇa paratra paraśabdaprayogasiddhiḥ tatra gauṇamabhidhānam | na punaḥ
gauṇo’stītyetāvatā prayogaḥ kalpyate | na cātra gārhapatye indraśabdaprayogo’nyataḥ siddhaḥ,
kartavyatopadeśāt anena mantreṇābhidhānaṃ kartavyamityevaṃparamidaṃ vākyaṃ aindrayā gārhapatya-
mupatiṣṭhata iti, na punargārhapatye indrapadaṃ prayuktamityevaṃ param | ato nirapekṣatvādabhi-
dhānavṛtteḥ yathābhidhānaṃ viniyogaḥ | atrābhidhīyate---gārhapatyābhidhānameva kartavyaṃ mantreṇa |
kutaḥ ? yathopadeśaṃ hi kāryaṃ, na yathā kāryamupadeśaḥ | asyārthaḥ---upadeśanibandhanaṃ
kāryaṃ na kārye’vagate’nyataḥ upadeśavarṇanam | atideśo hi saḥ yaḥ kāryānurūpaḥ |
aindrayā gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhata iti cāyaṃ upadeśaḥ | ataḥ upadeśāt mārhapatyābhidhānameva

[p. r718]

kāryamiti manyate | nanu ca yathākāryamupadeśo’pīti saptamādya uktam ityanena
pūrvāparavirodhamāśaṅkate | pariharati---viniyogārthaḥ tatra kāryaśabdenoktaḥ na punarāptyādayaḥ
tena bhinnatvātkāryasya nāsti virodhaḥ | saptamādye niyogārthaṃ kāryamaṅgīkṛtya kāryānuguṇa upadeśa
uktaḥ | iha tu āptyātmakaṃ kāryamabhipretya yathopadeśaṃ kāryamuktam | nanu ca viniyoga
eva sandihyate | yadi hi upadeśa eva niścitaḥ syāt tadā yathopadeśaṃ kāryamiti
gārhapatyopasthānaṃ kāryaṃ niścīyeta | na copadeśo niścitaḥ | kimiti na niścita iti cet |
anīpsitepi dvitīyādarśanāt | kārakamātre dvitīyā dṛśyata ityarthaḥ | pariharati---kimidaṃ
anīpsitamiti | uttaraṃ---tathā yuktaṃ cānīpsitamiti | rāddhāntī brūte---sutarāṃ
tarhi dhātvarthe prati prādhānyam | yadi hi tathāyuktasūtreṇa iyaṃ dvitīyā tadā sutarāṃ ghātvarthā-
pekṣayā sādhyatvaṃ gārhapatyasya, kriyājanyaphalabhāgitā ca prādhānyaśabdenocyate | anyathā hi
tathāyogo’pi na syāt | tathāyuktaṃ cānīpsitamityatra tathā yuktaśabdenaivamucyate
yathā īpsitaṃ kriyayā yuktaṃ kriyāphalabhāgitayā tathā’nīpsitamapi kriyāphalabhāgitayā kriyayā
yuktaṃ tatra dvitīyeti | tatra yadi gārhapatyasyopasthānalakṣaṇaphalayogitā nāṅgīkriyate tadā tathāyogo
na syāt | tataśca tenāpi sūtreṇa dvitīyā na bhavet ato dvitīyādarśanāt gārhapatyasya
kriyāphalabhāgitā’vagamyate | yadyanīpsitasya prādhānyaṃ tadā sutarāmiti yaduktaṃ tatredānīṃ
kastarabarthaḥ ? etāvattatra vaktavyam dhātvarthaṃ prati prādhānyamiti | uttaraṃ---anīpsitamapi
pradhānamiti | asyārthaḥ---yadyapyanīpsitatā tathāpīha prādhānyaṃ anyatra īpsitatve prādhānyaṃ

[p. r719]

ataḥ sutarāṃ prādhānyamityucyate | nanu coktaṃ nādṛṣṭā śabdaśaktiḥ śakyate kalpayitumiti |
anenedamāha---yadyapyupastheyatā gārhapatyasya gamyate tathāpi mantrasyāśakteḥ tathā viniyogo na
nirvahatīti vibhaktivyatyayo vā bhavet | upetyeti vā adhyāhriyatāmiti | atra rāddhāntī
---kimatrādṛṣṭaṃ vacanāt dṛśyata eva yadā vacanenedaṃ kāryaṃ avagataṃ tadā tadbalena yathā
nirvahati tathā kriyate | nirvahati cedaṃ gauṇatvenendraśabdasyeti nādṛṣṭakalpanā doṣaḥ
vacanādeva gauṇatvāśrayaṇena śaktidarśanādityuktavāt | pūrvapakṣyāha---na vacanāt padārtha-
śaktiḥ śakyate’vagantumiti | siddharūpāyāṃ śaktau kāryaikaviṣayasya śabdasya prāmāṇyaṃ
nopapadyata ityarthaḥ | yadi śabdācchaktirnāvagamyate kathaṃ tarhi yāgāderniyogārthasiddhau
śaktiḥ | uttaraṃ---kāryāvagamyatvāt | kāryabhūtena niyogena sahānvitābhidhānāt
sādhanatayā yāgādayo’bhidhīyante | na cāśaktānāṃ taditi teṣāṃ tatra śaktirniścīyate
iha tu tadviparītam | yadyatrāpi pratipattavyamiti vacanaṃ syāt bhavetkāryadarśanam |
pratipattirhi kāryabhūtā | sā cetthaṃ kāryatvenāvagamyate | yadi indraśabdāt gārhapatyaḥ
pratyetavya iti vacanaṃ bhavet endayāgārhapatyamupatiṣṭhata iti idantvanyaparaṃ vacanaṃ endrayā gārhapatya-
mabhidhatta iti mantrasya kāryamācaṣṭe na padasyetyarthaḥ | yadidamasmābhiruktaṃ mantraviniyoga-
paratvena padakāryapratipādakatvābhāvādvacanasya na vacanāt śaktirdṛśyata iti | tadidamuktaṃ
saṃbandhasyāśāstrahetutvāditi | nanu mābhūta śabdasya śaktiḥ gauṇaṃ tarhyabhidhānaṃ bhaviṣyati
pariharati---nanu prayogasiddhau gauṇamabhidhānamityuktam | rāddhāntamāha---atrāpi kārya-
(1) G. sya śāstra

[p. r720]

tayā siddha ityaviśeṣaḥ1 asyārthaḥ---anena mantreṇa yeyaṃ gārhapatyopasthāpanakāryaśeṣatā
tayaivendraśabdasya gārhapatye prayogassiddhaḥ | anyathā gārhapatyaḥ kathamanena mantreṇābhidhīyata
ityarthāt gauṇīvṛttiḥ | nanu sūtre guṇādveti goṇīvṛttiruktā, kathaṃ bhāṣyakāraḥ2 indateraiśvarya-
karmaṇaḥ’ ityādinā yaugikatvamāheti tatrāha-guṇa śravaṇaṃ3 pradarśanārthaṃ sūtre iti matvā yogā-
dvetyuktaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa |

tathā’’hvānamapīti cait | nanu kathamatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ yāvatā haviṣkṛdehīti trirava-
ghnannāhvatītyāhvāne mantro viniyujyata iti pratibhāti ityatrāha---atrāpyavaghnannāhvayatītyā-
hvānena āhūyate aneneti vyutpattyā mantreṇāvaghātaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ | anena mantreṇāvaghātaṃ
kurvannāhvayatītyarthaḥ | tenāvaghātasya mantrasya ca saṃbandho vidhitsitaḥ | nanu katha mantro’va-
ghātaṃ vakti ityatrāha---guṇādabhidhānaṃ bhaviṣyati yogādvā, haviṣkṛtpatnī tadguṇayogāt
avahantirapyucyate | yadvā haviḥ karotīti vyutpattyā mukhyayaiva bṛttyā yogādabhidhānam | nanu
conenāvaghāto nāhvātuṃ śakyate ataḥ kathamāhvayatirityatrāha---āhvayatītyapyanuvādaḥ yo hyehīti
brūte sa āhvayatyeva | aptyārthaḥ---cetanaviṣayepyetadevāhvāturāhvānaṃ yadehīti śabdoccāraṇaṃ,
āhūyamānastu ātmānamavabudhyatāṃ mā vā | asti cātra ehīti śabdaprayogaḥ tasmādāhvayatī-
tyanuvādaḥ | yadi mantrāvaghātasaṃbandho hi vidhīyate kathaṃ tarhi tririti śabdo’nveti |
na hyavihitasrirabhyāso labhyate | vidhīyate cedvākyabhedaḥ | uttaraṃ---śākhāntare pāṭhāt
(1) R. &amp; G. ityadoṣaḥ (2) P. 755 (3) R. grahaṇa

[p. r721]

prāptatvādayamayyanuvāda eva | evaṃ śākhāntare trirāvṛtto mantraḥ paṭhyate | ato’pyayamanu-
vādaḥ | ucyate---trirityetāvadvidhīyate anyatsarvamanūdyate | asyārthaḥ---āhvānasya
mantrasya cātra saṃbandhaḥ sa ca mantro rūpādevāhvāne prāpnoti iti na vidheyaḥ | tathā ‘avaghnannā-
hvayati’
 ityatra avaghātakālopi prāpta eva | kāryakāla eva hi sahāyānveṣaṇaṃ prāpnoti |
kevalamantrasya trirāvṛttiḥ aprāptā sā vidhīyate | nanu trirityapi śākhāntare pāṭhāt prāptaṃ
na nityavatprāptaṃ, aniyamāt | kathamaniyamḥ ? kvacit dviḥ paṭyate ayaṃ mantro yataḥ
tasmādaniyamaḥ | ato’vaghātādivanniyamārthaṃ vacanam | upasaṃharati---ato’vaghnanniti
lakṣaṇaiṣā avaghātena kālo lakṣyata ityarthaḥ | nanu cāyameva rāddhāntarapakṣe doṣaḥ yaduta
lakṣaṇā; nāyaṃ doṣaḥ anyataḥ prāpteḥ | yadā tririti vidhīyate tadā padāntaraṃ yathānvayaṃ
yāti tathā varṇayitavyam | tatrāvaghnanniti mukhyayā vṛttyā anvayāsaṃbhavāt lakṣaṇāśrayaṇamadoṣaḥ |
nanu ca pravṛttāvaghātasya naiva mantraḥ prāpnoti havipkṛdāhvānakālo’pi nityavanna prāpnoti |
tena so’pi[tasyāpi] vidheyatayā vākyabhedāpattiriti | pariharati---kālasyānyataḥ prāptatvādidama-
codyam | kāryakāla eva hi sahāyānveṣaṇetyuktam | tasmānna gauṇe viniyoge
pramāṇamasti | tasmādayamatra vicārārthaḥ---kiṃ kālalakṣaṇābhayāt tririti pākṣiko’nuvādaḥ
uta nityavadanvitatrirabhyāsabalena kālalakṣaṇā āśrīyatāṃ iti | pūrvapakṣavādī vadati---pakṣe
paraṃ śabdārthahānāt pakṣikānuvādaḥ na punarlakṣaṇeti | rāddhāntī vadani---nityavadanvitasya
pakṣe’nvayabādhaḥ prāpnoti | tasmādvaraṃ lakṣaṇā | tasyāṃ hi satyāṃ sarvapadānāṃ nityavadanvaya

[p. r722]

iti evaṃ tāvat gauṇapakṣamāśritya rāddhāntitam | yadi punarayaṃ mantro’vaghāta eva
mukhyārthaḥ syāt nāvaghāte lakṣaṇā kalpitā bhavet | śakyate ca haviṣkṛcchabdena
avaghāto vaktuṃ patnīvat | yathā patnī yaugikena haviṣkṛcchabdenocyate tathā avaghāto’pi |
ato mantro’vaghāte prāptatvāt na vidhīyate trirāvṛttireva kevalā vidhāyiṣyata iti | satyaṃ
śakyate | āhvānaṃ tu ananvitārthaṃ prāpnoti | mantragatamehītipadaṃ2 āhvānātmakaṃ
ananvitārthaṃ prāpnoti | tena liṅgāttāvat tanmātrasya prāptirnāsti | athāprāpto’vaghāta-
mantro’pi vidhīyate tatrāha---tririti ca aniyataṃ tyāt | tasmādvaraṃ lakṣaṇaiva | tayā ca
liṅgamaikāntikam | tathā patnī haviṣkṛditi śakyā haviṣkaraṇayogena vaktuṃ vāk‌sādṛśyaṃ ca
haviṣkṛta ucyate | tacca strītvena | tacca patnyāṃ aikāntikaṃ 3puliṃṅgasyāvaghātaśabdasya
darśanāt |

tathotthānavisarjane | nanu codāharaṇāntaramevedaṃ kimatrātidiśyate | na hi anya-
tvādṛte sādṛśyamupapadyate | anyathā tadeva hi syāt idamastyanyatvaṃ pūrvasminnadhikaraṇe
(1) P. 758 (2) G. hantipadaṃ (3) G. anaikāntikam

[p. r723]

trirityetāvadvidhīyate anyatsarvamanūdyate | iha punaḥ kālasaṃyogo vidhātavyaḥ aprāpte ca
kālasaṃyogavidhāne lakṣaṇā na nyāyyeti pūrvapakṣavādī adhikaraṇavaiṣamyaṃ manyate | asyārthaḥ
-trirityetāvati vidhīyamāne sarvasyānūdyamānatvāt avaghnannityasya lakṣaṇā yuktā | iha punaḥ
kālasaṃyogona prāptaḥ sa vighātavyaḥ | na ca vidhau lakṣaṇā yukteti viśeṣaḥ pūrvadhikaraṇāditi |
rāddhāntī tu praiṣārthānanvayatulyatvāt tathaivetyuktavān | yathā pūrvatra praiṣārtho nānvīyate tathā-
’trāpyacetanatvāt 2utthānavisarjanayoḥ praiṣārthānanvayaḥ | nanu vidhivākye mukhyatvāśrayaṇena
mantre kimiti praiṣārtho vivakṣito na bhavati | ucyate---vighivākye lakṣaṇayāpi hi padā-
rthānāmaśeṣāṇāmanvayaḥ | mantre tu sarvathaiva preṣārtho’na3nvitaḥ ityavivakṣā’’śrayaṇīyeti |

sūktavāke ca | iha sūktavākena prastaraṃ praharatītyudāharaṇe ki [haratau4 sūktavāka-
ścodyate utātrāpi kālalakṣaṇetyudāharaṇadarśanena saśayaḥ samarthitaḥ | sūktavākeneti tṛtīyayā
mukhyasūktavākaśabdaśravaṇāt haratau sādhanatvena sūktavākaḥ codyata iti pratīyate | harateśca
praharaṇamātravācitvāt na sūktavākaḥ sādhanatvenaivānvayaṃ yātīti kālalakṣaṇeti ca pratibhātīti |
kiṃ tāvadyuktaṃ ? agnīdagnīn viharetivat anyārthatvānmantrasya yathā praiṣārthatvānmantrasyotthāne na
viniyogaḥ tathā sūktavākasya devatāprakāśakatvāt haratau ca devatāyā ananvayāt na
viniyogo yuktaḥ | ayaṃ cāparo viśeṣaḥ yathā tatra ‘agnīdagnīn vihara’
 ityanena śakyate
utthānaṃ vaktuṃ agnimindhe ityanayā vyutpattyā yathā’gnīcchabdenotthānaṃ śakyate vaktuṃ na tathā
(1) jha. 759 (2) G. āhān (3) G. vivakṣita ityarthaḥ ato vivakṣā
nāśrayaṇīyeti (4) G. haraṇe

[p. r724]

sūktavākena haratiḥ kenacitprakāreṇābhidhātuṃ śakyate | tasmāt kālavidhipratipattau ca
saṃbhavantyāṃ nārthakarma kalpitaṃ bhavati | yadi sūktavāko haratau sādhanatvena codyate tadā devatā-
prakāśakatvāt sūktavākasya devatāyāśca yoge’nanvayāt yāgarūpatā harateraṅgīkaraṇīyāḥ
tataścārthakarmatā | pratipattau ca saṃbhavantyāṃ nārthakarma kalpitaṃ bhavati |

upadeśo vā | atrocyate---neyaṃ kālalakṣaṇā | kiṃ tarhi mantropadeśo’yam |
ayamāśayaḥ---sūktavākaśabdena mantraviśeṣaḥ śrutyā ucyate | kālastu lākṣaṇikaḥ | na ca
śrutisaṃbhave lakṣaṇā nyāyyeti mantra eva karaṇaṃ vidhīyate | nanu cānyārtho manva ityuktaṃ, na
haraterabhidhāteti | mā bhūt haratervācakaḥ haratidevatāyā vācako bhaviṣyati | yadyapi
na sākṣāddharatiṃ ayaṃ mantro’bhidhātuṃ śaknoti tathāpi tatsādhanabhūtadevatāprakāśanadvāreṇa
bhaviṣyati haraṇe karaṇam | nanu ca nirvṛttayāgadevatāvācako’yaṃ, nivṛttā ye yāgāḥ teṣāṃ
yā devatāḥ tā eva mantro vakti tataścānyadīyasādhanavacanaḥ kathaṃ haratiḥ devatāvācako bhaviṣyati |
na ca haratiryajatiḥ haraṇaṃ prakṣepaṇamātramucyate na yāgaḥ | na cāyāge devatā sādhanaṃ
bhavati | tasmānna sādhanadvāreṇāpi prakāśanasaṃbhavaḥ | ucyate---nirvṛttayāgadevatā-
vacanatvepi tatsaṃskāro yāgo bhaviṣyati paśupuroḍāśavat | yathā paśupuroḍāśaḥ agnī-
ṣomasmaraṇārthaḥ tathā’yamapi yāgaḥ pūrvayāgasaṃpratipannadevatātvāta devatāsmaraṇārtho bhaviṣyati |
nanu cāyaṃ haratirna yajatirityuktam | tenāyāgasya sato na devatāsaṃskārārthatvaṃ bhaviṣyati |
pariharati---haratiryajatiḥ mantrasaṃbandhāt | tathāhi-spaṣṭena vākyena haratau mantro viniyujyata

[p. r725]

ityavagamyate tena haratireva yajatirbhaviṣyati | nanu caivaṃ sati saiva lakṣaṇā prāpnoti, yasyā
dṛṣṭatvāt pūrvapakṣaparityāgaḥ prakṣepamātravacanena haratinā yajatirlakṣaṇayopasthāpanīya iti | pariharati
---na lakṣaṇā yāge sati haratyarthasya prāptatvāt | ālametetivat | asyārthaḥ-na
haratinā yajatirlakṣyate | kintu haratyartho’yaṃ yāge sati prāpta evānūdyate yathā agnīṣomīyaṃ
paśumālabhetetyālabhbhaḥ | nanu ca yuktaṃ tatra dravyadevatāsaṃbandhena gāgasya kalpyamānatvāt |
iha tu yāgopasthāpane na kiñcit pramāṇamasti | ucyate---sūktavākena prastaraṃ praharatīti
sūktavākaharaṇayoḥ saṃbandho’vagamyate | sa ca saṃbandho’tra vidhīyate | tenāyamarthaḥ saṃpadyate
tathā yateta yathā sūktavākaharaṇayoḥ saṃbandhaḥ saṃpadyata iti | tatra sūktavākena homātmake
yāge devatāprakāśanadvāreṇa kriyamāṇe bhavati sūktavākasya haraṇasya ca kriyākārakalakṣaṇaḥ
saṃbandhaḥ | homātmakepi yāge anuṣṭhīyamāne upādhibhūtaḥ prakṣepaḥ saṃpadyate | mantreṇa ca
devatāmuddiśya homātmako yāgaḥ śakyate kartuṃ iti pāramparyeṇāsti sādhyasādhanabhāvaḥ | tasmādyathā
dravyadevatāsaṃbandhavidhānenārthādyāgalābhaḥ tathehāpi mantraharaṇasaṃbandhavidhānena yāgalābhaḥ | tenaivātra
pūrvapakṣasiddhāntaviracanā---sūktavākasya haraṇe sādhakatvānupapatteḥ praharaṇena homātmako yāgo
lakṣayitavyaḥ | na cāsau yuktaḥ ākhyātārthe pradhāne lakṣaṇāprasaṅgāt | varaṃ sūktavākena
kāla eva lakṣyatāmiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntastu na haraṇena yāgo lakṣaṇīyaḥ saṃbandhavidhānādeva
homātmako yāgaḥ kalpyatāṃ; tathā sati sarvapadānyeva śrautārthāti saṃbhaviṣyantīti | atra ca
yajatyātmakatvāt haratyātmakatvaṃ yajatereva yaducyate tadupacāreṇa draṣṭavyam | prakṣepo hi haraṇaṃ; na
ca yāgaḥ prakṣepātmakaḥ | vastutastu homarūpe yoge upādhibhūto haratiḥ arthaprāpta iti | evaṃ ca
sati ‘idaṃ havirajuṣata avīvṛghata, ayaṃ yajamāna āyurāśāste’
 ityupapannārthaḥ mantro bhaviṣyati |
anyathā hi vṛttamidamā(na) nirdeśyaṃ syāt | pūrvapakṣe hi vṛttaṃ haviḥ sūktavākena kīrtanī-
yam | tatra sannihitābhidhāyaka idaṃśabdo nopapadyate | rāddhānte tu vartamānaṃ prastarākhyaṃ havirucyata
iti yuktaḥ idamā nirdeśaḥ | āśāsta iti ca bhūtanirdeśaḥ pūrvapakṣe sūktavākasya karmāntarā-

[p. r726]

saṃbandhāt vṛttakarmagataivāśīrucyate | tatra vartamānanirdeśo nopapadyate | rāddhānte tu
karmāntaragatā vartamā(ne)naivāśīrucyata iti nānupapannaṃ kiñcit | upasaṃharati---tasmāt suṣṭhu
śobhanaṃ ucyate haratiryajatiriti | eṣa copacāravādo bhāṣyakārasyeti mantavyam |
yaduktaṃ pratipattau saṃbhavantyāṃ nārthakarma nyāyyamiti | bhāṣyakāreṇa sviṣṭakṛdvadavirodho
bhaviṣyatītyuktam | yathā sviṣṭakṛti dravyamapi pratipādyate devatāśceṣṭāḥ smaryante tathehāpi
prastaraśca pratipādyate devatāśceṣṭāḥ smṛtā bhavanti yāgepi satīti nāsti virodhaḥ prastaramiti
dvitīyāyāḥ praharaṇasya ca prahāryapradhānatayeti |

kṛtsnopadeśāt | sthitaṃ tāvat vacanāddharatiryajatiḥ sūktavākamantrastheti |
idamidānīṃ cintyate---kiṃ sakalasya sūktavākasya paurṇamāsyāmamāvāsyāyāṃ ca prayogaḥ
uta devatāpadavibhāgeneti | anenāvāntarasaṅgatimāha saṃśayaṃ ca pūrvapakṣābhāvenākṣeptuṃ
anubhāṣate | sthite hi vākyena praharaṇe sūktavākasya viniyoge pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā nānyathā |
ākṣepamāha---nanu cinteyaṃ nopapadyate sthitamarthaikatvādekavākyatvam | na cātra pradhāna-
devatāpadānāṃ praśliṣṭapaṭhitānāmatra athaikatvaṃ saṃbhavati | nāpi vibhajyamānasākāṅkṣatvam |
(1) P. 764

[p. r727]

tasmādbhinnānyetāni vākyāni bhinnānāṃ caikavacananirdeśe sati kathaṃ kṛtsnaḥ prayoga
āśaṅkyate | asyārthaḥ-praśliṣṭaparipaṭhitānāṃ mantrāṇāmarthaikatvāta vibhāge sākāṅkṣatvāt
ekavākyatvamuktam | na cātra pradhānadevatāpadānāmarthaikatvam | na ca vibhāge sākāṅkṣatā |
ato bhinnānyevaitāni vākyāni | bhinneṣu vākyeṣu ekavacanāntena śabdena ekasyaiva vākyasya
viniyogo na sarveṣāṃ ekavacanavirodhāt yathā paśunetyeka eva paśurgṛhyate | ataḥ sakalamantra-
prayogāśaṅkā nopapadyate | yathā āgneyyā āgnīdhramupatiṣṭhate ityatra āgnecyetyukte na kṛtsnā
āgneyyaḥ prayoktavyā ityāśaṅkā bhavati | mukhye ca saṃbhavati na gauṇe viniyoga ityapi
sthitam | yadi sakalasyobhayatra viniyogaḥ syāt tadā ubhayadevatāvācināṃ padānāṃ parasparaṃ
ubhayatra gauṇārthatā syāt | na ca mukhyenaiva viniyogena mantre kṛtārthepi gauṇe viniyoga
ityapi sthitaṃ gauṇamukhyādhikaraṇe | ato vibhajyaiva viniyogo yuktaḥ | ucyate---yathāha
bhavān, kintu pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | sūktavāko vartata iti vākyasamūhe ekavacana-
midam | evamatrāpi sṛktavākeneti samūhagatamevedamekavacanam | ataḥ samūha evātra
karaṇam | asyārthaḥ-sūktavāko vartata iti sakale mantre paṭhyamāne adhyetṝṇāmadhyāpakānāṃ
śrotṛṇāṃ ca [ā]yata eva loke vyapadeśaḥ | sasa cābhinnavākyābhiprāyeṇa | anyathā bahuvacana-
prasaṅgāt | tasmādekavacanadarśanādavagacchāmaḥ samūha eva sūktavākaśabda iti | evaṃ ca
sūktavākena prastaraṃ praharatītyatrāpi samūha eva viniyukta iti kṛtsna evobhayatra
prayojyaḥ |

[p. r728]

nanu ca samūhasyaikārthatā na saṃbhavati na bhinnadevatāvācināṃ padānāmekārthabhāvaḥ
saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ | pariharati---vacanāt kalpyate yathottame prayāje | asyārthaḥ ---yathā
uttame prayāje, uttamaśabdaḥ svabhāvāt triprabhṛtīnāmantyamāha | tena svāhākāre yathā nānā-
devatāvācināmapi padānāmekārthabhāvaḥ tathehāṣi vacanāt kalpyata itīmāmāśaṅkāṃ nivartayituṃ
idamadhikaraṇam | kṛtaḥ pūrvapakṣaḥ | idānīṃ rāddhānto’bhidhīyate | nātra samūhaviniyoge
pramāṇamasti | kutaḥ ? sūktavāko vartata ityanyathāpyekavacanopapatteḥ | asyārthaḥ
-sūktamanenocyata iti sūktavākaśabdena vākyamucyate vākyena hi sūktamucyate na vākyasamūhena,
tasmādvākyameva sūktavākaḥ | bhinnāni caitāni vākyāni | tasmādekavacanaśravaṇāt na
sakalasya viniyogaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi sūktavāko vartata iti sakala ekavacanamupapadyata iti |
ekābhidhānāddhi pāthakānāṃ sūktabāko nartata iti yujyate’bhidhānam | sarvāṇyapi tāni
sūktamekaṃ vadanti | tena tadekatvāt kārakarūpatāmevāśritya bahuṣvapyekavacanaprayogo na
virudhyate yathā padaṃ vākyamiti bahuṣvapi varṇeṣu padeṣu ca ekakāryanibanghanaḥ ekavacanaprayogaḥ
iha punaḥ sūktavākena prastaraṃ praharatīti na liṅgabādhenaikavākyatvaṃ kalpayituṃ śakyaṃ yathā-
liṅgaṃ viniyogopapatteḥ | yena hyekamidaṃ vākyaṃ prastarapraharaṇe viniyujyata ityāśrīyate
tenāpi bhinnadevatāvācināṃ padānāṃ svasāmarthyabādhenaikārthatvaṃ kalpayitavyam | taccānupapannam |
yathāliṅgamapi viniyogopapatteḥ | tasmāt sarveṣāṃ sūktavākavacanena viśeṣāgrahaṇāt yena

[p. r729]

kenacit sūktavākena kṛte praharaṇe kṛtaṃ bhavati sūktavākena prastaraṃ praharatīti yathā-
coditānuṣṭhānam | nanu bhāṣyakāreṇa darśapūrṇamāsasamudāye sūktavākasamudāyaviniyogāt

liṅgakramasamākhyānāt | ādhastye adhikaraṇe uktaṃ---prakaraṇāt pratyekaṃ śeṣatve

pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | anenāvāntarasaṅgatimāha | liṅgācchebatve atantre kramasamākhye |
liṅgena mantrasya śeṣatve saṃbhavati kramaḥ samākhyā ca kāraṇameva na bhavatīti na viśeṣopasaṃhāraḥ |
tasmādyāvadaindreāgnakarma mantrasya viniyogaḥ | nanu ca liṅgaṃ naiva kevalaṃ viniyojakamiti
rāddhānte vakṣyate yadi hi kevalaṃ liṅga viniyojakaṃ syāt tadā kramasamākhyānirapekṣaṃ
sarvatra viniyuñjīta | na caivamiṣyate | tatkartha sarvatra viniyojakāśaṅkā | viniyoja-
katve ca balābalam | asyārtha---sthite viniyojakatve kramasya samākhyāyāśca liṅgena saha

[p. r730]

balābalacintā na cādyāpi tayoḥ viniyojakatvamuktam | tena nedamadhikaraṇamārabdhumadhunocitam |
kimarthaṃ ca prāk śrutiliṅgādhikaraṇāt balābalacintā | atrābhidhīyate---naiveyaṃ balā-
balacintā | yadi balābalacintā syāt tadā syādeva pūrvoktadoṣaḥ | na tveṣā balābala-
cintā | kintu sāpekṣaṃ liṅgaṃ viniyojakaṃ ityatra pratipādyate | tarhi nirapekṣaṃ liṅgaṃ
viniyojakamiti pūrvapakṣeṇa bhavitavyam | na cāyaṃ pūrvapakṣo yuktaḥ | sāmarthyamātrasya
viniyojakatvānupapatteḥ | idamanena kartavyamiti na sāmarthyamātrāt jñānamutpadyate | tadida-
māha---kathaṃ tarhi pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | uttaraṃ---liṅgamapi śrutyādivat pramāṇāntaranirapekṣamiti
balābalasūtre ‘śrutiliṅgavākyaprakaraṇasthānasamākhyānāṃ samavāye’
 ityatrānukramāt bhrāgyataḥ
pūrvapakṣo’yam| tulyavadanukramaṇāt kasyacidiyamāśaṅkā śrutyādivannirapekṣaṃ liṅgamiti |

 ityevaṃ rūpe pramāṇāntarāvasite
sati śeṣiviśeṣe liṅgaṃ viniyojakamiti | yathā ca liṅgaṃ na kevalaṃ viniyojakaṃ tathā
śrutiliṅgādhikaraṇe nipuṇataramupapādayiṣyāmḥ | etacca bhāṣyakārāyamāṇo nibandhakāra
āha-yataḥ sāpekṣaṃ liṅgaṃ viniyojakaṃ ataḥ kramasahitaṃ samākhyāsahitaṃ ca viniyojakamiti |
nanu ca samākhyānamātrādapi prakṛtamudāharaṇaṃ siddhayati | tathā hi---kāmyayājyānuvākyā-
kāṇḍamiti samākhyāyāṃ satyāṃ kāmyāyāmiṣṭau yājyākārye viniyogaḥ siddhayati | nanu caika-

[p. r731]

sminnapi karmaṇi anekayājyānuvākyāyugalasya viniyojakatvaṃ syāt | tathā dvitīye’pi
karmaṇi prathamapaṭhitasya yugalasya viniyogaḥ | naitadevam | ekena yugalena karmaṇāṃ kṛtārthatvāt
nānekayugalaviniyoge pramāṇamasti | tenaikenaivaikatra bhavitavyam | tatra vināpi liṅgaṃ
kramākhyena pramāṇena prathamapaṭhite karmaṇi prathamasyaiva viniyogo bhaviṣyati, prāthamyāta | ane-
naiva nyāyena dvitīye dvitīyasya viniyoga iti | tathā liṅgakramādapyayamartho labhyate tatra
krameṇa saṃbandhe sati liṅgena yājyākārye viniyogasiddheḥ | na ca sāmidhenīkārye viniyogaḥ
śaṅkanīyaḥ, aindrāgnatvādāsāṃ ṛcāṃ āgneyatvācca sāmidhenīnām | tasmāt kimarthaṃ liṅgakrama-
samākhyānāditi ? liṅgakramāt samākhyānāditi ca ubhayaṃ sahitamuktaṃ yāvatā ekamevocya-
tāmityāśaṅkyāha---ubhayapramāṇasāhitye ca prayojanamuktaṃ bhāṣye | tadyathā 2yadi
liṅgakramādityetāvaducyeta tadā pāthikṛtīye karmaṇyāgneyasya yugalatya sāmidhenīkārye’pi
viniyogaḥ syāt” | tathāhi tad‌yugalaṃ agnidevatāke pāthikṛtīye karmaṇi yājyākārye3paryāptaṃ
sāmidhenīkārye’pi syāt | āgneyyo hi sāmidhenyaḥ tasmādyājyākārya eva viniyogo
yathā syāditi vaktavyaṃ samākhyānāditi | nanvevaṃ tarhi tadevāstu kiṃ liṅgakramādityanena ?
ucyate | kāmyayājyānūvākyākāṇḍamiti yā samākhyā tayā yājyākārye viniyogo bhavet |
ata punaḥ nirvṛtte yājyākārye liṅgakramāta sāmidhenīkārye viniyogassiddho bhavati | samākhyā tu
kāmyayāgasaṃbandhamātrahetutayā ādaraṇīyetyataḥ etadavasthitaṃ yatra liṅgakramānuguṇā samākhyā tatra

tadvaśena sāmānyasaṃbandhamātrapareti varṇyata iti |

uta dāśatayībhyaḥ evaṃliṅgā āgamayitavyāḥ iti | tatra dāśatayīgrahaṇaṃ na prakṛtatyāgāya

kintu vipratipannatvenopādānāyeti matvā saṃśayaṃ kurvannāha---āgneyyā āgnīdhramupatiṣṭhata
ityevamādiṣu liṅganirdeśeṣu kiṃ yāvalliṅgaṃ grahītavyā uta prakṛtānāṃ grahaṇamiti | tatra

mityāha | kā punaryuktiḥ vidheyasya prakṛtābhivānasāmarthyaṃ na saṃbhavatīti tatrāha---uddeśyapadaṃ
hi prakṛtāpekṣaṃ bhavati na punarvidheyārthasamarpakamapi | kimiti ? uddeśyatvaṃ hi nāma
prayojanavadarthatā | prayojanavāṃścāsau arthaśceti prayojanavadarthaḥ tasya bhāvaḥ tattā prayojana-
vānarthaḥ uddeśyo bhavatītyarthaḥ | na ca sāmānyaṃ prayojanavadbhavati na vrīhyādisāmānyaṃ
prayojanavadbhavati upāttaviṣayatvāt prayojanavattāyāḥ | ya evopātto viśeṣaḥ sa eva
prayojanavattāyā viṣayaḥ | sa eva prayojanavānityarthaḥ | bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi sāmānyaṃ prayojanavadeva

[p. r733]

syāpi viniyoge na kaścidvirodhaḥ | tena kimiti sāmānyavacanaśśabdo viśeṣe’vasthāpyata
iti | ataḥ prakṛtamaprakṛtaṃ ca yāvalliṅgaṃ mantrajātaṃ vidheyam | kathaṃ tarhi bhāṣyakāro
dāśatayībhya āgamayitavyā ityāha nanvevameva vaktavyaṃ yāvalliṅgaṃ vidheyamiti tatrāha
---kiṃ prakṛtābhirliṅgavatībhirupasthātavyaṃ uta dāśatayībhvaḥ evaṃliṅgā āgamayitavyā iti
dāśatayīgrahaṇaṃ na prakṛtapratipakṣatayā | prakṛtatyāgāya na dāśatayīgrahaṇaṃ kintu viprati-
pannatvāt teṣāṃ tadevoktam | etaduktaṃ bhavati bhāṣye kiṃ yāvalliṅgaṃ grahītavyā uta
prakṛtā evaivaṃ liṅgā iti | atra bhāṣyaṃ---upadiṣṭopadeśo hyevaṃjātīyakasya na nyāyya”
iti | tadayuktam | upadiṣṭā ṛcastatra tatropadiśyante viniyogavidhibhede ca kathamanyāyyatā
anenābhiprāyeṇāha---kimidamupadiṣṭopadeśo na nyāyya iti | uttaraṃ---idamidaṃ upadiṣṭa-
taiva hi tasya viśeṣaḥ na cāsau vidheyāṅgamityuktam | asyārthaḥ---yena hi prakṛtānāmeva
viniyoga ityāśritaṃ tenaivaṃ vaktavyaṃ upadeśena viśiṣṭā vidhīyante iti | upadeśena yadi
viśiṣṭā vidhīyante tadā prakṛtatā labhyate | na cāsāvupadeśo vidheyāṅgaṃ, na vidheyamupalakṣayitu-
masāvupadeśaḥ kintu vidhātumevetyuktam | abhidhānāttu yat prakṛtatvaṃ tattulyaṃ tadāha---prakṛtatvaṃ
punarabhidhānāt tulyam | atra bhāṣyamāha---virodhe sati prakaraṇaṃ liṅgena bādhyate

na caitayorvirodha iti tadanupapannaṃ, yato’styevātra virodhaḥ liṅgena hi aprakṛtānāmapi viniyogaḥ

“dāśatayyāṃ punaḥ mantravyaktau”
3 ityādibhāṣyam | tadanupapannamiva pratibhāti yasmāta
pūrvapakṣepi dāśatayyāṃ mantravyaktāvupādīyamānāyāṃ naiva prakṛtamantrapratītirbādhyate | talliṅgavatīnāṃ
sarvāsāmeva grahaṇamiti pūrvapakṣakaraṇāt ityāśaṅkya vyācaṣṭe---dāśatayyāṃ punaḥ mantravyaktāviti
prakṛta eva saṃpanne liṅgārthe niṣpramāṇakaḥ prakṛtabādha iti manyate | asyārthaḥ---liṅgasya
(1) P. 775 (2) P. 776 (3) P. 776

[p. r735]

hyetatsāmarthyaṃ yat liṅgavatī mantravyaktiḥ viniyojyeti | sā ca prakaraṇavaśena prakṛtaiva grāhyeti
pratibhāti | tatra yadiṃ dāśatayyapi mantravyaktiḥ pratigṛhyeta prakṛtaiva mantravyaktirgrāhyeti
pratītirapabādhikā syāt | bādhaśca niṣpramāṇakaḥ | aparamapi bhāṣyam---1“liṅgajanitastu
pratyayaḥ”
 ityādi tadākṣipati---liṅgārthasaṃpattau satyāṃ ko’yaṃ mithyābhāvaḥ yadi liṅgārtha-
styajyeta syālliṅgajanitasya pratyayasya mithyābhāvaḥ prakṛtagrahaṇe tu saṃpanna eva liṅgārthaḥ |
samādhatte---ayamabhiprāyaḥ---nāyaṃ sāmānyopasaṃhārārthaḥ śabda iti svargakāmo yajetetivat na
2saṃpattyānyaprayogārho’pi śāstrārthaḥ paryavasita iti śakyate vaktum | asyārthaḥ---yo’ya-
māgneyīśabdaḥ nāsau sāmānyopasaṃhārārthaḥ sāmānyaṃ liṅgavattvaṃ tat prakṛte upasaṃhniyatāmiti
naivamarthaḥ | kiṃ tarhi yā evaṃliṅgā3mantravyaktiḥ tasyā viniyogaḥ syādityevamarthaḥ |
tataśca yathā svargakāmo yajeteti śāstrārthaḥ puruṣāntaraprayogārho’pi kenaciduddiṣṭamityetāvatā
śāstrārthasaṃpattyā paryavasita iti na śakyate vaktuṃ sarvasvargakāmivyāpyatvāt | tathā ayamapi
sarvaliṅgavanmantravyāpitvāt | ataḥ prakṛtamātragrahaṇe bhavatyeva liṅgajanitasya pratyayasyāśeṣa-
mantraviniyogaviṣayasya mithyābhāva iti | nanu ca “4vyaktirapadārtha”
 iti bhāṣyam---tadanupapannaṃ
yadyapi vyaktirapadārthaḥ tathāpi kathaṃ prakṛtānāmeva grahaṇaṃ ityāśaṅkya vyācaṣṭe---nanu vyaktirapadārtha
iti sāmānyopasaṃhṛtirevopanyasta | prakaraṇavaśena prakṛta eva mantre sāmānyamupasaṃhniyata
ityarthaḥ | ‘etadeva’
 iti bhāṣmam | tasyārthaḥ---na liṅgavattā śabdārtho bhavatīti | tadupapādayati
---etadeva na vijānīma iti pratyayārthapradhānatvāt taddhitāntānāṃ na tadvattā aṅgamiti

[p. r736]

manyate | ‘sāsya devatā’
 ityadhikāre agnerḍhaka iti ḍhak‌pratyayaḥ taddhitaḥ agniśabdāt
agnirasya devatetyarthe vihitaḥ | tena yasyāgniḥ devatā mantrasya sa āgneyaśabdenocyate |
na punarasyānyārthābhidhānasāmarthyayogitā | yaśca asya śabdārthaḥ tasya viniyogo’vagataḥ
mantraścāsyārthaḥ tena mantraḥ aṅgaṃ na tadvattā | tasmāt na prakṛtopasaṃhārārthaṃ na
prakṛtaviniyogārthamidaṃ śāstraṃ---āgneyyā’’gnīdhramupatiṣṭhata iti | 1“nanvetaduktaṃ kāryāntare
prakṛtasyopadeśa’ ityādibhāṣyam | tadanarthakaṃ pratibhāti pūrvamapyasyārthasyoktatvāt | atha
pūrvoktamapi parihartumupanyasyate tanna, parihārādarśanāt | athocyeta “anantaramevocyata”

ityādinā parihāro datta iti | tanna | na hi liṅgavanmātragrahaṇādupadiṣṭopadeśaḥ parihṛto
bhavati | tasmādanarthakamityetadevamāśaṅkyāha---nanvetaduktaṃ kāryāntare prakṛtasyopadeśāt
nāsāvarthāntare upadekṣyata ityupadiṣṭopadeśaparihārāyāyamupanyāsaḥ | parihārābhāvānna
parihārārtha iti manvānaḥ pṛcchati---kimitīti | liṅgavanmātragrahaṇāditi darśitam | asyārthaḥ-
liṅgenopalakṣitānāṃ mantrāṇāṃ grahaṇāddhetoḥ upadiṣṭopadeśaparihāro’yamiti darśitaṃ bhāṣye | kathaṃ
punaḥ parihṛta ityatrāha---nātra upadiṣṭatā hetuḥ | mantragrahaṇe hi yad‌yupadiṣṭatā hetuḥ syāt
tadā upadiṣṭenaiva rūpeṇa viniyogo vaktavyaḥ | sa ca viniyuktaviniyogavirodhānna nyāyyaḥ |
yadā punaḥ upadeśānantarāpekṣeṇa liṅgenopalakṣaṇena viniyogaḥ tadā na kiñcit duṣyatīti,
nanu caivaṃ sati prakṛtabādhaḥ prakaraṇabalena prakṛtā eva grāhyā iti yeyaṃ pratītiḥ tasyā bādhaḥ
uttaraṃ---bhavatvabhidhānāt prakṛtabādho na doṣaḥ śrutibalena prakṛtabādho bhavannapi na doṣaḥ |
(P.) 777

[p. r737]

“2api ca na liṅgaṃ prakaraṇaṃ vā’nugrahītavyaṃ”
 ityādi bhāṣyam | tasyārthaḥ---na prakaraṇānu-
grahāyānuṣṭhānaṃ, kiṃ tarhi prakṛtasya phalavattvāt sarvāśca mantravyaktayo viniyogena phalavatya iti
sarvāsāmeva grahaṇamiti | imamasya bhāṣyasyārthaṃ vyācaṣṭe---api ca na liṅgaṃ prakaraṇaṃ
vā’nugrahītavyamiti nānugrahārthā śāstrārthopasaṃhṛtiriti yaduktaṃ tadidaṃ kvacidapyanu-
grahāt śāstropasaṃhṛtirnāsti svargakāmādivaditi |

tadākhyo vā prakaraṇopapattibhyām | atrocyate | rāddhāntī tu prakṛtepi vidheyaḥ
pratyayo viniyogapratyayaḥ naiva na ghaṭate api tu ghaṭata eva iti manvānaḥ prakṛtasyaiva
grahaṇamityuktavān | asyārthaḥ---yadi viniyuktaviniyogavirodhena prakṛte vidheyapratyayo na
ghaṭate | ato viniyuktaviniyogabādhakaparihārāya liṅgavanmantro viniyujyate | yatastu khalu
prakṛtepi viniyujyamāne vidheyapratyayo ghaṭate ataḥ prakṛtatyāge pramāṇābhāvāt prakṛtasyaiva grahaṇa-
miti | nanu yad‌yupadiṣṭatvenopadeśo bhavati tatra prakṛtamātragrahaṇam | upadiṣṭatvena
copadeśe duṣpariharo viniyuktaviniyogavirodhadoṣaḥ | anenābhiprāyeṇāha---kathamiti |
uttaraṃ---uddeśyatvaṃ hi prayojanavato bhavati | yatra hyuddeśyatā tatropadiṣṭenaiva rūpeṇa
grahaṇam | iha tu vidheyatvānna kāryāntarasaṃbandha upakaroti | tasmāt kāryāntaraviniyoga-
nirapekṣeṇa rūpeṇopādānāt prakṛtānāmapi viniyoge nāsti viniyuktaviniyogavirodhaḥ | tadāha

---1prakṛtatvaṃ punaḥ vidheyepi prakṛtapratyayaṃ naiva bādhate | yathā uddeśye prakṛtapratyayaṃ naiva
bādhate tathā vidheyepi naiva bādhate kāryāntaraviniyuktatvaṃ hi bādhakaṃ na prakṛta[tva]mātraṃ,
avirodhāt | prakṛtapratyaya eva tarhi kuta ityatrāha---na hi sannihite viśeṣe sāmānyaśabdaḥ
tamativartate nīlotpalamitivat gāmānayetibadvā sāmānyenopādhināśabdaḥ sāmānyaśabdaḥ
viśeṣavartī sannihite viśeṣa sannihitaṃ viśeṣaṃ nātivartate sāmānyenopādhinā devatāsaṃbandhena
cāyamabhinirvṛtta āgneyīśabdaḥ sāmānyaśabdaḥ sannihite mantre na tamativartitumarhati |
prakṛtamātragrahaṇe dṛṣṭāntamāha---nīlotpalavaditi | atra nīlaguṇasaṃbandhārhatvāt vyakteḥ
ākṛtivacano’pyutpalaśabdaḥ ākṛtisaṃbandhalakṣaṇasāmānyopādhipravṛtto’pi vyaktiparaḥ sannihitā-
yāmeva vyaktau vartate | dṛṣṭāntāntaramāha---gāmānayetivadveti | pūrvatra guṇayogādvayakti-
vacanatā | iha punaḥ ānayanalakṣaṇakriyāyogāditi dṛṣṭāntadvayopādānam | upasaṃharati
---tasmāt na prakṛtapratyarthī sāmānyaśabdo bhavitumarhatīti | prakṛtā evaivaṃ liṅgavyaktayaḥ
parigṛhyante iti siddham | 2atra sūtraṃ “sarveṣāṃ copadiṣṭatvāt”
 iti | tasyārthaṃ vyācaṣṭe
sūtraṃ paṭhitvā---yaduktaṃ yadasmābhiḥ pūrvamuktaṃ rāddhāntavādī punarityādinā tadidam | tadāha

liṅgakramasamākhyāgyāṃ | idamidānīṃ cintyate yathā prakṛtatayā liṅgamupasaṃhṛtaṃ
kimevaṃ samākhyāpi svanimitta evopasaṃharati uta yathāliṅgaṃ viniyoga iti |
anenāvāntarasaṅgatiṃ bhāṣyakārīyasaṃśayakāraṇaṃ cāha | yathā āgneyyetyatra liṅgaṃ prakaraṇe-
nopasaṃhṛtaṃ prakṛtānāmeva viniyogāt tathā kiṃ bhakṣaṇānuvākasamākhyāpi svanimitte bhakṣaṇa eva
upasaṃharati uta yathāliṅgaṃ viniyoga iti | kiṃ tāvad‌yuktaṃ samākhyāpi prakṛtavanniyama-
yatyeva2 viśeṣe liṅgaṃ ? asyārthaḥ---samākhyayā sāmānyasaṃbandhe sati liṅgasya viniyojakatvāt
samākhyā liṅgaṃ niyamayatīti kṛtsnasyānuvākya bhakṣaṇa eva viniyogaḥ | nanu liṅgāntaravirodhaḥ
prāpnoti | grahaṇāvekṣaṇaliṅgavirodhaḥ syādityarthaḥ | pariharati---na prāpnoti virodhaḥ |
kutaḥ ? viśeṣaṇatayāpi liṅgāntarasyavirodhāt | yadi bhakṣaṇaviśeṣaṇatayāpi grahaṇābhidhānaṃ
tathāpi na virodha iti | yasmāt svārthe yadi svaniṣṭhatayā parārthe yadi ca
paraviśeṣaṇatayā tathāpyabhidhānaṃ grahaṇādīnāmaviśiṣṭam |

nanu pārārthye grahaṇādīnāṃ lakṣaṇā prāpnoti | pariharati---yadyapi lakṣaṇā tathāpya-
bhidhānamaviśiṣṭaṃ grahaṇaprakāśakena hi grahaṇameva prakāśyate nānyat tatra paraviśeṣaṇārthaṃ na
svaniṣṭhaṃ itīdaṃparatāviśeṣoyaṃ nābhidhānaviśeṣaḥ | yathā gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityabhidhāna-

guṇābhidhānāt | pūrvādhikaraṇāpavādo’yamityanenāvāntarasaṅgatiṃ pūrvapakṣañcāha
---pūrvādhikaraṇanyāyenaivātra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | liṅgabalena tṛptiprakāśanepi mantrasya viniyogaṃ manyate |
yathāpavādaḥ tathā’’ha---aprayogābhidhāno mantraḥ ananuṣṭhānārthakatṛptyabhidhāno mantraḥ prayogā-
bhidhānena bhakṣābhidhānena sahaikavākyatāṃ nīyate samākhyayā | sā hi kṛtsnamevānu-
vākaṃ bhakṣaṇasaṃbandhamāpādayantyapi liṅgena bādhitā | yatra punaḥ liṅgaṃ bādhitumevaṃ na śaknoti
ananuṣṭheyasya smaraṇānarhatvāt tatra tātparyābhāvāt tasya samākhyāyā bhakṣaṇe ciniyoga iti | nanu
ca liṅgāt tadabhidhāna eva mantro’stu tadabhidhānādeva cādṛṣṭaṃ kalpyatāṃ ityatrāha---liṅgaṃ hi
dṛṣṭārthatayā samākhyayopasaṃhṛtam | yatra punaḥ dṛṣṭārthatā nāsti tatrādṛṣṭakalpanāyāṃ pramāṇaṃ
nāstīti samākhyaiva viniyoktī | adṛṣṭakalpanāto hi lakṣaṇā śreyasī | lakṣaṇayā
tṛptyabhidhāno hi mantraḥ bhakṣaṇa eva viniyujyate | samākhyayā ca svanimitta eva viniyukta
iti siddhaṃ bhakṣaṇenaikavākyatvādekamantratvam |

liṅgaviśeṣanirdeśāt | atredṛśī sthitiḥ indrapītasyeti kimayaṃ tṛtīyāsamāsaḥ uta
bahuvrīhiḥ | indreṇa pītamiti kiṃ soma ucyate, indreṇa pītaṃ yasmin savana iti savana-
mevocyata iti mūlavicāraḥ | atra ca tṛtīyāsamāsasya uttarapadārthapradhānatvāt bahuvrīheḥ

 iti pratīyate grahaṇamānayoḥ aindrasya mantrasya samānatvam | yatra

tatsaṃskārāṃścāpekṣanta iti vikārabhāvaḥ | upapadyate cāsau abhyāsāpūrvāṇāṃ bhedābhyupagamāditi
atideśena prāptimabhyupagamya cintā kṛtā kasyohena upalakṣaṇaṃ kasya neti | paramārthatastu

yāgasyābhyasyamānatvāt | ataḥ sarvatropadeśena dravyalābhāt dravyāśritatvācca saṃskārāṇāṃ
saṃskāranirākāṅkṣatvānnāsti prakṛtivikārabhāvaḥ | namanaviśeṣavidhānaṃ ta viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ

gṛhyate mīyate ca iti prakṛtivikāraśaṅkā | endrapradānaṃ prakṛtibhūtaṃ śeṣāṇi vikṛti-
bhūtānītyāśaṅkā | tenātideśike kṛtvācitā kā devatā ūhenopalakṣyatāmityādikā |

punarabhyunnīteṣu | atra āśrāvaṇādapanayaḥ prasthitadevatāyā iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
saśeṣe camase punassome devatāntarārtha gṛhīte yadāśravaṇaṃ kṛtaṃ tena pūrvadevatāśeṣasyāpi
devatāntarārthamupādānāt pūrvadevatāpanayanāt na sā devatā upalakṣaṇīyeti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
agrahaṇānneti rāddhāntaḥ | grahaṇena hi devatāsaṃbandho dravyasyāpādyate | taduddeśenā-
grahaṇāt taccheṣaḥ camasastho devatāntaroddeśena na gṛhyate | yacca gṛhītaṃ tāvanmātrasyaivāśrāvaṇa-
miti pūrvadevatā upalakṣaṇīyeti |

sajūśśabdasya sahārthatvāt pāne sahabhāvena tvaṣṭuranupraveśāt devatātaddhitavirodhānāpādakatvāt
tvaṣṭāpi devatā | ataḥ sopyupalakṣaṇīya iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | nācoditatvāditi rāddhāntaḥ

1triṃśacca | trayastriṃśati tu trayastriṃśatamityatra agneḥ 2mādayitṛtvadarśanāt
punarāśaṅkā | agniḥ mādayitṛmantre kīrtyate na pātā tena trayastriṃśaddevatāḥ pradhānatayā kīrtyanta
iti devatābhāvāśaṅkā | 3mādayitṛtaiva tatprādhānyāt devatābhāvamāpādayatīti rāddhāntaḥ |
yadetat agneḥ mādayitṛtvaṃ tadevānyathā nopapadyate yadyagniḥ pradhāno na bhavati | īśāno hi
vilambayitukāmaḥ 4hīnamāhvayati | tena 5mādayitṛtveneśānatvāt agneḥ devatātvamavagamyate |
nahyanyena prakāreṇa tasyeśānatā’sti ityagnirevānena mantreṇa devatātvena kīrtyata iti na trayastriṃ-
śato devatābhāvaḥ | ato nopalakṣaṇīyeti rāddhāntaḥ |

6vaṣaṭakāraśca | anuvaṣaṭakāradevatāpi taduddeśenāgrahaṇānnopalakṣyā | devatā-
tvādanuvaṣaṭkāradevatāpyupalakṣaṇīyeti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | satyaṃ devatā, tathāpi yaduddeśena somo gṛhītaḥ
sā devatā upalakṣaṇīyetyetat | sthānapatitā’nuvaṣaṭkāradevatā nopalakṣyate |

7chandaḥ pratiṣedhastu | sthitāduttaram-ekayāgatvāttu samānavidhānaṃ syāt | tasmādaindra
eva samantrakaḥ somaḥ itītarat pradānamamantrakaṃ anāmnānāt | prakṛtivikārahetubhūtaṃ
(1) P. 794 (2) āhvātṛtva (3) āhvātṛtaiva (4) arthinamā (5) āhvā-
tṛtvenā (6) P. 797 (7) P. 798

[p. r745]

yaduktaṃ pūrvapakṣiṇā tadaparihṛtyādhikaraṇāntaramārabdham---aindrāgne punariti | atrāyamabhiprāyaḥ
---upadeśaḥ aindravat aindrāgnepi nāstītyupadeśopapādanāt idameva vaktavyam | punaścopadeśābhāve
sthite atideśo’pi nāstītyevamarthaṃ prakṛtivikārabhāvāpādikā yuktiḥ nirākaraṇīyeti |
aindrāgne punaḥ pānamevopalakṣaṇaṃ na devateti “āgneyaṃ caturdhā karoti”
 iti sthite’pi
punaḥ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | āgneye devatātaddhitaḥ ataḥ kevalāgnidevatāko gṛhyate | iha tu na
devatātvenopalakṣaṇaṃ kintu pānena | yattu ubhābhyāṃ pīyate so’pi indrapīta iti śakyate
vadituṃ iti | devatopalakṣaṇam pānābhāvāditi rāddhāntaḥ | pāne pramāṇābhāvāt vigraha-
vattādinirākaraṇāt devatātvenaivopalakṣaṇam | na ca dvidevatā, indradevatyā ityāgneyatulyatā |
ekasaṃbandhe’pi tulyaśruterasabhavenānekasaṃbandhyapi gṛhyata iti pratyudāharaṇamātrametat |

chandaśca devatāvat | devatayā tulyaṃ chanda iti manvānasya na pṛthagvyākhyānam |
anuṣṭup‌chandasa iti chandontaravidhānāt namatītyasya parihāraḥ viśiṣṭavidhānamidamityarthaḥ |
aindraḥ somo gṛhyate mīyate ca iti samānavidhāne sthite yathāliṅgaṃ mantraḥ | tatra samantrakaṃ
grahaṇaṃ mānaṃ ca | anyatra mantraśūnyam | tasmādidamapyakāraṇaṃ prakṛtivikārabhāvasya |
(1) Bhashya 801

[p. r746]

ataḥ sthitaṃ samānavidhānam | sthite samānavidhāne idānīmindrapītasyeti savanasāmānādhi-
karaṇyāt sarve mantravanta iti rāddhāntaḥ | asyārthaḥ---indrapītaśabdo’yaṃ bahuvrīhiḥ indreṇa
pītaṃ yasmin savana iti | nanu sarvīpasarjano bahuvrīhiḥ tṛtīyāsamāsād‌durbalaḥ | ucyate |
tṛtīyāsamāsa evātra na saṃbhavati | yo’pīndrāya dattaḥ nāsau bhakṣyate | yaśca bhakṣyate na sa
indrapīta ityasaṃbhavāt | bahuvrīhyāśrayaṇena savanasāmānādhikaraṇyāt sarve somāḥ
samantrakā iti |

śruterjātādhikāraḥ syāt |

atra māṣyakāreṇa sandehaḥ kṛtaḥ---kimṛg‌jātimadhikṛtya ete śabdāḥ ityādinā |
tadvayācaṣṭe---uccairṛcā kriyata iti kimayaṃ vedadharmaḥ uca ṛgdharma iti saṃśayaḥ | ākṣipatti
---ko’yaṃ vedo nāma ? nanu cargapi veda eva ityekagranthaḥ | asyārthaḥ---ko’yaṃ vedo nāma
na ca vidhivākyameva kevalaṃ vedaśabdenocyate kintu ṛgapi | tataśca vedadharmatvepi ṛgdha-
rmatā na nirākriyate | tenāpratipakṣatvāt saṃśaya evānupapannaḥ | samādhatte---ucyate---vidhāyakaṃ
vākyaṃ vedaśabdenocyate | saṃśayavākye vedaśabdo’yaṃ vidhāyakavākyaparatvena prayuktaḥ
tenātra vidhāyakaṃ vākyaṃ vedaśabdenocyate | nanvevamapi saṃśayo’nupapanna eva | kiṃ
vidhāyakadharmaḥ utargdharma iti | ṛcāmapi kāsāṃcita vidhāyakatā’styeva yathā kapiñjalānā--
labhata iti | kiñca vidhāyakasya vākyasyānaṅgatvāt tatra dharmā vidhīyamānā vidhyasaṃsparśinaḥ
anarthakā evāpadyanta itīmāmāśaṅkāmapaninīṣurāha---etaduktaṃ bhavati---yadṛgvedaśabdavācyaṃ
vidhāyakaṃ vākyaṃ tadvidheyadharmaḥ uta yatra pādavyavasthā tasmin mantra iti | asyārthaḥ
---uccairṛcā kriyata ityasya vacanasya kimayamarthaḥ yadṛcā yadṛgvedena ṛk‌prāyaśākhādhītena vidhinā
kriyate yattenānuṣṭhetvena jñāpyate taduccaiḥ kartavyam | yat ṛgvedādhītena vidhinā’nuṣṭheyatvena
(1) B. P. 803

[p. r748]

jñāpyate’dhikāravidhiḥ tasyoccaiṣṭvaṃ dharmaḥ sa ca sākṣādanena saṃbaddhaumaśaktaḥ tadaṅge śabde niviśate |
atha yadṛcā mantreṇa kriyate taduccairiti kāryāntarasmaraṇajanakatayā vyāpṛtāyā ṛcaḥ uccaiṣṭvaṃ
vidhīyata iti | kiṃ tāvadyuktaṃ ? uccairṛceti vacanāt ṛgdharmo’yaṃ iti | asyārthaḥ
---uccaiḥ ṛcā kriyata iti vacanāt ṛk‌śabdasya mukhyayā vṛttyā mantravacanatvānmantradharmo’yamiti |
nanu vedopakramametadvākyaṃ tenopakramānuguṇatayā ṛgādiśabdenāpi vedaścocyata iti |
pariharati---ko doṣaḥ ṛkṣvapyayaṃ vedaśabdo vartata iti | asyārthaḥ---vedyate aneneti
vyutpattyā vartamāno vedaśabdaḥ prayogavaśena apauruṣeyaśabdaviṣayatayā paṅkajādiśabdavanniyamyamānaḥ
ṛkṣu vidhiṣu ca mukhyaḥ tatrāvirodhādvedaśabdo’pi mantrapara eva | tenāyaṃ mantradharmaḥ |
evaṃ ca mantradharmatve sati na vikalpo vedāntarapaṭhitasya ṛg‌jātasya | vidheyadharmapakṣe tu
anekadharmavidheyatayā ekatrāpi mantre anekadharmaprasaṅgāt vikalpa āpadyate |

vedo vā prāyadarśanāt | atrocyate---vedadharmo’yam | adhikāravidhidharmaḥ iti
yāvat | kutaḥ ? vedaśabdopakramatvādvākyasya | tenopakramavaśenopasaṃhāragataṛkśabdo
vidhilakṣaṇārthaḥ iti | nanu copakramopasaṃhāravaśāt ṛkṣu vartata ityuktam | satyamuktam |
tattu nopapadyate | kathaṃ nopapadyate ? evaṃ nopapadyate | upakrame yaḥ padārthaḥ parijñāto
(1) P. 805

[p. r749]

bhavati tadviśeṣapratipattireva kevalaṃ upasaṃhārādapekṣyate | na punaḥ tadvirodhyarthāntaram |
yaḥ khalu padārthaḥ prathamaśrutaḥ tamaparityajyaiva tadviśeṣa eva kevalamupasaṃhāreṇa apekṣyate na
punastadupamardyāpyarthāntaram | ato’virodhidharmavidhānamevoccaiṣṭvādividhānameva kevalaṃ
vedenopakramapratītena saṃbadhyate | ṛk‌śabdastvānuguṇyādanuvādaḥ | upakramagatavedaśabdānu-
guṇyāt vedānuvāda eva ṛk‌śabdo vedamanuvadati lakṣaṇayā | kathaṃ punaḥ nargvedaśabdenocyate |
asyārthaḥ---upakramavaśenopasaṃhāragatark‌śabdo lakṣaṇayā varṇyata iti vadataḥ tava siddhāntavādinaḥ
ayamāśayo lakṣyate-vedaśabdo’yaṃ na ṛkṣu vartata iti | taccedamanupapannam kathaṃ vedaśabdo narkṣu
vartate yāvatā vartetaiva | tataścāvirodhāta ṛk‌para eva vedaśabdo bhaviṣyati | uttaraṃ-kena vokata
nocyata iti | kintu vidheyatayā na vidhāyakavat | asyārthaḥ---vedaśabdo’yaṃ yaugiko
vedayatīti tatra vidhāyakavākyasya nirapekṣaṃ vedatvaṃ vidheyasya tu mantrajātasya viniyogāpekṣamartha-
prakāśanakaraṇatvamiti sāpekṣanirapekṣatayā na tulyā vedaśabdasya pravṛttiḥ vidhividheyamantravidhyoḥ |
ataḥ upakramagatena vedaśabdena nirapekṣatayā vidhirevocyate | tataścopakramagatavidhivāciveda-
śabdānuguṇyāt ṛk‌śabdo’pi vidhipara eveti adhikāravidhidharmo’yam | dṛṣṭaścāyaṃ ṛk‌śabdo
vede ṛgbhiḥ prātardivi deva riyate iti | tena lakṣaṇāpyeṣā nātīvāprasiddheti darśayati |
kuta etat ? vede ṛgādiśabda iti yajurvede tiṣṭhati madhye ahnaḥ iti sahayogāt
(1) G. bhedena

[p. r750]

vedapara evāyaṃ ṛk‌śabda ityarthaḥ | dharmopadeśaśca sāmnāṃ pṛthak‌tvena yassāmnaḥ uccaiṣṭ‌vo-
padeśaḥ sa evamevopapadyate anyathā mantradharmapakṣe hi ṛg‌vidhānāt ṛk‌śabdenoccaiṣṭ‌va-
vidhānasiddheḥ pṛthagvidhānamanarthakaṃ syāt | na ca prajāpaterhṛdayādiṣu anṛgāśriteṣu sāmasu
vidhānamarthavat jyotiṣṭome teṣāmabhāvāt | mantradharmapakṣe hi jyotiṣṭoma eva niveśaḥ | na
ca tatra prajāpaterhṛdayādīnāṃ saṃbhava iti |

trayīvidyākhyā ca tadvidi1 | trayīvidyaśabdaśca tadvidi vedatrayavidi vartate |
kiṃ tataḥ ? idaṃ tato bhavati vede ṛgādiśabdānāṃ prayogo dṛṣṭa iti yo vedatrayavit saḥ
trayīvidya iti ucyate ṛgādiśabdaśca trayyāṃ prayuktaḥ ṛgyajussāmāni trīṇi trayīti | tasmā-
dṛgādiśabdo vede dṛṣṭa iti | yatpunarvedāntaroktasya vihitasya svarāntarāpattiriti
vidhānānna doṣaḥ pramāṇabalādāyāto vikalpaḥ na doṣāya bhavatītyarthaḥ | prakaraṇabādhaśca
vākyādiṣyata eva | tena prakaraṇālocanāsamutthāṃ jyotiṣṭomāṅgatāṃ vākyena bādhitvā sakala-
kratvaṅgateti | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ vedasaṃyogepi prakaraṇaṃ nānugṛhyate ṛgdharmavidhānavat | yathā
ṛgvarmavidhau prakaraṇānugrahaḥ tathā’trāpi kiṃ na syāditi | ucyate---ṛcāmaṅgatvāt taddharmaḥ
prakaraṇena gṛhyata iti yuktam | aṅgāśrito hi dharmo’dhikāravidhinā gṛhyata iti yuktam |
(1) Bhashya P. 808

[p. r751]

vedadharmatve punarvidhānamupalakṣaṇam | na ca tadaṅgamiti yāvadvidhānaṃ dharmavidhiḥ |
vedaprameyādhikāravidhidharmapakṣe’dhikāravidhirupalakṣaṇaṃ, tatra dharkasaṃbandhāsaṃbhavāt | tadaṅgeṣu
saṃbandheṣu dharmaḥ | na cādhikāravidhiraṅgamiti yāvadadhikāravidhiṃ dharmasaṃbandhaḥ |

guṇamukhyavyatikrame | ‘ya evaṃ vidvānagnimādhante’
 iti yājurvaidiko vidhiḥ ‘ya
evaṃ vidvān vāravantīyaṃ gāyati’
 ityādhānaprakaraṇe sāmavaidika iti kṛtvācintyate |
yājurvaidike pradhāne sāmavaidikamaṅgamiti kṛtvācinteyam | kimatra cintyate ? yājurvaidike
bhavatūpāṃśutvaṃ sāmavaidikecoccaiṣṭvaṃ bhinnādhikaraṇatve copāṃśutvoccaiṣṭbayoravirodha ipi |
atra samādhiḥ---satyamevaṃ yadyaṅgapradhānavidhī bhidyete tadā vidhibhedāt tannibandhano dharmavidhiḥ
syāt yathātvabhedaḥ tathā phalavatsannidhāvaphalaṃ tadaṅgamityatra vakṣyati | abhedecobhayaveda-
vidhānādbhabati saṃśayaḥ | ākṣeptā’’ha---tathāpi na saṃśayaḥ ubhayavedavidhānāt
vikalpo bhavatu | pariharati---na vikalpaḥ saṃbhavati | evaṃ sati aṅgavidhiranuvādaḥ
(1) B. P. 809

[p. r752]

ityaṅgavidheḥ pradhānavidhitantratāṃ darśayati | asyārthaḥ---syādvikalpaḥ yadyumayadharmaprāptiḥ
syāt | vidhibhede cobhayadharmaprāptiḥ | na pradhānavidhito’ṅgavidhīnāṃ bhedo’sti |
tena pradhānaikavidhidharmeṇaiva bhavitavyamiti na vikalpaḥ | ākṣeptā’ha---tathāpi kassaṃśayaḥ
niścita eva pradhānavidherdharmaḥ | samarthayitā saṃśayamāha---kimasāvaṅgavidhirākṣiptasvavidheya
eva pradhānatantro bhavati uta pradhānenakībhūto vidheyaṃ vidhatta iti | yadyātmīyaṃ vidheyaṃ
vidhāya paścādekatāṃ yāti tadā prathamāvasthāyāṃ 1vihitatvādvāravantīyasya sāmavaidikenaiva
svareṇa bhavitavyam | atha pradhānenaikībhūtasyaiva vidheyatvaṃ tadā yājurvaidikenopāṃśutvenaṃ
bhavitavyamiti saṃśayaḥ | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī manyate svārthaṃ vidhāya prayojanavaśāt pradhāne-
naikībhavati vāravantīyaṃ gāyatītyasya svārthaḥ | atassāmavaidikasvarayuktasyaivāṅgabhāvaḥ |
asyārthaḥ---svavākyenaiva vākyāntaranirapekṣeṇaiva svārtho jñāpyate | tasya tu niradhikārāyā
prayuktyabhāvenāprayojakatvāt vāvayasyārthāntarānupraveśaḥ | ataḥ pradhānavidhyananupraviṣṭenaivāṅga-
vidhinā aṅgasya vihitatvāt tannibandhanasvarayuktasyaiva pradhānavidhāvaṅgateti vāravantīyasya sāma-
vaidikena svareṇa bhavitavyam | atrābhidhīyate | satyaṃ, vihitasvārtha eva nirjñāpita-
svārtha eva pradhānena vivinā ekībhavati | na punuraṅgatāmapi vidhāya | tenāṅgatvasya
aṅgavidhinā anavabodhitatvāta | tena tanna kriyate na svārthajñānamātreṇāṅgānuṣṭhānam | kiṃ
tarhi aṅgatvajñānena | ataḥ tannibandhano na bhavati svaraḥ| yadyaṅgavidhiraṅgatāṃ na jñāpayati
kastarhi jñāpayatītyatrāha---pradhānavidhiraṅgatāṃ vidhatta iti saptamādye vakṣyate | sa evādhi-
(1) G. abhihitatvāt

[p. r753]

kāravidhiḥ sannihitamarthaṃ svārthatayā jñāpayati | na punaravāntaravidhayaḥ svārthānāmaṅgabhāvaṃ
jñāpayantīti grāhakavyutpattyā saptamādye vakṣyati | aṅgatayā ca vāravantīyasya prayogaḥ na
vāravantīyaṃ gāyatītyasya vidherviṣayatathā | na svarūpajñānādanuṣṭhānaṃ karmaṇām vinopayoga-
jñānāt, duḥkhātmakatvāt karmaṇāmiti | upasaṃharati---ato na svārthavidhiḥ prayogavidhiṃ
bādhitumarhati svārthasya prayogārthatvāt | aṅgavidhiḥ prayogavidhinibandhanaṃ na dharmaṃ
bādhitumarhati tadarthatvāt prayogavidhyarthatvādaṅgavidheḥ | anena caitaduktaṃ bhavati | yadyapyaṅgavidhiḥ
svārtha pravṛttaḥ tathāpi pradhānavidhinibandhanenaiva dharmeṇa bhavitavyaṃ tasya pradhānaguṇatvāditi |
tadidaṃ dvayaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇoktam ---guṇasyānuṣṭhānena mukhyaḥ saguṇaḥ kathaṃ syāditi guṇa-
syānuṣṭhāne pravartata ityanena granthena 3na guṇavidheḥ svārthe pravṛttirityuktaṃ ‘guṇapravṛttyā cet’

ityādinā bhāṣyeṇa | prayogāṅgatvācca guṇāvidheḥ pradhānaguṇena bhavitavyamityuktam |
prayogaḥ pradhānavidhiḥ |

nibandhanaṃ guṇaṃ bādhata iti | anenāvāntarasaṃgatiruktā | adhyāyasaṅgatiśca pradhānasvara-

rbhavati | kutaḥ prameyāddhi pramāṇāvagatiriti sthitam | prayogo hi prameyaḥ, prayogavidhi:
