asaṃyuktaṃ prakaraṇāt | “kimetāvantyeva pramāṇāti”
 iti bhāṣyam | tadā-
kṣipati---kimetāvantyeva pramāṇānīti ko’yaṃ praśnaḥ | yadi tāvat sūtrārtha eva na
śakyate vyākhyātuṃ praśnena vināpi ityabhiprāyaḥ tadayuktam | kimatra praśnena yāvatā
yadasaṃyuktaṃ tat prakaraṇena viniyujyata iti | śakyata eva sūtrārtho vyākhyātum |
(1) Bhasya P. 816 (2) G. omits na (3) R. pradhānatvagamaka

[p. r756]

nanu ca kenāsaṃyuktamiti na vidmaḥ ? evaṃ tarhi etadeva praṣṭavyam kenāsaṃyuktamiti | kaveda-
mupayucyate kimetāvantyeva pramāṇānīti | athāyamabhiprāyaḥ, kimiti śrutyā liṅgena
vākyena vā yadasaṃyuktaṃ tatprakaraṇena viniyujyata iti | kimetāvantyeva catvāryeva
pramāṇāni | yadyanyadapi syāt tadā tenāpi viniyogo bhavatyeveti | yadyapyevaṃ praśno ghaṭate 
tadāpi uttaraṃ tāvadasaṃbaddham anyadapyastīti | kathamasaṃbaddhaṃ ? yadyanyadapyasti
kimiti śrutyā liṅgena vākyena cāsaṃyuktaṃ prakaraṇena viniyujyata ityucyate, catuṣṭa-
yātirekeṇāpi viniyogo vaktavya eveti | etacca praśnasyopapattimabhyupagamyocyate | paramārthatastu
praśno’pi nopapadyate eveti śrutiliṅgavākyaiḥ asaṃyuktaṃ prakaraṇena viniyujyata iti tenānyapramāṇā-
nirākaraṇāt praśno’pi nopapadyata iti | etacca “tathāpyuttaraṃ tāvat”
 ityatra tathāpāti
śabdaprayogādavagamyate | athājñānameva naiva śrutiliṅgavākyebhyaḥ pramāṇamanyadvijñāyate
iti tena pramāṇāntaramajānata eṣa praśnaḥ kimetāvannīti tathāpyevamapi vyākhyāyamāne svarūpaṃ
vaktavyaṃ nāsaṃyuktaṃ prakaraṇāditi yujyate kartavyasyetikartavyatākāṅgasyeti vakṣyamāṇava-
ddhaktavyam | yathā kartavyasyeti prakaraṇasvarūpaṃ vakṣyati tathā vaktavyaṃ na punarasaṃyuktaṃ

[p. r757]

prakaraṇāditi vaktuṃ yujyate | ataḥ praśnārtho’naupayika iva dṛśyate | atrābhidhīyate
-ayamabhiprāyo bhāṣyakārasya | lakṣaṇādāvuktaṃ śeṣalakṣaṇamucyate śrutyādīni ca viniyoge
kāraṇānīti ca | tatra tāvat śrutiliṅgavākyaiḥ viniyojyaṃ śeṣatvamuktam | prakaraṇa-
viniyojyaṃ punaḥ kimatra bhaviṣyati | nanu ca prayājādayaḥ santyevaṃ prakaraṇaviniyojyāḥ
kimiti praśnaḥ ? bāḍhaṃ praśnārthaḥ praśnasya prayojanaṃ vidyate prayājādīnāṃ hi ekaniyogalakṣaṇa-
saṃbandhaṃ caturthe bakṣyati darśapūrṇamāsādibhiḥ kimatrānyat pramāṇāntaraṃ mṛgyate | asyārthaḥ
---ekenaivātrādhikāraniyogena sarveṣāṃ prayājāgneyādīnāṃ saṃbandhaṃ caturthe vakṣyati tataśca ‘samidho
yajati’
 ityevamādibhiḥ karaṇopakārakatayā’nvitaiḥ paramāpūrvamanvitamucyata iti śrauta evāyaṃ
viniyogaḥ kimatrānyena pramāṇena | evaṃ ca tad‌bhūtādhikaraṇokta eva saṃbandho’nvitā-
bhidhāyibhiḥ padaiḥ sarvatra śabdārthāvagamaheturiti vyāpakaṃ bhavati | tad‏bhūtādhikaraṇovata
eva saṃbandho’nvitābhidhānadvārakāpūrvānvayapratipādakapadalabhyaḥ sarvatra śabdārthāvagamaheturiti
vyāpakaṃ bhavati | evaṃ ca sati pramāṇāntare punaranviṣyamāṇe nānvitābhidhānanibandhanaḥ
(1) Bhashya 652

[p. r758]

saṃbandhaḥ śakyate darśayitum | kutaḥ ityatrāha---prakaraṇādibhirvākyārthānāṃ saṃbandhā-
vagatiḥ prayājādiniyogānāṃ vākyārthānāṃ, vākyārthabhūtasyādhikāraniyogasya ca saṃbandhaḥ
prakaraṇādibhiravagamyate | na ca vākyārthasya ca vākyārthasya saṃbandhe padābhidhānalakṣaṇaḥ
saṃbandhaḥ | kasmādityāha---anvitaṃ padaṃ padārthāntareṇa padārthamācaṣṭe | tena padārthānā-
mevānvitābhidhānalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ na punarvākyārthaṃ vākyārthāntareṇānvitamabhidhatte vākyam |
vākyasyākhaṇḍasyāvācakatvāt | bhāgaśo hi vākyādvākyārthāvagatiriti tatra tatroktam |
upasaṃharati---ato darśapūrṇamāsārthatayā prayājādivākyārthānvayamaśābdaṃ manvānasya praśnaḥ |
vākyārthānvayamaśābdaṃ manyate | padārthānvayastu bhavati śābdaḥ tasmiṃstu pakṣe na śrutyādyatirikta---
pramāṇasiddhiriti manvānasyāyaṃ praśnaḥ | nanu caitāvantyeva pramāṇānīti aśābdaṃ vākyārtha
saṃbandhaṃ manvānasya praśnena bhavitavyam | na punaḥ kimetāvantyeva pramāṇānīti | ucyate
---etadevoktaṃ yadyasaṃyuktaṃ śrutyādibhiḥ na tarhi viniyojyamapi aśābdatvādvākyārthayoḥ
saṃbandhasyeti pramāṇāntarākṣepa evāyaṃ yatastvayā siddhāntavādinā 1asaṃyuktamiti vadatā
etāvantyeva pramāṇāni ityuktam | ataḥ upapanno’yaṃ praśnaḥ asaṃyuktagrahaṇe sati
(1) G. saṃyukta

[p. r759]

kimetāvantyeva pramāṇānīti | asyārthaḥ---siddhāntavādinaivāsaṃyuktagrahaṇe tu kṛte pūrvapakṣa-
vādyāha --- kiṃ tavāpyetāvantayeva pramāṇānīti sammataṃ yena śrutyādyasaṃyuktaṃ2 asaṃyuktamiti brūṣe |
anyathā śrutyādyasaṃyuktamapi kimityasaṃyuktaṃ syāta ityevaṃ praśnaḥ | itarastu rāddhāntavādī
nāvaśyaṃ padārthānvitamevedaṃ śābdam | aidamarthyenāpi śabdaḥ saṃbandhaṃ pratipādayatyeveti
manvāno netyuktavān | asyārthaḥ---na mayā asaṃyuktaśabdaṃ prayuñjānena sarvathā’saṃprayuktatva-
muktam | kintu prakaraṇasya pṛthak pramāṇatvadarśanāya śrutyādyasaṃyuktaṃ prakaraṇena viniyujyata
ityuktam | yastu tavābhiprāyaḥ vākyārthānāṃ parasparasaṃbandho’śābda iti so’pyayuktaḥ nāvaśyaṃ
padārthānvitameva śābdaṃ śabdaprameyam | kintu aidamarthyānyathānupapattyā gamyamānamapi śābdaṃ
bhavatyeva | kasmādityāha ---aidamarthyenāpi śabdaḥ saṃbandhaṃ pratipādayatyeveti | asyārthaḥ
--- na prathamameva padārthairitikartavyatābhāvenānvitaiḥ anvitaṃ paramāpūrvamabhidhīyate, kintu aidamarthya
mātreṇānvitaiḥ | pradhānavidhireva svārthamavāntaravākyārthairanvitaṃ abhidhatte | avāntaravidhayastu
svārthān svarūpamātreṇaiva sannidhāpayanti | ata eva ca saptamādye pradhānavidhirevāṅgatāṃ vidhatta
ityuktam | tena svavākyenopāttakaraṇāvarudve paramāpūrve prakārāntareṇānvayānupapatteḥ karaṇopakāra-
katayaivānvayāt na pramāṇāntataramastīti pūrvapakṣiṇokte sāmānyenaidamarthyānvayāsaṃbhavāt karaṇoḥ-
pakārakatayā viśeṣānvayāsaṃbhavāt śeṣānvayābhidhānena arthabhedo vidyata iti karaṇānvayasidhyarthaṃ
prakaraṇaṃ nāma pramāṇāntaraṃ astīti manvāto rāddhāntī netyuktavāt | itarastu rāddhāntino
bhāvamanababudhyamānaḥ pṛcchati---kimaparaṃ kāraṇamiti “praśnenaivopakrama”
 iti bhāṣyam | 
tasyārthaḥ praśnenaivopakramaḥ pūrvapakṣa iti tadvayācaṣṭe---pūrvoktasya praśnārthasya vivaraṇametat |
(1) Bhashya P. 817 (2) asaṃyuktamātraṃ

[p. r760]

kā nāmeyo nadīti | kasyacit jñātanadyarthasyaivākṣepābhiprāyaḥ praśna upapadyata iti | sa cāyaṃ
praśnārthaḥ ‘‘kathaṃ punarasaṃyuktasyaiva viniyoga”
 ityena manthena prakaṭīkṛtaḥ | tadidamaida-
marthyalakṣaṇaṃ kathayati | iti kartavyatārthitvāt iti | yadītikartavyatārthitvādityanena
śeṣatvamuktaṃ tadā aidamarthyalakṣaṇaṃ aidamairthyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pramāṇaṃ yasya tata tathoktam | nanu coktaṃ
ekaniyogalakṣaṇasaṃbandho’yaṃ, kimidamitikartavyatārthitvaṃ nāma | ekaniyogatvenaiva
saṃbandhasiddheritikartavyatārthitvaṃ nāma na pramāṇāntaramiti | ucyate---ekaniyogalakṣaṇa-
saṃbandho nopapadyate yadītikartavyatālakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandho na syāt iti | asyārthaḥ---ekaṃ 
sādhikāraniyogaṃ prati aidamarthye’vagate’pi yāvaditikartavyatālakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandho nāvagamyate tāva-
nnirviśeṣaviṣayasyaidamarthyasyānyathānupapatteḥ karaṇāvaruddhatvācca ekaniyogalakṣaṇa eva saṃbandho-
nopapadyate | kathaṃ nopapadyate itayatrāha---na hyasaṃbandhavidheyasya ekavidhānatā upapadyate |
asaṃbandho vidheyo yasya vidheḥ, yadvidhirvidheyena saha sa vidhiḥ tena sahaikavidhānatāmeva na labhate
vidheyanānātvāt | svabhāvato nānābhūtathorvidhyoḥ vidheyona nānābhūtena nānābhūtavidheyayośca
yeyamekavidhānatā sā vidheyayoḥ parasparasaṃbandhaṃ vinā nopapadyate | tenāvāntaravidhyarthānāṃ
svavidhividheyānāṃ pradhānavidheritikartavyatārthitvalakṣaṇena prakaraṇena pradhānavidhividheyena karaṇena
saha karaṇetikartavyatālakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ pradhānaniyogenaidamarthyalakṣaṇassaṃbandho’nvitābhidhānanibandhana

[p. r761]

eva | tadākṣiptastu yaḥ karaṇetikartavyatālakṣaṇassaṃbandhaḥ so’pi śābda eva | atra ceyaṃ prakriyā
avāntaravidhyarthā vidheyāḥ pradhānavidhiṃ pratyupādīyamānatvāt yaddhi yatsiddhayarthamupādīyate
tat tadvidheyamiti tantre vyavahāraḥ pradhānavidhistu na vidheyaḥ | nanu pradhānavidhyanupraveśaḥ
kathamitikartavyatāyāḥ yāvatā2 karaṇamātraparyavasāyinyeva setyatrāha---upapadyate ca karaṇasyeti
kartavyatayā saṃbandhaḥ | karaṇasyetikartavyatayā niyogasya saṃbandhaḥ anyatrāpi laukike
sādhye darśanāt | lokikasyāpi sādhyasyetikartavyatayā saṃbandhaḥ karaṇaṃ tu dvāramātramiti
lokapratītam | sādhyaścāyaṃ niyogārthaḥ | upasaṃharati---tasmādupapannaṃ itikartavyatā-
rthitvāditi vidheyasaṃbandhakāraṇam | vidheyayoḥ saṃbandhakāraṇamiti | vyākhyāṃta cedam |
yadi vākyārthayorapi saṃbandhaḥ śābdaḥ kimiti tarhi vākyasyaivārthena autpattikaḥ saṃbandho
darśitaḥ yāvatā vākyasamūhasyāpi darśayitavyaḥ | ucyate---asūtrārthajño bhavān kriyārthe-
neti vadatā sarvasyaivāsya prāmāṇyaṃ darśitam | yāvaddhi kriyārthatayā niyogārthatayā prati-
pādyate tasya sarvasyānvayaḥ śābda eva na pratibhāmātrakalpita iti sarvasyāsya padārthānvayalakṣaṇasya
vākyārthānvayalakṣaṇasya ca prāmāṇyaṃ darśitam | nanvevaṃ vadatā niyogārthena sahaidamarthyalakṣaṇaḥ
saṃbandha eva śābda ityuktaṃ na punarvidheyānāmapi parasparasaṃbandha ityatrāha---aidamarthyalakṣaṇameva
saṃbandhaṃ vedavido manyanye | tatsiddhayarthaṃ tu padārthānāmapi anvitābhidhānaṃ dṛṣṭam |
asyārthaḥ---padārthānāmapi yaḥ parasparasaṃbandhaḥ sa aidamarthyalakṣaṇasaṃbandhasidhyartha eva | sarvasyātra
(1) G. kāraṇa

[p. 762]

niyogaidamarthyalakṣaṇa eva saṃbandhaḥ tenehāpi vākyārthānāṃ niyogaidamarthyalakṣaṇassaṃbandhaḥ |
tatsiddhayarthastu vidheyasaṃbandha iti | niyogaidamarthyameva ca śabdasya pramāṇāntaratve hetuḥ
tatra pramāṇāntarānavagamāt | anvitārthābhidhānaṃ tu loke’pi tulyam | devadattaḥ
kāṣṭaiḥ sthālyāmodaṃna pacatītyevamādiṣu laukikeṣvapi | adhikaraṇārthamupasaṃharati---ataḥ
prakaraṇaviniyojyamapi śeṣatvamastīti siddham | prayojanaṃ tu saptamādyapūrvapakṣanyāyena
vyākhyātamiti na vyākhyātam |

kramaśca deśasāmānyāt | atredānīṃ kaḥ praśnārthaḥ pūrvādhikaraṇe hi prakaraṇaṃ nāma
pramāṇāntaraṃ nāstītyanenābhiprāyeṇa yuktaḥ praśnaḥ | atra punaḥ kaḥ praśnārthaḥ | uttaraṃ
---aidamarthyalakṣaṇameva saṃbandhaṃ vede vedavido manyanta ityuktam | sa ca saṃbandhamātramevā-
pādayiṃtu samarthaḥ tāvanmātreṇaivopapatteḥ na punaḥ karaṇaviśeṣasaṃbandhamapi | api ca aidamarthya-
vyatirekeṇa deśasāmānyameva na saṃbandho’sti yo’nyathānupapadyamānaḥ viśeṣakaraṇasaṃbandhaṃ kalpa-
yatīti pramāṇāntarabhūtakramākṣeparūpaḥ2 praśnaḥ | nanu caitāvantyeva pramāṇānītyākṣepeṇa
bhavitavyam | kimidaṃ ‘kimetāvantyeva pramāṇāni’
 iti | atrocyate---atrāpyasaṃyukta-
(1) Bhashya P. 819 (2) R. kṣepaparaḥ

[p. r763]

grahaṇaṃ viṣayāntarapradarśanārthatayā’nuvartate | tasmin satyayaṃ praśnaḥ pūrvavadityanavadyam |
itara āha-satyamaidamarthyavyatirekeṇa saṃbandho nāsti tathāpi pramāṇāntaramastyeva aidamarthya-
viśeṣāvagatau sthānasāmānyaṃ nāma | tadidamuktaṃ kramaśca deśasāmānyāditi | asyārthaḥ
---dabdhirnāmāsīdityanumantraṇamantraḥ kenacidanumantraṇīyena sahaikāvākyatāmākāṅkṣati tena
sannihietenaikavākyatāṃ yāti | yatraiva sthānena ekavākyatāpūrvakaḥ śeṣabhāvaḥ tatra sthānenaiva
viśeṣakaraṇasaṃbandhaḥ iti | prayojanaṃ tu darśitameva |

ākhyā caivaṃ tadarthatvāt | atredānīṃ praśnārtho nopapadyate aidamarthye sati
sthānabat viśeṣāvagame hetutvāt samākhyāyāḥ | asyārthaḥ---adhvaryvādayaḥ prakaraṇe
paripaṭhitāḥ niyogenaidamarthyāpannā kathamaidamarthyaṃ bhajantāmiti ādhvaryavamityādibhiḥ 2karmaviśeṣā-
bhidhāyinīmiḥ samākhyābhiḥ viśeṣakāryasaṃbandhāvagamāt tad‌dvāreṇaiva śeṣatvamiti samākhyāyāḥ
prāmāṇyāt tadākṣepāya na praśna upapadyate | ucyate---astyatra viśeṣaḥ sthānasamākhyayoḥ
yuktaṃ tāvat sthānasya viśeṣāvagamahetutvaṃ vaidikatvāt | veda eva hi upāṃśuyājakrame
‘dabdhirnāmāsi’
 ityanumantraṇamantraḥ paṭyate | tatra sannihitenānvitābhidhānaṃ loke vyutpannamiti
(1) Bh. P. 803 (2) G. dharma

[p. r764]

sannihitopāṃśuyājadevatayaiva asīti mantragataṃ padaṃ saṃbadhyate | tena yuktaṃ sthānasya viśeṣā-
vagamahetutvam | samākhyā punaḥ laukikī ādhvaryavamityādikā | na ca laukika-
mabhidhānaṃ vedārthāvagamaheturbhavati | yathā pacatītyevamādiṣu | asyārthaḥ---laukika-
mabhidhānaṃ vedānanupraviṣṭaṃ na padārthāvagamaheturbhavati yathā pacatītyādiḥ | anyathā pacatītyāderapi
vedārthāṅgabhūtapākavidhāyakatvaṃ syāta iti yataḥ samākhyāyāḥ vedārthāvagamahetutvaṃ nāsti ataḥ
apramāṇatvāt samākhyāyāḥ idamuktaṃ kimetāvantyeva pramāṇātīti | atrocyate---samākhyā-
yā laukikatvepi vaidikasyārthasyāvagamahetutvam | ādhvaryavādisamākhyā tu karmaviśeṣāṇāṃ
kartṛviśeṣāvagamahetutvaṃ pratipadyate | kutaḥ ? abhidhānaṃ hi kṛttaddhitasamāsānāṃ saṃbandhini
prasiddhaṃ vaidikasyārthasya cedamabhidhānaṃ yadyapi svayaṃ laukikam | asyārthaḥ---yathā pacatī-
tyādi laukikamamidhānaṃ na vaidikārthasaṃbaddhaṃ na tathā hautrādisamākhyā vaidikakarmaviṣayatvāt iyaṃ
ca samākhyā taddhitāntā taddhitāntaiścāndhiānaṃ prakṛtyarthasaṃbandhini dṛṣṭam | karmasu ca kartṛviśeṣā-
śravaṇāt kartṛviśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ samākhyayā karmasaṃbandhitvenāvagatā hotrādayaḥ eva kartāro vijñāyante |
atassamākhyā viśeṣamavagamayati | samākhyāyā buddhisthatvena 1kartṛviśeṣeṇaitāni karmāṇyabhidhīyanta
ityarthaḥ | nanu ca laukikamidamabhidhānaṃ, tacca pramāṇāntarapūrvakaṃ tena pramāṇāntareṇa viśiṣṭapuruṣa-
sabanvamavagamya laukikamabhidhānaṃ prayoktavyam | na ca prāk samākhyāyāḥ saṃbandho’vagataḥ iti kathaṃ
samākhyayā niyamaḥ ityatrāha---na cedaṃ smṛtivat pramāṇāntaramapekṣate autpattikatvācchabdārtha-
(1) G. kartṛviśeṣāṇyetāni

[p. r765]

saṃbandhasya | asyārthaḥ---smṛtau hi vākyaṃ puruṣapraṇītam | tacca vākyaṃ puruṣapraṇītamucyate
yatpuruṣeṇa pramāṇāntareṇārthaṃ pratītya tatpratipādanāya prayujyate | taduktaṃ1 “kṛtiścātra
pramāṇāntaradṛṣṭārthapratipādanam | na ca varṇādikāryatā”
 iti | tena tatra pramāṇāntarāpekṣā |
iha tvādhvaryavamityādipadaṃ, na ca padaṃ pauruṣeyaṃ anāditvāt | ato’tra na pramāṇāntarāpekṣā |
tasmād‌yuktaṃ ākhyā caivaṃ tadarthatvāt iti | kuto yuktam---yato viśeṣāvagamahetuḥ
sāmānyataḥ kriyākṣepeṇa kartṛmātre’vagate viśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ samākhyā yasmādviśeṣāvagamahetuḥ,
tasmādviśeṣānvitābhidhānasiddhayarthaṃ kartṛviśeṣamupasthāpayatīti samākhyāpi pramāṇamityarthaḥ |
prayojanamuktam |

śrutiliṅgavākyaprakaraṇasthāna | atra bhāṣyam---kā punarityādi | tadākṣipati
---kā punaratra śrutiḥ kimidaṃ liṅgam | nanu cāyaṃ praśno nopapadyate | kimiti nopapadyate ?
yasmāduktāni viniyoge kāraṇānītyuktam | atraiva bhāṣyakāreṇa | na cānirjñātasyo-
ktatā saṃbhavati | tasmādvipratiṣiddhaṃ uktāni viniyoge kāraṇānīti kā punaratra śrutiḥ kiṃ
liṅgamiti ca | uktānīti padaṃ nirjñātatāmāha | kā punaḥ śrutiḥ kiṃ liṅgamiti ca
anirjñātatām | na caikasyārthasya yugapat nirjñānaṃ anirjñānaṃ ca saṃbhavati |

(1) Bhashya 824

[p. r766]

athāyamabhiprāyaḥ viniyoga eva śrutyādibhiruktaḥ na punaḥ śrutyādīnaṃ svarūpamiti
tenoktatā ca praśnaścopapadyate iti yadyapi viniyoga evokto na śrutyādīnāṃ svarūpaṃ, evaṃ
tarhyevameva vaktavyaṃ uktaḥ śrutyādibhirviniyoga iti na punarevaṃ vaktavyaṃ uktāmi
viniyoge kāraṇātīti | kiñca viniyogoktyāpi viniyoge kāraṇānyuktānyeva bhavanti |
kutaḥ ? kāryataḥ kāraṇaparijñānāt viniyogaḥ kāryaṃ śrutyādīni viniyoge kāraṇānīti
kārye cāvagate kāraṇamavagataṃ bhavati | śrutiviniyogo yasya kāryaṃ tata śrutyākhyaṃ pramāṇamiti |
itarapramāṇebhyaśca śābdasyāyaṃ viśeṣa ityāhaarthāvacchedyameva hi śābdaṃ viśeṣato bhavati
svarūpato varṇamātrāvagamāt | idaṃ padaṃ vākyaṃ iti yo’yaṃ vibhāgaḥ sa kāryavibhāga-
nibandhana eva | na cārthāvagamādṛte kāryāt svarūpata eva śābdasya bhedaḥ svarūpato
varṇamātrāvagamāta | tena viniyoge’pyukte kāraṇajñānasyodbhūtatvāt praśno nopapadyate |
ākṣepāntaraṃ cāha---śrutiliṅgayośca viśeṣamabhidhāya punarvākyapraśne ko’bhiprāya iti na
vidmaḥ | yayoreva hi pramāṇayoryasmin viṣaye balāvalasaṃpradhāraṇā tayoreva svarūpapraśnaḥ tatra
yuktaḥ, nāparasya | athāyamabhiprāyaḥ pṛcchataḥ vākyasvarūpe pṛṣṭe---saṃhatyārthamabhidadhati
padāti vākyaṃ ityuttare ukte śrutiliṅgayorvākyādviśeṣānupapattiḥ | ato vākyasya
vākyasya caiṣā saṃpradhāraṇeti codyaṃ kartavyamityevamarthaṃ idaṃ vākyaviṣayaṃ praśnottaraṃ praśnaśca uttaraṃ ca
(1) viniyogoktyāvinaiva praśno na ghaṭate

[p. r767]

praśnottaram | tadapi nopapadyate yasmādgārhapatyaśrabaṇaṃ śrutiḥ, indraśabdasyābhidhānasāmarthyaṃ ca
liṅgaṃ, saṃhatyārthamabhidadhati padāni vākyaṃ iti spaṣṭameva trayāṇāṃ parasparamanyatvamupalabhā-
mahe | nanu saṃhatyeti vadan miliṃta padajātaṃ apūrvamarthamabhidhatte itīva grantho dṛśyate | sa
cāyamanupapannaḥ | samudāyasya vācakatvaniṣedhāt | ekaikameva hi padamanvitaṃ svārthamabhidhatta
iti tatra tatroktam | ucyate---abhidadhatīti bahuvacanaprayogāt ekaikaśa eva abhidhāna-
muktam | kintu ekaikenaiva padenānvitaṃ svārthamabhidadhatā yenānvitaṃ tadupasthāpakaṃ padamape-
kṣitam | ato’rthāntaropasthāpakatayā padāntarāpekṣitayā saṃhatyetyuktam | etacca
vākyārthamātṛkāyāṃ tadbhūtādhikaraṇe coktamasmābhiḥ | tasmādandhapadamevedaṃ bhāṣyamiti
granthajñānamānino manyante | atrābhidhīyate | satyamarthāt pramāṇāvagatiḥ tathāpi
tūpapadyata eva praśnaḥ kā punaratra śrutiriti | atrāyamāśayaḥ---viniyogābhidhānena pramāṇa-
svarūpamapyuktaṃ bhavati tadabhiprāyeṇaivedamuktaṃ uktāni viniyogekāraṇānīti | na ca svarūpa-
jñāne sati śrutiliṅgapraśnasyānavakāśa iti upapadyate praśnaḥ | kathamupapadyate ? uttaraṃ
---satyaṃ gārhapatyaśravaṇaṃ śrutiḥ satyaṃ cendrābhidhānasāmarthyaṃ liṅgaṃ tathāpi padāntaranirapekṣaṃ
naiva śakyate’vagantum | eendrayā gārhapatya upasthātavya iti śrutiviniyojyatvaṃ,
nāpīndraparatā mantrasya laiṅgikī padāntaranirapekṣā | ataḥ tulye padāntarasavyapekṣatve

[p. r768]

kāryagamyayoḥ śrutiliṅgayorviśeṣamajānan pṛcchati---kā punaratra śrutiḥ kiṃ liṅgamiti | atha
kimiti adhyāyādau idaṃ na nirṇīta śrutyādīni ca viniyoge kāraṇānītyasminneva bhāṣye
atrocyate---tatrāpyetadutsūtrameva vaktavyam | yadi hi tatredaṃ sūtroktaṃ tadā sūtraṃ
vivṛṇvan bhāṣyakāro brūyāt tatrāpyetadutsūtramiti viśeṣābhāvādihaivoktam | iyāṃstvatra
viśeṣaḥ śrutiliṅga ityasya sūtrasya vyākhyātavyatayā aupoddhātikatayā saṃbaddhamabhidhānaṃ
bhavati | jñātasvarūpayorhi bhedāvagame sati balābalavivekasyāvasara iti bhavati svarūpajñānamau-
poddhātikam | kathaṃ punaridānīṃ tulye padāntarasaṃbandhe gārhapatyaśabdasya śrutitocyate ?
yāvatā vākyameva1 dvayamapyetaditi | iyamucyate | padāntarasaṃbandho mantragato viniyoga-
vākyagataśca indrābhidhāne gārhapatyābhidhāne vā’viśiṣṭaḥ | gārhapatyaśravaṇameva tu gārha-
patyābhidhāne vyāpriyate | tenānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ gārhapatyaśravaṇameva gārhapatyābhidhāne
vyāpriyate | nanu dvitīyā vyāptiyata ityuktam | indre’pi hyupastheye gārhapatyaśabdaḥ
saptamyantatayā’nvayaṃ yātyeva | na punardvitīyeti saiva gārhapatyopasthāne karaṇam | satyaṃ
gārhapatyaśravaṇe sati | sāpi na prātipadikārthanirapekṣā gārhapatyaviniyoge prabhavati,
(1) G. vākyenanvayama

[p. r769]

indre’pi dvitīyārthopapatteḥ | ataḥ padasyaiva darśitaṃ na prātipadikamātrasyeti | dvitīyā
anuktāpyupalakṣyate | dvitīyopādānepi prātipadikamapi iti padameva gārhapatyābhidhāne
vyāpriyate | evamindrābhidhāne indropasthāne viniyogepi mantre padāntaramudāste gārhapatyā-
bhidhānepi tulyatvāt | tasmāt padāntarāṇāmudāsīnatvāt śrutiliṅgayorvākyāt bhedaḥ |
evaṃ ca sati 1padāntarāpekṣatvena vākyatve saṃbhāvyamāne sati etadvivekajñānāyaiva vākyepi
praśnottaramityupapannameva | upasaṃharati---ato’sādhāraṇapratītinimittatayā satyapi
saṃhatyārthābhidhāne śrutirliṅgamiti cocyate | yato bhedaḥ śrutiliṅgayoḥ | tasmādupapannaṃ
śrutiliṅgayorvirodhe kimubhayaṃ viniyojakaṃ uta śrutireveti | prakaraṇapraśnābhiprāyaṃ tu
svasthāne prakaraṇapraśnasthāne vākyaprakaraṇavirodhādhikaraṇe vakṣyāmaḥ vyākhyāsyāma ityarthaḥ |
sthānasamākhyayośca praśnabhiprāyaṃ vakṣyāma ityanuṣaṅgaḥ | nanu cevaṃ sati śrutiliṅgayo-
rbhede sati saṃpradhāraṇamevaṃ vaktavyaṃ kiṃ śrutirliṅgaṃ cobhayaṃ pramāṇaṃ uta śrutirvalīyasīti | iha
punarbhāṣye śrutiliṅgayoḥ kiṃ balīyasī śrutiḥ āhosvilliṅgamiti na ghaṭate | kutaḥ ?
yasmādidamapi kāraṇaṃ idamapi śrutirapi kāraṇaṃ liṅgamapi kāraṇaṃ iti vakṣyati iti
(1) padārthāpekṣatvena

[p. r770]

tasmāttadanusāreṇa dvayorapi viniyojakatvaṃ uta śrutereveti saṃśayo yuktaḥ | tasmādyathaiva pūrva-
mupanyastamasmābhiḥ tathaivopanyasanīyam | na tu kiṃ śrutirvalīyasī āhosvilliṅgamiti |
yadi tulyabale ete kāraṇe iti ca pūrvapakṣahetudarśanāt | tadanurūpeṇa hetumatā ubhaya-
viniyojakatvena bhavitavyam | ākṣipyopasaṃharati---tasmādanyathaivopanyāso’nyathaivopa-
saṃhāra iti | viruddhaṃ phalamapi pūrvapakṣasiddhāntayoḥ evamevopanyastam---kimindrasya
gārhapatyasya copasthānaṃ kartavyaṃ uta gārhapatyasyaiveti | tasmādupakramopasaṃhārayoḥ sāmyaṃ
vaktavyamiti | ākṣepāntaraṃ cāha---yadapi caivaṃ tarhi liṅgavākye virudhyamāne iha
saṃpradhāryete na śrutiliṅge iti tadapyayuktameva | sarvatra samavetaṃ viviktaṃ ca dṛśyata
ityuktatvāt vivekasya | ākṣepāntaramāha---yā tvatra śrutiḥ sā liṅgena virudhyate |
vākyaṃ punaḥ lakṣaṇayāpyupapadyata evetyuktvā athaveti punaḥ śrutyantaropanyāsaḥ kimaparito-
ghāt uta vikalpārtha iti etadapi na jñāyate | pūrvaṃ gārhapatyaśravaṇaṃ śrutimuktvā athaveti
dvitīyāśrutiṃ darśayati | tatra kimaparitoṣeṇedamucyate atha vā pakṣāntaravibhaveneti na jñāyate |
(1) G. dvitīyāṃ

[p. r771]

ucyate---yastāvadayamupanyāsaḥ kiṃ balīyasī śrutiḥ āhosvilliṅgamiti tatrāyamabhiprāyaḥ
kiṃ liṅgopamardanenaiva śrutiḥ yathā pramāṇaṃ tathā liṅgamapi śrutyupamardanenaiva pramāṇamiti |
yadā cobhayaṃ ubhayopamardanenaiva pramāṇaṃ tadopapadyata evāyamupanyāsaḥ kiṃ śrutirbalīyasī
āhosvilliṅgamiti | kathamupapadyata ityāha---etaduktaṃ bhavati na vidmaḥ kiṃ balīya iti |
tenāyaṃ bhāṣyārthaḥ---kiṃ liṅgāt śrutireva balīyasī āhosvicchrutistāvadbalīyasyeva | evaṃ
liṅgamapi śruterbalīya iti | tasmādubhayaṃ pramāṇam | ubhayaprāmāṇye ca ubhayo-
rabhidhānam | ata eva cāniyama ityuktam | yadi tulyabale ete kāraṇe iti ca
viśeṣāgrahaṇāduktamityanuṣaṅgaḥ | bhāṣyamanubhāṣyākṣipati---tato vikalpa iti | ko’yaṃ
vikalpaḥ | nanu cāsmin pakṣe samuccaya eva indre ca gārhapatye ca viniyogānmantrasya |
samarthayate---abhidhānavikalpamaṅgīkṛtyaitaduktaṃ na prayogavikalpam | asyārthaḥ---yadā
gārhapatyābhidhānāya prayujyate tadā mantreṇendro nābhidhīyate | yadā indropasthānāya na tadā
gārhapatyasya ityabhidhānasya vyavasthito vikalpaḥ | mantraprayogasya tu agnau indre ca samuccaya
(1) Bh. P. 824

[p. r772]

eva śeṣibhedena pratipradhānaṃ guṇāvṛtteriti | ‘yadi tu liṅgaṃ balīya’
 ityetadapi bhāṣyaṃ
upanyāsavat draṣṭavyam | balīyastvaṃ ca śrutivat svaviṣaye nirapekṣaviniyojakatvam |
ukte’pi śrutiliṅge vākyānāṃ vivekaḥ | evaṃ tarhīti punaḥ praśno na kevalāt gārhapatya-
śravaṇāt tadupasthānāvagatiḥ padamātrasyāvācakatvāt | tāvanmātrasya śrutitvamupadarśitam
taccāvācakam | yacca vācakaṃ tadvākyamiti | evaṃ codite tadidānīṃ rāddhāntavādyabhiprāyaṃ
vivṛṇotīti vadan pūrvoktavivaraṇametaditi darśayati---satyaṃ vākyādṛte nāyamavagamaḥ
gārhapatyaśravaṇāt tadupasthānāvagatiriti | etacca yā tvatra śrutirityanena granthenoktam
indrābhidhānepi tulyatvāt padāntarāṇām | kathaṃ tulvatvaṃ ? balīyasyapi hi liṅge
indrepyupastheye aindrayopatiṣṭhata iti etat gārhapatyaśabdena sahaikavākyatāmabhyupetyaiva
yadi hi nopoyāt tadā liṅgena vākyaṃ virudhyeta vākyaliṅgavirodha eveha bhavet na
śrutiliṅgavirodhaḥ | iha gārhapatyaśravaṇādanantaraṃ yo’rthaḥ pratīyate’gniviśeṣātmakaḥ sa liṅgena
viruddhayate | ataḥ śrutiliṅgayorevāyaṃ virodhaḥ | 1atha vā nātraikavākyatvāditi
(1) G. kathaṃ vā

[p. r773]

pakṣāntaropanyāsaḥ sakalapadapradarśanārtha iti vyākhyātam | nāparitoṣeṇa nāpi pakṣāntara---
tayā śrutyantaropanyāsaḥ | kintu pūrvaṃ kevalaṃ prātipadikamuktaṃ, adhunā vibhaktisahitam |
vibhaktisahitasya ca viniyojakatvamiti darśitaṃ prāk | kiṃ punaratra yuktaṃ ? ubhayatra
svārthāvagamāt | vivṛṇoti---liṅgādapi liṅgārthaḥ indropasthānātmako’vagamyate | śrute-
rapi śrutyartho gārhapatyopasthānātmako’vagamyate | vākyasya caudāsīnyāt ubhayatrābhidhāna-
meva2 padamātramevobhayatra mantre viniyojake ca viruddhayate | na cābhidhāne kaścidviśe-
ṣo’vagamyate ityatastulyabale ete kāraṇe ityuktam | evaṃ cātra pūrvapakṣakaraṇe ‘vacanāttva
yathārthaṃ’
 ityanena saha spaṣṭamapaunaruktyam | tatra hi pūrvapakṣe liṅgasyaiva viniyojakatvamuktvā
siddhānte śrutistāvadviniyoktuṃ śaknotītyuktam | ihedānīṃ śruteḥ sthite viniyojakatve
liṅgasyāpi viniyojakatvamāśaṅkyata ityuktam | atra bhāṣyaṃ---“nanu liṅgasya bhaṅgurasyeva rūpaṃ
savicikitso hi liṅgāt pratyaya”
 iti | tadākṣipati---keyaṃ vicikitsā nāma ? uttaraṃ
---asamyak‌pratyayaḥ | kimatrāsamyaktvaṃ ? uttaraṃ---nahīndrābhidhānāt indro’bhidhātavya
iti pratyayo bhavati | na hi śaktimātreṇa idamanena kāryamiti kartavyatājñānaṃ bhavati |
bhavati tu gārhapatyaśravaṇāt anena gārhapatya upastheya iti kartavyatājñānaṃ vidhāyakavākya-
gatatvāt gārhapatyaśabdasya | nanu cāvidhāyakatvālliṅgasya prāmāṇyameva nāsti kathaṃ
(1) Bhashya 842 (2) G. adds evameva

[p. r774]

virodhaḥ | nāpramāṇena pramāṇasya virodha ityarthaḥ | pariharati---satyaṃ, abhidhānasāmarthyamātraṃ
naiva pramāṇaṃ itikartavyatāvagame sati anyato viśeṣāvagame2 pramāṇam | kaiḥ kartavyatā-
vagamaḥ ? prakaraṇādibhiḥ sāmānyenātropakartavyamityavagate kiṃ kurvateti viśeṣākāṅkṣāyāṃ
yacchaknoti kartuṃ tatkurvateti viśeṣakārye liṅgaṃ viniyojakaṃ bhavatyeva | 3kartavyatāvagame
cābhidhānamātramavaśiṣyata ityuktaṃ bhāṣye | tadidaṃ liṅgakramādhikaraṇe sanyāsikīkṛtaṃ
prakaṭīkṛtam | pūrvapakṣamupasaṃharati---tasmāt tulyabale ete kāraṇe iti | atrāparaṃ bhāṣyaṃ
“balīyānapi hetuḥ”
 iti tadvayācaṣṭe---na ca tāvat balīyān | uktena nyāyena śrutiliṅgayo-
stulyabalatvāt | anvāruhyāpi na virodho’stīti manyate | “nanu cāyameva virodhaḥ
sakṛdupasthānaṃ coditaṃ asakṛdabhinirdhartyata iti | naivam | upastheyabhedāt pratipradhāna-
mā/?/bartante guṇā iti nyāya evaiṣaḥ na virodhaḥ”
 iti bhāṣyamanubhāṣya ākṣipati---nanu cedama-
virodhottaraṃ na ghaṭate na hyatropastheyadvayaṃ śrūyate | gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhata iti gārhapatya evaikaḥ
upastheyaḥ śrūyate nendro’pi | na liṅgāt kartavyatāvagamo’stīti | tasmādanuttaramidaṃ
bhāṣyam |

(1) Bh, P. 844 (2) G. vagamaḥ (3) G. kartavyanavagame

[p. r775]

ucyate---prakaraṇāt kartavyatāvagamāt | indrapadādapīndrābhidhānaṃ pratīyata
iti prakaraṇasavyapekṣālliṅgādapi kartavyatāvagamaḥ iti manvānena 1upastheyamedādityuktam |
vākyasāmānyādvā | asyārthaḥ---viniyojake’pi padamātrameva gārhapatyaṃ iti viniyojakaṃ
padāntarāṇāmudāsīnatvāt mantre’pi indrapadamaviśiṣṭamiti tadapi viniyojakaṃ saṃbhavati
vākyasāmānyāt sādhāraṇyāt; ubhayatrāpi tulyatvādityuktaṃ prāgeva | evaṃ tarhi gauṇa-
mukhyatā noṣapadyata iti punarvirodhopanyāsaḥ | nanu ca pratipradhānaṃ guṇāvṛttau satyāṃ
naivāyaṃ virodho ghaṭate | asyārthaḥ---pramāṇadvayenobhayatra virodhāt pratipradhānaṃ guṇānāmāvṛtteḥ
dvirayaṃ mantraḥ prayoktavyaḥ | tatraikasmin prayoge mukhyatā anyasmiṃśca gauṇateti na virodhaḥ |
yadi hyekasmin prayoge gauṇamukhyārthatocyate tato virodho bhavediti | pariharati---avini-
yuktamantrāpekṣo’yaṃ virodhaḥ | aviniyukto yo mantraḥ tadapekṣo’yaṃ gauṇamukhyārthatāpatyā
virodhaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati---svādhyāyakāle yasya mantrasyaikaḥ prayogaḥ sa gauṇamukhyārthoṃ na yukta
iti | kathaṃ tarhi na virodha ityatrāha---viniyoge tu avirodhaḥ | pradhānabhedena vini-
yogabhedāt | tadbhede ca pratipradhānaṃ prayogabhedādavirodha iti siddho’tra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ
rāddhāntamupakramate---atrocyate śrutiliṅgayoḥ śrutirbalīyasī | kutaḥ arthaviprakarṣāt |
kimidaṃ arthaviprakarṣāditi | arthasya viprakarṣo’rthaviprakarṣaḥ | kaḥ punararthaḥ śrutyarthaḥ
(1) G. indropastheya

[p. r776]

anenedaṃ kartavyamityevaṃrūpaḥ | atra gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhata iti sannikṛṣṭaḥ śrutyarthaḥ pramā-
ṇāntarānapekṣatvācchruteḥ indra upastheya iti viprakṛṣṭaḥ | kathaṃ ? uttaraṃ---“kadācana
starīrasi”
 iti mantreṇendra upastheya iti na śrūyate satyapīndrābhidhānasāmarthye vacanābhāvāt |
anupasthānīya indra ityavagamyate” iti bhāṣyamanubhāṣyākṣipati---nanu ca pūrvamevoktaṃ satyāṃ
kartavyatāyāṃ indro’bhidhīyate yena liṅgena sāmarthyalakṣaṇena tadindrābhidhānaṃ pramāṇam |
tataścobhayoḥ indragārhapatyayorabhidhāne syāttulyataiva | ato dvayoḥ śrutiliṅgayoḥ
prāmāṇyamiti | kimidaṃ punarākṣiptam ? liṅgaprāmāṇyaṃ samāhitaṃ ca samarthanamucyate
“dharmasya śabdamūlatvādaśabdamanapekṣaṃ syāta iti yadetat prakaraṇaṃ liṅgaṃ ca ubhayametada-
śābdam | na cātikrāntapratyakṣaviṣaya(eva) evaṃlakṣaṇake’rthe śabdamantareṇa paricchedo’va-
kalpate | ato manyāmahe viprakṛṣṭa śrutyarthālliṅgamiti”
 ucyate---anena bhāṣyeṇa aśābda-
metaditi 1viśeṣaṃ pradarśayituṃ punarākṣiptaṃ liṅgasya prāmāṇyaṃ samāhitaṃ ca | ucyate---
śabdaśca pūrvatra yojyaḥ | nanu caivaṃ sati aśābdatve liṅgasya prāmāṇyameva hīyate |
(1) G. viśeṣaṃ

[p. r777]

kuto virodhaḥ ? prāmāṇye hi virodho bhavati viruddhārthopasthāpakatvāt | athāyamabhiprāyaḥ
asati virodhe śrutyā smṛtivat liṅgasya prāmāṇyaṃ yathā śrutyavirodhaviṣaye smṛteḥ prāmāṇyaṃ
tathā liṅgasyāpi iti | tadayuktam | kutaḥ ? smṛterhyarthamavagamya 1virodhādviparyayaḥ
kalpyate auduṃbarī sarvā veṣṭayitavyeti sarvaveṣṭanaṃ smṛteravagamya śrutivirodhādarthasya viparyayaḥ
kalpyate | iha punarupasthānaṃ naivāvagamyata ityuktam | kathamanavagamyamāne’rthe’numānaṃ
saṃbhāvyate tasmāt durgṛhītastenasaṃbhāṣitavadasaṃbandhaṃ pratibhāti | durgṛhītaṃ durācaritaṃ
yena stenena tadbhāṣitavat asaṃbaddhamidaṃ bhāṣyamityarthaḥ | punarapi bhāṣyakāraḥ prakaraṇā-
vagatakartavyatāpekṣālliṅgādevārthāvagartiṃ darśayati---śrutisaṃbhave ca avagativākyasādhāra-
ṇye sati kathamayaṃ viśeṣa iti vaktavyam | vākyasya śrutau liṅge ca sādhāraṇyenodāsī-
natvāt dvayorapyavagatiryukteti bhāvaḥ |

atrābhidhīyate---kartavyasyetikartavyatākāṅkṣasya vacanaṃ prakaraṇasiti prakaraṇasya
lakṣaṇam | tena caidamarthyalakṣaṇaḥ karaṇaidamarthyalakṣaṇa eva saṃbandhaḥ prakaraṇena pratīyate,
na punaranvita/?/padārthalakṣaṇaḥ anvitāḥ padārthā lakṣaṇaṃ pramāṇaṃ yasya padārthānvayalakṣaṇasya sa
prakaraṇānnāvagamyata ityuktaṃ prakaraṇādhikaraṇe | tataḥ kiṃ ? idaṃ tataḥ | anvitapadārtha-
(1) G. viparyayavirodhaḥ

[p. r778]

lakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ padārthebhya evāvasīyate | aidamarthyalakṣaṇastu kevalaṃ kāryata eva na
padārthānvaye1-2 vyāpāro’vagamyate kāryasya | prakaraṇasya kāryato hi karaṇaidamarthyamevā-
vagamyate | na padārthānvayaḥ kāryamevetikartavyatākāṅkṣaṃ prakaraṇam | tena prakaraṇāta
kevalakaraṇaidamarthyāvagamāt padārthānvayo nāvagamyate | evamapi ko viśeṣaḥ ? ayamasti
viśeṣaḥ padārthānvitalakṣaṇaḥ | kevalasaṃbandhe padārthairanvito yo vākyārthaḥ saḥ lakṣaṇaṃ yasya
padārthānvayarūpasaṃbandhasya tasmin sati paścāt kāryayogaḥ kāryeṇa sahetikartavyatālakṣaṇaḥ
saṃbandhaḥ padārthānvayalakṣaṇastu prathamata eva | kārye tu prakaraṇarūpe sati paścāt padārthānvayaḥ
sāmarthyarūpaliṅgabaleneti so’yamarthaviprakarṣaḥ | śrutau prathamaṃ padārthānvayaḥ liṅge tu paścāditi |
bhavatu padārthānvayaviprakarṣo liṅgasya tathāpi kathaṃ śruterbalīyastvaṃ ityatrāha-anvayapradarśanārthaṃ ca
tārtīyaṃ pramāṇaṣaṭkaṃ śeṣaśeṣivido manyante | tathāpi kimityatrāha---sa cāyamanvayaḥ śrutau
prathamataramavagamyate | tāvataiva grāhakasya mantrasamāmnānasya ca kṛtārthatvāt | na kāryataḥ
pāścātyāyāṃ padārthānvayāvagatau pramāṇamasti | kutaḥ ? na hi anvitasyānvayo dṛśyate
yataḥ | tasmādyuktaṃ liṅgācchrutibalīyasīti | atrādhikaraṇe bhāṣye ānumānikābhidhānaṃ
ca padārthānvayāpekṣam | padārthānvayo hi prakaraṇaliṅgābhyāṃ pāścātya iti pāścātyātma-
(1) padārthānvaya (2) padārthebhyaḥ

katvāt anumāna ivānumānika ityucyate | aśābdaṃ cocyate sākṣādanvitapadārthalakṣaṇātvā-
bhāvāt | nanu samuccayenātra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ kathaṃ vikalpasya cānyāyyatvāt iti rāddhānte’bhidhī-
yate ityatrāha---vikalpasya cānyāyyatvāditi mantravākyāpekṣam | etadeva vivṛṇoti
---nindraśabdasyābhidhānadvayaṃ nyāyyamityabhiprāyaḥ | indraśabdo hi kadācidnārhapatya-
mabhidhatte kadāciccendramiti na nyāyyamiti | ato vikalpasyānyāyyatvāt viniyuktasyaiva
mantrasya viniyogo na ghaṭata iti darśito hetuḥ vikalpasyānyāyyatvalakṣaṇaḥ | yaduktaṃ
avirodha iti pūrvapakṣānubhāṣaṇam | so’yaṃ virodho yaduta indraśabdasya mantravākyā-
pekṣayā’bhidhānadvayaṃ nopapadyata iti | sakṛduccaritaṃ mantravākyam tena caika evārtho-
’bhidhātavyaḥ na punararthadvayaṃ sakṛduccaritasyānekārthatbānupapatteḥ | nanu ca viniyogadvayena
prayogadvayānna virodhaḥ ityatrāha---aidamarthyasya ca anvayalabhyatvāt śrutāvuparataiva kartavyatā-
pekṣeti nākāṅkṣādvayamupapadyata iti virodhaḥ | asyārthaḥ---śrutau padārthānvayāt prathamamavagatāt
karaṇaidamarthyasya labhyatvāt prathamamavagatena śrautena kāryaviśeṣeṇa uparataiva kāryaviśeṣāpekṣeti
nākāṅkṣādvayamupapadyata iti ubhayatrāpi viniyogāt asti mantravākyāpekṣayā virodhaḥ | yadidaṃ
śrautādviniyogāt prāk kāryāpekṣitvaṃ mantrasya grāhakagrahaṇasya ca tadidamakṛtasāmarthyatvaṃ
mantravākyasya viniyogasya coktam vākyaprakaraṇaśabdābhyāṃ bhāṣyagatābhyām | vākya-
śabdena mantravākyāmucyate | prakaraṇaśabdena grāhakaniyoga ityarthaḥ | kṛtaṃ sāmarthyaṃ yayo-

ayamāśayaḥ---yāvanti hiṃ padāni prayujyante tāvanti saṃbhūyaikamarthaṃ pratipādayanti | śaknuvanti

prakaraṇamityuttare datte pūrvapakṣalābho bhaviṣyatīti | bhāṣyārthastu--- “kartavyasya vidhyarthasya iti-
kartavyatākāṅkṣasya yadvacanaṃ pratipādanaṃ tatprakaraṇam”
 | kathaṃ tatprakaraṇaṃ ityapekṣite satyāha
---“prārambho hi saḥ tasya kartavyasya vacanakriyāyāḥ”
 pratipādanakriyāyāḥ kartavyaṃ paripūrṇaṃ

abhidhānaṃ bhavati prārambhaḥ | tadākāṅkṣitaiva kathamityāha---“sa eṣa vidhyādirvidhyantāpekṣaḥ”
 sa
eva kartavyatārūpo vidhyādiḥ vidhyantaṃ itikartavyatāṃ ākāṅkṣati | tena vinā pratyetumanuṣṭhātuṃ
cāśakyatvāt iti | nanu bhāṣye itikartavyatākāṅkṣaniyogapratipādanaṃ prakaraṇamuktaṃ na
punaritikartavyatākāṅkṣo niyogaḥ | ataḥ kathamitikartavyatārthitvalakṣaṇaṃ prakaraṇamucyate | kathaṃ
kāryameva itikartavyatākāṅkṣaṃ prakaraṇaṃ vakṣyatīti---ucyate | bhāṣyakāreṇa yatpratipādanaṃ
prakaraṇamuktaṃ tatpratipādyamānāvastha eva kartavyatārūpo vidhiḥ itikartavyatākāṅkṣa iti darśayitum |
ataśca niyoga evetikartavyatākāṅkṣaḥ pratipādyamānaḥ | tasya ca yā tṛtīyākāṅkṣā prakaraṇa-
mityavirodhaḥ | idānīṃ vākyaptakaraṇayorvirodhe tulyabale ete kāraṇe iti yadvadati tatra
ko’bhiprāyaḥ? pṛcchataścāyamāśayaḥ | vākyameva balīyaḥ padārthānvayasaṃbandhakāraṇatvāditi |
2praṣṭurabhiprāyaṃ kathayati---kāryārthatvādanvayasya ubhayaṃ kāryeṇa viniyujyata iti | ayamarthaḥ
---kāryārthassakalapadārthānāmanvayaḥ ato vākyenāpi yasya viniyogaḥ tasyāpi kāryeṇaiva | prakaraṇe-
nāpi yasya viniyogaḥ tasyāpi kāryeṇaiveti ubhayavirodhe biniyogastulya iti | yadvā kāryādṛte
anvayasya bādhitatvāt pratyuta prakaraṇameva balīyaḥ | tatpratyayatvādvākyasya | evaṃ
manvānenoktaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa liṅgena vākyasya bādhitatvāditi | asyārthaḥ---iti-
kartavyatākāṅkṣā hi kāryameva prakaraṇam | tadvirodhe cānvayo bādhyata iti | yathā prayā-
(1) Bh. P. 853 (2) R. prapṛṣṭamabhi

[p. r788]

jaśeṣeṇetyatra saktūniti ca | ataḥ pratyuta vākyāt prakaraṇameva balīyaḥ
kāryapratyayatvādvākyasya | pratīyate’neneti pratyayaḥ kāryameva vākyena pratyāyyate |
atasyadvirodhe vākyaṃ durbalam | pradhānavirodhe guṇānāṃ durbalatvāditi | anenāśayenoktaṃ
bhāṣyakāreṇa---“1liṅgena bādhitatvādvākyasya prakaraṇenāpi bādho yukta”

iti | atrocyate---atrāpyarthaviprakarṣādityukto hetuḥ | kathamarthaviprakarṣa uktaḥ ? kāryārtha-
tayā’nvayaḥ kalpyaḥ uktametat śrutiliṅgādhikaraṇe “prakaraṇenānvitābhidhānāvagatayā kāryārtha-
tayā itikartavyatātvenānvayaḥ kalpyaḥ”
 | na ca kalṛptānvayasya kāryārthatve siddhe’nvayāntaraṃ,
viniyuktaviniyogavirodhāt liṅgavacchakyate kalpayituṃ k‌lṛptānvayasya viniyojyasya
vākyena punaḥ prakaraṇāt anvayāntaraṃ na śakyate kalpayituṃ viniyuktasya viniyogavirodhāt |
yathā liṅge śrutiviruddhe | yadi yāvadvākyārthānvayānusāreṇa niyogārthatā na syāt tadā
prakaraṇenānvayāntaraṃ kalpyetāpi | iha punaḥ vākyāvagatānvayānugrahe na kāryārthatā’paiti2
iti prathamāvagatatvāt vākyagamya evānvayo balīyāt | tasmādvākyaṃ balīyaḥ | nanu yadyartha-
viprakarṣādeva prakaraṇabādhaḥ tadā śrutiliṅgādhikaraṇenaiva gatārthatā | tatraivārthaviprakarṣeṇāsyokta-
tvāditi tatrāha---durjñānaviprakarṣajñāpanāyādhikaraṇaprapañco’yaṃ liṅgavad‌draṣṭavyaḥ | asyārthaḥ
---satyaṃ viprakarṣeṇa durbalatvaṃ satu viprakarṣa eva durjñāna iti tatparijñānāya adhikaraṇaprapañco’yamiti
udāharaṇamuktaṃ bhāṣye sūktavākanigadaḥ | atha prakaraṇasya ca kramasya ca virodhe kimudā-
haraṇamiti | adhikaraṇasūcanaivātrāpi | pūrvapakṣavādī kramaprakaraṇayostulyabalatvaṃ manyate
(1) Bh. P. 853 (2) G. kāryārthatā’nveti

[p. r789]

kathaṃ ? samarthanaikadeśyuttaramāha---bhāṣyakārastāvat ajñānameva tulyabalatve pramāṇaṃ
vyapadiśati “na tāvadviśeṣamupalabhāmaha”
 iti | ākṣeptā dūṣayati---kimajñānāttulyatvaṃ
siddhayati | na sidhyatyeveti bhāvaḥ | jñānasya hi tulyabalatvamatulyabalatvaṃ vā
jñeyatayā viṣayaḥ | tathā nājñānasya | ekadeśyevāha---nanu cājñānamapi pramāṇatayopanyasta-
meva abhāve2 | ākṣeptaivāha---vyavahāramātraṃ tadityuktaṃ prathamapāde | api cābhāvasya ye
pramāṇatāmapīcchanti teṣāmapi mate pramāṇāntaraprameyābhāvamātraṃ ca prameyam | pramāṇāntareṇa
yatpratīyate ghaṭādi tasyābhāvamātraṃ abhāvasya prameyam | na punaranyat | ata iha
---tattulyabalatvamatulyabalatvaṃ ca na pramāṇāntaraprameyābhāvamātraṃ śakyate vaktuṃ ato nābhāvaḥ
prameyam | saṃprati nirākṛta ekadeśyāha---kathaṃ tarhiṃ etadbhāṣyaṃ “na tāvadviśeṣamupalabhāmaha”

iti | ākṣeptā āha---bhāṣyakāra evātra praṣṭavyaḥ | samarthanavādyāha---atrocyate
durvidagdhavacanamātramevedaṃ ‘bhāṣyakāra evātra pṛcchyatāṃ’
 iti | yasmādayamabhiprāyo
bhāṣyakārasya---nātrānayorviśeṣo jñāyata iti | anayoḥ kramaprakaraṇayoḥ atra videvanādi-
viniyojakatve viśeṣo na jñāyata iti | yadi viśeṣo jñāyeta tadā ekameva viniyojakaṃ
(1) Bh. P. 854 (2) G. bhāṣye

[p. r790]

syāt na viśeṣaṃ jānīmaḥ | tasmādubhayaṃ viniyojakam | nanu ca prakaraṇamevetikartavyata-
yā’nvayaṃ sakalakaraṇasaṃbandhitayā videvanādīnāṃ bodhayatīti viśeṣaḥ gamyate ityatrāha---sarvatra
prakaraṇānusyūteḥ kramo’pi kāryavānavagamyata eva | sarvatra prakaraṇasya adhikāraniyo-
gasyānusyūteḥ anusyūtatvāt kramo’pi kāryavāneva krameṇāpi viniyogaḥ kāryata eva | kāryataśca
bhavan sannidhiranvitamavagamayati | yadi hi kāryasaṃbandho na bhavet tadā sannidhiranvayaṃ na
gamayedeva | yatastu kāryavān sannidhiḥ | ato’nvayaṃ gamayatyeva | pratyuta prakaraṇamevātra
bādhyatayā’vagamyate | viśeṣopasaṃhārāt sannidheḥ | prakaraṇena hi sāmānyena sakala-
karaṇasaṃbandho bodhyaḥ | tatra sannidhānalakṣaṇena krameṇa viśeṣakaraṇa evopasaṃhāraḥ kriyate |
kasmāt punarviśeṣeṇopasaṃhāraḥ kriyate ityatrāha---na hi kalṛptasya sannidheḥ kalpanā śakyate
vaktum | asyārthaḥ---prakaraṇena pramāṇena sarvakaraṇasaṃbandhe kartavye avaśyaṃ sarvaiḥ saha sannidhiḥ
kalpyaḥ asannihitasaṃbandhānupapatteḥ | ākāṅkṣāsannidhiyogyatvānāṃ saṃbandhe hetutvāt | na ca klṛptaṃ
sannidhimanādṛtya sannidhānakalpanā yuktā anvayasyeva | yathā’nvaye k‌lṛpte na saṃbandhasya kalpanā
evamatrapīti | ataḥ krama eva balīyāniti manyate | nanu ca satyapi sannidhāne ākāṅkṣā-
viśeṣābhāvāt na saṃbandhitvena bhavitavyamiti kramasyāpi prakaraṇāpekṣāsti tathā nākāṅkṣāṣi
sannidhānamantareṇa saṃbandhaheturiti parasparāpekṣitvāt dvayoḥ tulyabalatvameva yuktam na tu kramasya
balīyastvamiti tatrāha---tatpunaridamanvāruhyavacanamityuktamādye’dhikaraṇe | prauḍhavāda evāyaṃ
na punaḥ paramārthataḥ | evaṃ tulyabalatvenāyaṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ ityetat śrutiliṅgādhikaraṇe darśitam |
sarvatra caivaṃ draṣṭavyam |

[p. r791]

atrocyate---kramāt prakaraṇaṃ balīya iti | kutaḥ ? nahyanākāṅkṣitasya kramaḥ saṃbandha-
hetuḥ yasyākāṅkṣā nāsti na tena saha krameṇa saṃbandhaḥ sannidhānamātraṃ hi kramaḥ | na ca
sannidhānamātrās saṃbandho’vagabhyate bhāryā rājñaḥ puruṣo devadattasyetivat | yathā bhāryāpada-
saṃbandhenaiva nirākāṅkṣeṇa rājña ityanena puruṣasya saṃbandho na bhavati tathetyarthaḥ | bhaktvākaṅkṣāta
eva saṃbandhaḥ tathāpi kimityāha---ākāṅkṣā ca prakaraṇavatāṃ tulyeti sarvapradhānasaṃbandho
videvanādīnām | nanu ca kramavato’pi ākāṅkṣātaḥ kimiti saṃbandho na bhavatītyāha
---kramāddhayākāṅkṣā | ākāṅkṣāto’nvayaḥ anvayāttādarthyamiti so’yamarthaviprakarṣaḥ |
asyārthaḥ---sannidhisamuccāraṇena hi svavākyopāttapadapadārthasaṃbandhanirākāṅkṣasya abhiṣecanīya-
vākyārthasyāpi vidyamānataiva videvanādipadārthāntarākāṅkṣā kalpayitavyā | kalpayitvā cānvayaḥ
kalpyaḥ | kalpite cānvaye tādarthyaṃ kalpyam | tadidaṃ kramanibandhanaṃ tādarthyaṃ vilambitam |
prakaraṇavatāṃ tu itikartavyatākāṅkṣāṃ utthitaiva aparipūrṇatvāt | tayā ca karaṇaidamarthyaṃ
kalpyata iti śīghraṃ prakaraṇatastādarthyam |

yaduktaṃ kramādākāṅkṣopasaṃhāra iti | tadayuktam | sarbārthatvepi kasyacit sanni-
dhānamavaśyaṃbhāvīti pāṭhārthameva tat | asyārthaḥ---1yadi tadgatānāmavāntaravidhīnāmiti-
kartavyatākāṅkṣā syāt tadā yasya sannidhāvāmnānaṃ tatraivetikartavyatātvamupasaṃhriyate iha tvekasya
rājasūyādhikāravidheritikartavyatā iti nopasaṃhārassaṃbhavati | tasmādekādhikāravidhiṃsabandhibhiḥ
(1) G. parihṛtānāmavā

[p. r792]

karaṇaiḥ itikartavyatayā videvanādayassaṃbadhyante | ekasannidhānaṃ tu avaṃśyaṃbhāvi anyathā
paṭhitumaśakteriti pāṭhārthameva sannidhānaṃ, na punaḥ saṃbandhopasaṃhārārthaṃ bādhitasyahyanugraho nyāyya
ityuktam |

atra bhāṣyaṃ---“prakaraṇavataḥ sannidhau āmnātena paripūrṇenāpi avakalpena ekavākyatvaṃ”

iti | nanu vināpi paripūrṇeṃnetyanena sidhyatyevābhimataṃ kimidaṃ paripūrṇenāpīti | samādhatte-
etaduktaṃ bhavati---na cetkramaḥ pramāṇaṃ ekavākyataiva nāsti iti | nanu ākāṅkṣā-
sannidhiryogyatvamiti ekavākyatve hetuḥ satyaṃ vākyārthāvagatau trayameva hetuḥ
iha punarvākyārthasya vākyārthasya ca saṃbandhaḥ | sa cetikartavyatā-
rthitayaiva saṃbadhyata ityuktam | kramaḥ punaḥ vākyārthasya ca sannidhānamityuktam |
bhāryā rājñaḥ puruṣo devadattasyetyatra pradarśitam | ayamabhiprāyaḥ---codyanirākaraṇārthamidamuktaṃ
paripūrṇenāpīti | evaṃ hi paraḥ paricodayati kramaprakaraṇe parasparāpekṣayā tulyabale eva | na
hi prakaraṇenāpi itikartavyatātvena saṃbandhaḥ āpādyamāno vinā sannidhānenāpādyate | yasmā---
dākāṅkṣāsannidhiyogyatvānāṃ militānāmeva saṃbandhāvagatihetutvam | tena prakaraṇena kramo-
’pekṣaṇīya iti | tatredaṃ nirākaraṇam paripūrṇeneyamekavākyatā | vākyārthībhūtasya hi
sādhyātmanaḥ vidhyarthasya vākyārthabhūtenaiva aṅgavidhinā itikartavyatāṃśaniveśinā saṃbandhaḥ |
yā ca paripūrṇenaikavākyatā sā itikartavyatārthitayā; ekasya cādhikāravidheritikartavyatārthitā

[p. r793]

nāṅgavidhīnāmiti sarvakaraṇaireva saṃbandho videvanādīnāmiti | akṣarārthastu---yadi kramaḥ
pramāṇaṃ nāṅgīkriyate ekavākyataiva sarvakaraṇairapi saha videvanādīnāṃ na syāt | ākāṅkṣā
sannitiḥ yogyatvamiti trayaṃ militamekavākyatve hetuḥ | tasmāta kramo’pyādaraṇīya iti |
parasparāpekṣāyāṃ tulyaṃ prakaraṇaṃ ākāṅkṣālakṣaṇasannidhānaṃ ca dvayamapīti krameṇāpi viniyogo
yukta iti | uttaraṃ---satyaṃ vākyārthāvagatau trayameva hetuḥ | iha punaḥ vākyārthasya
vākyārthasya ca saṃbandhaḥ sa cetikartavyatārthitayaiva saṃbadhyate na punaḥ kriyākārakādya-
pekṣitayetyuktaṃ prakaraṇādhikaraṇe | ekasya cādhikāravidheritikartavyatārthiteti tadvaśenaiva
sarvatra prakaraṇasaṃbandho nyāyyaḥ | kramaḥ punaḥ itikartavyatākāṅkṣāśūnyo’vāntaraprakaraṇābhāvāt
sannidhānamātraṃ kramaḥ | na ca sannidhānamātrāt saṃbandhaḥ yathā bhāryā rājñaḥ puruṣo deva-
dattasyeti darśitam |

atha kramasamākhyayorvirodhe kiṃ balīya iti cintyate---kramo’hyākāṅkṣāvataḥ sannidhau
bhavati viśeṣāvagamahetuḥ | samākhyā punaḥ viśeṣanimittaiveti tulyabale ete kāraṇe iti |
tasmādubhayaṃ viniyojakam | asyārthaḥ---‘śundhadhvaṃ’
 iti mantraḥ śundhanaṃ prakāśayati | tatra
ye śudhyante te ākāṅkṣyante | tairvinā vākyasyāparipūrṇatvāt | atassannidhau sānnāyya-
pātrāṇi saṃbadhyante | tena teṣāṃ śundhanaprakāśakatvāt sānnāyyapātreṣu mantrasya viniyogaḥ |
samākhyayā puroḍāśapātreṣu, viśeṣavartitvāt samākhyāyā ityākāṅkṣāvataḥ śundhanamantrasya
sannidhau sānnāyyapātrāṇāṃ paṭhitatvāt kramo’pi bhavati viśeṣaviniyogāvagamahetuḥ | samākhyā
punaḥ pauroḍāśikamiti viśeṣanimittaiva | “adhikṛtya kṛte granthe”
 ityasminnadhikāre
“paurauḍāśapuroḍāśāt ṣṭhan”
 iti pauroḍāśaśabdāt puroḍāśamadhikṛtya kṛte granthe ityasminnarthe
ṣṭhan vihitaḥ | paurauḍāśaśabdena puroḍāśasaṃbandhīnyucyante | tena tatpratipādakaṃ kāṇḍamiti

[p. r794]

viśeṣapātrasaṃbandha eva mantrasyetyavagamyate | tasmādubhayaṃ kramasamākhyātmakaṃ viniyojakamiti |
evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate---kramo balīyān vaidiko hyasau, samākhyā punarlokikī | asyārthaṃ
tṛtvato’navabudhyamānaścodayati---nanu caivaṃ vadatā samākhyā naiva pramāṇamityuktaṃ bhavati |
asyārthaḥ---tvaṃ kila siddhāntin laukikī samākhyetyevaṃ vadasi | evaṃ vadatā laukikatvena samā-
khyā vedārthanirṇayaheturna bhavatītyabhyupagatam | tathā ca sarvatraiva samākhyāyāḥ prāmāṇyoccheda iti
pariharati---maivaṃ vocaḥ | arthaviprakarṣa evānena prakāreṇocyate | kathaṃ punarlaukikatvenārtha-
viprakarṣaḥ ? ucyate---samākhyā hi saṃbandhanimittā satī sannidhānādyavagamayati | lauki-
katvena tāvat samākhyā vedārthe na sākṣātsaṃbandhaṃ vidadhāti | kintu saṃbandhanibandhanā satī
saṃbandhasidhyarthaṃ ākāṅkṣāsannidhiyogyatāṃ kalpayati | kramaḥ punaḥ svayameva sannidhiḥ | na
ca pratyakṣasya sannidheḥ punaḥ sannidhānāntarakalpanā ghaṭata iti | pratyakṣasya sannidheritya-
nādare ṣaṣṭhī | pratyakṣaṃ sannidhānamanādṛtya na sannidhānāntarakalpanā yuktetyarthaḥ | so’yamartha-
viprakarṣaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi samākhyā ? saṃbandhaṃ vinetyarthaḥ | samavāyādibhirupapadyate
brāhmaṇagrāmavat | tasmin kāṇḍe puroḍāśasya samavāyāt bhūyastvāt prāthamyādvā pauroḍāśi-
kavyapadeśaḥ brāhmaṇagrāmavat |

[p. r795]

atha yata tatra tatrābhidhīyate ‘idamanena vādhyate’
 ‘idamanena bādhyata’
 iti tatra
yadbādhyate kiṃ tat bādhakaviṣayaṃ prāptaṃ uta aprāptamiti kasyaṃ vyākhyānamidaṃ bhāṣyamiti |
bhāṣye ākṣipte ekadeśyāha---na kasya cidapīti brūmaḥ | śrutiliṅgetyasya na kaścidavayavo
vyākhyāyate’nena | ato na kasyacidapīdaṃ vyākhyānamiti | ākṣeptā āha---kiṃ tarhi
anenātra kriyate | nirākṛta ekadeśyāha---bhāṣyakāra evātra praṣṭavyaḥ yenedamuktam |
paramasamarthanavādyāha---atrābhidhīyate---śrutyādīnāṃ balābalacintāśeṣabhūtamidaṃ bhāṣyam | kathaṃ
kṛtvā ? utraraṃ---balābalacintā hi bādhyāpekṣiṇī yatkilābalaṃ tadbādhyam | yacca
balavat tadbādhakam | tena bādhyaṃ vinā balābalacintā nāsti | tadidānīṃ bādhyaṃ kiṃ
bādhakaviṣayaṃ prāptaṃ sat bādhyate utāprāptameva ubhayathā cābhihito doṣaḥ | yadi prāptaṃ
kathaṃ bādhyate aśakyatvāt | athāprāptaṃ kiṃ bādhyeta vādhyasyābhāvāt | nanu prāptabādho
daśame pradarśitaḥ | kimiti prāptau satyāṃ virodho darśitaḥ | tatkila viruddhamucyate
yatkvacinna dṛśyate | prāptatā ca bādhyatā ca dṛśyata eva | tatkathamanayorvirodhaḥ | daśame
hi prāptasya prākṛtasya padārthajātasya bāgho darśitaḥ | samādhatte---kāryataḥ prāptau na doṣaḥ |
prāptasyāpi bādhe na doṣaḥ | kāryato hi prāptasya na kāryāntaraṃ kalpayitavyam | tena kalṛpta-
(2) P. 858

[p. r796]

kāryapurassaratvāt prāpteḥ kāryalopena kṛtakṛtyatvena vā bādho yuktaḥ | anuṣṭhānabādhātmakaḥ
---iha tūpadeśaḥ cintyate tathāpi kimityāha---upadeśena ca prāptidurbādhā | kutaḥ ?
tatraiva daśame darśayiṣyāmaḥ | upadeśe hi yathopadeśaṃ kāryamityaśakyaḥ kāryato’pi bādha
iti | ihaiva kasmāt tanna pradarśyate | pradarśitameva “pratipradhānamāvartante guṇā”

ityanena bhāṣyeṇa darśitamevedaṃ upadeśena prāptirdurbādheti | kathaṃ pratipradhānāvṛttyabhidhānena
abādhitatā darśitā bhavati | uttaraṃ---dvayoḥ prāmāṇyābhyupagamanena pratipradhānābhidhānāt
darśitameva | dvayoḥ śrutiliṅgayoḥ prāmāṇye’bhyupagate pratipradhānaṃ guṇā āvartanta iti
yasmādabhihitaṃ tasmādaśakyo bādha iti darśitam | evaṃ hi vadatā yathopadeśaṃ kāryamityā-
śritaṃ bhavati | tasmādupadeśena prāptaṃ upadeśāntareṇa na śakyaṃ bādhitum | prāpteścā-
viśeṣānnopadeśataḥ prāptasya bādhaḥ | kāryataḥ prāptasya tu atideśataḥ prāpteḥ upadeśaprāptena
bādho yuktaḥ | tasmādidamayuktaṃ anena bādhyamiti | na kevalaṃ prāpta evāyuktaṃ
aprāptepyayuktaṃ prasaktyabhāvāt | yatkhalu prasaktaṃ tadbādhamarhati nānyat |

atredānīṃ upadeśabādhavido’bhidadhati | sāmānyataḥ prāptiṃ manvānā viśeṣamapa-
śyantaḥ smṛtipramoṣanimittaṃ prāptijñānaṃ vyapadiśanti śuktikāyāmiva rajatajñānam |
viśeṣāvagamāttu tatteṣāṃ nivartata iti bādhaśabdaprayogaḥ | asyārthaḥ---barhidarvesadanaṃ

[p. r797]

dāmītyevamādau yā liṅgasya svaviniyogaviṣaye prāptiḥ pramāṇato’vadhṛtā viniyojakāviniyojaka-
yoḥ yatsamānaṃ rūpaṃ liṅgayoḥ tatassāmānyataḥ tāmaviniyojake prāptiṃ pramāṇatāṃ manvānāḥ
smarantaḥ prāptismaraṇasya ca prāptijñānādviśeṣamapaśyantaḥ smṛtipramoṣanimittaṃ prāptijñānaṃ
smarāmītyasyāṃśasya pramoṣo nimittaṃ yasya vyapadeśasyeti kriyāviśeṣaṇam | prāptiṃ jānīma
iti bhrāntā vyapadiśanti | śuktikāyāmiva rajatajñānaṃ yathā rajatasādhāraṇaṃ yadrūpaṃ śuktikāyāṃ
tāvanmātreṇa tasyāṃ gṛhītāyāṃ yadrajate smaraṇajñānamupajāyate tasya smṛtirūpatāyā agrahāt
viśeṣamapaśyanto rajatajñānāt rajatasmaraṇameva rajataṃ jñānamupadiśanti rajataṃ jānīma iti |
tathehāpi prāptismaraṇaṃ prāptijñānaṃ vyapadiśanti | viśeṣadarśanāt prāptismaraṇametanna
prāptijñānamiti viśeṣadarśanāt tatteṣāṃ nivartyate paramārthato’tra prāptirna jñāyate prāptisāmagrī-
virahāt sāmagrīvirahaścārthaviprakarṣeṇeti darśitaḥ | tasmācca prāptiriyaṃ smaryate na tu jñāyata
iti jñānataḥ prāptinibandhano’pi vyavahāro nivartyata iti bādhaśabdaprayogaḥ | paramārthatastu
svarūpajñānamevedaṃ ityetat saprapañcaṃ prathamapāda eva nirṇītam | tadayamatrārthasaṃkṣepaḥ
---viniyojakāviniyojakayoḥ sādhāraṇaṃ yadrūpaṃ aviniyojakasya liṅgādeḥ tadvaśena pramāṇatāṃ
viniyojakaliṅgavartinīṃ smarataḥ tasyāśca smṛteḥ viśeṣāgrahaṇena grahaṇādviśeṣamananusandadhataḥ
aviniyojakamapi liṅgādi viniyojakapramāṇamiti vyapadeśavyavahārau pravartete | tatrārtha-
viprakarṣeṇa liṅgādeḥ śrutyādivirodhe pramāṇatā nāsti asati pramāṇatve pramāṇatā na grahītuṃ śakyate
kintvanyatra dṛṣṭā smaryata iti viśeṣālocanena vyavahāravyapadeśau nivartete | evaṃ cāptāptaiva
liṅgādeḥ pramāṇatā smaryamāṇā viśeṣāgrahaṇena vyavahāraṃ gṛhyamāṇeva pravartayantī vyavahārāpahāreṇa
bādhyata iti vyapadiśyate |

nanu cāyamaprāptabādhalakṣaṇo bādhaḥ prathamapāda evoktaḥ ‘yasya ca duṣṭaṃ karaṇaṃ’
 ityatra
kimiti punarupanyastaḥ | aprāptabādhasiddhayarthaṃ nārabdhavyamidamadhikaraṇamityarthaḥ | samādhānaṃ
.
[p. r798]

---astyatra viśeṣaḥ tatra śuktikārthasyāgrahaṇāt | rajate ca smarāmīti pramoṣāt vyapadeśa-
siddhiḥ | asyārthaḥ---śuktikā rajatasādhāraṇena rūpeṇa gṛhyate | sā ca rajatāt
bhinnāpi na bhinnetyabagamyate bhedapratītihetubhūtarūpāntarāpratīteḥ | taduktaṃ---rūpāntara-
nibandhanā hyanyatvabuddhiḥ na vastvantaranibandhaneti | tasyāṃ ca tathābhūtāyāṃ rajate smṛti---
rārvibhavati | tadapi ca rajataṃ na śuktikāto bhinnamityanusandhīyate | ato gṛhyamāṇa-
smaryamāṇayoḥ śuktikārajatayoḥ viveko nāvasīyate | tathā yāpyasau rajatasmṛtiḥ sāpi
grahaṇādbhedena nāvasīyate | smṛtiriyamiti parikalpanāvirahāt | ataḥ grahaṇācchuktiviṣayāta
bhedenānavasīyamānā smṛtiḥ śuktikāyāṃ grahaṇanibandhanāveva vyapadeśavyavahārau pravartayati |
ataḥ tanmātrasmaraṇādeva vyapadeśasiddhiriti ghaṭetāpi aprāptabādhaḥ | iha punaḥ āgabhikatvena
dvayasyāpi tulyatāpattirbhavatyeveti punarārabdham | asyārthaḥ---śrutiliṅgayordvayorapi
pramāṇatvāt tulyatāpattirbhavati | tena viniyogaḥ smaryate iti na śakyate vaktum yāvatā liṅge-
nāpi pramāṇena pramīyata ityetadeva kasmānna bhavatīti śaṅkā’stīti tadapanodanāyedamārabdham |
kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ āgabhikatve dvayasya tulyatāpattirbhavati (tulyatāpattau cābādhakena
punaḥ kāraṇena śrutyā liṅgādidvayasyāpi tulyanāpratītirbhavati) tulyatāpattau ca satyāṃ kena
kāraṇena bādhaḥ ? atrocyate---svato viparyayābhāvāt | yadi hi svata evātrā-
viniyojakametalliṅgamiti viparyayajñānamutpadyeta tadā tulyatābuddhirna bhavati | na cātra śukti---
kādiṣvidha svayameva viparyayo bhavati yathā aparīkṣitakāraṇasvabhāvasyāpi puruṣasya
śuktikādiṣu svayameva viparyayajñānaṃ bhavati nedaṃ rajatamityādi na tatheha | kathaṃ tarhi

[p. r799]

bhavatītyāha---iha punaḥ kāraṇaparīkṣādvārakaḥ viniyogaviparyaya iti prārabdhavyamidamadhi-
karaṇaṃ viniyogakāraṇasvarūpaparīkṣānibandhano’tra viparyayaḥ akṛttārthayorgrāhakagrahaṇaprakaraṇa-
samāmnānayoḥ | viniyojakatvaṃ śrutyādīnāṃ, kṛtārthatvāt tayoraviniyojakatvamiti viniyojaka-
pramāṇasvarūpaparīkṣāyāṃ arthaviprakarṣeṇa liṅgādīnāṃ, sannikarṣeṇa śrutyādīnāmeva viniyojakatvamiti
sthite tāvataiva grāhakagrahaṇaprakaraṇasamāmnānayoḥ kṛtārthatvāt aviniyojakaṃ liṅgādīti viparyayaṃ
puruṣo jānātīti viniyojakasvarūpaparīkṣārthaṃ arthaviprakarṣajñāpanārthaṃ cedamadhikaraṇamārabdhavyam |
tadviśuddhayarthaṃ cedaṃ bhāṣyaṃ yo’sau tenādhikaraṇena bādha uktaḥ tadviśudhyarthamidaṃ bhāṣyam |
kīdṛśī punaranena bādhaśuddhiḥ kṛtetyāha---so’yamaprāptabādhaḥ | yathā cāprāptasya bādhaḥ
tathā darśitameva | tenāyaṃ bhāṣyārthaḥ “1prāptaṃ bādhakaviṣayaṃ pūrvavijñānamiti brūmaḥ”
 |
bādhakasya śrutyāderyo viṣayaḥ tatpūrvavijñānaṃ liṅgādijñānaṃ prāptamiti brūmaḥ | naiva paramārthataḥ
prāptam | kintu aprāptameva paramārthataḥ prāptamiti brūmaḥ praptigrahaṇasmaraṇayoḥ vivekānavadhāraṇāt |
kathaṃ prāptismaraṇamityāha---“2sāmānyasya kāraṇasya vidyamānatvāta”
 | pramāṇabhūtaliṅgādigatasya
sādhāraṇarūpasyāpramāṇepi satvāt | “3atha kathaṃ nivartate naiva nivartate”
 prāptismaraṇaṃ kintu
“mithyājñānameva taditi pratyayāntaraṃ bhavati”
 smaraṇameva grahaṇavyavahārapravartakaṃ mithyājñānamucyate
evaṃ ca svarūpajñānameva bādhakaṃ bhavati | tasminnavagate tannibandhanavyavahāranivṛtteḥ iti kṛtā
bādhaviśuddhiḥ |

ahīno vā prakaraṇādgauṇaḥ | idamidānīṃ cintyate---kiṃ niyogasannidhirbalīyān
(1) Bh. P. 859 (2) ibid 859 (3) ibid 861

[p. r800]

uta viniyogasannidhiriti | vākyaprakaraṇayorvirodhe vākyaṃ balīya iti sthite jyotiṣṭoma-
prakaraṇapaṭhitadvādaśopasattāviṣaye kiṃ vākyaprakaraṇayorvirodho’sti nāstīti cintyate | tatsiddhayarthaṃ-
midaṃ cintyate---kiṃ niyogasya jyautiṣṭomikasya yassannidhiḥ sa balīyān kiṃ tena sannidhānena
jyautiṣṭomikaniyogārthatā dvādaśopasattāyāḥ pratīyatām, uta viniyogataḥ sannidhiḥ viniyogataḥ
padārthasaṃbandhaḥ tena yassannidhiḥ 1ahīnaniyogasya sa balīyān tenāhīnaniyogārthatā | ahīna-
śabdena hi dvādaśāhaparimitayāgo dvādaśarātrātmaka ucyate, ahīnaśabdasya tannāmadheyatvāt |
na punarahīnādhikāraviniyogaḥ | tena dvādaśopasattā ahīnasyeti yāgasaṃbandho’vagamyate |
tatsaṃbandhena ca tadadhikāraniyogasannidhiravagamyate | tadvaśena tatsaṃbandha iti | etaduktaṃ
bhavati---jyotiṣṭomādhikārāpūrvasannidhānāt tatsaṃbandhapurassarā prakaraṇena kiṃ somayāgāṅgatā uta
padārthānvayavaśena ahīnāpūrvagṛhītateti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī kāryārthatvāt viniyogasya
prakṛtakāryasambhave prakṛtakāryasaṃbandho yuktaḥ iti manyate | asyārthaḥ---sarvo hi viniyogaḥ
padārthaḥ saṃbandho’dhikāraniyogārthaḥ | tena pradhānatvāt prakṛtaniyogasaṃbandha eva nyāyyaḥ |
kathaṃ tarhi viniyogaḥ ? padārthasaṃbandhaḥ | uttaraṃ---kāryārthatvādviniyogasya tadanuguṇaḥ
pradhānabhūtakāryasaṃbandhānuguṇaḥ vyākhyāyate gauṇyā vṛttyā | anena somayāga evocyate |
evaṃ tarhi vākyaṃ prakaraṇāddurbalamityuktaṃ bhavati | pariharati---neti | kutaḥ ?
prakaraṇāviśeṣe (sannidhānāviśeṣe hi) balīyo vākyam | 2ekaprakaraṇisaṃbandhe hi sthite
yadi tasminneva prakaraṇe vākyena prakaraṇena ca viniyogaḥ tadā vākyaṃ balīyaḥ yathā sūktavāke |
yatra punaḥ kāryasannidhireva nāsti kiṃ tatra vākyaṃ kariṣyati | na cātrāhīnakāryasanni-

[p. r801]

dhirasti | tasmāt kāryānuguṇaiva padārthavyākhyā nyāyyā na padārthānuguṇeti yuktaṃ na
hīyatta ityahīna iti |

asaṃyogāttu mukhyasya ||

atra bhaṇyate---uktamasmābhiḥ padārthānvayassaṃbandhakāraṇaṃ padārthayoranvayo yaḥ saḥ
aidamarthyasaṃbandhasya kāraṇamityuktamasmāgniḥ | kāryato’pyanvaya eva gamyate | yāvaddhi padārthā-
nvayo nāvagamyate tāvat kāryānvayasyānirvāhaḥ | tena kāryato’pyanvayo’vagamyate | nacānva-
yāvagame pratītānvayatyāgo yuktaḥ | na hi dṛṣṭe hastini hastipadenānumānaṃ saṃbhavati |
tasmādapratipakṣameva padārthavyākhyānaṃ yuktam | nanu kārye pratipakṣe kathamapratipakṣatā tatrāha
---padārtha eva hi padārthapratipakṣe bhavati na punaḥ kāryam | anyathā hi yadi kāryamapi
pratipakṣaṃ syāt tadā arthavādapadānāmapi kartavyatābhidhānataiva syāt | sarveṣāmapyartha-
vādapadānāṃ kāryānvayitaiva syāt na pratītyanvayitāmātram | padārthānvayasaṃbandhavaśena hi teṣāṃ
stutyupayogitā | kāryasaṃbandhabalena tu sarveṣāmapyanuṣṭhānopadeśataiva syāt | kartavyatāśabdena
anuṣṭhānamucyate | tasmādahīnasyeti padārthānvayabalena kratvantarārthataiva nyāyyā |
(1) Bhashya P. 863

[p. r802]

nanu cātra tulyameva śrautatvaṃ, ekatra somayāge na hīyata ityahīnaḥ, anyatra ahīne ahnāṃ
samūha iti | ekatra naña्samāsaḥ anyatra taddhitaḥ “ahnaḥ khaḥ kratau”
 iti khaḥ | nanu ca
svaravirodhena nañ samāso’nupapannaḥ nañ‌samāseṃ hi ‘tatpuruṣe tulyārthatṛtīyāsaptabhyupamānā-
vyayadvitīyā kṛtyāḥ’
 iti pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaratvena samāsāntodāttatve bādhite ādyudātto’hīna-
śabdaḥ syāt madhyodāttaścāyamāmnāyate | taddhitapakṣe pratyayādyudāttatve madhyodāttatā
upapanneti taddhitānta evāyam | sa ca mukhyayā vṛttyā na jyotiṣṭome vartate
iti kathamubhayatra śrautatvamucyate | abhyupagamyāpyubhayatra śrautatvaṃ kṛtvācintā-
nyāyenāpi nyāyavyutpattiriti darśayitumidamuktam | tadāha-kimasmākamanayā cintayā ekatra
śrauto’nyatra gauṇa iti kṛtvā cintitam | na cedatra śrautagauṇatvaṃ saṃbhavati tadā udā-
haraṇāntaraṃ bhaviṣyati | yadvā rūḍhiṃ manyate bhāṣyakāraḥ | tena jyotiṣṭome yaugikatvāt
rūḍhiryogamapaharatīti nyāyāt rūḍhirevāyaṃ grahītavyaḥ | na ca somayāgeṣu mukhya iti siddha-
mekatra śrautatvamanyatra gauṇatvam | kutaḥ punaḥ bhāṣyakāro rūḍhiṃ manyate ityatrāha---satreṣva-
daśanāt prayogasya | nanvidamayuktaṃ paṅkajādiṣvapi rūḍhitvāpatteḥ te’pi hi padmavyati-
rekeṇānyatra paṅkajātepi na prayujyante | ucyate---yuktaṃ paṅkajādiṣu yaugikatvaṃ paṅkaśabdasya
jaśabdasya ca pṛthaka्prayogeṇa ka्lṛśaktitvāt | iha punarahīnaśabdasya na samūhe prayogo
dṛṣṭaḥ | tena ka्lṛptaśaktyanusāreṇa prayogānupapatteḥ śaktiḥ kalpyate | tatra samudāyasyaiva dvāda-
śāhādiṣu yajaticoditeṣu anavasthitakartṛkeṣu prayogadarśanāt śaktiḥ kalpyate | na punarahīna-
śabdasya yaugikatvam | yadi hi satrepi prayogo dṛśyeta tadā anekaśaktikalpanābhayāt ahīna-
śabdasyaiva śaktiḥ kalpyeta | na caitadasti | tasmāt samudāyaśaktyaivāyamahīnaśabdo vartata

[p. r803]

iti rūḍhirevāyam | kimarthaṃ tarhi taddhitavidhiḥ ? vyutpattimātraṃ taddhitavidhiḥ śunaḥ
śepādiṣu samāsavidhiriva | yathā’vibhaktāvayavatayaiva samudāyaśaktyā śunaśśepaśabde
vartamānepi samāsavidhiḥ “ṣaṣṭhyā vā’lugupasaṃkhyānaṃ śunaḥ śepapucchalāṅgūleṣvaluk”
 iti |
vyutpatteśca prayojanaṃ svaraviśeṣavidhiḥ | tasmādidamapyupapadyata evodāharaṇam |

dvibahutvayuktaṃ vā’codanāttasya | nanu ta pūrveṇaivādhikaraṇena gatamidam | na
hi pratītānvayatyāgaḥ kāryānurodhena yuktaḥ iti | pratītadvitvabahutvānvayatyāgaḥ prakṛta-
kāryānurodhena na yuktaḥ iti | samādhatte---satyaṃ kintvastyatrābhyadhikāśaṅkā | prakṛtepi
dvitvabahutvasaṃbhavāt | vākyaprakaraṇayoravirodha ityabhyadhikāśaṅkā | kathaṃ punaḥ prakṛtepi
saṃbhava ityatrāha---yathāśaktiprayoge nitye karmaṇi yajamānāntarapraveśamapi manyate pūrvapakṣa-
vādī patnyabhiprāyaṃ vā ekaśeṣābhidhānam | asyārthaḥ---nitye karmaṇyadhikṛtasya yathā-
śaktyanuṣṭhānam | tatra yadā eko yajamāno’śaktaḥ kṛtsnaprayogaṃ kartuṃ tadā yajamānāntareṇa
saha militvā kariṣyati | tatra caikakartṛtvaṃ kelamaṅgabhūtaṃ tyakṣyate | tasyāṃ cāvasthāyāṃ
jyotiṣṭomepyanekayajamānasaṃbhavaḥ | tathā patnīmapi gṛhītvā ekaśeṣābhidhānam---‘yajamānayoḥ’

‘yajamānebhyaḥ’
 iti kalpyate | tasmādanutkarṣaḥ pratipadvidhānasya |

na prakṛterekasaṃyogāt| atrocyate---yajamānāntarapraveśastāvanna ghaṭate | kutaḥ ?
yajamānasya hi yathā śaktuyādityucyate | tatra yanna saṃbhavati tasyaiva gauṇārthatā
tyāgo vā na punaryajamānāntaropādānena saṃprayogaḥ | asyārthaḥ---adhikārastāvadekasyaiva
jyotiṣṭome, 1ekakartṛtvāt viṣayasya adhikārabhede pratyadhikāraṃ kartṛbhedāt kartṛbhedabhinnāḥ
pratiyajamānaṃ bhinnā eva prayogāḥ | tatra yadi militvā ‘anekayajamānā yajeran ekatvāt
prayogasya ekasyaivādhikārasiddheḥ eka eva yajamānaḥ syāt nāneke | ato yathāśaktyanuṣṭhānepi
ekasyaivādhikārapūrvakakartṛtvaṃ yajamānasyocyate na punaryajamānayormilitayoḥ ityekayajamānake
prayoge yanna saṃbhavati tasya gauṇatā tyāgo vā na punaryajamānāntarapraveśaḥ | yadi paraṃ ekenānu-
ṣṭhānāśakteḥ kartravasthāyāṃ puruṣāntaramanupraveśyate, na ca tasya yajamānaśabdo vācakaḥ |
yajamānaśabdoddeśena ca pratipadviśeṣavidhānam | nanu ca yajamānoddeśena ca pratipadvidhāne
uddiśyamānaviśeṣaṇavivakṣānirākaraṇāt yajamānamātrasya ca prakṛte’pyadhikāre saṃbhavāt utkarṣo
nopapadyate | atra vadāmaḥ | dvayoḥ bahubhyaḥ iti coddeśyapadam | tacca yajamānaviṣayamiti
yajamānoddeśenetyucyate | tasmādupapannaḥ utkarṣaḥ | yadā cātra praveśyamāno yajamānaḥ tadā
na yajamānāntaropādānena prayogasaṃpat | kintu kartṛmātropādānena | sati caivaṃ na
yajamānadvitvaṃ bahutvaṃ vā saṃbhavati prakṛtau jyotiṣṭoma ityarthaḥ | yadapyuktaṃ patnyabhi-
prāyā dvayoryajamānayoḥ bahugya iti cābhidhānopapattiriti | tadapyayuktam | tasmānna
patnyabhiprāyaḥ itaretarayogo ghaṭate | yasmādekaśeṣe kevalāyāḥ striyastāvat grahaṇaṃ nāsti
(1) G. ekatvāt

[p. r805]

strīmātrasya kvacidapi adhikārābhāvāt | ekaśeṣe avaśyaṃbhāvī dvitīyopi pumānevāvagamyate |
ataḥ patnyabhiprāyo netaretarayogaḥ | kiṃ tarhi ? puṃdvayābhiprāyaḥ | nanu ca patnyabhiprāyo’pi itare-
tarayogo’styeva “kṣaume vasānau”
 iti tadvadatrāpi bhaviṣyatītyatrāha---ādhāne tu lakṣaṇayā, asaṃ
bhavāt śrautasya | asyārthaḥ---ādhāne lakṣaṇayā patnyabhiprāya itaretarayogaḥ śrautasya
puṃdvayābhiprāyeṇa itaretarayogasyāsaṃbhavāt prakṛte ādhāne puṃdvayābhāvāt | nanvatrāpi prakṛte
karmaṇi pumanekatvābhāvāt lakṣaṇā āśrīyatāmiti tanna, vākyāt pumanekatvapratītiḥ prakaraṇānna
śakyate bādhitum | ādhāne tu vākyata evādhānaśravaṇaṃ tacca prakṛtavācīti padārthāntarasya
pratipakṣatvāt yuktā lakṣaṇā | yuktyantaraṃ cāha - yathā patnyā saha itaretarayogo na ghaṭate
yāge tathā ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | sahabhāvena praviśantī patnī netaretarayogārhā | tenaiva ca
yajetetyekavacanāvirodha iti ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | yāgagrahaṇādyāge patnyā sahetaretarayogo na
saṃbhavati | abhidhānakriyāyāṃ tu saṃbhavatītyuktam | na ca bāhyakriyāpekṣe rājapurohitādi-
viṣaye itaretarayogasaṃbhave abhidhānāśrayo nyāyyaḥ | satriṇāmeva hi sa itaretarayogaḥ |
na patnīyajamānayoḥ | tasmāt dvitvabahutvābhāvāt atrāpi pūrvavat anvayānurodhena
prakaraṇabādhādutkarṣa iti |

jāghanī caikadeśatvāt | viniyogacintārūpatvāt adhyāyasaṅgatatvepi avāntara-
saṅgatyabhiprāyeṇa pṛcchati---kena prasaṅgenedamadhikaraṇamāyātam ? iti | uttaraṃ---anena
prasaṅgena idamāyātamiti | sthitaṃ tāvat vākyaṃ prakaraṇāt balīyaḥ sannidhāne

[p. r806]

cāsannidhāne ca kāryasya | atredaṃ sandihyate---kimayaṃ vākyārthaḥ uta ayaṃ iti vākyārtha eva
saṃśayaḥ | kiṃ jāghanyuddeśena patnīsaṃyājā vidhīyante uta viparītamiti | pūrvapakṣī
tāvat jāghanyuddeśena patnīsaṃyājavidhānaṃ manyate | kutaḥ ? evaṃ śrauto’rtho vihito
bhavati | saṃyājayantīti śrutyāvidhānaṃ, anyathā hi vākyārthavidhānaṃ syāt | jāghanī-
vidhānāt | nanu cāsminnapi pakṣe vākyārtha eva vihito bhavati | anyat karma asmin
dravye ucyate---jāghanyāṃ patnīsaṃyājā ucyanta ityarthaḥ | ucyate---anabhijño bhavān
vacanavyaktīnām | na brūmo vākyārthasya vidhisaṃbandho nāstīti vidheyodeśyabhāvena |
kintu na vidheyo vākyārthaḥ | kintu vākyārtho na vidhīyate | idameva vivṛṇoti
---etaduktaṃ bhavati---na sannikṛṣṭārthatyāgena viprakṛṣṭārthavidhānaṃ yuktamiti samānapadopāttaṃ
karma parityajya na padāntaropāttaviprakṛṣṭadravyavidhānaṃ yuktamiti | ayaṃ cāparo’smin pakṣe
lābho bhavati jāghanyā paśūpādānakalpanā na kṛtā bhavati | asmin hi pakṣe yatra jāghanī
paśuyāge prāptā tatraiva tatpratipattaye patnīsaṃyājavidhānamiti tatpratipattitvādaprayojakatvam |
patnīsaṃyājeṣu tu jāghanīvidhāne prakaraṇa eva niveśāt upādeyā patnīsaṃyājanirvṛtyarthā jāghanī |
tasmādatrāpyutkarṣaḥ |

[p. r807]

codanāvā | atrābhidhīyate | jāghanyevātra patnīsaṃyojeṣu vidhīyate | kutaḥ ?
yāgavidhāne hi patnyartho’paro vidhātavyo bhavati jāghanyā patnīḥ saṃyojayantīti | yadi
paśuyāge jāghanīpratipattyartho’yaṃ yāgo vidhīyate tadā patnyartho’paraḥ kartṛtvena vidheyaḥ | darśapūrṇa-
māsaprakaraṇe tu jāghanīvidhau prakṛtānāṃ patnīsaṃyājānāṃ patnīkartṛtvādanūdyate patnīriti |
itarasmin viśiṣṭavidhānāśrayaṇe vākyārthasya vidhānaṃ vidhigauravaṃ ceti jāghanyeva vidheyā
ityanutkarṣaḥ | codayati---nanu cātrāpi patnīsaṃyājāścodakena sannihita eva | tena patnī-
kartṛkāḥ prāptā evetyatrāpi pakṣe’nuvāda eva ghaṭiṣyate | pariharati---satyaṃ sannihitāḥ
vidhīyamānatvena tu prakaraṇāntare prayojanānyatvamiti karmāntarāpattiḥ | punaścodayati
---nanu cāsannidhānaṃ tatra karmāntaratve hetuḥ na vidhīyamānatā, sannidhānaṃ cātra, tena
karmāntaratvānupapattiḥ | yadi ca yāvatkarmasvarūpavidhānāt anyo’tra vidhiḥ na syāt
tadā abhyāsanyāyena bhaveti karmabhedaḥ | iha tu jāghanīsaṃbandhārthatvāt na karmasvarūpa-
vidhānārthatā | tadāha---na cānanyārthatvamatra jāghanīsaṃbandhārthatbādvāvadhasya | tasmānnāyaṃ
patnīvidhānanibandhano vākyabhedarūpaparihāra upapadyate rāddhāntinaḥ | ucyate |
anabhijño devānāṃ priyaḥ śāstrabhedasya | vidhānasannidhirhi vihitavidhāne bhedamavaruṇaddhi |
asyārthaḥ---yatra vihitaṃ vidhīyate tatra vidhānasannidhirena bhedabhavaruṇaddhi nivārayati |

[p. r808]

na padārthasannidhiḥ | ayamāśayaḥ---prakṛte vidhau punarvidhyantare śrutipramāṇābhāvasya pratyabhi-
jñāyamānatvāt na vidheyāntaraṃ śakyate kalpayitum | ṣadārthamātre tu sannihite pramāṇābhāvāsyā1-
pratijñāyamānatvāt vidheyāntaropasthāpakatvāt bhavatyeva śāstrāntaram | iha ca prākṛtā vidheyāḥ
yadyapi codakena sannihitāḥ tathāpi na vidheyatayā kintu padārthatayā | uddeśyatve hi bhavati
padārthasannidhirapyavagamahetuḥ padārthamātrāpekṣatvāduddeśyabhāvasya padārthamātramuddeśo’pekṣate na
pramāṇābhāvāt | tena padārthasvarūpamātreṇa sannihitasyoddeśyatvaṃ bhavati | navaprayājādivat
tasmāt karmāntaravidhānāt aprāptapatnyarthavidhāne yukto vākyārthabhedaparihāraḥ | tatra ca
etāvataiva pūrvapakṣasiddhāntayoḥ siddhatvāt kimarthaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa yatra saṃbandho vidhīyata ityādinā
saṃbandhavidhānārthaṃ prakāradvayamupanyastam | anyataratra anyatarasaṃbandhividhānaṃ saṃbandhasya ca
sākṣādvidhānamiti tadāha---yatpunaridaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa ubhayasaṃbandhavidhānaṃ ubhayasyānya-
taratra vidhānaṃ saṃbandhasya ca sākṣādvidhānamupanyastaṃ tatkimarthamidam | ucyate---nāntarīyaka2
sākṣādvidhānavivekapradarśanārtham | yatrānyatarasaṃbandhī aprāpto vidhīyate tatra nāntarīyakaṃ
saṃbandhavidhānam | yatra punassaṃbandhinau prāptau uddiśya svaśabdena saṃbandho vidhīyate tatra
svayameva saṃbandhavidhānamiti vivekaṃ darśayitumityarthaḥ | vivekapradarśanamidānīṃ kimarthaṃ ?
anyatarāprāptyartham | anyatarasyāprāptiṃ darśayitum | neha prakaraṇe ubhayaprāptirupapadyate tasmāda-
nyataradvidheyam | sā ca jāghanyeva vidheyā patnīsaṃyājānāṃ prāptatvāt | tasmādanutkarṣaḥ |
(1) G. sya pratya (2) R.&amp; G. nāntarīyakavidhiviveka

[p. r809]

yatpunaridaṃ na paśuṃ prayuṅkata iti pūrvapakṣe uktaṃ tadapi tulyaṃ asmadīyepi pakṣe
laukikyāḥ kevalaṃ jāghanyāḥ prāpteḥ saṃbhavāt 2paśuprayukterasaṃbhavāt iti |

santardanaṃ prakṛtau | atrāpyutkarṣāśaṅkā dīrghaśabdasyānanvayāt | ‘dhṛtyai’
ityartha-
vādānupapatteśca | yadi hi prakṛta eva somayāge niveśaḥ syāt tadā dīrghaśabdo’nanvitaḥ
syāt ‘dhṛtyai’
 ityarthavādaśca nopapadyate | dāraṇāśaṅkāyāṃ hi dhāraṇamāśāsitavyaṃ bhavati | na
ca tāvatyeva some dāraṇāśaṅkā | anutkarṣavādī tu latādairdhyeṇāpyanuvādaṃ manvānaḥ dīrghasya
vā somasya iti prakṛtāvevaitadvidhānamiti manyate | dīrghaśabdo’yaṃ yajamānaparatayā
latāparatayā cānvīyata iti prakaraṇānugrahāya prakṛtāveva niveśaḥ | arthavādastu na prakaraṇa-
saṃbandhaṃ bādhituṃ kṣamaḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu somāpekṣaivātra dīrghatā mantavyā | na latāpekṣeti
etaccopalakṣaṇamātraṃ; yajamāno dīrgha ityetadupalakṣyate | evaṃ dīrghaśabdaḥ somasamānādhikaraṇo
bhavati, pravṛttiviśeṣakaraśca | “ghṛtyai”
 iti ca nātirekādvinā arthavādopapattiriti |
somātirekeṇa vinā dāraṇāśaṅkābhāvāt nārthavāda upapadyate | tasmādadhikapradānāsu saṃsthāsu
niveśa ityuktavān | nanu cāsminnapi pakṣe prakaraṇādutkarṣa eva nityādhikārādutkarṣa eva
kāmyāsu saṃsthāsu niveśāt | saṃsthā api hi vikārāḥ iti saṃsthādhikaraṇe vakṣyate | pari-
(1) P. 871 (2) paśuprasakte

[p. r810]

harati---yāgābhiprāyo’yamutkarṣaḥ saṃsthāsvapyekatvādyāgasya | kāryāpekṣayā adhikāra-
niyogāpekṣayā tu utkarṣa evetyadoṣaḥ | yatpunaridamuktaṃ sīsena krīṇāti itivadvikalpa iti
bhāṣyamanubhāṣitam | dūṣaṇamāha---nanu ca tatra samuccayo na vikalpaḥ ‘daśabhireva krīṇāti’

iti | etacca dvādaśe uktam | uttaraṃ---vidhānavikalpamaṅgīkṛtyaitaddarśitam na prayoga---
vikalpam| asyārthaḥ---yadi sīsena krīṇātīti krayavidhiḥ vāsasetyādayo guṇavidhayaḥ
yadi vāsasetyeva krayavidhiḥ itare guṇavidhaya iti vidhāne vikalpaḥ prayoge samuccaya eva |
na nvekena vihite kathamanyena guṇavidhānaṃ, utpattiśiṣṭaguṇāvarodhena karmāntarāpatteḥ | naitadevam |
sannipatyopakārakāṇāṃ kāryaikyāvagame bhedābhāvāditi |

saṃkhyāyuktaṃ kratoḥ prakaraṇāt syāt | avāntarasaṅgatimāha---vākyārthasamavāyā-
pekṣiṇī prakaraṇādutkarṣacintā gatā | vākyārthasya yaḥ samavāyaḥ prakaraṇe samavetatā
tadapekṣiṇī prakaraṇādutkarṣacintā gatā, prakaraṇāntare vākyārthasya samavetatvāt | tatra
vākyārtha utkṛṣyata ityuktam | idānīmabhidhānavipratipattirvākya eva cintyate---kimayaṃ
prathamaśabdo jyotiṣṭoma eva vartate uta jyotiṣṭomaprathamaprayoga iti | asyārthaḥ---‘na
prathamayajñe pravṛñjjāt’
 iti prathamayajñe pravargyapratiṣedhaḥ pratīyate | tatra kiṃ prathamaśabdena
(1) P. 876

[p. r811]

darśapūrṇamāsādiyajñāntarāpekṣayā jyotiṣṭoma evocyate tataśca jyotiṣṭomasya sarveṣu prayogeṣu na
pravrañjitavyam, uta jyotiṣṭomasyaiva prathamaprayogaḥ prayogāntarāpekṣayā ucyate | tena
tasminneva na pravrañjitavyam | itareṣu prayogeṣu pravargyeṇa bhavitavyamiti | pūrvapakṣamāha
---“eva vāva prathamo yajño yajñānāṃ”
 iti śravaṇāt vispaṣṭaiva yajñārthavacanatā avagamyate |
asyārthaḥ---vispaṣṭaiva prathamaśabdasya jyotiṣṭomavacanatā’vagamyate yajñaśabdasāmānādhikaraṇyāt |
tasya ca prayojyavacanatvāt | bhavati ca jyotiṣṭomaḥ prathamo yajñaḥ ‘eṣa vāva prathamo yajño yajñānāṃ
yajjyotiṣṭoma”
 iti śravaṇāt | atassarvasminnapi jyotiṣṭome na pravrañjitavyam | atra
bhāṣyaṃ “evaṃ ca prakaraṇamanugrahīṣyate”
 iti | tatra kadācit bhrāntiśīlasya pravargyavidhyabhi-
prāyo’yaṃ prakaraṇānugrha ucyate | sa ca prathamaprayogaviṣayepi pratiṣedhe ghaṭata eva | tasmāda-
sādhako’yaṃ pūrvapakṣasyeti bhrāntiḥ syāt | tāmapanetumāha---pratiṣedhavidhyabhiprāyaṃ taditi |
yadi jyotiṣṭoma eva pratiṣedhaḥ syāt tadā prakaraṇamanugrahīṣyate sarvajyotiṣṭome pratiṣedha-
siddheḥ |

naimittikaṃ vā | atrābhidhīyate---satyamasmāt prayogādavagamyate | ‘eṣa vāva
prathamo yajño yajñānāṃ yajjyotiṣṭomaḥ’
 ityasmāt prayogāt satyaṃ jyotiṣṭoma eva prathamo’vagamyate
na tadīyaprayogaḥ | kintu “ya etenāniṣṭvā athānyena yajeta”
 iti prayogādapi
prāthamyamavagamyate | asyārthaḥ---yathā eṣa vāveti prayogāt jyotiṣṭomasyaiva prāthamyamavagamyate
(1) Bh. P. 876 (2) ibid 877

[p. r812]

tathā ya etenetyetadālocanayā prayogāt prathamo na punaḥ svarūpeṇeti | kathaṃ ya etenetye-
tadālocanayā prayogāt prāthamyaṃ ? uttaraṃ---kartṛsaṃbandhitayā śrūyamāṇatvāt ya iti
śabdopāttakratusaṃbandhitayā śravaṇāt | tathāpi kimityāha---kartṛśca prayogeṇa saṃbandho na
prayojyena | prayogāṅgaṃ hi kartā na prayojyasya dravyadevatāmātrasādhyatvāt | sarvatra
karaṇādyeva kriyānupañjakaṃ antaraṅgatvāt kartā tu karaṇādi vyāparanneva 1kriyāyāṃ samavaitīti
mantavyam | tena prayogayogācca jyotiṣṭomasya prāthamyam | 1prayoge eva hi prathamaśabdaḥ
śrautaḥ prayojye lākṣaṇikaḥ śrutilakṣaṇāviśaye ca śrutirādaraṇīyā | tena prathamaprayoga eva
pratiṣedhaḥ | yuktyantaraṃ cāha---prayogapare vākye eṣa vāva prathamo yajño yajñānāṃ iti
śrutam | asyārthaḥ---prayogaparametadvākyaṃ, jyotiṣṭomasya prathamaprayogaḥ kartavya iti vidhīyate
tatra ca ‘eṣa vāva’
 iti śrutaṃ prayogaparameva vijñāyate | tenāyamartho bhavati---eṣa vai prathamo yajña-
prayogāṇāṃ yajjyotiṣṭomaprayogaḥ, ya etena jyotiṣṭomena prayogeṇāniṣṭvā anyena prayogeṇa
yajeta kartasyaiva āpattiḥ antaḥ jyāniṃ jīyate jyāniṃ yāti vā pramīyate mriyate vā iti |
tena prayogasyaivedaṃ prāthamyaṃ na prayojyasya | tadāha---na punaḥ prayojyaniṣṭhā | kvacidasya
prathamaśabdasya prayogo vidyate jyotiṣṭomasyaiva | ataḥ prayogavacana evāyaṃ prathamaśabdaḥ |
tenāyamartho bhavati na prathamayajñaprayoge pravṛñjyāditi | nanu ca yajñaśabdaḥ prayojyavacanaḥ
sa kathaṃ prayoge ghaṭiṣyate tatrāha---yajñaśabdaḥ prayojyaviṣayatvāt prayogasya prayoge’pyupapadyata
eva | prayojya eva prayogo bhavati | tena prayojyaśabdena vyapadeśo nānupapannaḥ | tenaiṣa
(1) R. kriyayā

[p. r813]

prathamaprayojyaḥ yajñānāmityarthaḥ | evaṃ ca prakaraṇa eva pravargyavidhirupapanno bhavati |
asyārthaḥ---kauṣītakribrāhmaṇe jyotiṣṭomaprakaraṇe “purastādupasadāṃ pravargyeṇa pracaranti”
 iti
pravargyavidhiḥ śrūyate | tatra yadi jyotiṣṭomaprayogamātre pratiṣedho bhavet tato vikalpa---
parihārāya prakaraṇāntaraṃ nīyate | tataḥ prakaraṇabādhaḥ | atha prakaraṇāvirodhāya tatraiva
avatiṣṭhate tato vikalpaḥ syāt | asmanmate tu naimittikatvāt pratiṣedhasya na kaścidviśeṣa
iti |

pauṣṇaṃ peṣaṇaṃ vikṛtau | pauṣṇaṃ peṣaṇaṃ uttaravivakṣayetyuktam | anenādhikaraṇa-
prayojanamuktam |

tatsarvārthamaviśeṣāt | idamidānīṃ cintyate---kiṃ yāvatpauṣṇaṃ tatsarvaṃ prapiṣṭaṃ
kartavyaṃ uta carureveti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī viśeṣāgrahaṇāt sarvārthamiti manyate
carupaśupuroḍāśārthaṃ peṣaṇaṃ sarveṣāṃ pauṣṇatvāviśeṣāt | nanu ca puroḍāśe peṣaṇasya prāptatvāt
na śakyate vidhātum | uttaraṃ---na, pauṣṇatayā aprāpteḥ | asyārthaḥ---satyaṃ svarūpeṇa
(1) Bh. P. 880 (2) Bh. P. 881

[p. r814]

peṣaṇaṃ prāptaṃ pauṣṇatayā na prāptam | 1tacca nāma na vidheyaṃ yat sarvātmanā prāptam | yattu
kena ciccātmanā aprāptaṃ tadvidheyaṃ bhavatyeva tasmāt sarvārtham | nanu ca peṣaṇe kriyamāṇe
hṛdayādyakṛtivināśaḥ paśau | tataśca ākṛtināśaprasaṅgāt peṣaṇena na bhavitavyam |
uttaraṃ---bhavatu ākṛvināśaḥ peṣaṇavacanāt, kimiti vacanaṃ na kuryāt | sarvaṃ kuryādi-
tyarthaḥ | atrocyate---puroḍāśe tāvat peṣaṇasya prāptatvānna vidhiḥ | nanu pauṣṇatayā
’prāptirityuktam | uttaraṃ---kāryata eva prāptiriṣyate na saṃbandhitayā | asyārthaḥ
---kāryārthaṃ padārthānāṃ prāptiriṣyate na punaḥ saṃbandhitvārtham | iha ca puroḍāśe yat
prakṛtivatpeṣaṇaṃ prāpta tenaiva kāryaṃ kṛtamiti na punaḥ peṣaṇasya kāryamasti | tataśca kāryābhāve
sati peṣaṇāntarasyānuṣṭhānābhāvāt na punarvidhānamavakalpate, anuṣṭhānaparyantatvādvidheḥ iti |
anyathā arthavādapadānāmapi kāryārthataiva syādityuktam | yadi yasyāpi kāryaṃ nāsti tasyā-
pyanuṣṭheyatā, tadā arthavādapadānāmarthaḥ karaṇetikartavyatānanupraviṣṭo’pi kartavyaḥ syāt |
nanu caivaṃ sati prakaraṇādvākyaṃ dubalamityuktam | asyārthaḥ---vākyāt puroḍāśepi
peṣaṇe prāpte yadi kāryābhāvāt puroḍāśepi peṣaṇaṃ na viniyujyate tadā prakaraṇādvākyaṃ
dubalamityuktaṃ bhavati | pratītānvayatyāgenāpi yadyanvayaḥ kalpyate tadā dvādaśopasattā
ahīnasyetyatrāpi kimityanvayo na kalpyata iti bhāvaḥ | uttaraṃ---kārye sati anvayakalpa-
nā, ato balavattā uktā vākyasya | yatra hi vākyāvagate’nvaye kāryalābhaḥ tatra vākyaṃ
(1) G. tannāmna vidheyam

[p. r815]

balīyaḥ | kāryaśū्nye’nvaye na vākyāvagatānvayasvīkāraḥ | kiṃ tarhi anvayāntarakalpaneti |
tasmāt kāryābhāvāta puroḍāśe peṣaṇasya vidhānaṃ na saṃbhavati | paśau tarhi saṃbhavati
na hi, tatrāpi sāpekṣatvaprasaṅgāt śāstrasya ākṛtibādhasāpekṣaṃ hi paśau vidhānam |
na ca nirapekṣe caruviṣaye saṃbhavati sāpekṣāśrayaṇaṃ yuktamiti uktaṃ śabdavidbhiḥ |
tasmāccarāveva peṣaṇaṃ kartavyamiti śabdāt pratīmaḥ | atra bhāṣyakāreṇa “yadyapi tadā-
kṛtiḥ pratīyeta tathāpi utsādanadeśaṃ prati muhyeyuḥ”
 ityuktam | tadanupapannam | piṣṭe hṛdayādau
punastadākṛtirna śakyate kartuṃ pāriṇāmikatvāttasyā, ityāśaṅkāmapanetumāha---yatpunaridamuktaṃ
yadyapītatyādīnā bhāṣyeṇa tatraivamapi sāpekṣatvaṃ naiva śakyate parihartumityabhiprāyaḥ |
asyārthaḥ---satyapyākṛtisapādane pūrvārdhādikoddharaṇadeśabādhasāpekṣatvaṃ na nivartayituṃ śakyata
ityuktam | anvārohamātraṃ caitat | paramārthatastu ākṛtiḥ peṣaṇe kṛte naśyatyeveti
pūrvamevokto hetirityarthaḥ | nanu carāvapi carutaiva hīyeta viśadasiddhaudane caruśabda-
prayogāt | na ca piṣṭamaya odano bhavati | uttaraṃ---na carutā hīyeta piṣṭakacarāvapi
paktisāmānyāditi | paktivacanatvāccaruśabdasyetyevamabhihitaḥ peṣaṇepi sati
caruśabdaprayogaḥ | upasaṃharati---tasmāccarāveva peṣaṇamiti siddham |

(1) Bh. P. 882

[p. r816]

ekasminnekasaṃyogāt | nanu caikadevatāke vihito dharmo na dvidevatāke saṃbhavati
caturdhākaraṇavat indrapītamantravacca tatrāha---atra bhāṣyakāreṇa indrapītāgneyaśabdābhyāṃ viśeṣo
darśitaḥ pūṣā prapiṣṭabhāgo bhavatīti yathā adantako hīti hetuvacanaṃ somāpauṣṇe nema-
piṣṭaścaruḥ iti | asyārthaḥ---prapiṣṭaṃ bhajatīti prapiṣṭabhāk pūṣocyate sa ca dvidevatākepi
yadi peṣaṇaṃ syāt tadā prapiṣṭabhāka्bhavati iti | anyathā na prapiṣṭabhāgo bhavet tathā
adantako hīti hetuvacanaṃ pūṣanimittakaṃ peṣaṇaṃ darśayati somāpauṣṇo nemapiṣṭaścaruḥ ityatra nemapiṣṭatā
’nūdyate | sā ca pūṣamātranimitte peṣaṇe’vakalpate somāpauṣṇacaruvidhau nemapiṣṭa ityanuvāda
eva yuktaḥ; nemapiṣṭavidhāne hi somāpauṣṇasya lakṣaṇāt taduddeśena carurityavivakṣitaṃ syāt
ityevamādyadhikāśaṅkānirākaraṇārthamidamadhikaraṇam | atrocyate---kathamāsāmāśaṅkānāṃ
nirākaraṇaṃ ? uttaraṃ---kāryaprayuktatvāddharmāṇāmityukto hetuḥ | asyārthaḥ---kārya-
prayuktā dharmāḥ kārye asaṃbhavantaḥ tatsaṃbaddhe yāge’vakalpante | tataśca pūṣā prapiṣṭabhāga iti
vacanāt pūṣalakṣite yāge devatāvidhiravasīyate | devatātvena hi puṣā yāgaṃ lakṣayituṃ śaknoti
saṃbandhasya lakṣakatvāt | na ca dvidevatāke pūṣṇo devatābhāvassaṃbhavati | tena tatra
dharmavidhiranyāyyaḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ kāryeṇa saṃbandho devatāpadayuktānāṃ bhavati | uttaraṃ
(1) P. 883

[p. r817]

---niṣprayojanatvāddevatāyāḥ | nanvevaṃ yāgepi saṃbandho na syādityāha---kāryārthatvācca
yāgasya kāryaprayuktaṃ pauṣṇa lakṣaṇayopapadyate devatayā yāgo lakṣyate | devatā cedupalakṣaṇam na
dvidevatāke pūṣā lakṣayituṃ kṣamaḥ | adevatā hi sa pūṣā tatra dvidevatāke karmaṇi |
adantakatvādīnāṃ tu hetuvannigadatvenārthavādatvamuktam | arthavādāśca guṇavādena vyākhyātāḥ
nemapiṣṭasya ca nyāyāpetatvāt nyāyate’prāptatvāt dvidevatāke peṣaṇasya viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ
bhaviṣyati | tasmādekadevatākeṣu caruṣu peṣaṇamiti siddham |

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

tṛtoyādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ

nivītam | kathaṃ punaḥ vidhinātvekavākyatvāt stutyarthena vidhināmiti
sthite’rthavādatve punarvidhānāśaṅkā | ekavākyatve hi na vidhidvayamavakalpata iti na
nivītatvādividhisambhavaḥ | pākṣikīṃ prāptiṃ manvāno vidhiṃ manyata iti cet | artha-
vādo hi anuvādatayā | na ca nivītādi nityaprāptam | ataḥ pakṣe’nuvādasyāpyanupapatteḥ
vidhirāśrayaṇīya iti | tadapyayuktam | ‘prāyādi’
tyanena parihṛtatvāt | prāyikīmapi
prāptimāśrityārthavādo bhavatītyuktaṃ prāyāt ityanena sūtreṇa | yathā “anūtavādinīvāk iti,
prācīnāvītamiti vidhānaṃ aprāptatvāt | tatsaṃyogādidamapi vidhīyata iti cet ‘prācīnāvītaṃ
pitṛṇāṃ”
 iti pakṣe’pi na prāptam | atastadvidheyam | taccedvidhīyate vākyaṃ bhinnaṃ tāvadāśritam
tataśca nivītamapi vidhīyata iti | tadayuktam---tasyāpi vacanāntareṇa prāptatvāt upapadyata
evārthavādaḥ | evamākṣipte samarthayate---prakaraṇāṅgatayā’prāptiṃ manvānena idamadhikaraṇa---
mārabdham | prakaraṇasyāṅganiyogasya aṅgatayā pakṣe’pi prāptyabhāvāt prāyāt ityanena
nyāyenārthavādatvānupapatteḥ nivītaṃ vidhīyata iti manvānenedamadhikaraṇamārabdham | rāddhāntamāha
---vidhānepi prakaraṇāṅgatā na saṃbhavatīti sthitamarthavādatvam | yadyapi vidhīyate tathāpi
(1)P. 889

[p. r819]

manuṣyāṇāmiti śravaṇāt svatantrādhikārāpatteḥ prakaraṇāṅgatā na saṃbhavati | kintu puruṣadharmatā
puruṣadharmaścet prāyādityupapadyata evārthavādaḥ | nanu cātithyādāvaprāptirvidyata iti yuktaṃ
vidhānaṃ manuṣyāṇāmiti śravaṇe satyapi manuṣyapradhāne ātithye karmaṇi ekāntato’prāptatvāt
kimiti vidhānaṃ na saṃbhavatīti | na | tadanvayānavagamāt | ātithyakarmānvayo nāvagamyate |
api tu manuṣyamātrānvaya eva | sa ca prāyādibhiḥ arthavādātayopapadyata iti siddhamanuvādatvam |
tadāha---manuṣyamātrasaṃbandhitāyāṃ tu prāyādibhirupapadyata ityarthavādataiveti siddham |

digvibhāgaḥ | paruṣiditam | tadvadityatideśaḥ kimabhyadhikaṃ paśyataḥ ?
uttaraṃ---anyathā hyupavīte prāptiḥ anyathā ca dikṣu anyathā ca paruṣidinādiṣu iti viśeṣa-
pratipādanārtho’yamatideśaḥ | pūrvapakṣasiddhāntau tāveva | kintvarthavādavarṇanāya prāptirva-
ktavyā | sā ca kvacana kathaṃ cidityadhikaraṇabhedaḥ |

(1) P. 898-899

[p. r820]

akarmakratusaṃyuktam | uktaṃ nivītādhikaraṇena kratudharmatayā nivītādi pratīyate
manuṣyādiśravaṇāditi prāyeṇa prāptatvādanuvāda iti | idānīṃ nānṛta vadet ityatrāpi
tadvadevānuvādaṃ manvānasya pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | anenāvasarasaṅgatiṃ pū्rvapakṣaṃ cāha | arthastu
nivītaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ iti śruteḥ na kratudharmatayā nivītādi pratīyate | api tu puruṣadharmatayā
puruṣadharmaścet prāyeṇa puruṣārthatayā nivītādiprāpteḥ anuvāda iti nivītādhikaraṇe uktam |
idānīṃ nānṛtaṃ vadeta iti pratiṣedhasyānuvādaṃ manvānasya pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | codayatiḥ---nanu ca tatra
manuṣyaśabdaśravaṇāt puruṣamātrasaṃbandhāvagateḥ kratudharmatayā saṃbandhānavagateśca yukto’nuvādaḥ
prāyikīṃ ca prāptimāśrityārthavādaḥ | iha tu puruṣaśabdāśravaṇāt na puruṣārthatā’vagamyate | api
tu prakaraṇāt kratvarthataivāvagamyate | na ca kratusaṃbandhitayā prāptirasti iti nānuvādo ghaṭate | pari-
harati---atrāpi pūrvaprāptapratiṣedhānuvādāt gamyata eva pumān yasyāsau pratiṣedhaḥ | atrāpi
‘nānṛtaṃ vadet iti | yasyāsau smārtaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ sa pumān adhikāritayā’vagamyata eva | kutaḥ ?
pūrvaprāptasya pratiṣedhasyānuvādāt | tatra ca puruṣasyādhikārasmaraṇāt | nanu nāyaṃ pratiṣedhānuvādo
ghaṭate | tatra pratyavāyaparihārārthino’vikārāt | iha ca prakaraṇāṅgatayā niradhikāratvāditi tatrāha
---sarvatra pratiṣedhaśāstre hi pratiṣidhyamānakriyākarturevādhikāraḥ iti | asyārthaḥ
