---na smārte pratiṣedhe pratiṣidhyamānakriyānuṣṭhānanibandhanapratyavāyaparihārārthino’dhikāraḥ
pramāṇābhāvāt | na hi niṣiddhā kriyā pratyavāyaṃ janayatīti kiñcana pramāṇamasti | nanu ca
pratiṣedhaśāstrameva pramāṇaṃ, kathaṃ tatpramāṇaṃ ? pratyavāyaṃ vinā nivṛttyanupapatteriti cenna;
1adhikārāvagamana eva nivṛtterupapannatvāt | nanvadhikāro na śrayate| satyamarthāt bhaviṣyati
dravyārjananiyamavat | yo hi yatra pravṛttaḥ saḥ tataḥ nivartayitavyaḥ, nānyaḥ ityarthādeva
pravṛttimānadhikārī labdha iti sādhūktaṃ---sarvatra pratiṣedhaśāstre pratiṣidhyamānakriyākarturevā-
dhikāra iti | ihāpi ca pratiṣidhyamānakriyākartuḥ anṛtavadanapratiṣedhaḥ anyasya pratiṣedhānu-
papatterityarthaḥ | ataḥ pūrvaprāptasyaiva pratiṣedhasyāyamanuvādaḥ | nanu ca kratvarthatayaivānṛta-
vadanaprāptiriti pratiṣedho’pi tadartha eva yuktaḥ | na hi anyārthe pravṛtto’nyārthe niyoktuṃ
śakyata iti tatrāha---vidyate ca prāptirarthataḥ | anṛtavadanasya arthataḥ prāptirvidyata
eva | kratvarthatayāpi puruṣasya prāptimātratayā ca puruṣasya pratiṣedhopapatteḥ na
prakaraṇārthatā | yadi ca kratvarthāyāmeva prāptau pratiṣedha upapadyate na puruṣārthāyāṃ tadā
kratvarthaprāptipratiṣedhaḥ2 kratvarthaḥ syāt | na caitadasti prāptimātrādapi pratiṣedhopapatteḥ |
nanu cāyamanuvādaḥ kasya vidheḥ śeṣaḥ ? ayamāśayaḥ | vidhiparatayā prāmāṇyamuktam |
tatra vidhyuddeśasya kāryānvayitayā vidhiparatvam | arthavādapadānāṃ tu stutiparatayā pratītyanvayi-
tayā | anuvādastu na sākṣādvidhiparaḥ | teta vaktavyametat kasya vidheḥ śeṣa iti
anyathā vidhiparatvābhāvāt | uttaraṃ---na kasyaciditi brūmaḥ | vidhyantareṇaikavākyatvā-
bhāvāt na kasyacidapi vidherayamanuvādaḥ śeṣaḥ | yadi vidhyantaraṃ nānupraviśati nivītavat
tarhi kathamanuvādaḥ | uttaraṃ---prātiranuvādatve hetuḥ nārthavādatā | yadi hi arthavādatā

anuvādatve hetuḥ syāt tadā vidhyantarānupraveśaṃ vinā’rthavādānupapatteḥ anuvādo vidhyantaraśeṣaḥ
syāt | kintu prāptiranuvādatve hetuḥ nārthavādatā | yadyayaṃ prāptārtha eva kimiti tarhi
nivītavadevārthavādo na bhavati | tatrāha---ekavākyatvena hi arthavādo bhavati na prāpti-
mapekṣate, yathā ‘jārtilayavāgvā vā juhuyāt’
 iti | jartilayavāguhomo hi yadyapi na prāptaḥ
tathāpi ‘payasāgnihotraṃ juhoti’
 ityanenaikavākyatvāt arthavādatvena vyākhyāyate | tena
anuvādaḥ prāptimātrāpekṣaḥ | arthavādastu ekavākyatāmātrāpekṣa iti vivekaḥ | anṛdyamāna-

nanu cedaṃ śrutivākyaṃ tasyāḥ smṛtermūlaṃ bhaviṣyati | yadi śrutivākyasya smṛtivākyasya
ca samānārthatvenānuvādo’yamucyate tarhi tasyāḥ smṛteḥ mūlamapekṣamāṇāyāḥ idameva śrutivākyaṃ
mūlaṃ bhaviṣyati iti kathamanuvādatā | uttaraṃ---na bhavitumarhati atra hi śruyamāṇasyārthasya
prakaraṇārthatāvasīyate na puruṣārthatvam | anuvādapakṣe punaḥ puruṣārthatayaiva prāpteḥ siddhā
puruṣārthatā bhavati | asyārthaḥ---yadyetadvākyaṃ mūlaṃ bhavet tadā anṛtavadanapravṛttapuruṣa-

smṛteśca puruṣārthatā bhavatīti na tasyāḥ pratiṣedhavidhirmūlatāṃ gantumarhatīti | anuvāde tu puruṣa-

kratvarthatayaivāvagamyamānasya kathaṃ puruṣārthatayā’nuvādo yuktaḥ kalpayitum | asyārthaḥ
---tvayā etaduktaṃ yadi mūlabhūtamidaṃ bhavet tataḥ kratvarthaṃ bhavatīti | evaṃ ca vadatā

ntaratve ca nānuvādaḥ śakyate vaktum | asyārthaḥ---svatastāvadidaṃ vākyaṃ prakaraṇāṅgabhūta-

anṛtavadanakartā cādhikārī, kartṛvācitāśaṅkā cākhyātānāṃ pāṇiniprabhṛtīnāṃ yathāśrutavacana-
bodhananibandhanā | nanvevaṃ tarhi prayājādīnāmapyaṅgatā na syāt | teṣvapi kartṛśravaṇāt

[p. r825]

svatantrādhikāraprasakterityatrāha---niyogābhedāttu prayājādīnāmaṅgatvamavagamyate | sarveṣā-
mekaphalānubandhatvāt | asyārthaḥ---prayājādiṣu kartaiva kevalaṃ śrūyate na tadviśeṣaṇam |
prayājādaya eva yadi kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ na tarhi viṣayaḥ | atha viṣayaḥ na tarhi kartā viśeṣyate |
tataśca kathamaviśiṣṭo’dhikārībhavet ? tasmātteṣāṃ pṛthagadhikāraniyogābhāvāt ekaniyogānupraveśāt
aṅgatvasiddhiḥ | saṃskārakarmaṇāmapi kratvarthasya saṃskāryatayā’vagateḥ yuktaivāṅgatā |
teṣvapi viṣayaniyojyaviśeṣānvayānavagateḥ na svatantrādhikārāpattiḥ | saṃskāryadravyādidvāreṇa
kratuśeṣatvāvirodhaḥ | pratiṣedhe punaḥ pratiśedhyasya akratvarthatvāt ākhyātena ca
karturabhidhānāt karturevādhikāraḥ pratīyate | asyārthaḥ---yadyapi kila nānūtaṃ vadedityanṛta-
vadanamātraṃ śabdādavagamyate tathāpi yadi vākye’dhikāro na śrūyate tadā prakaraṇagatamanṛtavadanaṃ
pratīyate | yadā punaḥ svavākya evādhikārī viṣayaśca labdhaḥ tadā anṛtavadanamapi svatantrameva
pratīyate | tenākhyātena karturabhidhānāt pratiṣedhasya cākratvarthatvāt karturevādhikāraḥ
adhikārāntarānupraveśe kāraṇābhāvāt | svavākye cādhikāraśravaṇāt |

evaṃ tāvat vidhisyaṃ puruṣārthaśceti darśitam | saṃprati abhyupagamyāpi kratvarthānṛtavadana-
pratītiṃ anuvādo’yamiti darśayati---na ca tatsaṃbandhāvagatiḥ kratvarthasaṃbandhānṛtavadanāvagatiḥ
svatantrādhikārepi viruddhā | kratorapi puruṣārthatvāt upanayanottarakālabhāvitvācca tadartha-
pravṛtteḥ siddho’nuvāda ityuktam | etaduktaṃ bhavati---kratvarthānṛtavadanapratiṣedho’pi
svatantrādhikārapuruṣārthasarvānṛtavadanapratiṣedhasyaiva smārtasyānuvāda iti |

[p. r826]

atrocyate---syādetadevaṃ yadyākhyātānāṃ kartā’bhidheyaḥ syāt na tvākhyātānāṃ
kartrabhidhānaśaktiḥ śakyate kalpayitum | kriyābhidhānādeva sarvakārakavagateḥ |
asyārthaḥ---tatra hi śabdasya śaktiḥ kalpyate yasya nānyato’vagatiḥ | sarvāṇi
ca kārakāṇi kriyayaivākṣipyante tairvinā kriyānupapatteḥ | kathaṃ tarhi pāṇineḥ sūtraṃ
laḥ karmaṇi ca bhāve cākarmakebhya iti ? | tathā kathamiyaṃ vṛttiḥ cakārāt
kartariceti | evaṃ vadatoḥ sūtravṛttikārayoḥ kārakābhidhāyino lakārā iti bhāvo
lakṣyate | pariharati---saṅkhyāvidhānārthametaditi darśitam | karmasaṅkhyā kartṛsaṅkhyā ca
vācyatvena vidhīyete na kartā karma cetyarthaḥ1 | karmakartṛbhyāṃ tu tatsaṅkhyā lakṣyate | kathaṃ tarhi
“karmavācini sārvadhātuke yak”
 ‘kartari śap’
 iti vṛttikārāḥ pathanti tatrāha---saṅkhyā-
viśeṣavidhānāśrayaṃ ca yak śab iti ca | atrāpi karmakartṛsaṅkhyāviśeṣābhidhānamāśritya
yak śap iti cānuśāsanam | tenātrāpi na kārakābhidhānamākhyātānāmiti | yadi
kriyākṣiptakarmakartṛsaṅkhyā’bhidhīyate tadā---anyakārakasaṅkhyābhidhānaṃ kimiti na bhavati ?
tulye gamyamānatve | karaṇādisaṅkhyāpi kimiti nākhyātenocyata ityarthaḥ | pariharati---na
lokaḥ paryanuyoktuṃ śakyate | kasmādityāha---durjayo hi mahājanaḥ | asyārthaḥ---kiṃ
vaye kurmaḥ karmakartṛsaṅkhyāmeva loko yadi vadet na karaṇādisaṅkhyām | nanu mānāma loko
vadatu pāṇiniḥ kimiti na smarati tatrāha---laukikaprayogamūlaṃ cedaṃ śāstraṃ bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ
(1) G. karmakartṛsaṅkhyā viśeṣavidhānārthaṃ vācyatvena bidhīyate kartetyarthaḥ

[p. r827]

kasmādityāha---prayuktasya hi niyamamācaṣṭe nāprayuktasyābhidhānam | tasmādaparyanuyo-
go’yam | upasaṃharati---ataḥ kratvarthapadārthamātrapravṛttyāśrayamevedaṃ pratiṣedhaśāstram |
kratvarthatayā padārthamātre anṛtavadane’dhikāravidhivākyārthasaṃbandhena yā pravṛttiḥ tadāśrayamevedaṃ pratiṣedha-
śāstram | nanu ca pratiṣedhyagatā pravṛttiḥ na kratvarthesyuktam | acoditatvāt | na
hi kratvarthatayā’nṛtavadanaṃ coditaṃ; acoditaṃ ca nāṅgamiti nānṛtavadane kratvarthā pravṛttiḥ |
uttaraṃ---naiva tādarthyena coditatvaṃ saṃbandhahetuḥ upakārakatayāpi saṃbandhitā upapadyata eva |
yadyapi tādarthyena codanā nāsti tathāpyupakārakatayā saṃbandho’nṛtavadanasya kratunā sahāstyeva |
saṃbandhini ca vidhiḥ kratvartho bhavatītyuktaṃ payo vrataṃ brāhmaṇasyetvevamādiṣu | yadyapi
bhojanaṃ kratvarthatayā na vihitaṃ tathāpi tasya kratorupakārakatvena tadāśritaṃ payo bhavatyeva kratvarthaṃ
evaṃ upakārakānṛtavadanapratiṣedho’pi kratvartho bhaviṣyatīti | nanu copakārakaviṣayatayā
pratiṣedhasya kratvarthapuruṣārthayorna kaścidviśeṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ---yadi tāvadaṅgabhūtamanṛtavadanaṃ
bhavet tadā puruṣārthena pratiṣedhena pratiṣeda्dhumaśakyatvāt prayojanavāneva kratvarthaḥ pratiṣedho bhavet
vikalpārthaḥ | yadā arthaprāptasya pratiṣedhaḥ tadā puruṣārthena pratiṣedhena pratiṣedhasiddheḥ kiṃ
kratvarthena pratiṣedheneti | uttaraṃ---yathāsti viśeṣaḥ tathoktam | kratvarthe pratiṣedhe tadati-
krame katorvaiguṇyāt tannivṛttaye śrautaṃ prāyaścittam | puruṣārthe ca pratiṣedhe tadatikrame puruṣasya
vaiguṇyāt tadapanayāya smārtaṃ prāyaścittamiti viśeṣaḥ |

[p. r828]

ahīnavatpuruṣadharmastadarthatvāt | kaḥ punaḥ nivīsaṃ manuṣyāṇāmityanena viśeṣaḥ
jañjabhyamānasyeti ? anena pūrvapakṣamākṣipati | ‘jañjabhyamāno brūyānmayidakṣakratū’
 iti
yadyapi jañjabhyamānasaṃbandhitayā mantravacanaṃ śrūyate tathāpi nivītaṃ manuṣyāṇāmitivat kimiti
na prakaraṇa eva niviśate nivītamapi | nivītaṃ manuṣyāṇāmiti manuṣyasaṃbandhitayā śrūyate
atha ca prakaraṇa eva niviśate tathedamapi kasmānna bhavatītyarthaḥ | ayamasti viśeṣaḥ
nārthavādāt parihāraḥ saṃbhavati avākyaśeṣatvāta | yathā ‘nivītaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ’
 iti
upavyayata ityasya vākyaśeṣatvādarthavādatayā prakaraṇa evānvīyate na tathā jañjabhyamāno brūyā-
dityasyānvayaḥ vidhyantareṇaikavākyatvabhāvāt | nanu ca vākyaśeṣatvābhāvāt mābhūdarthavādaḥ
anuvādastu bhavatu pūrvādhikaraṇapūrvapakṣe nānṛtaṃ vadeditivat tatrāha--nāpyanuvādo nānṛtaṃ vadedi-
tivat jañjabhyamānasaṃbandhitayā mantravacanasya anyato’prāpteḥ | nāpyarthaprāptasya viṣayatva-
saṃbhavaḥ nānṛtaṃ vadet itivat | yadi kratāvevārthaprāptā jañjabhyamānatā mantravidherviṣayo
bhavet yathā nānṛtaṃ vadedityasyārthaprāptamanutavadanaṃ tadā kratusaṃbandhijṛmbhaṇamukhena kratusaṃbandhāvagateḥ
kratvartho mantravidhiḥ syāt | na caitadasti | kutaḥ ? na hi jañjabhyamānatā kadācidapi
kratvarthatayā prāpnoti anṛtavadanavat | tasmāt jañjabhyamānasya mantravidhānamidaṃ na prakaraṇe
’vasthāpayituṃ śakyate | svavākya evānupādeyajṛmbhaṇaviśiṣṭasyādhikāriṇo mantravacanasya
(1) P. 944

[p. r829]

viṣayasya ca lābhe prakaraṇānupraveśe kāraṇābhāvāt | nanu caivaṃ sati phalakalpanā syāt |
adhikārāntarakalpanā syādityarthaḥ | bhavatu vacanāt, ko doṣaḥ ? vācanike’dhikārāntare
doṣa iti | ato vākyāt prakaraṇabādhaḥ siddhaḥ yathā dvādaśopasattā ahīnasya iti |
atrocyate na phalakalpanāmarhatyetadvākyam | idamatrākūtam | jañjabhyamānasambandhitayā
mantravacanaṃ pratīyate | tatra kimidamadhikārāntaraṃ jañjabhyamānaśabdaścādhikāriviśeṣasamarpako
bhavatu uta prakṛta eva kartā jañjabhyamānaśabdena na nirdiśyatāṃ tasya jṛmbhaṇanimittotpannavyalau-
kapraśamanadvāreṇa mantravacanaṃ saṃskārakamiti saṃśaye klṛpte prakṛte kārye sati na kalpanopapadyata
iti nādhikārāntarakalpanā nyāyyeti jañjabhyamānaḥ saṃskāryatayaivānvayaṃ yātīti | tadida-
mākūtaṃ vivarītukāmaḥ pṛcchati---kutaḥ | uttaraṃ---vākyaṃ hi padārthasaṃbandhamātre prabhavati
na kārye | vākyato hi padārthānāṃ saṃbandhāvagatireva, nāsti jañjabhyamānasaṃyogaḥ saṃskārya-
tayāpi | kartṛbhāvenaiva kartṛbhūta evānena mantravacanena saṃskriyate | kutastarhi kāryāvagama
ityatrāha---kāryaṃ hi niyogasamadhigamyameva | sarvaṃ hi niyogārthaṃ tena niyogārthānumataṃ
yat tat kāryam | ata eva ca saktvādiṣu vibhaktivyatyayaḥ | bhavatvevaṃ niyogataḥ kāryam
tathāpi kimityāha---prakṛtaścāyaṃ dārśapūrṇamāsiko niyogaḥ | tena prakṛtena grahaṇe sati
tada्grahaṇānuguṇaṃ kāryaṃ kalpayitavyamiti prakṛtakartṛsaṃskāraka evāyam | yadi prakṛtenāpi
niyogena na gṛhyeta tadā na padārthamātrasaṃbandhāvasāyyeva vākyaṃ bhavet kintu kāryāvasāyyeva |
sarvamevedaṃ viniyogakāraṇam | nanu bhavatu prakṛto niyogaḥ tathāpi sa eva kāryamiti
nāsti pramāṇaṃ, yena tadvalena tadanuguṇaṃ kāryaṃ kalpyeta ityata āha---na ca kāryāntarakalpanā

[p. r830]

klṛpte sati saṃbhavati iti prakaraṇa eva niveśaḥ | nanu ca jañjabhyamānatā na kratvartha-
tayā’nṛtavadanavat saṃbhavatītyuktam | tena kathaṃ kratvanupraveśaḥ | uttaraṃ---mābhūt
jañjabhyamānatā kratvarthā | kartustu kratvarthatā prakaraṇasaṃbandhaheturbhaviṣyati | tasmāt kratoḥ
kartuḥ dharmo’yamiti siddham | ahīne punaḥ kāryatvāt yuktaḥ utkarṣaḥ | ahīne
tatsaṃbandhitvena yo’dhikāraniyogassaṃbandhī pratīyate sa kāryabhūta iti svavākye kāryāvagamādyukta
utkarṣaḥ dvādaśoṣasattāyāḥ | iha tu jañjabhyamānamātrasambandhena na kāryasaṃbandho’vagamyata iti
kalpye kārye klṛptāśrayaṇaṃ nyāyyamiti viśeṣaḥ |

śaṃyau ca sarvaparidānāt | ‘na brāhmaṇāyāvaguret’
 iti prakaraṇa eva pratiṣedho
niviśate prakaraṇādhītatvāt, arthācca kratusaṃbandhitayā aprāpteranṛtavadanavaditi pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
rāddhāntamāha--śaṃyau punaḥ śatena yātayāditi phalaśruterna prakaraṇasaṃbandhakalpanā nyāyyā
svavākyasthā hi kāryāvagatirbalīyasī | yadi svavākye phalaṃ nāvagamyeta tadā prakṛtā-
vikārānupraveśenānuṣṭhānasiddheḥ kāryāntaraṃ na kalpyeta, aṅgameva syāt | iha tu svavākye
phalamavagamyate śatayātanādiparihāraḥ | svavākyasthā hi kāryāvagatiḥ śreyasī, antaraṅgatvāt
(1) P. 949

[p. r831]

tasmāt puruṣamātradharmo’yaṃ ‘na brāhmaṇāyāvaguret’
 iti | nanu ca jañjabhyamānepi‘prāṇā-
pānāvevātmandhatte’
 iti phalaśravaṇamastyeva | paraharati-na, dhatta iti vartamānopadeśāt
sādhyatayā nāvagamyate phalam | atastadadhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ na bhavati vartamānatvādeva |
yatkhalu bhāviphalaṃ tanniyogasya sādhyatvayogyatayā adhikāriviśeṣaṇībhūtaṃ phalatayā’nveti na
punaraniyogasādhyamidamiti darśitam | iha punaḥ śaṃyau bhaviṣyadarthatāṃ yātayāditi liṅa-
vagamayati yātayaterdhātoḥ | tena yātanā bhaviṣyatīti pratīyate | atastatparihāro’pi
bhaviṣyatīti viśeṣaḥ | liṅarthatayā liṅśabdena pañcamalakāro’bhidhīyate pañcamalakārāntarastvayaṃ
yātayāditi śabdaḥ | etattvatra vaktavyaḥ---kathamatra śatayātanāyāḥ apagoraṇasya ca sādhyasādhana-
bhāvo’vagamyate | kathaṃ ca apagoraṇaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ kālāntare yātanāheturbhavīti | taducyate
śamyumadhikṛtyaitaducyate yo brāhmaṇāyāvaguret taṃ śatena yātayāditi | tatra cedamavagamyate
brāhmaṇāpagoraṇaṃ nimittaṃ śatayātanā ca naimittikī iti | na ca pratyakṣavirodhaḥ anantara-
bhāvitvenāpratīteḥ | na ca kriyāphalatayā yātanā pratīyate yenānantarabhāvinī bhavet | tatra
pratītanimittanaimittikabhāvānurodhena kiñcidavāntarakāryaṃ kalpyate yatkṛtvā avagoraṇena yātanā
niṣpādyate | “na brāhmaṇāyāvaguret”
 iti niṣedhānuguṇatayā cāyamarthaḥ pratīyata iti |
yātanā nāvivakṣitā śakyate āśrayitum | adhikāriviśeṣaṇatā tvatra niṣidhyamānakriyā-
kartṛtāyā eva | yātanāparihārastu niṣedhavidhiviṣayībhūtanañabhihitavagoraṇanivṛttiviśeṣaṇatayā
1viṣayāṃśānupraveśāt na niyojyaviśeṣaṇam | 2aniyogaphalatvāt | avagoraṇena hi
yātanāyāṃ 3sādhyāyāṃ tannivṛttau yātanāparihāro bhavasyeva niyogānapekṣa iti viṣayaphalamevedaṃ na
(1) G. viṣayāṅgānu (2) G. sanniyoga (3) G. svādhyāyāntaranivṛttau

[p. r832]

niyogaphalamiti mantavyam | ata eva coktaṃ sarvatra pratiṣedhaśāstre nividhyamānakriyākartu-
revādhikāra iti | evaṃ ca viṣayaphalata evānuṣṭhānalabdheḥ nādhikārānupraveśa iti puruṣadharma
evāvagoraṇapratiṣedhaḥ |

prāgaparodhānmalavadvāsasaḥ | atra bhāṣyakāreṇa sandehaḥ kṛtaḥ---kiṃ malavadvāsasā
saha darśapūrṇamāsāṅgasya saṃvādasya pratiṣedhaḥ uta puruṣasya sarvatra pratiṣedhavidhiriti | sa
cāyuktaḥ apratipakṣatvādvacanavyakteḥ | evaṃ hi vaktumucitaṃ---kiṃ darśapūrṇamāsāṅgasya saṃbādasya
pratiṣedhaḥ uta saṃvādamātrasyeti | yadi vā pratiṣedhaḥ kiṃ kratvarthaḥ uta puruṣārtha iti imāṃ
bhāṣye’nupapattiṃ pariharati---malavadvāsasā saha saṃvādapratiṣedhe sandehaḥ kratvarthasaṃvādaḥ prati-
ṣidhyate uta puruṣārtha iti | bhāṣye tu puruṣārthasaṃvādapratiṣedhapakṣasya phalamidaṃ kīrtitam
puruṣasya vidhiriti | kiṃ prāptaṃ ? kratau saṃvādasya “patni patnyeṣa te lokaḥ”
 ityevaṃvidhasya
saṃbhavāt kratvarthaḥ saṃvādaḥ pratiṣidhyate ‘nānṛtaṃ vadet itivat’
 | yathā kratvarthānṛtavadanaṃ
pratiṣidhyate tathā kratvarthasaṃvādaḥ pratiṣidhyate iti prāpte | ucyate | na kratvarthasyāyaṃ
pratiṣedhaḥ prāptatvāt kratāvasaṃvādasya | tena kratvarthasaṃvādābhāvāt puruṣārthasaṃvādapratiṣedha
evāyaṃ puruṣārthaḥ | kratvarthatayā hi ‘tāmaparudhya yajeta’
 iti prāpta evāsaṃvādaḥ | na hi
aparuddhayā saha saṃvādaḥ saṃbhavati aparodhanaṃ kratuvyāpāraudāsīnyakaraṇam | tasmāt puruṣārthaḥ
pratiṣidhyate pratiṣedho’pi puruṣārtha eva | vākyaṃ ca prakaraṇāt balīya ityuktameva | atra
(1) P. 951

[p. r833]

cādhikaraṇe ‘na malavadvāsasā saha saṃvadet’
 iti saṃvādamātrapratiṣedha evāyaṃ ityaṅgīkṛtya vicāraḥ
kṛtaḥ upagamanapratiṣedho’yamiti kṛtbācintoddhāṭanam | upagamanapratiṣedhāṅgīkaraṇe hi pūrvapakṣāśaṅkaiva
nāsti | kratāvupagamanābhāvāt | saṃvādastu kratāvastīti tatpratiṣedhāśaṅkopapadyate | aparodha-
vacanaṃ ca sākṣātkartṛtvapratiṣedhārtham | na punassā anyenāpi na saṃbhāṣaṇīyeti matvā pūrva-
pakṣitam | aparodhe tu sakalayajñasaṃbandhaniṣedhā/?/t na saṃbhāṣaṇīyeti rāddhāntaḥ | saṃprati
kṛtvācintāmuddhāṭitāṃ gṛhītvā codayati---nanu ca puruṣārthatayāpi prāpta evāyamasaṃvādaḥ
‘nākalyāṃ nārīmabhiramayet na rajasvalām’
 iti yo’yamupamamanapratiṣedharūpo lakṣaṇayā
asaṃvādaḥ prāpta eveti kathaṃ puruṣārtho’pi niṣedha iti | uttaraṃ---asyāḥ smṛteridameva mūlaṃ
bhaviṣyati | tena prāgastyeva rāgata upagamanaprāptirityupapannaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ | anuvādo vā
tatsmṛtiprāptārthasya | nanvanuvādaścet kratāveva kimiti na bhavati ityatrāha---sarvathā tāvat
kratvarthatā nāsti | puruṣasaṃbandhitayā pratiṣedhaḥ pratīyamānaḥ kathaṃ kratvartho bhavati | kratva-
ṅgatvenāvidhānādupagamanasya | anuvādaścet kimiti puruṣasaṃbandhamatikrāmet iti nāstyanuvādepi
kratvarthatā | kṛtvācintoddhāṭanāyāha---nanu ca saṃvādamātrapratiṣedho’yam | kathaṃ
smṛterasyāḥ mūlaṃ anuvādo vā | ucyate | ‘nāsyā annamadyāt’
 | abhyañjanai vai striyā
annam” iti “upagamanaṃ vai striyā annaṃ”
 ityupagamanapratiṣedha evāyaṃ sayuktikaṃ kṛtvā--
cintoddhāṭanaḥ upagamanapratiṣedharūpasaṃvādapratiṣedhavākyārtho vedenaiva vyākhyātaḥ ityupagamanapratiṣedha
evāyam | tasmānmūlaṃ vā smṛteḥ prāptasyānuvādaḥ[vā]iti | anuvādagrahaṇaṃ prauḍhavādaḥ |
paramārthatastu smṛtereva mūlamiti |

[p. r834]

aprakaraṇe tu taddharmastato viśepāt | ‘hiraṇyaṃ bhāryaṃ’
 ityanārabhyavādāt puruṣārtho’yaṃ
vidhiḥ na hyatra kratussannihito’vagamyate | tasmāt puruṣārtha iti rāddhāntamupakramya
pūrvapakṣamāha---nanu prayojanavataḥ saṃskāro na niṣprayojanasya saṃskāryatā yuktā | dvitīyā-
nirdeśācca hiraṇyasaṃskāra evāyaṃ yuktaḥ saṃskṛtaṃ kratorupakariṣyatīti | asyārthaḥ
---dvitīyānirdeśāditi dvitīyārthatayā kṛtyasya karmaiva dvitīyāśabdenocyate | tena suvarṇaṃ
bhāryaṃ iti karmārthakṛtyapratyayasya nirdeśāt, hiraṇyasaṃskāra evāyaṃ dhāraṇaṃ nāma | na ca
niṣprayojanaḥ saṃskāramarhati | tasmāt saṃskṛtaṃ kratorupakariṣyatītvevamarthaṃ dhāraṇena hiraṇyaṃ
saṃskriyate iti kratvartha evāyaṃ saṃskāra iti | rāddhāntamāha---atrābhidhīyate | syādetadevaṃ
yadi saṃskṛtasya hiraṇyasya karmārthatopapadyate | na tvatra karmārthatā hiraṇyasyāsti |
taddarśayati---na tāvat prakṛtaṃ karmāsti | na ceha karmacodanā dravyadevatāsaṃyogābhāvāt |
tasmāt dravyasaṃskārānarthakyāt puruṣadharmo’yamiti | nanu cātrāpi phalaṃ kalpayitavyaṃ,
adhikāriṇo’śravaṇāt adhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ kalpayitavyam | uttaraṃ---niyogānāṃ kāryārthatvāt
prakṛtasya kāryasya avidyamānatvāt na doṣaḥ adhikārikalpanāyām | adhikāri-
(1) P. 952

[p. r835]

niyogārthatvāt parasparapadārthasaṃbandhasya prakṛtasya vā adhikāraniyogasyābhāvāt balādadhikārāntara-
kalpanā na doṣaḥ viśvajiddhat | tasmāt hiraṇyadhāraṇaṃ karmakarturdharmaścodyate iti | punaḥ
pūrvapakṣī vadati---yadi kartṛsaṃskāratvena phalaṃ kalpyate kimiti dravyasaṃskāratayāpi kratūpa-
kāro na kalpyate tulyatvāt kalpanāyāḥ ko viśeṣa iti bhāvaḥ | ayamasti viśeṣaḥ
ekavākyāvagatena hi arthavādena kartṛsaṃbandhe phalakalpanopapadyate | antaraṅgatvāt
na punaraprakṛtena kratunā saha saṃbandhakalpanā | arthaviprakarṣāt bahiraṅgabhūtatvāt kratu-
saṃbandhasya | etaccābhyuccayamātraṃ avyāpakatvāt | idānīṃ vyāpikāṃ mūlayuktimāha---ayaṃ
cāparo viśeṣaḥ sarvatra draṣṭavyaḥ kartṛsaṃbandhe hyadhikārasiddhiḥ | adhikṛtasya ca phala-
kalpanā nyāyyā | na punaḥ dravyasaṃbandhe prayojanakalpaneti saktuṣu darśitam |

asyārthaḥ---kartṛtvaṃ hi kāryabhūtamadhikārasya tena kartṛsaṃbandhe’vagate yuktā kāraṇabhūtā-
dhikārasiddhiḥ | na cāviśiṣṭo’dhikārī bhavati iti viśeṣabhūtaphalakalpanāpi 1kāryā |
dravyasaṃbandhe tu kriyayā avagate na dravyasya prayojanakalpanā nyāyyā pramāṇābhāvāt |
na ca dravyaṃ prati kriyāyāstādarthyāvayama eva pramāṇaṃ, niṣprayojane tādarthyānupapatteriti |
yatastādarthyameva dravyaṃ pratyanavagatam | na ca dvitīyayā tādarthyamiti tatra tatroktam |

(1) G. nyāyyā

[p. r836]

śeṣo’prakaraṇe’viśeṣāt sarvakarmaṇāt | ‘yena karmaṇerset tatra jayān juhuyāt’

iti sthitaṃ tāvat jayāhomāḥ karmāṅgamiti ṛddhisādhanakarmasaṃyogāt phalāntarāśravaṇācca |
idīnāṃ sandihyate---kiṃ karmamātrāṅgaṃ uta vaidikasyaiveti | karmamātraśabdenātra smārtavaidika-
karmaṇāṃ grahaṇaṃ na kṛṣyādīnām; teṣāṃ anvayavyatirekasamadhigamyāṅgatvāt vaidikāṅgānupapatteḥ |
bhāṣye tu kṛṣyādigrahaṇaṃ āśaṅkāmātreṇa na paramārthataḥ | aviśeṣāt sarvārthateti pūrvapakṣa-
vādī manyate | nanu cānāhitāgnerāhavanīyābhāvāt nāyaṃ homaḥ saṃbhavati | asyārthaḥ
---jayāhomāstāvat ‘āhavanīye juhotī’
ti vacanāt āhavanīye kartavyāḥ | tatra yadi
laukikānāṃ smārtānāṃ karmaṇāmaṅgaṃ syāt tadā teṣāmanāhitāgnerapi saṃbhavāt jayāhomaḥ prāpnoti |
na cānāhitāgnirāhavanīye hotuṃ śaknoti | na cedaṃ vācyaṃ āhitāgnirāhavanīye hoṣyati,
anāhitāgnistu neti | nityavadviniyuktāhavanīyabādhaprasaṅgāt | na cedamapyucitam 
---anāhitāgneraṣṭakādikarmāṇi jayāhomarahitāni bhaviṣyanti āhitāgnerneti | ekameva niyogaṃ
prati aṅgatvānaṅgatvānupapatteḥ iti varaṃ vaidikānāmeva tretāvatāṃ karmaṇābhaṅgaṃ jayāhoma iti sthitiḥ |
pariharati---anāhavanīya evāyaṃ bhaviṣyati pradhānānurodhāt | na guṇānurodhena pradhāna-
syāṅgatyāgo yuktaḥ guṇa eva hi tyājyaḥ pradhānānurodhenetyaṅgavido manyante | asyārthaḥ
---yena karmaṇertsediti sāmānyena śravaṇāt jayāhomaḥ sarvakarmārtha iti sthite yatra pradhāne
aṣṭakādikarmaṇi anāhitāgnitvādāhavanīyo na bhavati tatra kiṃ pradhānamastīti jayāhomaḥ
kriyatāṃ, utāhavanīyo nāstīti na kriyatāṃ iti saṃśaye pradhānasyānugrahāt homāṅgamāhavanīyaṃ
(1) P. 957

[p. r837]

parityajya jayāhomaḥ kartavyaḥ na punaḥ aṅgasya jayāhomasyāṅgabhūtāhavanīyānurodhena
pradhānasyāṣṭakāderaṅgabhūtasya jayāhomasya tyāgo yuktaḥ | guṇa evāhavanīṃya-
styaktumucitaḥ pradhānānurodheneti aṅgavido manyante | rāddhāntamāha---(satyamāha
bhavān) satyamevaitat | kintvatrāyaṃ nyāyo na saṃbhavati | aṅgaguṇavirodhe ca tādarthyā-
diti | kasmāt ? vaidike tāvadāhavanīyabādho nopapadyate na ca laukikasyaiva
smārtasyaivāṅgamiti śakyate vaktuṃ tvayaivoktamaviśeṣāditi | ayamāśayaḥ---yatra niścita-
maṅgatvaṃ tatrāṅgaguṇavirodhanyāyaḥ | iha tu jayāhomasyāṅgatvameva niścitam | tatra yasminna-
ṅginyāśrīyamāṇe viniyojyasvarūpaṃ na bādhyate tasyaiva śeṣitvaṃ yuktam | āhavanīyayuktaṃ cāṅgi-
svarūpamiti tadanurodhena vaidikeṣveva viniyogaḥ tatrāhavanīyasaṃbhavāt | smārteṣvanāhitāgnerāha-
vanīyābhāvāt ityāhavanīyāṅgāvirodhena viniyoga evopasaṃhriyate | na ca yena karmaṇertsediti
virodhaḥ aviśeṣoddeśāt | ato bhāvābhāvayorvirodhāt āhavanīyabhāvābhāvayorvirodhāt
āhavanīyasya bhāvata eva viśeṣāvadhāraṇaṃ yuktam | na punarabhāvataḥ anavagamaheturasau
anavagamaṃ pratyeva heturasau na punarviśeṣāvagamahetuḥ tasmādvaidika eva jayāhoma iti
sthitam |

[p. r838]

doṣattviṣṭirlaukike syāt | pauṇḍarīke śrūyate-yāvato’śvān pratigṛhṇīyāt tāvato
vāruṇāñcatuṣkapālānnirvapet” iti aśvapratigrahoṣṭirasti | tatra ‘yāvato’śvān pratigṛhṇīyāt’
 iti
kiṃ laukike’śvapratigrahe iyamiṣṭiḥ uta vaidike iti saṃśayaḥ | tatra pūrvapakṣamāha---laukike
doṣadarśanāt śūdrādanyasmādvā pāpakarmaṇaḥ kṛte doṣo bhavatīti | doṣavatyaśvapratigrahe iya-
miṣṭiḥ śrūyate doṣavattā ca laukikasyāśvapratigrahasyāsti yataḥ śūdrādanyasmādvā pāpakarmaṇaḥ kṛte
pratigrahe doṣo bhavati | tasmāt doṣavān laukikaḥ pratigraha iti tatraiveyamiṣṭiḥ | atra
bhāṣyaṃ ‘na doṣasaṃkīrtanaṃ prāyaścittaviṣayatiśeṣaṇaṃ, kintu prāyaścittastutyarthena”
 iti | tadvayākhyātu-
mupakramate---kimidaṃ prāyaścittaviṣayaviśeṣaṇaṃ kiṃ vā stutyartheneti | idamidaṃ---asya
varuṇagrahākhyasyārthasyāpanuttaye yadā prāyaścittaṃ tadā prāyaścittaviṣayaviśeṣaṇam | yattu
khalu doṣanirdhātārthaṃ tat yatraiva doṣaḥ tatraiva kartavyamityarthāt prāyaścittaviṣayabhūtapratigrahasya
varuṇagraho viśeṣaṇaṃ yatraiva ca varuṇagrahaḥ tatraiva tannivṛttaye’śvapratigraheṣṭiriti | yadā tu kāryā-
ntarārthaṃ tadā stutyarthena, doṣanirdhātārthaṃ cedaṃ vākyapaurvāparyālocanayā doṣanirdhātārthaṃ
prāyaścittaṃ cedaṃ pratīyate | tasmāllaukike bhavitumarhati | atra bhāṣyaṃ---“nanu vaidikepi
pratigrahe apratigrāhyāt pratigṛhṇataḥ pāpamasti iti prāyaścittena bhavitavyam | ucyate |
(1) 958 (2) 959

[p. r839]

bhavedetadevaṃ yadi pratigrahasya karturiṣṭirbhavet | sā tu khalu yathā hetukartuḥ tathottarādhikaraṇe
vakṣyāmaḥ”
 iti | tadākṣipannāha---(atra codayati)---asaṃbaddhamidaṃ bhāṣyam | yadi
laukike pratigrahīturiṣṭirnabhavati tataśca vaidikepi (kimiti pratigrahīturiṣṭirnabharthati |
tataśca vaidikepi) aśvaṃ apratigrāhyāt pratigṛhṇato doṣo’stīti satyapi doṣanirṣātārthe
prāyaścitte kasmāllaukika eva pratigrahītuḥ prāyaścittāśaṅkā | atha upakramāvagatārthavāda-
darśanāt dāturiṣṭirityucyate laukike’pi tulyaṃ, tasminnapi pakṣe upakramālocanayā dātureva
na ca tasya doṣo’sti dānasyāniṣiddhatvāt | ato doṣanirdhātārthatve nāsti laukike prasaṅgaḥ
atha laukike pratigrahapakṣe’pi dāturevetyabhiprāyaḥ śūdradanyasmādveti bhāṣyamasaṃbaddhaṃ
dāturhi doṣo darśayitavyaḥ na pratigrahītuḥ doṣanirdhātārthaṃ dāturiṣṭiriti vadatā dātuḥ doṣaḥ
kathanīyaḥ | na ca śūdrādanyasmādvā pratigrahe dāturdoṣo bhavati api tu pratigrahītureva | na
ca tasyeyamiṣṭiḥ upakramavirodhāt | kathaṃ coparitane’dhikaraṇe pratigrahīturiyamiṣṭiriti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | vedo vā prāyadarśanāt | ityupakrameṇopasaṃhārāvadhṛteḥ | vedo vā prāyadarśa-
nādityupakramānusāreṇopasaṃhāro nyāyyaḥ ityuktam | tenehāpyupakramagatārthavādālocanayā dāturi-
ṣṭiriti pratīyate | atrābhidhīyate---nedamasaṃbaddhaṃ bhāṣyaṃ; ayamabhiprāyaḥ pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ |

[p. r840]

laukike’śvapratigrahe doṣaśravaṇaṃ viṣayaviśeṣaṇatayaiva1 upapadyate | laukikāśvapratigrahaviṣayā-
yāmiṣṭau āśrayamāṇāyāṃ doṣaśravaṇaṃ phalasamarpakatayā pratigrahalakṣaṇaviṣayaviśeṣaṇatayaivopapadyate |
tasmin pakṣe doṣanirdhātārthā satīyamiṣṭiḥ saṃbhāvyadoṣasya pratigrahītureva bhavati | pratigrahītaivā-
dhikārīti yāvat | kathaṃ punaḥ pratigrahītaivādhikārī yāvatā upakramavaśena dātaivādhikārī yukta
iti | uttaraṃ---yasmādasmin pakṣe laukikapratigrahāśrayaṇapakṣe---pratigrahīturiyamiṣṭiḥ yadi ca
tasminnapi pakṣe dātureveyamiṣṭiḥ syāt tataḥ dānasya smārtasya laukikasyāṅgaṃ kalpayitavyametadiṣṭi-
rūpaṃ phalāntaraṃ adhikārāntaraṃ vā | dānāṅgatā tāvanna saṃbhavati nimittatvenāsyopakṣayāt
upakṣīṇatvāt dānasya śeṣitvānupapatteḥ | atha viparivṛttyā punaraṅgitvaṃ dānasya kalpyate
tadā idamevāyuktaṃ yannimittatvenopakṣīṇasya punaraṅgitvakalpanaṃ pramāṇābhāvāt | nanu
idameva pramāṇa yaddātṛgatadoṣakīrtanaṃ tatrāha---guṇavādenāpyarthavādopapatteḥ | evamaśvapratigraho
nidānaṃ doṣasya | yaddāturapi tāvat doṣo bhavati kimaṅga punaḥ pratigrahītuḥ iti pratigrahadoṣa-
pradarśanaparatayāpyarthavādopapatteḥ na śakyate dāturdoṣaṃ parikalpya tannirharaṇadvāreṇa dānāṅgatā
kalpayitumiti | nanu ca dānamātranimittamadhikārāntaramevedaṃ bhavitumarhatītyatrāha---pāpapramo-
canasya phalatayā vā vākyenāvagateḥ | tena yathā tasyānvayo bhavati tathā varṇayitavyam | tasya
ca pratigrahītari niyojye yukto’nvayaḥ na dātari tadāha---na ca dātuḥ pāpaṃ śakyate kalpayituṃ
(1) R. tathaiva

[p. r841]

coditatvāt loke vede ca dānasya | tasmāllaukikapakṣe pratigrahautureveyamiṣṭiḥ
vaidikapakṣe tu prakaraṇa eva niveśāt prakaraṇenāṅgatvakalpanāt phalāntarakalpanābhāvāt dātureve
yamiṣṭiḥ syāt | tatra ca doṣapramocanaśravaṇamavivakṣitaṃ syāt dāturdoṣābhāvāt | ato
laukikapratigrahaviṣayaiveyamiṣṭiḥ iti | tatra hi yathoktena nyāyena pratigrahīturiṣṭirbhavati
pratigrahītuśca doṣa iti doṣapramocanaśravaṇaṃ vivakṣitaṃ syāt | tato’yamatra sandehārthaḥ---laukike
pratigrahe pratigrahīturiyamiṣṭirbhavatu atha vā vaidike dāturiti | tatra pāpapramocanaśravaṇāt laukike
pratigrahīturiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu yathā śrutasya tāvatpāpasya aśvapratigraho na
nidānatayā pramāṇāntareṇa prasiddhaḥ na jalodarasyāśvapratigrahaḥ nidānatayā pramāṇāntareṇa
siddhaḥ | nāpi vacanāt | vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | yadi vacanamidaṃ jñāpayet aśvaprati-
graheṇa jalodaraṃ bhavati tacceṣṭyā’panīyata iti tato’rthabhedādvākyabhedaḥ syāt | ekaṃ cedaṃ
vākyam | ato na vacanato jalodaranidānatā aśvapratigrahasya | tasmāt pāpamātrabacanaṃ
jalodaravacanaṃ vaidikepyupapadyata eva rakṣaṇapoṣaṇādinā duḥ svadarśanāt pratigrahītuḥ |
nanu vaidikapakṣe dāturiṣṭayā bhavitavyamiti dātureva doṣo darśanīyaḥ | kimidaṃ pratigrahītṛdoṣa-
kīrtanaṃ uttarādhikaraṇasya adyāpyasiddhatvāt | yuktaṃ pratigrahīturdoṣadarśanam asminnadhikaraṇe
kratvaṅgatayā dātureveṣṭāvadhikāra iti sidhyati | uttarādhikaraṇe tu kiṃ dātā yajñe uta
ṛtvigiti kartṛvicāro bhaviṣyati | tena pratigrahītari kartari ārtvijadoṣadarśanamupapannam |
vaidikapratigrahaviṣayeṣṭipakṣe ca phalaṃ ca kalpayitavyaṃ bhavati karmāṅgatayopapatteḥ | prakaraṇa-
viniyojyatayā karturvābhavatu pratigrahīturvā yadi dātṛkartṛkeyamiṣṭiḥ | yadi ca
pratigrahītṛkartṛkā tathāpi pauṇḍarīkaprakaraṇāt tadaṅgamityarthaḥ |

[p. r842]

acoditaṃ ca karmabhedāt | atra codayati---yuktaṃ tāvat yadā kartuḥ tadā
karmāṅgaṃ ekādhikārasaṃbandhāt---yadā punaḥ pratigrahītuḥ tadā kathaṃ ? pratigrahītuḥ adhi-
kārāntaratvenāsaṃbandhāt iti bhrāntasyedaṃ codyam | uttaraṃ---upakramopasaṃhāravaśāt prati-
grāhayeditivat vipariṇāmātkalpyate | asyārthaḥ---yathā rāddhānte upakramavaśāt prati-
grāhayeditivipariṇāmaḥ tathā kṛtādvipariṇāmāt pratigrahītṛkartṛkatvepi ekādhi-
kāripuruṣasaṃbandhāt aṅgatā kalpyate | tameva vipariṇāmaṃ darśayati---ṛtvijo yājanīyā
upavāsavat| yathā upavāsaṃ kāryante ṛtvijaḥ tathā yājanīyāḥ | varuṇoddeśena catuṣka-
pālena yājayitavyā ityarthaḥ | tena sādhūktaṃ dāturvā bhavatu pratigrahīturveti | yatpunaruktaṃ
vedo vā prāyadarśanādeveti manyate | nanvayaṃ doṣaḥ paramārthato nāstyevetyuktaṃ tatrāha---arthavādī-
bhūto’pi hi doṣaḥ pratigrahīturevāñjasyenopapadyate | rakṣaṇapoṣaṇādiduḥkhadarśanāt |
nanvevamupakramagatadātṛkīrtanaṃ kathaṃ ghaṭiṣyata ityatrāha---upakramo’pi laukikapakṣavat dāturapi
tāvaddoṣaḥ kiṃ punaḥ pratigrahīturiti vyākhyāyate | etaccoktamasmābhiḥ | rāddhāntavādītu

avaśyaṃ tāvat ‘nirvāpayet’
 iti kalpanīyam | tasmādvaramupakramavaśāt ‘pratigrāhayet’

iti yuktaṃ manyate | vipariṇāmasyāvaśyakalpanīyatvāt varamupakramānurodhena vipariṇāmaḥ
kalpyatāmiti nyāyyam | arthavādasyāvaśyakarmavyatāparatvena bhāṣye varṇanā kṛteti na
punaruktaṃ ‘vedovā’
 ityanenādhikaraṇena | adhikaraṇadvayaṃ tu ekīkṛtya vyācakṣāṇasyāyamāśayaḥ
---arthavādārthapratipattyā pūrvādhikaraṇe pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ samīcīnārthavādārthadarśanena nirākaraṇīyaḥ |
avaśyaṃ kartavyatātmakapāramārthikārthavādārthavarṇanaṃ cottarādhikaraṇāyattamiti |

pānavyāpacca tadvat | tadvadityatideśiko viśeṣaḥ ? na hi viśeṣamantareṇa
tadvaditi bhavati tadeva hi syāt | ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ doṣaśravaṇāt “indriyeṇa vā eṣa
vyṛdhyate vīryeṇa vā yaḥ somaṃ vamiti”
 iti doṣaśravaṇāt laukike somavamane prāyaścittamiti
viśeṣaḥ | nanu pūrvasminnadhikaraṇe doṣadarśanādeva laukika ityuktam | ihāpi doṣa-
darśanādeva laukika ityucyamāne nāsti viśeṣaḥ | pariharati---satyamanupapattiḥ tatroktā
doṣasya jalodaralakṣaṇasya | iha tu glāniḥ vamanādupapadyata evendriyāṇāmiti adhi-
kāśaṅkā | pramāṇaparigṛhītaivendriyāṇāṃ vyaddhiḥ | yata eva pramāṇaparigṛhītaiveyaṃ ata eva
vamanārthaṃ taditi parihṛtaṃ doṣatvam | anyathā glānireva nāstīti parihāro dātavyaḥ

[p. r844]

syāt | kathaṃ punaḥ vamanārthatvābhidhānena doṣaparihāraḥ tatrāha---glānyarthameva hi yat na
tasya glānirdoṣatvena śakyate kalpayitumiti | yaddhi vamanārthaṃ tadvamansya glānyavinā-
bhāvitvāt glānyarthameva | yacca glānyarthaṃ tasya glānirna doṣa iti śakyate vaditumiti
parihṛtameva doṣatvam | yasmāddoṣatvaṃ nāsti tasmāt tannirharaṇārthatā tāvadasya somendrasya
karmaṇo nāstīti pūrvavadevedaṃ karma phalakalpanābhayāccoditasomapānavamananimitta-
kamityarthaḥ | coditaṃ yatsomapānaṃ tasya yat vamanaṃ tannimittakamidaṃ karma | tena kratu-
saṃbandhisomapānavamananimittatvāt kratvaṅgabhūtamevedaṃ karmeti sthitam |

tatsarvatrāviśeṣāt | kiṃ yāvantaḥ somavāminaḥ tāvatāṃ vamana etatprāyaścittaṃ
uta svāmina eveti bhāṣyakāreṇa sākṣādanuktamapyarthādavagamyamānaṃ saṃśayaṃ kathayati |
aviśeṣāt sarveṣāṃ vamana iti prāpte; ucyate---svāmina etat prāyaścittamiti |
kutaḥ ? uttaraṃ---na yajamānasya ṛtvijāṃ caikā codanā saṃbhavati | ekatra yajeteti
codanā anyatra yājayediti | asyārthaḥ, yadi yajamānavamane ṛtvigvamane caikā codanā
syāt tadā aviśeṣaḥ syāt | na cāsāvekā codanā saṃbhavati yasmādekatra yajamānavamanapakṣe

[p. r845]

yajeteti codanā yassomaṃ vamiti sa somendraṃ caruṃ nirvapet iti tadā vākyārthaḥ syāt |
anyatra tu ṛtvigvamanapakṣe yājayediti codanā | idaṃ hi pūrvoṃktena kratvarthaṃ karmeti sthitam |
tena nāyamarthaḥ yassomaṃ vamiti ṛtvika्tamṛtvijaṃ somendraṃ caruṃ nirvāpayediti | na caika-
sminvākye ubhayametatsaṃbhavati| tasmānnāviśeṣāt sarveṣāṃ vamane somendraṃ karma |
nanvevamapi ṛtvigvamana evāstu tatrāha---kasmāt ? avipariṇatā eṣā codanā | ṛtvigva-
manapakṣepi hi yajamānasyaivādhikārādavaśyaṃ vipariṇāmaḥ kalpyaḥ avipariṇatā ceyaṃ codanā |
sā ca svābhivamana evāvagamyate yasmādyajamānavamananimittakamidaṃ karma tasmāt phalavat---
sannidhāvaphalaṃ tadaṅgamiti bhavati | anyathā hi ṛtvigvamane nimitte sati avipariṇa-
teyaṃ codanā ṛtvijāmevādhikāriṇāṃ svatantraṃ karma vidadhyāt | tasmādadhikāribhedādanaṅgameva
syāditi phalavatsannidhāvaphalaṃ tadaṅgamiti na sidhyati | tadidaṃ ṛtvigvamanapakṣe avi-
pariṇatacodanāśrayaṇe’naṅgatvamuktam nānyadīyo yāgo’nyasyopakarotīti | tasmānna ṛtvi-
gvamananimittam | nahyanyasya coditamanyasyopakarotītyarthaḥ| upasaṃharati---ata-
ssiddhamevedaṃ svāmivamananimittamiti |

sarvapradānaṃ haviṣastadarthatvāt | atra bhāṣyaṃ ki kṛtsnaṃ haviragnaye dātavyamiti
tadanupapannam | devatāṃ prati dānābhāvāt | tathā śeṣayitavyaṃ kiñcit kiñcicca dātavyamiti
(1) P. 974

[p. r846]

tadvayācaṣṭe---ihedānīṃ sandihyate āgneyo’ṣṭākapāla iti kiṃ kṛtsnasyaiva tyāgaḥ | uta
dvirhaviṣo’vadyati caturavattaṃ juhoti iti vacanāt tāvanmātrasyaiveti tyāga evadāna-
śabdenocyate lakṣaṇayā | nanu cātra sandeha eva nopapadyate | āgneyo’ṣṭākapāla iti
parisamāptā yāgacodanā | tena sarvasyaiva tyāga iti rāddhāntavacanavyaktyabhāvāt sandeha
evānupapannaḥ | nanu ca ‘dvirhaviṣo’badyati’
 ‘caturavattaṃ juhoti’
 iti śravaṇāt dvayavadānamātrasya
tyāgaḥ pratibhāti ityatrāha---dvirhaviṣo’vadyatīti saṃskāracodanaiṣā haviṣaḥ ‘agnestṛṇānya-
pacinotī’
tivat | nanu ca na kevalamavadānaṃ śrūyate juhotītyapi śrūyate | tena
dvayavadānenaiva yāgaḥ kartavya iti pratīyate | uttaraṃ---haviṣassakāśāt apanītasya
pratipattirbhaviṣyati | yathā rāddhānta eva yāgottarakālaṃ sviṣṭakṛdādayaḥ śeṣāt śeṣa-
pratipattibhūtāḥ sviṣṭakṛdādayaḥ | atrābhidhīyate---naitadevaṃ bhavitumarhati kṛtsnasyaiva tyāga
iti yasmājjuhotiḥ yāgāśca svasaṃbandhidravyasādhyāḥ dravyatyāgātmakau homayāgau
svadravyeṇaiva sidhyataḥ | sakalasya ca parityāge homoyāgāśca sviṣṭakṛdādayo
nopapadyante | asyārthaḥ kṛtsnasya tyāgapakṣe dvayavadānamakṛtvaiva puroḍāśastyaktavyaḥ anyathā
kṛtsnatvasyānupapatteḥ | tataśca puroḍāśāt dvayavadānamātraṃ kṛtvā hotavyaṃ sviṣṭakṛdādayaśca
yāgāḥ kartavyāḥ te nopapadyante tyaktasya dravyasyāsvakīyatvāt | tasmānna kṛtsnastya-

[p. r847]

ktavya ityasti śaṅkā | nanu ca dravyāntarāt homāḥ sviṣṭakṛdādayo bhaviṣyanti tatrāha
---aṣṭākapālaviṣayā codanā | tasyaiva haviṣṭa्vāt śeṣaśabdasya prakṛtadravyaviṣayatvācca |
ata upapadyate saṃśayaḥ | asmiṃśca saṃśaye kasya pakṣasyādhyavasānaṃ yuktaṃ ? pūrvapakṣavādī
manyate | apratipakṣatvādāgneyacodanāyāḥ tatsavyapekṣatvāditarāsāṃ pūrvaiva balīyasīti |
tadidamuktam---sarvapradānaṃ haviṣastadarthatvāditi | kathaṃ tarhi ‘caturavattaṃ juhoti’
 iti
homaḥ ‘sviṣṭakṛte samavadyati’
 iti caturthīprayogāt sviṣṭakṛddevateti niścīyate |
1tena dravyadevate niścīyete tena dravyadevatāsaṃbandhāt sviṣṭakṛdyāgaḥ | tau ca
homayāgau tyakte dravye na saṃbhavataḥ | uttara---gauṇo vā yajyartho juhotyarthaśca grahītavyaḥ
prakṣepamātrarūpaḥ | taddharmakaṃ vā dravyāntaramutpādyam | etadeva vivṛṇoti---dbirhaviṣo-
’vadyati havidharmakādavadyatītyarthaḥ |

kṛtatvāttu karmaṇaḥ sakṛtsyāt dravyasya guṇabhūtatvāt | atrābhidhīyate---satyamevaṃ
yathā bhavānāha---yadi ‘āgneyo’ṣṭākapāla’
 iti tyāgaparyantaḥ taddhitaḥ syāt, syāde-
(1) G. omits tena.....yete

[p. r848]

tadevaṃ, yāvatā sāsya devateti saṃbandhimātratayā’vagateḥ tadekadeśatyāgādapi saṃbandhitā
upapadyata eva | tadidamuktam akṛtsnasaṃbandhe’pi taddhitasaṃyogasyopapattiḥ tato
grahīṣyata iti | nanu apradīyamānasya kathamasaṃbandhe kṛtsnasyāgneyatā ? uttaraṃ---darśitastato
grahītasya iti saṃbandhaḥ dvayavadānasya | evaṃ ca sati āgneyacodanāyāmupapannāyāṃ “catu-
ravattaṃ juhoti”
 ‘sviṣṭakṛte samavadyati’
 iti gauṇīvṛttiḥ na śakyate kalpayitum |
havirdharmakasya vā dravyasyotpādaḥ tathā caturavattahomenaiva kṛtaśśāstrārthaḥ ‘āgneyo’ṣṭā-
kapāla’
 iti sviṣṭakṛdādidarśanaṃ copapannaṃ bhavati | ‘śeṣādiḍāmavadyati’

samavadyati’
 iti śeṣadarśanaṃ copapadyate | anyathā aśeṣe śeṣaśabdaḥ syāt | yatpunaridamā-
śaṅkitaṃ dvayavadānasma saṃskāratvāt na sarvasya parityāge virodha iti | tadidamāśaṅkā-
mātrameva | haviṣo yāgārthatvādityuktam | asyārthaḥ---yatpunaridamāśaṅkitaṃ dvayavadānaṃ
svīkṛtāvayavadravyasaṃskāraḥ, tadeva homena pratipādyate tasmiṃśca pratipāditepi pariśiṣṭena
kṛtsnena puroḍāśenāgneyaḥ kariṣyate | tena na kaścidvirodha iti | atrocyate---syāde-
tadevaṃ yadi tadartho yāgaḥ syāt | yadi yāgena haviḥ saṃbandhayitavyaṃ syāt | havistu
yāgārthaṃ tena sakṛta dvayavadāne kṛte kṛtatvādyāgasya dravye satyapi na punaryāgaḥ kartavya
ityuktaṃ bhāṣye |

[p. r849]

aprayojakatvādekasmāt kriyeran śeṣasya guṇabhūtatvāt | iha śeṣakāryāṇyudāha-
raṇam | kiṃ sakṛtkṛtvoparantavma, uta yāvaccheṣamāvṛttiriti| asyārthaḥ---kimeka-
syādeva śeṣāt śeṣakāryaṃ kṛtvā uparantavyaṃ uta sarvebhyaḥ śeṣebhyaḥ pratyekaṃ śeṣakāryāṇi
kāryāṇīti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī kṛtatvātkāryasya sakṛtkaraṇaṃ manyate | ekasmādapi
śeṣāt śeṣakārye kṛtaśśāstrārtha iti sakṛtkaraṇaṃ manyate āgneyavat | nanu ca prati-
pradhānaṃ guṇā āvartanta iti śeṣasya aprādhānyādāvṛttireva pratīyate | parihartumāha
---ayamabhiprāyaḥ pūrvapakṣavādinaḥ | pradhānopakārāya hi śeṣasya saṃskāraḥ sakṛtkṛtena ca
kṛtaṃ kāryamiti nāvṛttiṃ manyate | upakāryaikyāt sakṛdeva śeṣakāryaṃ kartavyam |
na śeṣatvāt saṃskāryatvaṃ, api tu pradhānopakārāya | nanu ca grahādāvapyevameva prāpnoti |
tatrāpyeka eva grahaḥ saṃmārjayitavyaḥ syāt | uttaraṃ---prāpnotyeva (vaṃ) yadyatra
1na samādhīyate | yadyatraiva bahūnāṃ sammārgaḥ pratipādyate tatra tarhi kā cintā

śeṣakāryāṇi tathāpi śeṣibhedādbheda eva yuktaḥ | nanu ca kṛtatvācchāstrārthasya sakṛdityuktam |

ucyate---śeṣasaṃskāradvāraṃ hi pradhānasyopakāryatvamuktam | na ca śeṣe viśeṣo’vagamyate |

[p. r851]

mukhya eva nimitte saṃbhavati na naimittikopaghātaḥ śakyate vaktumiti rāddhāntaḥ |
prathamopanipātini śeṣe nimittasya śeṣalakṣaṇasya saṃbhavānna naimittikaśeṣakāryopaghātaśśakyate

kṛtatvānnaimittikasya prathamabhāvinyeva nimittopasaṃhṛtiriti yuktaiva akriyā śeṣakāryāṇā-
miti |

bhakṣāśravaṇāt | nanu kathamatra śeṣeṇa parikrayāśaṅkā viniyuktaviniyogavirodhāt
ityatrāha---idaṃ brahmaṇa iti dvayavadānopasaṃharāt yāgasya śeṣeṇa parikrayaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣa-
vādī | yāgaṃ prati dravyasya yat tādarthyaṃ tat dvayavadānamātreṇopasaṃhṛtaṃ iti śeṣasya anyatrā-
tādarthyāt viniyuktaviniyogavirodhābhāvāt ‘idaṃ brahmaṇe’
 iti caturthāśravaṇāt śeṣeṇa brahmādīnāṃ
parikrayaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | rāddhāntavādī tu tasyāpyavadeyārthatayotpatteḥ yāgārtha-
taiveti nārthāntare viniyogamarhatīti manyate | śeṣasyāpi dravyasya dvayavadānārthatayotpatteḥ
yāgārthatvam | tena nānyatra viniyogamarhatīti nānena parikrayaḥ | yadi sakala eva
yāgārthaḥ kathaṃ tarhi sviṣṭakṛdādiyāgaḥ yāgāntare viniyogo nopapadyate | neha[na hi]
devatāyai saṅkalpitena sveneva vyavaharanti śiṣṭā iti bhāvaḥ | uttaraṃ---atyaktatvāccheṣasya
vacanāccopapadyate | asyārthaḥ---atyaktatvena śeṣasya svatva tāvannāpagataṃ kintu satyapi

[p. r852]

svatve śiṣṭācārānna svatvavyavahāro bhaveti | śrautavacanavirodhena śiṣṭācāre bādhite na
kācidanupapattiḥ | nanu ca dānamapi vacanādbhaviṣyati dānaṃ pratīyata iti | pariharati
---satyaṃ yadi vacanamasti na tvatra dānaṃ śrūyate | nanu ‘idaṃ brahmaṇa’
 iti tādarthyaśravaṇāt
dānaṃ pratīyate | tatrāha---tādarthyaśrutistu upakārādapyupapadyata eva ityupakāro darśitaḥ |
bhakṣyamāṇaṃ hi dravyaṃ nāntarīyakīṃ prītimutpādayatyeva | tatrāpi tu śeṣabhāvo viparīta eva |
dravyameva pradhānaṃ pratipādyatayā | puruṣa eva guṇabhūtaḥ pratipādakatayā ityanavadyam |

caturthaḥ pādaḥ

[p. r853]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

tṛtīyādhyāyasya pañcamaḥ pādaḥ

ājyācca sarvasaṃyogāt | yāvadvaviḥ kiñciccoditaṃ pradhānaṃ tadīyāt śeṣāt
samavadyatīti sthitam | upāṃśuyāje saṃśayaḥ kiṃ dhravāsthādaviśeṣāt1 aṅgapradhāna-
sādhāraṇatvādanavadeyaṃ sviṣṭakṛte, utāṅgapradhānaśeṣamapi pradhānaśeṣamityavadeyam | gṛhītā-
dvā jauhavāt camasat | yathā camase tathā’vadeyaṃ uta śeṣābhāvādanavadeyamiti |
aviśeṣādavadeyamiti prāpte aṅgapradhāne prati sādhāraṇatve’pi śeṣatvāviśeṣāda्dhrauvādavadeyaṃ
camasena ca viśeṣābhāvāt jauhavādevāvadeyamiti prāpte rāddhānta ucyate---na sādhāraṇe’vadeyaṃ
kevalapradhānaśeṣatvābhāvāt | śiṣṭamapi tat sādhāraṇameva | bhavatu sādhāraṇaṃ tathāpi
kimityāha---pradhānaśeṣācca sviṣṭakṛdavadānam | ya eva hi kevalapradhānaśeṣaḥ tasmādeva
sviṣṭakṛdavadānaṃ puroḍāśādiṣu | na tvaṅgapradhānaśeṣāt | yatra hi pradhānaśeṣādavadānaṃ tatra
kevalapradhānārthatā puroḍāśādau | yatra punaḥ aṅgapradhānaśeṣāt tatrāṅgapradhānārthatā | na cedaṃ
vaiṣamyamāśrayituṃ nyāyyaṃ avaiṣamyeṇopapattau | na ca tadeva kevalapradhānaśeṣa iti śakyate vaktuṃ
(1) P. 989 (2) G. api śeṣāt

[p. r854]

sādhāraṇatvādeva | yattu gṛhītasyaiva pratipattiścamasavaditi, tadayuktaṃ kutaḥ ?
uttaraṃ---caturavattaṃ juhotīti śravaṇāt | caturavattasya kṛtsnasyaiva homasaṃbandhāt śeṣayi-
tavye pramāṇābhāvāt | sviṣṭakṛdavadānavacanameva pramāṇamiti cet | na hi śeṣamanta-
reṇa sviṣṭakṛdavadānamupapadyate | tena śeṣayitavye sviṣṭakṛdavadānavacanameva pramāṇamiti cet,
na pramāṇaṃ, anyataḥ prayukte svayamaprayukte puroḍāśādiśeṣe’rthavatvāt | yattu camasava-
dityuktaṃ, tanna2 anuvaṣaṭa्kāraśruterananyaviṣayatvāt camasasya ca grahaṇamātraśruteḥ
‘somasyāgne vīhītyanuṣaṭa्karoti’
 iti yeyamanuvaṣaṭa्kāraśrutiḥ sā ananyaviṣayā, tena bhavati
śeṣayitavye pramāṇam | camasasya ca grahaṇamātraṃ śrūyate | na punarhomaḥ | tenāgrabindu-
nipātenāpi śāstrasyāvirodha iti śeṣasaṃbhavādyuktaṃ camase śeṣakāryam | darśanaṃ pratyuktam |

sākaṃ prasthāyye | kena viśeṣeṇāyamatideśaḥ ? yāvatā atrāpi śeṣābhāva eva |
uttaraṃ---prakṛtivaditi prāpteḥ kāryārthatayā śeṣaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | yuktamupāṃśuyāje
śeṣo nāstīti | śeṣakāryāṇāmeva tatsaṃbandhitvena prāpteḥ aniścitatvāt prakṛtitvādupāṃśuyājasya
(1) P. 994 (2) R. tatra

[p. r855]

vikṛtibhūtatvātsākaṃ prasthāyyasya prakṛtivaditi śeṣakāryāṇi prāpyante | teṣu prāpteṣu
tādarthyena dravyaṃ śeṣyate ityasti śeṣaḥ | na | vacanātkāryaṃ balavaditi rāddhāntaḥ |
“saha kumbhībhirabhikrāman juhoti”
 iti vacanāt saha kumbhībhirabhikramaṇasya ca dṛṣṭārthatvāt
sarvahomaḥ pratīyate | yo’pi sviṣṭakṛdādiprāptyā śeṣatvasaṃbhavaḥ, tatra vacanasya kāryato
balīyastvāt kāryaprāptasviṣṭakṛdādibādha eva nyāyya iti śeṣābhāvānna sviṣṭakṛdādīni kāryāṇi
sākaṃ prasthāyye iti rāddhāntaḥ |

sautrāmaṇyāṃ ca | nanu ‘uttare’gnau payograhān juhoti dakṣiṇe’gnau surāgrahān’
 iti
gṛhītasyaiva dravyasya homaśravaṇāt śeṣābhāvānna prakṛtitaḥ sviṣṭakṛdādiśaṅkā tatrāha---sautrā-
maṇyāṃ ‘ucchinaṣṭi na sarvahutaṃ juhoti’
 iti punarapi śeṣaśaṅkāyāṃ sviṣṭakṛdiḍaṃ manyate |
tasyānyatra viniyogāditi rāddhāntaḥ | tasya śeṣasya anyatra viniyogāt saṃbandhāt
brāhmaṇena parikrītena bhakṣaṇena pratipādyatvāt na sviṣṭakṛdiḍamiti rāddhāntaḥ |

dravyaikatve | sarvapṛṣṭhe tu pratipradhānaṃ śeṣasaṃskārādavṛttiṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī |
bahutvāt pradhānānāṃ pratipradhānaṃ ca śeṣakāryaprāpteḥ dravyasya vipulatvāt bhavitavyameva pratipradhānaṃ
(1) P. 994 (2) P. 996

[p. r856]

śeṣasaṃskāraiḥ | agṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatayā kṛtaḥ śāstrārtha iti rāddhāntaḥ | ya eva hi śeṣa-
saṃskāraḥ kṛtaḥ sa eva sarveṣāṃ śeṣāṇāmiti pratibhāti agṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt | ataḥ
kṛte śāstrārthe nāvṛttau pramāṇamastīti rāddhāntaḥ | nanu sādhāraṇe naiva sviṣṭakṛdastītyuktam
ājye | kathamatra sādhāraṇe sakṛdapi sviṣṭakṛt | pariharati---satyaṃ---akṛtatvāt kāryānta-
rāṇāṃ guṇapradhānatvācceti2 viśeṣaḥ | satyaṃ ājye sviṣṭakṛnnāsti | tatra hi eṣā yuktiḥ |
upāṃśuyāje kṛtamātra eva na bhaveccheṣakāryaṃ kāryāntarāṇāmakṛtatve pratipattyanarhatvāt | kṛteṣu
kāryāntareṣu guṇaprādhānyāccheṣiṇāṃ guṇapradhānabhāvenāvasthitānāṃ yāgānāṃ sādhāraṇaṃ śeṣatvā-
diti | ‘na punassādhāraṇaṃ dravyaṃ’
 ityetāvatī yuktiḥ | iha tu śeṣo’pyayaṃ pradhānānāmeva |
ataśśeṣakāryaṃ kāryam |

aindravāyave | nanu cātra pūrvapakṣa eva nāsti vacanāt | dviraindravāyavasya bhakṣa-
yatīti vacanāt | na hi pūrvapakṣe vacanasyānyārthatā śakyate vaktum | atrocyate
---ayamabhiprāyaḥ pūrvapakṣavādinaḥ somasaṃskārārthatvādbhaśravaṇasya dviriti prayogāntarāpekṣo-
’nuvādo bhaviṣyati | asyārthaḥ---bhakṣaṇena somaḥ saṃskriyate sa ca sakṛt bhakṣaṇenaiva saṃskṛta
(1) P. 997 (2) G. pradhānatvāya

[p. r857]

iti na dviḥ bhakṣaṇamupapadyate | tasmātprayogāntarābhiprāyeṇa dvirityanuvādaḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu

nyāyyam |

some’vacanādbhakṣo na vidyate | some śeṣabhakṣo’sti nāstīti saṃśayaḥ | nanu
saṃśaya evāyamayuktaḥ somasya śeṣabhāvāt | yuktaṃ puroḍāśādīnāṃ dvirhaviṣo’vadyatīti

nāsti | kathaṃ tarhi vacanānyetāni---‘dviraindravāyavasya’

ṣaṭa्karoti’
 iti ca | ākṣeptā āha---atra hi bhavāneva bhāṣyasamarthanāyodyataḥ

‘aindravāyavasya’
 ‘āśvinaṃ’

āśvinaṃ iti ca śakyate vaktum | uttaraṃ---dharmalakṣaṇārtho bhaviṣyati | aindravāyavasya
aindravāyavadharmakasyetyarthaḥ | tasmādanupapannamevedaṃ kiṃ some vidyate na veti | athai-
tebhyo vacanebhyaḥ śeṣāśrayebhyaḥ śeṣayitavyamiti kalpyate | na | aprayojakatvā-
ccheṣāṇāṃ na śeṣakāryāṇi dravyaṃ prayuñjate | kutaḥ ? saṃskāryapradhānaṃ hi saṃskārakarma
bhavati | saṃskāryaṃ dravyamucyate saṃskāryeṣu pradhāneṣu apradhānabhūtāssaṃskārāḥ | na cā-
pradhānaṃ pradhānaṃ prayuṅkte | tasmānna śeṣakāryairdravyaṃ prayujyate | tasmādvaktavyo’tra nyāyaḥ
śeṣasaṃbhave | ucyate---nātra sarvahome pramāṇamasti | yasmāt aindravāyavādiṣu tādarthyena
grahaṇam | gṛhītasya ca ekadeśenāpi kṛte kṛtaiva aindravāyavatā | kimiti sarvasya śeṣaḥ
ayamabhiprāyaḥ---rasībhūtassomaḥ devatoddeśena parigṛhītaḥ | tasya ca dravyasya devatoddeśaḥ evaṃ
arthavān bhavati yadi tāmeva devatāmuddiśya tyajyate | tasya ca tyajyamānasya ‘aṇvyā dhārayā
juhoti’
 iti niyamāt krameṇa tyāgaḥ | tatra tu agrabindunipātena kṛte devatoddeśena grahaṇe-
’rthavati na sarvahome pramāṇamastītyagrabindumātreṇa yāgopasaṃhārādasti śeṣaḥ | nanu ca sarvasya
yāgaśeṣatā śrūyate ‘somena yajeta’
 iti tena kṛtsnenaiva yāgaḥ kartavya iti kṛtsnasya homaḥ |
pariharati---na kṛtsnenaiva yāgaḥ | tatrāpyekadeśenaiva somenetyupapannatvāt | avayava-

[p. r859]

dvāreṇāpi karaṇasaṃbandhopapatteḥ na kṛtsnahome pramāṇam | tathā cāsya rasasya grahaṇaṃ

iti vināpi dvirhaviṣo’vadyati iti vacanaṃ ekadeśenāpi yāgopasaṃhāraḥ prāpta eva | kimitye-
tasmā1dupasaṃhāropanyāsaḥ kṛtaḥ | yathākathaṃcita tatrāpyupasaṃhārasiddheḥ | tatrāpi
puroḍāśe yathākathaṃcit yena kenacidavayavenopasaṃhāraḥ prāpnoti | rāddhāntamāṃśaṅkayati
---niyamārthaṃ taditi cet | dvayavadānavacanaṃ niyamārthamiti cet kṛtsnapradāne’pi prāpte’va-

niyame śrute sarvapradāne śaṅkā na yuktā | kiñca ekadeśenāpyupapadyata iti naitāvatā sarvaṃ
na hotavyam | sarvahomenāpyupapadyata eva | tena somaśeṣasadbhāvo nityavannopapadyata eva |

śrutatvāt | uttaraṃ---śrute’pi vacane avakhaṇḍyāvakhaṇḍya dātavyaṃ iti vacanārthāvagamāt |
avadāyāvadāya hotavyaṃ iti manyamānena sarvapradānamāśaṅkitam na sarvapradānaṃ yāgārthatvāt
dravyasyetyupasaṃhāraḥ | yāgārthaṃ hi dravyaṃ tena sakṛtkṛto yāgaḥ na guṇānurodhenāvartayitavyaḥ
ityupasaṃhāro dvayavadānena yāgasya | tasmādekadeśaniyama evāyam dvirhaviṣo’vadyatīti |
ekadeśena kṛtsnena vā yāge prāpte ekadeśe niyamo’yaṃ na punaraprāptaikadeśahomavidhiḥ |
yata evāvayavenāpi śāstrārthasaṃpattiḥ | ata eva laukikāḥ pāyasādi devatoddeśena
kṛtvā tata uda्dhṛtya kiñciddevetāyai datvā bhavati ca devatoddeśena grahaṇe’rthavati śeṣaṃ
yatheṣṭaṃ viniyuñjate | anyathā kṛtsnameva devatāyai dada्yuḥ tasmādvidyamāne śeṣe upapadyate
saṃśayaḥ kiṃ bhakṣaṇenaiva śeṣapratipattiḥ uta neti | tatra vacanābhāvānna bhakṣaṇeneti pūrvaḥ
pakṣaḥ, bhakṣaviśeṣavidhānādasti bhakṣa iti dvitīyaḥ | anyathā hi asati bhakṣe bhakṣa-
viśeṣavidhānaṃ dviraindravāyavasyetyādi nopapadyate | kāmaṃ mopapādi na punarvacanādṛte
śakyate bhakṣaḥ pratipattum | na cedaṃ nopapadyate yadā bhakṣayati tadā dvirityevaṃ viśeṣa-
vidhānopapatteḥ | evaṃ tarhi yadi nityavadbhakṣaṇaṃ na prāpnoti tadā etānyena viśiṣṭasya
bhakṣaṇasya vidhāyakāni bhavantviti rāddhāntaḥ |

[p. r861]

camaseṣu samākhyānāt | camaseṣvidānīṃ vacanābhāvānnāsti bhakṣa iti pūrva-
pakṣaḥ | tat camasataivānyathā nopapadyate yadi tatra camanaṃ na syāt | tasmāt ‘ayamasya
camasaḥ’ 
 ‘ayamasya camasaḥ’
 iti vacanāni ‘praituhotuścamasaḥ’
 ityevamādīnyeva bhakṣavidhāyakāni
bhavantu yathaiva viśeṣavidhānamanyathā nopapadyata iti pūrvatra vacanaṃ tathehāpi camasatvānupapatteḥ
camanavidhānamiti | nanu dravyāntaravidhānenāpi camasatā upapadyate ityatrāha---dravyāntara-
camanaṃ 3takṣaṇādi ca na kalpitaṃ bhavati | dravyāntarameva tāvaccamanīyaṃ kalpanīyam |
klṛptasomadravyasaṃbhave tasya cāyāgaśeṣatvāt | tacchuddhaye takṣaṇādi kalpayitavyam | some tu
tatra pānepi na somenocchiṣṭā bhavati iti vacanāt na takṣaṇakalpanā | tasmāt somacamana-
nimittaiva camasatā | sā ca vināvacanaṃ nopapadyata iti vacanānyetāni “praitu hotuścamasaḥ”

ityevamādīni |

udgātṛcamasamekasya śrutisaṃyogāt | atra catvāraḥ pakṣāḥ kimekasyodgāturbhakṣaḥ,
uta sarveṣāmṛtvijāṃ, atha vā subrahmaṇyavarjitānāṃ chandogānāṃ trayāṇāṃ, atha vā caturṇāmapi
chandogānāṃ udgātṛprastotṛpratihartṛsubrahmaṇyānāṃ iti | tatra taccamasatā anyathā nopapadyata iti
camanavacanānyetānīti sthitam |

(1) P. 999 (2) P. 1001 (3) G. bhakṣaṇādi

[p. r862]

tatra prathamapūrvapakṣavādī udgātṛpratipadikasya ṛtvigviśeṣe rūḍhatvāt prātipadikārthasya ca
pradhānatvāt tadanurodhitayā ‘pāśān’ 
 itivat bahutvamavivakṣitamiti manyate | dvitīyapūrvapakṣavādī
tu vacanārthasyāvivakṣātaḥ prātipadikasya lakṣaṇikatvavarṇanameva nyāyyam | ubhayorapi
vibhaktiprātipadikaborevamarthavattā bhaviṣyati | pāśe tu prātipadikasya lakṣaṇīyābhāvena
lakṣaṇayā varṇanāsaṃbhavāt agatyā vacanasyāvivakṣetyudgātṛlakṣitānāṃ sarveṣāmeva ṛtvijāṃ bhakṣa
ityāha---apasubrahmaṇyavādī tu gānakriyākāritayā yaugikenodgātṛśabdena trayāṇāṃ gāna-
kāriṇābhudgātṛprastortṛpratihartṝṇāṃ bhakṣa ityuktavān | rāddhāntavādī tu rūḍhiryogamapaharati iti
yaugikatve niraste ekodgātṛsamākhyātapravacanādhyetṛtvāt udgātṛśabdena chatrinyāyena sarveṣāṃ
chandogānāṃ lakṣaṇā | tataśca teṣāmeva bhakṣa ityāha---prodgātṝṇāmityatra kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ na
gānaṃ lakṣayati samākhyaiva kimiti lakṣayati| asyārthaḥ---kena punaḥ kāraṇena gāna-
kriyākartṝn na lakṣayati | yaugikaḥ udgātṛśabdaḥ kimiti na bhavatītyarthaḥ | samākhyaiva
kimiti lakṣayati kena hetunā udgātṛśabdo lakṣaṇayā vartata iti yāvat | rāddhāntaikadeśī
samarthayamāna āha---ucchabdasyāvivakṣā prāpnoti gānakāriṣu lakṣyamāṇeṣu | yadyudgāyatī-
tyadgātocyate tadā eka evodgānakārī na tu trayo’pi | yadi trayo’pi gānakāriṇaḥ
udgātṛśabdena gṛhyante tadā ucchabdasyāvivakṣā prāpnoti | tathā ca darśitam | anyadeva
gānamanyadevodgānamiti | samākhyāne tu lakṣayati---nāyaṃ doṣaḥ1 | tatra vyutpattimanta-
reṇa udgātṛśabdaḥ ṛtvigviśeṣavacano lakṣaṇayā anyānapi chandogānupasthāpayatīti nāsti
ucchabdasyāvivakṣādoṣaḥ pūrvapakṣī punarāha---nanu codgānaṃ nāma gānameva nāsti | tena
pralambata ityādivat arthāntarānupasthāpakatvāt ucchabdasya gānakāriṇa udgātṛśabdenokta
(1) naiṣa doṣaḥ

[p. r863]

iti nāstyucchabdāvivakṣādoṣaḥ | arthavato hi avivakṣādoṣaḥ nānarthakasya | bhaktirhi sā
sāmnaḥ | sāmabhāgo bhaktiḥ | yā dvitīyābhaktiḥ yāmudgātā ṛtviga्gāyati sā codgītha-
śabdavācyā rūḍhayaiva | na punaḥ tatrocchabdanvito gāyatirvartate | nanūdgīthaṃ gāyannu-
dgāyatītyucyate | ataḥ ucchabdasahito gāyatistatraiva vartate ityatrāha---yaḥ punaḥ udgāye-

tu gānameva | tatrāpi gāyatirgītimātra brūte | kevalamucchabdaḥ tatrodgīthaśabdasyāvayava-
bhūtasmamudāyalakṣaṇārthaḥ evamucchabdasahitasya gāyateḥ samudāyaśaktirna kalpitā bhavati iti |

rūḍho’yamudgātṛśabdaḥ ṛgvigviśeṣe | na punarasau yaugikaḥ; gānakāritayā sarvatra prasakteḥ
yadi hi udgānaṃ nāma gānaviśeṣaḥ syāt tadā tatkāritayā ṛtvigviśeṣe śabdavṛttirlabhyate na
caitadasti | tasmāda्‌rūḍha evāyamiti | paramasiddhāntī tvāha---yata eva rūḍho’yamudgātṛśabdaḥ
ata eva samākhyeva lakṣayituṃ prabhavati na gānakriyeti rāddhāntitam | gānakriyāyā lakṣa-
katve yaugikatvaprasaṅgāt | na ca rūḍhau satyāṃ yaugikatvāśrayaṇaṃ yuktam | nanu rūḍhyāśrayaṇe
bahutvaṃ nopapadyate | lakṣaṇayopapadyata iti cenna | lakṣaṇaiva doṣaḥ | atrābhidhīyate---rūḍhyā
yaugikatve parihṛte vacanāt tadanuguṇā lakṣaṇā | na punaḥ vacanamupalabhya parāvṛttya yaugikatvā-
śrayaṇamiti | yata eva samākhyā lakṣayati---ata eva ca apasubrahmaṇyavādino’pi
apanyāyavādina eva| ekapravacananiveśena lakṣaṇāyāṃ sarvepi tatpravacanakāriṇo lakṣyante
iti na śakyate subrahmaṇyaṃ parityuktum | nanu ‘niṣadyodgātārasstuvanti’
 iti subrahmaṇyavarjitā

vādinā śaṅkitam | vākyaśeṣāt | asyārthaḥ---“yathācamasamanyāṃścamasāṃścamasino
bhakṣayanti | athaitasya hāriyojanasya sarva eva lipsante”
 iti vākyaśeṣe camasināṃ
saṅkīrtanāt sarvaśabdaḥ camasiṣveva vartata iti camasināmeva hāriyojane bhakṣaḥ | 2na camasī
grāvastuditi tasya bhakṣābhāvaḥ | asandigdhatvāt prakṛtavacanatayā sarvaśabdasya nopasaṃhāro
yuktaḥ vākyaśeṣāditi rāddhāntaḥ | asyārthaḥ---sarve hāriyojanasyeti nissandigdhasakala-
ṛtvikpratyayaḥ | prakṛtāśca ṣoḍaśartvijaḥ | prakṛtavacanaśca sarvaśabdaḥ | sa yadi cama-
siṣveva avasthāpyate tadā sarvaśabdārtho bādhyeta | tasmādvākyaśeṣādupasaṃhāro na yuktaḥ |
yadi vākyaśeṣe camasiśabdaḥ parokṣārthavṛttiḥ syāt tadāpyupasaṃhāro’stu | na ca camasiśabdasya
parokṣārthatā camasāṃścamasino bhakṣayanti | hāriyojane tu sarve bhakṣayantīti mahābhāgatayā
hāriyoṃjana stūvate iti rāddhāntaḥ |

 iti guṇaśravaṇāt na

hi viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ bhavati yatra hi viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvaḥ saṃbhavati | na ca prāthamyabhakṣaṇayo-
rviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvaḥ saṃbhavati | akārakatvāt prāthamyasya | kārakaṃ hi kārakavibhaktyā kriyāṃ
prati viśeṣaṇatayā upādīyate | tena tatra yukto viśiṣṭavidhiḥ | yatra punarakārakatvāt na
kārakavibhaktyā nirdiśyate na tatra kriyāsaṃbandha iti kuto viśiṣṭavidhānam | na ca prāthamyaṃ
kārakaṃ, kramo hi saḥ | sa ca padārthāśritaḥ padārthaguṇo na kārakaṃ, padārthanivṛttāvavyā-
pārāt | tasmāt rāddhānta evānupapanna iti | samādhātā āha---yata eva viśiṣṭa-
bidhānaśaṅkā nāsti | ata eva pūrvapakṣavādī na viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ saṃbhavatītyāśaṅkitavān |
ākṣeptā’’ha---bhavatu pūrvapakṣaḥ, kathaṃ tarhi rāddhāntaḥ ? samādhātā āha---prathamabhakṣa
iti padadvayasya ekārthābhidhānāt abhihitavidhānācca ekārthavirdhi manyate | asyārthaḥ
---yadi viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvo na bhavet tadā na syādekārthavidhiḥ | yadā punaḥ prathamabhakṣa
iti samāsaḥ tatra ca padadvayena prāthamyaviśiṣṭaḥ eka eva bhakṣarūpo’rtho’bhidhīyate | yaccābhi-
dhīyate tadeva vidhīyate iti abhidhānādeva ekārthavidhiḥ | ākṣeptā āha---tadayuktam

iti vadatā tvayā pratipadamarthānabhyupagamāta sphoṭa evābhyupagataḥ | sa cāyuktaḥ | samāseṃ
hi tada्bhūtādhikaraṇanyāyasya pratipadābhidhānasādhakasya naivāpagatirasti | tena kathamekārthā-
bhidhānam ? samādhatte---satyamevaṃ, tathāpi pūrvottarapadasaṃbandhāyattā samāse ca padārthā-
ntarasaṅgatiḥ | asyārthaḥ---nāhaṃ sphoṭapakṣamaṅgīkṛtya ekārthābhidhānaṃ samāse bravīmi, kintu

padadvayena viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhūtau arthau abhidadhatā viśiṣṭa eko’rthaḥ pratipādyate | tasya ca
padārthāntareṇa paścātsaṃbandhaḥ | vākye tu padārthāntarasaṃbandhānāṃ parasparaṃ saṃbandhaḥ | nīla-
mutpalamānaya iti dvayorānayatinā saṃbandhaḥ pūrvaṃ, paścāt guṇaguṇisaṃbandhaḥ | samāse tu
‘nīlotpalamānaya’
 iti guṇaguṇibhāvena parasparānvitayoreva ānayanasaṃbandha | tenehāpi
prathamabhakṣa iti prāthamyabhakṣayoḥ guṇaguṇibhāvena saṃbaddhayoḥ karotyupāttena vidhinā saṃbandhaḥ, na
punaḥ parasparāsaṃbandhayoḥ | tadā hi anekārthavidhānaṃ bhavati | samāse ca parasparasaṃbandhaḥ
pratīyata iti yuktamuktaṃ samāsena ca vidadhato nānekaguṇavidhānaṃ duṣkaramiti | ataḥ
siddhamatra viśiṣṭavidhānam | evaṃ ca vadato bhāṣyanibandhanakārasya abhihitakramo
viniyojyo vidheyaśca | yaḥ punaḥ pāñcamikaśrutyādigamyaḥ vidhivitānākhyo’bhidhānaviniyoga-
śūnyaḥ nāsau vidhīyata iti darśanaṃ lakṣyate | sarvatraiva hi śabdaśaktivaśenāyaṃ śāstrārthaṃ
varṇayati | vivaraṇakārastu vastusvabhāvanirūpaṇena | tena tanmate sarvatraiva kramasyā-
vidheyatvamiti tatraivoktam |

homābhiṣavābhyāṃ ca | ‘abhiṣutya hutvā bhakṣayati’
 ityapi bhakṣavidhānam |
kasmātpunaḥ bhakṣaṇaṃ vidhīyate iti darśanaṃ ? abhiṣutya hutveti kimiti homabhiṣavāveva na
vidhīyete bhakṣaṇamanūdyata evārthaprāptaṃ iti tatrāha---homābhiṣavayoḥ prāptatvāt tatra | nanu
ca homābhiṣavāveva bhakṣaṇāṅgatayā kimiti na vidhīyete | bhakṣaṇaṃ vā taṅgatayā kimiti na

pratyakṣopadeśāt | hotṛcamasaḥ ityevamādyābhiḥ samākhyābhiḥ sakalacamasa-
saṃbandhāvagateḥ abhiṣutya hutvā ityanyatra camasebhyo bhakṣaḥ iti manyate pūrvapakṣavādī |
sakalasya camasya hotrādisaṃbaddhatvāt camasavyatirekeṇa homābhiṣavanimittaṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ bhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ |
evaṃ prāpte ucyate---abhiṣavanimittako’pi bhakṣaḥ camaseṣu bhavatyeva | nanu hotṛcamasa
iti sakalacamasāvagatirityuktaṃ, satyamuktaṃ, na punarabhiṣavanimittatāṃ bhakṣaṇasya bādhi-
tumarhati | abādhite ca bhakṣasya homābhiṣavanimittatve prāpnotyeva camaseṣvapi tannimitto bhakṣaḥ
yasmāt prāpakāṇyetāni vacanāni na punaḥ parisaṅkhyāpakāni | kuta ityāha---hotṛcamasa
ityasyāṃ saṃbandhāvagatau na saṃbandhāntaranivṛttiḥ śakyate’vagantum | prāptimātrāvagateḥ |
prāptau hi satyāṃ kasyedamityapekṣāyāṃ anyanivṛttiḥ pratīyate | sāmānyataḥ prāptau satyāṃ
kasyedamiti viśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ viśaṣanirdeśāt anyanivṛttiḥ pratīyate---yathā ‘āgneya’
 ityukte

naindrāgnasyāvagatiḥ | iha punaḥ prāptiphalatvādvākyasya nānyanivṛttiḥ śakyate’vagantum

nanu ca prāptāvapi prāpakatvepi vākyānāṃ sakalāvagatervirodho vidyata eva | sakala-
saṃbandhāvagamāddhi sakalaścamasibhiḥ bhakṣyata iti lakṣyate | tenānyasmin bhakṣaṇāntare sati
virodho’styeva | pariharati---satyaṃ virodhaḥ syāt---yadi samākhyayā prāptiḥ | iha punaḥ
praitu hotuścamasa iti vacanāt prāptiḥ | asyārthaḥ---satyaṃ yadi sakalacamasasaṃbandhaḥ hotuḥ

dravyamasti sa tāvat sannihitaṃ bhakṣayati paścāditare iti | nanu vaṣaṭa्kartuḥ prathamabhakṣa iti
vacanāt kathamadhvaryorbhakṣaḥ | na vacanapratipakṣo nyāyya iti bhāvaḥ | uttaraṃ---bhakṣamātra-
vidhānādapi vaṣaṭa्kartureva prathamaṃ bhakṣa iti manvānasya punaridamadhikaraṇam | asyārthaḥ

evetyabhiprāyaḥ | yājyāprayoga evedamanavānaṃ avicchedātmakaṃ ghaṭate tathā yājyāyā api
yāganiṣpattyarthatvāt taduccāraṇepi bhavatyeva yāgaṃ prati kartṛteti anavānayāgakartṛtvarūpadvayaṃ yājyā-
prayogamātrepyupapadyata iti na vaṣaṭkārāpanaye kiñcitpramāṇamasti | etadeva vivṛṇoti---yājyābhi-
dhāne’pi yajeḥ kartṛtvamupapadyata evetyarthaḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu manyate---satyamupapannam |
na punaḥ vaṣaṭa्kārāpanaye hi kiñcitpramāṇamasti iti savaṣaṭa्kārameva yājyāprayogamātrepyu-
papadyate iti vyāpāramaṅgīkṛtya kartṛtvaṃ yuktamiti | asyārthaḥ---yatraiva hotā prāptaḥ
tatraiva yajamāno’pi niyujyate savaṣaṭa्kāre ca hotā prāptaḥ iti, savaṣaṭ्kāra eva yājyāvyāpāre
ca hotā prāpta iti savaṣaṭa्kāra eva yājyāvyāpāre yajamānaviniyoga iti na vaṣaṭa्kārāpanayane
kiñcitpramāṇamasti | tataścāpanīte vaṣaṭa्kāre nāsti hotuḥ vaṣaṭa्kāranimitto bhakṣaḥ iti |
yattu pravaro nāpanīyata iti tadatraupadeśikatvāt yathā pravaro nāpanīyate
tathā darśitam | asyārthaḥ---pravarastāvadaupadeśikaḥ sa yatraiva saguṇaḥ kartuṃ śakyate
tatraiva kartavyaḥ hotari cāsau saguṇaḥ kartuṃ śakyaḥ, na yajamāne bhraṣṭāvasaratvāt |
vikṛtau hi bādhāt viguṇamapi kriyate na punaḥ prakṛtāviti darśitaṃ bhāṣye |

phalacamaso naimittiko bhakṣavikāraḥ | phalacamasaḥ ijyāvikāraḥ uta bhakṣavikāra
ityasmin saṃśaye brūmaḥ | naivātra saṃśayaḥ yasmāt tamasmai bhakṣaṃ prayacchet iti bhakṣasaṃba-
(1) P. 1016

[p. r874]

ndhitvena vidhānāt bhakṣe somaṃ vikaroti na yāge yatraiva hi śrūyate tatraiva vidhīyamānaḥ
tatsādhānabhūtameva nivartayati | kārakaṃ hi bhakṣe phalacamasaḥ śrūyate na yāge tamasmai bhakṣaṃ
kuryāditi śravaṇāt na tena yajeteti | evaṃ tāvat yāgasaṃbandhitvenāśravaṇāduktam |
idānīṃ satyapi saṃbandhaśravaṇe aśakyatāmāha---na cejyāvikāraśśakyate vaktum utpatti-
śiṣṭatvāt somasya | somena yejetatyutpattāveva yāgaḥ somānvitaḥ pratīto nānyānvitaṃ
pratyetuṃ śakyate | vidhīyamāne ca dravyāntare ‘guṇaścāpūrvasaṃyoga’
 ityanena nyāyena
---karmāntaratvāpattiḥ | tataśca bhakṣavikāro’pi na syāt dūreṇejyāvikāraḥ | atrā-
bhidhīyate---nejyāvikāramantareṇa bhakṣaṃ vikartuṃ samarthaḥ phalacamasaḥ | kutaḥ ? ijyā-
śeṣasya hi somasya bhakṣaḥ | na cāśeṣabhūte bhakṣyamāṇe sa vikṛto bhavati | asyārthaḥ
---yadi somenaiva yāgaḥ tadā na śakyate yāgaśeṣapratipattibhūtabhakṣavikāritāpi phalacamasasya
vaktum | yadi hi somenejyate sa eva bhakṣayitavyaḥ | na ca phalacamasena tasya bhakṣaṇaṃ
nivārayituṃ śakyate | nahyanyasmin pratipādite anyatpratipāditaṃ bhavati | tasmāt
pratipattibhūtabhakṣasaṃbandhānupapatteḥ ijyāśeṣataiva phalacamasasyāpi mantavyā | śrutabhakṣasaṃbandha---
siddhayarthaṃ aśrutamapi yāgasaṃbandhitvaṃ kalpayitavyam | nanu cotpattiśiṣṭatvāt somasya yāge
vidhānaṃ na saṃbhavatītyuktam | pariharati---yāgānuvādena vidhāne hi apratyabhijñāna-

muktam na punaḥ kāryeṇāpi saṃgandhe | asyārthaḥ---yadi yajiśabdena yāgamanūdya phalacamaso
vidhīyate tadā guṇādbhinnaṃ yāgāntaraṃ syāt | yadā tu kāryeṇāyaṃ saṃbandhaḥ tadā nāsti bhedaḥ
tadyāgasaṃbandhibhakṣasaṃbandhena hi yāgānupraveśe’ṅgīkriyamāṇe na yāgāntaraśaṅkāpi bhavatīti |
kāryataścāyaṃ saṃbandhaḥ bhakṣaṃ kuryāditi śrūyate | yathā bhakṣo bhavati tathā kartavyam | yadi
cāsau yāgaśeṣo bhavati tadā bhakṣo bhavatīti---kāryata evāyaṃ saṃbandhaḥ | nanu ca bhāṣyakāreṇa
yāgasaṃbandhābhāvaṃ codayitvā yājayediti saṃbandha uktaḥ | tena yāgānuvādena phalacamasa-
saṃbandha ukto bhāṣyakāreṇa na kāryataḥ | samādhatte---yāgānvayapradarśanārthaṃ taduktaṃ na punaḥ

bhakṣaṃ kuryādini copasaṃhāra imamevārthaṃ bhakṣasaṃbandhadvāreṇa yāgasaṃbandhirūpaṃ prakaṭīkaroti |

darbhataruṇakādiguṇavidhayaśca pradarśitāḥ |

anuprasarpiṣu sāmānyāt | daśa daśa ekaikaṃ camasamanuprasarpantī’ti vidhānāt
śata brāhmaṇāḥ somān bhakṣayantīti anuvādaṃ manvānasya nābhakṣo rājeti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
(1) 1021

[p. r876]

rājasūye rājño’dhikārāt yajamānacamase prāpte rājño eva na syāt yadi śataṃ brāhmaṇāḥ somān
bhakṣayantīti vidhiḥ syāt brāhmaṇavidhau abrāhmaṇasya rājño nivṛttirbhaveti | yadā daśa daśa ekaikaṃ
camasamanuprasarpantītyeva vidhiḥ tadā saṅkalananibandhanā ca śatasaṅkhyā’nūdyate śata brāhmaṇā iti
brāhmaṇaśabdaśca brāhmaṇeṣu bhūyastvena lakṣaṇayā vartate tadā rājño’pi yukta iti | anuvādaścāyaṃ
yuktaḥ anyathā codakaprāptabhakṣānuvādena anekaguṇavidhānādvākyabhedaḥ syāt iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
rāddhāntastu satyameva yadyanuvādaḥ syāt | na cānuvṛtte1 rājani anuvādaśśakyate
vaktum ayathārthānuvādaprasaṅgāt | na ceti caśabdaḥ tuśabdasyārthe | yadyanuvādaḥ
syāt tadā syādeva rājño’pi bhakṣaḥ natvayamanuvādaḥ | 2rājanyānivṛttau brāhmaṇaśabdasya
ayathārthānuvādaprasaṅgāt | vidhau tu nāyathārthatā| tasmādvidhirevāyaṃ śataṃ brāhmaṇā iti| nanu cānu-
vāde ayathārthānuvādatvamadoṣaḥ bhūyastvena lakṣaṇā bhaviṣyati | anuvāde yathāprāptamanūdyata iti
brāhmaṇābrāhmaṇāḥ yathā prāptāḥ tathā teṣāmanuvādo lakṣaṇayeti | uttaraṃ-satyaṃ yadyekāntato’nuvādaḥ |
iha tu vidheyatāpyupapadyata eveti nāyathārthatvamāśrayituṃ śakyate | tasmāt dvayamapyetadvidhīyate
---śataṃ brāhmaṇāḥ daśa daśa ekaikaṃ casamaṃ iti ca | nanu ca ‘samaṃ syādaśrutatvāt’
 iti
nyāyena śate vibhajyamāne ekaikatra daśadaśaprāpteḥ daśa daśa ekaikaṃ camasamanuprasarpantītyanu-
vādaḥ | kathaṃ ayaṃ vidhiruktaḥ iti codyamātraṃ na tu pūrvapakṣaḥ | yasmādanuvādatvepi
