[p. r877]

yathārthānuvādatayā ayamevānuvādo yuktaḥ na punaḥ śataṃ brāhmaṇā iti ayathārthaprasaṅgāt | tasmāt
codyamātramevedam | pariharati---na ekaikatra śataprāpteḥ pratyuddeśaṃ vākyasamāpteḥ | ekai-
katra camase śataṃ syāt tanmābhūditi daśasaṅkhyā vidhīyate | adhikaraṇārthamupasaṃharati
---tasmādabhakṣo rājā brāhmaṇavidhānāditi | yatpunaratra śataṃ brāhmaṇāssomān ityanekārtha-
vidhānaṃ manyate tadayuktaṃ yasmāt karmāntaravidhānenāpi prākṛtakāryārthatāṃ daśame vakṣyāmaḥ |
asyārthaḥ---bhakṣāntaramevedaṃ anekaguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ vidhīyatāṃ tathā dṛṣṭārthatayā prākṛtabhakṣaṇaṃ
nivartayiṣyatīti | etaccābhyupagamamātram karmāntaravidhānenāpītyapiśabdena prakaṭīkṛtam |
paramārthatastu daśadaśa ekaikaṃ iti vidhiḥ tatpūrvakaṃ śataṃ brāhmaṇa iti brāhmaṇamātravidhiḥ śata-
mityetadanuvāda iti sarvaṃ samañjasam |

iti śrīṛjuvimalāyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya
pañcamaḥ pādaḥ

[p. r878]

atha tṛtīyādhyāyasya ṣaṣṭhaḥ pādaḥ

sarvārthamaprakaraṇāt | kiṃ punaratrakāraṇaṃ pakṣadvayopanyāsasya ? kasya 1pakṣa-
dvayopanyāsasya ? uttaraṃ---codakādanārabhya vidhirbalīyān sāmānyato dṛṣṭatvācceti
prathamasya tāvatpakṣasya codakādanārabhyavidhirbalīyānityasya idaṃ prayojanam yatpūrvapakṣa-
samarthanam | kathaṃ ? tatrāha---naivātra dviruktatā vidyate prakṛtivikṛtyoḥ sruksaṃbhavāt |
asyārthaḥ---yadi vikṛtyarthatve anārabhyavidheḥ dviruktatālakṣaṇo doṣaḥ syāt tadā syādeva
kevalaprakṛtyarthatā | yāvatā naiva vikṛtyarthatvepi dviruktatādoṣaḥ kintu prakṛtāvupadeśāprāptāṃ
vikṛtāvapi codakaprāptāṃ srucamuddiśya parṇatā vidhīyate | tenāsāvubhayatraupadeśikīti | ato
vikṛtāvapi punaścodakato na prāpnotyeva | codakaprāpteruttarakālabhāvitvāt | etaduktaṃ/?/
bhavati | anārabhya vidhiṃ varjayitvaiva tāvat prakṛtāvārabhya vihitāneva padārthān gṛhītvā
upakāraḥ kathyate | taduttarakālaṃ ca vikṛtiṣvatideśaḥ; evaṃ prakṛtivikṛtiṣu srucāṃ
prāptānāṃ samānavidhānena anārabhya vidhinā parṇamayītvādikaṃ vidhīyata iti | dvitīyapakṣopanyāsa-
samarthanamāha---evaṃ tarhi sāmānyatodarśanena prakṛtyarthatetyaparasya pakṣasyopanyāsaḥ prāya-
(1) P. 1025 (2) G. pakṣadvayasya

[p. r879]

darśanavat | yadi sruksaṃbhavāt prakṛtivikṛtyoraviśeṣaḥ evaṃ tarhi tathā prāyadarśanena nirṇayaḥ
tathā’trāpi sāmānyato dṛṣṭena saṃbhavati prakṛtyarthatāniścayaḥ | prakṛtau saṃkṣepavistāropadeśaḥ |
ayamapi saṃkṣepavistāra eveti prakṛtyarthatā pratibhātītyayaṃ tāvat anantarapakṣo rāddhāntā-
bhāsarūpo nirākriyate | 1na sāmānyato dṛṣṭaṃ śabdārthāvagatau hetuḥ | ayamāśayaḥ---vaidikaṃ
hi śeṣatvam | na ca tatra sāmānyato dṛṣṭasyānumānasyāvasaraḥ | siddhārthaviṣayatvāt
pratyakṣādīnāṃ na kāryasvarūpe śaktiḥ | prāyadarśanaṃ tu śabdārthāvagatau heturbhavatyeva śabda-
pramāṇaśeṣatvena loke dṛṣṭatvāt | tena śabda eva viśeṣaviṣaye prāyadaśanenopasyāyate | na
punaścānumānavat svatantraḥ pramāṇam | tacca prāyadarśanamapīha nāsti | vikṛtāvapi tathā
vidhopadeśadarśanāt | tadāha---nāpi prāyadarśanaṃ prakṛtāviva vikṛtāvapi saṃkṣepavistāro-
padeśadarśanāt | idānīmaparasya pakṣasya nirasanaṃ codakāpekṣaścedanārabhya vidhiḥ prakṛtau
pūrvamupadeśo’pekṣaṇīyaḥ upadeśāpekṣatvādatideśasya | asyārthaḥ---vikṛtau sruksaṃbhavārthaṃ
codakāpekṣaścet anārabhyavidhiḥ tarhi pūrvaṃ prakṛtāvupadeśo’pekṣaṇīyaḥ | kutaḥ ? upadeśāpekṣa-
tvādatideśasya | upadeśabalena hi prakṛtau klṛpte upakāre tanmukhena vikṛtāvatideśaḥ padārthānāṃ
bhavati | tena upadeśasamāptyarthaṃ prakṛtau tāvadanārabhyavidhiḥ niviśate ityaṅgīkaraṇīyam |
na cānārabhyavidhinirapekṣaiva prakṛtāvupakārakalpanā, sakalāṅgasaṃpādyatvāditikartavyatāyāḥ |
upadeśena cet prakṛtāvupasaṃhṛtaḥ svārthatvena gṛhīto’nārabhyavidhiḥ tadā vikṛtau
praveśārthaṃ srukprāptaye nātideśamapekṣate | vikṛtāvapi codakaprāptāṃ srucamapekṣya na parṇamayītvā-
(1) G. sa

[p. r880]

dikaṃ vidhatta iti yāvat | kutaḥ ? kāryata eva vikṛtau prāptatvāt parṇatādīnām |
na ca prāpte punarvidheravakāśo’sti | ato dvirukterasaṃbhavāt adviruktatvāditi sthitaḥ
sūtrokto hetuḥ | atra bhāṣyākāreṇa yāgenetikartavyatā saṃbadhyata ityuktam kastasyopayoga
ityatrāha---yastu yāgasaṃbandhopanyāsaḥ asāvavaśyaṃbhāvī codaka iti darśayitum | avaśyaṃ-
bhāvitve cādviruktatvasya siddhiruktā | asyārthaḥ---adviruktatvalakṣaṇahetusamarthanārthaṃ avaśyaṃ
bhāvitāṃ codakasya darśayituṃ yāgasaṃbandhaḥ itikartavyatāyā upanyastaḥ | yadi hi yāga-
nirākāṅkṣā vaikṛtā bhaveyuḥ tadā codako na bhaveti codakābhāve ca parṇatā na prāpnoti iti sā
vidhīyate | yadā tu yāgenetikartavyatā saṃbadhyate na pātraiḥ tadā yadyapi ca pātrāṇyanārabhya-
vidhinaiva nirākāṅkṣībhavanti tathāpi yāgasyākāṅkṣā’stītyavaśyaṃ codaka utpādayitavyaḥ | sa
cedutpādayitavyaḥ tarhi parṇatāpi prāptā | na ca prāptāpi sā bādhituṃ śakyate iti sthitaḥ
dvirukterasaṃbhavaḥ siddhāntahetuḥ |

prakaraṇāviśeṣāttu vikṛtau virodhi syāt | pūrvapakṣamāha---nanu cānārabhyādhītaṃ
sāptadaśyaṃ yacca prakaraṇādhītaṃ pāñcadaśyaṃ ubhayamapi śrutyaiva sāmidhenīsaṃbandhitayā
(1) 1029 (2) P. 1029

[p. r881]

codyate viniyujyate | ko’yaṃ prakaraṇopanyāsaḥ ? ekagranthenāha--naivātra
prakaraṇaṃ viniyojakaṃ yena prakaraṇavidhirbalīyān | asyārthaḥ---saptadaśa sāmidhenī-
ranubrūyāditi yadanārabhyādhītaṃ sāptadaśyaṃ ca ‘pañcadaśasāmidhenīranubrūyāt’
 iti
prakaraṇādhītaṃ pāñcadaśyaṃ ubhayamapi śrutyaiva sāmidhenīsaṃbandhitayā codyate, viniyujyate na
prakaraṇeneti | yadi prakaraṇena viniyogaḥ syāt tadā yatprakaraṇādhītaṃ tadeva viniyujyate
nānyat | na cātra prakaraṇaṃ viniyojakam | ata ubhayorapi viniyoga iti | āśaṅkayati
---athāyamabhiprāyaḥ prakaraṇasavyapekṣā eva śrutyādayaḥ viniyojakā iti saptamādye sthā-
syati | tena yasyaiva prakaraṇapāthaḥ tasyaiva śrutyādiviniyogo nānyasyeti | dūṣayati---
satyaṃ grāhakalakṣaṇaprakaraṇasavyapekṣāḥ na tu viniyojakamapekṣante | grāhakasya savya-
pekṣā ca anārabhyavidherapi tulyaiva | pāñcadaśyamapi sāmidhenīsaṃbandhamukhenaiva gṛhyate
sāmidhenīsaṃbandhaśca anārabhyadhītasyāpi tulya iti dvayoḥ grāhakagrahaṇāviśeṣāt | ato’trāpi
dvirukterasaṃbhavāt prakṛtādeva sāptadaśyamiti yuktam | rāddhāntamāha---astyatra viśeṣa
prakṛtatve hi pāñcadaśyasya grāhakapratyabhijñānaṃ saṃbhavati avyavāyāt, anārabhyavidhe
kāryato grāhakāvamarśaḥ | asyārthaḥ---yatkila sādhikāragrāhakāvasthāniyogasannidhau śrūyate
tattadarthamiti śrutyaivāvagamyate | yattu punaḥ dūre śrūyate tasya tadīyapadārthasaṃbandhakartavyatā

[p. r882]

nyathānupapattyā tada्grāhakasaṃbandhaḥ | tena yadeva śīghraṃ gṛhītaṃ tadeva śīghraṃ viniyujyate
viniyogasāmagrīśaughrayāt | yatpunarvilanbitaṃ tatpratipakṣākrāntatvāt śeṣiṇo na tatra niviśata
ityanyatra viniyujyate | tadāha---evaṃ ca sati na klṛpte pāñcadaśye pratipakṣe saṃbhavati
sāptadaśyakalpanā saṃbhavati iti na prakṛtau niveśaḥ | pratipattyanurodhinī hi kalpanā na
pratipannopamardinī | tasmādvikṛtau sāptadaśyamiti yuktam | ihādhikaraṇe etadvayutpādyate
kiṃ padārthasaṃbandhamukhena prakṛtamaṅgaṃ gṛhyate uta sannidhānamātreṇeti | tatra padārthasaṃbandhamukhena grahaṇaṃ
pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | anyathā dvādaśopasattāyā api jyotiṣṭomagrāhyatvopapatteḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu
sannidhānamātreṇa grahaṇamāha | dvādaśopasattāyāstu balīyasā viniyogasannidhānena niyogasannidherbā-
dhitatvāt na jyotiṣṭomena grahaṇam | na ca sannidhānamātreṇa grahaṇe’tiprasaṅgaḥ sannidhānasyaiva
sarvathā bhāvāditi |

naimittikaṃ tu prakṛtau | kathaṃ punaratra pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā ? | ayamāśayaḥ---yathā
prakṛtau camasaḥ śrūyate tathā vikṛtāvapyatidiśyate tenobhayatra kratvarthāvirodhāt kena viśeṣeṇa
vikṛtau niveśa āśaṅkyate ? uttaraṃ---itthamāśaṅkā | kratvarthe bādhyamāne upadeśaḥ
pīḍito bhavati | na copadeśādṛte sāda्guṇyāvagatirbhavati | yathopadeśaṃ kāryaṃ prakṛtau
(1) P. 1032

[p. r883]

tena yadi camaso bādhyeta prakṛtau kratorvaiguṇyaṃ syāt vikṛtau punaḥ kāryata eva sāda्guṇyāvagati-
rbhavati ityekakāryatvāt puruṣārthenāṣi kratoḥ sāda्guṇyaṃ śakyate vaktum kāryaviśeṣāt
kāryato hi vikṛtau padārthāḥ prāpyante | tatra puruṣārthenāpyekārthakāriṇā kāryaṃ yadi
nirvartitaṃ bhavati tadā nāstyeva vaiguṇyāvagatiḥ padārthapradhānabhāvatvāt | tasmāt puruṣārthānāṃ
paraṃ vikṛtau niveśaḥ | satyamevaṃ yadi prakṛtāvapi kāryaviśeṣo na syāt | iha tu dṛṣṭārthatvā-
dupadeśasya camasādīnāṃ tulyaḥ kāryaviśeṣaḥ | tena puruṣārthena kṛte kārye camaso bādhyate
bādhite cānanuṣṭhīyamāne kratorvaiguṇyaṃ nāsti | nanu kena viśeṣeṇa godohanena camaso bādhyate |
kāmyatvāditi cet kā tarhi vārtā phalacamase tasmādvaktavyaṃ phalacamase | taducyate |
phalacamase viśeṣāvagamācca sāmānyato balīyastvaṃ viśeṣopadeśasya padādivadeva,
viśeṣabhūtapaśukāmarājanyādiviṣayasya viśeṣopadeśasya sāmānyabhūtadarśapūrṇamāsajyotiṣṭomādhikṛta-
mātraviṣayāt sāmānyata upadeśāt āhavanīyopadeśādivat valīyastvamiti prakṛtāveva niveśo
naimittikānāmiti |

iṣṭayarthamagnyādheyaṃ prakaraṇāt | atra bhāṣyakāraḥ iṣṭayarthamādhānaṃ prakaraṇāditi
pūrvapakṣayati | tadākṣipati---kathaṃ punaratreṣṭeḥ prakaraṇaṃ ? iṣṭerapi hi naivādhikāraśrutirasti |
(1) 1037

[p. r884]

ādhānasyaiva hi sādhikārasya vidheḥ prakaraṇam | taduktaṃ---“ākāṅkṣādvayasya tatprasṛteḥ”

iti | na ca pavamāneṣṭeradhikāraśrutiriti | tasmānneṣṭeḥ prakaraṇamiti śakyate vaktum |
evamākṣipte sati ekadeśī samādhātāraṃ pṛcchati---kathaṃ tarhi pūrvapakṣaḥ ? iti | samādhātā
ekadeśinameva parihāsena pṛcchati---bhavato vā ko’bhiprāyaḥ iti | ekadeśyāha
---na kiñcit pratibhāti | paramasamādhātā āha---atrābhidhīyate dvayamapyetaduhāraṇam |
kimādhānaṃ pradhānaṃ uteṣṭiriti | asyārthaḥ dvayamapyekattāvat agnyarthaṃ na bhavati | tathā
hi---viniyuktā agnayaḥ dvayasya śeṣiṇaḥ aviniyuktā vā ? viniyuktāgnyarthatve parṇatādi-
vatkatvaṅgatvaprasaṅgaḥ | aviniyuktāstu kāryaśūnyāḥ saktuvanna śeṣitāmarhanti iti sthite
adhikāraḥ kalpayitavyaḥ | tatra kiṃ pavamāneṣṭiṣvadhikāraḥ kalpyatāṃ tā eva pradhānaṃ bhavantu,
yadi vā ādhāne adhikārakalpanā tadeva pradhānamiti dvayamapyetadādhānapavamāneṣṭirūpamudāharaṇa-
miti | tatra dvayoḥ prādhānyamayuktamiti manvānasya iṣṭeścāṅgabāhulyāt prādhānyāropeṇa
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | bhūyastvenobhayaśrutirityanena nyāyena aṅgabāhulyāt prādhānyaṃ kalpayitvā tadaṅga-
mādhānamiti yuktam | paramārthatastu agnyarthatā dvayornāstīti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntastu
dvayorapyadhikārābhāvāt | agnyarthatayā ca viniyogopapatteḥ dvayoragnyarthatvam |
yadyapi bhūto viniyogo nāsti tathāpi bhāvināpi 1viniyogena agnīnāṃ śeṣitvopapatteḥ agnyartha-
mevedaṃ dvayamiti | tathā ca liṅgadarśanam iti |

(1) G. niyogena

[p. r885]

tatprakṛtyarthaṃ yathānye | sthite hyagnyarthatve kimidānīṃ prakṛtyarthamādhānaṃ uta sarvārtha-
miti | anena bhāṣyakārīyasaṃśayo’nubhāṣitaḥ | atra kāraṇabhūtaṃ saṃśayamāha---etaduktaṃ
bhavati---kiṃ kratvarthānāmādhānaṃ uta āhitānāmagnīnāṃ kratvarthateti | yadi kratvarthānā-
magnīnāṃ ādhānaṃ tadā parṇātādivat kratoraṅgam | tataśca tadvadeva prakṛtyarthatā’sya syāt |
atha tu āhitānāmagnīnāṃ kratvarthatā tadi svatantrāgnīsaṃbandhāt na kratoraṅgam | tenāhavanī-
yādimātrasaṃbandhāt āhavanīyādīnāṃ ca sarvakratvarthatvāt taddvāreṇa sarvārthamādhānamityucyate
paramārthatastu na kasyacidaṅgamiti | imamarthaṃ praśnapūrvakaṃ prakaṭīkaroti---kaścātra viśeṣaḥ ?
ayamasti viśeṣaḥ | yadi kratvarthānāmādhānaṃ tadā parṇamayītvavat prakṛtigāmitvamādhānavidheḥ |
atha tvāhitānāṃ kratvarthatā tadā homādimātraśeṣatayā agnīnāṃ sarvārthamādhānamityucyate
pūrvapakṣamāha---kiṃ punaratra yuktam ? evaṃ manyate---parṇatādivat kratusaṃbandhānāmevāgnīnāṃ
ādhānamiti | rāddhāntamāha---āhitāgneradhikārāt āhitānāṃ kratvarthateti yuktam |
asyārthaḥ---yathā’dhikṛtenaivāvaghātādi kriyate tathā ādhānam | kintu āhitogneḥ sataḥ
karmavidhiṣvadhikāraḥ niyatopāyatvādagnīnāṃ prayogakāle ca kartumaśakyatvāt | ataḥ puruṣeṇa
lohasuvarṇādivat prayogāt bahiragrayaḥ upārjyante | upārjitāgneśca karmaṇi samarthatvādadhikāraḥ |
tataśca prāgadhikārābhāvāt grāhakagrahaṇāsiddheḥ agnīnāṃ viniyogābhāvāt na tanmukhena paṇītāvat

[p. r886]

kratvaṅgabhāvaḥ | yathā vā āṅitāgneradhikāraḥ tathā ṣaṣṭhe yathāviniyogamadhikāra iti
varṇayanto vakṣyāmaḥ | yadā ādhānamanaṅgaṃ tadā vasantādikālavidhiścopapannataro bhavati |
itarathā aṅgatve sati ‘aṅgānāṃ mukhyakālatvāt’
 iti mukhyakālatve prāpte jyotiṣṭhome vasanta-
vidheranuvādaḥ | pradhānakāla eva prāpteḥ darśapūrṇamāsādiṣu kālāntareṣvanuṣṭhīyamāneṣu aprāptā-
dhānakālavidhiḥ | basante anuṣṭhīyamāneṣu prāptādhānakālavidhyanuvādaḥ | na caika eva vidhiḥ
kadācidanuvādaḥ kadācidvidhiriti yuktam | vaiṣamyaprasaṅgāt | āsminmate tūpapannataro
bhavati | sarvatra svakālavidhānāt tadaṅgakādhānavidhāyakatvāditi |

tāsāmagniḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ pavamāneṣṭiṣu pavamāneṣṭisaṃskṛtā agnayaḥ āśaṅkyante |
yā agnyarthā iṣṭayaḥ tā api pavamāneṣṭaya eva | tasmādanāśaṅkanīyamevedamāśaṅkitam |
asyārthaḥ---yadeva yasya kārthaṃ tadeva tasya kāraṇaṃ na bhavati pavamāneṣṭavaścahavanīyādīnāṃ
kāraṇam | kathaṃ tā evāhanīyādīnāṃ sādhyā bhaveyuḥ | asaṃbhāvanīyatvādasyārthasya neyamāśaṅkopa-
padyate---atrocyate idamevātra cintyate kimayamatideśaḥ prakṛtau yāvadupakārakaṃ tāvadatidi-
śati uta yathākāryamatideśa iti | na pavamāneṣṭiṣu pavamāneṣṭiprāptiratra cintyate | kintu
(1) P. 1040

[p. r887]

idavātra cintyane yo’yamatideśaḥ sarvatra sa kiṃ yāvadupakārakaṃ prakṛtāvaṅgamanaṅgaṃ vā tatra
tāvadatiśati | tenānaṅgabhūtānāmapi pavamāneṣṭīnāmatideśaḥ syāt | atha yathākāryaṃ yāvadeva
upakāraṃ janayati tāvadevātidiśyate aṅgaṃ tu upakāraṃ janayati na punaḥ anaṅgamapi | tenāṅga-
mātramatidiśyata iti yāvat | tataśca pavamāneṣṭīnāṃ vikṛtiṣu anatideśaḥ tāsāmanaṅgatvāt |
tatra pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ yāvadupakāramatideśa iti | upakārasaṃbandhaṃ hi padārthajātamati-
diśyate | anaṅgabhūtasyāpi copakārakasya upakāra(ka)saṃbandho’stīti tasyāpyatideśo yukta
iti | rāddhāntī punarāha---yathākāryamatideśa iti | yāvadupakārajanakamaṅgabhūtaṃ tāvadati-
diśyate | yena hi vinā upakāro na śakyate pratipattuṃ tadevopakarapratīpattau kāryapratipattau
jātipratipattau vyaktivat nāntarīyakatayā pratīyamānaṃ vikṛtiṣvatidiśyate | yaccopakārajanakaṃ
tena vinā upakāro na śakyate nirūpayitum | yattu upakārakapadārthajanakaṃ tadupakārānanu-
praviṣṭima[na]ṅgabhūtam | tena vināpi janakapadārthavargāvacchinnaḥ śakyata evopakāraḥ pratipattu-
miti na tadvadatidiśyate | upadeśe tu viparītam | upadeśe hi yāvadupakārakamapyupadeśo
bhavati yathā pavamāneṣṭīnāṃ kratūpakāritā | anaṅgabhūtā api pavamāneṣṭayaḥ upadiśyanta eva |
tasmādidamatideśākhyaṃ padārthaprāptipramāṇaṃ upadeśavanna draṣṭavyam | nanu mā nāma upakāraka-
mātramatidiśmatām | tathāpi1 tasyāṅgabhūtapadārthajanakatayā ākṣepo’styeva | kimanati-
deśārthavyutpādanena | ucyate yadyatideśaḥsyāt atidiṣṭameva padārthajātamaṅgamiṣyate tataśca
vikṛtiṣu pavamāneṣṭīnāmaṅgabhūtatvāt svāṅgasaṃskṛteṣvevāgniṣu vikṛtayo bhaveyuḥ | vikṛtyāhisā
hyagnayaḥ vikṛtyapavarge apavṛjyerat | tasmāt 2saprayojanamanatideśavyutpādanam | upadeśa-
viśeṣapradarśanārthaṃ caitat upadeśalakṣaṇe cintitam | yo hi manyate yathā’tideśe
(1) G. tavāpi (2) G. saprayojanamāhātideśa

[p. r888]

yāvatkāryatideśaḥ tathopadeśenāpi yāvatkāryeṇāpi bhavitavyam | tataścādhānapavamāneṣṭayu-
padeśo nopapadyate | atha yāvadupakārakamupadeśaḥ atideśo’pi tathā syāditīmāmā-
śaṅkāmapanetumupadeśādviśeṣa uktaḥ | upadeśe yāvadupakārakamapyupadiśyate atideśe
tu yāvatkāryameveti darśayitumupadeśalakṣaṇe cintitam | anyathā yadyati---
deśopadeśavivekāspadamadhikaraṇaṃ na syāt kintu pavamāneṣṭiprāptyaprāptivicārārthaṃ
syāt | tadā anvārambhaṇīyādhikaraṇenaiva gatārthaṃ syāt | tatra hi ākṣepataḥ
prāptiścintitā | iha tu sā na cintyate | kintu atideśa eveti viśeṣaḥ | agnyarthatayā ca
pavamāneṣṭīnāṃ upadeśānyatve yatnaḥ | pavamāneṣṭīnāmagnyutpādakatvācca pavamāneṣṭiṣu
pavamāneṣṭisaṃskṛto’gniḥ na prāpnotīti pavamāneṣṭiviṣaye’nena abhyuccayena upadeśānyatve
yāvadupakāraprāpakatve yatnaḥ kṛto bhāṣyakāreṇa | tadarthameva pavamāneṣṭayudāharaṇam |

tulyaḥ sarveṣām | kathaṃ punaridamadhikaraṇaṃ prārabdham | samādhātā āha
---yathaiva bhāṣyakāreṇa | ākṣeptā āha---na tathā śakyate’vagantum | pṛcchati---tasmā-
diti | ākṣeptā’’ha---tasmāt yadi tāvadidaṃ jyotiṣṭomaprakaraṇaṃ tadā paśuyāgānāṃ
(1) P. 1042

[p. r889]

prakaraṇābhāvāt śrutyādibhireva paśuyāgasaṃbandhāt viśasanādīnām | kimidaṃ prakaraṇāviśeṣā-
diti sarvārthatocyate | prakaraṇāviśeṣāditi dvivā saṃbhavati prakaraṇasyāviśeṣāditi vā, prakaraṇe
aviśeṣāditi vā | tatra tāvat ṣaṣṭhīsamāso nopapadyate paśūnāṃ prakaraṇābhāvāt |
atha prakaraṇe jyautiṣṭomike nāstyatra viśeṣa sarvapaśūnāmityaviśeṣatocyate | dūṣayati
---kathaṃ tarhi sūtraṃ ‘asti tu prakaraṇe viśeṣa’
 iti | na jyotiṣṭomaprakaraṇe kaścidasti viśeṣaḥ
sarvapaśūnām | atha paśūnāmevāvāntaraprakaraṇaṃ asti jyautiṣṭomikaṃ mahāprakaraṇaṃ tadantargatānāṃ
dīkṣaṇīyādīnāmavāntaraprakaraṇamiti ghaṭata evedaṃ asti tu prakaraṇe viśeṣa iti, tatrāha
---na cāvāntaraprakaraṇaṃ nāma kiñcidasti jyautiṣṭomikamahāprakaraṇe ekasyaiva vidheḥ sādhi-
kārasya karaṇetikartavyatākāṅkṣā, na punaḥ niradhikārāṇāmavāntaravidhīnāmapi | svayamiti-
kartavyatābhūtatvāt sādhyasyetikartavyatākāṅkṣā, na punaḥ sādhanasya | itikartavyatākāṅkṣā ca
prakaraṇam | tenaikasminnevādhikāravidhau nāsti prakaraṇadvayasaṃbhavaḥ | prakaraṇāntare cābhyupa-
gamyamāne itthaṃbhāvapūrakatvaṃ itikartavyatāṃśapūrakatvaṃ agnīṣomīyāderna syāt | kasmādi-
tyāha---kimityeva hi tadā jyotiṣṭomasaṃbandho’bhyupagataḥ syāt | evaṃ ca dīkṣaṇīyādeḥ
itikartavyatābhāva eva hīyeta | kāraṇataiva syāt | asyārthaḥ---yo hi vādī avāntara-
prakaraṇamicchati tena dīkṣaṇīyādiniyogānāṃ sādhyatvasiddhaye sādhikāratvamicchatā prakṛta eva
jyotiṣṭomāpūrvapravṛttaḥ puruṣo’dhikārī vaktavyaḥ | tathā ca sati adhikāriviśeṇabhūtameva

[p. r890]

jyotiṣṭomāpūrvaṃ svargavat, sādhyabhāvena ca apūrvamadhikāriviśeṣaṇam | yacca sādhyaṃ sat
adhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ tatparyantaṃ viṣayībhūtasya bhāvārthasya 1karaṇatvam | yadeva hi niyoge karaṇaṃ
tadeva phalepi karaṇaṃ nānyat | sādhyaṃ hi vivṛddhaṃ bhavati | iha na punaḥ sādhyāntaraṃ phalaṃ
na punaḥ sādhyāntarakalpanā | tataśca dīkṣaṇīyādikarmaṇaḥ svaniyoge (karaṇe eva)
karaṇabhūtasyādhikāraviśeṣaṇībhūtaṃ apūrvamapi prati karataiveti itikartavyatābhāvo hīyate |
nacotpattiśiṣṭaguṇāvarodhadoṣaḥ kāryataḥ karaṇatvopapatteḥ phalacamasavat | adhikāraviśeṣaṇī-
bhūtaṃ hi prati etatkaraṇatvaṃ na punaḥ sādhyatvena nirdeśyakaraṇalasamarpaṇam | athocyeta evaṃ sati
itikartavyatākāṅkṣāyāḥ2 pradhānavidheranivṛttiriti; satyaṃ, tathāpi na pramāṇamantareṇetikartavyatātvaṃ
śakyamadhyavasātum | adhikāravidheritikartavyatākāṅkṣaiva pramāṇamiti cet, na, avagate hi
pradhānavidhisaṃbandhe kathaṃbhāvākāṅkṣā karaṇopakāradvārarūpaṃ parikalpayantī prakaraṇaśabdābhidheyā
pramāṇaṃ bhavati | na cāsmin pakṣe prāgadhikāravidhisaṃbandho’bhyupagataḥ | prāgavagame adhikāra-
vidhisaṃbandhasyābhyupagamyamāne adhikārakalpanāvakāśa eva nāsti | tatsaṃbandhe’vagate prakaraṇena
viniyoge sati pradhānavidhiprayojyatayānuṣṭhānasiddheḥ | anuṣṭhānalābhārthaṃ hi niyogo niyojyā-
nvito’bhidhīyate iti loke dṛṣṭaṃ na punaḥ adṛṣṭena rūpeṇa niyojyānvitābhidhānasya upakāraḥ |
ata evādhyayanavidhāvapi na niyojyakalpanā, adhyāpanavidhiprayuktyaivānuṣṭhānasiddheḥ | tena
prāganavagatādhikāravidhisaṃbandhānāṃ dīkṣaṇīyādīnāṃ sannidhānamātre paramāpūrvapravṛtto’dhikārī
kalpyate | tatra ca svarasena yo’rthaḥ pratīyate sa grāhyaḥ | svarasena ca karaṇa-
tāmevāvagacchāmaḥ iti netikartavyatātvasiddhiḥ | api ca yadyaṅgavidhiṣu adhikārā-
kāṅkṣā tadā svavākyaśrutānyeva kimiti varmādīni rātrisatravadadhikāriviśeṣaṇatayā nāvatiṣṭhante ?
kimiti sākṣācchruṃta svarga kāmapadamevādhikārisamarpakatayā nānuṣajyate ? | tathā payovratādiṣu
kimiti prāṅa्mukhaniyamavat bhojanapravṛtto’dhikārī na syāt | kiñcānuṣṭhānasiddhaye’dhikāra-
kalpanā | tatra varaṃ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyanena vacanena saptamādyapūrvapakṣavata
adhikāravantaṃ yajimuddiśya ‘samidho yajati’
 ityevamādiḥ itikartavyatayā samidādividhirā-
(1) G. karaṇatvāya (2) G. kṣayoḥ

[p. r891]

śrīyatām yada् darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajeta samidho yajatītītthaṃ iti | tataśca
uddiśyamānaviśeṣaṇasyāvivakṣitatvāt adhikāravat yāgamātrasaṃbandha eva samidādīnāṃ syāt
ityupadeśata eva aindrāgnādiṣvitikartavyatālābhaḥ syāt | kiñca yadyapi paramāpūrvapravṛtta
evādhikārī yadyapi ca dīkṣaṇīyāderitikartavyatātvenānvayaḥ tathāpi sarvairitikartavyateti na
siddhayati | api tu pratyekameva apūrvasyādhikāriviśeṣaṇatvenānvitatvāt pratyekamiti-
kartavyatātvaṃ syāt svargavata | tathā hi---svargo’gnihotradarśapūrṇamāsādiṣu pratyekamevādhi-
kāriviśeṣaṇatvenānvitaḥ | pratyekaṃ nirapekṣaireva saṃbadhyate | tathā’trāpi syāt | adhikāri-
viśeṣaṇatayāpi sādhyabhūtasyopāyopeyabhāvamantareṇānvayo na paryavasyati ityupāyopeyatvaṃ kalpyam |
dvividhastūpāyaḥ kareṇetikartavyatālakṣaṇaḥ | tatra karaṇatvenānvaye’sati itikartavyatātvenānvaya
ekaikavākyocca dīkṣaṇīyādayo nirapekṣā evāvagamyante iti nirapekṣamevetikartavyatātvaṃ syāt |
yasya tu mate svavākyamātravartinā paramāpūrveṇa sannidhānamātreṇaiva dīkṣaṇīyādisaṃbandhaḥ tasya
khalakalpe’dhikāravidhau ekasminnaṅgānāṃ kapotavat nipatitatvāt tadanurūpamekameva karaṇopakāra-
lakṣaṇadvāraṃ kalpyeteti yuktamuktaṃ savairevetikartavyateti | na ca sannidhānamātreṇaiva
saṃbandho loke na dṛṣṭaḥ | dṛṣṭakāryādiṣviva adṛṣṭepi sannidhānamātreṇaiva saṃbandhopadeśādanuṣṭhāna-
darśanāt | na ca sannivānamātreṇa saṃbandhe’tiprasaṅgaḥ | sannidhānasya sarvatrābhāvāt |
yadi sannidhānamakiñcitkaraṃ kimiti paramāpūrvapravṛtta eva sannihitepvaṅgeṣu adhikārī bhavati |
yadi tu sarvatra padārthānvayamukhenaiva adhikārisaṃbandhaḥ na punassannidhānanātreṇa, tarhi sāmidhenī-
dvāreṇa saṃbaddhayoḥ prakaraṇāprakaraṇādhītayoḥ pāñcadaśyasāptadaśyayoḥ tulyatā syāt iti kṛtamiti-
vācālatayā | prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ | dūṣaṇāntaraṃ cāha---darśapūrṇamāsayośca prayājāderiti---
kartavyatāprasaṅgaḥ prakaraṇaviniyojyatayā | asyārthaḥ---prayājādaye’pi daśapūrṇamāsayoḥ
prakaraṇaviniyojyāḥ | tataśca teṣāmapi paramāpūrvapravṛtta evādhikārī | tena sādhikāratvāt
tatrāpi itikartavyatā prasajyeta | yadi hi śrutyādiviniyojyā eva prayājādayo bhaveyuḥ

[p. r892]

tadā’dhikārikalpanānavakāśa eva parṇatādivat | asaṃyuktānāṃ tu anuṣṭhānamalabhamānānāṃ
adhikārikalpanā paramiti matvoktaṃ prakaraṇaviniyojyatayeti | nanu bhavatu prayājādebhiti-
kartavyatā laukikītyata āha---tataśca ‘avyaktāsu tu somasya’
 iti saumikītikartavyatā
prāpnoti dīkṣaṇīyāderiva daśipūrṇamāsikīṃ | asyārthaḥ---satyāmitikartavyatākāṅkṣāyāṃ
avyaktatvena codanāliṅgena prayājādiṣu saumikī itikartavyatā’tideśena prāpnoti | na
ceṣyate | athocyeta kathaṃ pradhānasyetikartavyatā’ṅgeṣu ityatrāha---dīkṣaṇīyāderiva dārśa-
pūrṇamāsikī | na pradhānaguṇabhāvaḥ sādhyatve viśeṣabhāvahati | tathā ca pradhānayorapi
darśapūrṇamāsayoraṅgeṣu dīkṣaṇoyādiṣvitikartavyatā dṛśyate iti | atra kaścitpaṇḍitaṃmanya
āha---yadi prayājādiṣu saumikavidhyantābhyupagamaḥ tarhi 1vinā somopakārakalpanayā darśapū-
māsayorupakārakalpanā nāsti | darśapūrṇamāsayorupakārakalpanayā binā somepyupakārakalpanā
nāsti iti parasparopakārakalpanayā āpatitā itaretarāśrayateti | tadapisvapramāṇānabhi-
jñasya mūrdhnā’bhidhātena girividāraṇāsaṃśasanam | tathāhi---svāṅgairevopakāraḥ kalpyate na
punaraṅgāṅgairapi | prayājādyaṅgāni pradhānavidheraṅgāni na bhavanti avāntarāpūrvapratiṣṭhitatvāditi
navame uktam | tataśca vināpi prayājāderātideśikīmitikartavyatāṃ darśapūrṇamāsayorupakāra-
kalpanā | tathā somepi vinaiva dīkṣaṇīyāvidhyantaṃ ātideśikamiti ghaṭata eva paraspara-
maṅgeṣvatideśaḥ | yāvadaṅgamatidiśyata ityuktam | tena yāvadeva saumikamaṅgaṃ tāvat
prayājādiṣvatidiśyate na punardīkṣaṇīyādṛṅgāni | tathā dīkṣaṇīyadiṣvapi yāvadaiṣṭikamaṅgaṃ
tāvata evātideśo na punaḥ prayājādyaṅgasyāpi | yadi paraṃ samāpte’tideśe aṅgānāmākṣepataḥ
prāptiḥ saiva itaretarāśrayatayā ātmāśrayatayā vā svayaṃ bādhyata iti sūkṣmadṛśāmeṣa viṣayaḥ |
pṛcchati kimiti vā na bhavati iti | evaṃ manyate adhikṛtādhikāreṇa prayājādīnā-
mapi sādhikāratvāt sādhyabhūtānāṃ bhavatu saumikī itikartavyatā, pramāṇata eva bhāvitvāt
(1) G. vidhinā

[p. r893]

iti | uttaramāha---uktamavāntaraprakaraṇābhāvāditi | yadyavāntaraprakaraṇaṃ syāt tadā bhavedapyati-
deśaḥ na tvasāvasti | kuto nāstītyāha---na hi ekasmin prayogavacane nānā kimityā-
kāṅkṣā saṃbhavati | ekasmin prayogavacane na ubhayarūpā kimityākāṅkṣā
saṃbhavati | ekenaivādhikāravidhinā prayogasyānuṣṭhānalakṣaṇasyoktatvāt | anuṣṭhānalābhāya hi
avikāriviśeṣaṇākāṅkṣā | labdhe punaḥ anyato’nuṣṭhāne kimadhikāriviśeṣaṇā-
kāṅkṣyā | atha manyase nānyenānuṣṭhānamākṣiptamiti tatrāha---ekaścāyaṃ prayogavacanaḥ
setikartavyatākasya prayogasyaikaśabdyāditi vakṣyati | eka evādhikāravidhiḥ sarvaṃ
niradhikāraṃ sannihitaṃ svārthatvena gṛhītvā viniyujya prayogamākṣipatīti darśitaṃ aikaśabdāditi
tasmānnāsti nānāvidhādhikāriviśeṣaṇākāṅkṣeti | niradhikāratayā prayājādīnāṃ sādhyatvāna-
vagamāt netikartavyatākāṅkṣā’stīti nāsti saumikadharmātideśaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi dīkṣaṇīyāderiti-
kartavyatākāṅkṣā vinā cākāṅkṣāṃ nāsyātideśikā dharmāḥ | abhyupagataṃ cedaṃ tvayā dṛṣṭāntamabhi-
dadhatā dīkṣaṇīyāderiva dārśapaurṇamāsikītikartavyateti | uttaraṃ---mābhūvannātideśikadharmāḥ |
na punaḥ kimityākāṅkṣādvayamekasmin prakaraṇe saṃbhavati | asyārthaḥ---nābhyupagamena
vastu vyavasthāpyate | yadyabhyupagamo nyāye’smin sthite na ghaṭate mā ghaṭiṣṭa | etaccānvāruhya-
vacanam | paramārthatastu bhavatyeva dīkṣaṇīyādiṣu dharmaprāptiḥ | yasmādaṅgabhūteṣvapi tāvat
apūrvāṇi teṣvaṅgīkaraṇīyāni | anyathā kṣāṇikānāmaṅgatvānupapatteḥ ityuktamekādaśe
bhāṣyakāreṇa | tāni cāpūrvāṇi prākṛtopakārāpekṣīṇīti prayājādidarśanena kalpyate | taduktaṃ
---vikṛtitve punaḥ liṅgameveti sthitiḥ | etacca kṛtsnavidhānādhikaraṇe vakṣyāmaḥ |

[p. r894]

evaṃ sthite avāntaraprakaraṇābhāve yadidamucyate prakaraṇātsavanīyasyeti sthānāccāgnīṣo-
mīyasyeti tatsarvamidaṃ andhapadam | asamīkṣya pravṛttatvāt | tasmāt mahatā prayatnena
bhāṣyaṃ vyākhyeyam ityākṣepaḥ | samādhānaṃ tu---atrābhidhīyate | prakaraṇaṃ tābadidaṃ jyoti-
ṣṭomasya | aupavasathye’hani dharmāścodyante | na ca paśavastasminnahani sarve sannihitāḥ |
sannihitavacanaṃ coddeśakaṃ padam | ataḥ kasyāyamuddeśaka iti cintyate viśasanādi-
vidhiṣu | etaduktaṃ bhavati---nātra paśūnāṃ prakaraṇamaṅgīkṛtya cintyate | kintu jyotiṣṭo-
masyaiva prakaraṇam | viśasanādayaśca śrutyaiva paśusaṃbandhitayā viniyujyante | tatra paśuśabdaḥ
uddeśakaḥ uddeśakaśca śabdaḥ sannihitavacanaḥ | tatra dharmavidhiṣu kaḥ paśuṣu sannihitaḥ
yo’yamuddeśakaiḥ śabdairupādīyate iti cintyate | tatra prakaraṇāviśeṣāt sarve paśavo
jyotiṣṭomārthatayā prakṛtā iti sarvārthatāṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |

apareṇa tu pūrvapakṣiṇā ahassāmānyasannidhānāt ekasminnaupavasthye’hani
savanīyasya dharmāṇāṃ ca vidhānāc savanīyasya sannidhiviśeṣo vidyata iti asti tu prakaraṇa-
viśeṣa ityuktam | nanu cāvāntaraprakaraṇāṅgīkāra evātra dṛśyate yata evamāha---savanīya-

[p. r895]

prakaraṇe paśudharmāssamāmnātā iti tatrāha---prakṛtalakṣaṇaṃ sannidhānalakṣaṇaṃ prakaraṇamatrābhipretam |
na punaritikartavyatākāṅkṣālakṣaṇam | tena nāsti virodhaḥ | yata eva sannidhānalakṣaṇaṃ tatra
prakaraṇamabhipretaṃ ata eva sthānādagnīṣomīyasyeti yuktam | anyathā itikartavyatā-
lakṣaṇāt prakaraṇāt sthānasya durbalatvāt sthānasya viniyojakatvameva na syāt | sannidhāna-
pakṣe tu yuktamubhayostulyabalatvam | vidhānena hi savanīyāstatra sannihitāḥ | sthānena hi
agnīṣomīyaḥ iti yuktamubhayaśeṣatvam | aprakṛtasiddhyarthaṃ ca sautye’hani vidhānaṃ
savanīyasyetyevamādi yaduktaṃ tadupapannārthaṃ bhavati | anyathā aharantare satyapi vidhāne na jyoti-
ṣṭomavat prakaraṇābhāvaśśakyate vaktum | kathaṃ punaḥ sannidhānaviśeṣeṇāgnīṣomīyārthatve
siddhe jyotiṣṭomaprakaraṇe cāmnānasya arthavattve tasminneva prakaraṇe paśudvayasyātideśaḥ
kṛtaḥ ? na hyekaḥ prayogavacanaḥ upadeśena atideśena ca tāneva dharmān gṛhṇāti | prayoga-
vacana evāṅgānāmitikartavyatāṃ gṛhṇāti iti bhrāntasyedaṃ codyam | uttaramāha---asya codyasya
parihāramatideśe vakṣyāmaḥ “kṛtsnavidhānādapūrvaḥ soma’ ityatra | “prāptyupādānayorbhedāna-
bhijñasyaiṣā vidhiparicodanā”
 ityanena aṅgavidhiryaḥ sa eva 1svāṅgānāṃ prāpako liṅgadarśanotthāpita-
prākṛtopakārākāṅkṣaḥ | prayojakatā tu pradhānavidheriti tatraiva vakṣyati | asmin punaradhi-
karaṇe upadeśaviśeṣo darśitaḥ | upadeśaviśeṣatvācchaiṣalakṣaṇasya | tenopadeśastāvadevaṃ
rūpaḥ | yadi paśudvaye atideśo ghaṭate ghaṭatām | atha na ghaṭate mā ghaṭiṣṭeti bhāvaḥ |
(1) G. savanāṅgānāṃ

[p. r896]

kaḥ punaraupavasathye’hani savanīyeṣu vidhīyamāneṣu sādhyeṣu āśvinakāle vākyabhedaḥ
pūrvapakṣavādinā paricoditaḥ, pūrvapakṣavādī hi aupavasathye’hani savanīyānāṃ vidhānaṃ sādhayituṃ
āśvinakāle savanīyavidhāne vākyabhedamāha ko’sau1 vākyabheda ityarthaḥ | kathaṃ vā
sautye’hani savanīyeṣu vidhīyamāneṣu sādhyeṣu satsuṃ ‘āśvitaṃ grahaṃ gṛhītvā trivṛtā’

ityasya vākyabhedaḥ pūrvapakṣiṇaṃ prati rāddhāntinā paricoditaḥ | kiñcobhayatra vākyabheda eva
coditaḥ | tataśca tulyatvāt kathaṃ siddhāntasiddhiriti manyate | uttaramāha---anekaguṇa-
vidhānameva pūrvapakṣī vākyabhedaṃ manyate | parivyāṇakālalakṣaṇastāvadeko guṇaḥ aparaśco-
pākaraṇa kālalakṣaṇaḥ iti guṇadvayavidhānaṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | rāddhāntavādyapi vapāpracāra-
mātrotkarṣaṃ manvānaḥ punarālambhavidhāne ca vākyabhedameva coditavān dvayārthatāmuktavān |
asyārthaḥ---rāddhāntavādī tu aupavasathye’hani samāmnātasyālambhasya punassautyehani vidhāne,
tathā āśvinakāle vidhāne ca vākyabhedamuktavān | na ca vapāpracāreṇa sautyāhassaṃbandhinā2
arthāt sautyamevāhaḥ prāpitaṃ tasya paścālambhasyāśvinakālatāmātraṃ vidhīyata iti śakyate
vaktum | vapāpracārasya guṇabhūtatvāt paśuyāgaguṇabhūto hi vapāpracāraḥ sa paśuyāga-
guṇabhūtamālambha na śaknotyutkraṣṭum | tena vapāpracāra eva sautye’hani |
paśvālambhastvaupavasathya eva |

(1) G. tenāsau (2) G. saṃbandhinī

[p. r897]

nanu arthādutkarṣaḥ paśvālambhasya; na hi pūrvedyurālambhe paśorvapoddharaṇadeśaṃ yajamāno muṣṭinā
1pidhāyāsituṃ śaknoti | avaśyaṃ hi tena hastenāhārādi kartavyam | tathā vihāro’pi

ucyate | yadyavaśyaṃ muṣṭinā pidhāyāsituṃ tadā muṣṭiśabdena tṛṇamuṣṭiḥ syādityāśrīyatām |

prayukta”
 iti | tadākṣipati---kimidaṃ jyotiṣṭomaprayuktaṃ iti ? ekagranthenāha---nanu
ca sarvamevedaṃ jyotiṣṭomaprayuktaṃ tatprakaraṇāmnānāt | eka eva hi sādhikārobidhiḥ
prakaraṇāmnātaṃ sakalaṃ prayuṅkte svāṅgaṃ aṅgāṅgamapi | samādhitte---ucyate---viniyogābhi-

ityucyate | na punaḥ jyotiṣṭomādhikāravidhinā na prayujyate | api tu tenaivānuṣṭhāpyata iti |

dohadharmāḥ sāyaṃdohārthā eveti pūrvapakṣaḥ | rāddhāntastu naiva dadhipayasoḥ kālabhedena

dīkṣaṇīyādayo dharmāḥ śaṅkyeran tadā naimittikānāṃ na bhaveyuḥ | na ca grahaṇānāṃ dharmasaṃbandhaḥ |
kintu yāgasya | sarvagrahaṇasaṃskāryadravyakaścāyaṃ yāgaḥ somena yajeteti | ya
eva yāgaḥ sarvagrahaṇasaṃskāryadravyakaḥ sa evāyamukthyādipvapi sarvairgahaiḥ yaḥ saṃskāryaḥ somarasaḥ
sa eva dravyaṃ yasyeti vigrahaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati---devatāviśeṣoddeśena ca yāni grahaṇāni kriyante
tairyo rasabhāgaḥ upakalpyate tenāyaṃ somayāgaḥ kriyate | ukthyādiṣvapi tāvata yāgaśeṣasoma-
grahaṇameva | tena tatrāpi sa eva yāgaḥ kartavya iti | nāsya yāgasya ukthyādiṣu
anyā utpattirvidyate | api tu somena yajetetyevotpatiḥ | sa cedyāgaḥ grahaṇāntareṣūkthyā-
diṣu itikartavyatāpi saiva yaiva aindravāyavādigrahaṇaiḥ yāge kriyamāṇe iti yāvat1 |
yadi yāvadgrahaṇaviśeṣo’gniṣṭomāntaḥ yāgasya dharmaprāptau nimittaṃ syāt tadā grahaṇāntareṣu
ukthyādiṣu na syāditikartavyatā | na caitadasti | tadāha---na ca grahaṇaviśeṣo yāga-
syetikartavyatāprāptau nimittaṃ, kiṃ tarhi prayogacodanaiva anuṣṭheyatvena codanaiva dharmaprāptau
nimittam | na ca grahaṇāntareṣu prayogāntaracodanā yāgasya | ukthyādiṣvapi grahaṇā-
ntareṣu na yāgasya prayogāntaracodanā | kiṃ tarhi ? ya eva somena yajeteti prayoyaścoditaḥ
sa evokathyādiṣvapi | aindravāyavādiṣviva guṇānāmeva hi grahaṇaviśeṣāṇāṃ tatrokthyādiṣu
phalāya codanā na yāgasya | yathā godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayediti na darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ
(1) R. iti yāvat --- iti nāsti

[p. r907]

prayogaścodyate | tasmādbhavantu grahaṇāti grahaṇadharmopadeśaśūnyāni phalacamasavat | na
punaryāgo’pi yāgadharmairasaṃbaddha iti śakyate vaktum | sarvasaṃskāreṣu ekaprayogasya
ekasyānuṣṭhānasya coditatvāt prayogagrāhyatvāditikartavyatāyā ityuktaṃ prāk | yata
eva samānavidhānāḥ sarvasaṃsthāḥ ata evāgniṣṭomagrahaṇam | ‘yadyagniṣṭomaṃ juhoti’
 iti |
dyagniṣṭomāntasyaivaupadeśikā dharmāḥ ttadā antareṇāpyagniṣṭomagrahaṇaṃ agniṣṭomasyaiva syāt | 
tatrāgniṣṭomagrahaṇamanarthakaṃ syāt | samānavidhāne tu viśeṣasaṃskārasaṃbandhārthaṃ agniṣṭomagrahaṇaṃ
samuccayaśca pradarśitaḥ | paśūnāṃ samānavidhānena hi āgneyasya sarvasaṃsthāsu aupadeśikatvāt
vaiśeṣikaiḥ ukthyādisaṃsthāsaṃbandhibhiḥ paśubhiḥ samuccayo ghaṭate | tena tasmin sati 
dvitīyatṛtīyadarśanamupapadyate itarathā ātideśikasyāgneyasya ukthyādisaṃsthābhāgbhiḥ
aupadeśikairvādhe sati samuccayo na syāditi | atra codayati---paśūnāmadṛṣṭārthatayā
samuccaya eva nyāyyaḥ | tathā ca vakṣyati prājāpatyeṣu cāmnānāt iti | atrocyate
---savanīyasamākhyayā sarvapaśūnāṃ tulyakāryāvagamāt yukta eva bādhaḥ | tasmādupapannaṃ
liṅgadarśanam | atrābhidhīyate---satyamevaṃ syāt yadi, saṃsthāntareṣūkthyādiṣu prayoga-
codanayaiva yāgakartavyatā labhyate yathā godohanena darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ| iha tu agniṣṭoma-

[p. r908]

saṃskāraparyantaiva yāgasya prayogacodanā | iha tu anaimittikāḥ kratvarthāḥ ye
agniṣṭomāntā grahaṇākhyāḥ saṃskārāḥ tatparyantaiva yāgasya somena yajetetyanenānuṣṭhāna-
codanā | ata ūrdhvaṃ kāmasaṃyogena codyante grahā ukthyādayaḥ ta eva yāgacoda-
nāmākṣipanti | yathā ainadravāyavādayo nityā grahāḥ āvṛttim | evaṃ cet na
yāgacodanayā dharmāḥ śakyante grahītuṃ ukthyādiṣu | ataḥ phalacamasavat
nityānityasaṃyogavirodho yuktaḥ | ukthyādiṣu yāgasyānuṣṭhānacodanā naimittikī kāmyairgrahai-
rākṣipyamāṇatvāt | nityāśca yāgasaṃbandhino dharmāḥ te nityānuṣṭhānena gṛhītā bhavitumarhanti
anityānuṣṭhānagṛhītatve tu nityānityasaṃyogavirodhaḥ syāt iti siddhamatrāpi phalacamasavat
nokthyādiṣu grahaṇākṣiptasya ekasyāpi yāgasya dharmā iti | kāryataśca dharmaprāptiḥ | ataḥ
samuccayagrahaṇaṃ vacanāt bhaviṣyati | asyokthyādigrahaṇākṣiptayāgaviṣayasyādhikāravidherupa-
kārāpekṣāyāṃ sannihitanityasaṃsthādhikāraniyogopakāragrahaṇāt kāryato dharmāṇāṃ prāptiḥ | tataścau-
padeśikena bādhe prāptaṃ vacanāt samuccayo bhaviṣyati | agniṣṭomaśabdaścānuvādo bhaviṣyati | 
“yadyagniṣṭomo juhoti”
 iti vacanena | na anuvādo doṣaḥ anuvādakānyapi hi vacanāni
dṛśyante yathā “dvayoḥ praṇayanti”
 iti | tathā homādīnāmabhāvadarśanamupapannaṃ bhaviṣyati |
“yadyukthyaḥ paridhimanakti na juhoti”
 iti pracaraṇīhomaśeṣasya pratipattiriyam | tatra
samānavidhānapakṣe yadyagniṣṭomo juhotīti agniṣṭomahomaḥ prāpnoti iti | tathā ukthye paridhya-

[p. r909]

ñjanasya | tanna ‘na juhoti’
 iti homābhāvānuvādo na ghaṭate anuvādasarūpaṃ cedam | vartamānā-
padeśāt | prakṛtivikārabhāve caupadeśikena paridhyañjanena tulyakāryeṇa bādhitatvāt homā-
bhāvānuvādo yujyate | upadeśataḥ prāptau yathopadeśaṃ kāryamiti ubhayorupadeśabalena samuccayaḥ
kalpyaḥ | kāryatastu prāptau klṛpta eva kārye vidhānamiti yuktaiva prākṛtapratipattibhūtahoma-
nivṛttiḥ iti |

iti ṛjuvimalāpañvikāyāṃ
tṛtīyasya ṣaṣṭhaḥ pādaḥ

[p. r910]

atha ṛjuvimalāyāṃ
tutīyādhyāyasya saptamaḥ pādaḥ

prakaraṇāviśeṣādasaṃyuktam | barhirdharmāśca kimaṅkapradhānārthā uta pradhānārthā eveti
sandehe prakaraṇāt pradhānārthā ityuktvā upakārakatvenāṅgapradhānārthatāmāha | anena yathā-
śrutamadhikaraṇamanubhāṣitamākṣeptum | ākṣipati---tadayuktam | śrutyādibhirhi tādarthyamavagamyate
nopakāreṇa | yatpradhānārthatvaṃ pratibhāti na tadupakāreṇa śakyane’nyathākartum | upakāralakṣaṇasya
viniyogapramāṇasyābhāvāditi | samādhatte---ucyate | śrutyādaya evopanyastā aṅgapradhānārthatva-
siddhaye | barhiṣi havīṃṣyāsādayatīti haviśśabdena sarvahaviṣāmevābhidhānāt aṅgapradhānārthatā
barhiṣaḥ śrutyaiveti | yadi śrutyā śeṣatvaṃ upakāragrahaṇaṃ tarhi kimartham ? uttaraṃ---kadā-
cit tadabhāvāt śruteḥ saṅkocaḥ syāt | asti cāsāvekarūpa upakāra iti tadabhidhānam |
asyārthaḥ---yadidamupakārābhidhānaṃ tadāśaṅkānivṛttyartham | śeṣatvaṃ sarvatropakāravyatirekeṇa
nāsti | tena aṅagapradhānahavirarthaḥ upakāro’vepaṇīyaḥ | tatra yadekatra dṛṣṭa upakāraḥ anyatrā-
dṛṣṭarūpaḥ syāta tadā dṛṣṭenaivopakāreṇa yatra śeṣatvaṃ tatraiva śeṣatā syāt seyamāśaṅkā mābhūditi sarvatra
(1) P. 1073

[p. r911]

dṛṣṭa ekarūpa upakāra iti darśayitumupakārābhidhānam | yadyaṅgapradhānārthatā evaṃ tarhi vākyenaiva
śebasya varhirdharmāṇāmanvayaḥ | vākyena cedanvayaḥ barhirmātre piṣḍapitṛyajñavartinyapi 
kimiti na bhavati | prakaraṇena hyanvaye yasyaiva prakaraṇaṃ tasyaiva dharmāḥ syuriti| codyaṃ
pariharati - grāhakatve sati śeṣatvamuktam | “prakaraṇaviśeṣādvikṛtau virodhi syāt ”
 ityatra
grahakeṇa gṛhītaṃ viniyujyata ityuktaṃ saptamādye | grāhakaścātra dārśapūrṇamāsikaprayoga-
vidhiḥ tatsannidhānāddharmāṇāmiti nānyadīyadharmāṇāṃ prāptiriti | liṅgadarśanamuktama |

phalasaṃyogāttu | atna bhāṣyakāraḥ keśaśmaśruvapanādīnāṃ kimaṅgapradhānārthatā uta
pradhānārthataiveti vicārayati | tatna kāraṇabhutaṃ sandehaṃ vaktumudāharaṇaśuddhimāha - iha purūṣa-
saṃskārā udāharaṇamiti | yadyapyatna dvitīyānirdeśo nāsti tathāpi lokata eva vapanādīnāṃ
purūṣasaṃskāratvena darśanāt purūṣasaṃskārā evaite | saṃprati kāraṇasandehamāha---te kiṃ kartṛbhutasya
utādhikṛtasyeti | dve hi purūṣasyāvasthe karmaṇi | ekā tāvadadhikṛtāvasthā aiścaryalakṣaṇā,
aparā kartravasthā | tadyadi kartravasthasya purūṣasya saṃskāraḥ tadā karturaṅgapradhānārthatvāt tatsaṃskāro-
pyaṅgapradhānārthaḥ ityagrīṣomīyapaśuvikāre paśubandhepi vapanādayaścodakātsyuḥ | athāddikṛtāva---
sthasya saṃskāraḥ tadā pradhāna eva purūṣasyādhikārāt tadarthā vapanādayaḥ syuḥ iti | katṛbhūtasya
evaṃ karmāṅgatopapadyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ kartā hi karmaṇo’ṅgam | yadi 
(1) P. 1077

[p. r912]

tasyaiva te saṃskārā bhaveyuḥ tadā karmāṅgatā dharmāṇāmapi syāta | adhikṛtāvasthastu pradhānabhutaḥ
tasya saṃskārā na karmaṇo’ṅgam | anaṅgāvasthāsaṃbandhitvāt akarmāṅgabhūtasya ca niyoga---
saṃbandhitaiva hīyate | tasmāt kartṛbhūtasyeti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntamāha---kimityaṃ-
śopanipātinī itikartavyateti saptamādye vakṣyāmaḥ | ākāṅkṣādvayasya tatprasūteḥ
asyārthaḥ---yā kācidirktavyatā sā sādhyāṃśopanipātinī | na śādhanāṃśe | kutaḥ ?
kena kathamityākāṅkṣādvayasya 2tatkartṛtvāt | sādhyameva setikartavyatākaṃ karaṇamākāṅkṣati |
na punaḥ karaṇamitikartavyatāmākṣipati iti saptamādye darśitam | evaṃ sthite kiṃ kartṛbhāve
yogyatāmāpādayati utādhikṛtabhāve ayaṃ saṃskāra iti cintyate | tatra yadarthaḥ kartṛbhāvaḥ
saṃskāro’pi tadartha eva yuktaḥ | adhikṛtabhāvārthaṃ hi kartṛtvam | aiśvaryalakṣaṇasyādhi-
kārasya saṃpatyarthaṃ puruṣaḥ kartṛtāmavalambata iti | pradhānabhūtādhikṛtāvasthasyaiva puruṣasya saṃskāro
yuktaḥ adhikāraniṣpattyarthatvācchāstrasya | tasmādadhikṛtasya saṃskāra iti yuktam | 
phalabhūtādhikāranirvṛttau ca sāhāyyaṃ vāñchantau’dhikṛtasaṃskārā api bhavantītikartavyatābhūtā
yāgasyetyuktaṃ bhāṣye |
 
cikīṃrṣayā ca saṃyogāt | barhirdharmādhikaraṇena kimatrānyatvaṃ yenedamadhikaraṇa-
mārabhyate | yathā tatra barhirāderaṅgapradhānārthatā tathā’trapi vederaṅgapradhānārthataiva yuktā |
(1) P. 1078 (2) R. tatkṛtatvāt

[p. r913]

kimarthamidamadhikaraṇamārabhyate | uttaraṃ---pradhānacikīrṣāṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | asyārthaḥ
-pūrvapakṣavādī pradhānasyaiva cikīrṣāṃ manyate | tathāpi kimityāha---yadarthā cikīrṣā tadarthānye-
vāṅgānītyabhiprāyaḥ | yadeva hi cikīrṣitaṃ tadarthānyevāṅgānīti yuktam | etenaiva nyāyena
saṃrvatraivam---yadapi sāmānyatopadiṣṭaṃ---barhiṣi havīṃṣyāsādayatīti tadapyatrodāharaṇamityava-
seyam | ata eva tadarthānyaṅgānīti bahuvacanam |
 
tathā’bhidhānena | abhimarśane tu sākṣāt pradhānaśabdasaṃyogāt pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
caturhotrā paurṇamāsīmiti paurṇamāsīśabdasya pradhānavacanatvāt pradhānahaviṣāmeva abhimarśanaṃ,
pradhānasaṃbandhīnyeva havīṃṣi abhimarśanaṃ prati pradhānaśabdenopasthāpyante na punaraṅgahavīṃpyapi | eva-
mudāharaṇadvaye nyāyena vacanena ca pūrvapakṣaṃ kṛtvā saṃprati rāddhāntamāha---satyaṃ pradhānaṃ cikīrṣyate 
tattu sāṅgamadhikāranirvṛttāviti rāddhāntaḥ | ko brūte pradhānaṃ na cikīrṣyata iti |
kintu tadeva cikīrṣyata iti na śakyate vaktum | adhikāranirvṛttyarthaṃ tasya cikīrṣā |
sāṅgaṃ ca pradhānamadhikāraṃ nirvartayati | nāṅgarahitam | tena sāṅgaṃ cikīrṣyata iti | 
evamabhimarśanamapyadhikārasādhanasyaiva | tatrāpi hi dvitīyādarśanena īpsitatamatvāvagateḥ |
īpsitatamatā ca sādhanasyaiva, na kevalaṃ karaṇamātrasya | sādhanaṃ ca setikartavyatākaṃ karaṇa-
mityubhayoḥ vedyabhimarśanayoḥ aṅgapradhānārthatā | tulyāsiddhāntatayā ca dvau pūrvapakṣau kṛtvā
siddhāntitam |

(1) P. 1079

[p. r914]

dīkṣādakṣiṇaṃ tu vacanātpradhānasya | dīkṣādakṣiṇamapi sāṅgādadhikāranirvṛtte.
sāṅgasyetyāśaṅkya vacanātpradhānasyeti rāddhāntaḥ | kutaḥ ? anena pūrvapakṣarāddhāntau
dvāvapyuktau | kartṛsaṃskāratāṃ ca dīkṣāyā abhyupagamyeyaṃ cintā | tatra sāṅgasyādhikāra-
nirvartakatvāt sāṅgamevānuṣṭheyam | tadaṅgabhūtaḥ kartā | kartṛsaṃskāro’pi tadaṅgabhūta eveti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | vacanaṃ tu sāṅgapradhānābhiprāyeṇa varṇanīyam | na vacanasyānyārthatve pramāṇa-
mastīti rāddhāntitam | saṃprati codayati---yuktaṃ tāvat dīkṣā pradhānasya | yadā punardakṣiṇā
pradhānasya tadā kathamaparikrītairaṅgāni kriyante ? tāni cārtvijyādīni | kena voktaṃ 
aparikrītairaṅgāni kriyanta iti | 3prākṛtamānatikāraṇaṃ bhaviṣyatyeva | dīkṣaṇīyā-
diṣvaiṣṭikeṣu codakaprāptā dakṣiṇā bhaviṣyati | yadi tatra prākṛtī dakṣiṇā na tarhi prasaṅgo
dakṣiṇāyāḥ | yathā prasaṅgaḥ tathā prasaṅgalakṣaṇa eva vakṣyāmaḥ | darśanamuktam |

tathā yūpasya vediḥ | ardhamantarvedīti śrūyate kiṃ vediḥ yūpāḍgaṃ uta neti saṃśaye

[p. r915]

śruteraṅgamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | ardhamantarvedīti vacanāt vediraṅagaṃ yūpasyeti pūrvaḥ
pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntastu---bahirvedipadaikavākyatvāt na śrutyarthavidhānaṃ saṃbhavatīti deśalakṣaṇā |
ubhayavidhāne vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | asyārthaḥ--- “vajro vai yūpaḥ, yadantarvedi minuyāt nirdaheta
yadvahirvedi anavaruddhaḥ syāt ardhamantarvedyardhaṃ bahirvedi minoti | avaruddhaḥ syāt na nirdahati”

iti upakramopasaṃhārayoḥ ekavākyatvāt ekamidaṃ vākyāmiti niścīyate | yadyantarvedīti
śrutyarthaḥ parigṛhyate tadā bahirvedipadaikavākyatā nopapadyate ityekavākyatāsiddhayarthaṃ deśalakṣaṇaiva
nyāyyeti |
 
śāmidhenīstadanvāhuriti | kaḥ punaḥ pūrveṇāsya viśeṣaḥ | yathā pūrvatra deśa-
lakṣaṇā, tathā’trāpi havirdhānena deśo lakṣyatāmiti | uttaraṃ---ayamasti viśeṣaḥ padāntarā-
saṃbaddhaṃ hyatra havirdhānaṃ śrūyate | nāntarvedivat bahirvedipadasaṃbaddham | tasmādaṅgāṅgibhāva
eva nyāyya iti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | śruteḥ | tatra hi śrutyarthaparityāge bahirvedipadaika-
vākyatā kāraṇam | iha tu tadabhāvāt śrauta evārtho grāhyaḥ | tena havirdhānāṅgatā
sāmidhenīnām, sāmidhenyaṅgatā vā havirdhānasya yuktā | na viniyuktaviniyogavirodho nāma
kaścidvirodho’sti | tathā hi---ekaiva dadhijātiḥ kratau puruṣe ca viniyujyate | yaddhi na

yathā dṛśyate tadviruddhamityucyate tadāha---vijñānaviparītaṃ hi viruddhamabhidhīyate | itamevā-
viruddhaṃ yadyathā jñāyate tathā iti | rāddhāntavādī tu ubhayoḥ anyārthatvāt deśalakṣaṇasaṃbandha
eva nyāyya iti manyate | ayamāśayaḥ---yadyapi vastuvirodho nāsti tathāpi yadanyasaṃbandhitayā
pratipattumeva na śakyate, yathā na devadattīyaṃ yajñadattīyamiti pratipattiḥ | iha havirdhānamiti
havissaṃbabandhaḥ pratīyate tathā samidhenīriti samindhanasaṃbandhaḥ pratīyate tena śrautārthānvayānupapatteḥ
deśalakṣaṇayā saṃbandho’vasīyate | tathā ‘havirdhānaṃ’
 ‘sāmidhenyaḥ’
 iti samākhyā na
pīḍitā bhavati | anyathā hi śrutyarthaparigrhe, havirdhānaṃ sāmidhenīnāṃ sāmidhenyo vā
havirdhānasyeti devadattasyedaṃ yajñadattasyeti yathedamasaṃbaddhaṃ tathedamapi syāt tasmādatrāpi
pūrvavat deśalakṣaṇaiveti yuktam | āgneyyadhikaraṇavacca sāmānyaviśeṣasaṃbandhe vākyabhedaḥ
syāt | asyārthaḥ---yathā āgneyyadhikaraṇe aprakṛtānāṃ grahaṇe tāsāṃ sāmānyasaṃbandhe
viśeṣasaṃbandhe ca vākyabhedaḥ, tathehāpi syāt atrāpi sāmānyena tāvaddhavirdhānasaṃbandhaḥ punaśca
yasmin sunvanti havirdhāne tatsaṃbandha iti vākyabhedaḥ syāt |

ranyaireva padārthāḥ kartavyāḥ | tathā ‘maitrāvaruṇaḥ preṣyati cānucāha’ iti sākṣādanya eva
śrūyate | tathā ādhvaryavaṃ ityādyāḥ samākhyāḥ santi iti kathamatra saṃśayaḥ ? pūrvapakṣā-
bhāvāditi | samarthayate---ayamabhiprāyaḥ-kartṛgāmikriyāphalaṃ atra śrūyate | adhikāra---
vidhisāmarthyāt kartṛtvamatrādhikāriṇaḥ śrūyate | tatra yadi paraḥ padārthān kuryāt kartṛtvameva
vihanyeta | atastadvirodhāt adhikārasāmarthyāvagatakartṛtvāvirodhāt parikrayaṃ manyate
gauṇaṃ tena nāyaṃ parikrayaḥ parikrayavadbhaviṣyati parikrayo’dṛṣṭārtho vā bhaviṣyati | tadāha
---adṛṣṭārthaṃ vā manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | maitrāvaruṇādiśabdaśca kathañcita yajamānaparatayā
varṇanīyaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi ādhvaryavamitvādyāḥ samākhyāḥ? karmaṇāmadhvaryusaṃbandhābhāvāt |
granthasaṃyogādupapannā bhaviṣyanti | granthā evādhvaryavaḥ tadvihitaṃ ca karma ādhvaryavaṃ
bhaviṣyati | tasmāt svayaṃ kartṛtaiva nyāyyeti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | aniyamavādī tu
dravyotsargamātraṃ svayameva kartavyaṃ svatvatyāgalakṣaṇatvāttasya, śeṣaṃ tvanyaḥ svayaṃ vā kuryāditi |
aniyamaṃ yo vadati , sa manyate parikrītairapi kriyamāṇaṃ tenaiva kṛtaṃ bhavati | kṛtaḥ ?
sa hi teṣāṃ prayoktā prayoktuśca kartṛtvasmaraṇaṃ tatprayojako hetuśceti cakārāt kartṛtva-
smaraṇāt | adhikāraśca niyoganibandhanāṃ siddhimākṣipati | na punarāhatyaiva kartṛtvam
tena na kaścittasya virodhaḥ | utsargastu svadravyatyāgalakṣaṇatvāt svayameva kartavyo yajamānena
evaṃ kartṛtve upapanne na parikrayasya gauṇatā adṛṣṭārthātā vā śakyate kalpayitum | samā-

[p. r918]

khyāśca ādhvaryavamityevamādyāḥ karmaṇāṃ mukhyā bhaviṣyanti na granthadvārikāḥ | nanu
grantha evādhvaryavādayaḥ samākhyāḥ na karmaṇi ityatrāha---na hyadhyetāraḥ adhvaryuśabdābhidheyāḥ
adhyayanakāle | yadyadhyetṝṇāmadhvaryuśabdenābhidhānaṃ syāt tadā tadyogāt granthe samākhyā
syāt | na caitadasti | karmakāla eva tadvayapadeśa iti karmakarāṇāmevādhvaryvādiśabdā vācakāḥ
tadyogāt karmaṇyeva mukhyā saṃjñeti | kathaṃ punaraniyanaṃ manyate ? parikrayaṇādīnāṃ
nityavacchravaṇāt | yuktaṃ cotsargadvāreṇāpi kartṛtvopapatteḥ nityamanyaireva śeṣaḥ kartavya iti |
ayamabhiprāyaḥ---yadā svayamanutsāhī tadā parikrayaḥ yadā’sau parikrayaḥ tadā anyaireveti
manyate | asyārthaḥ--- dṛṣṭārthaparyālocanayā aśaktipakṣa eva puruṣāntarāpekṣāyāṃ parikrayādaya
iti | śaktena tu svayaṃ kartavyamiti | rāddhāntavādī tu manyate nityavadāmnānāt parikrayasya
yadā svayaṃ na śaknuyāt tadā parikrayeṇeti na śakyate kalpayitum | tena nityaṃ
parikrītaireva śeṣakāryaṃ kartavyam | nanu sāmarthyāt bhaviṣyati | yadā na śaknu-
yādityetat sāmarthyāt bhaviṣyati lokavat | pariharati---na hi śrutau pratyarthinyāṃ sāmarthye
prabhavati | śrutiścātra nityameva parikrayaṃ bravīti | yadetyaśravaṇādityuktam | loke tu
dṛṣṭārthatvādyadhathārthamiti yuktam | niyogastvatrādṛṣṭarūpaḥ parikrītapuruṣasādhya eveti nityaṃ
parikraya upādātavyaḥ |

[p. r919]

tatrārthāt kartṛparimāṇam | evaṃ sthite utsargādanyatra parikritakartṛtve pareṣāṃ
saṅkhyā’niyamaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | kāryārthaṃ hi puruṣā upādīyante | tatra yadā
yāvadbhiḥ kāryaṃ tāvanta upādeyāḥ | nanu stokeṣūpādīyamāneṣu kathaṃ samākhyā ghaṭiṣyate ?
kathaṃ ca varaṇabheda upapadyate ? vṛtatvāditi | tatrāha---ekasminnapi samākhyāvaraṇayośca
upapatteḥ | ekatraiva samākhyānāmupapattiḥ vṛkṣatarunyāyena | tathā ekasyaiva tatatkarmārthatayā
varaṇabheda upapadyate | rāddhāntavādī tu utpattau tāvat samākhyāto bhedaṃ manvānaḥ | śrutau
tāvadādhvaryavādisamākhyā pratīyate | tatra tadbhedāt sajñināmapi bheda evāvabhāti |
ma punareka eka saṃjñau codanāvagamyatvāt saṃjñānām | etā hi saṃjñāḥ codanāta evāva-
gamyante na lokataḥ | yadi lokato’vagamyeta tadā tadanurūpo’bhedo bhedo vā bhavet |
codanāvagamye yathācodanaṃ saṃjñābhedāt saṃjñibheda iti yāvaccodanaṃ puruṣāḥ yāvatyaḥ
utpatticodanāḥ tāvantaḥ puruṣā ityuktavān | evaṃ darśapūrṇamāsādiṣvapītyuktam | atrādhi-
karaṇe pūrvapakṣasiddhāntaracane īdṛśī---ekatrāpi saṃjñānānātvadarśanāt na bhedaḥ saṃjñināmiti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntastu puruṣāṇāmetāḥ saṃjñāścodanāvagamyāḥ | tatra ca śabdabhedāt
adhikārthatvameva nyāyyam | na punarekasyaiva śabdārthasya anekaśabdatvādupapattiḥ2 | yatra
punaḥ pramāṇāntareṇa darśanaṃ tatra kiṃ kriyatāmiti |
 
(1) P. 1089 (2) śabdatvāpattiḥ

[p. r920]

camasādhvaryavaśca tairvyapadeśāt | camasādhvaryavaśca kiṃ ṛtvik‌saṅkhyāsaṅkhyethāḥ
uta saṅkhyāntaraparicchedyāḥ ityākṣeytuṃ saṃśayo’nubhāṣitaḥ | ākṣepamāh---nanu ca
hotṛkāṇāṃ camasādhvaryava iti vyapadeśāt anyaireva camasādhvaryubhirbhavitavyam | tataśca
pūrvapakṣābhāvāt saṃśaya eva nāstītyarthaḥ | parasparavyapadeśādapyetadupapadyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
anyonyamṛtvijāṃ ṛtvigantarāpekṣayā sa vyapadeśa iti na ṛtvik‌saṅkhyātiriktāścamasādhvaryavaḥ |
na ṣaṣṭayantānāṃ parasparasaṃbandho vidyata iti saddhāntavādī vyatiriktaṃ manyate | asvārthaḥ
---sarva eva ṛtvijaḥ ṣaṣṭhayantena nirdiśyante ‘hotrakāṇāṃ’
 ‘madhyataḥ kāriṇāṃ’
 ityevamādiṣu
vākyeṣu | na ca ṣaṣṭhayante parasparaṃ sabandhāvagatiḥ yathā devadattasya bhrātā yajñadattasya bhrāteti
tābhyāmanyaḥ pratīyate na punaḥ parasparaṃ tayoḥ saṃbandha iti ṛtvigdhyatiriktāścamasādhvaryavaḥ |
utpattau ca saṃjñābhedāt yad‌yutpattau saṃjñābhedo na syāt tadā laukikīyaṃ samākhyeti
yathālokaṃ varteta | tataśca bhedo na syāt | uttpattau saṃjñābhedāt yukto’rthabhedaḥ | tasmādanye
camasādhvaryavaḥ iti |
 
utpattau bahuśruteḥ | idamidānīṃ sandihyate---kimaniyatā uta bahava iti |

[p. r921]

prātipadikārthasiddheraniyatāḥ | ekasminnapi prātipadikārthasiddheraniyatāḥ | nanu kathaṃ
bahuvacanaṃ camasādhvaryūn vṛṇīta iti tatrāha---aniyame’pi pakṣe bahuvacanopapatteḥ |
rāddhāntamāha---na, nityavadbahuvacanasya śrūyamāṇatvāt pākṣikatvānupapatteḥ | nanu ca
camasādhvaryūṇāmūddeśyatvāt grahavadavivakṣitā saṅkhyetyatrāha---utpattau ca bahuvacanasya
vivakṣitatvāt | asyārthaḥ---yadyapi camasādhvaryavaḥ iha vākye uddeśyāḥ tathāpi teṣāṃ
saṅkhyā’navagatā ityasti saṅkhyāpekṣā kiyanta utpādyanta iti | tenāpekṣitatvāt vivakṣitā
saṃkhyeti | grahe tu vijñātatvāt saṅkhyāyāḥ apekṣaiva nāstīti yuktā tatrāvivakṣā |
tadāha---asiddhārthalakṣaṇaṃ prātipadikaṃ na vacanamapekṣate notpādyavacanam | tasmādvahavaḥ |
idamevātra vyutpādyate utpādyagatā saṅkhyā uddeśyatvepi vivakṣiteti |

daśatvaṃ liṅgadarśanāt | sthite bahutve kimaniyamaḥ, uta niyataiva kācit
bahuvacanābhidheyā saṅkhyā, uta tritvasaṅkhyaiveti saṃśayaḥ | anena avāntarasaṅgatimāha
---kapiñjalavat tritvasaṅkhyaiveti aniyama iti vā prāpte daśapeye vikṛtibhūte siddhavat
daśa camasādhvaryudarśanāt daśaiveti niyamaḥ | svāmidaśatvācca | atra ca aniyamapakṣa

[p. r922]

upakramamātram | kapiñjalānyāyena traya eveti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ daśapeye daśadarśanādiha
daśa bhaveyurityatrāha---anyatrāpi niyatā saṅkhyā upadeśaṃ niyantumarhati avirodhāt |
yadyatra trayo bhaveyuḥ tadā siddhavaddarśanaṃ nopapadyate daśapakṣe tu bahuvacanasya na kaścidvirodhaḥ daśā-
nāmapi bahutvāt | tathā coktaṃ kāraṇadatikrama iti | nanu tenaiva tarhi gatārtha-
midam | na | anyavartitvāt daśasaṅkhyāyāḥ pūrvapakṣiṇo’dhikāśaṅkā |

śamitā ca śabdabhedāt | yathā saṃjñābhedāt camasādhvaryavaḥ ṛtvigbhyo bhidyante
evaṃ śamitāpi śabdabhedādbhidyate | anenāvāntarasaṅgatiruktā | yuktimāha---saṃjñā-
bhedaścopapanno bhavati | anyathā ekasyaivārthasyānekaśabdatvamanyāyyamāśritaṃ syāt iti |
rāddhāntamāha---nātra bhedaḥ utpattinibandhano hi bheda uktaḥ | na cāsyotpattirasti |
asyārthaḥ---na śabdabhedamātrādbheda uktaḥ | api tu utpatticodanābhedāt | utpatticoda-
nāyāṃ hi alaukikī saṃjñā bhavati | anyathā tu alaukikārthāntarābhāvāt prakṛtānāmanya-
tarasminnayaṃ śabdo vartata iti niścīyate | yadyasyotpattirnāsti kathaṃ tarhi saṃjñānyatvaṃ
(1) P. 1092

[p. r923]

nārthāntaraṃ vinā saṃjñānyatvamupapadyata iti autsargiko nyāyaḥ | uttaraṃ---naiveyaṃ saṃjñā, kiṃ
tarhi ? yaugikābhidhānameva śamayatīti śamitā | tena yaḥ śamayati paśuṃ sa prakṛtā-
nāmanyatamo bhaviṣyati | evaṃ cādhvaryavamiti samākhyā upapannā bhaviṣyati | śāmitra-
mapyādhvaryavamiti samākhyāyate | yadi cādhvaryureva śamitā evamupapadyate samākhyeti |

upagāśca liṅgadarśanāt | kena viśeṣeṇāyamadhikaraṇātideśaḥ ? yāvatā upagā-
śabdo’pi yaugikaḥ | na2 cāsyotpattirasti | ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ upagā iti gānakāriṇāṃ
saṃbandhāt camasādhvaryava iva hotrakāṇāmiti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | asyārthaḥ
---gātṝṇāmupagāḥ saṃbandhino bhavanti | na ca ṛtvigantarasaṃbandhitā ṛtvijaḥ | tena
yathā camasādhvaryavo hotrakāṇāṃ saṃbandhino’nye ca ṛtvigbhyaḥ tathā upagā iti pūrvapakṣavādī
manyate | utpatteraśravaṇādityatidiṣṭo rāddhāntaḥ | utpatteraśravaṇādarthāntarābhāvāt
teṣvevābamupagāśabdo yaugikaḥ | yaśca gātṛsaṃbandhaḥ sa gānopacayakāritvanibandhano na svāmi-
karmakaratvanibandhanaḥ | liṅgamuktam |

[p. r924]

vikrayī tvanyaḥ| somavikrayī punaḥ kimṛtvigeva utānya iti teṣāmṛtvijā-
mevānyatama iti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | codyamāha---nanu cācodito vikrayaḥ codi-
tasya ca ṛtvijaḥ kartṛtvenopādīyante | asyārthaḥ---yaccoditaṃ tatkratoraṅgam | yaccāṅgaṃ
tadyajamānena kartavyam | tadeva kartuṃ puruṣā upādīyanta iti kathaṃ vikrayaḥ ārtvijaḥ |
pariharati---krayopādānaśeṣaṃ vikrayaṃ manvānasya pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | krayastāvaccoditaḥ
na cāsau vikrayaṃ vinā sidhyati | na kaścitsomavikraye svataḥ pravṛttaḥ somavikrayasya pratiṣiddha-
tvāt | kratvarthatve tu vikrayasya nāsti pratiṣedhaḥ iti krayopādānaśeṣatayā vikrayo’pyaṅgam |
ataḥ ṛtvijaiva kartavyaṃ itarāṅgavaditi pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu lobhādeva vikretu-
rākṣepasiddheḥ dravyārjanavanna śeṣaṃ manyate | yadyapyasti pratiṣedhaḥ tathāpi kecillobhāt ati-
krāntaniṣedhāḥ svata eva somavikraye’pi pravṛttāḥ | tānāśritya prayogavidhiḥ na somavikraya-
mākṣipati yathā dravyārjanam | api ca śūdrādīnāṃ sarvavikrayābhyanujñānāt niṣedhātikramo’pi
nāsti ityanārtvijameva | ato’nyaḥ somavikrayīti siddham |

karmakāryāt sarveṣām | nanu cātra rāddhānto na ghaṭate | kathaṃ na ghaṭate ? ākṣeptā
(1) P. 1094

[p. r925]

āha---utpattāvapi parisaṅkhyaiva prāpnoti yadyutpattireva ṛtvika्śabdasya pravṛttau nimitte
tathāpi parisaṅkhyaiva prāpnoti saptadaśartvija iti | kutaḥ ? camasādhvaryūṇāmapyutpatti-
śravaṇāt | utpattinimittatve teṣāmapi ṛtvika्śabdapravṛttau saptadaśeti saṅkhyānupapattau
parisaṅkhyaivāśrayaṇīyā | atha parisaṅkhyaiveṣṭā’bhidhānasya ṛtuyajananimittanirasanamanarthakaṃ
syāt | ṛtuyajananimittatvepi ṛtvigabhidhānasya parisaṅkhyaiveyaṃ bhaviṣyati saptadaśaṛtvija
iti rāddhāntaṃ saṃbandhayati---atrocyate | utpattau nimittabhūtāyāmaṅgīkriyamāṇāyāṃ
naivaṃ parisaṅkhyā prāpnoti ekaikatrodgātrādiśabdatulyatvāt ṛtvika्śabdasya nātrāpahnavanīyaṃ
kiñcidasti | asyārthaḥ---tatra parisaṅkhyā bhavati yatrāpahnavanīyaṃ kiñcidasti, utpattau śabda-
nimittāyāṃ aṅgīkriyamāṇāyāṃ yathodgātrādiśabdaḥ ekaikavālaukikārthaḥ tathā’yamapi ṛtvik-
śabdaḥ ṣoḍaśasu codanaikasamadhigamyavācakabhāvaḥ | tena yatraiva vedena prayuktaḥ teṣāmeva
vācaka iti niścīyate nānyeṣāmiti nāsti parisaṅkhyeyam | ṛtuyajananimitte punaḥ
ṛtvika्śabde’rthāpahnavaḥ | sarveṣāmṛtau yajanāt | bhavatu ko doṣa ityatrāha---na cāsau śakyate
vacanaśatenāpi | 1vacanapravṛtteḥ sati nimitte durvāratvāt | yata evotpattinimittā ata
eva laukikīyaṃ saṃjñā | ata evoparitanamadhikaraṇamupapannaṃ bhavati | vaidikaprayogeṇa
ṛtvika्śabdārthaniścaye yatraiva prayujyate tatraiva vācaka iti niścīyate | tena ṣoḍaśānāmeva
ṛtvika्tvaṃ sidhyati | anyathā ṛtuyajananimittatve sarveṣāmṛtvika्śabdābhidheyatā syāt |
evaṃ ca vadatā pūrvādhikaraṇasya viśeṣaparyantatāvyavasthāpanamātramidamityuktaṃ bhavati |

(1) G. śabdavṛtteḥ

niyamastu dakṣiṇābhiḥ | ke ta iti saṃśayaḥ | ‘ṛtvigbhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dadāti’
 iti
prakramya dakṣiṇāviśeṣavidhipare vākye saṃpradānatvena kīrtayati yān te ta iti rāddhāntaḥ |
tathā ye yajamānāsta ṛtvija iti ca darśitam | yajamānānāmevārtvijye vidhānāt
ṛtvikkarmakaratayā yajamāneṣveva codakaprāptadīkṣākartṛviśeṣavidhipare vākye yān śabdān
prayuṅkte tacchabdavācyā ṛtvija iti siddhamadhvaryvādīnāmevartviktvam |

svāmisaptadaśāḥ | ‘saptadaśa ṛtvijaḥ’
 iti śrūyate | tatra kiṃ yajamānena saha
saptadaśa uta yajamānavyatiriktā anye saptadaśeti saṃśayaḥ evaṃ ca bruvatā gṛhapatiśabdo
yajamānaparo bhāṣye iti vyākhyātam | kiṃ prāptam ? yajamānādanye saptadaśa yasmāt
karmakarasaṅkhyaiṣā | na svāmī karmekaraḥ ‘karmaṇi bhṛtau’
 iti bhṛtipūrvaṃ karma kurvāṇe karmakara-
śabdo vyutpāditaḥ na svāmī bhṛtyakarma kurute3 | nanu mā nāma bhṛtyakarma karotu | kurute
tāvadasau tena kimiti tameva gṛhītvā saptadaśeti vyapadeśo na bhavati ityatrāha---ekadharma-
yuktānāṃ cāvāntarasaṅkyopapadyate | ityanya eveti yuktam | yadi hyekadharmayogo nāśrīyate
(1) P. 1094 (2) P. 1099 (3) R. bhṛtyāḥ karma kurvate |

[p. r927]

tadā sarveṣāṃ padārthānāṃ ekasaṅkhyaiva syāt avāntarasaṅkhyayā vyavahāro na syāt | asti
cāsau | tatra hi ekadharmayoga eva nimittam nartvijāṃ yajamānasya ca ekadharmayoga iti na
tadabhiprāyeṇa saṅkhyopapadyata ityanya eva saptadaśo yajamānāditi | atrocyate | svāmi-
saptadaśāḥ saptadaśaḥ svāmī yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ | nanu ca karmakarasāmānyaprayuktaiṣā avāntara-
saṃṅkhyā | pariharati---kena voktaṃ na karmakarasāmānyaprayukteti | kathaṃ tarhi svāmi-
saptadaśāḥ saptadaśaḥ | uttaram---na svāmi/?/yā yajamānaḥ saptadaśaḥ | kiṃ tarhi ? kartṛ-
tayaiva | etadeva spaṣṭayati---yajamānāpekṣaiṣā saṅkhyā nādhikāritayā | nanu ca ‘sadasyaṃ
vṛṇīta’
 iti varaṇotpattimaṅgīkṛtya eṣā saṅkhyā | na ca yajamāna utpadyate | asyārthaḥ
---yajamānotpādyatālakṣaṇaikadharmayogādeṣā saṅkhyā bhavati | asti ca yajamānenotpādyo’nyo’pi
saptadaśaḥ sadasyo nāma | tathā hi tasya varaṇaṃ samāmananti “sadasyaṃ vṛṇīte”
 iti |
atrāpi ca ṛtvika्śabdo lākṣaṇika eva | satyapi sadasye puruṣāntare tatra ṛtvika्śabdā-
prayogāt | ata eva bhāṣyakārīyaṃ sandehaṃ parityajya nibandhanakāreṇānyathā sandeho
darśitaḥ | bhāṣye ca sadasyagrahaṇamupalakṣaṇam | etaduktaṃ bhavati---yajamānādanyaṃ yaṃ kañcidabhi-
pretya iyaṃ saṅkhyā uta yajamānamabhipretyeti | codyaṃ pariharati---ucyate | ṣoḍaśānāmeva
varaṇenotpattiḥ | nanu ca sadasyaṃ vṛṇīte’ iti sadasyaḥ saptadaśo bhaviṣyati yadi brahmaṇye-
cāyaṃ sadasyaśabdo vartate | asyārthaḥ---dakṣiṇayā puruṣāṇāmupādānamiti vakṣyāmaḥ | varaṇaṃ

[p. r928]

tadanugrāhakam | tena yadyapi sadasyasya varaṇaṃ śrūyate tathāpi dakṣiṇāśravaṇāt na sadasyo
nāmānyaḥ kaścit saṃbhavati | dakṣiṇā ca brahmaṇaḥ śrūyate | varaṇaṃ ca tasya brahmaśabdena
noktam | hotrādīnāṃ tu varaṇaṃ hotrādiśabdenaiva | tena brahmaṇa evedaṃ varaṇam | śaknoti
ca tatra yogikayā vṛttyā sadasyaśabdo vartitum | sa hi sadasi bhavaḥ | tasmāt svābhi-
saptadaśāḥ parigṛhyante | atha kasmādekaścamasādhvaryurna parigṛhyate notpādyapuruṣābhi-
prāyā saptadaśasaṅkhyeti | pariharati---na te yajamānena varitavyā ityuktam tenotpattiḥ
teṣāmekarūpā na bhavatītyarthaḥ | tasmāt karmakaraiḥ ṛtvigbhiḥ sāmānyāt samatvāt
yajamānasya nityakarmakaratvāt yajamāna eva saptadaśo na camasādhvaryuḥ aśakti-
viṣayatvāditi |

te sarvārthāḥ prayuktatvāt | idamidānīṃ sandihyate---kiṃ sarvārthā ṛtvijaḥ
yāvatyuruṣakāryaṃ yajamānasya tāvataiḥ kriyate,uta yāvadvacanamiti | kratuprakaraṇaṃ eva
yāvatpuruṣakāryaṃ tāvattaiḥ sarvaiḥ kartavyaṃ aniyamena, uta yadeva yasya vacanena samākhyayā vā
prāptaṃ tadeveti | agnayaśca svakālatvāditi kibhidaṃ ? ekagranthenāha---nanu cādhānasya
sarvārthatāṃ vadatā agnīnāṃ sarvārthatvamuktam | kimidaṃ punassarvārthatocyate | asyārthaḥ
(1) P. 1102

[p. r929]

yadi sādhyatayā sarvārthatā’gnīnāmucyate, tadayuktam, sādhitatvāt | atha nirākāryatayocyate,
tanna, sthitasya nyāyasyoddhārābhāvāt | vadati ca svakālatvādagnayaḥ sarvārthā iti | ‘samadhigata-
metadi’
ti sthitamevārthaṃ darśayati | tasmādayuktamityākṣepaḥ | samādhatte---saiveyaṃ sarvārthatā atra puna-
ssādhyate svakālatvāditi | asyārthaḥ---etaduktaṃ bhavati bhinnena sūtreṇādhikaraṇāntarametat
sarvārthatāmākṣipya samarthayitumiti | tatrāyamākṣepaḥ pūrvamuktaḥ āhitāgneradhikāraśravaṇāt
āhitānāṃ kratvarthatā | tena(na) viniyuktānāmagnīnāṃ utpattiḥ ityādhānaṃ sarvārthamuktam |
tatredamucyate---nanvetadevāyuktaṃ āhitāgneradhikāra iti yathā’dhikṛtaḥ puroḍāśādikamutpādayati
tathaivāgnīn ityetadeva yuktamiti | tatredaṃ samādhānam svakālatvādādhānasyāntataḥ kartumaśakteḥ
āhitāgneradhikāra iti | atra codayati---nanu ca svakālatvāt yāvadagnikāryaṃ tatsarvaṃ
agnibhirityucyate yathā yāvatpuruṣakāryaṃ tatsarvamṛtvigbhiriti | asyārthaḥ---eka-
sūtropāttatvādagnīnāṃ ṛtvijāṃ ca īdṛśī sarvārthatocyate | ṛtvijāṃ ca yāvatpuruṣakāri-
tayā sarvārthatvamucyate | tenāgnīnāmapi svakālatvāt sakalāgnikāryakāritaivocyata iti |
ekadeśī pṛcchati---tataḥ kiṃ ? ākṣeptā āha---tadetannopapadyate 1samadhigatametat

hetoḥ | tasmāt sūtradvayametat | yataḥ sūtradvayam ata eva ādhastyasūtrasya

praiṣānuvacanaṃ metrāvaruṇasya | ‘maitrāvaruṇaḥ preṣyati cānucāhe’
ti kiṃ praiṣamātra
anuvacanasaṃyuktamasaṃyuktaṃ ca sarvaṃ tathā anuvacanamātraṃ tu yatpraiṣeṇa saṃyuktamasaṃyuktaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ

varuṇaviniyogadarśanāt | darśanamuktam |

camasāṃścamasādhvaryavaḥ | kiṃ camasaiścamasādhvaryavo juhvati utādhvaryureveti saṃśaye

svāmikarma parikrayaḥ | samākhyayā ādhvaryavamiti prāpte adhikāraśrutiviro-
dhāt yājamānamiti rāddhāntaḥ | asyārthaḥ---ādhvaryavamiti samākhyāte kāṇḍe pari-
krayatya paṭhitatvāt adhvaryukartṛkameva prāptam | na cedamāśaṅkanīyam kimiti parakīyakarmakaraṇāya
paraḥ puruṣāntarāṇi parikrīṇīte iti, yataḥ samadhikadhana2lobhādayamartho loke dṛśyata iti | evaṃ
prāpte brūmaḥ---adhikāraśruteryajamānasya sarvatra kartṛtāmāpādayantyāḥ pradhānabhūtāyā virodhāt
samākhyābādhena yājamāna eva parikrayaḥ | adhvaryuparikrītapuruṣāntarakṛtāḥ padārthāḥ na
yajamānena kṛtāḥ nāpi kāritāḥ | nanu laukikīmadhvaryuprayuktimāśritya yajamānakāritā
bhaviṣyati | netyucyate | yāvadeva śrūyate tāvadevāśritya śāstrārthavarṇanā yuktā | na ca parikrayā-
ntaramadhvaryoḥ śrūyate | yāvatī cādhvaryoḥ dakṣiṇā’’mnāyate na tāvatyā yajamānasya puruṣāntarapari-
krayārthaṃ adhvaryuṃ prati prayojakatā saṃbhavati | tasmādādhvaryavatve parikrayasya adhikāraśrutivirodhaḥ |
vacanādṛtvijāmapi parikrayaḥ syāt | tatra ca parikrayāntarakalpanā’nupapanneti |

saṃskārāstu puruṣasāmarthye | vapanādīnāṃ samākhyayā nādhikāro virudhyata iti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | anena pūrvādhikaraṇādviśeṣamāha | yathā pūrvatrārtvijatve adhikāraśrutivirodhaḥ

virodhāt samākhyābādhaḥ |

reva mametyupapannaṃ bhavati yajamānaprayojyapakṣe |

dvayāmnāteṣūbhau | kecinmantrāḥ yājamānakāṇḍe ādhvaryave ca samāmnāyante |
tatra saṃśayaḥ---kiṃ dvābhyāṃ kartavyāḥ utādhvaryuṇaiveti | kathaṃ punaḥ dvayāmnānāt dvayoḥ
kāṇḍayorāmnānāt dvābhyāṃ ca adhvaryuyajamānābhyāṃ samākhyātā ekena vaktavyā iti
pratibhāti pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ | samādhatte---ekakāryatvānmantrāṇāmekārthaprakāśanakāryakāritvāt
ekaprayojyatāṃ ekādhvaryuprayojyatāṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | adhvaryoreva karmakaratvāt
tenaiva smaraṇārthaṃ prayoktavyā mantrā iti | rāddhāntavādī tu ekasyādhvaryoranuṣṭhānasmaraṇāt
anyasya ca yajamānasya pratyavekṣaṇāt āmnānadvayasyāpyarthavattvāt dvābhyāmapi prayokta-
vyā iti manyate samākhyādvaye cānugṛhītaṃ bhaviṣyati |

[p. r941]

jñāte ca vacanaṃ na hyavidvān vihito’sti | “klṛptīryajamānaṃ vācayati”
 iti
śrūyate | ki vidvāneva vācayitavvaḥ utāvidvānapi” iti saṃśayaḥ bhāṣyokto’nubhāṣitaḥ
ākṣeptum | ākṣipati---kathaṃ punaḥ viduṣo’dhikāre aviduṣyāśaṅkā | yadi hi
aviduṣo’dhikāraḥ syāt tadā tasya vācanaṃ syāt | na ca tasyādhikāro’sti | samādhatte
---vācayatītyetasmādeva | na hi viduṣi vācayatīti dṛṣṭārthatā saṃbhavati iti viduṣyādhi-
kāriṇi svayameva bruvati vācayatītyadṛṣṭārthaṃ bhavati | aviduṣi dṛṣṭārtham | tasmādaviduṣo’dhi-
kāraḥ | nanu yadyaviduṣi dṛṣṭārthatā evaṃ tarhi aviduṣa eva vācanaṃ syāt | samādhātā
āha---bhavatu ko doṣaḥ | ākṣeptā āha---viduṣo’dhikārahāniḥ prāpnoti | tatpakṣe
vācanāṅgābhāvāt sakalāṅgopasaṃhārī prayogavidhiḥ yasminneva tathābhūtaḥ saṃbhavati tasyaivādhikāraḥ |
samādhātā āha---nādhikāraṃ nivārayāmaḥ aviduṣo’pyadhikāro’sti idaṃphalamidapradhi-
karaṇaṃ pūrvapakṣadaśāyām | asyārthaḥ-dvayorapyadhikāraḥ avidvatpakṣe ca dṛṣṭārthatayā vācana---
maṅgam | rāddhāntamāha---nādhikāramaviduṣo’pyākṣeptuṃ arthādupasthāpayituṃ samarthamidaṃ
vacanam | kutaḥ? brūvantameva hi vācayatīti śabdo bravīti iti nābruvataḥ pravṛtti-
māha | ya eva prakṛtyarthe pravṛttaḥ tatprayojakatva eva ṇijutpadyate nānyatra | viduṣaśca
(1) P. 1121

[p. r942]

prakṛtyarthe pravṛttiḥ nāviduṣaḥ | dṛṣṭārthatvaṃ uktasyoccāraṇenāpi bhavati | tena vidva-
dviṣayatvādasyāpi vācanasya nāviduṣo’dhikāraḥ | ato yathādhikāraṃ vācanamiti siddham |
yasyaiva viduṣo’dhikāraḥ tasyaiva vācanamiti yāvat |

yājamāne samākhyānāt | iha dvandvānyudāharaṇam | kiṃ yajamānena kartavyāni,
utādhvaryuṇeti saṃśayaḥ | kathaṃ saṃśayaḥ ? ekātrādhvaryave padārthānāmāmnānāt anyatra
yājamāne samabhyāśīkaraṇāt | padārthānuṣṭhānena ca samabhyāśīkaraṇasaṃpattiḥ | tena yatra
padārthāḥ tena samabhyāśīkaraṇamapi kriyatām, uta yatra samabhyāśīkaraṇaṃ tena padārthā api
kriyantāṃ iti | tatra viśeṣasamākhyāyā yājamānāni | sāmānyenādhvaryave prāpte viśeṣeṇa
yājamānasamākhyayā adhvaryusamākhyābādhena yājamānāni dvandvāni | nanu bhavatu samabhyāśīkaraṇaṃ
yājamānam | padārthastu kathamityatrāha---padārthapūrvakatvāt samabhyāśasya | anuṣṭheya-
padārthasamabhyāśaḥ padārthānuṣṭhānenaiva saṃpadyata iti padārthā api yājamānā eva | atrocyate
---padārthāstāvadādhvaryavā eva samāmnātāḥ anupajātavirodhittvādadhvaryuṇaiva kartavyāḥ |
(1) 1122

[p. r943]

pūrvabhāvino hi padārthāḥ | teṣāṃ tāvadadhvaryukartṛtaiva | akriyamāṇe padārthe na yajamānena
samabhyāśaḥ śakyate kartumiti so’pyādhvaryava eva | viśeṣasamākhyāpravṛttistu darśitā |
yajamānena idaṃ cedaṃ ca kurvityabhidhānāt keṣuciccānumantraṇaprayogāditi |

vipratiṣedhe karaṇaḥ | ṛtvigantarakāryopadeśe kimātmīyaṃ karma karotu virodhe
utātidiṣṭimiti saṃśaye atidiṣṭaṃ pratyakṣavidhānāditi siddham | spaṣṭatvāt pūrvapakṣo
na likhitaḥ | pratyakṣavihitenaikakāryakāriṇā ātideśikasya bādhaḥ | na cātraikaṃ
kāryamastīti nāsti bādhaḥ | yadi paraṃ virodhādanuṣṭhānāśaktiḥ tatraikamanuṣṭhāsyatīti | rāddhāntastu
ekakāryatvābhāvepi tāvadasti virodhaḥ | tatra pratyakṣavihitasyānanyagatitvāt balīyastvaṃ,
itarasya prakṛtāvarthavatteti |

praiṣeṣu parādhikārāt | praiṣapraiṣārthau kimekena kartavyau uta dvābhyāmiti saṃśaye
(1) 1123 (2) 1127

[p. r944]

kathaṃ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ aśakyatvāt ityatrāha---prāptakālabhrāntyā ekeneti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
agnīdagnīna vihara iti prāptakāle loṭbhaviṣyati | tenānuṣṭhātā evaṃ brūte-agnīt agni-
viharaṇasya prāptaḥ kālaḥ | tena nāstyaśakyateti samākhyānāt dvayamapyādhvaryavameveti pūrvaḥ
pakṣaḥ | yuṣmadarthāparigrahe kāraṇābhāvāt praiṣa evāyaṃ loḍiti rāddhāntaḥ | madhyama-
puruṣāvivakṣāprasaṅgāt na prāptakāle loṭ | kintu praiṣa eveti aśakyatvāt dvābhyāṃ kartavyau
praiṣapraiṣārthau iti |

adhvaryustu darśanāt | idānīṃ sthite kartṛdvaye2 kimadhvaryuḥ praiṣārthaṃ praiṣarūpamarthaṃ
karotu utānya iti saṃśaye “yadanvañcaṃ sphayaṃ dhārayet vajro vai sphayaḥ vajreṇaivādhvaryuḥ
kṣiṇvīta”
 ityarthavādāt anyaḥ praiṣaṃ karotīti | anyatra sphayadarśanāt | yasya ca haste
sphayaḥ sa preṣakaḥ tena agnīt preṣakaḥ preṣyaścādhvaryuḥ | samākhyāpyarthavādapadānuguṇyena
bādhyate | evaṃ prāpte mantraliṅgādadhvaryuḥ praiṣaṃ karotīti | ‘agnīdagnīn vihara’
 iti
mantre agnīvaḥ praiṣārthāvagateḥ arthavādācca mantrasya balīyastvādanuṣṭhānabodhakatvāt samākhyānārtha-
saṃbhavācca adhvaryureva praiṣakaḥ | arthavādastu guṇavādena gauṇyā vṛttyā parihṛto
bhāṣye ‘adhvaryuśabdenāgnīdeba gauṇyā vṛttyocyata iti |

ṛtvik phalaṃ karaṇeṣvarthavattvāt | kaḥ punarasyāśīrmantrairviśeṣaḥ ? āśīrmantrā
yājamānā eveti nirṇītam | tatheme mantrā yājamānā eva kimiti na saṃbhavantīti viṣaya-
śuddhireva nāsti | ādhvaryavatve hi cinteyamupapadyate ki yajamānaphalaprakāśaḥ utartvik‌phala-
prakāśa iti na yājamānatve | uttaraṃ---samākhyātaḥ sthite adhvaryukartṛtve agnyanvādhānasya
śrutyā mantravidhānam | na ca guṇānurodhena pradhānabādhaḥ śakyate kartum | tasmādādhvaryabo
mantraḥ | agnyanvādhānaṃ tāvat ādhvaryavameva | tadaṅgabhūto mantro’pi tasyaiva | na
punarmantraliṅgālocanayā mantre yajamāne prayoktari siddhe’gnyanvādhānamapi yājamānameva |
pradhāne’gnyanvādhāne kartṛbādhaprasaṅgāt | tena mantragatakartṛbādhādādhvaryavo mantra iti
sthite kasyāśīriti cintyate | liṅgādadhvaryoḥ | vaktā hi uttamapuruṣeṇa saṃbadhyate
sarvatra hi vaktrabhiprāyeṇa uttamapuruṣo bhavati karomīti | yathā asmacchabdottamapuruṣau
ca samānaviṣayau | tenāsmacchabdo’yaṃ ‘mamāgnevarcaḥ’
 iti | tatra kartṛbhūtādhvaryu-
viṣaya eveti | ākṣipātaṃ---nanu karmasaṃbaddhaphalasaṅkīrtanāt karmārthatā
mantrāṇām | na ca yaśaḥ karmaphalaṃ codanāvagatamiti kathaṃ tatprakāśakatayā karmārthatā |
uttaraṃ---karmaprayuktayaśaḥ prakāśayataḥ upapannaiva karmārthatā iti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | rāddhā-
ntavādītu yaścārthādartho na sa codanārtha iti manbānaḥ karmasaṃbaddhameva phalaṃ prakāśayan mantraḥ
(1) P. 1130

[p. r946]

karmārtho bhavati iti manyate | asyārthaḥ---na yaśaḥ karmaphalatayā codanāto’vagamyate; kintu
arthāt | yaścārthādarthaḥ na sa codanārtha coditaṃ ca phalaṃ mantreṇa prakāśayitavyam |
nānuṣaṅgikamapi | ato yajamānasaṃbaddhameva phalaṃ varcaḥ śabdena prakāśyate | mama yajamāna-
syeti mantrārthaḥ | asmacchabdena bhaktyā yajamāna evocyata iti |

karmārthaṃ tu phalaṃ teṣāṃ svāminaṃ pratyarthavattvāt | yatra punaḥ dṛṣṭārthameba phalaṃ asantāpa-
lakṣaṇaṃ tat kiṃ ṛtvijāmeva utātrāpi karmaphalameveti | kiṃ ṛtvik‌saṃbandhyasantāpa
āśāsyate ‘mā mā santāptaṃ’
 iti uta yajamānaphalameva karmajanyaṃ saṅkīrtyata iti sandehe
uktena nyāyena karmaphalameveti vadāma iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | atrocyate---yaścārthādartho
na sa codanārtha iti anupakārakaṃ phalamaṅgīkṛtya karmānupakārakaphalābhiprāyeṇaitaduktam |
yatpunaḥ karmaṇa upakaroti yathā’santāpaḥ | asantaptaḥ adhvaryuḥ karma kartuṃ śaknoti |
tena yatkarmopakārakaṃ tadupādānaśeṣatayā bhavatyeva karmārthamiti tatprakāśakānāmapi
mantrāṇāṃ na karmārthatā’pahīyate ityārtvija evāsantāpaḥ kīrtyate iti |

(1) P. 1132 

[p. r947]

vyapadeśācca | yatra ca vyapadiśyata evartvijaḥ phalaṃ taddarśitaṃ “tannau saha"

iti | etaccāpavādamātram | tannau saheti dvivacanasyāvivakṣākāraṇābhāvāt ṛtvijo’pi
bhavatyeva phalam |

dravyasaṃskāraḥ prakaraṇāviśeṣāt | vedirvedidharmāścetyuktamuttaravivakṣayeti |
vedirvedidharmāścāṅgapradhānārthā ityuktameva | tadeva tu uttaracintārthaṃ smaryate |

nirdeśāttu | sthitamaṅgapradhānārthaṃ barhiriti prakṛtau | vikṛtāvidānīṃ cintyate
-kimatrāpi aṅgapradhānārthe barhiṣi dharmā bhavanti yāvantyaṅgāni prākṛtāni aprākṛtāni ca
kiṃ tadarthe barhirdharmā bhavanti | uta prākṛtakāryaprayukta eveti prakṛtau yatkāryāpanne
barhirdharmā dṛṣṭāḥ vikṛtāvapi tatkāryāpanne eveti | tatrāṅgapradhānasaṃbandhitāsāmānyena
prakṛtivadvikṛtāvapīti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | asyārthaḥ---prakṛtau aṅgapradhānārthā dharmāḥ

[p. r948]

vikṛtāvapi tadarthā eva | anyathā prakṛtivadvikṛtirna syāt | prakṛtau ca sarvāṅgapradhānārthā
dharmāḥ vikṛtāvapi sarvāṅgapradhānārthā iti | rāddhāntavādī tu manyate kāryamukhena dharmāṇāṃ
vikṛtau nirūpaṇam | na yathāprakṛtisaṃbandhitayā | asyārthaḥ---vikṛtāvupakāralakṣaṇa-
kāryākāṅkṣāyāṃ prākṛta upakāraḥ prāptyate | sa ca padārthairvinā pratyetubhaśakyaḥ iti padārthai-
ravacchinnaḥ pratīyate | tatkāryamukhena vikṛtau padārthānāṃ nirūpaṇam | na punaryathāprakṛtau
sabanvaḥ tathā vikṛtāvapi saṃbandhayitavyā iti | sambandhasāmānyena hi sarvetropadeśaḥ
syāt | kāryamukhena hi padārthaprāptiratideśaḥ svatantrapadārthaprāptirupadeśa iti viśeṣaḥ |
yadi vikṛtāvapi kāryanirapekṣa eva padārthasaṃbandhaḥ tadā upadeśa eva syāt | tataścohadarśanaṃ
nopapadyate | upadeśe hi yathopadeśamevāvikāreṇānuṣṭhānaṃ syāt | tasmāt siddhaṃ
kāryāntarāpanne aprākṛte yūpāṅgabarhiṣi barhirdharmā na kartavyāḥ aprākṛtatvāt | añjanā-
jye ca nājyadharmāḥ iti |

virodhe ca śrutiviśeṣāt | anekatra śeṣabhāvo na nyāyya iti paribhojanīyādanyat
kartavyam | atra spaṣṭatvāt pūrvapakṣo na likhitaḥ | tathā hi---apracchinnāgrau darmau
(1) P. 1134

[p. r949]

prādeśamātrau pavitre karoti iti svarūpamātreṇa viniyogāt viniyuktavirodho nāstīti

vyāpṛtasyaiva sato’nyatra kārakatvābhāvāt paribhojanīyādbarhiṣo darbhāvupādāya pavitre

apanayastvekadeśasya| pūrvādhikaraṇanyāyenānekatra yāge aindravāyavapātranidhāne
ca śeṣabhāvo na nyāyya iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | dvayavadānopasaṃhārāt pratipatteśca rāddhāntaḥ |
asyārthaḥ---na tāvadanekatra kārakabhāvavirodhaḥ dvayavadānamātreṇa yāgopasaṃhārāt pariśiṣṭasyā-

iti uta kevalapradhānārthā iti | prakṛtivadaṅgapradhānārthatā dharmāṇāmiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
upāṃśutvaṃ kartṛdvāreṇāṅgam | upāṃśubhāvena hi akhinnaḥ kartā karma kartuṃ śaknoti | aṅga-

pradhānārthaśca kartā | tena yathā sākṣādupadiṣṭāyāṃ prakṛtau aṅgapradhānārthatā evaṃvidhasya
2karmaṇaḥ tathā vikṛtāvapīti naupadeśikātideśikaiḥ upadeśaḥ saṃbadhyata iti rāddhāntaḥ |
atulyatvādaupadaśikena pradhānena ātideśikaiścāṅgaiḥ upāṃśutvopadeśo na saṃbadhyata ityarthaḥ |

sannidhānaviśeṣāt | śyene tu aupadeśikasaṃbandhābhāvāt ātideśikaireva
