tatkāle vā | liṅgadarśanabhrāntyā sutyākālānāmevātra vidhānamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
asyārthaḥ---sādyaskre ‘saha paśūnālabhata’
 iti sahatvaṃ śrutam | tat sutyākāla eveti
tato’vagacchāmaḥ | yatsāmānyavihitaṃ tat sutyākāla eva bhavatīti | anayā bhrāntyā
sutyākālānāmevāṅgānāṃ dṛtinavanītavidhiriti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | pradhānāsattistu mūlayuktiḥ pūrva-
pakṣe | aviśeṣāt sarveṣāmiti rāddhāntaḥ | pratyāsannānāmapratyāsannānāṃ ca pradhāna-
saṃbandhāviśeṣāt navanītavidhiriti rāddhāntaḥ nyāyālliṅgopapattiruktā | sutyākāla eva yaḥ
paśūnāṃ sahālambhaḥ so’gnīṣomīyānubandhyayoravaiṣamyāditi bhāṣye uktam |

māṃsantu savanīyānām | ‘tarasāḥ savanīyāḥ puroḍāśā bhavanti’
 iti śrūyate |
tatra savanīyapuroḍāśayorvivakṣāyāṃ vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt kasyāyathārthatvaṃ yuktamiti
carcāyāṃ dṛṣṭavyabhicārāt puroḍāśasyāyathārthatvamiti rāddhāntitam | asyārthaḥ---tarasāḥ
savanīyāḥ puroḍāśā ityatra savanīyoddeśena vā tarasā vidhīyante, puroḍāśoddeśena vā | tatroddiśya-
mānānāṃ savanīyānāṃ na puroḍāśo viśeṣaṇatvena vivakṣituṃ nyāyyaḥ vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | nāpi
(1) P. 1121

[p. r952]

puroḍāśasyoddiśyamānasya savanīyo viśeṣaṇam, vākyabhedaprasaṅgādeva | ataḥ ekasya nityānu-
vādatvena varṇanam | na ca sarve savanīyāḥ puroḍāśā eveti puroḍāśāpuroḍāśeṣu daṇḍinyāyena
lakṣaṇayā puroḍāśaśabdo varṇanīyaḥ | tathā puroḍāśasya sarvasva sabanīyatvābhāvāt savanīyā-
savanīyeṣu savanīyaśabdaḥ | tatra kasya lakṣaṇayā varṇanaṃ kriyatāmiti cintāyāṃ savanīyaśabdasya
prathamopanipātino yathārthatvaṃ yuktam | puroḍāśabdasya jaghanyatvāt dṛṣṭavyabhicārācca | dṛṣṭo
hi prakṛtau puroḍāśāpuroḍāśeṣu puroḍāśaśabdaḥ tadvadihāpi, prakṛtivadatidiṣṭatvāditi puroḍāśa-
śabda eva lakṣaṇayā varṇanīya iti nyāyyam | atra bhāṣye “tarasāḥ puroḍāśāḥ savanīyā
bhavanti”
 ityudāhṛtam | tatra puroḍāśaśabdasyaiva prāgbhāvitvāt yathārthatvaṃ nyāyyamiti
rāddhānto na śakyate vaktumiti matvā nibandhanakāreṇa ‘tarasāḥ savanīyāḥ puroḍāśā
bhavanti iti’
 iti viparyasyodāhṛtamiti |

iti mahāmahopādhyāyaśālikanāthamiśrakṛtāyāṃ
ṛjuvimalāyāṃ pañcikāyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya
aṣṭamaḥ pādaḥ

[p. r953]

atha ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

caturthādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ

athātaḥ | atrādhikaraṇe upādānalakṣaṇo vidhivyāpāraḥ pramāṇāntarabhūtaḥ kvacidaida-
marthye kvacidanuṣṭhāne kvacidadhikāre kvacidutpattau vidyata iti pratipādyate | tadyathā prokṣaṇādīnā-
manvitābhidhānataḥ pratipannaniyogaidamarthyānāṃ śrutyādisiddhanaiyogikavrīhyādidvāraidamarthyānāṃ paścādau
pādānikaṃ tadubhayapratītinirvāhakaṃ dvāraidamarthyasādhyaṃ karaṇaidamarthyam | tathā nimittaparyante-
’dhikāre sāṅgeṣu pradhāneṣu prayoga upādānata eva | tathā viśvajidādiṣu adhikārakalpanā
aupādānikī | sauryaṃ caruṃ nirvapet brahmavarcasakāma ityādiṣu kevalādhikāracodanāsu utpatti-
niyogākṣepa upādānādeva | na khalu utpattiniyogaṃ vinā tatrāṅgopaskṛtātpradhānāt adhikāra-
niyogasiddharupapadyate | karmasvarūpāṇāṃ kṣaṇikatvena upakāryopakārakatvābhāvāditi darśita-
mekādaśe bhāṣyakāreṇa | sa cāyamupādānalakṣaṇo vidhivyāpāraḥ śrutyādipramāṇaṣaṭ‌kātirekī
tārtīyaviniyogāpekṣa iti tatra tatra vakṣyāma iti lakṣaṇayorapi hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ | kratvartheti
ṭīkāyā ayamarthaḥ---kratvarthapuruṣārthacintā kimiti pṛthak pratijñāyate athātaśśeṣalakṣaṇamityane-
naiva pratijñātatvāt | na ca vācyaṃ śeṣatvaṃ tatra pratijñātaṃ na punaraidamarthyamiti | yasmāta
aidamarthyalakṣaṇameva śeṣatvaṃ śeṣaḥ parārthatvādityuktam | pṛthagadhyāyārthābhāvāt adhyāyā-
(1) P. Bhasya 1193

[p. r954]

ntaramayuktamityapi dūṣaṇaṃ hṛdi vartate | atha matamityādi---tatrāyamabhiprāyaḥ vainiyogikaṃ
śeṣatvaṃ vicāritam | idānīṃ punaḥ aupādānikaṃ pṛthak pratijñāyate | tathāhi---puruṣārthatā
yāgasya svargasādhanabhāvaḥ svargasya cādhikāripuruṣopabhogyatā 1na śrutyādibhiravagamyate | tathā
coktaṃ---tasya lipsārthalakṣaṇeti | tatrāha---kratvarthagrahaṇaṃ tarhi pratyuktam | yasmāt
kratvarthatā sarvaiva śrutyādigamyeti na ceyamapi puruṣārthatālakṣaṇeti śakyate vaktum |
yasmānna śabdādṛte yāgaḥ svargārtha iti jñānamupapadyate | na ca tatsādhyaḥ svargaḥ puruṣārthaḥ
adhikāryarthaḥ iti jñānamutpadyate śabdatadbhāvabhāvitvāt śabdāt puruṣārthajñānaṃ śrutyādikṛtameveti
bhāvaḥ | yadapi ceti śrutyādisaṅkīrtanaṃ yattadapyanarthakamityarthaḥ | yadapi ca kratvartha-
puruṣārthayoḥ iti | bhāṣyakāro hi prayojakāprayojakādīnyatrāntarbhāvayati | tasyedaṃ
prayojanam---kathaṃ nāmottaro vicāraḥ pratijñātārthaviṣayaḥ syāditi | sa punarayamantarbhāvo
nopapadyate | antarbhāvo hi bhavan svarūpato vā syāt śeṣatvaidamarthyayoriva kāryato
vā ? sarveṣāmekaṃ kāryaṃ yathā viśeṣaliṅgādīnāṃ vaikṛtacodanāparipūraṇaṃ kāryamiti tatkāraṇa-
tvena pratijñāyate | sarveṣāmuttaraṣaṭ‌kārthānāmantarbhāvo bhavati | sa lakṣaṇato vā, sarveṣāmeka-
(1) G. omits na

[p. r955]

meva lakṣaṇaṃ yadi syāt yathā codanābhedādīnāṃ tallakṣaṇatayā pratijñāte sarve vedārthā antarbhave-
yuḥ | trayamapi cātra nāsti yasmāt svarūpaṃ tāvat pṛthagabhidhānādeva bhinnam |
yadyeṣāmekameva svarūpaṃ syāt tadā tatra tatrādhikaraṇe bhedenaiṣāmabhidhānaṃ pratipādanaṃ na syāt |
lakṣaṇamapi yena nyāyenaiṣāṃ svarūpaṃ lakṣyate tadapi svarūpabhedādeva bhinnam | na hyekena
lakṣaṇena svarūpabhedo bhavati pramāṇāyattatvāt prameyasiddheḥ, tadbhedādeva bhedo yataḥ prameyasyeti |
kāryamapi yatprayojakaphalavidhipradhānāni puruṣārthe’ntarbhāvayatā puruṣopakāraḥ | tathā aprayo-
jakārthavādapradhānāni kratvarthe’ntarbhāvayatā kratūpakāro bhāṣyakāreṇa darśitaḥ | tadayuktam |
nyāyavirodhāt pradeśāntaraviruddhaṃ ca arthavādādhikaraṇoktavirodhāditi | arthavādaḥ
stutyarthaḥ ukto hi yasmāt | prādhānyamapi ca upakāryopakāranibandhanam | yadupa-
kāryamupakārakasya tat tatpradhānam | na punaḥ puruṣārthatayā prādhānyam | kintūpakāryatayā |
prayojakamaṣi prayojyaṃ prati prādhānyamātmanaḥ pratipādayati | na punarātmanaḥ parārthatām |
prāvānyena hi prayojakatvaṃ, na punaḥ puruṣopakārakatvena1 | tasmāta kāryato’pi nāntarbhāvaḥ |
tadidaṃ rātriprayāṇakamivādeśakaṃ bhāṣyam rātrau bhavaṃ rātriprayāṇakaṃ, avidyamānamārgakathaka-
puruṣakaṃ vyāmohamupajanayati rātribhavatvena svapratyayavirahāt ādeśikapuruṣābhāvācca |
parapratyayābhāvāt deśīti mārgādeśika ucyate | ‘hetave puruṣārthānāṃ jyāyasāṃ deśine pathaḥ |
(1) G. katvaṃ

[p. r956]

iti | yathā ca ‘aviśrāmyaṃ anālambyaṃ apātheyamadeśikam | iti | atrocyate---dvayamapye-
tad‌yuktaṃ bhedena prayojakādīnāmupanyāsaḥ antarbhāvakathanaṃ ca tena tatra tāvanna kiñci-
dvācyam | yatpunastādarthyaṃ śeṣatve hetuḥ tena śeṣatvapratijñānāt tādarthyapratijñānāt
kratvarthapuruṣārthapratijñāpi kṛtaiveti | tatrāpi satsu śrutyādiṣu tadityuktaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa
śrutyādisaṅkīrtanena | tena kevalaniyogavyāpāranibandhanapratijñeyamityuktaṃ bhavati | nanu
caivaṃ sati kratvarthatā pratyuktetyapyuktam | tasyāḥ śrutyādinibandhanatvāt | bhavedetadevaṃ
yadi vainiyogikamucyate kratvarthaṃ; iha tu upādānanibandhanaṃ sarvaṃ kratvarthapuruṣārthaprayojakā-
prayojakaphalavidhyarthavādāṅgapradhānātmakaṃ pratijñāyate aupādānikamapi kratvarthamastīti bhāvaḥ |
puruṣārthatā tu yadyapi śābdī tathāpi vainiyogikī na bhavati iti lipsādhikaraṇe vakṣyati |
kiṃ punaratropādānaṃ nāma iti pūrvapakṣaṃ karoti | tatrāyaṃ bhāvaḥ---na śrutyādyatirekyupādāna-
lakṣaṇaṃ vidhivyāpārātmakaṃ pramāṇaṃ loke vyutpannamiti | rāddhāntaṃ karoti---niyogākṣepa
upādānam | satyaṃ na loke vyutpannaṃ tathāpi śabdena pratipādito’rtho yenārthāntareṇa vinā na
nirvahati tadākṣipatīti sāmānyatastāvalloke prasiddhirasti dvārakarmībhāvādau | vede ca
niyogaśśabdena pratipādito’rthāntaraṃ svanirvāhāya ākṣipatīti nānupapattiḥ | kiṃ punaru-
pādānasya prameyamiti tatrāha---tatkṛtaṃ ca prokṣaṇādeḥ kratvarthatvam karaṇārthatvamiti
prāgevāktam | viniyogāt punaḥ vrīhyarthataiva | naiyogikavrīhyādyaidamarthye viniyogasya
sāmarthyāt na karaṇaidamarthye | nanu yadi vidhyākṣiptamidaṃ cintyate atideśo’pi cintyaḥ

[p. r957]

syāt | na | ekopadeśaparisamāpyamiha cintyate | atideśaśca upadeśāntarārthaparyālocanayā
parisamāpyate | tasmādiha na cintyate | bhedārthapratijñānaṃ tarhi kimiti | bhedena
arthasya pratipādyasya prayojakāprayojakāṅgapradhānādeśca pratijñā kimiti | kimiti vā kena
prakāreṇāntarbhāvaḥ | avagatihetubhedāt bhedena upanyāsaḥ | avagatihetṛnāṃ nyāyānāṃ
bhedāt bhedena pratipādyatvāt kratvarthatādīnāṃ bhedena upanyāsaḥ | upakārasāmānyādantarbhāvaḥ
aprayojakāṅgārthavādāḥ kratūpakārā iti kratvarthe’ntarbhūtāḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati---kratvarthagrahaṇena
kratūpakārakasyāpi grahaṇam | arthavādasyāpi pramāṇakoṭiparyavasāyinaḥ pramāṇasyānuṣṭhānopakārakatvāt
kratūpakārakatāprayojakapradhānaphalavidhayaśca puruṣopakārasāmānyāt puruṣārthāntarbhāvina iti
siddham | “prayojakaḥ kaścit puruṣārtha”
 iti kaścid‪grahaṇaṃ prayojakasyāpyapuruṣārthatā-
jñāpanārtham yatheti kathanopakrama eva vaktuṃ kasyeti praśnaḥ | ācāryakaraṇavidheḥ
svādhyāyādhyayanaprayojakasyetyuttaramuktam | asyārthaḥ---ācāryakaraṇavidheḥ svādhyāyādhya-
yanaprayojakasyāpi svatantrakartṛrūpapuruṣaśabdavācyamāṇavakārthatvābhāvāt apuruṣārthatvam2 |
yadīyaṃ hi karma yo vidhiḥ prayuṅ‌kte sa tatpuruṣārtha eva | yathā darśapūrṇamāsādayaḥ | ācārya-
karaṇavidhistu māṇavakīyamadhyayanaṃ prayuṅ‌kte | natu māṇavakārtha iti siddham |

yasmin prītiḥ puruṣasya | “tasya lipsārthenaiva bhavati na śāstreṇa”
 iti
viruddhamuktam | anena hi kila bhāṣyeṇa yāgasya svargasādhanatātmikā puruṣārthatā svargasya ca
(1) P. 1193 (2) R. rthatvāt

[p. r958]

adhikāribhogyatātmikā śāstreṇa na gamyata ityuktam | tacca viruddham | yasmāt dvayamapye-
tat na śāstrādṛte’vagamyate | saṃbandhaśāstramaṅgīkṛtyaitaduktamiti | viniyogaśāstramabhi-
pretya idamuktaṃ nādhikāraśāstram | adhikāraśāstrādevopādānavaśena dvayamapīdaṃ gamyate yataḥ |
ata evoktaṃ arthenaiva bhavatīti | arthaśca niyogasiddhiḥ tasyā eva vākyārthatvāt
taccopādānamityuktam | atra cādhikaraṇe kratvarthapuruṣārthayoḥ pratipādayitavyayoḥ lakṣaṇa-
mucyate | tathāhi---na puruṣārthatvaṃ tāvat prītyutpādakatvam | nityeṣu niyamaniyogeṣu
cāvyāpteḥ | na ca sādhikāritvam, adhyayanādiṣvasaṃbhavāt | nāpi aupādānikaidamarthya-
bhāktvam, aupādānikakratvaṅgeṣu saṅkhyādiṣu atiprasaṅgāt | 1tasmiṃścāvivicyamāne puruṣārthatve
kratvarthatāpi tadbhadena durlabheti vivekāya lakṣaṇārambhaḥ | tatra puruṣārthalakṣaṇamaupādānikameva |
yasyaidamarthyaṃ sa puruṣārtha iti yathā yāgaḥ svargaśceti | nanu ca kratvarthatāpyupādānenaivoktā
saṅkhyādīnāmityanenātiprasaṅgadoṣamāśaṅkate | viniyoge satītyuktamiti parihāraḥ |
asyarthaḥ---viniyoge sati saṅkhyādīnāmaupādānikī śeṣatā | tathā hi---vastusvabhāvataḥ
tātsthyena liṅgaviniyoge sati saṅkhyādīnāmaupādānikaśeṣatvaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | tena viniyogena
vinā aupādānikaṃ yasya śeṣatvaṃ sa puruṣārthaḥ iti nāstyatiprasaṅgaḥ | arthādeva hi puruṣārtha
iti viśeṣaḥ | kimidamavibhāgāditi | bhāṣyakāro hi prītyā’vibhāgamāha | tadayuktam
nimittaparyanteṣu adhikāreṣvasaṃbhavāt | anayā śaṅkayā brūte kimidamiti | tatrottaraṃ
---puruṣasya kratusambandhāvibhāgāditi | anena cedamuktaṃ yasmāt kratuṣu puruṣo’nuṣṭhātā’sti
(1) G. tasmiṃścāpivivicya

[p. r959]

tasmāt sādhyabhūto vidhiḥ śaknotyākṣeptuṃ yāgasyaidamarthyamiti | etacca ṣaṣṭhe nipuṇataramupapāda-
yiṣyāmaḥ | nanvevaṃ puruṣārthatvamātramuktaṃ bhavati tatrāha---pariśeṣādviniyoge sati iti
kratvarthatoktā bhavati | tasmādubhayamapyatra likṣatam |

evaṃ vā | nanu guṇaphalacodanā etā ityuktam | ko’yaṃ punarvicāraḥ ? dvitīye
tṛtīye ca guṇasya godehanādeḥ phalāya codanā etā ityuktam | tataśca phalaṃ prati śeṣatā
kevalopādānagamyeti puruṣārthatve sthite punarvicāro’narthakaḥ | ucyate | pradarśitamasmābhiḥ
viniyogatastaduktamiti | viniyogaparyālocanayā kratvarthatvaṃ na saṃbhavatīti hetoḥ
tatpuruṣārthatvamuktam | iha punaḥ upādānato dṛṣṭaprayojanasiddhisaṃbhave nādṛṣṭārthatvaṃ saṃbhavatīti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | godohanādeḥ praṇayanādinirvṛttiḥ dṛṣṭaṃ prayojanam paśvādisiddhiradṛṣṭam | tatra
kiṃ niyogaḥ dṛṣṭena dvāreṇopādatte uta adṛṣṭena dvāreṇopādatta iti cintāyāṃ dṛṣṭasya śīghra-
bhāvitvāt tenaiva dvāreṇopādatte | tataścopādānārthatvādviniyogasya viniyogo’pi tathaiva |
paśukāmapadaṃ ca adhikṛtamātropalakṣaṇam | vākyena ca godohanena praṇayet iti praṇayane
godohanasya viniyogāt kratvarthatvamiti | na, yathāviniyogaṃ kāryāvagamāt | viniyuktasyaiva
hi kāryakalpanā nāviniyuktasyeti rāddhāntaḥ | asyārthaḥ---yathāviniyogaṃ viniyo-

[p. r960]

gānusāreṇa; kāryāvagateḥ dvārakāryāvagamāt rāddhānta iti saṃbandhaḥ | kasmādevamityāha
---viniyuktasyaiva hi kāryakalpanā nāviniyuktasyeti kratvarthasyāpi kratuviniyogasiddhiḥ |
kāryabhūtā kalpanīyā | tatkalpanāyāṃ ca viniyogaśśaraṇam | vaniyogabalena ca dvārakārya-
parigrahaḥ | na cātra kratvarthatayāpi viniyogo’sti | tasmāt dṛṣṭamapyetatkāryaṃ na1 viniyomā-
pekṣamiti | ato viniyogato’pi viniyogaparyālocanayā niyogataśca upādānaparyā-
locanayā puruṣārthataiva guṇaphalacodanāsviti sthitam | bādarigrahaṇaṃ punaḥ upakāra-
pradarśanārthaṃ na punaḥ parārthatvāditi śeṣahetunivṛttyartham | nanu ca bhāṣyakāraḥ upakārataḥ
śeṣatāmāha, na punarupādānataḥ yato bādarimatamupanyasyati sma | taccānupapannam |
nirākṛtatvādasyeti | tatrāha---bādarigrahaṇaṃ upakāro dṛṣṭo dravyādīnāmastīti pradarśa-
yitum | tataścopādānataḥ karaṇaśeṣateti | na punaḥ parārthatvāditi śeṣatve yo hetuḥ
tannivṛttyartham | kasmāt ? viniyoge hi yasmāt saḥ parārthatvāditi heturuktaḥ |
nanu bādariṇāpi viniyoga evāsābupanyastaḥ upakāro hetuḥ | na punastanmukhenopādānaṃ
ityevamarthaṃ, tena bādarimatopanyāsāt upakārata eva śeṣatokteti pratibhāti | ata evāsau
nirākṛta iti parihāraḥ | yata evopakārataśśeṣatā bādariṇoktā ata eva bādarirnirā-
kṛtaḥ | tena punarāśaṅkā nopapadyate | kimiti tarhyupanyāsaḥ ? atrāha---iha punaḥ kāryata eva
upakārata evopādānamiti na punarupanyasto bādariḥ upakāro’tra dṛṣṭo’sti | tathā ca

[p. r961]

tadbalena bādariḥ śeṣatāṃ yāvadāheti darśayitum | kathaṃ tarhi siddhāntaḥ---ayathāviniyoga-
tayā tūktena nyāyena nirasyata iti virodhaḥ |

evaṃ vā | iha dravyārjananiyamā udāharaṇamiti | pūrvapakṣahetutayedamupanyastam |
nātra viṣayaphalālocanayā dhanakāmo’dhikārī yuktaḥ | tathā sati vidhyānarthavayāt niyamo’pi
saṃbhavati | kāmyatvena rāgataḥ pravṛttau satyāṃ anyatrāpi pravṛtteḥ śāstrīyānuṣṭhānaparyanuyogā-
bhāvāt1 | kratvarthatve punaḥ tatsādhyatvena kratuniyogasya jñāpanāt tatraiva pravṛtteḥ phalato niyamaḥ
sidhyatīti | tatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | niyamaniyogānāṃ svātantraye satyadhikāraśūnyatvāt
sākṣādaśrutatvāt viṣayaphalālocanā lakṣyate | tasya ca vidhyānarthakyenānucitatvāt; kāma-
śrutiniyogena ca tadaṅgadravyotpādakatve2 sati adhikārasiddherviśvajinnyāyavirodhāt |
evaṃ ca pratigrahādīnāmeva kratvarthatvena dravyasyāpi tadārjitasya kratvarthatvāt tadvayāpattimān3
kratvanadhikṛta eveti ekādhikārāvagamāt | kṣāmavatyādīnāmapi spaṣṭaiva karbhārthatopapannā
bhavatīti | anyathā kratvasaṃbandhadravyavyāpattimato’dhikāre sati svātantryameva syāt |
upasaṃharati---tasmādanadhikāriṇo vidherānarthakyam | arthaśūnyatā | satyapi niyogabodhake
liṅādiśabde adhikāriṇaṃ vinā anvitābhidhānāsiddheḥ | arthaśūnyataiveti kratvarthataiva |
nanu ca kratvarthatve yatheṣṭaviniyogalakṣaṇaṃ svatvameva na bhavatīti svadravyatyāgātmako
yāga eva na saṃvarteteti pradhānavirodhānnāṅgatvaṃ yuktaṃ---pralapitamidaṃ kenāpi | kasmādayaṃ

[p. r962]

pralāpa ityatrāha---ārjanaṃ svatvaṃ nāpādayatīti vipratiṣiddham | atrāyaṃ bhāvaḥ---na yatheṣṭa-
viniyoganibandhanaṃ svatvaṃ balātkārahṛteṣvatiprasaṅgāt | bālādidhaneṣvaprasaṅgācca | kiṃ tarhi
svatvalakṣaṇaṃ ? ucyate---uktaṃ hi bhagavatā bhāṣyakāreṇa “yo yadyathābhiprāyaṃ viniyoktu-
marhati tattasya sva”
miti | tena yatheṣṭaviniyogārhatayā svatvaṃ, tadarhatā ca tatra tasya yatra
pratigrahādayo loke prasiddhā upāyāḥ pravṛttāḥ | tataśca pratigrahādinibandhanaṃ svatvaṃ teṣvapi
satsu nāstīti vipratiṣiddham | ārjanaṃ ca nānārūpaṃ kvacijjanma, yathā paitṛkeṣu | kvaci-
tpariṇayaḥ yathā daṃpatyordhanamityevamādiṣūhanīyam | smṛtikārāśca lokaprasiddhopāyameva kurvate
vyāmohanivṛttaye vyākaraṇādivat | kathaṃ tarhi kratvarthatā ? yadi yajamānasya svaṃ tadā
na kratoriti bhrantiḥ vanīvananavat tadarthe svatvamityabhiprāyaḥ | vaniḥ bhṛtiḥ vanana
yācanaṃ yathā vanīvanane svatvamapi kratvarthaṃ na prītyarthaṃ tathetyarthaḥ | anyadaidamarthyaṃ anyatsvatvami-
tyabhiprāyaḥ | atrocyate---neme adhikāraśūnyā niyamavidhayaśśakyante vaktum | kutaḥ ?
jīvanāya, pravṛttāḥ jīvyate yena dravyeṇa tasmai pravṛttā adhikāriṇo bhaviṣyanti |
anena dhanakāmanā nādhikāriviśeṣaṇam | kintu dhanārjanapravṛttiḥ yadā yadā dhanārjanāya
pravṛttā bhavanti tadā tadā pratigrahādividhinā niyuktatvādupāyāntareṇa pravartamānasya vihitāti-
kramo bhavatīti siddhaḥ phalato niyamaḥ | na ca dhanāya pravṛttasyādhikāraḥ kāmyādhikāraḥ, yena
rāganibandhanā tatra pravṛttiriti tadatikrame vihitatikramo na syāt | jīvanaśabdena ca dhanamabhida-
dhato’yamabhiprāyaḥ saṃbhavati tatra jijīviṣoḥ pravṛttyavyabhicāraḥ vyabhicarati tu kratusaṃbandha-
jīvanamiti na tanmukhena kratupraveśa iti | nanu jīvanapravṛtto’dhikārī na śrūyate tatrāha

[p. r963]

---na ca śabdādevādhikāraḥ adhikārādevādhikāraḥ| asyārthaṃ:---abhidadhato’yamabhiprāyaḥ
saṃbhavati asya śabdo’stīti | naivamadhikāraḥ | kintu adhikārarūpo’rtho yasyāvagamyate
tasyādhikāraḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati | na śabdo’dhikārisvarūpopādhitāṃ yātīti |
3sa tvadhikārarūpo’rthaḥ kvacit śabdāvagamyaḥ kvacit pramāṇāntarāvagamyaḥ iti na kaści-
dviśeṣaḥ | gamyate cātra pratigrahādisvabhāvālocanayā jīvanāya pravṛttasyādhikāraḥ | na hi
kaścit prekṣāvān dhanāya pravṛtto’pi na pratigrahādīn karotīti | ataḥ svatvasyāviśeṣāt
aidamarthyenāviśeṣitatvāt sarvapuruṣārthanirvṛttau jīvanakratusiddhirūpāyāṃ na viśeṣaśśavayate’va-
dhārayitumiti sarvaireva kartavya iti | ‘api ca’
 ityādibhāṣyamanubhāṣya “ko’syārtha”

ityākṣipati---kimidaṃ tantraṃ ? jīvanamiti cet aniyamāttarhi jīviṣyati | niyamasya
kratvaṅgatvāt na puruṣārtho niyamo yataḥ | yathā kratāveva avaghātaniyamāt puruṣārthaṃ
dalanādyapi bhavati | atha kratvantarāṇi tantraśabdābhidheyānyabhipretāni teṣāṃ parilopaḥ
syāt tathāpi pratikratu kratuṃ prati niyamāt aniyamena dhanamārjayitvā’nuṣṭhāsyatīti
na parilopaḥ syāt | atrocyate---kratvantarāṇyeva tantraśabdābhidheyānyabhipretāni |
(1) P. 1196 (2) P. 1197 (3) G. dhātvadhi

[p. r964]

na cārjanasya viśeṣopādānatve pramāṇamasti | asyārthaḥ---yadā dravyamukhenārjanaṃ kratvarthaṃ
tadā dravyasādhyatvāt kratṛnāṃ sarveṣāmārjanamidamiti syāt | na punarviśeṣopādānatve pramāṇa-
masti | ‘asmā idamārjanamupāttaṃ asmai na’
 iti viśeṣābhāvāt | bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi
kimityāha---na ca sarvatantraṃ sarveṇa dravyeṇa śakyate kartum | tyaktasya punastyāgāsaṃbhavāt |
tatra yadi kenacid‌dravyeṇa kaścideva kratuḥ kriyeta tadā kratvantaraṃ prati tādarthyaṃ bādhitaṃ
bhavediti tantraparilopaḥ sarvakarmaṇāṃ dravyārjanenāṅgena upakrāntatvāt | ayamaparo doṣaḥ |
nānyasya tantre pratate’nyattantraṃ pratāyate’ iti darśanaṃ nopapadyate iti | pratyakṣeṇa kratvantara-
tantramadhye kratbantaradarśanāt ‘atha mataṃ’
 ityādinā | 1evaṃ ca satītyādibhāṣyaṃ vyākhyātu-
mupakramate---atha naivedaṃ kasyacidekasyāpi kratoraṅga kintu sarveṣāṃ militānāmaṅgaṃ yathā
prāyaṇīyodayanīyayoraikādaśināḥ tatra vibhajya saṃbandhāt tantraparilopo na bhaviṣyatīti
bhāvaḥ | eva tarhi naivedaṃ prayogavacanena gṛhyate sarvakratugāminaḥ prayogavacanasyābhāvāt
dvādaśāhaprayogavat | anupāttaprayogavacanasyāṅgataiva nāsti iti | yasyādhyayanena
prayogavacano nopāttaḥ na svīkṛtaḥ yasya prayogavacano nāmnātaḥ tasyāṅgataiva nāsti | tataścāyaṃ
bhāṣyārtho bhavati | ekasyāpi prayogakālāt bahiretatsarvāṅgamabhipretaṃ sat anaṅgameva syāt |
nanu ca bhāṣye anupakārakamityāha nānaṅgamiti tatrāha---asatyaṅgatve yasya kilāṅgatā
nāsti tasyāprayogeṇānupakārakatvamiti so’yamaṅgatābhāva evānupakārakatvena2 darśitaḥ |
(1) P. 1196-10 (2) G. katve

[p. r965]

na cādhānavadbhavitumarhati; na ca dravyārthamevedamārjanaṃ ādhānavadagnyarthameva1 bhavitumarhati |
asyārthaḥ---yathā ādhānamagnyarthaṃ na puruṣārthaṃ tathā’’rjananiyamo’pi dhanārtha eva bhavitumarhati |
dravyamukhena cādhikārāntaramanupravekṣyati | na ca tantraparilopo bhaviṣyati | agnivadvirodhā-
bhāvāt | etadapi nāsti jīvanāya pravṛttasyādhikārāvagamāt | yasya hi nādhi-
kāro’sti sa parādhikāramanupraviśati | dravyārthatvena tu svata evādhikāro’vagamyate |
ādhāne tu nārthādapyadhikāro nāsti, adhikārāntarānapekṣāṇāmagnīnāmapuruṣārthatvāt | jīvanārthatā
tu svata eva dravyasyāsti, tatrādhikāro’sti | ataḥ puruṣārthamevedaṃ bhavati nādhānavat |
tasmāt kratvarthatve2 dravyārjanasya tantraparilopaḥ sthito doṣaḥ | kṣāmavatyādīnāmāhitā-
gneradhikāraśravaṇāt | tasya ca kratusaṃbandhārhatvāt | bhavatu kratusaṃbandhitvaṃ kratvarthatāpi phala
kalpayitum | etacca dvayamabhyupagamyavādenoktam | puruṣārtha evāyaṃ naimittiko’stu | na
kācitkṣatiḥ | tasmāt puruṣārthā dravyārjananiyamā iti sthitam | vicāraprayojanaṃ cāha
---ataḥ niyamātikrama iti | atikramaḥ puruṣasya na kratoḥ | ato niyamāti-
krameṇārjitenāpi kratusiddhiḥ | na cātra bācyam | tarhi cauryārjitenāpi kratusiddhiriti |
yato na cauryaṃ svatvāpādakaṃ loke prasiddham |

(1) G. anyārthameva (2) G. kratvarthadravyā

[p. r966]

tadutsarge karmaṇi | yasmin pratiḥ purupasya tasya lipsā arthalakṣaṇetyuktam | iha
tu prateradhikāriviśeṣaṇasya sākṣādaśravaṇāt; ‘nodyantamādityamīkṣeta’
 ityetāvanmātraśravaṇāt
arthataścānavagamādadhikāriviśeṣaṇasya yathā ‘na kalañjaṃ bhakṣayet’
 iti bhakṣaṇapravṛtteḥ
ato’dhikāriśūnyatāvidheḥ na cānadhikāraṃ puruṣārthaṃ bhavati iti kratvarthasyekṣaṇasyāyaṃ
pratibedhaḥ | nanvayaṃ yadi īkṣaṇapratiṣedhaḥ tadā īkṣaṇapravṛtto’dhikārī ganyata eva | na ca
kratāveva paramīkṣaṇapravṛttiḥ yadṛcchayāpi saṃbhavāt | atrocyate---satyaṃ kratubāhyamapīkṣaṇamasti
tathāpi prayuktilāghavāt kratugatekṣaṇapratiṣedha evāyaṃ vijñāyate | nanvevaṃ dravyārjananiyamo’pi
kimiti na kratvarthaḥ | bhavatu tadapyatrodāharaṇam | yatra pratiṣedhyaṃ kratau nāsti tatpratiṣedhaḥ
kratvarthatayā nāśaṅkyate yathā kalañjabhakṣaṇasya | niyamo vā astu vrataprakramāt niyamo-
vā’yaṃ tathāpi kratvarthaḥ | nanu niyamapakṣe kena saṃbandhena kratāvantargatiḥ pratiṣedho hi
pratiṣedhyamukhena kratāvantarbhavet | ucyate---anīkṣaṇaniyamasaṃkalpo’pi yadi svatantra eva
syāt tadā prayuktikalpanāgauravamiti kratāvevānupraveśaḥ | nanu caivaṃ sati viśvajidādayopyatro-
dāharaṇaṃ bhavantu | bhavatu na kaściddoṣaḥ | atrābhidhīyate---prītyutsargepyetaduktaṃ
bhavati adhikārāśravaṇe’ṣyadhikāro yuktaḥ kalpayitum | na punaḥ kratusaṃbandhakalpanā |
kaḥ punaranayorviśeṣaḥ ? ayamasti viśeṣaḥ---yathaiva adhikāre śrūyamāṇe gamyamānatatkartṛtaiva
nyāyyā | yasyādhikāraḥ śrūyate tasyādhikārānyathānupapattyā tatkartṛtā kalpyate | kāryatayā
hi niyogaṃ yaḥ pratipadyate so’dhikārī | viṣayasādhyaśca niyogaḥ | sa yadi viṣayaṃ na

[p. r967]

saṃpādayet tasya niyogaḥ kārya eṃva na syāt iti viṣayasaṃpādakatayā kartṛtvāvagamāt |
tacca kartṛtvaṃ sākṣādvā puruṣāntaraprayuktyā vā yadyapyubhayathā upapadyate tathāpi sākṣāt saṃbhave
paramparānupapatteḥ tatkartṛtaiva nyāyyā | na punaḥ puruṣāntarakartṛtākalpanā, vinā vacanā-
dārtvijeṣveva | evaṃ kriyākṣepeṇa gamyamānakartravagamāt adhikārakalpanaiva nyāyyā |
adhikāraṃ vinā kartṛtvānupapatteḥ | na cānyatrādhikāreṇānyatrāpi tadaṅgatayā’dhikāranupraveśena
kartṛtvasiddhiḥ | atraivādhikāre sākṣātsaṃbhavati paramparānupapatteriti | evaṃ ca nyāyataḥ
sthite’dhikāriṇi prayuktikalpanāpi na doṣaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi kartradhikaraṇamiti ‘nānṛtaṃ
vadet’
 ityatrāpi tarhi gamyamānakartṛtvasiddhayarthaṃ sākṣādadhikārakalpanāt kimarthaṃ karturvācyatva-
nirākaraṇaṃ ? nirākṛtepi vācyatve, gamyamānepi kartari tasya kartuḥ svatantrādhikārāpatteḥ |
uttaraṃ---tatra hi viniyogo’dhikāra1kalpanāyāḥ pratipakṣaḥ | iha tu apratipakṣā adhikāra-
kalpanā iti | anṛtavadanapratiṣedhe hi sādhikāraniyogasannidhānāt tena gṛhītatvāt
prakaraṇena ca viniyuktatvāt nādhikārakalpanāvakāśo’sti | iha tu anārabhyāmnānāt
nādhikāragrahaṇamasti iti nādhikārāntarasaṃbandho’vakalpate | tenādhikārakalpanāyāṃ 2prasṛtāyāṃ
svatantrādhikārakalpanaiva netyanavadyam | evaṃ tāvat samāhitamidamadhikaraṇam | idānīṃ suhṛdbhāve-
nādhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ nirūpayati---kiṃbhūtaḥ punaratrādhikriyate “etāvatā hainasā viyukto
bhavati”
 iti śravaṇāt jīvata eva vā’dhikāraḥ | asminnudāharaṇe etāvatā hainasā pāpena
viyukto bhavatītyarthavādaḥ | tatra yadi jīvanavānadhikārī bhavati tadā nityatvāt atrākaraṇe
vihitātikramadoṣa evainoyogo bhavatīti sālambano’rthavādaḥ | viśvajidādivattu svarge’dhikāri-
(1) G. rikalpa (2) G. prakṛtāyāṃ

[p. r968]

viśeṣaṇe kalpyamāne kāmyasyākaraṇe’pi na doṣaḥ | tatrārthavādasyātiparokṣā vṛttiḥ syāt
ato nityo’yaṃ jīvanānupālanīyo niyamaḥ svadharmatayā |

nanu ca prasajyapratiṣedho nyāyyaḥ asyārthaḥ---nanu prasajyapratiṣedho’yam | tatra
niṣidhyamānakriyākataiṃvādhikārī | kimiti jīvanavānucyate | satyaṃ svavākyamātrālocanayā
evaṃ, kintu tasya vratamityanvayāvagateḥ lakṣaṇaiva nyāyvā | anīkṣaṇasaṅkalpo vratarūpo lakṣyate
tena 1nāyaṃ prasajyapratiṣedhaḥ | nanvevaṃ ādhānavat kimita saṅkalpo’pi dravyārthoṃ na syāt |
tatrāha---ādhānavadapyatra dravyārthatā naiva saṃbhavati sākṣācchrutyabhāvāt | aviniyukta-
dravyasaṃyogāt dṛṣṭasya ca saṃskārasyābhāvāt naiva dravyārthatā | yadi punaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ
saṃskāraḥ syāt | kiṃ dṛṣṭārthatā śakyate vaktum | asyārthaḥ---sarvaṃ śabdāvagamyaṃ
codanāsviti sthitam | yadyapi saṃskāro dṛṣṭaḥ tathāpi tadarthatā na yukteti | bāḍhaṃ śakyate
darpaṇapramārjanavat loka evopayogadarśanāt | atrābhiprāyaḥ---kalpanīye prayojane pratyakṣamapi
prayojanaṃ nirākāṅkṣīkaraṇayogyaṃ na tyaktuṃ śakyata iti | tasmāt puruṣārtho niyamassaṃkalpa-
rūpo’yamiti yuktam | dṛṣṭārthāsaṃbhavena ca karkarīkarṇādayo’pi2 puruṣārthā ityuktam | 
gokarṇalakṣaṇānyapi 3kakarīkarṇyāḥ kartavyāḥ ityevamādīni kratusaṃbandhini dravye dṛṣṭasaṃskārā-
bhāvena purupārthānyeva gomāneva cādhikārīti sthitam |

(1) G. nāprasajya (2) G. kartarikaraṇādayo’pi (3) G. kartarīkarṇyaḥ

[p. r969]

dravyāṇi tu | nanu tṛtīye uddhananādiṣveva sphayādīnāṃ viniyoga uktaḥ |
tatkathamihānyatrāpyāśaṅkyate tatrāha---uktamityādi | etaduktaṃ bhavati---sphayaścetyādibhiḥ
sahoddhananādivākyānāṃ viniyogavicāraḥ kṛtaḥ | tatroddhananādivākyāni viniyojakanīti
sthitam | idamidānīṃ cintyate---etāni vai daśa yajñāyudhānīti kimidaṃ śāstrāntaraṃ sphaye-
noddhantītyevamādibhyaḥ uta teṣāmevānuvādaḥ iti cintāntaraṃ darśayati | nanu dvitīyādhyāya-
cinteyaṃ śāstrabhedābhedaviṣayā | kimiti caturthe cintyate | ucyate | pṛthak‌prayuktyarthaṃ
dvitīyārthavamarśaḥ kriyate | yadi śāstrāntaramidaṃ syāt tadā asya sākṣādyajñasādhanatvena
pṛthagidaṃ prayoktavyaṃ syāt abhedapakṣe tu śāstrābhedāt bhedena prayuktirnāsti | kīdṛśo
dvitīyārthāvamarśaḥ ityatrāha---kiṃ bhedalakṣaṇayuktamidaṃ vākyaṃ guṇāntaravidhānapakṣe sādhyabhedāt,
guṇavatkarmāntaravidhānetu anubandhabhedāt, utānuvādakatayā sādhyānubdhabhedābhāvāt abheda-
lakṣaṇayuktamiti | pūrvapakṣavādī sphayenoddhantītyevamādibhirvākyaiḥ sākṣādyajñasaṃbandhānava-
gamāt yajñāyudhānīti vidhyantaraṃ manyate | ayamabhiprāyaḥ---yajñāyudhānīti ṣaṣṭhīsamā-
so’yam | ṣaṣṭhī ca yadyapi saṃbandhamātre bhavati tathāpi sākṣātsaṃbandhe saṃbhavati na paramparā
āśrayituṃ nyāyyeti sākṣādyajñasaṃbandha evāvagamyate | na cāsau prāptaḥ yenāsāvanuvādaḥ
syāt | ‘ato vacanāni tvapūrvatvāt’
 ityayamapi vidhiḥ | 1sa ca āgneyādiṣveva dravyaṃ
vidhatte | guṇāntarāvaruddhatvādvā teṣāṃ guṇāntarayuktaṃ karmāntaramiti | nanu vājapeyasya
yūpa itivat praṇāḍyāpi saṃbandha upapadyate | yuktaṃ yūpābhāvātkratostatra | na ca
(1) G. na ca

[p. r970]

vidhāsyata iti vācyam | saptad‌śāratnitāvidhānaparatvāddvākyasya | anuvāde yathā prāptiḥ
praṇāḍyāpyāśrīyate | iha punaḥ vidhānepi saṃbhavati kiṃ nopapadyata ityabhiprāyaḥ |
atrābhidhīyate---yajñāyudhānīti tāvallakṣaṇā | lakṣaṇā cetsākṣāt vyavadhāne ca na
kaścidviśeṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ---yajñāyudhaśabdena tāvat na sākṣāta tatsādhanamucyate, kintu
tatsaṃbandhī lakṣyate | saṃbandhilakṣaṇā cet na sākṣādvayavadhāne ca kaścidviśeṣaḥ | saṃbandhitvā-
viśeṣāt | tena yajñāyudhaśabdena sākṣātsaṃbandho bodhyate iti aprāptārthatā na śakyate
vaktum | nanvevaṃ prāptārthatāpi naikāntataḥ śakyā vaktum | satyam---kintu vidhiśūnya-
midaṃ vākyaṃ kalpyavidhikam | kalpanā cānupapattau satyāṃ, nacānupapattiḥ uddhananadvāraka1vyavahita-
saṃbandhāśrayaṇenopapatteḥ | tasmādanuvādo’yaṃ na vidhiḥ | atraiva yuktyantaramāha---pramāṇānta-
rānusāriṇī ca lakṣaṇā | na ca vidyamāne pramāṇe pramāṇāntarakalpanā śakyā | sphaye-
noddhantītyubhayasiddhatvāt | loke ca lakṣaṇā prasiddhā gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityādau | tatra
yadgaṅgākūlaṃ lakṣayati, tat1 pramāṇāntareṇa kūle ghoṣadarśanāt | tadevamihāpi pramāṇāntara-
prasiddhatvāt uddhananādidvārikaiva saṃbandhitā āśrayituṃ yuktā | tasmādanuvādo’yam |
evaṃ ca vikalpaḥ parihṛto bhavati | yadyāgneyādiṣu dravyavidhiḥ tadā puroḍāśādibhiḥ saha
yo vikalpaḥ sa parihṛto bhavati | liṅgaṃ copapannaṃ bhavati | yadi karmāntaravidhānaṃ tadā
devatābhāvena avyaktatvāt saumikavidhyantaprāptyā āhṛtisaṅkhyādarśanaṃ nopapadyate | tathā
somasthānāpattyā abhiṣutānāṃ sphayādīnāṃ paridhānīye karmaṇi na syāddarśanam |

(1) G. dvārakasya

[p. r971]

tatra caikatvamayajñāṅgam | nanu caikatvādi vibhaktyāpi kāryānvayitvenābhihitam |
tataśca kāryāṅgatvāt tadanupādāne kāryameva na saṃvarteta ityatrāha---ekatvaṃ viniyogābhāvānna
yajñaṅgamiti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ | asyārthaḥ---yadyapyekatvaṃ grāhakagṛhītaṃ tathāpi
viniyogābhāvāt dvārasabandhābhāvāt tadabhāve ca sāmānyasaṃbandhānirvāhāt na yajñāṅgamiti |
kathaṃ punaḥ ekatvaṃ na viniyujyate ? vibhaktayo hi prātipadikārthasya svārthasaṃbandhama-
bhidadhati | svārthasya karaṇatvādeḥ prātipadikārthasaṃbandhitvāt | na punaḥ svārthabhūtai-
katvādisaṃbandhitā vibhaktayo’bhidadhati | tasmādekatvaṃ karaṇatvaṃ ca prātipadikārthasyaiva |
naikatvasya karaṇabhāvaḥ karaṇatve cāsati kriyāsaṃbandhābhāvādayajñāṅgamiti | karaṇasaṃbandhe
hi karaṇatayā kriyāsaṃbandhe prātipadikārthasaṃbandho na syāt | yathā aruṇayeti kriyā-
saṃbandhe dravyaguṇasaṃbandho na bhavati | tasmāt pratīyamānamapyetat na kriyayā saṃbadhyate
yathā pūrvo dhāvatītyaparapratyayaḥ | pratīyamānamapītyapiśabdena evamāha---kriyāsaṃbandhābhāvena
1kāryānvayitayā anabhidheyameva | yadyapyabhidhīyate tathāpyanaṅgamiti | liṅgadarśanāni
caivamupapannāni bhavantīti | evaṃ prāpte ucyate---na viniyogādeva kriyāṅgatā kiṃ tarhi
(1) G. iti yat (2) R. bhāvenākārya

[p. r972]

upādānādapi śeṣatā bhavati | yathaiva karaṇībhūtasyaivopādānaṃ evamekatvayuktasyāpi |
yathaiva hi akārakabhūtasyānupādānaṃ evamasaṅkhyākasyāpi | na hyakārakasyopādānaṃ asaṅkhyā-
yuktasyāpi saṃbhavatītyabhiprāyaḥ | paśustāvat viniyukta upādātavyaśca | sa ca
saṅkhyayā vinā na śakyate upādātum; śrutaṃ ca tatsthamekatvam | tadapi ca kāryānvayitvena
śrutam | tadanupraveśāyāvaśyamāśrayamapekṣate | ataḥ 2tadvadevāvacchedakatayā svīkriyate |
svīkṛtaṃ ca viniyuktasthaṃ tātsthyenāṅgatāṃ yāti | saṅkhyā hi svasāmarthyenaiva svasaṅkhyeyaśeṣa-
bhūtā | sā hi prāk prātipadikārthamātre sthitā samprati upādānadaśāyāṃ viniyuktasthā
jātā | śeṣabhāvastu tasyāḥ vastusvabhāvalakṣaṇālliṅgāt ityanavadyam | tathā ca laukikāḥ
gāmānayetyādiṣu tathaiva pratipadyante | nanu bhāṣyakāraḥ vākyabalena kriyāśeṣatāmāha
nopādānata iti tatrāha---tadidamuktaṃ yadasmābhiruktaṃ tadetat bhāṣyakāreṇāpyuktaṃ “na śruti-
rbrūte vākyārtho nāstīti”
 anena granthena | kathamuktamityatrāha---na viniyogo brūte upādānaṃ
nāstītyarthaḥ | nanu cānya evārthaḥ pratīyate na śrūtyā vākyasya virodho’stīti | yataḥ
3śrutyarthāparityāgena vākyāt kratvarthatāvagamyate | virodhe na daurbalyamuktam |
(1) P. 1205 (2) G. tadaivāva (3) G. śrutyaparityāgena

[p. r973]

atrottaraṃ---uktamasmābhirviniyogo na saṃbhavatīti | vākyenāpi viniyogo nāsti kriyā-
saṃbandhāvagamābhāvāditi | tasmādyathāśrutaṃ na vyākhyeyamidaṃ bhāṣyam iti | kathaṃ tarhi vākya-
śabdaḥ ? samabhivyāhāraparyāyo’yaṃ na viniyojakaparyāyaḥ | ekasmin vākye kriyā
caikatvaṃ ca samabhivyāhṛtam | tadbalena copādānaśeṣateti| tasmādeva kriyāṅgatocyata
ityarthaḥ | tasmāt siddhamupādānaśeṣatvaṃ saṅkhyāyā liṅgasya ca | nanu ‘vasantā
prātarāgneyīṃ kṛṣṇagrīvīmālabheta’
 ityatra liṅgaṃ prakṛtibhāgābhihitaṃ vibhaktyā viniyujyate prātipadi-
kābhidheyamiveti kathamupādānaśeṣatā ? ucyate---prātipadikena yo’rthaḥ pradhānabhūtaḥ pratipādyate
tatraiva strīpratyayābhidheyamapi liṅgaṃ vibhaktyarthaśca kārakatvaṃ saṅkhyā cāvagamyate | na punaḥ
strītvayukte pradhānāśrayamātrāpekṣatvādguṇanām | prātipadikabhāgena prathamoccaritena liṅga-
śūnyasya prātipadikārthamātrasya avagatatvāt tanmātreṇa vibhaktyarthaḥ saṃbadhyata iti tulyaṃ
saṅkhyayā | evaṃ darśanānyupapannāni | pūrvapakṣadarśanaṃ cānuvādaḥ |

evaṃ vā | liṅgādhikaraṇam punarevaṃ veti vadataḥ ko’bhiprāyaḥ ? asyārthaḥ
---pūrvādhikaraṇe hi liṅgamapyupādānaśeṣamityuktam | kimaparamiha punaścintyata iti |
aparicchedakatvaṃ liṅgasya pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | paricchedikā hi saṅkhyā dravyasya parimāṇa-
mācaṣṭe | asyārthaḥ---saṅkhyāntareṣūpādīyamāneṣu kārakaparimāṇānyatvenānuṣṭhāne(nai)’nekatvā-

[p. r974]

vagamāt yuktā saṅkhyāviśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ 2tasyā upādānaśeṣatā | liṅge tu dravyāntaropādānā-
bhāvenānuṣṭhānaviśeṣānāpādakatvāt viśeṣāpekṣā nopapadyata | ato nopādānaśeṣateti | śravaṇā-
viśeṣālliṅgamapyupādīyata iti rāddhāntaḥ | yathā saṅkhyā śrutā tathā liṅgamapi | nanu
cāparimāṇāpādakatvāt liṅgaṃ nopādātavyamityuktamiti pūrvoktasmāraṇam| parihartuṃ
nātra parimāṇamupādāne upayujyate viśeṣaniṣṭhatvādupādānasya yathā’nuṣṭhānaviśeṣākāṅkṣā
tathā kārakaviśeṣākāṅkṣāpi | tena kārakaviśeṣāpādakaṃ liṅgamapyupādeyam | atassaṅkhyā-
vadupādānamiti siddham | śeṣaṃ pūrvavat |

āśrayiṣvaviśeṣeṇa | ihāśrayiṇaḥ padārthā udāharaṇam | sandehaṃ kartuṃ udāharaṇa-
mupanyastam | nanu bhāṣyamayuktaṃ “kiṃ yajimātraṃ saṃskāraḥ devatāyāḥ, uta yajinā’dṛṣṭaṃ
devatāyāṃ kriyata”
 iti yasmāt saṃskāramātramadṛṣṭamakurvat anarthakam | avaghātādiṣvapi

dṛṣṭamastītyatrāha---dvividhamākhyātamuktam | paraprāptaniyogānuvādakaṃ avahantītyāgneyā-
dicodanāvagataniyogānuvādakaṃ svaniyogasaṃbandhitayā cādhikārasādhanaṃ samidho yajatī-
tyādibhinnotpattiniyogābhyupagamāt | ihedānīṃ sandihyate---kiṃ paraprāptaniyogānuvādātmakami-
damākhyātaṃ uta svaniyogasaṃbandhitayā’dhi1kārasādhanamiti | etaduktaṃ bhavati---saṃskāra-
mātrapakṣe niyogāntaraṃ nāstīti devatāyāmadṛṣṭaṃ kriyata iti | atrādṛṣṭamapūrvamabhimatam |
tenāyaṃ bhāṣyārthaḥ---devatāyāṃ saṃskriyamāṇāyāṃ yajinā paramapūrva kriyata iti | tenāpūrvāntara-
sadasadbhāvasaṃśayo’yamiti yāvat | kutaḥ saṃśayaḥ aṃśena homaparyantatayā dravyaprakṣepāṃśena
dravyavirecanasya, tathā’ṅgabhūtamantrāṃśena devatāsmaraṇasya ca dṛṣṭasya saṃbhavāt aṃśena yāgāṃśenā-
dṛṣṭasya ca saṃbhavāt tenāṃśena dṛṣṭāntarasyāsaṃbhavāt pūrvapakṣavādī manyate dṛṣṭe dravyadevatā-
gatopakāre saṃti adṛṣṭakalpanā apūrvāntarakalpanā na nyāyyā | 2taddvāreṇādhi-
kārāṅgabhāvopātteḥ | nanu yajyartho’tra na niṣpadyate tena aṃśena dṛṣṭopakārābhāvāt
adhikārānanupraveśāt tatprayuktyabhāvāt yajyartho na niṣpadyeta anuṣṭhānād dṛṣṭopapattyartho
bhaviṣyatītyabhiprāyaḥ | yathā’vaghātādiṣu mantrādikam | atrābhidhīyate---dṛṣṭo-
papattyarthataiva na ghaṭate kasmāt ? niyogasiddheravivakṣitatvāt | utpattiniyogasiddhera-
(1) G. vikāra (2) G. vyākhyātadvāreṇa

[p. r976]

vivakṣitatvāt | tathāpi kimityāha---niyogāddhi1niyogārthe pravṛttiḥ | atrābhiprāyaḥ
niyoge’vivakṣite na śuddhaṃ yajimātraṃ kṣaṇikaṃ adhikāraniyogārthaṃ bhavati asati ca tadarthatve
na tatra pravṛttiḥ yasmāt niyogādyā pravṛttiḥ sā niyogārthe na punaraniyogārthe | nanu yāgāṅgasya
dravyadevatādvāreṇāṅgatā’stītyuktam | ucyate---na tāvat sākṣāt dravyadevatārthatā yāgasya |
kintu aṅgabhūtamantrāt | sā cāṅgamukhena tatra cetaretarāśrayatā; nirūḍhe niyogārthatve’ṅgasaṃbandhaḥ
aṅgasaṃbandhe na dravyadevatārthatayā niyogārthatā | yasmānna svarūpamātre aniyogake dharmasaṃbandho
ghaṭata iti | atha tadarthaṃ 2adhikārārthaṃ utpattiniyogo vivakṣyate siddhamapūrvārthatvam |
3paścādadhikāraniyogārthatve yāgasya nirūḍhe’ṅgasaṃbandhe sati tad‌dvāreṇa dravyadevatāgata-
dṛṣṭasaṃskāraniṣpattyarthamityanumanyata eva | tasmāt tṛtīyaprakāro’yaṃ niyogānuvādakaḥ
svaniyogasaṃbandhibhyāmiti paramatenopasaṃhṛtam |

svamatamāha---na ca tṛtīyaprakāro’yaṃ niyoganiṣpattestulyatvādeva svaniyoga-
saṃbandhasya vidyamānatvāt | samidādivat | nanvevaṃ sati dravyābhāvepi sviṣṭakṛto na
lopaḥ tatrāhaṃ---prayojanaṃ tu dṛṣṭamadṛṣṭaṃ vā bhavatu prayojanavaśācca anuṣṭhānamanuṣṭhānaṃ vā
bhavatu asyārthaḥ---yadyapi niyogasiddhiḥ tathāpi dṛṣṭatvāt dravyādisaṃskāra eva
prayojakaḥ | niyogārthastvaprayojaka eva | tena dravyābhāve sviṣṭakṛto lopaḥ tasmāt
siddhā svaniyogārthatā |

(1) vini (2) G. utpatti niyogārthaṃ (3) paścādyadyadhikāra

arthe samavaiṣamyamato dravyakarmaṇām | atikrāntastṛtīyaviṣayaḥ iti | ko’yaṃ
tṛtīyaviṣayaḥ ? yadi tṛtīyaviṣayo’yaṃ kimiti caturthe cintyate ityanenābhiprāyeṇāya praśnaḥ |
atrottaraṃ---pārārthyapratipattiḥ | sā’tikrāntā | ayamabhiprāyaḥ-yadyapyeṣā pārārthyapratipattiḥ
sā’tikrāntā | ayamabhiprāyaḥ---yadyapyeṣā pārārthyapratipattiḥ tathāpi śrutyādibhirna bhavati
niyogavyāpāranibandhanaiveti iha cintyate | idānīṃ punaḥ sthite pārārthye kevalamupādānameva
cintyate | prayuktiḥ upādānam | taccintyate | kena prayojanenāmikṣādīnāmupādānaṃ

pārārthyamapi tannimittakameva na niyoganimittakam | ata idamuktaṃ tṛtīyaviṣaya iti |
tṛtīyasyāyaṃ viṣayaḥ na punastārtīyaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ śakyate’vagantum | ato vidhinimittaka---
mevāsya pārārthyamiti caturthe cintitam | etacca vyākhyātaṃ “na śrutyādibhiḥ sarvasya

[p. r978]

pārārthyaṃ śakyate pratipādayituṃ”
 iti | etadevātra kathyate | tathā ca yāgaphalayoḥ

svargaṃ prati phalasya puruṣaṃ prati śeṣatvaṃ vakṣyati | yata eva śrutyādibhiryā1gaphalayoḥ pārārthyaṃ
2nāvagamyate ata eva bādarereṣā bhrāntiḥ dravyaguṇasaṃkāreṣveva śeṣatvamiti | śrutyādīnāṃ

asyārthaḥ---dadhyānayanamāmikṣāyā vājinasya copakārakam | yadi paramāmikṣāṃ prati śeṣaḥ
upakāranibandhanā ca prayuktiḥ na śeṣatvanibandhanā, ekatvādīnāṃ prāgaśeṣabhūtānāmapyupādānenaiva
1śeṣatā yataḥ, tasmādumayaṃ prayojakamiti | atrābhidhīyate---nātra prayojakatvaṃ vājinasya |
kutaḥ ? śeṣabhūtasya hi prayojanākāṅkṣā | sā ca yasya śeṣabhūtā tenaiva prayojanena nivṛtteti
na prayojanāntaraṃ prayojakatvena kalpayituṃ śakyate | asyārthaḥ---śeṣabhūtaṃ dadhyānayanaṃ
kena prayojanena prayujyatāmiti prayojakaviśeṣākāṅkṣā | tatra yasyaiva payasaḥ śeṣabhūtaṃ tadeva
prayojakaṃ yuktam | śabdataḥ tatsaṃbandhitvenāvagamāt | āmikṣā ca payaḥ tasyāmeva tadrasa-
pratyabhijñānāt | etacca vivaraṇapañcikāyāṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | tena saiva prayojikā na
vājinamiti siddham | kathaṃ punaḥ aśeṣabhūtasyopādānaśeṣatvamekatvādeḥ | aśeṣabhūtasya
prayojanākāṅkṣaiva nāstīti | kathamaśeṣabhūtasyaikatvādeḥ upādānaśeṣatvaṃ aśeṣatve upādāna-
syaivānupapatteḥ | atrocyate---śeṣasyāvagamāt śeṣabhūtaṃ hi yat paśvādi tadekatva---
śūnyaṃ nāvagatamiti na tadrahitamupādīyata iti | anyathā’navagamāt avagama---
pūrvakatvāccopādānasya | kiñci śeṣabhūtasya prayojyatvānyathānupapatteḥ śeṣabhūtasya hi
paśoḥ prayojyatvamanyathā nopapadyate | kasmāt ? na hi anavacchinnamupādātuṃ śakyata
ityuktam | na hi saṅkhyānavacchinnamupādātuṃ śakyata iti śeṣopādānārthamaśeṣasyāpyupādānaṃ
(1) G. śeṣakāryataḥ

[p. r980]

yatpunaḥ na śeṣabhūtaṃ na ca śeṣasthamavagamyate tena vināpi śeṣatvāvagamāt na śeṣopādāne-
nāpyapekṣyate tena vināpi śeṣopādānasiddheḥ na tat prayojyaṃ bhavitumarhati yathā dadhyānayanaṃ
na vājinasya śeṣabhūtaṃ tathā pradarśitaṃ bhāṣye | tathā ca liṅgaṃ pradarśitam |

padakarmāprayojakam | kaḥ punarādhastyenādhikaraṇenāsya viseṣaḥ | athaiva hi
āmikṣāśeṣabhūtaṃ dadhyānayanaṃ tayaiva prayujyate na vājinena, tathā krayaśeṣabhūtā ekahāyanī tadā-
nayanamapi tanmukhena krayaśeṣameveti krayeṇaiva prayoktavyaṃ na padapāṃsubhiḥ | ayamasti viśeṣaḥ
---tatra hi dadhyānayanamāmikṣānirvṛttisamakālameva vājinaṃ niṣpādayati | ato yuktaṃ
vājinaniṣpatteḥ na kāraṇāntarāpekṣā | asyārthaḥ---yadyapi dadhyānayanamābhikṣāśeṣabhūtaṃ
tathāpi yadi vājinamapi tathā bhedena niṣpadyate tadā kāraṇāpekṣāyāṃ prakṛtaṃ dadhyānayanaṃ tatrāpi
kāraṇatvena viniyujyeta | taccāmikṣaikaniṣpattikamiti na yuktastatra kāraṇāntarāpekṣayā prakṛtasya
dadhno viniyogaḥ | iha punaḥ saptamaṃ padamityukte bhavatyākāṅkṣā kasyeti prakṛtatvādeka-
hāyanyā iti gamyate | bhinnaniṣpattikatvādatra kāraṇāntarāpekṣāyāṃ ekahāyanīmātraṃ padā-

[p. r981]

pekṣitaṃ saṃbadhyate ityubhayārthāṃ krayapadapāṃsvarthāṃ ekahāyanīṃ manyate | evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate |
satyaṃ yadi kasyetyapekṣopapadyate | tadā tasyāmākāṅkṣāyāṃ svarūpamātreṇaivaikahāyanī saṃbadhyeta
na krayaśeṣabhāveneti bhavedubhayārthā ekahāyanī | kasyeti punarihākāṅkṣaiva nāsti yasmāt
anirjñātasaṃbandhyantare saṃbandhinyupadiśyamāne bhavatyākāṅkṣāpūrvikā prajñā | yatra
saṃbandhyantaraṃ pūrvaṃ pratītaṃ nāsti tatra ākāṅkṣapurassarā sambandhini buddhiḥ āvirbhavati |
yatra punaḥ saṃbandhyantarapūrvaka evopadeśaḥ tatra yathāvagamaṃ saṃbandhāvagamāt yathā nirjñāta-
saṃbandhipurassaramevopadeśaḥ iti samarthitamaśvapratigrahe | ataḥ krayārthaiva ekahāyanī
saptamapadasaṃbandhinīti na prayujyate krayārthāyā eva tasyāḥ prakṛtatvāt tathābhūtāyāḥ saṃbandhi-
tvenāvagatatvāt | atra bhāṣyaṃ ‘prasaṅgamupajīvatā’
 iti prasaṅgaśabdaśravaṇāt bhrāmya-
taścodyam | nanu cedaṃ prayojakāprayojakalakṣaṇam; kaḥ prasaṅgasyāvasaraḥ | vidyamāne hi
prayojakatve prasaṅga uktaḥ “evameva prasaṅgaḥ syāt vidyamāne svake vidhau”
 iti |
iha prayoktṛtaiva nāstīti | ucyate---śāstraprasaṅgamaṅgīkṛtyaitraduktam na prayogaprasaṅgamitya-
doṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ---aruṇayaikahāyanyā piṅgākṣyā somaṃ krīṇātīti śāstreṇa ekahāyanyu-
pāttā | saiva śāstrāntarepi saṃbadhyate saptamaṃ padaṃ gṛhṇātīti na punaranuṣṭhānaprasaṅgo’yaṃ bhāṣya-
kāreṇokta ityadoṣaḥ |

[p. r982]

arthābhidhānakarma ca bhaviṣyatā | ‘puroḍāśakapālena tuṣānupavapatīṃti nātra
śāstraprasaṅgaḥ saṃbhavatīti prayoktṛtvaṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate tuṣopavāpasyāpi | na ca
nāntarīyakaḥ śakya upavāpo vaktum bājinavat | tasmādubhayaṃ prayojakamiti |
asyārthaḥ---‘puroḍāśakapālena tuṣānupavapati’
 ityatra puroḍāśakapālasya sākṣādevopādānaṃ na
punaḥ prakṛtatvena grahaṇaṃ yathaikahāyanyāḥ | tena svavākye śrutaṃ puroḍāśakapālamupavāpepi
vākyena viniyujyate | nanu puroḍāśakapālamiti puroḍāśasaṃbandhikapālaṃ śrapaṇaśeṣamucyate |
na cānyaśeṣasyānyaśeṣatvaṃ saṃbhavati | anyatra vyāpṛtasyevānyatra vyāpāraḥ | tatkathamupavāpepi
vākyena viniyogaḥ | ucyate---ekavākyatā hi guṇapradhānabhāvena vinā na nirvahati |
tatra ca tuṣopavāpasyāgnyabhijvalanārthasya na kapālaṃ prati tāvat guṇatayā saṃbandhaḥ | kintu
kapālasyaiva taṃ prati | na ca viniyuktaviniyogavirodhaḥ adhiṣṭhānalakṣaṇayā viniyogopapatteḥ |
viniyuktaviniyogavirodho hi 2pratipattivirodhaḥ | sa ca yadi viniyuktenaiva rūpeṇa viniyo-
go’bhyupagamyate tadā syāt | yadā punaḥ viniyogopalakṣitaṃ dravyameva viniyujyate
tadā nāsti virodhaḥ | na ca nāntarīyakastuṣopavāpaḥ yena kāraṇāntarāpekṣābhāvāt
viniyogo na syāt | tasmāt viniyogāviśeṣāt ubhayaṃ prayojakamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
atrābhidhīyate | satyaṃ viniyoge nāsti śāstraprasaṅgaḥ | nāpi nāntarīyaka-
(1) P. 1212 (2) G. pratīti virodhaḥ

[p. r983]

bhāvaḥ | kintu upāttasyāyaṃ viniyogo nopādānakalpanā kramate | asyārthaḥ---puroḍāśa-
kapālamiti puroḍāśasaṃbandhitvena kapālaṃ lakṣyamāṇaṃ tadeva lakṣyate yasyaiva puroḍāśasaṃbandhā-
dhiṣṭhānatā, yathā gaṅgyā kūlaṃ lakṣyamāṇamapi svasaṃbandhitayaiva lakṣyate | tacca kapālaṃ
śrapaṇārthamupāttaṃ ityupāttaviṣayo’yaṃ viniyogaḥ | ata āha---nopādānakalpanā kramata iti |
tadidamuktaṃ bhaviṣyatāsaṃyogo’yaṃ na bhūtavartamānābhyāmiti | bhaviṣyatā saṃyoge hi
upāttaviṣayataiva | nopādānāvakāśo’sti yadi punaḥ bhūtavartamānābhyāṃ saṃbandhaḥ kimupādānaṃ
syāt iti praśnamātram | “ucyata”
 ityuttaram | na bhūtasya saṃbandhī yaḥ
tasya bhaviṣyadarthatayā anupāttasyaiva pālanaṃ bhaviṣyati pālanabhūtamevopādānaṃ yataḥ |
vartamānasyāpi yaḥ saṃbandhī tasyāpyanyatra vyāpṛtatayāpyanyatra viniyogo na saṃbhavatī-
tyarthādanyadupādeyam | bhaviṣyadarthatve punaḥ anyārthamevopāttasya svīkṛtasya dhāritavyasya
viniyogaḥ saṃbhavatīti siddhamaprayojakatvam | nanvevaṃ sati yatra puroḍāśaśrapaṇaṃ nāsti
vikṛtau tatra kathamupavāpaḥ puroḍāśakapālaṃ vinā kārya iti tatrāha---tuṣopavāpastu kartavyo na
kartavya iti nāsyādhikaraṇasyāyaṃ viṣayaḥ | sarvathā’dhikaraṇasiddhāntasiddheḥ agnihotra-
havaṇīnyāyena kartavya eveti gamyate |

[p. r984]

paśāvanālaṃbhāt | asminnadhikaraṇe hṛdayādyavadānavacanaṃ saṃpravyādhavacanaṃ ca
tulyaṃ prakṛtapaśuviṣayatayā ‘agnīṣomīyaṃ paśumālabheta’
 iti dvayoḥ prakṛtagrahaṇamiti
pūrvapakṣavādī dvayorapi prayojakatāṃ manyate | asyārthaḥ---agnīṣomīyaṃ paśumālabheteti yadyapi
paśorevāgnīṣomīyatā āpātamātreṇa pratibhāti tathāpi viśasanaśravaṇāt paśornaṣṭatvāt paśu-
saṃbandhināṃ dravyāṇāmagnīṣomīyatā | śukṛllohite ca paśusaṃbandhinī eva | tena tayorapi
devatāsaṃbandhaḥ | yāgārthadravyaprakṛtidravyatayā ca paśoragnīṣomīyatā | na caivaṃ vācyaṃ
hṛdayādyartha eva paśuriti | yasmādubhayatrāpi prakṛtitayā paśorgrahaṇam | hṛdayādivākye ca
na sākṣācchravaṇamasti | devatāsaṃbandhācca yāgaśeṣabhūtayoreva lohitaśakṛtoḥ nirasanasaṃpravyādhau
śeṣapratiṣattibhūtau bhaviṣyataḥ | kathaṃ tarhi tayoḥ prayojakatāśaṅkā | ucyate---na nirasana-
sapravyādhayoḥ prayojakatvamucyate | kintu lohitaśakṛtoreva | te ca yāgārthatayā yāgārtha-
prakṛtidravyasya paśoḥ prayojake yukte evetyadoṣaḥ |

atrābhidhīyate---hṛdayasyāgre’vadyatīti yāgopasaṃhṛtiriyam | atrābhiprāyaḥ
--- satyaṃ na paśoḥ sākṣādyāgārthatā | kintu svasaṃbandhidravyopakārakatayā paśusaṃbandhināmeva
dravyāṇāṃ sākṣādyāgārthatā | sā tu hṛdayādiṣveva upasaṃhṛtā | aṅgaistṛtīyasavana iti
śravaṇāt | etaduktaṃ bhavati | aṅgairiti śravaṇāt aṅgānāṃ tāvaccheṣatā’stīti
niścīyate | tatrāpi hṛdayādīnāṃ prākṛtakāryāvadānasaṃskāradarśanāt yāgakāryopayogitayā

[p. r985]

ca tadupapatteḥ | teṣāmeva yāgārthatā nāpareṣāṃ, paśusaṃbandhināṃ lohitaśakṛtādīnāmanaṅgatvāda-
hṛdayāditvācca | śakṛtsaṃpravyādhastu tāvanmātraparyavasāyitvāt nirasanasaṃpravyādhayoḥ
styānarūpatvena kāryāntarānanupraveśāt | yāgārthaprakṛtapaśusaṃbandhisaṃpātāyātapratipattyarha-
dravyāśrayatvāt pratipattiḥ | nanu saiva dravyaṃ prayokṣyata ityāśaṅkya 1prasiddhaṃ pratipatteḥ
dravyaprayojakatvāsaṃbhavaṃ darśitavān | pratipattiśca na prayojikā ‘na pratipādyaṃ pratipattyartha-
mupādīyate’
 iti nyāyāt | ato na prayojakaṃ paśoḥ śakṛtādītyadhikaraṇārthoṃpasaṃhāraḥ |

ekadeśadravyaṃ cotpattau | nanu cātra pūrvapakṣa eva nopapadyate upāttadravya-
viṣayatvāt ‘uttarārdhāt sviṣṭakṛte samavadyati’
 iti (puroḍāśa) śruteḥ upāttapuroḍāśaviṣaya
evottarārdhaḥ pratīyate | sa ca kṛtakāryadravyaviśeṣa iti dravyapratipattiriyam | na ca
pratipattiḥ prayojikā | kathaṃ punaḥ upāttadravyaviṣayā ityāśaṅkyāha---ikṣukhaṇḍavaccheṣāvagateḥ |
yathā ‘ikṣukhaṇḍamasmai prayaccha’
 iti sannihitasyaivekṣoḥ khaṇḍaḥ pratīyate, nāsannihitasya
tathehāpīti sannihitapuroḍāśahutaśeṣāvagateḥ upāttadravyaviṣayatā | satyamevam | kintu
atra puroḍāśasyānyārthatvāt | āgneyayāgārthatvāt nānyatra kāryāntare sviṣṭakṛdyāge

(1) G. pratiṣiddham

[p. r986]

grahaṇe’pi puroḍāśakapālanyāyena adhiṣṭhānamātratayāpi viniyogānupapatteḥ | ato dravyāntara-
mupādeyamiti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | rāddhāntavādī tu na dvayavadānamātropasaṃhāraḥ dvayavadāna-

bhidhāraṇaṃ pradhānabhūtānāṃ haviṣāmarthakarma | tathāpi prayājārthamupāttasya śeṣasya śravaṇāt
dravyāntaraṃ nopādeyam | yathā puroḍāśakapāleneti śruteḥ kapālāntaramupādīyate artha-
karmatve’pi | tasmāt prayojane viśeṣābhāvāt nārabdhavyamidamadhikaraṇamiti | pariharati
---dravyopādānaṃ tāvat tadvannāstīti sthitameva dhāraṇasya tūpādānaṃ haviṣaḥ prādhānyānnā-
nyārthatayā1 virudhyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ---tatra bhaviṣyadarthatvāt kapālānā-
mupādānaṃ dhāraṇaṃ ca na prayuñjītaivopavāpaḥ; prayājaśeṣasya tu bhūtaprayojanasaṃbandhitvāt yadyapi
kapāladravyaṃ nopādeyaṃ tathāpi prayājaiḥ pariniṣpannaiḥ dhāraṇasyāprayuktatvāt anyārthadravyasyāpi
arthakarmapakṣe haviṣāṃ prādhānyāt dhāraṇaprayuktiḥ na virudhyate | prādhānye hi haviṣo yadeva
haviḥ nābhidhāryate tasyaiva saṃskāraparilopaḥ syāditi yuktameva dhāraṇam | tatsidhyarthaṃ ca
pātrāntaramupādeyamiti |

kathaṃ punarhaviṣaḥ prādhānyaṃ dvitīyāśravaṇādabhidhāraṇasya tadarthatvāt prayājaśeṣasya

kṣitadravyasyādhiṣṭhānarūpasya puroḍāśakapālavat viniyogopapatteḥ siddhāntastu na prādhānyaṃ

śrūyate tathāpi haviṣāṃ prādhānyaṃ na yuktam tadarthatve’bhidhāraṇasyādṛṣṭārthatvaprasaṅgāt | prayāja-

tasmāt pratipattireveyaṃ prayājaśeṣasyeti | kathaṃ tarhi havīṃṣyabhidhārayatīti bahuvacanam |

 iti | santi cātra bahūni havīṃṣi |

prayājaśeṣaśabdena virudhyate | asyārthaḥ---nahyanyārthatvenānyaśeṣatopapadyate viniyuktaviniyoga-

prayājāḥ2 syuḥ puroḍāśakapālavat | yathā puroḍāśakapāle upalakṣaṇamātraṃ puroḍāśaḥ tathā
atrāpi prayājāḥ syuḥ | na caitadyuktam, anupalakṣaṇārthatve saṃbhavati upalakṣaṇamātratvānupapatteḥ
puroḍāśakapāle tu gatyantarābhāvādyuktam | iha tu saṃbhavati gatiḥ mukhyaprātipadikānvayāhānāt
tadāha---pratipattau punaḥ nāyaṃ virodha iti prātipadikārthānurodhinī guṇabhūtārthavādinī
vibhaktiriti yuktamanyārthatvam, dvitīyārthe vyatyayena tṛtīyeti yāvat | sthite cātra
prātipadikānurodhena vibhaktyarthadarśa/?/ne pāśādhikaraṇe ubhayārthānugrahāya utkarṣa āśaṅkitaḥ prāti-
padikārthaliṅgabalīyastvena nirākṛta iti | tṛtīyārthānavagamāt havīṃṣītyapi nāñjaso’rthaḥ
saṃbhavatīti | atrāpyanyārthataiva nyāyyā dvayoḥ karmaṇorekasmin vākye saṃbandhāt |
saṃbandhānuguṇaḥ saptamyartho draṣṭavyaḥ | darśamuktam |

tataścaupabhṛtasya ājyasya prayājānūyājārthasya taddharmāṇāṃ ca prayojakaṃ utamiśrī-
karaṇārthamevedaṃ jauhavasaṃskārārtham | tataśca paraprayuktadravyasādhyatvādaprayojakamānyasya
dharmāṇāṃ ceti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī samānayanamātraśravaṇānmiśrīkaraṇamātraṃ manyate |
aṣṭāvupabhṛti gṛhṇātīti ca sarvaprayājārthatāṃ manyate | kutaḥ ? viśeṣāśravaṇāt tasmāda-
dṛṣṭārthamevedaṃ samānayanamiti | asyārthaḥ---uparitanaprayājadvayaṃ yaṣṭumityevaṃ tāvanna śrūyate
tatra na śakyate tadarthataiveti vaktum | yadi caupabhṛtasya grahaṇasyoparitanaprayājadvayārthatā
avagatā syāt tadā tasmin yakṣyamāṇe idamapi samānayanaṃ tadarthamiti gṛhyatāpi | na caivaṃ
śrūyate | kintu sarvaprayājārthatā | sā ca yadyadṛṣṭārthaṃ samānayanaṃ tadā saṃbhavati miśrīkaraṇena
hi jauhavadravyaṃ saṃskriyate | tacca sarveṣāṃ kṛtānāṃ kariṣyamāṇayośca iti tatsaṃskārārthatvādyuktā
sarvārthatā | itarathā tu dvayārthatā syāt na ca sā yuktā | tasmādadṛṣṭārthaṃ samānayanam |
evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate | prayājadvayasādhanārthamevedaṃ samānayanam | kutaḥ ? evaṃ
dṛṣṭārthatā samānayanasya bhavati upabhṛti ca prayājārthasya grahaṇasya | asyāthaḥ---yadyapi
samānayanamātraṃ śrutaṃ, tathāpi prayojanākāṅkṣāyāṃ cikīrṣitaprayājadvayanirvṛttireva prayojanaṃ yuktam
dṛṣṭārthatvāt, na punaḥ adṛṣṭasaṃskāraḥ pramāṇābhāvāt | dṛṣṭenaiva nirākāṅkṣatvāt | tathopabhṛti

ca yadgrahaṇaṃ tadeva dṛṣṭārthaṃ bhaviṣyati anyathā 1adṛṣṭasaṃskāradvāreṇa praṇājārthatve 2adṛṣṭārthaṃ syāt
nanu cāviśeṣeṇa tadaupabhṛtaṃ sarvārthaṃ śrūyate śrutānusāreṇa cādṛṣṭārthatāpi nyāyyā |
atrottaraṃ---nāviśeṣaśrutiḥ sāmarthyena viśeṣe’vasthāpyamānā śabdārthatāṃ jahāti | yadi
viśeṣe’vasthāpyamānā uparitanaprayājadvaye sāmānyā prayāja(dvaya)śrutiḥ śabdārthatāṃ jahyāt tadā

vādaḥ tathā jauhave prayājagrahaṇamapi syāt | atha kevalaprayājaśravaṇāt prayājārthatā 1tarhi

sminnadhikaraṇe sthite tadadhikaraṇasiddhāntalābhaḥ, ata evaivaṃ ca samānayanaṃ dṛṣṭārthaṃ bhavatītī-
hopanyāsaḥ |

tadaṣṭasaṅkhyaṃ śravaṇāt | atra bhāṣye kimaupabhṛtamaṣṭasaṅkhyena grahaṇena saṃskiyate uta
catussaṃkhyā guṇabhūtā dvayorgrahaṇayoriti saṃśayo’sti | tatra phalasaṃśayaṃ ṭīkākāraḥ karoti
---aṣṭāvupabhṛti gṛhṇātīti śrūyate | atra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ dve ete caturgṛhīte utāṣṭagṛhīta-
mekamevedamiti | nanu samānayanādhikaraṇe idamuktaṃ dvau prayājau yaṣṭumekamidaṃ caturgṛhītaṃ 
samānīyata iti | kayā yuktyā ? evaṃ ca dṛṣṭārthatā bhaviṣyatītyanayā yuktyetyarthaḥ
tadevedamākṣipyate aṣṭasaṃkhyāśravaṇāt | asyārthaḥ---yadi dve caturgṛhīte prayājānūyājārthe
upabhṛti syātāṃ tadā yadevātra prayājārthaṃ tasyaiva tatsamānayanaṃ ca yadi bhavati tadā dṛṣṭārthatā
bhavati iti samānayanādhikaraṇasiddhānto bhavati | yadā punaḥ aṣṭagṛhītamekamidaṃ tadā
yatsamānīyate tanmātrasya prayājārthatā’navagatā | yāvataścāṣṭagṛhītasyāvagatā na tāvatsamānī-
yata iti noparitanaprayājārthamidaṃ samānayanaṃ syāt | nanvevamadṛṣṭārthaṃ samānayanaṃ syādityatrāha
---dṛṣṭārthatā ca samānayanasyopapadyata eva | ādhāre prācīne prayājatraye ca vyāpṛtasya
jauhavasyājyasya svakāryaṃ pratyaśaktasyānugrahāya samānīyata iti | ato’tra
(1) Bh. P.1222

pū्rvapakṣavādī śrutatvādaṣṭasaṅkhyākameva grahaṇamiti manyate | rāddhāntastu bhinnaviṣayatvāt
catussaṅkhyāṣṭasaṅkhyayoḥ nānyatarasyāprāptiḥ śakyate varṇayitum | asyārthaḥ---caturavattaṃ
juhotīti home catussaṅkhyā śrūyate | ‘aṣṭāvupabhṛti gṛhṇāti’
 iti ca aṣṭasaṅkhyā grahaṇe |
tena bhinnaviṣayatvāt virodhābhāvāt ubhayamapi viniyujyate | na ca homārthatvādgrahaṇasya
tulyārthatāṃ prāpya virodho vaktuṃ śakyate, yasmāt grahaṇe’ṣṭasaṅkhyā śrutā home catussaṅkhyā-

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

caturthādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ

svarustvanekaniṣpattiḥ | nanvatra eka eva viṣayaḥ saṃśayo vicāraḥ nirṇayaḥ prayojanaṃ
cādhikaraṇadvaye’pi dṛśyata iti kathamadhikaraṇabhedaḥ tatrāha---ekamatrādhikaraṇaṃ vākyāntarābhi-
prāyeṇa varṇitam | dvitīyastvekavākyamaṅgīkṛtya | yuktayastvekavākyavicāre vākyāntarā-
pekṣiṇyāṃ ca mīmāṃsāyāṃ tulyā iti pradarśitam | asyārthaḥ---prathame’dhikaraṇe ‘yūpasya
svaruṃ karoti’
 iti ‘svaruṇā paśumanakti’
 iti ca vākyadvayamudāhṛtya cintitam | kiṃ kiṃ-
pūrvakamiti | kiṃ yūpasya svaruṃ karotītyetatpūrvakamiṃda svaruṇā paśumanaktīti, atha vā
viparītamiti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī yūpasya svaruṃ karotītyetatpūrvakaṃ svaruṇā paśumanaktītyāha
---yuktiśca śrutyā vidhānam yadi svarukartavyatā vidhīyate śrutyā vidhānaṃ bhavati itarathā
vākyena vidhānaṃ syāt | yadā hi svaruṇā paśumanaktīti viniyogaḥ pūrvabhāvī syāta tadā
añjanakāryakāritayaiva svaruśabdo vartate tadā ca yūpasya svaruṃ karotītyasya vacanasyāyamarthaḥ
saṃpadyate---yo’sāvañjanakāryakāritayā prāptaḥ svaruḥ sa yūpasya grahītavya iti | evaṃ ca
yūpasaṃbandhavidhiparatvādvākyasya vākyena vidhānaṃ syāt | śrutiśca vākyādbalīyasī tataśca
svarukartavyataiva vidhīyate | yūpasyeti yūpakāṣṭhasya khadirāderityarthaḥ | tathā ca jo/?/ṣaṇādi-
kamapi svarūtpādakatayā saṃbadhyate | nanvetadayuktam yasmāt ‘yūpaṃ joṣayati’
 ‘yūpaṃ chinatti’

[p. r997]

‘yūpaṃ takṣati’
 iti yūpasaṃbandha eva joṣaṇāderavagamyate na svarusaṃbandhaḥ | ucyate---yūpaṃ
joṣayatītyāderayamarthaḥ khadirādi joṣayati yūpaṃ kartuṃ iti, na punaḥ prāk‌siddhaṃ yūpaṃ joṣayatīti |
tatra ca nāparaṃ yūpaṃ kartumiti viśeṣaṇaṃ vivakṣituṃ nyāyyam vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | kintu kāryārtha-
tvātsaṃskārasya1 kāryadvayasya yūpasvarvātmakasyaikakaisya prakṛtitvāt tadarthameva joṣaṇādikaṃ
khadirāderavagamyate | ataśca yūpaśabdaḥ prakṛtakāryapradarśanārtha eva syāt | tenāyamarthaḥ saṃpadyate
---joṣaṇādibhiḥ khadirādayo yūpībhavanti svarurbhavanti ceti | atra ca pakṣe viśiṣṭotpatti-
nibandhanaḥ svaruśabdaḥ | tena svaruḥ pṛthagutpattiṃ prayojayatīti manyate | jātyantarāśaṅkā-
vacanamapyevamevopapadyate | tataśca yūpaśabdaḥ prakṛtakāryapradarśanārtha eva syāt nānyatheti |
siddhāntī tu svaruṇā paśumanaktītyetatpūrvakamidaṃ yūpasya svaruṃ karotīti manyate, yasmāt yūpasya
svaruṃ karoti iti yūpasaṃbandhaḥ svaruḥ śrūyate | na ca yūpena saha prakṛtivikārabhāvo ghaṭate
yūpanāśaprasaṅgāt | na ca yūpakārakāṇi khadirādīni yūpaśabdena lakṣayitvā 2svaruprakṛtitā
yūpasyeti bhaviṣyatīti śakyate vaktum | lakṣaṇāprasaṅgāt | tasmādyūpaśabdasya śrautānvaya-
paryālocanayā svaruṇā paśumanaktīti viniyogapūrvakamidaṃ yūpasya svaruṃ karotīti | tatra ca pakṣe
añjanakāritayaiva svarutvasiddheḥ svarustāvanna kartavyaḥ kintu kimīyena añjanaṃ kartavyamityapekṣāyāṃ
yūpasyeti vidhīyate3 sa kathaṃ yūpasya bhavati yūpamanuniṣpadyata iti | tataśca svarukartavyatāyā-
macoditāyāṃ khadirādisaṃbandhaṃ ca4 joṣaṇadikaṃ yūpasyaiva śeṣa iti yūpa eva prayojakaḥ svarustvaprayo-
jakaḥ | śakalaśruteśca svarurekadeśabhūtaḥ vidyamāne cāvayavini nāvayavinaṃ prayuṅkte | asmiṃśca pakṣe
karotiḥ upādāne vartate upādāne ca karoteḥ mukhyārthataiva yathā kāṣṭhān kurviti | tadevamidamiha
vicāryate---kiṃ śrutyarthavidhānabalena lakṣaṇā’stu uta lakṣaṇāparihārāya vākyārthavidhānamiti |
tatra pūrvapakṣavādī śrutividhānameva balīyaḥ pratyakṣatvāt vākyasya vyavahitānvaye parokṣaṃ kalpanīya-
mityāha | rāddhāntī tu syādetadevaṃ yadi vyavahitānvayaḥ syāt | kintu bhāvārtha eva
vidhiḥ prāptāprāptavivekena guṇavidhirityucyate | tena vākyārthavidhānepi vyavahitānvayo
nāstīti lakṣaṇā na nyāyyeti manyate | jātyantarāśaṅkā tu prāgetasmāt anuniṣpannagrahaṇa-
(1) R. saṃskāryasya (2) G. svarūprakṛti (3) G. adds sa kathaṃ pūrvasyeti
vidhīyate tayā (4) G. na joṣaṇādikāryotpattiḥ

[p. r998]

vidhistutaye | pratiyūpaṃ svarudarśanaṃ ca liṅgamuktam | dvitīye tvadhikaraṇe svaruṇā paśuma-
naktītyetadeva vākyamudāhṛtya cintitam | yo’yaṃ svaruḥ añjane viniyujyate sa kiṃ
utpādyaḥ utopādeya iti | tatra tathaiva śrutividhānabalāt utpādya iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | tathaiva
yūpasaṃbandhasyotpādakatayā’nupapatteḥ yūpakāṣṭhalakṣaṇāyāṃ ca lakṣaṇaiva doṣaḥ | tasmādyūpānuniṣpanna-
evopādeya iti rāddhāntaḥ | abhyuccayayuktayaśca pūrvottarapakṣayostā eva | ādāne ca
karoterapi pravṛttiriti tathaiva rāddhānta iti yuktam | yuktayastulyā ubhayorapyadhikaraṇayoḥ |
viṣayabhedāt saṃśayaprakārabhedāccādhikaraṇabhedaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati---yadi vākyadvayāṅgīkāreṇa
paurvāparyavimarśaḥ kriyate tathāpi svaruraprayojakaḥ | atha ekavākyāśrayaṇenopādānavimarśaḥ
tathāpi svaroraprayojakataiveti |

śākhāyāṃ tatpradhānatvāt | kimarthamidamadhikaraṇamasmin prayojakāprayojaka-

digabhidhānaṃ yadi vā śākhābhidhānaṃ sarvathā nātraupādanikaṃ cintyate | kintvabhidhānaṃ tenāsaṅgata-
miti | ucyate---vidhita eveyaṃ cintā | aupādānikānuṣṭhānaviśeṣacinteyamityarthaḥ | kīdṛśī
setyāha---kiṃ vṛkṣaprāgdeśāt śākhopādānaṃ siddhānte hi śākhāvāde vṛkṣasya prāgdeśamapekṣya
śākhopādānaṃ kāryaṃ prācyāśśākhāyā āharaṇīyatvāt vṛkṣaprāgdeśāditīyaṃ lyablope pañcamī |
uta vihāraprāgdeśādānayanopādānamiti | pūrvapakṣe hi na śākhā prācītyucyate kintu
vihāradeśasya prācī dik | tāṃ ca deśadvāreṇa śākhā āhartavyā | tena śākhānayanameva

deśaviśiṣṭamupādeyam | viniyogavadabhidhānamanuvartate upādānamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | yathā-
viniyuktamevopādīyate nānyat | tathā abhihitamevopādeyaṃ tasyaiva viniyuktatvāt | abhidhānaṃ
ca mukhyayā vṛttyā prācyā diśa eva na śākhāyāḥ rāddhāntastu aparihāryalakṣaṇamidama-

phalabhūtatvādanupadeśyam | tasmāttātparyavṛttyā pūrvapakṣe’pi lakṣaṇaiveyamiti sākṣātkarmabhūtāṃ

pakṣadvaye’pītyayameva pakṣo nyāyyaḥ na hi pradhānakarmaṇā vinā karaṇaṃ pratyetumeva śakyata
ityavaśyaṃ pradhānakarma nirdeṣṭavyam | śaknoti prācīśabdaḥ prakṛtāṃ prācīṃ śākhāṃ lakṣaṇayopasthāpa-

kṛtatvāt labdha ityalakṣaṇīya evetyanavadyam |

śeṣāt | yadi hi parivāsana ubhābhyāṃ mūlāgrābhyāṃ prayujyate tadā śākhāharaṇamapi

yitvācchākhāśabdasya tenoddiśya parivāsanavidhānāt parivāsanena cāvayavino vināśāt na

śabdaḥ samūlamagramabhidadhyāt tadā śākhāśabdacoditāni kāryāṇi na syuḥ | tathā’gre ca
śākhayā vatsānapākarotīti mukhyārthaḥ śākhāśabdo na syāt | tathā chedanena śākhāvināśaśca
syāt | na caitadasti | tasmācchākhāśabdo’yamagrārtha eva | tasyaiva cāyaṃ mūlacchedanena

iti tadarthameva śākhāharaṇaṃ parivāsanaṃ ca | tasmādupaveṣo’prayojakaḥ prayojanamuktaṃ
paurṇamāsyāmupaveṣasyābhāva iti |

haraṇe tu juhotiḥ | nanu cedaṃ haraṇaṃ prastarapratipattyarthameveti pūrvapakṣamākṣipati |
tatrāyamarthaḥ-haraṇaṃ pratipattiḥ, kathaṃ tatra śākhā guṇabhūtā bhaviṣyatīti | atredamākūtam-prastara-
pratipattyarthamevedaṃ haraṇaṃ prastareṇa tulyayoginī ca śākhā tena sāpi guṇabhūtā na yuktā |
nanvetaddharaṇaṃ yāga evetyuktam | kathametat pratipattiḥ ityetadabhyupagacchati---yadyapīti |
abhyupagamyāpi yāgatāṃ pratipattitvamāha---yadyapi haratiryajatiḥ māntravarṇiko devatāvidhiḥ
tathāpi pratipattikarmaivaitat yathottamaḥ prayājaḥ svāhākāraṃ yajatīti | uttamaśabdaḥ 1tripra-
bhṛtīnāmantyamāha---pratipattau ca na dravyasya guṇabhāvaḥ | nanu ca yathā uttame prayāje ājyaṃ
(prati)pratipattāvapi guṇabhūtaṃ tathā’tra śākhāpi bhaviṣyatīti cet iti | atrāyaṃ
bhāvaḥ-sahayoganibandhanā tavadiyaṃ tṛtīyā na bhavati | na hi śākhā prajñātapraharaṇasaṃbandhā

rājñā sahā’’yāti rājñā saṅgataḥ, ityatra tu rājñā siddhena1 saṃbandhaḥ | tasmātkārakavibhakti-
rityevāśrayaṇīyamiti | pariharati---naivaṃ dravyasyāpyatra pratipattiḥ na ca dravyapratipattau
dravyasya guṇabhāvaḥ śakyate pratipattumiti pūrvoktamevākūtam | tatra punaḥ devatāsmaraṇārtho
yāga iti yukto dravyasya guṇabhāvaḥ tatretyuttame prayāje ityarthaḥ | atra yatpūrvapakṣavādinā
uktaṃ-nanu yathā uttamaprayāje ājyaṃ guṇabhūtaṃ pratipattāvapīti tatra pratipattiśabdaśravaṇāt dravya-
pratipattirūpatāpi vivakṣitaiveti matvā kaściccodayati---nanu ca uttamaḥ prayājo naiva pratipattiḥ |
dravyaṃ hi yatra pratipādyate 2tatpratipattikarma tena na pratipattirūpateti | atrottaraṃ
---kenedamuktaṃ pratipattitvaṃ ? nātra pratipattitvaṃ vivakṣitam | kintu kārakārthatā vivakṣitā na punaḥ
pratipattirūpateti | kārakārtho yāgaḥ pratipattikarmetyucyate | atra kārakārthatve kvaciddeśa-
virecanāt saṃskāraḥ prastarapraharaṇe | kvacit smaraṇaṃ uttame prayāje iti | prayojana-
viśeṣā hyete | tasmānna doṣaḥ | prakṛtamākṣepamupasaṃharati---ato na śākhā guṇabhūteti
śakyate pūrvapakṣayitumiti | ucyate---pratipattāveva tṛtīyāśravaṇāt guṇabhāvaṃ pūrva-
pakṣavādī manyate | nāvaśyaṃ pratipattau sarvameva pradhānaṃ bhavati | yadyathāṃ śrūyate tattathaiva
bhavitumarhati yathā juhvā juhotīti guṇabhūtaiva juhūḥ | atrāyamabhiprāyaḥ-haraṇaṃ tāvat
(1) R. adds ātmagamana (2) G. tatpratipattirūpameveti &amp; adds tena etc.

[p. r1004]

prastarapratipattyarthamevāvagata, tatra śākhayā saheti1 śravaṇe pūrvāprasiddhatvena sahārthe’nupapannayā
tṛtīyayā kārakalakṣaṇayā śākhāyā guṇabhāva evocyate | sahaśabdaśca nipātatvāt
kathañcidanvayaṃ neyaḥ | śākhā ca juhūvadvā, anyathā vā kathañcidguṇabhūtaivetyavagantavyam |
atrābhidhīyate---bhavedetadevaṃ yadyatra sahaśabdo na śrūyate | sahaśabdaśravaṇe tu tulyayogitvaṃ
prastareṇa saha śākhāyā avagamyata eva | tasmācchākhāpi pratipādyata eva | anyathā
hi tulyayogo na syāt | ataśśākhāyāmapi dvitīyārtha eva prastaravad‌draṣṭavyaḥ |
asyārthaḥ---sahaśabdastāvat tulyayogitāṃ samabhivyāhriyamāṇayorarthayorāha | śākhāprastarau ceha
samabhivyāhriyete iti tayoḥ praharaṇe tulyayogitā | nanu ca tṛtīyā nopapadyate | yadi
nopapadyate mopapādi | vibhaktīnāṃ vyatyayasyāpi darśanāta | evaṃvidha eva viṣayo vyatyayasya |
sahaśabdasya tu nārthāntare darśanamiti tasya balīyastvamiti | yadi śākhāyāmapi prastarepi
[iva]dvitīyārtha eva haraṇakarmatvāt tarhi dvitīyā prāpnoti tadāha---kathaṃ tarhi tṛtīyeti | na
ceyaṃ kārakalakṣaṇā kintarhi ? sahayogalakṣaṇārthā | asyāthaḥ---na kriyāpekṣeyaṃ vibhaktiḥ
kiṃ tarhi ? sahayogāpekṣā | kathaṃ tarhi vibhaktivyatyayo bhāṣyakāreṇoktaḥ ? tatrāha
---prasiddhena ca sahayogo lakṣyata iti vibhaktivyatyaya uktaḥ | prasiddhatvātprastarasya
praharaṇe tathedamapi draṣṭavyam | aprasiddhatvācchākhāyā iti | teneyaṃ vacanavyaktiḥ
prastareṇa saha śākhāṃ praharatīti | śeṣamuktam | saktuvaiṣamyaṃ prayojanaṃ ceti |

(1) G. śrūyamāṇayā

[p. r1005]

utpattyasaṃyogāt | apaḥ praṇayatīti kāryadvayāsaṃbandhamapāṃ praṇayanamutpannam |
paścācca kāryadvayaṃ śrutamiti aviśeṣeṇa dvayoḥ prayoktṛtvaṃ manyate | asyārthaḥ---apāṃ
praṇayanotpantau saṃyavanaṃ ninayanaṃ ca kāryaṃ na śrutaṃ paścāttu dvayaṃ śrūyata iti dvayorapi prayojakatāṃ
manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | nanu ca ‘apo ninayati’
 iti dvitīyā śrūyate | asyārthaḥ
---dvitīyāśravaṇādapāṃ prādhānyam | ninayanaṃ ca tatpratipattyarthameva | saṃyavane tṛtīyāśravaṇāt
guṇabhāva eveti tadeva prayojakamityabhiprāyaḥ | atrottaraṃ---anīpsitatvāt kārakamātre
bhaviṣyatītyabhiprāyaḥ iti | tathā yuktaṃ cānīṣsitamityanīpsitakarmaṇi dvitīyetyarthaḥ |
kathaṃ punaranīpsitatvamucyate sarbā evātra pratipattayaḥ udāharaṇam | tatredaṃ cintyate kimupa-
yuktasyāpi dravyasya cikīrṣitatvaṃ saṃbhavati neti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī manyate dravyaṃ hi kriyāyā
upakaroti iti cikīrṣitaṃ bhavati | yatpunaḥ kṛtaprayojanaṃ tasya kimarthā cikīrṣā |
tasmādarthakarmaivedaṃ ninayanam | tatra cāpāṃ karaṇabhūtānāmapi kriyājanyaphalabhāgitā astīti
anīpsitakarmatayā tathāyuktamiti dvitīyeti | atrocyate---nānīpsitatve heturasti
kṛtaprayojanamapi hi tat pratipādyatayā iṣyata eva | atrābhiprāyaḥ---yadyapi pratipattiṣu
kṛtaprayojanaṃ dravyaṃ tathāpi cikīrṣā ghaṭata eva---kimidaṃ saṃprati kriyatāṃ ? upekṣyatāṃ, yadi vā
taddeśāntare tyajyatām ? iti | etattu loke’pi pratītameva | tathā lokavadvedepi vyavahāraḥ
tatra yadyasya śrūyate tat 2tatsāpekṣaṃ svīkriyate | śrūyate cātrāpāṃ tāvadantarvedideśaninayanam |
(1) P. 1235 (2) G. tasyāpekṣitaṃ

[p. r1006]

na ca tadanupayogitayā2 na nirvahati, kṛtakṛtyasaṃskārasyāpi loke dṛṣṭatvāt | yathā devadatta-
śiraso’vatāritāyāḥ srajaḥ śucideśanidhānam | tatra ca devadattasaṃskārameva laukikā
manyante | tathehāpi śeṣiṇaḥ saṃskāro bhaviṣyatīti kalpyate | nanu cānīpsite’pi
kriyājanyaphalasaṃbandhāvyabhicārāt tāvanmātrameva dvitīyārtho nyāyyaḥ nepsitatvaṃ, vyabhicārāt |
tataśca mukhyārthatve kuta etadavagamyate anīpsite karmaṇi neyaṃ dvitīyeti tatrāha---na cepsite
saṃbhavati anīpsitāśrayaṇamupapadyate, tatpurassarāvagateḥ | yadyapyubhayatra dvitīyā mukhyārthā
tathāpi sarvatra kriyāyāḥ prayojanākāṅkṣitvāt tādarthyaṃ tāvat kvacidvaktavyam | tatra yadeva
tajjanyaṃ dṛśyate tadeva prathamopanipātīti prayojanamavabhāti | yacca prayojanaṃ tadeva tayā
kriyayepsitamiti īpsitatvameva pūrvāvagamyamānamapekṣitatāṃ yātīti tattyāgena nāparaṃ parikalpyate
tasmāt tatpurassaraṃ prathamaṃ yasyāḥ 3tādṛśyā avagateḥ hetorityarthaḥ | tasmāt saṃyavane
kṛtārthānāmapāṃ pratipattireva ninayanamiti siddhamaprayoktṛtvam |

prāsanavanmaitrāvaruṇasya | ‘maitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍaṃ prayacchati’
 iti pratipattirapāmi-
veti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | yajamānadhāraṇe kṛtārthatvāt daṇḍasya maitrāvaruṇadhāraṇaṃ pratipattireva daṇḍasya
dvitīyānirdeśāt | nanu ca karmaṇā yamabhipraiti sa saṃpradānamiti siddho guṇabhāvaḥ |
(1) P. 1236 (2) R. na vā instead of tayā (3) G. tasyāḥ
tādarthyāvagateḥ

[p. r1007]

sasaṃpradānake karmaṇi | asyārthaḥ---karmaṇeti karaṇabhāvasmaraṇāt yatra saṃpradānamasti tatra
karmaṇo guṇabhāvaḥ | tena na prādhānyaṃ daṇḍasyeti neyaṃ pratipattiḥ | atrottaraṃ---saṃpradānaga-
prītyutpādakatayaiva karmaṇo guṇabhāvaḥ | na punaḥ karmāntarasādhyatayā, karmabhāvānupapatteḥ |
asyārthaḥ---na dānaṃ pratīdaṃ karaṇaṃ kintu saṃpradānaprītyutpattau | yadi hi dāne’pi
karaṇatā syāt tadā karmabhāvo na bhavet | tasmāt dānaṃ prati karmatvāt tatprati prādhānyāt
dānaṃ pratipattireva yukteti karmāntaraṃ dīkṣitadhāraṇāt dānaṃ tatsādhyaṃ yasya tat tathoktaṃ tasya
bhāvastattā 1tayā punarna guṇabhāvaḥ | sthite pūrvapakṣe codayati---kathaṃ tarhi ‘daṇḍī praiṣānanvāha’

ityevamādidarśanam ? atrāyamabhiprāyaḥ---yadīdaṃ maitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍadānaṃ syāt tadā
tādarthyameva dānasya syāt | tacca tādarthyamevaṃ bhavati yadi dīyamāno daṇḍaḥ kāryaṃ kurvāṇasya
sāhāyye vartate tena 2yāvatpradhānaṃ dhāraṇamupapadyeta | daṇḍārthe tu dāne maitrāvaruṇagamanamātreṇaiva
kṛtaḥ śāstrārthaḥ iti daṇḍo na dhāryeta tadā tatpraiṣeṣu daṇḍadarśanaṃ nopapadyata iti | uttaraṃ
---yadṛcchayā prāptasyāyamanuvādaḥ | yadṛcchayā prayojanāntaranirapekṣaṃ kriyāmātraṃ loke bahula-
mupalabhyate 3tathā dhṛtasya daṇḍasyedaṃ darśanaṃ bhaviṣyati | nyāyataḥ pratipattau sthitāyāmityarthaḥ |
atrābhidhīyate---maitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍaṃ iti vyapadeśāt daṇḍī praiṣānanvāheti ca darśanāt
arthakarmaivedaṃ na pratipattiḥ | atrābhiprāyaḥ---dvitīyātastāvat śabdārthatayā tādarthyameva na
pratīyate | kintu kriyājanyaphalabhāgitā | sāpi maitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍadāne na viruddhā |
(1) R. tatpratyayaḥ tena na guṇabhāvaḥ (2) G. yāvatprāsanaṃ (3) G. tadvadetasyedaṃ
daṇḍasya

[p. r1008]

prathamāvagatatvena tādarthyasvīkāra ityuktam | sa cāpratipakṣe śabde | atra tu maitrāvaruṇa-
ṇāya daṇḍa iti vyapadeśo virudhyate | yasmādatra vyapadeśe maitrāvaruṇaprādhānyaṃ pratīyate |
itthaṃ ca tatprādhānyaṃ bhavati yadi tadarthaṃ dānaṃ, tathā ca pūrvoktanītyā daṇḍī praiṣāniti ca darśanāt
arthakarmatā’vasīyate | tasmādanīpsitadvitīyeyamiti yuktam | nanu ca yadṛcchayā prāpta-
syāyamanuvāda ityuktam | ayameva doṣaḥ pākṣikatvaprasaṅgāt nityavacchravaṇavirodhaḥ
syāt | kathaṃ tarhi dvitīyā ? tathā yuktaṃ cānīpsitamityuktam | tadayuktamiva prati-
bhāti | 1anupādeyaṃ hi tatrodāhṛtam ahiṃ laṅghayati kaṇṭakān mṛdnāti iti | upādeyaśca
daṇḍo dātuṃ yajamānenaiva dhāryamāṇatvāt | pratipattāvevānupādeyatvāt2 dvitīyopapattiriti bhāvaḥ |
tathā ca saktuṣu vibhaktivyatyaya uktaḥ upādeyatvāt anīpsitatvaṃ na bhavati | upādeyaṃ
hi kriyāsidhyarthamīpsitameveti | ucyate---3suṣṭhūktaṃ saktuṣu, homārthaṃ hi tatropādānam
iha tu na prayacchatyarthamupādānam | upāttameva hi prayacchati | atastathāyuktamevedaṃ karma
nepsitaṃ nāpi vibhaktyantarayogyam | yajamānadhāraṇārthameva hi daṇḍa upāttaḥ pratīyata
ityanupādeyatā | etacca parihāravibhabamātreṇoktam | paramārthatastu yatra sūtre anīpsita-
śabdena kriyānirvartyamabhipretaṃ na punastatkriyārthatvena hyanupāttamanīpsitaṃ, na cātra kriyāyā
(1) G. anuvādoyaṃ (2) yāvadupādeyatvāt (3) R. yuktaṃ
