daṇḍa īpsitaḥ prayacchaterarthakarmatvāt | ata eva gāṃ dadātīti 2gordānārthatvenopādāne’pi
dvitīyopapattiḥ | atrāpi yadyapi ca daṇḍasyopāddānaṃ na prayujyate dhāraṇaṃ tvavaśyaṃ prayoktavyaṃ
ityupādeyatvamaparihāryam saktuṣu tu tathā yuktatā nāstīti vyatyaya uktaḥ | yasmāt na
tasmin bhasmībhāvo homāt kintu upādhibhūtāseṃcanāditi dvitīye darśitam | kiṃ punardhāraṇa
meva prayujyate utotpattirapīti cintāntarametat | asti tāvat prayoktṛtvamiti
rāddhāntaḥ |

utpattau yena saṃyuktam | adhikaraṇāntaramuktamiti | asyārthaḥ---idamadhikaraṇaṃ
bhāṣya evoktam | tatra ca siddhānte kṛtārthatvābhidhānāt pūrvapakṣe akṛtārthatā’bhimateti gagyate |
pūrvapakṣavādī evaṃ manyate sakalaprayogānta eva kṛtārthatā viṣāṇāyāḥ | prayogamadhye punaḥ
kaṇḍūyanasya saṃbhavāt akṛtārthatayā na pratipattarhā | tena cātvālaprāsanaṃ na pratipattiḥ
kintvarthakarmaivedam | ucyate---nītāsu dakṣiṇāsu cātvāle kṛṣṇāviṣāṇāṃ prāsyatīti śrutam |
tena prāgeva kaṇḍūyanaṃ kṛṣṇaviṣāṇayā kāryāmiti rāddhāntaḥ | nanu 3aviśeṣaśrutaṃ kathaṃ
viśeṣe’vatiṣṭhate | pratipattividhānāditi cet na, itaretarāśrayāpatteḥ | maivam |
dvitīyāśravaṇena pratipatteranyatassiddheḥ |

saumike ca kṛtārthatvāt | pūrvapakṣamākṣipati---kaḥ punaratra prayājaśeṣeṇa viśeṣaḥ ?
yathā prayājaśeṣeṇetyasatyāmapi dvitīyāyāṃ prayājaśeṣasya pratipattiḥ | evamatrāpi yatkiñcitsoma-
liptaṃ tenāvabhṛthaṃ yantīti pratipattireva yuktā | samādhānamāha---nanu ca prayājaśeṣeṇeti
(1) P. 1238 (2) G. godānamātrabodhane’pi (3) G. ananvayaśeṣaḥ śrutaḥ

[p. r1010]

prayājārthatā’vagamyate | somalipteneti karmārthatā | kimiti na viśeṣaḥ | prayājaśeṣo
nāma prayāje vyāpṛtasyājyasya śeṣaḥ | na cātadarthaṃ tatra vyāpṛtamiti tādarthyaṃ tatrāvagamyate |
somaliptānāṃ tu somalepanamātraṃ na tādarthyamākṣipatīti punarākṣepamupodbalayati | ucyate---soma-
liptenetyapi somayāgārthatayaiva somasaṃbandhaḥ | yasya hi somayāgārthatā sa niyamena
tatra vyāpriyamāṇaḥ somalipto bhavati nānyat tena prayājaśeṣatulyateti | evaṃ tarhi avabhṛtha-
śruteḥ karmāntarayogaṃ somaliptasya manvānaḥ śeṣatāṃ manyate | asyārthaḥ---avabhṛthaśabda-
stāvat prakṛtatvādavabhṛthasya nānyatra vartate | tasya ca pradhānakarmatvāt abhidhāraṇavat
somaliptārthatā na saṃbhavati | na ca guṇapradhānabhāvamantareṇa saṃbandhaḥ | tasmāt puroḍāśa-
kapālavat adhiṣṭhānalakṣaṇayā somaliptasyaivāvabhṛthaśeṣabhāvena viniyogaḥ | evaṃ ca tṛtīyā
yathārthaiveti | evaṃ caiṣa(evaṃ) vākyārtho bhavati---avabhṛthaṃ kartuṃ yadapo’bhyavayanti tat
somalipteneti | atrocyate---karmāntarayoge’pi nānyaśeṣo’nyaśeṣatayā pratipattuṃ śakyate
anyatra vyāpṛta ivānyatra | tasmātkarmaśabdo lakṣaṇayaiva pratipattavyaḥ | asyārthaḥ---śrauta-
somaliptārthagrahaṇe tāvat anyaśeṣaḥ somayāgaśeṣaḥ anyaśeṣatayā avabhṛthaśeṣatayā pratipattuṃ na
śakyate(yathā) anyatra vyāpriyamāṇamivānyatra tena ca lakṣaṇayā saṃbandhaḥ | tatrāpi cintā
---kiṃ somaliptaśabdaḥ adhiṣṭhānalakṣaṇayā varṇyatāṃ, utāvabhṛthaśabdo deśalakṣaṇayeti | śrutyartha-

[p. r1011]

vidhānānugrahāya avabhṛthaśabda eva deśalakṣaṇāparaḥ iti nyāyyam | yantīti ca prakṛtyartho-
’ntarṇīṃtaṇijartho vihito bhavati | anyathā’nuvādamātraṃ dhātvarthasya syāt |
vākyena ca somaliptavidhānaṃ bhavati | tadidamuktaṃ bhāṣye---śrutiśca vākyādbalīyasīti |
tenāyamarthaḥ---somaliptamavabhṛthadeśaṃ nayantīti |

kartṛdeśakālānāmacodanam | nanu cānuvādapakṣe’pratipādakatvāt vākya-
syānarthakyameva prāpnoti | atretthaṃ kecit pūrvapakṣayanti---śaktasya kāryakaraṇāt aśaktasya
cākaraṇāt śaktireva kāraṇaṃ na dravyaṃ vyabhicārāt | sā ca śaktiḥ kāryagamyā samādiṣvapya-
viśiṣṭaiva | saiva ca kriyāyāṃ kārakatvena codayitavyā | sā ca nityaprāptaivetyanuvādāḥ
kārakaviniyogā iti | tadabhiprāyeṇa pūrvapakṣākṣepaparo’yaṃ granthaḥ | yadā hi śaktiḥ kārakaṃ
tadā samādīnāṃ kriyānvayo nāsti | ananvitasya ca na pratipādakatvaṃ ityapratiṣādakatā |
apratipādakatvāccānarthakyameva syāt anuvādapakṣe | atrottaraṃ---na brūmo viniyoge
ānarthakyamiti | viniyoge itthamevānvito vākyārthaḥ pratīyate | prayoktṛtvaṃ tu na
saṃbhavati | kathaṃ na saṃbhavati ? nityaprāptatvāt kārakagrāmasya dravyasya hi atra niyamaḥ

[p. r1012]

pratīyate | na kārakāṇām na cākārakasya1 prayoktṛtvaṃ saṃbhavati | tasmāt viniyoga
evāyaṃ viṣayaviśeṣo’vagamyate na prayoktṛvyāpāra iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | atrāyamabhiprāyaḥ
---dravyameva tāvat śaktimatkārakaṃ tadbhāve kāryadarśanāt tadabhāve cādarśanāt | etāvacca kāraka-
lakṣaṇam | yadi(paraṃ) dravyamātrameva kārakaṃ kimiti tarhi mantrauṣadhādipratibandhe’pi na karotīti
vyabhicāre parihāro vaktavyaḥ | tatrāpekṣaṇīyaṃ kiñcidrūpamastīti yasmānmantrauṣadhādyapaharaṇīyamatīndriyaṃ
kimapi rūpamastīti śaktiḥ parikalpate | na ca sā kārakam | kārakānugrāhikā hi
sā | na punaḥ svayameva kārakam | tataśca śaktimad‌dravyameva kārakamiti dravyasyaiva
kārakatvāt samādīnāṃ kriyārūpatvasaṃbhavāt anvitābhidhānopapattiriti viniyogenā-
syānarthakyam | kintu prayoktṛvyāpāre upādānaparyālocanayā anuvādakatvamucyate | yasmā-
dviniyuktasyāpi yadupādānaṃ tatkārakatayaiva | kārakatā ca sarveṣāmaviśiṣṭā | yadi paraṃ dravya-
mātratayā tadbhedaḥ | na ca dravyamupādīyate | tadrūpasya kriyāsaṃbandhābhāvaditi kārakatvena
niyamābhāvādana2rthakamanuvāda eveti | tasmādviniyogāvasthāyāmevāyaṃ niyogaviṣayasya yāgādeḥ
viśeṣo’vagamyate nopādānadaśāyāmiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | atrocyate---yadi dravyādanyat
kārakatayā’vagamyate tato yathā bhavānāha | iha tu dravyameva kārakatayā’vagamyate
tasyābhidhānādvibhaktyutpatteḥ | asyārthaḥ---samādaya eva tāvat kriyāyāḥ kārakatayā-
’vagamyante | te ca bhinnā eva kārakabhūtāḥ tāneva vidhirapyupādatta iti bhinnaviṣayaḥ
(1) G. kārakasya prayojyatvaṃ (2) G. dānarthakyam

[p. r1013]

prayoktṛvyāpāraḥ samādiviśeṣavyatirekeṇānyasyāviniyogāt | etadeva vivarītuṃ codayati
---nanu ca kārakaṃ hi kriyāprayuktyaiva prayujyate | na ca kārakaśūnyaṃ karmāsti iti |
asyārthaḥ---kārakaprayuktistāvat pṛthak nāsti | kintu kārakaśūnyā kriyā nāstīti
kriyāprayuktyaiva kārakaṃ prayujyate | tasya ca kārakarūpasyāviśeṣādaniyataviṣayamevopādānam |
satyam na kārakaśūnyaṃ karma | na punaḥ sāmānyaṃ kiñcidasti kārakaṃ nāma | yadeva hi
vibhaktyā dravyaṃ guṇo vā samarpyate tadeva kārakam | tasmāt viniyogavaśāt
prayoktṛtve’pi bhinnaṃ kārakamiti prayukterniyamasaṃbhavaḥ | nanu bhavatu viniyoge niyamaḥ
upādāne tu kathamityāha---uktaṃ tu viniyogānuvartī niyogaḥ sati saṃbhava iti | yadeva hi
viniyogato niyogasādhanatayā’vagataṃ tadeva niyogasiddhayarthamupādeyam | anyopādāne
sādhanābhāvānniyogāsiddhiprasaṅgāt | evaṃ ca niyamārtho 2vidhirucyate na punaranyatheti |

tathā dravyeṣu | nanu vibhaktibhiḥ guṇajātyorapi kārakatvena viniyogāt kartrādivadeva
virdhiyukto nānuvādaḥ tatrāha---yuktaṃ tāvat dravyasyopādānaṃ vibhaktibhiḥ tasya kārakatayā-
(1) P. 1240 (2) G. na vidhirucyate

[p. r1014]

’vagateḥ | guṇastu tadviśeṣaṇamātrameva śvetādiṣu | yatra punaḥ aruṇayeti vibhaktyaiva
kārakatvenāvagatiḥ tatra pūrvādhikaraṇenaivāvagatam | ato dravyopādānārthatvāt guṇa-
jātyoravivakṣaiveti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | asyārthaḥ---kārakavibhaktyanupātte tadviśeṣaṇamātra-
tayaiva ‘śuklānāṃ vrīhīṇāṃ caruṃ’
 iti śrute guṇajātī ihodāharaṇam | tatra sākṣāttayoḥ
kriyāṅgatvenāśravaṇāt vibhaktyarthavacca dravyaviniyoge (a)bhinnaprātipādikabodhyatvenāpi viniyojya-
tvābhāvāt anuvādo’yam niyogasaṃsparśābhāvena ca niyamānupapattiḥ | ato dravyopādā-
nārthamevedaṃ na guṇajātyupādānārthamiti | atrocyate---satyaṃ dravyopādānārthamidam |
etadeva hi dravyaṃ tadrahitaṃ na śakyate tadviśiṣṭamupādātumiti guṇajātyorapi dravyava-
dupādānasiddhiḥ | asyārthaḥ---bhinnapadavācyatayā tāvat dravyaṃ tābhyāṃ viśeṣyate | tacca viśiṣṭaṃ
2tatkriyāṅgamiti tathāvidhadravyasādhyo niyogaḥ tadanyaguṇopādāne tathāvidhadravyābhāvādaṅga-
vikalatayā na niyogassidhyatīti tayorapyupādānaṃ niyamena kāryamiti niyamārtho vidhiḥ |

saṃskāre ca tatpradhānatvāt | saṃskāre punaḥ ko viśeṣaḥ ? ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ atra |
adhikāraśūnyo hi saṃskāravidhirbhavati saṃskāryārthatvātsaṃskārasya | nātrādhikṛtārthaṃ
(1) P. 1241 (2) G. tadyāgāṅga

[p. r1015]

karmāvagamyate | tasmādavivakṣaiveti pūrvābhyāṃ viśeṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ---yaḥ khalu adhikāra-
niyogaviṣayaḥ so’dhikāraniyogasaṃsparśādadhikāravidhinā upādīyate | tatra na tāvat
saṃskārakriyā sākṣādvidherviṣayaḥ, saṃskāryārthatvāt saṃskārasya | yasya hi niyogasiddhireva
kāryaṃ sa viṣaya iti viṣayajñāḥ | tasmādadhikāraviṣayatvābhāvānnātra adhikṛtapuruṣārthatā
saṃskārāṇām | tasmānniyogāsaṃsparśādatrāpi niyamānupapatteranuvāda iti | atrocyate
---satyamadhikāro nāvagamyate | adhikṛtadravyārthatā tu pratīyate | tataḥ kim ? idraṃ
tato bhavati | saṃskāraviśiṣṭaṃ dravyaṃ adhikāraviṣayaviśeṣaṇatayā’vagamyate | tataśca
guṇavat tadapyadhikāravidhinopādīyata iti tadvadityucyate | asyārthaḥ---evaṃvidhadravya-
sādhyayāgasādhanatvānniyogasya nānyadapi dravyamupādeyam | tena vinā tathāvidhadravyābhāvena
yāgābhāvānna niyogasiddhiriti darśitam | yadidamadhikāraviṣayānupraveśāt puthagviṣayatvābhāvenā-
bhinnaniyogatvādanuvādakatvaṃ vrīhīnavahantītyādividhīnāṃ tat pradarśitaṃ evaṃ satyaṅgavidhiranuvāda
iti |

yajaticodanā dravyadevatākriyam | nanu asaṅgatamidaṃ padārthasvarūpamātrakathana-
masmin śāstre, kimutādhyāye ityāśaṅkya saṅgatimāha---ke punaste’dhikāraviṣayā2 yadanu-
praveśena kārakaṃ tadviśeṣaṇaṃ, tatsaṃskārā adhikāravidhyanupraveśinaḥ | ke punaste’dhikāravidhi-
(1) P. 1241 (2) G. viṣayaniyogāḥ

[p. r1016]

viṣayāḥ ? iti pūrvoktena prasaṅgatiriti yāvat | uttaraṃ---yāgādānahomāḥ svargakāmo
yajeta, āyuṣkāmaḥ suvarṇaṃ dadyāt saubhāgyakāmo juhuyāt ityevamādayaḥ | etaccopa-
lakṣaṇamātram | anye’pi ye kāmaśrutisaṃyuktāḥ upavāsavratādayaḥ tapaśca | kaḥ punaḥ
svatvatyāgātmakatve sati yāgadānahomānāṃ parasparaṃ viśeṣaḥ ityāśaṅkānivṛttyarthaṃ svarūpavarṇana-
mityuktam | asyārthaḥ---yāgo’pi dravyatyāgātmakaḥ homadāne api | ko viśeṣaḥ
eteṣāmityāśaṅkā bhavati | tāṃ nivartayituṃ sūtrakāreṇa svarūpaṃ varṇyate | tena sūtratrayeṇa
taduktamiti | devatoddeśena dravyatyāgo yāgaḥ | sa evāsecanādhiko homaḥ | parasvatvā-
pādanātmakastyāga eva dānamiti | etaduktaṃ bhavati | śabdārthatāyāṃ 1viśeṣo’sti |
upādhiviśeṣavaśena śabdapravṛtteḥ | tyāgātmakatve tu na kaścidviśeṣa iti | kathaṃ punarevaṃ-
vidhe’rthe yāgaśabdasya vyutpattiḥ ? vācyabhūtaṃ pratītya saṃbandhinamarthaṃ(ca) avadhārya tatra
śabdasya śaktyadhyavasānam | na cāyamarthaḥ pramāṇāntarasamadhigamanīyaḥ | devatābhāvasya
pramāṇāntareṇāvagantumaśakyatvāt tathedamapi vācyam |

kā punariyaṃ devatā nāma ? tathoddeśo’pi kimātmakaḥ tathātyāgo’pi kiṃrūpa iti |
atrocyate---āgneyādicodanāvasitadevatābhāvān agnyādīnuddiśya yadā puroḍāśādikaṃ
kaścittyajati tadā bālān vṛddhāstatraivamāhuḥ---agnyādīnayaṃ yajata iti | yadā sa
eva svadravyatyāgamātraṃ karoti tadā yajati śabdaṃ na prayuñjate | tenānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ
devatoddeśena dravyatyāgo yāga iti yajyarthe vyutpattiḥ bālānāmanayaiva diśā |
(1) G. tāyāmaviśeṣo nāsti

[p. r1017]

te’pi vṛddhāḥ bāladaśāyāṃ vyutpadyanta ityanāditayā vṛddhavyavahārasya na kiñcid‌duṣyati | nanu
kathaṃ devatārthe vyutpattiḥ ? ucyate | āgneyādihaviṣi sūkte ca codite yadi kaścidagni-
muddiśya tyajati agniguṇavacanaṃ stotraṃ śastraṃ vā prayuṅak‌te tatraivaṃ laukikāḥ prāhuḥ---agniratra
devateti | tatra bālāḥ paribhāvayanti yamuddiśya havistyajyate yaḥ stṛyate, praśasyate vā
sā devateti | atrāpi vṛddhavyavahāraparamparānāditvāśrayaṇena na kaściddoṣaḥ | tathaiva
vyutpannāḥ sāsya devatā ityadhikāravihitapratyayāntaśabdaśravaṇena vede’pi devatābhāvaṃ pratipadyante
yā ca tatra devatātvena codyate tatraiva tāṃ devatāmāhuḥ nānyāmuddiśyānyasmin haviṣi tyajya-
māne’pi iti laukikavyavahārānusāreṇa coditatvamapi devatāyā evetyadhyavasyanti | na caivaṃ
vācyaṃ vedādeva devatārthaṃ pratipadya vyavaharamāṇeṣu vyutpattiriti vṛddhavyavahārapūrvako devatārthapratyayo
na syāditi | yataḥ pūrveṣāmapi vyutpattau vṛddhavyavahāra eva hetuḥ | tatpurassarā tu vedārtha1-
pratītiḥ | tatpūrvakaśca vyavahāra iti tatpūrvikā aparasya aparasyāpi vyutpattiriti | uddeśaśca
saṃpradānakārakasyaiva svaśabdenābhidhānam yathā ‘agnaya’
 iti | tyāgo’pi svāminaḥ audā-
sīnyasaṅkalpaḥ laukikatvācca svatvasya | yatraivaṃ svāminaḥ saṅkalpo bhavati ‘mamaitanna’
 iti
tatraiva tasya svamidamasya svāmitvaṃ na bhavatīti laukikavyavahārāt saṅkalpamātrādeva svatvanivṛtti-
rityanavadyam | tadevaṃ yāge vyutpattisaṃbhavāt homaśabde’pi vyutpatterupapattiḥ | yato yajati-
codanācodita eva yatra viśeṣadeśādhāradravyaprakṣepoṣādhiko homaḥ śabdaprayogādavagantuṃ śakyate
eva prakṣepopahito homa iti |

nanu kimarthamuktaṃ parasvatvāpādanātmakaṃ iti dānaviśeṣaṇam | ucyate---na
tyāgamātraṃ dānaṃ api tu saṃpradānakārakībhūtapuruṣasvīkāro’pi | sa caivaṃvidho na
kevalatyaktṛpuruṣamātreṇa saṃpādyaḥ | api tu svīkartrāpi | na hi svīkāriṇaṃ vinā
svīkaraṇaṃ bhavati | tena tyāgaḥ parasvīkaraṇarūpanirvṛttaye svīkartāramapekṣate | anyathā
hi tyāgamātrameva syāt | na caivaṃ prāk‌svāminā tyakte yadi samakālamagrataḥ2 tyaktureva
kaścitsvīkaroti3 tadā tadīyameva tatsyāt | maivamāśaṅkitavyam | avocāmaivedaṃ laukikaṃ
(1) G. devatārtha (2) R. kālamagrataḥ (3) R. svīkāro’sti

[p. r1018]

svatvamiti | tatra yatsvatvāpattiparatayā svāmī tyajati tasya svīkaraṇe tadīyameva syāt
nānyasyeti laukikavyavahāraḥ | nanu yatsvatvāpattiparatayā svāmī tyajati sa yadi na svīkaroti
tadā’pi tyakturdānāt phalaṃ syāt tyāgamātrasya tadvayāpāratvāta tasya ca tena kṛtatvāt iti |
keyaṃ mandatā bhavataḥ ? uktamasmābhirna tyāgamātraṃ dānamiti | kintu svīkaraṇarūpamapi |
yathācoditānuṣṭhāne ca phalaṃ coditārthasya | coditārthaśca yamuddiśya tyāgaḥ tatsvīkaraṇamapīti
pratipattavyam | coditaṃ yathā bhāvi tathā yatitavyam | tatra kaścit prasijighṛkṣuḥ tyāgamātre-
ṇaiva svīkaroti | kaścit prārthanādibhiḥ iti saṃpradānasvīkaraṇarūpāpattaye samāśrayaṇīyaḥ |
svīkartā ca tadānīṃ ṛtvik‌sthānīya iti kṛtamatibahunā |

vidheḥ karmāpavargitvāt | atra bhāṣyaṃ viniyogameva cintayatītyasaṅgatimāśaṅkate
---kaḥ punarupādānalakṣaṇe’sya saṃbandhaḥ ? eṣa saṃbandhaḥ kimupāttasyātithyārthaṃ dhāraṇa-
mātraṃ dvābhyāṃ upasadagnīṣomābhyāṃ prayujyate, uta niriṣṭakasya punarupādānam, uta
pṛthagevopādānaṃ dharmamātrātideśakamide vacanam, utta sādhāraṇameva upādānamiti |
idameva cintayitukāmaḥ bhāṣyakāraḥ kāraṇasandehaṃ kṛtavānityabhiprāyaḥ | atra pūrvapakṣotthāna-
bījaṃ yāvanti padāni śrūyante tāvatāmeva tāvadanvayo vācyaḥ nāśrutapadāpekṣayāpi | tatra
yadātithyāyāmityatra yacchabdaḥ śrūyate | sa ca pūrvavṛttasaṃbandhyantarasavyapekṣaḥ | ātithyā
cātra samabhivyāhriyate | tenātithyāpekṣa evāsau varṇanīyaḥ | tenaiṣa saṃbandho bhavati yadā-
tithyāsaṃbandhibarhiriti | tatra yadyātithyopāttatayaiva tatsaṃbandhitvaṃ tadā prathamaḥ pakṣaḥ | atha
vyāpṛtatayā tadā dvitīyaḥ | atra rāddhāntamāha---anyaśeṣasya tāvanmukhyasya anyatra

[p. r1019]

viniyogo nāsti | pratipattivirodhāt yathā’nyatra vyāpṛtasyānyatra vyāpāraḥ vastuvirodhāt
ityuktaṃ somaliptādhikaraṇe | viniyogānusārī copādānamitīdamapyuktam |
āmikṣādhikaraṇe | tasmādviviniyuktatvāt ātithyāyāmiti ātithyopāttasya agnīṣo-
mopasadbhayāṃ dhāraṇamātraṃ prayujyata iti na ghaṭate | niriṣṭakopadeśaśca śiṣṭācāra-
virodhānna bhavati na hi niriṣṭakena śiṣṭā yāgamācaranti | lakṣaṇāśrayaṇācca prayogānte
ātithyāsaṃbandhasyābhāvāt sambandhe tenāsaṃbandhirūpatā lakṣaṇīyeti lakṣaṇaiveyamiti
nopapadyate | evaṃ dharmātideśo’pi lakṣaṇābhayādeva barhirdharmā lakṣaṇīyā iti tyajyate |
tasmāt sādhāraṇāvagatireva śrutyopapannā bhavati | tasmāt sādhāraṇamevopādānamiti
siddham | lavanasaṃbandhāpekṣaśca yacchabdo draṣṭavyaḥ | lavanapūrvakatvādbarhiṣaḥ | yadidaṃ
barhirlūyate tadātithyāyāḥ, tadupasadāṃ tadagnīṣomīyasyetyavirodhādvarṇanīyam |

iti śrīśālikanāthamiśraviracitāyāṃ

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

caturthādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ

dravyasaṃskārakarmasu | nanu ca bhāṣyakāraḥ phalavidhyarthavādacintāṃ karoti sā
cāsaṅgatā punaruktā ca, arthavādādhikaraṇena stutyarthatāyā uktatvāditi śaṅkāyāṃ nirākaraṇamāha
---uktamadhyāyādau sarvatra niyogavyāpāraścintyata iti | ihedānīṃ phalasaṃbandhitayā
puruṣasya vidhivyāpārassaṃbhavati neti cintyate | vidhivyāpāranibandhanaṃ phalavidhitva(pakṣa)
māśaṅkyānuvādakatvamucyata iti nāsaṅgatiḥ | punaruktatāpi nāsti | sthite puruṣaphala-
saṃbandhāsaṃbhave’sminnadhikaraṇe kathaṃ prāmāṇyasaṃbhava iti pramāṇalakṣaṇe’rthavādādhikaraṇe
cintitam | tenaitatpūrvako’sau vicāra iti yāvat | tathā yo’pi tatra pramāṇalakṣaṇe
audumbarādhikaraṇe pūrvapakṣaḥ asāvapi kriyākārakasaṃbandho’vagamyamānaḥ phalasiddhikriyāyā
audumbarayūpakārakatvasya ca sādhyasādhanalakṣaṇassaṃbandho’vagamyamānaḥ kathaṃ kriyāntaraviṣayaṃ niyogaṃ
pratipādayati | udumbareṇa yūpaḥ kartavya iti yūpakārakaviṣayaṃ niyogaṃ kathaṃ pratipādayati
svayaṃ vihitatvābhāvāt vidheyatvābhyupagame ca 2vidheyāntarānupraveśāt | na hi vidheyadvayasyaika-
(1) P. 1244 (2) G. vidhānā

[p. r1021]

vākyatvaṃ saṃbhavati | ekavākyatā cāvagamyate | na cāvagamyamānā tyaktuṃ yuktā | tasmāt
pramāṇāntaramapekṣaṇīyam | nacaitadasti ityaprāmāṇyaṃ syāditi | asyārthasya nirākaraṇā-
yoktam | tena phalāṅgīkaraṇenānyaccintitamiti na punaruktatā | pūrvapakṣaśca 1siddhāntopo-
ddhātatayā cintyata iti matvā nirākāryanirākaraṇāya pūrvapakṣa ityuktam | iha tu svaviṣayo
niyogo na saṃvatīti cintā | kathaṃ ? yasyetyevamādīnāmadhikāriviśeṣaṇatayā’saṃbhavāt2 |
asyārthaḥ---ihāpāpaślokaśravaṇādīnāṃ parṇamayajuhvādīnāṃ ca sādhyasādhanasaṃbandha evanāstīti
pratipādyate yasmāt ‘yasya parṇamayī juhūrbhavati na sa pāpaṃ ślokaṃ śṛṇoti’
 iti apāpaślokaśravaṇa-
madhikāriviśeṣaṇatayā na śrūyate | tenātra phalāpahnava evādhikaraṇārthaḥ | tenaudumbarādhi-
karaṇenāpi na punaruktatā | kathamasaṃbhavaḥ ? ayamāśayo’tra pūrvapakṣavādinaḥ pṛcchataḥ
---evaṃ vidhādvākyālloke sādhyasādhanabhāvo’vagamyate | tadvadvedepyavagatiḥ 3avagamyate cet
4svavākyakārya eva phalavattvāt svātantryameva syāt | na ca sādhyasādhanabhāvo’dhikāri-
viśeṣaṇatvamantareṇa saṃbhavatītyaupādāniko’dhikāriviśeṣaṇabhāvaḥ pratīyate | uttaraṃ-tatsaṃbandhasya
niyogasyānupapatteḥ iti | tenaivāśayena pṛcchati---kimiti | uttaraṃ---siddhavadupa-
deśāditi | yathā devadattaḥ odanaṃ pacatīti puruṣopalakṣaṇaṃ pratīyate odanapākaḥ |
na punaḥ puruṣeṇodanaḥ paktavya iti sādhyabhūtaḥ odanapāko na pratīyata ityarthaḥ |
tathehāpyapāpaślokaśravaṇamapi vartamānāpadeśāt | upasaṃhāravyājena pūrvapakṣaṃ nirākaroti-
tasmānnāyaṃ śabdārtho yasya parṇamayī juhūrbhavati na sa pāpaṃ ślokaṃ śṛṇotīti | pratyayāntarā-
(1) G. siddhāntotpannaḥ kena (2) G. viśeṣaṇatayoḥ saṃ (3) G. avagate ca 
(4) R. svavākya eva

[p. r1022]

gamyatvādasyārthasya | anena lokavaiṣamyamāha | loke’pi yadevaṃvidhāt vākyāt sādhyasādhana-
bhāvādhyavasānaṃ tat tathāvidhasya vākyasya pramāṇāntareṇa tatparatvasya niścitatvāt | na ca
vedavākyetātparyamanyato’vagantuṃ śakyate tadāha---na ca tatpratyayāntaraṃ tātparyāvadhāraṇakāraṇa-
masti iti prathama evādhyāye arthavādādhikaraṇe yo’rtha uktaḥ stutirūpaḥ sa eva nyāyya iti
sthitaṃ evaṃ bhūtānāmāpātamātreṇa laukikavākyasāmānyato darśanena phalamiva samarpayatāṃ ālocya-
māne nimittaśūnyānāmarthavādatvamiti |

naimittike vikāratvāt | atra bhāṣye ‘godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayedityevamādīnāṃ
vākyānāṃ kratvarthapuruṣārthātmakārthadvayapratipādakatayā pūrvapakṣitam | tadākṣipati---kathaṃ punaratra
dvayarthaṃ vākyamupanyasyata iti | asyārthaḥ---nedamekaṃ vākyamanekārthaṃ yuktaṃ yasmāta padānāṃ
saṃbhūyakāritā vṛddhavyavahārāvagatā | tripadaṃ cedaṃ vākyam | tatra na paśukāmapadaparityāgena
arthaśśakyate pratyetum | tatsvīkāre ca puruṣārthatvameva na kratvarthatvamiti | athocyeta
agṛhyamāṇāviśeṣatayā’rthadvayamiti | tanna | paśukāmapadaprayogeṇa viśeṣagrahaṇāt | yatrāpi
ca 2padāntarāvirodhāt viśeṣo na gṛhyate tatrāpyekārthataiva vākyasya | viśeṣagrahaṇaṃ tu
prakaraṇādibhiḥ | tadidamāha---yatrāpītyādi | athocyeta kimiti vākyasyaiva dvayarthatā
(1) P. 1248 (2) G. padāntarānurodhāt

[p. r1023]

na saṃbhavati tatrāha---dvayarthatā1cābhidhānasya pramāṇalakṣaṇa eva nirākṛtā | vājapeyādhi-
karaṇe vidhyanuvādajñātājñātatvāśravaṇāt | ekadā caikatra tadasaṃbhavāt | ihāpi praṇayana-
saṃbandho vidheyaḥ kratvarthapakṣe; puruṣārthapakṣe(tve)tvanuvāda iti | tasmādasaṃbhavatpūrvapakṣamevedama-
dhikaraṇaṃ pratibhāti | atrocyate---evamevaitat ekārthatvameva anvitābhidhāne | vini-
yogāvasthāyāṃ 2puruṣārthatvameva, na kratvarthatvamityarthaḥ | prayojanadvayaṃ tvatra saṃbhavatīti
kratvarthatāmapi pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | asyārthaḥ---ekasyāpi viniyogasya prayojanadvayaṃ
kratvarthapuruṣārthātmakaṃ saṃbhavatīti kratvarthatāpyuktā | kasmāt punaḥ prayojanadvayasaṃbhava ityatrāha
---saṃbandhānusāri copādānamityuktam | cakāro hetau | yasmātsaṃbandhānusāryupādāna-
mityuktam | asti cātra praṇayanenāpi saṃbandhaḥ tena kratvarthatve apaḥ praṇayatītyādinā
praṇayanādau vihite ‘camasenāpaḥ praṇayati’
 ityādinā vihite tatsādhane kratvarthasādhano-
pādānāvasthāyāṃ godohanamevopādeyamityavagamyate | anyasya saṃbandhānavagamāt | anyapare’pi
vākye saṃbandhāvagatirastītyetāvatā tadanusāryupādānavaśāt ekatvāderiva kratvaṅgatayā viniyukta-
praṇayanasthatāvagateḥ3 iti | nanu viniyogānusāryupādānamityuktam ābhikṣādhikaraṇe na
saṃbandhānusāri | naivam, viniyogo hi yasmāt saṃbandhahetutayoktaḥ na punarviniyoga-

’dhikārasya sthāpitatvāt tatrāha---ihādhikāraśūnyā vidhaya udāharaṇam | ayamāśayaḥ

dviśeṣākṣepaḥ | kiñca aparipūrṇe vākye loke’dhyāhāro dṛṣṭaḥ aparipūrṇaṃ ca taducyate

nopapatteḥ paripūrṇatā’sti kathaṃ tarhi ‘vasantāya kapiñjalānālabhate’
 grīṣmāya kalaviṅkān

satyadhyāhāraḥ | uttaraṃ---yuktaṃ kārakāṇāṃ kriyāpekṣatvāt kārakapadānāṃ kriyāpade-
’śrūyamāṇe’paripūrṇatvāt bhavatyadhyāhāraḥ | na punarniyogo’dhikāramapekṣata ityuktam |
tena vināpi viṣayeṇa sahānvitābhidhānopapatteriti | tasmādyāvadvacanaṃ vacanaṃ na puna-
radhyāhārāpekṣamiti | atrābhidhīyate---kārakamiva kriyāpekṣaṃ niyogamapyadhikārāpekṣaṃ
vadāmaḥ | asyārthaḥ---yathā kārakasya kriyayā sahānvitābhidhānaṃ tathā niyogasyāpi na
kevalaṃ viṣayeṇa sahānvitābhidhānam | api tu niyojyapadenāpi | ubhayānubandhavyatirekeṇa
anvita evābhidhātuṃ na śakyate | yathā kriyā ekenāpi kārakeṇānvitā’bhidhīyate dvābhyāṃ
bahubhirapi tatheha nāsti | sarvathā anubandhadvayānvito’bhidheyaḥ nānyatheti adhikāryanubandhā-
śravaṇe aparipūrṇatvādadhyāhāro yuktaḥ | ubhayānvita evābhidheya iti kutaḥ ? loke tathā
darśanāt | abhyāja gāṃ śuklāṃ’ ityetāvanmātramanapekṣya niyojyaviśeṣaṃ vaktāro na bhavanti
yasmāta devadattaṃ yajñadattaṃ bā’dhikṛtya liṅādayaḥ prayujyante | nanu devadattādayaḥ kartṛ-
viśeṣaṇaṃ tena vināpi niyojyena loke prayogo’stīti1 codyārthaḥ | yathā kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ
na bhavati tathā ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | yasya hi viṣayānupraveśaḥ śabdavirodhānna saṃbhavati so’dhi-
kāriviśeṣaṇaṃ yathā svargakāma iti yāvajjīvamiti ca | atrāpi saṃbodhanavibhaktiyogaśca

 ityuktam | tatreyamāśaṅkā bhavati yadyapyuddeśyatā tathāpyu-

---1yathoddiṣṭakāmābhiprāyaṃ vidhānaṃ iti bhāṣyakāreṇoktam | ye pratikratvantaraṃ kāmā
uddiṣṭāḥ ekaikena ta eveha nirapekṣā uddeśyā iti | nanu kasmāt yathoddiṣṭā eva sarvaśabde
nocyante ityatrāha---anirdiṣṭānāmavagamābhāva ityabhiprāyaḥ | yato’nirdiṣṭānāmanavagatiḥ
tena nirdiṣṭāpekṣī sarvaśabdaḥ “sarvaṃ kṛtaṃ”
 itivat | yato nirapekṣāḥ kāmā adhikāra-
hetavaḥ ataḥ pratikāmaṃ adhikārabhedāt yāvadadhikāraṃ prayoyogabheda iti | yata eva
paryāyeṇa phalotpādaḥ ata eva viruddhānāmapi sarvasvāraphalamaraṇa, prājāpatyaphalāyuḥ prabhṛtī-
nāmapi viruddhānāṃ cādhikārassiddho bhavati | sakṛtprayoge hi maraṇāyuṣoranupapattiḥ |
tasmādyogasiddhirveti sūktam |

evaṃ vā | nanu ca lakṣaṇasaṅgatirevātra nāsti | kimitīha loke kāmā utāmuṣmi-

tadā’niyamaḥ iha vā’muṣmin veti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī śarīragrahaṇasya kāraṇāntarā-
darśanāt paśvādīnāṃ ca kāraṇāntareṇopapatteḥ śarīragrahaṇapūrvakaḥ phalākṣepa iti manyate |

vaktavyo vā viśeṣaḥ | atra kaiścit anārabhya vidhānaṃ viśeṣa uktaḥ “darśapūrṇamā-
sābhyāmiṣṭvā somena yajeta”
 iti nedaṃ kasyacitprakaraṇe vākyam | kaḥ punaratnānārabhyavādena

yajetetyevamādiṣu | ayamabhiprāyaḥ---darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmityādiṣu nāmnā tāvatkarma dūrasthamapi
pratyabhijñāyate | tathā somena yajetetyapi dravyeṇa karma pratyabhijñāpyata eva | tenaikamapi
karma vidhātuṃ na śakyate | anārabhyavidhāne ca naikamapi karma prakṛtamasti | vājapeya-
prakaraṇe tu bṛhaspatisaveneti prakṛtavājapeyādhikārasannidhānāt yajistāvat aṅgavidhipara

dhātvarthaḥ pratibhāti tatra vidheyasya bhinnatvāt tasmin prayuktaṃ nāmadheyaṃ na pratyabhijñāmāpādayati |
vidhito dhātvarthasya prakaraṇāntaraṃ pratibhāti | adhikārāntaraṃ prati karmaṇo vidhīyamānatvāt |
tathā yatrāpi guṇadvayaśravaṇaṃ tatrāpi karmavidhiḥ kārakaniyamārthamuktam | tasmānnāmataḥ

evaṃ tāvat anārabhyavādamātraṃ yatra śākhāyāṃ tatra kālārtho niyoga1 uktaḥ |

saṃbandha eva yuktaḥ tatrāha---bhavatu tatrāṅgāṅgibhāvaḥ, kā no bādhā ? anārabhyavādamātraṣareṇai-
vādhikaraṇena siddhāntopapatteḥ | atha vā parihṛtametadaṅgāṅgitvaṃ śākhāntarādhikaraṇe sarva-
śākhāpratyayamekaṃ karmeti vadatā | kathaṃ parihṛtaṃ ? yāvatā yasya yo’rthaḥ pratibhāti tasya
tenaivārthena bhavitavyamiti bhāvaḥ, tadevedaṃ śāstramiti vākyasya pratyabhijñāyamānatvāt ekaṃ
śāstraṃ yuktaṃ na śāstrāntaramiti | atra pṛcchati |---bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi na vidmo’nārabhya-
vādasya yo’rthaḥ kālārthoṃ niyoga iti tena śāstraṃ pratyabhijñāyatāṃ, sa eva śāstrāntare
prakaraṇāntarādhītepi bhavatu, uta prakaraṇepi yo’rtho’ṅgāṅgibhāvaḥ teva śāstraṃ pratyabhijñāyatāṃ sa
evānārabhyavāde’pi śākhāntarīye bhavatu | uttaraṃ---avirodhādanārabhyavādena pratyabhijñānaṃ
bhedavido manyante | anārabhvādhītasya yo’rthaḥ sa evārabhyādhītasyāpi avirodhāt | anāra-
bhyabādārthe sarvatrāśrīyamāṇe nāmnā dravyeṇa ca yatpratyabhijñānaṃ na tasya virodhaḥ prakaraṇādhītārthe tu
pratyabhijñānaṃ virudhyata iti | tasmātsūktaṃ kālārthassaṃyoga iti | darśanamuktam |

na nimittānantaraṃ putrajanmānantaraṃ prayogaḥ saṃbhavati | nityavatkālānupasaṃhārāpekṣe ca
śaucānupasaṃhārāditi svakāla eva paurṇamāsyādike | upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat śucinā kartavyo’yaṃ
prayogaḥ | etacca naimittikasya prādhānyānnimittabādhamaṅgīkṛtyoktam | na cānantarye’sati
paramārthato nimittavyāghāto’sti bhūtapratyayayogāt jāta iti nimittasya na ca pratīkṣaṇe
kālaśaucayorbhūtatvamapaiti | tasmāt prathamādhikaraṇaṃ[pṛthagadhikaraṇaṃ]niṣprayojanamiva pratibhāti |

[p. 1054]

na ca tāvanmātreṇa kālaśaucāpekṣāpi sidhyati | putre jāte kṛte ca jātakarmaṇi prayogasyā-
śaṅkyamānatvāt | tatra sthitvāpi yadeva kāraṇaṃ vaktavyaṃ sāṅganaimittikānugrahātmakaṃ
paraṃ tadeva prathamamupanyasyatāmiti bhāvaḥ | atrābhidhīyate---nimittānantaraṃ naimitti---
kamityautsargiko nyāyaḥ na ca kāraṇādṛte kālotkarṣo’vagamyate | asyārthaḥ---nimittaṃ
taducyate yasmin sati bhavati naimittikaṃ taccennimittamāgataṃ naimittikamapi tena tadanantarameva
yuktaṃ vilambakāraṇābhāvāt | nanu ca kāraṇamuktaṃ sāṅgasya naimittikatvam | parihartu-
mupakramate---etadeva na vidmaḥ kiṃ nimittakāle niyogāvagamāt viniyogāvagamyamaṅgaṃ
tyajyatāṃ uta niyogakālasyotkarṣa iti | niyogasya pradhānatvāt niyogārthatvācca
viniyogasyāṅgatyāge viniyogavido manyante | yathā rajasvalādisparśanena pūrvāhṇādi---
kālaḥ snānāṅgatvena vihita upekṣyate | jīvanādikaṃ tu na nimittasaptamīnirdiṣṭam | iha
tu svabhāvālocanayā jananānantaryaṃ niyogasya pratibhāti | nimittasya hīdameva nimittatvaṃ
yattasmin sati niyogaḥ | nimittaṃ cedbhūtaṃ niyatamanuṣṭhānaṃ prāpnoti niyogārthatvādanuṣṭhānasya |
ata evaiṣā prayuktirna śakyate 1naimittikānugraheṇa bādhitum | ata etadbādhanārthaṃ pūrvamadhi-
karaṇam | kathaṃ bādhyet ? etadāha---yadi hi niyogakāla evānuṣṭhānaṃ kriyate
yadartho niyogaḥ kumāragataphalārthaḥ tadeva hīyate | ayamabhiprāyaḥ---kāmyeṣu karmasu phalaṃ
(1) naimittikagraheṇa

[p. 1055]

prayojakamanuṣṭhānasya na niyogaḥ tena phalābhāve prayojakābhāvādananuṣṭhānamiti | kathaṃ punaḥ
phalābhāvaḥ ? kumārasya prāṇaviyogaprasaṅgāt | jātakarmaṇi kṛte stanapraṇidhānam | jñātakarma
ca pitrā kartavyam | sa cediṣṭau vyāpriyate kathaṃ jātakarma kriyeta | akṛte ca
jātakarmaṇi stanadānaṃ nāsti | apītastanaḥ kathamiva dhārayetprāṇān | tasmāñjātakarmānantaraṃ
phalābhāvādiṣṭorniyogakālavagatirevotkraṣṭavyā | kathaṃ tarhi uttarādhikaraṇe rāddhāntaḥ ?
tatrāha---utkṛṣṭā cet svakāle nāṅgatyāge pramāṇamasti iti dvitīyamadhikaraṇaṃ likhitam |
asyārthaḥ---nimittasvabhāvalabhyānantaraniyogabalena tāvadanantarānuṣṭhānaṃ nāstīti siddhe sāṅga-
mevānuṣṭheyaṃ kāle śucitā ceti |

pradhānenābhisayogāt | vājapeyeneṣṭvā bṛhaspatisavena yajetetyatra saṃśayaḥ---kimeko
niyoga uta niyogadvayamiti | anena bhāṣyakārīyaṃ saṃśayaṃ vyācaṣṭe | etaduktaṃ bhavati
---kimeka evāyaṃ niyoga uta bhinna iti | ekatve ekaprayogatā bhinnatve bhida iti | tatra
pūrvapakṣamāha---nanu ca niyogadvitve bhinnaniyogābhisaṃbandhāt aṅgāṅgitvaṃ na syāt | eka-
prayogānupraveśanibandhano hyaṅgāṅgibhāvaḥ | asti cāṅgāṅgitvaṃ tasmānna prayogabhedaḥ |
rāddhāntamāha---bāḍhamapapadyate | viniyogāddhi aṅgāṅgitā | asti cātra viniyogaḥ

[p. 1056]

prayogānyatvena niyogānyatvaṃ, tena dvayamapi bhavatu aṅgāṅgitvaṃ prayogabhedaśca | nanu ca
prayogānyatve niyogānyatvācchāstrabhedaḥ prāpnoti | abhinne śāstre śāstrārthabhūtaniyogabhedānu-
papattiḥ | bhavatu prayogāpekṣayā na kaściddoṣaḥ | viniyogāpekṣayā tu ekamevedaṃ śāstram
kathaṃ punarekatve śāstrasya prayogabhedaḥ | ekaśāstratvamekaśāstrārthatve tathā ca prayogabhedo na
syāt | uttaraṃ---kena bāryate vidhānabhedavat anubandhanimittakam | vidhānabhedavat
prayogavidhibhedāt kālarūpānubandhabheda nimittakaḥ | yadyathā’nubadhvate tattathā tantraṃ prayogaṃ
bhinatti | yat kālādi yathā’nubadhyate anubandhībhavati tattathā tantraṃ prayogaṃ bhinatti |
iha ca prayogānubandhabhede prayogabhedaḥ | tasmānna śāstrāntarāpattiḥ kevalaṃ prayogabhedaḥ |
yathā ca prayogabhedaḥ tathā darśitam | iṣṭe्vati bājapeyābhinirvartitiḥ pṛthagbhūto bṛhaspatisavaḥ
pratibhāti | tena bhinnānuṣṭhānānubandhatvādanuṣṭhānabhedaḥ | aṅgaṃ ca prakṛtakāmādhikāragṛhīta-
tvāditi yuktam | tathā ‘agniṃ citvā’
 ityagnisaṃskārabhūtacayanābhinirvṛttitaḥ pṛthagbhūtā
sautrāmaṇī prayogāvagamādaiṣṭikakāla eva prayoga iti darśitaṃ bhāṣye |

iti śrīśālikanāthamiśraviracitāyāṃ ṛjuvimalāyāṃ
pañjikāyāṃ caturthādhyāyasya
tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ

[p. 1057]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

caturthādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ

prakaraṇaśabdasāmānyāt | atra bhāṣyakāro rājasūyaśabdasya sakalaprakṛtotpattivacana-
tayā anijyānāmapi prādhānyamāha | tadākṣipati---nanu ca nāmadheyavicāre yajisāmā---
nādhikaraṇyānnāma bhavatītyuktam | kathaṃ punarayajyarthasyāpyetadabhidhānaṃ śaṅkyate | ataḥ
pūrvapakṣa evāyaṃ nopapadyate | asyārthaḥ---rājasūyena svārājyakāmo yajeta’ iti yāgastāva-
dvidhīyate | tatra rājasūyaśabdo’pi yadyarthāntaravacanaḥ syāt tadā vākyabhedo bhavet | tena 
yāgānāmevedaṃ vacanamiti niścīyate | evaṃ ca yāgānāmetannāmeti yāgānāmeva prādhānyam |
kathamayāgānāmapi prādhānyāśaṅkā | ucyate | satyamevam---iha tu niyogaikyāt yuga---
padabhidhānaṃ dhātvarthasya; niyogaikyaṃ ca prakaraṇāyattamiti sarvaprakṛtābhidhāyī dhāturiti
pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | asyārthaḥ---yajinā sarve’pi yāgā yadvaktavyāstantreṇa tatrāpyadhi-
kāraniyogaikyameva kāraṇam | adhikāraniyogo hi prakṛtatvāt sarvameva svīkaroti | svīkṛtāśca
yajinā adhikāraniyogasya viṣayatayā samarpaṇīyā iti sarveṣāmeva tantrābhidhānaṃ(niyogaikyaṃ)
prakṛtatvanibandhanam | tacca prakṛtatvaṃ yajīnāmayajīnāṃ cāviśiṣṭamiti prakṛtānubandhasamarpaṇe nirjñāta-
(1) P. 1269

[p. 1058]

tātparyo yajiḥ ayajiṣvapi daṇḍinyāyena vartate | na ca vācyaṃ---iti kartavyatayā cāyajināṃ
niyogaikyam iti | yasmāt sākṣātsaṃbandhe sambhavati paramparāśrayaṇamayuktameva | sākṣācca
sarveṣāṃ niyogārthatve’vagate tatsamarpaṇatayā nirjñātatātparyo yajiḥ lakṣaṇayā ayajiṣvapi
vartamāno nānupapannaḥ | evaṃ ca yajiśabdasāmānādhikaraṇyāt rājasūyaśabdo’pi sarveṣāṃ vācaka
iti kāryabhūtaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇoktam | nāmadheyavicāraśca dhātvabhihito’rtho nāmnā’bhidhīyate
ityetāvati paryavasitaḥ | tasmādupapadyate pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā | evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---satyaṃ
prakaraṇānniyogaikyaṃ na punaḥ śrutānubandhaparityāge kāraṇamasti | asyārthaḥ---‘rājasūyena
svārājyakāmo yajeta’
 iti yāgaviṣayastāvanniyogaḥ prathamaṃ pratīyate | paścāttu tathābhūtena
niyogena sannihitāssarve padārthā gṛhyante | tataśca kathaṃ kasyānvayaḥ ityapekṣāyāṃ yāgānāṃ
karaṇatayā saṃbandhaḥ yāgānāṃ viṣayatvāt viṣayībhūtasya ca karaṇatvāt | tena yāgānāmeva
karaṇatvam | ayāgānāṃ cetikartavyatātvamiti | kathaṃ ? itikartavyatātva ayāgānāṃ
niyogakyāvirodhābhiprāyeṇa pṛcchati | atrottaramāha---itikartavyatāpi hi naiva niyogaikyaṃ
niruṇaddhi | tathā bhūtopadeśāditi vakṣyāmaḥ | ato yāgavyatiriktamaṅgamiti siddham |
etaduktaṃ bhavati---utpattiśiṣṭyāgaviruddhe niyoge karaṇatayā niveśāsabhavāditikartavya-
tathaivānvayaḥ, anyatrāpi sādhye darśanāditi |

madhyasthaṃ yasya tanmadhye | nanu cātrāpi kramāt prakaraṇaṃ balīya ityetadevodā-
hṛtam | asyārthaḥ---kramātprakaraṇaṃ balīya ityatrāpyadhikaraṇe tṛtīye etadevodāhṛtam |
(1) P. 1271

[p. 1059]

yadīhodāhṛta bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi kimityāha---sthitaṃ ca sarvārthatvaṃ, ataḥ punaruktamiti |
pariharati---satyaṃ viniyogāt sthitaṃ idānīṃ pṛthak‌prayojyatvāt abhiṣecanīyaniyogasya
tasya ca sannidhānāt tenaivaikyaṃ yuktamiti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | asyārthaḥ---pṛthak
prayoktṛtvenābhiṣecanīyasya tāvat prayuktibhedo’ṅgīkāryaḥ tadīyaprayuktimadhyapatitāśca videvanādayaḥ
atassannidhānāt sa evāsya prayojako yuktaḥ | tataśca prayuktiparyantatvādviniyogasya viniyogo’pi
tatraiveti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | na ca prakaraṇaviniyogavirodhaḥ | abhiṣecanīyāṅgatvepi
pratyekasaṃbandhahetutvāt prakaraṇasya; pratikaraṇaṃ itikartavyatayā saṃbandhāpādakatvādasyāpi
kāraṇatvāt | atassannidhiviśeṣātikramo nopapadyate | atrocyate---sthitamaikaśabdyama-
dhikāravidheḥ | ekādhikāraniyogasya sādhyatvenvayāttatsādhanatvenāṅgapradhānopadeśāt | ekaṃ
śāstramaṅgapradhānātmakam | tataḥ kim ? idaṃ tato bhavati yasyaiva prayogānubandhabhedaḥ
tasyaiva pṛthak prayogo’vasīyate svenetikartavyatākalāpena | yasya punaraṅgasyādhikāravidhi-
reva prayogaṃ jñāpayati na tasyāvāntaraprayogānupraveśaḥ śakyate’/?/gantum | tasmāt
kramāttaddeśasyaivānuṣṭhānamadhikāravidhisamadhigamyameva | asyārthaḥ---yathāviniyoga tāvat
prayuktiḥ viniyoge ca sarvārthatvameva | ato’dhikāravidhirevāsya prayojakaḥ na punaravāntara-

[p. 1060]

niyogaḥ, tadarthatvena viniyogābhāvāt tatsannidhānāstu tatraivānuṣṭheyamadhikāravidhinaiva prayujyata
iti viniyogāt prayukteśca sarvārthatvameva | prakaraṇāvirodhastvayaivoktaḥ | asminnaiva pakṣe
prakaraṇāvirodho yathā bhavati tathā tvayaivoktaḥ pratyekasaṃbandhahetuḥ prakaraṇamiti vadatā | tacca
prakaraṇaṃ pratyekasaṃbandhe eva na virudhyate | niyataikasaṃbandhe tu prakaraṇaṃ sarvasaṃbandhāpādakaṃ
virudhyata eva | tasmāt prakaraṇāvirodhāt sarvārthamiti |

prakaraṇāvibhāge | ākṣipati-kaḥ khalu āgnimārutādūrdhvamanūyājaiścarantīsyanenāsya
viśeṣaḥ | purastādupasadābhityanena purastāditi kālasya śravaṇāt kālārtha eva saṃyogo yukta iti
yāvat | na khalu kaścidityādi subodham | ayaṃ tarhi viśeṣaḥ | prakaraṇāt sarvasya prādhānyaṃ
itikartavyatāvyaṃ vā rājasūye | asaṃyuktaṃ ca prakaraṇādityuktam | yatsarvasya prādhānyaṃ
itikartavyatātvaṃ vā sarvān prati rājasūye uktaṃ tat prakaraṇāt | adhikāravidhyaṅgatayā 1ca
prakṛtagrahaṇātmakaṃ prakaraṇamihābhipretam | rājasūye cānabhivyaktarūpatvāt viṣayībhūtasya yāgasya
rūpavadyāgāpekṣā bhavati | tathetikartavyatāpekṣāpi | tatra yajatayo viṣayānupraveśinaḥ pradhānāḥ
ayajatayaścetikartavyatā | asaṃyuktaṃ ca prakaraṇādviniyujyata ityuktaṃ tṛtīye | saṃyuktāśca
(1) P. 1272 (2) R. vidhyākāṅkṣāyāṃ

[p. 1061]

saumyādayaḥ upasadbhirvākyena | evaṃ hi pūrvapakṣī manyate---upasadāṃ saumyeneti saṃbandhaḥ
na cānyathā saumyādayaḥ upasadbhissaṃbandhinaḥ ityaṅgāṅgitā | 1śeṣaṣaṣṭhyeveyaṃ yuktā nopapadaṣaṣṭhī |
prādhānye copasadāṃ ṣaṣṭhī | tena sāpekṣatvāt upasadāṃ saumyeneti saṃbandhaḥ purastāditi tu
saṃbandhāpekṣaḥ | paścāt prakṛtatvāt upasadapekṣameva bhaviṣyatīti | tasmāt vākyādupasa-
daṅgateti pūrvapakṣiṇo’bhiprāyaḥ | api ca sannidhānaviśeṣeṇopasadaṅgataiva yuktā, adhi-
kāraniyogāt | asyārthaḥ---upasadāṃ purastādityapi saṃbandhe upasatsannidhānaviśeṣāt
saumyādīnāmadhikāraniyogādapi buddhāvanusandhānāt tadaṅgataiva yukteti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
atrābhidhīyate---na tāvadupasadāṃ prakaraṇamasti | na ca buddhisannidhimātraṃ prakaraṇam |
kutaḥ | adhikāranibandhanatvāt prakaraṇasya | asyārthaḥ-sādhikāro hi niyogo’nvitā-
bhidhānanirvāhakaḥ kāryarūpaḥ pratīyate na niradhikāraḥ tena sarvaṃ tadarthameveti paścāt tadadhi-
kārānupraveśāt 2tadadhikārānupraveśāya nādhikārāntaramapekṣate tenaivānvitābhidhānasiddheḥ niradhi-
kārāṇāṃ kāryāvagame’pi3 4viniyojakaśaktirnāstīti na prakaraṇaṃ teṣāmasti | nanu ca
vākyādupasadaṅgatvamityuktam, nobhayapadasaṃbandhābhāvāt vākyaṃ saṃbhavati | asyārthaḥ
---na saṃbhavati vākyārthaḥ upasacchabdasya saumyapurastātpadābhyāṃ saha sakṛduccāraṇe saha saṃbandhā-
bhāvāt | tasmādekapadasaṃbandhe sthite purastādupasadāmityeva saṃbandhaḥ | atha kasmādupasadāṃ
saumyenetyeṣa saṃbandhaḥ na bhavati ? purastādityananvayaḥ prāpnoti | asyārthaḥ---purastā-

(4) R. viniyonyaṃkiñjinnāstīti

[p. 1062]

cchabdo’yaṃ nityasāpekṣavacanatvāt upasadbhirasaṃbadhyamāno nārthamavabodhayati | tena purastādityanena
saha tāvadananvaye abodhakameva vākyamāpadyeta | na ca vācyaṃ paścāt prakṛtatvādupasadā saṃbadhyata
iti, yasmādavaśyāpekṣaṇīyasaṃbandha eva tāvannyāyyaḥ | itarathā hyapratipādakameva syāt |
purastādityanena tu saṃbandhe śeṣiṇā vināpi kasyacidapi padasyānupapattyasaṃbhavāt purastāditi
kālavidhānāt saumyena carantītyaviruddham | tasmādidamapi1 ‘āgnimārutadūrdhvamanū-
pājaiścaranti’
 ityanenāviśiṣṭam |

phalavadvoktahetutvāt | nanu ca kathaṃ samapradhānabhāva āśaṅkyate yāvatā sāṅgrahaṇyā
evādhikārayogāt prādhānyam | anadhikārāṇāṃ vāmanahomānāmaṅgataiveti tatrāha---sāṅgrahaṇyāṃ
tu phalavatsannidhau aphalaṃ tadaṅgamiti siddhepyanuṣaṅganivṛttyarthamidamadhikaraṇam | darśitaṃ ca |
satyaṃ niradhikāraṃ sādhikārasyāṅgaṃ; āmanahomāstu sādhikārā eva, adhikārapadasya teṣvapyanuṣaṅga-
saṃbhavāt | na ca paridhimantrā vyavadhāyakāḥ asamānajātīyatvāt | samānajātīyakaṃ hi
vyavadhāyakaṃ bhavati yathā---‘saṃte vāyurvātena gacchatāṃ ityatra gacchatāmiti śabdo’vyāhṛtena
saṃyajatrairaṅgāni’
 ityatra gacchantāmiti kriyāpadāntareṇa ‘saṃ yajñapatirāśiṣā’
 ityatra vyavadhīyate |

yuktiśca paridhintrairvyavahitatvāt nānuṣaṅga iti | vyavadadhati ca paridhimantrāḥ adhikāra-
saṃbandhāyogyatvāt | adhikārabuddhivicchedakatvameva vyavadhāyakatvam | tadyathā samānajātīyasya

tathā vijātīyasyāpi tatsavandhāyogyasya | asaṃbandhi hi vyavadadhāti tatrāpi tadbuddhayanutpā-
danāt iti |

dadhigraho naimittikaṃ | nanu nimittāsaṃyogena codyamānamidaṃ kathaṃ naimittikamityāśaṅka-
yāha---anityadoṣanirharaṇārthasaṅkīrtanāt naimittikaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | asyārthaḥ---yadyapi
nimittaṃ na śrūyate tathāpi dadhigrahasya dvārāpekṣāyāṃ devatāntarāyavaiguṇyasamādhānamevārthavādasaṅkī-
titamapi kāryaṃ yuktam | tataśca phalato naimittikatvaṃ khādiravadubhayārthatāmācāryadeśīyaḥ |
siddhāntaikadeśī tu doṣanirharaṇārthametadadṛṣṭena dvāreṇa kratūpakārārthaṃ dadhigrahaṇaṃ khādirayūpavanmanyate
kā yuktirityāha---nityatve sati jyeṣṭhaśabdopapattiriti manyate | praśasyatvājjyeṣṭhatā
bhavati | praśasyatā 2cobhayarūpayogitayā’bhyadhikārthatvāditarebhyaḥ | rāddhāntastu nimitta-
saṃyogābhāvānna doṣanirharaṇārthatayā naimittikatvaṃ śakyate kalpayitum | kimiti na
śakyaṃte ? ekagranthenāha---naimittikadoṣanirharaṇānyathānupapattyā tasyāpi naimittikatvaṃ
bhavitumarhati | na hi nityasyānityadoṣanirghātārthatā na saṃbhavati iti | tasmādasāvapyanitya
iti | satyaṃ yadyanyathā nopapadyate | arthavādatayā tūpapadyata eveti na kalpanāmarhati |

[p. 1064]

asyārthaḥ---svarūpeṇa tāvat na nimittaśravaṇābhāvānnaimittikamatra pratibhāti | na cānityakārya-
balenāpyanityatā kalpanāmarhati | nityānityasaṃyogāvirodhenānityakāryakalpanānupapatteḥ |
na hi nityaṃ kartavyamanityaṃ doṣaṃ nirhartumiti yujyate tathāpi tāvat tadeva kāryamiti kalpyeta
yadi doṣanirharaṇaśravaṇamanyathā nopapadyeta | tacca stutyarthatvenārthavādatvenāpyupapadyate iti na
prayojanakalpanāmarhati | sārūpyādyuktaṃ bhāṣye |

vaiśvānaraśca nityaḥ syāt | anityadoṣanirharaṇārthatayāpyupapadyata iti yadyatyeta
vaiśvānare’pi nityatā bhavatīti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhimānaḥ | ukhāyāmasambharaṇanimittadoṣa-
nirharaṇamarthavādatayāpyupapatasyate pūrvavaditi pūrvapakṣavādino’bhimānamātramiti abhimānaśabdena
mandatāmāha pūrvapakṣasya | rāddhāntastu na brūmo doṣanirharaṇānnaimittikatvaṃ nimittasaṃyoga
evātra śrūyate | “yo vai saṃvatsaramukhyamabhṛtvā’gniṃ cinute savaiśvānaraṃ nirvapet”
 iti
abhṛtokhyapuruṣādhikāraśravaṇāt naimittikatvamevātra sākṣācachrutam | ato nityānityasaṃyoga-
virodhābhāvāt na doṣanirharaṇārthavādatvena varṇayituṃ nyāyyam | na ca nimittaśrutirartha-
vādatayā śakyate vyākhyātum | śrutyarthe saṃbhavati nārthāntarakalpanopapadyate yataḥ |
dadhigrahe tu puruṣasaṃyogo nāstīti | nityatvadarśanamuktam |

[p. 1065]

ṣaṭ‌citiḥ pūrvavattvāt | “saṃvatsara evainaṃ pratiṣṭhāyai nudate yo’gniṃ citvā na
pratitiṣṭhati | atha ṣaṣṭhīṃ citiṃ cinute”
 ityakṛtapratiṣṭhapuruṣasaṃyogānnaimittikataiveti kathaṃ
nityatā śaṅkyate ityatrāha---ṣaṣṭhīṃ citimiti pūraṇārthapratyavāvagamānnimittaśrutirarthavādatayaiva
varṇayituṃ nyāyyeti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ | ṣaṣṭhīśabdaḥ pūraṇārthapratyayāntaḥ | tena ṣaṭ
saṅkhyā yayā pūryate cityā sā citiḥ ṣaṣṭhī | na ca pañcasu nityāsu iyaṃ naimittikī citiḥ ṣaṭsaṅkhyāṃ
pūrayati | ekāvacchedānupraveśāt dvitvādisaṅkhyotpattiḥ | tasmādiyamapi nityā | nityānāṃ
ca nimittaśrutirāñjasyenānuṣapannā kathañcit arthavādatvena varṇayitavyeti | nanu ca
nimittaśrutibalāt ṣaṣṭhīśabda eva kimityanyathā vyākhyātavyaḥ ityanenābhiprāyeṇa pṛcchati
---kuta iti ? vidheyānurodhādadhikārakalpanā nyāyyeti manyate | yathāviniyogaṃ niyoga
iti tiryagadhikaraṇe siddhatvāt | rāddhāntastu satyamevaṃ yadi virodhaḥ syāt na tvatra virodhaḥ
kasmāt ? upadeśavivakṣayāpi pūraṇarūpapratyayārthopapatteḥ 2ekāpadeśakriyānupraveśāta
ṣaṭsaṅkhyāsaṃbhavāt nāvaśyamanuṣṭhānāpekṣayaiva bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ pūraṇasūtraṃ śakyate varṇa-
yitum | yadi hyanuṣṭhānāpekṣayā saṅkhyāniveśābhiprāyeṇa sūtraṃ bhagavatā pāṇininā praṇītamiti
niścayaḥ syāt tadā upadeśāpekṣayā ṣaṣṭhīśabdo na ghaṭeta na tvenadasti | tasmādvidheyasyā-

[p. 1066]

virodhaḥ | vidheyāvirodhe ca na nimittaśrutistyaktuṃ pāryate | tasmānnaimittikatva-
siddhiḥ | darśanopanyāsaparihārāvuktau |
2pitṛyajñaḥ svakālatvāt | nanu “amāvāsyāyāmaparahṇe piṇḍapitṛyajñena caranti”

---phalakalpanāto varaṃ lakṣaṇeti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ |
 asyārthaḥ---kimadhikārā-

viśeṣāt piṇḍapitṛvajñasya | nāmāvāsyāyāṃ śrautaḥ piṇḍapitṛyajñe ca lakṣaṇeti viśeṣo’va-
gamyate | ubhayatra kālayogasya saṃbhavāt | asyārthaḥ-yadi darśopadeśapūrvakaḥ piṇḍapitṛ-
yajñopadeśaḥ syāt tadā darśasyāmāvāsyākāla1 saṃbandhāvagamāt kālaśabdaḥ pūrvāvagatasaṃbandhānu-
sāreṇa lakṣaṇayā varṇyeta | yadā punaḥ parasparanirapekṣamidamupadeśadvayaṃ tadā ubhayatrāpi pūrvāvagata-
saṃbandhābhāvānna lakṣaṇā varṇayituṃ nyāyyā | na ca kālopadeśaḥ piṇḍapitṛyajñe na saṃbhavati |
itaretarāśrayatāṣatteḥ | amāvāsyākarmāṅgatve pradhānakālatvādaṅgānāṃ amāvāsyākālaḥ prāpnotīti
nopadeśaḥ syāt | kālānupadeśake cāmāvāsyāśabde śeṣisamarpake sati amāvāsyāṅgateti |
tasmāt pūrvāparaśrutistatkālalakṣaṇetyavirodhaḥ | anena copasaṃhāravyājena paricodanāntaraṃ
nirākaroti | evaṃ hi codyate---arthavādena hi(na)paurvāparyaṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñasya darśasya ca
śrūyate | taccaikaprayogānupraveśanibandhanam | ekaprayogapraveśitvaṃ cāṅgāṅgibhāve sati | tasmāda-
ṅgataivāvabhātīti paricodanā sā evaṃ parihriyate---yadā’ṅgatvakhyāpakaṃ pramāṇaṃ nāsti tadā yā
pūrvāparaśrutiḥ sā svatantrayoreva karmaṇoḥ kālalakṣaṇāmāvāsyāyāṃ2 darśasya piṇḍapitṛyajñaḥ3
pratipadi iṣṭiriti | tasmādarthavādenāpyavirodhaḥ | prayojanamuktam | paurṇamāsyupakramatvā-
ddarśapūrṇamāsayoḥ paurṇamāsyāṃ kṛtādhānasya amāvāsyāyāmāgatāyāmapi darśo na kāryaḥ | tatra
siddhānte piṇḍapitṛyajño bhavati itarathā pradhānābhāve na kriyeta | kuṇḍapāyināmayane cānaṅga-
tvānnātidiśyate piṇḍapitṛyajñaḥ |

 iti parivyāṇaṃ tāvat dvitīyāśrutyā

raśanāpi yūpasyāṅgamaruṇādaya iva somasyeti kathaṃ paśvaṅgatāśaṅkā ? ityatrāha---atrāpi
ktvāpratyayasya kriyāntarāpekṣitvāt ekaniyogatāṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | asyārthaḥ
---‘paśumupākaroti’
 iti tāvadupākaraṇasya paśvaṅgatvaṃ pratīyate | tathā parivyāṇamapi
ktvāpratyayenopākaraṇasamānakartṛkam | samānakartṛkatā caikaprayoganibandhanā | ekaprayogatā
cāṅgāṅgibhāvenetyupākaraṇāṅgameva parivyāṇam | nanu yadyapi ktvāpratyayādaṅgāṅgitā bhavati |
tathāpi viparītaṃ kasmānna bhavatītyatrāha---tatra ca paśvarthataiva prāpnoti yūpasyāpi tadarthatvāt |
asyārthaḥ---nopākaraṇaṃ parivyāṇārthaṃ tathā hi sati yūpārthataiva sarvasya syāt | sā cāyuktā
yūṣasyāpi paśvaṅgatvāt | tasmāt paśvaṅgameva raśaneti | siddhāntavādī tu samānakartṛkatā-
mātraṃ ktvāpratyayādavasīyate na niyogaikyamiti yūpasya dvitīyātaḥ prādhānyaṃ yuktamiti
siddhāntitavān | asyārthaḥ---yathā dvitīyātaḥ prādhānyāvagamaḥ na tathā ktvāpratyayāt,
kintu vimarśāntarāpekṣa iti 1dvitīyāvaśena yūpārthaparivyāṇānupraveśinī raśanāpi yūpārthā |
nanu caikaniyogādṛte samānakartṛkataiva na saṃbhavati | dvitīyātvanīpsitārthāpi saṃbhavati |
satyaṃ na saṃbhavati kintu adhikāraikyāt siddhaṃ samāna kartṛkatvam nanu tarhi kiṃ ktvā-
pratyayena, evamucyatāṃ---‘trivṛtā yūpaṃ parivyayati’
 iti | ucyate---kālārthā ktvāśrutiḥ |

svaruścāpyekadeśatvāt | ‘yūṣasya svaruṃ karoti’
 iti śrūyate | kathaṃ yūpārthatā

 iti svarumān yūpa iti
matvarthaḥ pratīyate gomānitivat tādarthyaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | dṛṣṭo hi tādarthye sati
(1) P. 1271

[p. 1070]

matvarthaḥ | evamatrāpītyabhiprāyaḥ | asyārthaḥ---‘yūpasya svaruṃ karoti’
 iti svarostāvat
kalpayitavyaṃ tādarthyam | ‘yūpasya svaruṃ’
 ityatra svarumān yūpa iti matvarthaḥ pratīyate |
matvarthaśca prāyeṇa tādarthye sati dṛṣṭa iti tatraiva tādarthyakalpanā yuktā | tenotpattivākye
prayojanāvagamāt yūpārtha eva svaruḥ | svaruṇā yūpamanaktīti ca tasyaivāñjanena pratipattiḥ |
rāddhāntavādī tu yavamatītyevamādiṣu anaikānyāt paśumanaktīti ca dvitīyānirdeśāt
dṛṣṭārthatvācca paśvarthataiveti manyate iti vyavahitena saṃbandhaḥ | asyārthaḥ---na tāvanma-
tuvarthasya niyamaḥ yavamatītyevamādiṣu vyabhicārāt | paśumiti ca dvitīyayā añjanasyāpi
kāryāntaraśravaṇāt | añjanā(tā)rthatve ca svaroḥ kriyānirvṛttyā dṛṣṭārthatvāccāñjanārtha eva svaruḥ
ṣaṣṭhī tu utpa1ttisaṃyogepyupapannaivetyadoṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ---pratīyamāno’pi tāvanmatvarthaḥ śeṣa-
tāvyabhicārī prāyikādapi kathā nānupapannā | añjane vākyena viniyogāt | nacāñjanameva
pratipattiḥ paśuṃ ityañjanasyāpi dvitīyayā kāryāntaraśravaṇāt | añjanārthatve ca svaroḥ kriyā-
nirvṛttiśca dṛṣṭo’rthaḥ | na/?/ ca ṣaṣṭhī matvarthamapyekāntato’vagamayati utpattisaṃyogenāpi yūpārthānu---
niṣpannatayā ca ghaṭamānatvāt | prayojanamuktam |

miti sthitam | idānīmadhikāravākyagataṃ yajiśabdaṃ prati cintā kiṃ sarve yāgā adhikāra

nāmatayā vartata iti nāmadheyavicāre sthitam | ataḥ pūrvapakṣavādī yāgamātravacano’yaṃ

masmin pakṣe bhaviṣyati ? yadi sarve yāgāḥ pradhānabhūtāḥ tadā prayājānāṃ pradhānatvāt
pradhānasya ca ‘1karmaṇastvapravṛttatvāt’
 iti vikṛtiṣvatideśanirākaraṇāt | asati ca prayāje
tadguṇavidhānāsaṃbhavāt | uttaraṃ tu prayājaprāptisiddhayarthaṃ atideśādanyat pramāṇāntaraṃ
mṛgyam | ‘yatra’
 iti vacanamidamutkarṣaṇīyam---yatra prayājāḥ tatra netavyam | prāptya-
bhāvena prāptidarśanaṃ vyāmoho vā kalpanīyaḥ | karmāntarāṇi vā | nāmataśca dharma-
grahaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati | na cet prākṛtāḥ prayājāḥ2 kimiti vikṛtāveva karmāntarāṇi na
vidhīyante | prayājaśabdaśca dharmātideśako bhaviṣyati | sarvathā atideśābhāve
yā kācidgartibhaviṣyati | nyāyastāvadayamuktaḥ sarveyāgāḥ pradhānabhūtā iti |
yatpunaḥ ‘ya evaṃ vidvānamāvāsyāṃ yajate’
 ‘ya evaṃ vidvān paurṇamāsīṃ yajate’

iti tatprakṛtasamudāyadvayaṃ kimiti na parigṛhyate dvivacanayogāt | tatra kālayogyupapada-
gataṃ dvivacanaṃ nāyathārthaṃ bhavatītyuktameva | uttaraṃ---uktaṃ ca dhātvarthānusāryupapadamiti |
asyārthaḥ---dhāturayaṃ tāvat yāgamāha | na yāgasamudāyam3 | na ca yāgadvayaṃ kāladvayayogi
prakṛtam | kintu bahavaste yāgāḥ prakṛtāḥ | teṣu cāgṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt sarveṣu yajirvartate te
ca bahava iti dvivacanaṃ na ghaṭate dvivacanānupapattau satyāṃ na bhedena nāma vartate | na cobhayapadānu-
rodhī kaścidyāgo’sti iti nopapadānusārī yajiḥ | kiṃ tarhi yajyanusāryupapadamiti |
darśaśabdāccānyatvāvagateḥ darśaśabdavācyo’yaṃ amāvāsyāśabdavācyatayā na dṛśyate loke |
tenāmāvāsyākālānuvādo nāvagamyate | tenānyatvamadhikāraviṣayasyāvagamyate | tena rāyajasūya-
vadanijyaivātrāṅgamiti sthitam | atrocyate---satyamidaṃ dhātvarthānusāryupapadamiti

[p. 1073]

tathāpi samudāyadvayasya prakṛtatvāt dvivacanasya cārthāntarānupapatteḥ | saṃbhavati ca
svārthe sarūpakalpanāyā asaṃbhavāt | asyārthaḥ---dhātvarthānusāryupapadamiti satyaṃ tathāpi
samudāyadvaye ayaṃ yajirvartata iti kimiti na bhavati samudāyadvayasya prakṛtatvāt | tataśca
samudāyadvayasyaivādhikārasaṃbandhaḥ | kutaḥ ? dvivacanasyārthāntarānupapatteḥ | saṃbhavati ca dvivacane
dvivacanasarūpamiti kalpanānupapatteḥ | nanu samudāyavacano’yaṃ yajiḥ na bhavatītyuktam |
satyamuktam | samudāyāvasthāpannayāgavacano bhaviṣyati | tathā ca sati dvivacanamāñjasyena
bhaviṣyati | tadāha---dhātvarthānusāritāyāṃ cāvirodha upapadasya | syāt | kathama-
virodhaḥ ? yasmāt naivedamupapadaṃ adhātvarthe pravṛttam | kintu dhātvartha eva kiñca
dhātvarthapravṛttau satyāṃ nāmno nārthaparityāga upapadyate | kālasaṃbaddhamidamupapadam | tatra
kālayogināmeva anuvādavākye nāmatayā jñātaṃ---adhikāravākyepi teṣāmeva grahaṇe tasyārthasya
tyāgo na bhavati tasyārthaḥ---prakṛtasamudāyadvayavacanādeva upapannai nārthāntarakalpane pramāṇamasti
tadā’tiparokṣavṛttyāśrayaṇamupapadasya kṛtaṃ na bhaviṣyati | kaścāsyārthaḥ ? prakṛtasamudāyadvaya
vacanatā | evaṃ tadanusāritve upapanne nārthāntarakalpanāyāṃ pramāṇamasti | nanu ca
prakṛtayāgaparityāge nāsti pramāṇaṃ dhātoḥ | kena voktamaparityāge pramāṇamastīti |
kintupapadārthavirodhābhidhāne nāsti pramāṇamityuktamasmābhiḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati---na
prakṛtaṃ parityajyate yajeteti yajiḥ prakṛta eva vartate | kintupapadānuguṇyāt kālayogiṣu

[p. 1074]

āpannasamudāyabhāveṣu vartata iti | yacca darśaśabdaśravaṇānna prakṛtapratyabhijñā’stīti
parihṛtaṃ tat vākyaśeṣeṣvekārthatvadarśanāditi | śakyatvācca darśaśabdenāpi
tadarthābhidhānasya candrādarśanena viparītalakṣaṇayā darśaśabdena tadarthābhidhānasya
amāvāsyābhidhānasya | tasmāt sūktaṃ phalavatsannidhāvaphalaṃ tadaṅgamiti | yatpunarida-
muktaṃ kasmānnānuṣaṅgo bhavaṣyatīti codyaṃ parihṛtaṃ tat anuṣaṅgato vara-
bhitikartavyatākāṅkṣāparipūraṇamiti | anyathā hyanavasthaivākāṅkṣāyā iti | yadyavaṃ
yadītikartavyatākāṅkṣāpūrakatve saṃbhavati nānuṣaṅga | kimiti vyavāyānnānuṣajyeta iti
sāṅgrahaṇyāṃ darśitam tatrāpyayameva nyāyo yuktaḥ | vaktavyo vā viśeṣaḥ |

ayamabhidhīyate | yuktaṃ tatra sāṅgrahaṇyāṃ vikṛtitvena prakṛtivaditikartavya-
tāyāṃ sthitāyāṃ nirākāṅkṣatvāt | atideśenākāṅkṣāparipūraṇasaṃbhavāt | atastatra
vyavāyādevānuṣaṅganiṣedhaḥ | iha punaḥ aparyavasitatvāt itikartavyatārthitāyāṃ sthitā/?/

[p. 1075]

prakṛtitvāt tatpūraṇenaivaikaniyogatvaṃ na punaradhikārāntarakalpaneti sūktam | aṅgavatstu-
tayaḥ cakṣuṣī vā ityādayaḥ | darśanāni ca vikṛtiṣu prayājādīnyuktāni |

jyotiṣṭome tulyāni | nanu jyotīṃṣi stomā asyeti bahuvrīhiṇā somayāga
eva pradhāno’vagamyate | athocyeta satyaṃ stomavattā somayāgānāṃ kintu jyotiṣṭomatā nāstīti
bahuvrīhyartha evātra nopapadyate iti ananvitasvārthatvāt yajimātrasyāpi kārakasaṃbandha1 iti |
tanna vākyaśeṣe trivṛdādistotrāṇāṃ jyotiṣṭvakīrtanāt | arthavādakīrtitenāpi vyapadeśo
dṛśyate2 eva ‘āgneyo vai brāhmaṇaḥ’
 itivat | tasmāta kathamatra pūrvapakṣa ityatrāha---iha tu
dvivacanādyabhāvāt na yāgamātrapratipattiḥ śakyate vārayitum | asyārthaḥ---yathā
darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiti dvivacanānurodhānna sarvayogeṣu vṛttiḥ tatheha nāsti kiñcit | tasmānna
yāgamātre vṛttiḥ śakyate vārayitum | nanu jyotiṣṭomaprātipadikādeva vārayituṃ śakyate
tatrāha---jyotiṣṭomaśabdasya ca nāmadheyatvāt pravṛttau nimittamabhidheyaṃ nānvayītyuktaṃ
nāmadheyavicāre| asyārthaḥ---na jyotiṣṭe maśabdo yajeḥ viśeṣakaḥ kiṃ tarhi, nāmamātram |
na hi vidhīyamānasya viśeṣaṇaṃ vivakṣituṃ nyāyyam | na ca aruṇaikahāyanīnyāyaḥ saṃbhavati |
jyotiṣṭomaśabdasyārthāntarābhāvāt bhāvārthamātrasya ca niyogena sākṣātsaṃbandhāt | tasmāda-
viśiṣṭaṃ yajimātramatra pradhānaṃ ayajirevātrāpyaṅgaṃ, rājasūyavat nāmāpi tadvat | atrocyate
(1) G. sya kārakasambandhaḥ (2) G. gṛhyate

[p. 1076]

---yadi nāmatvādananvayitvamuktamasmābhiḥ syādetadevaṃ, yadi yadyannāma tattat kāryānvayi bhavatī-
tyuktamasmābhiḥ tadā bhavedetadevaṃ, tatra punarananvayādevānanvayitvamuktam | na hi kathaścit
udbhidādayaḥ kārye’nvayavantaḥ | tena kārye’nanvayinaḥ uktāḥ, na satyanvaye nāmatvādananva-
yitvam | iha tvananvayo’vagamyamāno yajiviśeṣopasthāpakatayā na nāmatayā vārayituṃ
śakyate | nanu ca nāmataivātra hrīyate viśeṣaṇapadamevaitat tadā bhavati | tacca na-
yuktamiti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ naśchinnam tathāpi rāddhāntassiddha eva | etaccābhyupagamyavāda-
mātram | kintu viśeṣaṇapadamapyetannāmaiva, arthāntarānupapatteḥ | yajyarthasya viśeṣa-
mātrāvagamahetutvāt | ato’nvitamapi viśeṣaṇamātraniṣṭhamiti nāmaiva arthāntarānupādā-
nāt | asyārthaḥ---nedaṃ nīlādivadviśeṣaṇaṃ, kintu etannāmānuguṇyena yajireva
viśiṣṭe yāge vartate | yadi hi yāgamātre varteta tadā nāmno [’rthānvayaśca yāge syāt |
tasya ca somayāgavacanatvāt | tenaitadanuroddhaṃ viśiṣṭe yāge yajireva vartate]
’tiparokṣā vṛttirāśrayaṇīyā tenaitadvirodhādviśiṣṭe yāge vartate yajireva | jyotiṣṭo-
mapadamapi tatraiva nāmatayā vartate arthāntarānupasthāpakatvāt | nanvevaṃ vaiśvadevena
yajetetyatrāpi kimiti vaiśvadevapadānurodhāt yajiḥ vaiśvadevyāmikṣāsādhyeyāga eva na vartate |
tataśca vaiśvadevapadamapi tasyaiva nāmāstu na sarveṣām |

[p. 1077]

atrocyate---1dvividhaṃ hi nāma, kiñcit pravṛttaṃ, kiñcicca pravartyaṃ, yatra vākyāntarālo-
canayā yāge pravṛttiravadhāritā, tatpravṛttaṃ nāma | yathā darśapūrṇamāsau iti | kālavacanayo-
rapyanayoḥ anutpādāt āgneyādivacanatā | tathā jyotiṣṭomaśabdo’pi pravṛttanāma |
arthavādālocanayā yāge pravṛttyavagateḥ | pravṛttena ca nāmnā vākyāntareṇa yajirviśeṣe’vasthāpyate
tannirapekṣanāmatvāvagateḥ | rājasūyaśabdastu yadyapi rājā somaḥ sūyate’sminniti vyutpattyā
somābhiṣavasaṃbandhāt somayāge pravṛttanāma tathāpi na yajiṃ viśeṣe’vasthāpayitumalam, svārājya-
phalatvātkarmaṇaḥ | rājā sūyate asmāditi vyutpattyantasaṃbhavāt | yasya nānyato yāge pravṛtti-
ravagatā, kintu svavākyagatayajisāmānādhikaraṇyenaiva kalpyā tat pravartyaṃ nāma | yathā
vaiśvadevaśabdaḥ | sa hi sūktahaviṣoriti niyamāt sūktahaviṣoreva vartate na yāge | tena
vaiśvadevena yajeteti yajisāmānādhikaraṇyāt asya nāmatā kalpyā | tataśca pūrvaprakṛtayāgavartinaṃ
yajiṃ na viśeṣe’vasthāpayitumalamiti siddham | anuvādavākyagataśca yajireva yathotpattikāla-
yogiṣveva tava vartate teṣāmevāmāvāsyāpaurṇamāsīśabdau nāmanī tathā paurṇamāsyadhikaraṇe
uktamiti | iti nāmacatuṣṭayavivekaḥ |

caturthaḥ pādaḥ
(1) G. atrocyate---vaiśvadevaśabdo’pi yajisāmānādhikaraṇyāt
prayujyate | yajiśca prākṛteṣu sarveṣu yāgeṣu | na cāsauveśvadeva-
śabdena viśeṣe’vasthāpayituṃ śakyate | api tu sarveṣu vṛttisaṃbhavāt
viśvadevaśabdena yāge vaiśvadevaśabdo vartate viśveṣāṃ devānāmiti | na ca
saṃbandhaḥ viśvairdevaiḥ vihitairvā syāt vidhīyamānairvā | tatra vidhīyamānatā
tāvat na saṃbhavati āmikṣāyā vihitatvāt | anyatra cāśakyatvāt |
avidhīyamānatayā tu sarveṣāmapi vyapadeśo ghaṭate daṇḍivat yathaikapuruṣavarti-
nāpi daṇḍena sarva eva daṇḍhino’bhidhīyante | tena vyapadeśamātrasidhyarthe
vyapadeśānna vidhirāśrayaṇīyaḥ | vyapadeśaścedupapanno yajiṃ saṅkoca-
yitumarhati |

[p. 1078]

nanvevamatrāpi jyotiṣṭomaśabdo’pi somayāgasāhacaryāt yāgāntare varteta |
ucyate---adhikāravākye sāṅgo yāgo vidhīyate | nānūdyate | tena jyoti-
ṣṭomaśabdavācyayāgaviṣayo niyogaḥ pratīyate | na ca svārthatayā sarvān
yāgān gṛhṇāti | gṛhītānāṃ ca kathaṃ syādanvaya iti cintāyāṃ na sarve yāgāḥ
karaṇatayā’nvayaṃ yānti nāmno’tiparokṣapravṛttyāpatteḥ | tena somayāgā
eva sarve karaṇatayā’nvayaṃ yānti | tadā na ca nāmno’tiparokṣavṛttiprasaṅgaḥ |
tena somayāgasyeva prādhānyaṃ vaiśvadevenetītyanuvādaḥ | sa ca prakṛtāpekṣī |
prakṛtāpekṣitve ca sarveṣāmanuvādaḥ | nanvevaṃ ya evaṃ vidvān paurṇamāsīṃ yajate
ityanuvādaḥ syāt nākālasaṃbandhanibandhanenānuvādaḥ tathā tatraivoktam |

nibandhanameva nāmeti |

atha ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

pañcamādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ

śrutilakṣaṇamānupūrvyaṃ | atra bhāṣyakāreṇa prayojakāprayojakalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamityuktam |
tadākṣipati---nanu kratvarthapuruṣārthacintāpi tatra vṛttā | kimidaṃ prayojakāprayojakalakṣaṇaṃ
vṛttamiti | asyārthaḥ---yadi tāvat saṃkṣepeṇādhyāyārthaḥ kīrtyate tadevaṃ kīrtanīyam |
aupadānikaṃ vastuvṛttamiti | atha vistareṇa tataḥ kratvarthapuruṣārthāṅgapradhānaphalavidhyarthavādā
api kīrtanīyāḥ | kimiti prayojakāprayojakameva kīrtyate | teṣāmapi caturthe cinti-
tatvāditi | ucyate---kramacintāyāmasyaivopayogāt | prayojakāprayojakalakṣaṇameva
kramacintāyāmupayogi yasmāt tasmāttadeva kīrtyate nāparam anupayogitvāt | vakṣyamāṇaupa-
yikaṃ hi vṛttaṃ kīrtyate | yasya hi vakṣyamāṇamarthaṃ pratyupāyabhāvaḥ tadevātra kīrtyate | na
vṛttatayā vṛttamityetāvatā na vṛttaṃ saṅkīrtanīyam | kathamupāyatvaṃ kramacintāyāṃ prayojakā-
prayojakalakṣaṇasyetyāha---ekaprayogaprayuktānāṃ hi kramo bhavati nāprayuktānāmiti prayojakā-
prayojakasaṅkīrtanam | ekena prayogeṇa prayogavidhinā prayojyānāṃ svasidhyarthamākṣiptānuṣṭhānā-
nāṃ padārthānāṃ anuṣṭhānavitānarūpaḥ kramo bhavati | nāprayuktānām | tena prayojakatve jñāte
(1) P. 1284

[p. 1080]

kramajñānamutpadyate anuṣṭhānavaitatyena ca prayogaparyantaprayojakatvaṃ nirvahatīti parasparāpekṣitayā’sti
saṃbandha iti yuktaṃ prayojakāprayojakasaṅkīrtanam | yadi prayuktānāṃ kramaścintyate avidheya-
starhi kramaḥ | yadi prayuktānāṃ satāmuttarakālaṃ kramaścintyate avidheyastarhi kramaḥ | tataśca
pratītimātreṇa kramasvīkāre pratītānāṃ tadāśaṅkā na yukteti bhāvaḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ prayuktyuttara-
kālabhāvitvenāvidheyatvamityatrāha---vihitaṃ hi prayujyate nāprayuktasya vidhānamiti |
asyārthaḥ---tat vidheyamityucyate yadvidhinā svasiddhayarthamupādīyate | tathā coktaṃ śyenagranthe
---“yattu siddhayarthamupādīyate tadvidheyamiti tantre vyavahāraḥ”
 iti | tena vidheyatvaṃ prayuktatvena
vyāptam | taddarśayati---yadvihitaṃ tatprayujyate | yatpunarna prayujyate tadvidheyamapi na bhavati iti
vyāpakabhūtaprayuktinivṛttau vyāpyabhūtasya vidheyatvasya nivṛttiriti | pariharati
---kena voktaṃ kramo vidhīyata iti | nanu yadyavidheyaḥ kimiti tarhi cintyate vidheyatve
hi kramasya siddhānte kiṃ kramo vidhīyate na veti cintā yuktetyāśaṅkya cintāviṣayaṃ svarūpaṃ
cāha---na vihitavitānamevātra cintyate | kiṃ yathā pratipannavihitavitānamanuṣṭheyaṃ
utāniyameneti | vihitā ye padārthāsteṣāṃ prayogāvasthāyāṃ yo’yaṃ vitānaḥ sa evātra cintyate |
yo’yaṃ śrutyādibhiḥ pratipanno vihitavitānaḥ kiṃ tamananikramyānuṣṭheyaṃ anuṣṭhānaṃ kartavya utāniya-
meneti | pratipannaścāsau vihitavitānaśceti karmadhārayaṃ kṛtvā paścādyathāśabdena sahāvyayī-
bhāvaḥ | evaṃ ca vadatā asminnadhikaraṇe sarva eva śrutyādyavagamyaḥ kramaḥ kimādaraṇīyaḥ
uta neti cintyata iti pradarśitam | pradarśanamātratayā tu śrutikrama udāhṛtaḥ | kathaṃ punari-
tyatrāyamāśayaḥ---pratītamātreṇa kramādare pratītānādarāśaṅkā’nupapannā | 1vidheyatayā svīkāre tu
(1) G. vidheyayoḥ

[p. 1081]

pratītasyāpyavidheyatā yukteti | pariharati---viniyogaśūnyatvāt vihitavitānāvagamasya |
yathāpratipatti anuṣṭhānamāśaṅkyate iti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ | asyārthaḥ---vitānābhidhāyakaḥ
śabdaḥ kaścinnāsti | yaśca nābhidhīyate nāsau viniyujyate tārtīyaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ | nāpi
caikatvādivat viniyogenāpyupādānaśeṣī kriyate | tena tārtīyacāturthikaviniyogarahitatvāt
vitānapratyayasyādaraṇīyatā nāsti | avidheyatve’pi kramasyeyameva yuktiḥ abhidhānaśūnya
tayā1 viniyogābhāvenānaṅgatvāt | vidhinā ca svasiddhayarthatvenāprayukteriti | nāntarīyako
hi vidheyāvagatau vitānapratyayaḥ bahuṣu padārtheṣu ekena kartrā prayujyamāneṣu nāntarīyako’yaṃ
vitāna(vat prayogaḥ prayoga) pratyayo nābhidhānika ityarthaḥ | upasaṃharati---yaścārthādartho na sa
codanārtha iti manvānaḥ pūrvapakṣavādī aniyamaṃ manyate | tena pratītamātreṇāpi kramasvīkāre
anādarāśaṅkā upapadyata eveti samarthitam | nanu ca ‘adhvaryurgṛhapatiṃ dīkṣayitvā’
 ityatra
kramavidhānamevopanyastaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa | dīkṣāṇāṃ prāptatvāt ityanena granthena vidheyāntarā-
bhāvāt kramavidhānārthaiva śrutiriti vadatā | tenāvidheyatvābhidhānaṃ bhāṣyaviruddhamiti
vadati | idānīṃ pariharati---yadyevaṃ yadi vidheyāntarābhāvena kramavidhānamayuktaṃ tarhi yasmāda-
styevātra vidheyāntaraṃ anenāmī dīkṣitavyā iti kartṛviśeṣavidhānām | adhvaryvādi-
dīkṣāsu pratiprasthātrādikartṛviśeṣavidhānamapyatra pratīyate | nanu ‘adhvaryurgṛhapatiṃ’
 ityatra tarhi
(1) G. tayāpi

[p. 1082]

vidheyāntaraṃ nāsti adhvaryoścodakata eva prāptatvāt, atrāha---adhvaryuvidhāvapi niyamaḥ ye
yajamānāsta ṛtvija ityanena vākyena yajamānā evārtvijeṣu viniyuktāḥ | tenādhvaryustāva-
nnivartitaḥ | kintu kenacidyajamānena kecidyajamānā dīkṣayitavyāḥ | tatra ṛtvikkarma-
karatayā te yajamānā eva tena tenartvigviśeṣavācinā adhvaryvādiśabdenocyante | tatra
yenaiva kiñcidādhvaryavaṃ kṛtavatā adhvaryuvyapadeśo labdhaḥ tenaivāparamapyādhvaryavaṃ kartavyamiti nāsti
niyāmakaṃ pramāṇaṃ ata āha---adhvaryuvidhāvapi niyama iti | tena pratiprasthātrādi-
vidhirapi niyamārtha eveti mantavyam | kiṃ heyamevedaṃ bhāṣyaṃ ? yadi dīkṣāṇāṃ prāptatvena
kramavidhānārthaiva śrutirityāha---tadbhāṣyaṃ kiṃ heyameva | neti vadāmaḥ | yadidaṃ ktveti
paurvāparyamabhidhāne gamyate kartṛvidhau tadanuṣṭhānaupayikatayaiva kartṛvidhāvupayujyate na
saṃbandhividhāviti tanniṣṭhā śrutirityuktam | asyārthaḥ---na ktvāpratyayastāvat anuṣṭhānagataṃ
paurvāparyamāha | kintu samānakartṛkatāmeva | prayogagatāṃ tu pūrvakālatāṃ upādhitvena
svīkaroti | tena ktvāntaṃ pūrvaṃ prayoktavyaṃ; yacca pūrvaṃ prayujyate tadarthaḥ pūrvamavagamyate |
yacca pūrvamavagamyate tatpūrvamanuṣṭhīyate aparaṃ ca paścāt | tena prayogavitativiśeṣo’vagamyate
teva ktvāśrutibalena vitānāvagateḥ śrauto’yaṃ kramaḥ | atha kasmāt pāthakrameṇāyaṃ kramaniyamo
na bhavatīti cenna | dīkṣāṇāṃ codakataḥ prāptatvāt vākyasya ca kartṛviśeṣavidhānārthatvāt
yadi dīkṣaiva yāvadvidhīyate tadotpattivākyekrameṇānuṣṭhānamapi bhavet | yenaiva krameṇotpatti-
vākyānyuccāryante tenaiva tadarthāvagatiḥ avagatikrameṇa cānuṣṭhāne kramo’pi bhavati, avagati-
nibandhanatvādanuṣṭhānakramasya | codakaprāptā tu dīkṣā na vākyenāvagamyate iti na tatra
pāthakrameṇa kramāvagatiḥ kiṃ tarhi ktvāśrutita eveti | akṣarayojanātu---yadidaṃ ktveti
śruterupādhibhūtaṃ paurvāparyamabhidhāne śabdaprayoge’vagamyate kartṛvidhau tat kartṛvidhāvanuṣṭhānaupayika-

[p. 1083]

tayaivopayujyate vitativiśeṣāvagamaṃ vinānuṣṭhānābhāvāt | anuṣṭhānaupayikakramāvagamahetutvādanu-
ṣṭhānaupayikaṃ paurvāparyam | kartṛvidhānapakṣe tu dīkṣāṇāmavidheyatvānna pāthataḥ kramaḥ api tu
śruteḥ | saṃbandhividhau tu kramasaṃbandhidīkṣāvidhau tu pāthata evānuṣṭhāne’pi kramāvagateḥ nānu-
ṣṭhānaupayikatayopayujyate | kintu śabdasādhutāmātreṇeti hetostanniṣṭhā kramaniṣṭhāśrutirityuktaṃ
bhāṣyakāreṇa | tena bhāṣyākṣarāṇyevaṃ yojyāni | dīkṣāṇāṃ prāptatvādiha vākye tāsāma-
vidheyatvāt pāthataḥ kramānavagateḥ ktvāśrutireva kramavidhānārthā kramāvagatyartheti yāvat |
vidhānavācoyuktistu gauṇī vidheyavatkrame’pi tātparyaśruteḥ iti darśayitum | tena śrutilakṣaṇamānu-
pūvyamiti sūtraṃ adhvaryurbrahmāṇaṃ ityatra śrauta eva kramo na pāthakrama iti darśayituṃ sūtrakāreṇa
praṇītamiti darśitaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa | tenāye vicāraḥ---kiṃ ‘adhvaryurityādivākye śrutikramo’yaṃ
uta pāthakrama iti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī avadānavākyavat pāthakramo’yamityāha | rāddhāntavādī
tu codakata eva dīkṣāṇāṃ prāptatvāt utpattau nāsti dīkṣāṇāṃ pāthakrama iti nāyaṃ pāthakramaḥ |
kintu śrutikrama evāyamiti | cintāprayojanaṃ tu yatra codakataḥ prāpteṣu anyapare vākye
śrutirnāsti tatra pāthata eva kramo nādaraṇīyaḥ iti | evaṃ ca varṇakadvayamatra | prathamavarṇake
sarvakramādarānādaravicāraḥ | dvitīye tu kimayaṃ śrautakramaḥ uta pāthakrama iti vicāraḥ | ayaṃ
ca tṛtīyaḥ punaruktiparihāraḥ | dvitīyetu punaruktyāśaṅkāparihāre kramādarānādaravicāreṇa
ekameva varṇakamiti mantavyam | prathamastu parihāraḥ paramatenoktaḥ | ata evāparitoṣeṇa
parihārāntaramuktam | tṛtīyaparihārastu viṣayavyāptyartha iti | kasmāt punaḥ kramasya
vidheyatvameva paramārthato nāṅgīkriyate yena vidheya iva tanniṣṭhatāmātreṇa gauṇo vidheyavyapadeśo
varṇyata ityatrāha---aprayuktasyāvidheyatvameva heturuktaḥ | asyārthaḥ---yadi hyaprayuktasyāpi
vidheyatvaṃ syāt tadā kramo’pi paramārthata eva vidheyaḥ syāt na tvidamastīti | yadi
śrutyavagatapaurvāparyabalāyātaḥ kramaḥ śrauto bhavati evaṃ tarhi hṛdayasyāgre’vadyatyatha jihvāyā atha
vakṣasa ityetadapyudāharaṇamatra syāt | atrāpyagrāthaśabdābhyāṃ paurvāparyabalena kramāvagateḥ |

[p. 1084]

neti brūmaḥ | naitadudāharaṇamiti brūmaḥ pāṭhādavagatasyāyamathaśabdo’nuvādaḥ | idaṃ vākya-
mavadānavidhāyakaṃ tenotpattivākyena kramāvadhāraṇaṃ pāthata eva | kartṛviśeṣavidhau punartānyataḥ
kramo’vagantuṃ śakyate’nyadataḥ ktvāpratyayāt | kartṛvidhirayaṃ na dīkṣāvidhiḥ | tena
dīkṣāṇāṃ nāyamutpattiścodanālakṣaṇa iti nānyataḥ pāṭhādayaṃ kramo’vagantuṃ śakyate | ataḥ ktvā-
pratyayādvinā’nyaḥ śabdo’pi [vitā]nārthe vartata iti sūktaṃ prayojakāprayojakalakṣaṇasaṅkīrtanam |
saṃprati pūrvapakṣamāha---kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ nāntarīyako’yaṃ niyama ādriyate | na hyacodanā-
lakṣaṇasya codanālakṣaṇaniyoganirvṛttāvaṅgatopapadyate | yatkhalu acodanālakṣaṇaṃ na tasya
codanālakṣaṇaniyoganirvṛttāvaṅgatopapadyate | taduktaṃ---‘na cāvihitamaṅgaṃ bhavati” iti |
yaccāṅgaṃ na tanniyamenādaraṇīyam | siddhāntamāha-satyaṃ nopapadyate | satyamaṅgatā nopapadyate
yata evāṅgaṃ kramo na bhavati ata eva ca nānuṣṭhīyate | nāpyupādīyate padārthāstvanuṣṭhīyante
na kramaḥ, kintu anuṣṭhīyamāneṣu bahuṣu padārtheṣu ekakartṛkeṣvarthāt kramo vitatirūpa āpatati |
yadyanaṅgaṃ kramaḥ kimiti tarhi tanniyamādaraṇaṃ tatrāha---kintu arthaprāptaṃ kramāntaraṃ niruṇaddhi |
tasyānavagamādityucyate śrutyādibhiḥ | krame hyavagate kramāntarāvagatireva nodeti | yathā-
vagati cānuṣṭhānaṃ buddhipūrvakāriṇāmiti kramaniyamānādaraḥ | ato’napekṣatvamivāvidhīya-
mānamapyupakārāyaiva bhavatīti na doṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ---yathā’napekṣatvaṃ na vidhīyate
1tathānuṣṭhānopakāri bhavati tathā yadyapi kramo na vidhīyate tathāpyanuṣṭhānopayogi bhavati kramarahita-
(1) G. athavā’nuṣṭhānopakārā bhavanti

[p. 1085]

syānuṣṭhānāsaṃbhavāditi na doṣaḥ | punaruktyādyāśaṅkā tūktā | prathamaṃ punaruktatāśaṅkā |
punaścāptasmaraṇe prayojane darśite vṛttikāreṇa tatkāryamityāśaṅkā darśitā bhāṣye uktā |

arthācca | evaṃ tāvadananyaparā(rthā)kṣaratayā niyamitaḥ kramaḥ idānīṃ kāryānu-
rodhena kramo’pi niyamyate tatparatvāt padārthānuṣṭhānasya | pūrvaṃ tāvat ktvākṣarāṇāmananya-
paratayā kramaniyamamātraparatvāt kramaniyamo2varṇitaḥ | adhunā yadyapi kramaparāṇyakṣarāṇi na
santi tathāpi kāryānurodhena kramo niyamyate | yena kālayogena kāryaṃ saṃpadyate tena tāvat
paurvāparyamāśrayaṇīyam | tadvaśena yā vitatiḥ saivādaraṇīyā kāryaparatvātpadārthānuṣṭhānasya, kāryaṃ
nirvartsyatīti padārthā anuṣṭhīyante | tena tadanurodhenaiva 3kālaviśeṣaparigrahaḥ tannāntarīyakaśca
kramo’pyāśrayaṇīyaḥ | atra pūrvapakṣāśaṅketyantamuktvā anananyaparākṣatarayā śrutyā kramāvagamaḥ,
arthe tu na kiñcit avagatau pramāṇamasti prayojanamātratvāt prayojanasya | rāddhāntastu
prayojanamapi bhavati anuṣṭhānakālabhedāvagamadvāreṇa kramāvagatau kāraṇamiti---tatra ca pradarśitānyu-
dāharaṇāni | kiṃ pāthakramasyāyaṃ bādhaḥ | ‘agnihotraṃ juhoti’
 ‘yavāgūṃ pacati’
 iti

iti bruvan bhāṣyakāro bādhamiva darśayati | taccāyuktam | kramakopādhikaraṇena punaruktatvā-
dityāśayaḥ | neti brūmaḥ idamapi kramaniyamahetukāraṇaṃ na kasyacidvirodhitayā pradarśyate |

[p. 1086]

idamapyarthākhyaṃ kramaniyamasya yo hetuḥ kālaviśeṣaḥ tasya kāraṇaṃ pradarśyate | na kasya
cidvirodhitayā | balābalacantāyāṃ tu virodhe balīyastvaṃ ca pradarśyate kramakopīdhi-
karaṇe | iha punaḥ arthākhyapramāṇasvarūpapratipattyarthamevedaṃ vacanamupanyastam |

aniyamo’nyatra | yatra kramaniyamapramāṇamuktaṃ śrutyarthātmakaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ ca
pāṭhādikaṃ nāsti tatrāniyama eva | anuṣṭhānasvabhāvena kramamātrāvagateḥ | kimudāharaṇaṃ
tatrāha---yathā śākhāntarīyāṅgavidhānaṃ śākhāntara upasaṃhriyamāṇaṃ, tatra hi pāthato’pi
kramaniyamo nāstīti pratyudāharaṇamātramidam |

krameṇa vā niyamyeta | yatra punastātparyamakṣarāṇāṃ nāsti, yathā ‘adhvaryuḥ, ityādau;
na ca kāryato virodhaḥ yathā yavāgūpāke yathāpāthamanuṣṭhīyamāne | tatra kā kathā
kramaniyamasya | yathā ‘samidho yajati, tanūnapātaṃ yajati’
 iti | uttaraṃ---tatrāpi

[p. 1087]

pāṭhānniyamaḥ yatkramaḥ pāthaḥ prayogo’pi tatkramaka eva bhavati | nanu ca pāthakramāvagati-
rnimūlā | na tasyāḥ kiñcinnimittamasti | 1padānāṃ 2vākyārthāvagatihetutvāt
yadi pratyastamitapadāvibhāgadvākyādeva vākyārthaḥ pratīyate tadā vācakapadābhāve’pi vākyādeveya-
marthāvagatiḥ ityavadhāryeta | na caitadasti | nirmūlā cāvagatirna pramāṇam | pūrvo dhāvatītyatra
(parānvayaikye)parānvayāvagatirivapūrvasaṃbandhamūlā cāparāvagatiḥ syāt, nānvitāvagatiḥ |
tasmādyatheṣṭamatra kramaparigrahaṃ nyāyyaṃ manyate | maivaṃ vocaḥ | vākyakrama iva vākyārtha-
kramo’pi śabdanibandhana eva | yādṛśo vākyakramaḥ tādṛśo vākyārthakramaḥ so’pi śabda-
nibandhana eva | yathā na śabdādṛte śabdakramo’vagamyate na śrutyā vinā śrutikramo’vagamyata
ityarthaḥ | arthādṛte nārthakramaḥ | yasmiṃśca satyeva yadavagamyate avyabhicārāt tasya
tannimittamiti vadāmaḥ | nanu ca padānāṃ vākyārthapratipattinimittatvāt pādabhāve
kramāvagatiriyamudīyamānāpi pratibhā, na pramāṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | ucyate---yathā’tra śabdasya
nimittatā tathocyate | yattāvat prathamaṃ paṭhyate tattāvat prathamamavagamyate tathā
prayogakāle prathamaṃ smaryate yathāsmaraṇamanuṣṭhanamiti tasyānuṣṭhānamapi prathamamavagamyate |
tadanantarāvagatasya (smṛtasya) tadanantaramevānuṣṭhānam | evaṃ ca prayogavitatiravagamyata
iti | yata eva pāthakrameṇānuṣṭhānakramo’vagamyate | ata eva ca niyatakramamadhyayanaṃ
(1) R. padārthānāṃ (2) G. vākyāvagati

[p. 1088]

dṛṣṭārthameva bhavati | anyatra kāryaviśeṣāt yatra kāryaviśeṣo’vagamyate yathā yāvagūpāke
tatra hi niyatakramamadhyayanaṃ dṛṣṭārthaṃ na bhavati | yathaiva pāṭhādapi kramāvagatirbhavati
ata eva ca lokikāḥ pāthakrameṇa dhūpādiṣu krame’vagate vyutkramābhidhāne pratiṣedhaṃ
kurvate | adṛṣṭārtheṣvapi taddarśitam | dṛṣṭārtheṣu hi prayojanavaśenāpi kramo’vagamyate | adṛ-
ṣṭārtheṣvapi tu yo’yaṃ laukikānāṃ kramādaraḥ saḥ pāthakrameṇānuṣṭhānakramo’vagamyata ityetannibandhanaḥ |
etaddarśitaṃ bhāṣye | upasaṃharati-tasmādatrāpi na kramāntarakalpanā yukteti yuktam | yata eva
avagati nimittamātrasaṃbhavenāvagatimātreṇa kramasvīkāraḥ ata eva ‘adhvayurgṛhapatiṃ dīkṣayitvā’

ityevamādiṣu tātparyamātrāt kramasiddhiḥ | ktvākṣaratāptaryamātreṇa kramasiddhiḥ | mātra-
grahaṇena ktvāpratyayenāvidhānamanabhidhānaṃ cāsya kramasya darśitam | anabhidheye ca tātparyamaviruddhaṃ
lakṣaṇāyāmiva | kasmātpunavidheyatvābhāvepi tātparyamātreṇa kramasiddhiḥ tatrāha---nimittamātraṃ hi
atra parigṛhyate na punarvidhayaparābhidhānameva pratipattau hetuḥ | avagatimātreṇa svīkāro yasya
tasyāvagatihetumātraṃ parigṛhyate na punarvidheya eva yo’vagatihetuḥ avagatimātreṇa kramasvīkāra
ityuktamupasaṃharati---tasmāt sanimittā pratipattiḥ1 pramāṇamityuktaṃ pramāṇaparīkṣāyām |
ato yāvatī sanimittā pratītiḥ sā sarvaiva kramāvadhāraṇe anyatra vā vedārthānuṣṭhāno---
pakāriṇī pramāṇamiti mantavyam | darśanamuktam |

(1) G. pratītiḥ

[p. 1089]

pravṛttyā tulyakālānām | kimaparaṃ kramaniyame kāraṇamiti | praśnenaivopakramaḥ |
uttaraṃ---pravṛttiriti brūmaḥ pravṛttyāpi hi kramabuddhirupajāyate | pravṛttiranuṣṭhānaṃ tenāpyapara-
smin padārthe’nuṣṭheyarūpakramabuddhirupajāyate | yatkramakaṃ pūrvapadārthānuṣṭhānaṃ tatkramakamevottarapadārthānu-
ṣṭhānamapi pratīyata iti | kathaṃ punarvaidikatvaṃ pravṛttikramasya | nāyaṃ vedādavagamyat iti
bhāvaḥ | uttaraṃ---tadarthāśrayāt | tadarthaṃ vedārthamāśrayati yato vedādanuṣṭhānam prathama-
padārthasaṃbandhyavagamyate | tataśca dvitīyādipadārthakrama iti pāramparyeṇāsti vaidikatvam |
tasmātpravṛttirapi niyamahetubhūtā | yatpunaridaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇoktaṃ ‘evaṃ pradhānapratyāsatti-
rbhavati’
 iti tathā sauryādibhiśca sāmyamāśaṅkya punaḥ vaiṣamyābhidhānaṃ kastasyopayogaḥ?
ekagranthenāha---nātīva kramāvadhāraṇa upayujyata iti | yadā hi pūrvapravṛttikrameṇottara-
padārthakramo’vagamyate tadā pradhānapratyāsattyupanyāso na yuktaḥ | tathā yadaparamuktaṃ “yathā
sauryādiṣu kṛtsnaṃ guṇakāṇḍaṃ pradhānapratyāsattaye pratipradhānamapavṛjyate tathā prājāpatyeṣvapi syāt”

iti sāmyamupanyasya punarvaiṣamyamuktaṃ “ekapayogacodanācoditatvāt prājāpatyānāmaṅgapradhānayaugapadye
prāpte 2puruṣāśaktyā yuktaḥ kramānupraveśaḥ | sauryādīnāṃ tu ekaprayogacodanānupraveśābhāvāt
sahacodanā nāstīti ekaikasya kṛtsnaṃ guṇakāṇḍaṃ apavṛjyeta3 iti vaiṣamyamuktaṃ tadapi nopayogi |
astyatropayogaḥ, pravṛttirnāma kramanirapekṣa kvacidanuṣṭhānaṃ, nirapekṣaṃ cedamanuṣṭhānaṃ pravṛttaṃ na

[p. 1090]

punarapekṣāyāṃ prayojanamastīti manvānasyedamuttaraṃ---pradhānapratyāsattiḥ sahacodanā’vagatā
vaiṣamyāt pīḍitā syāt | sāmye punaḥ yathābhyanujñātaiva bhavatīti | asyārthaḥ-prathama-
pravṛttivaśena dvitīyādipadārthānuṣṭhānakramāvagamo niyamyata iti etāvati pradarśite iyamāśaṅkā-
’vatarati---pravṛttyavasthāyāṃ hi yathā prathamapadārthānuṣṭhānaṃ kramaniyamanirapekṣamavagamyate tathā
dvitīyādipadārthānuṣṭhānamapi | yathāvagati cānuṣṭhānaṃ sarveṣāmapi kramaniyamanirapekṣaṃ yuktam |
punardvitīyādipadārthānuṣṭhā[ne]kramaniyamāpekṣāyāṃ tu prayojanaṃ nāsti aniyatakramatayā prathama-
pravṛttiryā tathaivānuṣṭhānukramasiddhiriti na śakyate prathamapadārthapravṛttikrameṇottarapadārthakramāvagamaḥ
kartumitīmāmāśaṅkāmapaninīṣuḥ bhāṣyakāra uttaraṃ likhitavān prayogacodanayā sahāṅgaiḥ pradhānānāṃ
yaugapadyenānuṣṭhānasya coditatvāt puruṣaśaktyā yāvadvayavadhānamanupraviśati tāvadeva vidhiranu-
manyate nānyat | tatra yadi prathamapadārthapravṛttikrameṇottareṣāmapi padārthānāmanuṣṭhānaṃ bhavati
tadā sarveṣāmapi sadṛśameva vyavadhānam | itarathā kasyacidatisannidhānaṃ kasyacidativyavadhānamiti
vaiṣamye sati prayogacodanāvagate sannidhānavyavadhāne ubhe api bādhite syātām | anavagata-
sannidhānāśrayaṇepi hi yathāvagatasannidhānabādho bhavatyeveti prayogacodanānupālanāya punaḥ
kramaniyamāpekṣāstīti | prāvṛttikeca krame svīkriyamāṇe tadanupālanamiti tatsiddhayarthaṃ
prayogacodanāvagatapradhānapratyāsattisiddhayarthaṃ prāvṛttikakramaniyamo’bhyupagantavyaḥ | nanvevaṃ
sati prayogacodanāvagatapradhānapratyāsattinibandhano’yaṃ kramaniyama iti na prāvṛttiko vyapadeṣṭuṃ
yuktaḥ, tatrāha---sa ca niyamaḥ prathamapravṛttipūrvaka iti tayaiva vyākhyāte | sauryādidarśana-
midānīṃ kimarthaṃ ? ekaprayogānupraveśābhāve na yuktaṃ kṛtsnaṃ guṇakāṇḍāpavarjanaṃ sauryādiṣu |

[p. 1091]

prājāpatyeṣu tu ekaprayogacodanācoditatvāt yuktamekaikapadārthāpavarjanamiti spaṣṭatva dvaiṣa/?/sya
sauryādidarśanaṃ pūrvapakṣoktaṃ kimarthamiti | asyāpyasti prayojanaṃ idamapyāśaṅkānirākaraṇāyaivopa-
nyastamiti kā punarāśaṅkā ? tāmāha---yadi kramāvagatimātratayaiva krama ādriyate
sauryādiṣu kaḥ pradveṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ---sauryādiṣvapi bahuṣu pradhāneṣu ekaikasya padārthasya
sakalapradhānasaṃbandhitayā yugapadanuṣṭhānāśakteravaśyaṃbhāvini krame dvitīyādiṣvapi padārtheṣu
tathaiva kramo’vagamyate | avagatimātreṇa kramasyādara iti tasyāpi prāvṛttikaḥ kramaḥ prāpnotīti |
atha pradhānasya sāṅgasya vihitatvāt tatra sauryādiṣu kṛtsnameva guṇakāṇḍamapavṛjyeta naikaikaḥ
padārtha itīhāpi tathā syāt | athātra ekaikasyāpavarjanaṃ tatrāpi tathā syāditi sauryādisāmyā-
śaṅkā | saṃpratyāśaṅkānirākaraṇāyāha---na khalukaścit pradveṣaḥ | anuṣṭhānānaupayikatayā
tatra pratītamapi tyajyate | sauryādīnāṃ bhinnaprayogacodanācoditānāṃ sahānuṣṭhānābhāvāt
yadyapi 1prāvṛttikaḥ kramo’vagamyate tathāpi anuṣṭhānopāyabhūto’sau na bhavatīti tyajyate | prājā-
patyeṣu tu pradhānapratyāsattyanuguṇo’nuṣṭhānaupayika iti svīkriyate | avagatasyāpi parityāge
dṛṣṭāntamāha---varṇasvarūpamiva padeṣu | yathā varṇasvarūpaṃ ekaikavarṇasvarūpaṃ pratītamapi
2vācakāvasthāyāmekaikasya varṇasvarūpasyānupāyabhūtatvāt tyajyate | ekatvamiva graheṣu | yathā
grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭītyanupāyabhūtamekatvam | sādhanaparicchedadvāreṇa hi sakhyā upāyatāṃ yāti |
na ca paricchinne sādhane grahākhyasādhyasādhane punastadapekṣā’stīti | ubhayatvamiva haviṣṣu |
yathā ‘yasyobhayaṃ havirārtimārcchati’
 iti ubhayatvamanupāyabhūtaṃ tyajyate naitāvatā’nupāyabhūtasya
tyāgo dṛṣṭa ityetāvatā aupayikasyāpi upāyabhūtasyāpi tyāga upapadyate | upasaṃharati
(1) pravṛttitaḥ (2) vācakāvācaka

[p. 1092]

---ato’pekṣitaṃ pratipannamādriyate’ | pratipannamapyanapekṣitamupekṣaṇīyameva | nanu caivaṃ
sati pratipattiranarthikā prayojanaśūnyā prāpnoti iti | codyamātraṃ pariharati-kena voktaṃ
pratipatterarthavattvaṃ prayājanavattvam | arthavatpratipattireva arthavattve hetuḥ na prati-
pattimātraṃ, tacchūnyā prayojanaśūnyā pratipattiḥ | pratipattimātraparyantaiveti sūktam |
atrādhikaraṇe saṃkṣepārtho’yam---aniyatakrameṇa prathamapadārthe’nuṣṭhite dvitīyapadārthe punaḥ pradhāna-
pratyāsattyanugrahāya kramāpekṣā bhavati | tatra yenaiva krameṇa prathamapadārtho’nuṣṭhitaḥ sa eva kramaḥ
parigṛhyate | tasminneva gṛhyamāṇe yathāvagatapradhānapratyāsattyanugrahāditi |

sthānāccotpattisaṃyogāt | atra bhāṣyakāreṇa triṇavādiṣvadarśanāt samāmnāyakramasya-
vidyamānasyāviṣayatāṃ manvānairidamudāhṛtam ityaviṣayatāṃ matvaitadudāhṛtam-saha paśūnālabhata
iti | atretraṃ yojanā-atra kila bhāṣyakāreṇa prathamamudāhṛtaṃ triṇavenaujaskāmaṃ yājayediti |
stomavivṛddhau ānīyamānā ṛcaḥ kimaniyatena krameṇa prayoktavyā uta yathāsamāmnāyakramamiti
saṃśayaṃ kṛtvā triṇavādiśabdeṣu vidhāyakeṣu pāthakramābhāvādaniyama iti pūrvapakṣaṃ kṛtvā sthāna
krameṇaiva prayoktavyā iti rāddhāntayitvedamuktam---‘atra mantrasamāmnāyapāthakrameṇāyaṃ kramaniyama
iti’
 na pāthakramādbheda ityanena ca doṣeṇa sthānakramasyāyamaviṣaya iti matvā ‘saha paśūnālabhata’

(1) P. 1295

[p. 1093]

ityudāharaṇāntaraṃ dattam | atra bhāṣyakāreṇedamudāhṛtaṃ sahapaśūnālabhata iti saṃbandhaḥ | kiṃ
kṛtvetyatrāha---triṇavenojaskāmamiti sthānakramasyāviṣayaṃ matvā | kimityaviṣayatāṃ manyata
ityatrāha---triṇavādiṣvadarśanāt samāmnāyakramasya bhrāntaiḥ pairarvidyamānasya pāthakramasya
mantragatasyāviṣayatāṃ manyānairidamudāhatamiti triṇavādicodanāsu pāthakramasyādarśanāt
nāyaṃ pāthakramasya viṣaya iti manvānairidamudāhṛtam | paramārthatastu pāthakramasyāyaṃ
viṣaya iti vidhipāthakramo vā bhavatu mantrapāthakramo vā; sarvathā pāthakrama evāyamiti
hetoḥ nāyaṃ sthānakramasya viṣaya iti matvā bhāṣyakāreṇānyadudāhṛtamiti | atra
codāharaṇe adhikaraṇāntarasiddhamarthamupādāya saha paśūnālabhata ityanena savanīyasthāne
sahatvaṃ coditamiti sthite kaḥ kramaniyama iti cintyate---kimatrāpi pravṛttireva nimittaṃ
somavikārāṇāṃ somapūrvakatvāt some yā pravṛttiḥ1 pūrvamagnīṣomīyaḥ tadanantaraṃ savanīyaḥ
tadanantaramanūbandhya iti saiva kimatrāpi kramaniyamanimittaṃ uta nimittāntaramapi sthānākhya-
mastīti vicāraḥ | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī avaśyaṃbhāvini krame yadi pravṛttirneṣyate
prayogāntaravartitvāt pūrvāvagatapāṭhādeva niyama iti manyate | siddhāntavādī tu
sahavacanena sthānasyābādhitatvāt savanīyasyaiva pūrvamanuṣṭhānaṃ nyāyyamityuktavān |
asyārthaḥ---saha paśūnālabhata ityanena vacanena savanīyasthāne sarveṣāmālambhasya coditatvāt
savanīyasthānamabādhitam | āśvinagrahaṇottarakālatā tatra | yadyagnīṣomīyastasmin kāle’nu-
ṣṭhīyate tadā śrutakālabādhaḥ syāt | tasmāt savanīya eva pūrvamālabdhavyaḥ |

tatkramakāṇyeva prayoktavyānīti | nanu brāhmaṇepyananyapara eva anuṣṭhānakramapara eva

yaḥ pāthakramaḥ sa prayojyamantragataḥ sākṣātpratīyate | brāhmaṇapāthakramastu na sākṣātprayojyagataḥ

vikṛtiḥ | sadyaskāladvayahakālacintā | keyaṃ kramacintāyāṃ ? ityanena
saṅgatirnāstītyākṣipati | kramasiddhayartheti brūmaḥ sadyaskālatāyāmanyathā kramo bhavati |

manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | aharantarepi savanaśruteraviśeṣāt | tatra hi auṣa/?/deśikenā-
tideśikaṃ bādhyate yatra dvayorasaṃbhavaḥ | iha tu dvayamapi saṃbhavatīti prākṛtī dvayahakālatā
svīkriyate | vaikṛtī ca prāhṇādikālatā aharantaravartinyapi prāhṇādau prāhṇādiśabdasyā-
viśeṣāditi | rāddhāntī tu kālāntaraśravaṇānna prākṛtaṃ kālamapekṣata iti sadyaskālatāṃ
manyate | asyārthaḥ---sarvatra vaikṛta upadeśaḥ kāryaviṣayaḥ upadeśakalpyaṃ ca kāryam | tatra
prakṛtau dvayahopadeśena kālakāryaṃ yatprāptaṃ tatraiva vikṛtāvapi kālāntaramupadiśyate na punaraha-
ravayavakārye | prakṛtāvaharavayavakāryasyāklṛptatvāt | tataśca vaikṛtenaiva kālena prākṛtakārya-
siddherna punaḥ prākṛtakālāpekṣā’stīti sadyaskālataiva yakteti | nanu tathāpi kathamekāhasaṃbandho
labhyata ityāha---savanāni caikāhassaṃbandhānyeva śrutyā’vagamyamānāni na kāraṇādṛte
aharantarāpekṣāṇi yuktāni kalpayitum | asyārthaḥ---aharavayavabhūtāni prāhṇādīni |
tāni avayavībhūtamaharākṣipya paryavasyanti | tatra caikāhākṣepeṇaivopapattau nāharantarākṣepaḥ
