aṅgānāṃ mukhyakālatvāt | aśrūyamāṇāmutkarṣavākye’pakarṣavākye ca kimi-
tyutkarṣāpakarṣau | anena pūrvapakṣamāha | sarvaṃ śabdāvagamyaṃ codanāsviti kimityaśrūyamāṇā-
nāmutkarṣāpakarṣau bhavata ityarthaḥ | satyaṃ śrutasya padārthasyotkarṣo’pakarṣo vā śrūyate na
punaruparitanamādhastyaṃ vā akṛtveti | na cedidamavagamyate kramāvagatamanuṣṭhānaṃ kena vāryate ?
asyārthaḥ | yad‌yuparitanamanuṣṭhānaṃ parityajya kevala evotkarṣaṇīya iti śrutaṃ syāt tadā
kevalasyotkaṣaḥ syāt | yadā punarutkarṣamātraṃ śrūyate tadā prāgavagatakramabalena uparitanānya-
pyutkarṣaṇīyāni | tāni hi utkṛṣyamāṇānyabhimataṃ padārthaṃ kṛtvā kartavyānīti prakṛtāvavagatam |
ataḥ kramānugrahāya teṣamuparitanānāmapyutkarṣeṇa bhavitavyam | tathā’pakarṣe’pi ādhastyamakṛtvai-
vāpakṛṣṭaḥ padārthaḥ kartavya ityevaṃ yadyaśroṣyata tadā kevala evāpakṛṣyatām | na caivam |
ato’vagatakramānugrahāyādhastyamapi kartavyam | tatkṛtvā tatrāsau prakṛtau kṛta iti |
ataḥ kimatrotkarṣavākye aśratatvaṃ kariṣyati notkarṣamapakarṣaṃ ca vārayituṃ kṣama ityarthaḥ |
anyata eva kramādevotkarṣasiddheḥ | yadi śravaṇādevotkarṣa ityucyate tadā’śrutatvaṃ bhave-
dvighātakaṃ tasmād‌yuktamuktaṃ---tadādi vā’bhisaṃbandhāt tadantamapakarṣaḥ syāditi |

[p. r1101]

pravṛttyā kṛtakālānām | kimalaṅkaraṇādayo dharmāḥ praiṣottarakālaṃ kartavyāḥ uta
pāṭhādevāvagatāḥ saumikā iti | asyārthaḥ---nirvāpātparāñco ye padārthāḥ pauroḍāśikāḥ
alaṅkaraṇācca prāñcaḥ alaṅkaraṇatya ādayaḥ alaṅkaraṇātprāgbhāvinaḥ te kiṃ ‘savanīvānnirvapasva’

iti nirvāpapraiṣottarakālaṃ kartavyāḥ nirvāpapraiṣānantarakālaṃ hi nirvāpastāvatkartavyaḥ | tasmiṃstu
kṛte prakṛtau tadanantarabhāvino ye dṛṣṭāḥ prokṣaṇaprabhṛtayo’laṅkaraṇātprāgbhāvinaḥ te’pi kiṃ kartavyāḥ
alaṅkaraṇaṃ tu vacanenaiva bahiṣpavamānottarakāle śrutamiti tatraiva kartavyam, uta pāṭhādevāvagatā-
ssaumikāḥ padārthāḥ pracaraṇīhomādayaḥ kartavyāḥ | saumikāśca padārthāḥ pāthataḥ klṛptakramāḥ
tatra vacanabalānmadhye padārthāntaramanupraviśati | tatra yāvadvacanaṃ vācanikamiti nirbāpamātrameva
madhye kartavyam | tataḥ paraṃ ca pracaraṇīhomādaya iti ubhayathāpi pāthakrama eva | ato-
’niyama iti prāpte | ucyate | alaṅkaraṇasya prāgbhāvinaḥ kartavyā iti | kutaḥ ? anirjñāta-
kartavyatā hi pāṭhānniyamyate | yadanuṣṭhānamanirjñātaṃ tatpāṭhena niyamyate | na punarnirjñāta-
kartavyatāṃ niruṇaddhi pāthaḥ | nirjñātaṃ hi prakṛtau nirvāpānantaraṃ prokṣaṇādikamiti tadeva
kartavyam | ataḥ praiṣāt praiṣārthakartavyatā vijñāyate | tasmiṃstu kṛte tadanantarabhāvino’pi
kartavyāḥ | ataḥ paraṃ tu pāṭhānniyamaḥ |

nivṛttyarthamidamadhikaraṇam | abhisaṃbandhābhāvāt yāvadvacanamapakarṣa 4iti pāthato

yadyapyanūyājasvarūpaṃ tadeva tathāpi saṃbandhināṃ rūpabhedena nāsti kramakalpaneti |

tathā’pūrvam | paurṇamāsyāṃ pāṭhāt kramasambandho nirjñāta iti tadantasyāpakarṣaṃ
manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | pāthakramasya paurṇamāsyāmādṛtatvāt klṛptakramāyā vedeḥ amāvāsyāyāṃ
pratikṛṣyamāṇatvāt asyā arvācīnānāmapi pratikarṣaṃ manyate | rāddhāntavādī tu na paurṇa-
māsīprakṛtikaṃ1 āmāvāsyaṃ karmeti manvānaḥ apakarṣottarakālameva kramo niyamyate śeṣāṇā-
miti yāvadvacanamapakarṣa ityuktavān | prakṛtivikārabhāve hi sati śāstrārthāvadhāraṇapūrvakaṃ
śāstrārthāvadhāraṇam na svātantryeṇa | tena paurṇamāsyāṃ kramakalpanāmapekṣyāmāvāsyāyāṃ na
śāstrārtho varṇayitavyaḥ | tena yāvadeva vedimātramapakṛṣṭaṃ tāvadevāmāvāsyāyāṃ pratikṛṣyate |
taduttarakālaṃ ca tatra kramaniyama iti | kāryavirodhaśceha haviṣāṃ bhasmībhāvalakṣaṇo-
’bhyuccayaḥ |

sāntapanīyā tūtkarṣet | parārthamuddhṛte’gnau prasaṅgādṛte nāgnihotraṃ hotavyamiti
manvānaḥ pūrvapakṣavādī utkarṣamuktavān | atrādhikaraṇe’gnyuddharaṇakālayordvayoraṅgayoḥ kasya
tyāgo yukta iti cintyate | sāntapanīyārthamuddhṛte’gnau daivānmānuṣādvā pratibandhabalāt
sāntapanīyāyāmutkṛṣṭāyāṃ sāyamagnihotraṃ prati puruṣasya tāvadadhikāro vidyate jīvanasyādhikāri-
viśeṣaṇasya vidyamānatvāt | nanu ca tiryagadhikaraṇe sarvāṅgosaṃhārasamarthasyaivādhikāra
(1) P. 1307 (2) R. pravṛttikramaṃ

[p. r1104]

ityuktam | na ca sāntapanīyārthoddhṛtāgniḥ akṛtasāntapanīyaḥ punaragnihotrārthamagnyuddharaṇa-
mupasaṃhartuṃ śaknoti | apavṛtte hi karmaṇi alaukikena svarūpeṇa gārhapatyamīhavanīyaḥ praviśati
nānyathā | apraviṣṭaśca kathamudadhriyeta | tasmādagnyuddharaṇopasaṃhārāsāmarthyānnādhikārasaṃbhavaḥ
ucyate---satyamāha bhavān samarthasyaivādhikāra iti | kintu sāmarthyamevātra cintanīyaṃ kiṃ
taditi | tatrāhitāgnitvādanyannāgnyuddharaṇopasaṃhārasāmarthyam | asti cātra puruṣasyāhitā-
gnitvamiti agnyuddharaṇopasaṃhārasāmarthyamiti adhikārānapāyaḥ | yathā caivaṃ tathā sarvaśaktya-
dhikaraṇa eva nipuṇataramupapādayiṣyāmaḥ | tatrāpi satyadhikāre’ṅgalopenāpyanuṣṭhānamastīti
sthite cintyate---kimagnyuddharaṇamapahāya kālānugrahāya tasminneva kāle’gnihotraṃ hūyatāṃ, uta
dvitīye’hani sāntapanīyakaṃ kṛtvā punaragnimuddhṛtya kālamātraṃ parityajyāgnihotraṃ hūyatāmiti
cintāyāṃ prayogāṅgatvāt kālasyāṅgāṅgabhūtatvam | ato’ṅgabhūtoddharaṇamevātrānugrāhyam | na
ca parārthamuddhṛte’gnau tasminneva kāle’gnihotraṃ yuktaṃ kartuṃ, aprāsaṅgikatvādagnihotrasya yadi
hi kālo’trānugrāhyaḥ syāt tadā tantramadhyāvidhānāt niśīṣṭivadagnihotraṃ syāt | tataśca
parārthamuddhṛte’gnau bhaveta | na caitadasti | tasmādutkarṣa eva tasya nyāyya iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ
rāddhāntī tu nimittatvātkālasyānutkarṣaṃ manyate | etaduktaṃ bhavati---nimittarūpaḥ kālaḥ
tadabhāve’dhikāra eva nāsti | ato na kālāntare’gnihotraṃ hotavyam | agnyuddharaṇābhāve tu
adhikāsyānapāyāddhotavyamityanutkarṣo yuktaḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ kālonimittam ? yāvatā’ṅga
kāla uktaḥ | uttaraṃ---tasmin sati prayogāt na punaradhikāriviśeṣaṇatvāt, kālasya
puruṣānupasaṃhāryatvāt | yadā svayameva kālo nipatati tadā prayogaḥ kartavyaḥ ityetāvatā
nimittatā kālasyocyate na punarbhedādīnāmivādhikāriviśeṣaṇatvena | yadā svayamupanipātī
kālaḥ tadā kālāntare puruṣasya kāle karma kartuṃ sāmarthyaṃ nāstītyanadhikāraḥ | kālāntare

[p. r1105]

āhitāgnitayā tu agnyuddharaṇasāmarthyamastītyanapeto’dhikāraḥ | ato nimitte sati
naimittikena bhavitavyamityarthāt prāsaṅgikatvasiddhiḥ | ato’nutkarṣaḥ |

ṣoḍaśī ca | ṣoḍaśinyaṅgakālavirodhāt kādācitkatvāccotkarṣasya anutkarṣaṃ
manyate pūrvavādī | ‘taṃ parāñcamukthyebhyo gṛhṇāti’
 iti vacanena ṣoḍaśinaḥ ukthyottara-
kālatā vidhīyate | 1utkṛṣṭepi cokthyeṣu kālasyānapāya eva, śaṅkhābhāve’pi śaṅkhavelāyā
iva | yadi paramanutkarṣe ṣoḍaśinaḥ ukthyaiḥ saha yaḥ pratītaḥ kramaḥ sa parityajyate sa
ca tyaktumucita eva | utkarṣe ṣoḍaśyaṅgabhūtastotrasya samayāghyuṣitakālabādhaprasakteḥ | na ca
samayādhyuṣitakālacodanā atyantāsaṃbhāvyārthā | kādācitkatvādukthyotkarṣasya | yadā hi
nokthyotkarṣaḥ tadā sarvamavikalamupapannamiti aṅgāṅgabādhācca anaṅgakramabādho nyāyya iti |
rāddhāntavādī tu avaśyaṃbhāvitvācchāstrātikramasya varamaṅgaśāstrasyātikrama ityutkarṣa-
muktavān | yadyapyanaṅgaṃ kramaḥ tathāpi tāvat nāśāstrārthatā kramasya | tataśca dvayoḥ
śāstrārthatve varamaṅgaśāstrasyātikrama ityutkarṣaḥ ṣoḍaśino yuktaḥ |

anuṣṭhānena vyavadhānāvagamāt | rāddhāntastu vaikṛtaprayogavacanāt prākṛtapadārthāvagamāt
vyavadhānam | vaikṛtacodanārthāvacchedāyaiva hi prākṛtāṅgayuktaṃ pradhānaṃ paṭhitamiti pāthakramata
eva vaikṛtānāṃ prākṛtebhyaḥ paścādanuṣṭhānam | atassūktaṃ-ante tu bādarāyaṇa iti |

(1) P. 1316 (2) G. tāvadeva

[p. r1114]

kṛtadeśāttu | atrettha pūrvapakṣaracanā | sarvatra vaikṛtāni tāvatprākṛtānāṃ paścāt
kartavyānīti sthitaṃ ante tu bādarāyaṇa iti nyāyāt | tatra yāvanmātramapakṛṣyate tāvata
evāpakarṣo yuktaḥ na tadantāpakarṣanyāyena pūrveṣāmapi; vaikṛtatvenāklṛptakramaniyamatvāt yūpa-
pratikarṣavaditi | atra rāddhāntamāha---rājasūyepi videvanādavo vaikṛtena pāṭhena virudhya-
mānāḥ nānte’nuṣṭhānamarhanti | yatra śrutāstatraiva bhavitumarhanti | asyārthaḥ---parasparaṃ tāvat
videvanādīnāmabhiṣekasya ca vikṛtau pāthataḥ kramaḥ pratīyate | tatrābhiṣeke pratikṛṣṭe sati
cintyate---kiṃ videvanādayo’nte’nuṣṭheyāḥ utābhiṣekātpūrvamiti | atra vaikṛtapāthabalena
ya eva pāthakramaḥ pratīyate sa eva kalpyate | ante tvanuṣṭhānaṃ vaikṛtenaiva pāṭhena viruddhayate |
nyāyamātramitaraditi | kimida nyāyamātramiti ? ayamabhiprāyaḥ---antepi yadanuṣṭhānaṃ
tadapi vaikṛtapāthabalenaiva pradhānacodanāgṛhītatvāt prākṛtānāṃ vaikṛtānāṃ paścādeva pātha iti
pūrvoktanītyā | tadeva tulye vaikṛtapāṭhe kimidamucyate nyāyamātramitaraditi pūrvapakṣite
rāddhāntamāha---etaduktaṃ bhavati---nānte’nuṣṭhānaṃ kenacitkrameṇāvagamyate | kiṃ tarhi ?
pūrvāvagamavirodhādante’nuṣṭhānamiti nyāyamātramityucyate | asyārthaḥ---vaikṛtānāṃ
yadante’nuṣṭhānaṃ tanna kenacit śrutyādipratipāditena krameṇāvagamyate | kiṃ tarhi ? pradhāna-
codanāparigṛhītatvena prākṛtānāṃ yo vaikṛtebhyaḥ pūrvamavagamaḥ tasya virodhādante’nuṣṭhānamava-
(1) P. 1318

[p. r1115]

gamyate yathāvagamamanuṣṭhānamiti yaḥ pūrvamavagamyate sa pūrvamanuṣṭheyaḥ | anyathā’nuṣṭhānāvagamayo-
rvirodhaḥ syāt | na ca tadyuktam | avagamāyattatvādanuṣṭhānasyeti | yaścāsau prākṛtānāṃ
pūrvamavagamaḥ sa ānumānikaḥ tenāntenānuṣṭhānaṃ nyāyamātranibandhanam | yaśca vaikṛtānāṃ
pāthamātranibandhanaḥ parasparakramaḥ saḥ pratyakṣasamadhigamyaḥ | tatra pratyakṣasya śīghrapravṛttitvāta
tadvaśena yaḥ kramaḥ pūrvamaklṛptatvāt balīyāniti tadanugrahāyābhiṣekāt pūrvapaṭhitānāṃ videva
nādīnāmapi apakarṣaḥ | yūpapratikarṣe tu agnīṣomīyapraṇayanādīnāṃ yūpenaiva svaprāgbhāvibhirapi
padārthaiḥ pratyakṣa eva pāthakrama iti na pratikarṣaḥ |

prākṛtācca purastādyat | atrāpi prākṛtakramāvagamāt prayogacodanāta evānte-
’nuṣṭhānaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | asyārthaḥ---prākṛtānāṃ padārthānāṃ prakṛtāveva yaḥ kramaḥ
klṛptaḥ tatra madhye vinā vacanena vaikṛtasyānupraveśo na nyāyyaḥ iti prākṛtakramavaśena prayoga-
codanātaḥ pūrvaṃ prākṛtān padārthānavagamayantyāḥ sāvitrahomādīnāmapyante’nuṣṭhānaṃ nyāyyamiti-
na ca dīkṣaṇīyādiśravaṇaṃ madhye’nupraveśamavagamayitumalam | kramasyānabhidhānāta |
punaḥ śrutistu ananyārthā kramamevāvagamayati | yadyapi kramo nābhidhīyate tathāpi
pāthabalena kramo’vagamyate | tatra ca punaḥ śruterananyaparāyāstātparyamiti tadbalena
madhye’nupraveśaḥ |

vivṛddhiḥ | nanveṣā śeṣacintetyasaṅgatimāśaṅkyāha---kramārtha eva pratyekasamudāya-
saṃbandhaścintyate | pratyekaṃ saṃbandhe hi vyavadhānamahatvam | samudāyasaṃbandhe tvalpatvam |
vitatiḥ kramaḥ, sā pratyekasaṃbandhe mahatī; samudāyasaṃbandhe tvalpeti | tasmādyukto vicāraḥ |
kiṃ punaryuktaṃ ? pratyekamabhisaṃbandha iti | pratipradhānaṃ guṇā āvartanta iti nyāyāt |
nanu ca pṛthaktvaniveśinī saṅkhyetyuktam | asyārthaḥ---pṛthaktvaṃ yatra samavetaṃ tatra
dvitvādikā saṅkhyā niviśata iti dvitīya evoktam | tataścaikaikatra ṣaḍādisaṅkhyā kathaṃ
niviśata iti paraṃ samudāya eva saṅkhyāvidhānamastviti | satyamuktam vidhānavaśāttu
yathopapadyate tathā kalpanīyam | asyārthaḥ---yadā tāvata[sāhityaṃ]uddeśyagataviśeṣaṇaṃ syāt
vivakṣitatvāt vivakṣāyāṃ ca vākyabhedāpatteḥ | sāhityasyāvivakṣāyāṃ pratyekameva saṃṅkhyā
vidhīyata iti niścitam | tadanurodhenābhyāsanibandhanaṃ pṛthaktvamāśritya saṅkhyopapadyata iti
kalpanīyam---yathā ‘triḥ prathamāmanvāha triruttamāṃ’
 ityatra sāmidhenīnāṃ pañcadaśatvam | yata
(1) P. 1320

[p. r1118]

eva pratyekasaṃbandho nyāyyaḥ ata eva ‘pañcadaśānyājyāni’
 ityevamādiṣu pratyekasambandhaḥ
pañcadaśādīnāṃ, anyathā tatrāpi samudāyasambandha eva syāt | atrocyate---na vidhānaṃ
padārthamanyathā kartumutsahate | padārthānurodhī hi vidhiḥ na vidhyanurodhinaḥ padārthāḥ
agninā siñcedityevamādiṣu darśitam | asyārthaḥ---saṅkhyāpadārthastāvata dvitvādikaḥ
pṛthaktve sati niviśate | pṛthaktvaṃ ca yādṛśaṃ samudāye na tādṛśamabhyāse | tena vidheyabhūta-
saṅkhyāsvabhāvālocanayā samudāyasaṃbandha eva saṅkhyā vidhīyata iti padārthānusāreṇa vākyārthaḥ
pratīyate | vidhyanusāreṇa tūktena nyāyena pratyekasambandhaḥ | tatra padārthasvabhāvo vihanyate |
so’pyanyathākartuṃ na śakyata iti padārthānurodhāt samudāya eva saṅkhyāvidhirāśrayaṇīyaḥ |
tasmāt yāvatpṛthaktvaṃ tāvadabhyāsakalpanā na sambhavatīti samudāye sambandhaḥ | pṛṣadājye
tvetadviparītam | tatra hi kārakatvena dravyavidhānaṃ kriyāyāśca kārakam | na ca kriyā-
samudāyaḥ kriyā | tena samudāye saṃbandhe tūpapattiriti pratyekasaṃbandha eva nyāyyaḥ |
yadapyuktaṃ pañcadaśānyājyānīti āvṛttau ḍapratyayaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ tena tatra na saṅkhyā vidhīyate |
kiṃ tarhi ? viśiṣṭasaṅkhyākā āvṛttiḥ | sā ca pratyekasaṃbandhepyaviguṇaiveti na
vidhānapadārthayoḥ kaścidvirodha iti yathāvidhānameva sambandhaḥ |

kratvantareṣvapi dvādaśāhavikāreṣu yā[tā]vaccodakaprāptā yujyetāpyāśaṅkā | atadvikāreṣu sā
atyantamayuktā | samādhatte---uttamaśabdaśravaṇāt | kimidamuttamaśabdaśravaṇāditi uttamaḥ
paryāso bhavatīti padārthāṅgatāṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | uttamaśabdaśravaṇe sati uttamasya
tṛcasya paryāsatvaṃ vidhīyate na dvādaśāhikasyaiva | tenottamatṛcarūpapadārthāṅgatāṃ manyate
pūrvapakṣavādī (a)vihitaṃ ca kratvantareṣvapyuttamatvaṃ iti asya tatrāpi paryāsatā vidhīyate |
nanu ca padārthāṅgatve ānarthakyamuktam | yadi padārthamātre dharmo vidhīyate tadā dharmavidhe-
rānarthakyaṃ syāt apūrvasaṃbandhābhāvāt | ato dvādaśāhasaṃbandha eva uttamatṛce paryāsatā---
vidhiriti | pariharati---na tadānarthakyaṃ paryāse sambhavati sarvasya paryāsasyārthavattvāt |
vidheyaparyāsasvarūpamevedamīdṛśaṃ yena padārthamātrasambandhitayāpi vidhāne sati ānarthakyaṃ nāsti |
kratusambandhinī hi paryāsasaṃjñā | kratoreva hyantasya paryāsaśabdaḥ saṃjñā, nidhanādiśabdavata
nirupapadāt paryāsaśabdāt kratvantapratipatteḥ | yattu bhāṣyakāreṇa kṣetraparyāsa ityupanyastaṃ
tat sopapadasya gauṇaprayogadarśanena kratvantavacanatvapratipattaya eveti mantavyam | yatra hi
akratusaṃbandhi vidheyamavaghātādi tatra mābhūt padārthamātradharmatā, ānarthakyaprasaṅgāt | yatra
vidheyameva kratusaṃbandhi na tatrānarthakyaśaṅketi | evaṃ tāvata atadvikāreṣvasti pūrvapakṣaśaṅketi
darśitam | saṃprati dvādaśāhavikāra evodāharaṇamiti matvā tadvikāreṣvapi nirākaraṇīya
evāyamartha ityupanyāsaḥ | dvādaśāhavikāreṣu codakaprāptyāpi śaṅkopapadyata ityarthaḥ |
(1) mā. śravaṇaprāmāṇyāt

atrābhidhīyatte---nāyaṃ padārthadharmaḥ | kastarhi ? kratudharmaḥ | kathaṃ ? prakaraṇāt dvādaśāha-
niyogaikyamavagamyate na kratvantaraniyogaikyam | avagamyate | etaduktaṃ bhavati---yadyapi

na kratvantarasambandhe | nanu ca padārthānusārī niyogaḥ | anyathā dvādaśopasattvamapi

pramāṇamasti | tadatra vidhyānarthakyenāpūrvasaṃbandhiṣu vidhānamityevarūpaṃ rāddhāntābhāsaṃ nirākartu-
midamuktaṃ niyogaikyenaiva naiyogike rūpe vidhānaṃ nānarthakyaparihārāyeti | yadi paraṃ dvādaśāha-
vikāra evaitatsyāt tatra codakataḥ prāpteḥ na ca vā tatrāpi | atha vā dvādaśāhavikā-
reṣvapi nāyaṃ dharmaḥ kiṃ tarhi vṛṣaṇvatāṃ dharmaḥ vṛṣaṇvatāṃ tṛcānāṃ madhye ya uttamaḥ tṛcaḥ
tasyāyaṃ paryāsatvaṃ dharmaḥ | na ta te kratvantareṣu santi | tasmātteṣvapyāgantavo’nta eva |
uttamasya hi paryāsatvavidhānāt prāk‌paryāsādāgantanaḥ syuḥ | tannāstītyuktam---nanu ca
padārthadharmo na bhavatītyuktam | kathaṃ vṛṣaṇvatāṃ dharma ityucyate ? satyamuktaṃ padārthadharmo na

bhavatīti, na punaḥ kratvarthāparityāgenāpi | kratvarthaparityāgena padārthadharmo na bhavatītyuktam

ante tūttarayoḥ | nanu kathamatra pūrvapakṣaḥ ? yāvatā vacanāt gāyatryādiṣvevāgantūnāṃ

ha vai yajñasyodarāṇi gāyatrī bṛhatyanupṭup ca atra hyevāvapanti ata evodvapanti”
 itīdamapi

dvādaśāhavikāreṣu ca teṣāmabhāvāt paryāsatā na bhavati | iha tu viśeṣagrahaṇāta

teṣu mābhūdityatadvikāra evātrodāharaṇam | atrocyate---āvāpanibandhanametadvacanaṃ yāva-

viśeṣaḥ | upadiṣṭānāṃ vṛṣaṇvatāṃ tṛcānāmevottamasya paryāsatā, nānyasya; tena dvādaśāha-
niyogaikyaṃ tāsāmiti siddham |

graheṣṭakam | nanvasaṅgateyaṃ cintetyāśaṅkyāha---atrāpyaṅgāṅgicintā kramārthaiva |
/?/vanacitiśeṣatve praticitīṣṭakopadhānaṃ pratisavanaṃ ca grahaṇamiti kramānyatvaṃ bhavatīti |
kathamatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ yāvatā yāgāgnisaṃbandhitvenopadeśāta graheṣṭakasya taccheṣataiva yuktā |
(1) P. 1325 (2) G. prakṛti

[p. r1125]

uttaraṃ---kāryānupraveśānniyogārthasya citisavananiṣpattyā cānupraveśasya | asyārthaḥ
---yāvatkiñcidaṅgaṃ tatsarvaṃ niyogārthaṃ tasya sarvasya kāryānupraveśāt svayaṃ vā tat kartavyam
kiñcidvā tena kartavyam | na hyakāryānupraveśinā siddharūpeṇa niyogo niṣpadyate saṃpādyaviṣaya-
tvānniyogasya | tasmānniyogārthasya kāryānupraveśāt pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | nanvetāvatāpi pūrvapakṣo na
siddhayati yāgāgnyanupraveśitayā niyogārthasiddheḥ atrāha-citisavananiṣpattyā cānupraveśasya
kāryārthatvāditi pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ | yo’pyasau yāgāgnyanupraveśaḥ so’pi citisavananiṣpattyaiva
nānyathā | tasmāt yāgāgnyanupraveśasyāpi citisavananiṣpattyā kāryānupraveśāt citisavana-
niṣpattireveṣṭakopadhānasya grahaṇasya ca kāryam | yacca yasya kāryaṃ kartavyaṃ tattasya kāryamiti savana-
citi śeṣatvameva graheṣṭakasya | upasaṃharati---tasmāccitisavanaśeṣo’yamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
atrocyate---upadeśasiddhayarthaṃ hi kāryaṃ na kāryasiddhayartha upadeśaḥ | yāgāgnisaṃbandhaścāya-
mupadeśaḥ | asyārthaḥ---satyaṃ kāryānupraveśitā niyogārthasya | sā tu upadeśasiddhayarthā
kathaṃ nāmopadiṣṭasya niyogārthatā syāditi na punaḥ kāryamapekṣyopadeśaḥ | yāgāgnisaṃbandhaścāya-
mupadeśaḥ | tasmāttatsiddhireva kāryaṃ na citisavananiṣpattiḥ | yacca kāryaṃ taccheṣataiva
yukteti yāgāgniśeṣataiva graheṣṭakasya | nanu ca nyāyapautarukyamidaṃ aruṇayā krīṇātīti |
asyārthaḥ---aruṇādhikaraṇepyetaduktaṃ upadeśabalena āruṇyasya krayaniṣpattireva kāryaṃ, yadyapi
ca sākṣāt sādhanatā nāsti iti, ihāpi tadevocyate yadyapi sākṣādyāgāgnikāryatā nāsti

[p. r1126]

tathāpyupadeśabalena tadeva kāryamiti | tatrāsaṃbhavādapi bhavantīti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |
tannirākaraṇārthamidama | asmārthaḥ---atratyapūrvapakṣavādī evaṃ manyate tatrāruṇādhikaraṇe
anyena prakāreṇa kāryānupraveśābhāvāt yuktā upadeśabalenāruṇimnaḥ krayakāryatā2 | iha tu
citisavananiṣpattyāpi citikāryānupraveśitopapadyata iti imameva tatratyaṃ rāddhāntāmāsa
nirākartumidamadhikaraṇam | tatrāpi nāsambhavāt krayakāryatā kintu upadeśādeva | sambhavepi
kāryāntarasya upadeśabalenaiva kāryāśravaṇāt upadeśa eva kāryāśrayaṇe hetuḥ na kāryāntarasaṃbhava
iti | prayojanamuktama |

ante syuravyavāyāt | nanu “yāṃ vai kāṃcit brāhmaṇavatīmiṣṭakāmabhijānīyāta tāṃ madhya-
māyāṃ citāvupadadhyāta”
 iti vacane sati pūrvapakṣa evātra nopapadyate | uttaraṃ---na nopa-
padyate | sarvā brāhmaṇavatya ityanuvādaṃ manyate | sarvā eveṣṭakā brāhmaṇavatyaḥ sarvāsāmeva
brāhmaṇoktenādhikāravidhinā gṛhītatvāt | viniyuktatvāccādhikāravidhirevāṅgānāṃ viniyojakaḥ
sahakāra[ri]mātrāṇi śrutyādīni | tataśca yadyayaṃ vidhirāśrīyate tadā sarvāsāmeva madhyamāyāṃ
citāvupadhānāta cityantarābhāvaprasaṅgena madhyamatvānupapatteḥ vacanamidaṃ nirviṣayamāpadyate |ato’nu-
vādo’yaṃ madhyamāyāṃ citāvuṣadheyatvena yāḥ prāptāḥ tāsāmupadhānamanūdyate | na ca pramāṇāntamasti,
aupānuvākyānāmiṣṭakānāṃ madhyamāyāṃ citāvupadhānamityanupapannaḥ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | evaṃ sati anyā
anyā iti darśanamupapannaṃ bhavati-‘āvapanaṃ vā uttamā citiḥ yadanyā anyā iṣṭakā upadadhāti’

(1) P. 1326 (2) R. kriyākārthatā

[p. r1127]

iti | evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate | brāhmaṇavatyaḥ śrutiviniyuktāḥ parigṛhyante | kimiti |
mantraviniyojyatvāccheṣāṇām | samākhyā punaḥ laukikyevetyuktama | asyārthaḥ
---yadi grāhakābhiprāyeṇa brāhmaṇavatītyucyate tadā avaiśeṣikatvāta vacanamidamanarthakaṃ syāta
atha viniyogakāraṇābhiprāyeṇa tadā vaiśeṣikatvādvacanasyārthavattā | na cārthavattve saṃbhavatyānartha-
kyamucitam | kathaṃ punarvaiśeṣikatvamiti ? atredamuttaraṃ---mantraviniyojyatvāccheṣāṇāṃ
samākhyāviniyojyatvācca samākhyāyāśca laukikatvena brāhmaṇatvābhāvāt | tasmānmadhyamāyāṃ
citāvupadheyā iti | nanu mantreṇa ityādi nigadavyākhyātam---viśeṣābhiprāyeṇedamuktaṃ
vacanārthavattvāyetyadoṣaḥ | viniyogaviśeṣapramāṇāni śrutyādīni tadabhiprāyeṇa brāhmaṇavatī
grāhyetyarthaḥ | darśane tu mantrābhiprāyeṇānyatvaṃ varṇanīyam | anyamantrakatvenānyatvaṃ
vyākhyeyaṃ prāptipūrvakatvāddarśanasya |

prāglokaṃ pṛṇāyāḥ | atredṛśo viṣayaḥ agniprakaraṇe citiprakaraṇe citiviśeṣamanārabhyaiva
sāmānyena ‘lokaṃ pṛṇa chidraṃ pṛṇa’
 iti paṭhitamiti | pūrvapakṣamākṣipati---nanu cānte vādarāyaṇa
iti nopapadyate lokaṃpṛṇāpi brāhmaṇavatyeva | asyārthaḥ---‘yāṃ vai kāñcita’

(1) P. 1326

[p. r1128]

ityanenaupānuvākyakāṇḍapaṭhitānāṃ lokaṃ pṛṇāyāśca madhyamāyāṃ citau upadhānam1 |
lokaṃpṛṇā hi sāmānyena vihitā madhyamāyāmeva citāvupaghātavyeti vidhānamava-
kalpyate | tataścaikenaiva vacanena prāpnuvantīnāmiṣṭakānāṃ idaṃ na yujyate vaktuṃ---ante tu
vādarāyaṇa iti | samādhatte---nanu ca mantravidhānārthaṃ tadvacanaṃ, tena lokaṃ pṛṇā na
brāhmaṇavatī | punarākṣeptā’’ha---nanu ca sarvā eva mantradvāreṇa codyante | yā api
brāhmaṇavattvena sammatāḥ tā api mantradvāreṇa codyante, tena lokaṃ pṛṇāpi mantradvāreṇa coditā
brāhmaṇavatyeva | samādhātā āha---satyaṃ tathāpi iṣṭakābhidhānapradhāno’yaṃ nirdeśaḥ
‘citriṇīrupadadhāti’
 ityevamādiṣu | teneṣṭakā etā brāhmaṇavatyaḥ | iha punaḥ ‘lokaṃ
pṛṇa chidraṃ pṛṇa’
 iti mantrapradhāno’yaṃ nirdeśaḥ iti laiṅgikyeveyam | mantro hi prakaraṇe
paṭhitaḥ kañcidarthaṃ prakāśayannaṅgam | 2śaknoti cāyaṃ cāyaṃ mantraḥ iṣṭakāmabhidhātumiti mantra-
viniyogabalena iṣṭakāyā api viniyogaḥ | iha laiṅgikyeveyam | prakaraṇādhītamantraviniye-
/?/yatayā ceyaṃ prathamapraviṣṭā | aupānuvākyakāṇḍasamāmnātāstu brāhmaṇavatītvenānupraviśanti
ityāgantavaḥ | ato’nte tu bādarāyaṇa iti yuktam | nanu lokaṃ pṛṇāyā laiṅgikyā api
saṃpūraṇārthatayāva1gamādante niveśoyuktaḥ brāhmaṇavatīnāṃ tu prāglokaṃ pṛṇāyā iti yuktam |
ucyate---na tasyāḥ pūraṇārthatve pramāṇamasti | yo’pyarthavādaḥ so’pi mantrameva saṃpūraṇārthatayā
stauti neṣṭakāmiti | rāddhāntastu mantrārthavādānyathānupapatteḥ laiṅgikā api prāgeva
brāhmaṇavatya iti yuktam | asyārthaḥ---mantrasyāpi yo’yaṃ saṃpūraṇārthavādaḥ sa iṣṭakāyā-

 iti

-na ceṣṭibhirvinā’’havanīyādayo bhavanti iti na karmasvadhikāro’kṛtapavamāneṣṭeḥ puruṣasya |

“āgnāvaiṣṇavamekādaśakapālaṃ nirvapeddīkṣiṣyamāṇaḥ”

vikalpaḥ syāt | tasmādasmatpakṣa eva samuccayasiddhiḥ syāt | na cābhidhānābhāvādarthā-
bhāvaśśakyate vaktumiti darśitam |

aṅgavatkratūnām | nanu cāvaśyaṃbhāvini krame pāṭhānniyama uktaḥ | asyārthaḥ
---yatrānekeṣāṃ padārthānāṃ ekena kartrā’nuṣṭhānaṃ kartavyaṃ tatrāvaśyaṃbhāvini krame pāṭhāt krama-
niyama uktaḥ | ekādhikāravidhiprayuktānāṃ ca ekena kartrā’nuṣṭhānaṃ prayujyate na nānādhikāra-
prayuktānāṃ; atastatra kathaṃ pāṭhāt kramaniyama āśaṅkyate | kiñca kratvantaravaditi pūrvapakṣe
pravṛttyadhikaraṇe yadupanyastaṃ tatrāpi vyatireko varṇitaḥ vaiṣamyamuktam | bhinnaprayoga-
vacanatvānnaiṣāṃ krama upapadyata iti | atastenaiva vaiṣamyodbhāvanena kratvantarāṇāṃ pāramparyakrama-
niyamo nāstīti darśita eva rāddhāntaḥ | ataḥ kathamatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ ? ucyate---anekānuṣṭhāna-
nibandhano’yaṃ kramaḥ | anekāni kāmyāni karmāṇi kurvataḥ yukta eva kāminaḥ kramaniyama
iti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | ekādhikāraprayuktā vā bhavantu anekādhikāraprayuktā vā padārthāḥ
sarvathā yatrānekeṣāṃ padārthānāmekenānuṣṭhānaṃ tatra kramaḥ | anekāni kāmyāni karmāṇyekena
kāminā kartavyānīti krame sati pāṭhādavagataḥ kramaniyama ādariṣyate | kathaṃ tarhi sarvasvāre
pāthakamaḥ ? uttaraṃ---arthādbādho bhaviṣyati | diṣṭāṃ gatiṃ gatavati yajamāne parato’nuṣṭhānā-
bhāvāt arthākhyena pramāṇena bādho bhaviṣyatīti na doṣaḥ | atrābhidhīyate---vidhivitā-
cinteyaṃ na puruṣecchāvitānacintā | vidhīyanta iti vidheyāḥ padārthā ucyante | teṣāṃ
vitāḥ atrādhyāye cintyate | na puruṣecchāvitānacintā | kutaḥ ? acodanālakṣaṇatvāttasya

icchāvitānasya | yadi puruṣecchāyāḥ kramaniyamo bhavet tadā kāmyānāṃ karmaṇāṃ kramaniyamo
bhavet, adhikāriviśeṣaṇakrameṇādhikārasyāpi kramavattvāt | na punaḥ icchāyā aniyame
yathecchotpādaṃ aniyamenādhikārotpatteḥ | na cātra puruṣecchāvitānaścintyate | tasyācodanā-

lakṣaṇatvam | evaṃ tāvat acintanīyatoktyā puruṣecchāvitānasya tannibandhanaḥ kāmyānāṃ
kramaniyamo nāstītyuktam | saṃprati sākṣāt karmaṇāmeva kramo na śābda iti darśayati

dhikāravidhiprayuktānāmaṅgapradhānānāṃ yugapat 1prasaktānuṣṭhānānāṃ puruṣāśaktyā vitānasambandho bhavati
na punaradhikārāntaramapekṣate | tena bhinnādhikāraprayuktānāṃ kramāvatāro nāstītyarthaḥ | kutaḥ ?
asaṃbandhāt parasparamadhikārāṇāṃ saṃbandhābhāvāt | idamatrākūtam---ekādhikārānu-
praviṣṭānāṃ ekadeśasthānāmiva paṅ‌ktirūpā vitatiḥ nānyathetyarthaḥ | asaṃbandhasya
ca vidheyāsaṃbandhasya ca vitānasya śābdataiva na śakyate vaktum | tasmāt aśābdo’yaṃ
parasparakramaḥ kāmyānām | na ca śābdasyānuṣṭhātāro’śābdamādriyante | yadi tāvadekasmin
kāle bahavaḥ kāmā nipateyuḥ tadā yugapadadhikāre sati bahuṣu karmasu kramāpekṣā syāt | na
caitadasti | kāmānāyā yugapadutpādābhāvāt | tadāha---na ca sarvakāmasyādhikāraḥ | kutaḥ ?
yogasiddhayadhikaraṇe pratyuktatvāt | ataḥ kāmato’pi kāmayaugapadyādapi na vidheyeṣu karmasu
kramasaṃbandhaḥ | pāthamātre vā ādriyamāṇe sarvasvāre pāthakramaḥ kena vāryate | artheneti cet
tatrāha---arthasya saṃbandhaviṣayatvāt | arthākhyaṃ pramāṇaṃ vidheyasaṃbandhacodanālakṣaṇakrama-
2viṣayatvāt nācodanālakṣaṇe pramāṇaṃ bhavatīti kuto bādhakam | tasmāt kāmyeṣvaniyamaḥ |

(1) G. prayukta (2) G. viṣayatvaṃ

ya etenetyagniṣṭomaḥ | nanu asaṅgateyaṃ cintetyāśaṅkyāha---uparitanasyādhikaraṇasya
viṣayapradarśanārthamidamadhikaraṇam | uparitanaṃ hyadhikaraṇaṃ kramacintārthaṃ tasya viṣaya-
prasiddhayarthamityaupoddhātikatayā saṅgatirityarthaḥ | jñāte hi etenetyasyārthe tadapekṣo’nyenetyanya-
śabdārtho nirūpayituṃ śakyaḥ | “eṣa vāva prathamo yajño yajñānāṃ yajjyotiṣṭomaḥ ya etenā niṣṭvā
athānyena yajate”
 ityukte jyotiṣṭoma eva pratīyate sarvasaṃsthaśca jyotiṣṭomaḥ | ataḥ sarvāsāmeva
saṃsthānāṃ 2vāda iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | nityatvādagniṣṭomasaṃsthasya prakaraṇamiti rāddhāntaḥ | atra
hi agniṣṭomo jyotiṣṭomaprakaraṇe samāmnānāt asya vākyasya jyotiṣṭomasyaivānena yajñāntarā-
pekṣayā pūrvakālatocyate agniṣṭomasaṃsthā ca nityā | tena tatsaṃsthasyaiva prakaraṇam vikṛti-
bhūtāni saṃsthāntarāṇītyuktaṃ tṛtīye | anyathā nityānityasaṃyogavirodhaprasaṅgāt | tena
tatsaṃsthasyaivāyaṃ vāda iti |

athānyeneti | etenetyagniṣṭomasaṃsthasyāvamarśāt anyeneti saṃsthānta-
rāvamarśaṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | samānajātīyāpekṣo’yamanyaśabdaḥ | yathā ekapuruṣa āgataḥ
anyo netyukte puruṣāntarameva pratīyate tathehāpi saṃsthāpekṣayā’nyatve saṃsthāntarameva pratīyata
iti---rāddhāntī tu eṣa vāva prathamo yajño yajñānāṃ iti yajñaśruteḥ yajñāntaramevānyaśabdena
(1) P. 1333 (2) G. anuvādaḥ

[p. r1136]

pratīyata ityuktavān yajñasaṅkīrtanena yajñantaramevānyaśabdenocyate | yadyevametacchabde-
nāpi tarhi jyotiṣṭoma evocyatāṃ sarvasaṃsthaścāsāviti sarvasaṃsthasyaiva pūrvatvamapi syāta ityatrāha
---agniṣṭomasaṃsthasya nityatvāt ya etenetyucyate nityānityasaṃyogavirodhena nityasaṃsthasya
prakaraṇamiti pūrvatvavidhau nityatvādagniṣṭomasaṃstha evocyate | tathā cāpattivihārāvupapannau
bhavataḥ | bhede satyāpattivihārau nānyathā | jyotiṣṭomāpekṣayā tāvucyete na nityasaṃsthā-
pekṣayā vihāravākye ‘yajñāta’
 iti śravaṇāt sarve ca jyotiṣṭomā yajña iti | ato’rthavādava-
śenāpattivihāraviṣayo yajñāntarameva anyaśabdenocyate | āpattyākṣepaparihārau bhāṣya
evoktau |

ekastome vā | ekastomānāṃ vākyaśeṣādanyeneti grahaṇam | vākyaśeṣe eka-
stomāḥ kratavaḥ kīrtyanta iti ta evānyaśabdenāpi grāhyāḥ nacānyenetyanyatvamātrāvagateḥ
nacaikastomā grāhyāḥ anyaśabdādanyamātrapratīteḥ | nanu ca vākyaśeṣādviśeṣāvagatiri-
tyuktam | anyathā hi arthavādapadānāṃ atiparokṣā vṛttiḥ syādityarthaḥ | uttaraṃ

ṛjuvimalāpañcikāyāṃ

caturthaḥ pādaḥ

kramakopaḥ | ‘atra balābalacintā’
 ityanena arthācca ityadhikaraṇe balābalacintā
na kṛteti darśayati | pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ avidheyatvāt kramasya avagateśca
tulyatvādvikalpaḥ iti | rāddhāntastu vidheyavirodhādavagaterevātulyatvamiti śrutyarthayorbala-
vattvamuktam | āśvino daśamo gṛhyate taṃ tṛtīyaṃ juhoti [iti]grahaṇe daśamatā āśvinasya
daśamaprātipadikābhihitā vākyena viniyuktā aṅgabhṛtā niyogasiddhayarthamupādeyeti vidheyā |
tasyāṃ cāśrīyamāṇāyāṃ 1paurvāparyabalena viśeṣe sati yādṛśī vitatiravagamyate sā śrutipramāṇikā
akṣaramūlatvāt | tatra yadi pāthakrama ādriyate vihitaṃ daśamatvamaṅgabhūtameva nopapadyate |
ato vidheyavirodhāt pāthakramo durbalaḥ | tathā arthakrame 3yavāgvā vidheyāyāḥ pāthakrameṇa
homasya parastātpāke homaṃ prati sādhanatvānupapatteḥ vidheyavirodhāt pāthakramasya durbalatvam |
atra śrutyarthayorvirodhe kathaṃ ? uttaraṃ---naivodāharaṇamasti atha tvasti artha eva tarhi
(1) P. 1335 (2) G. paurvāparyaviśeṣe (3) G. yavāgūtpattipāthakrameṇa

[p. r1139]

balīyān kārye hyatrārthaśabdenocyate | kāryārthaśca kramaḥ | ataḥ śrutimapabādhate kāryaviṣa-
tvāttasyāḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati---śrutigamyaṃ kāryaṃ tena śrutyarthayorvirodha eva nāsti | atha
kāryaṃ kutaścit śrutinirapekṣamevāvagataṃ tadā tadvirodhinī śrutirnādaraṇīyā, kāryasya prameyatvāt
śruteśca pramāṇatvāt prameyārthatvāt pramāṇasyeti |

avadānābhidhāraṇa | atrāpi pravṛttikramamukhyakramayoḥ balābalacintā | pravṛtteḥ
pūrvapravṛttatvāt pravattirbalīyasīti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | dadhidharmāṇāṃ yā pravṛttiḥ
anuṣṭhānaṃ sā pūrvedyuḥ pravṛttā | tannibandhanaścaiva pravṛttikramaḥ iti pravṛttirbalīyasīti manyate |
kāryārthatvātkramasya avadānasya ca pradānopakramatvāt mukhyakramo balīyaniti rāddhāntaḥ |
yathā kāryaṃ nirvarṇyate tathā kramo grāhyaḥ kāryārthattvāttasya homaścāvadānādīnāṃ kāryaṃ, tena tadvaśena
ya kramaḥ sa ādaraṇīyaḥ | pradānopakramatvāccāvadānādīnāṃ yathāpradānameva kramo grāhya iti |
darśanamuktam |

(1) P. 1337

[p. r1140]

vacanādiṣṭipūrvatvam | atrāpyakṣaraparaṃ kramamaṅgīkṛtya cintā | akṣarebhyaḥ paraṃ1
akṣaraparaṃ akṣaravaśenāvagamyata iti yāvat | na punaḥ kramaparāṇyevākṣarāṇi kintu kāla-
parāṇi kālārthatvātsaṃyogasya | tena kālasaṃbandharūpe paurvāparye sati vitativiśeṣaḥ kramātmako-
’vasīyata ityakṣaraparatā | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī ‘somena yakṣyamāṇa’
 iti nākṣaraparatā kramasya
sambhavatīti manvānaḥ iṣṭipūrvatāmevāha | kathaṃ punarna saṃbhavati ? ādhānavidhānaparatvā-
dvākyasya | somena yakṣyamāṇo’gnīnādadhīta iti ādhānameva somena yakṣyamāṇasya vidhīyate
2na punarādhānānantaryaṃ somayāgasya vidhīyate | tadā hyevamucyeta ādhānāntaraṃ somena
yajeteti | na caivamuktam---darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiṣṭvā iti tu kālavidhānāt kramasiddhiḥ |
atrābhidhīyate---yeyamadyatanī vibhaktiḥ sā nādhānānantaryādṛte sambhavatīti tatrāpyarthāt
kramasiddhiḥ | somena yakṣyamāṇo’gnīnādadhīta iti śrūyate | tatreyaṃ bhaviṣyadadyatane vibhaktiḥ
adyatane’syāsaṃbhavāt ādhānānantaryaṃ lakṣayati | yadyānantaryamapi na lakṣayet anupapannārthaiva
hi syāt | tena somādhānayostāvadānantaryaṃ pratīyate | kva punariyaṃ na saṃbhavati ? kasyāṃ
punaravasthāyāṃ kimadhikāre prayoge yeyamadyatanī vibhaktiḥ ānantaryādṛte na saṃbhavati ?
adhikāre | yadi jyotiṣṭomādhikāre ānantaryamapi na syāt tadeyaṃ na saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ |
tasmādanantaramadhikāraḥ adhikārāyattaṃ cānuṣṭhānamiti somānuṣṭhānamanantaraṃ pratīyata iti
(1) G. parama (2) G. na punarādhānaṃ kartavyaṃ

[p. r1141]

ādhānavidhānepyarthāt kramākṣepaḥ | yadyapyādhānaṃ vidhīyate tathāpyanantarānuṣṭhānāvasitasom-
yāga ādadhīta iti śāstrārthāvagamāt | atassiddhaṃ ādhānānantaraṃ soma iti | abhyuccayo
darśitaḥ |

utkarṣādbrāhmaṇasya | asminnadhikaraṇe pūrvādhikaraṇārthasya viṣayaviśeṣe’vasthā-
panam | yadanyat tat prāsaṅgikameva | anenāsaṅgatidoṣaṃ pariharati | arthastu yadetat
pūrvādhikaraṇe sādhitamiṣṭipūrvatvaṃ somapūrvatvaṃ ca u/?/(na)kalpau iti tasya viśeṣe brāhmaṇaviṣaye1
’vasthāpanaṃ kriyate | ataḥ kiṃ trayāṇāmapi varṇānāmubhau kalpau uta brāhmaṇasya somapūrvatvameva
mūlavicāro’yaṃ yadanyat kimūrdhvaṃ somātkarmāntaramiti | tathā hi kiṃ kevalā paurṇamāsyutkṛṣyate
brāhmaṇasyomau kalpau iti | tatprāsaṅgikaṃ vināpyanena vicāreṇa pūrvapakṣasiddhāntayossiddhatvāt |
atra bhāṣyakāreṇa kṛtsnau darśapūrṇamāsāvutkṛṣyete brāhmaṇasya somapūrvatvameveti sādhayitvā’nantaraṃ
idaṃ vicāritaṃ-kimādhānakālabādhaḥ uta somakālabādha iti | tadākṣipati---yaḥ punarayamantarā-
garbhiṇyupanyāsaḥ sa kimarthaḥ | asyārthaḥ---pūrvasādhitameva kalpadvayaṃ gṛhītvedamadhikaraṇa-
mārabdhavyaṃ(bdhaṃ) anyathā’dhikaraṇānārambhāt | ataḥ somakālabādhanasādhanena kalpadvaya-
sādhanāt antarāgarbhiṇyupanyāso’narthakaḥ | uttaraṃ---evamartho’sau | yadyanantaraṃ somādhānaṃ
tadā brāhmaṇasya kalpadvayaṃ saṃbhavati anyasya ca svakāle punariṣṭipūrvatvameva niyamyate
(1) P. 1337 (2) G. viśeṣe

[p. r1142]

somaścādhānaṃ ca somādhānam | yadyanantaraṃ parasparamavyavahitaṃ syāta yadyādhānānantaraṃ somaḥ
syāt tadā kalpadvayasyātmalābhāt brāhmaṇasyānyasya vā kalpadvayaṃ saṃbhavati |
svakāle punaḥ somasyeṣṭipūrvatvameveti tadidamanupapannaṃ yasmādevamucyamāne’dhikaraṇārambhaṃ prati
hetutvāt aupoddhātikī cintā syāt na punarantarāgarbhiṇī | atra vadāmaḥ | antarāga-
rbhiṇītve ayaṃ hetuḥ | pūrvaṃ hi agnīṣomīyasya pratyabhijñānena karmāntaratve niraste sati
tadvadādhānasyāpi karmāntaratvaṃ nāstīti siddhe sati vidheyāntarābhāvāt somakālabādhe sati
somāvānayorānantaryasiddhiḥ | anyathā svakālāvasthita eva some purastādanantarakālabhāvyā-
dhānānantarameva vidhīyate | tataśca kalpadvayābhāvassyāditi 1kalpadvayasiddhayarthamantaraiveyaṃ cintā
kriyata iti | saṃprati mūlādhikaraṇapūrvapakṣamāha---kathaṃ punaridamavagamyate brāhmaṇasyaiva kalpa-
dvayamiti | na hi kalpadvaye’pi brāhmaṇaśravaṇamasti | ato’viśeṣāta sarveṣāmeva kalpadvaya-
miti | rāddhāntastu iṣṭipūrvatve haviṣa utkarṣāt | yasmādiṣṭipūrvatve’gnīṣomīyapuroḍāśā-
tmakasya haviṣa utkarṣaḥ sa ca muktasaṃśayo brāhmaṇasyaiva | tata iṣṭipūrvatvakalpe brāhmaṇasyaikapradhāna-
nyūnadarśapūrṇamāsaprayogāpatteriyamapekṣā upajāyate---kena prakāreṇa brāhmaṇaḥ prathamameva saṃpūrṇau
darśapūrṇamāsau kuryāta iti | tasyāṃ cāpekṣāyāṃ aparassomapūrvatvakalpaḥ upadiśyate ityapekṣita-
vidhiparyālocanayā brāhmaṇasyaiva somapūrvatvaṃ vijñāyata iti siddhaṃ brāhmaṇasyaiva kalpadvayam |
tadidamupasaṃharannāha---tasmāt somādhānaṃ tasyaiva anyathā hi brāhmaṇasyaivotkarṣe’napekṣi-
tārthavidhānaṃ syāt | brāhmaṇasyaivaikahavirutkarṣe yadi sarveṣāmeva somapūrvatvaṃ vidhīyate
tadā’napekṣitārthavidhānaṃ syāt | śeṣamuktam |

(1) P. 1325 (2) G. kalpadvayasyārthasiddhimantaraiveyaṃpuroḍāśastvanirdeśe | ekahaviṣa utkarṣa iti sthite tadviśeṣāvadhāraṇāyedama-
dhikaraṇam | anenāvāntarasaṅgatiruktā | uktaṃ ca bhāṣyakāreṇedamadhikaraṇam |

vikṛteḥ | pūrvaṃ nyāyato’saṃbhavamuktvā tasminnevāhani samāptyupakramau darśitavān
sasbhavārtham | atredamabhimatam---ihādhikaraṇe nyāyata eva kiṃ vikṛtīnāṃ sadyaskālatā[uta]
rdvayahakālateti cintyate | sadyaskālatāvacanamasaditi kṛvā cintā | atra pūrvapakṣavādī
prakṛtivikṛtyoḥ dvayahakālatāyāṃ paurṇamāsīkālasyālpatvenāsaṃbhavaṃ manvānaḥ pratyakṣaśrutatvāt prakṛte-
rdvayahakālasyānugrhāya pratipadyeva vikṛtīnāmupakramasamāptī ityāha | rāddhāntī tu codakaprāpta-
dvayahakālatānugrahāya caturdaśyāṃ vikṛtīnāmupakramaḥ pañcadaśyāṃ ca samāptirityuktavān | prati---
panmātra(pra)yoge tu iha pañcadaśīsaṃbandho dvayahakālatā cātyantabādhitau syātām | yathoktena
prakāreṇa kevalapratipatsaṃbandho bādhita iti | akṣarārthastvitthaṃ---pūrvanyāyato’lpatayā
paurṇamāsīkālasyomayasaṃbandhābhāvāt pratyakṣaśrutatayā ca prakṛterdvayahakālatvena vikṛterdvayahakāla-
tāyā asaṃbhavaṃ pūrvapakṣadṛṣṭyā bhāṣyakāra uktvā asminneva pañcadaśe’hani dvayahakālatvāya
caturdaśyupakrāntāyā vikṛteḥ samāptiṃ pratipadi samāpayiṣyamāṇāyāśca prakṛteḥ upakramaṃ
darśitavān dvayordvayahakālatāsaṃbhavārthamiti na vacanapratipakṣatā saṃbhavatīti vacanāta sadyaskālatā
(1) P. 1338 (2) G. pratipanmātrayogitve

[p. r1144]

vikṛtīnāmityuktavān | evaṃ nyāyato vicārya saṃprati tathā’nuṣṭhānaṃ mābhūditi vacanamuddhāṭitaṃ
na vacanapratipakṣatā nyāyamātrasya saṃbhavatīti | dvayahakālatākārye sadyaskālatāvidhānāta
sadyaskālataiva vikṛtīnāmityuktavān bhāṣyakāraḥ |

sānnāyya | tathā somavikārāḥ | atra sūtrakārastathāśabdaṃ prayuñjānaḥ adhikaraṇa-
dvaye’pi tulyo nyāya ityāha | sa kathaṃ tulyo nyāya ityatrāha---ihādhikaraṇadvayepi
nādhikārādṛte kālalakṣaṇā saṃbhavatīti kṛtādhikārasyaiva kramasyādhikāra iti tadvi-
kārāṇāmapyevameveti yuktam | asyārthaḥ---yadi sānnāyyāgnīṣomīyayoḥ somasyādhikāro
viśeṣe nāvasthāpyate tadā somasya darśapūrṇamāsayośca svakīyottarakālatālakṣakatvaṃ na syāt |
tasmāt sānnāyyāgnīṣomīyayoḥ yo’dhikārī sa kṛtasomaḥ eva | tathā kṛtadarśapūrṇamāsa eva
somādhikārīti yantrite cādhikāriṇi kālo niyantuṃ śakyate nānyathetyadhikaraṇadvayepi
tulyanyāyateti | yadi tulyo nyāyaḥ kimiti tarhi adhikaraṇadvayamiti tadāha---kimartha-
madhikaraṇadvayamiti | uttaraṃ-pūrvatra kartṛtānirdeśāt 1nāsomayājīti somayāgasya kartā
sānnāyye kartṛtayā’bhidhīyate | yadi ca somayājino’dhikāraḥ syāt tadā tathāvidhaḥ
kartā bhavet, nānyathā | kṛtasome cādhikāriṇi balāt somottarakālatā bhavatīti yuktaṃ
(1) G. somayājīti

[p. r1145]

tatrottarakālatvam | dvitīyādhikaraṇe tu darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiṣṭveti naivamiti
punarāśaṅkitavān | kartṛnirdeśābhāvāt | kathaṃ tarhi rāddhānte nyāyatulyatā ? uttaraṃ
---nādhikārādṛte kālalakṣaṇā sambhavatīti tulyatoktā | asyārthaḥ---atrāpi tu ktvā-
pratyayayogādibhiḥ nirvṛttadarśapūrṇamāsasamānakartṛkatā somasyāpyavagamyamānā kṛtadarśupūrṇamāsa eva
some kartetyavagamayāṃte | na cātathābhūte2’dhikāriṇi tathābhūtasya kartṛtopapadyate iti
labdhe’dhikāriṇi tadbalenaiva darśapūrṇamāsottarakālatā somasyāvagamyata iti tulyatoktā |

iti mahāmahopādhyāyaśālikanāthaviracitāyāṃ ṛjuvimalāyāṃ
